### [1] [PLECTA: Overlap-aware instance segmentation of dense strand networks from binary masks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21283)
*Oday Allan,Igor Ostanin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PLECTA 是一种确定性方法，仅依赖标记股线中心线的二值图像即可完成单股分割（strand segmentation），并将其建模为精确最大权重部分匹配（exact maximum-weight partial-matching）问题，在合成与真实电子显微图像上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碳纳米管薄膜（carbon-nanotube sheets）、胶原蛋白与纸张等材料由相互重叠的股线（strands）构成，其排布影响有效性质。从显微图像测量该几何结构需要在交叉与信号缺失处追踪单股，但易出现不同股线被合并或单股被切碎的问题。

Method: PLECTA 在每个交叉点（junction）判定哪些股线段彼此延续，允许段终止，并将该决策形式化为带未配对段显式惩罚的精确最大权重部分匹配问题，且不限制进入交叉的段数；输出允许交叉像素同属多条股线。另设一阶段利用对应灰度显微图（greyscale micrograph）估计各交叉处的上下层顺序。

Result: 在合成场景（synthetic scenes）中，PLECTA 在每个测试密度下对股线身份的解析准确率均高于三种已公开的替代方法；在独立发布的生成器（generator）以自身设置运行时该排序保持一致。

Conclusion: 完整流程在碳纳米管薄膜的扫描电子显微图（scanning electron micrographs）上从原始图像输出带交叉顺序估计的单股实例，且训练与流程输入均无需人工标注（manual annotations）。

Abstract: Many materials and tissues, including carbon-nanotube sheets, collagen and paper, comprise overlapping strands whose arrangement influences their effective properties. Measuring this geometry from micrographs requires individual strands to be traced through crossings and signal gaps, which merge distinct strands or split one strand into disconnected fragments. We present PLECTA (from Latin plecta, a braid), a deterministic method that segments individual strands using only a binary image whose foreground pixels mark strand centrelines. At each junction, PLECTA selects which strand segments continue into one another while allowing segments to terminate. It formulates this decision as an exact maximum-weight partial-matching problem, with an explicit penalty for unpaired segments and no restriction on the number of segments entering a junction. The output permits crossing pixels to belong to multiple strands. On synthetic scenes, PLECTA resolved strand identities more accurately than three released alternatives at every density tested. The same ranking held on an independently published generator run at its own settings. A separate stage uses the corresponding greyscale micrograph to estimate which strand lies on top at each crossing. The complete workflow is demonstrated on scanning electron micrographs of carbon nanotube sheets, from raw images to individual strand instances with estimated crossing order, without manual annotations for training or as pipeline inputs.

</details>

### [2] [Automating literature screening and knowledge synthesis with large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18588)
*Zhanzhao Li,Qiyao He,Weiqiang Chen,Kengran Yang,Kai Gong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该工作提出一个由大语言模型（large language model, LLM）驱动的统一框架，可自动完成文献筛选（literature screening）、结构化信息抽取（structured information extraction）与知识综合（knowledge synthesis），并在材料科学任务上验证了其准确性、效率与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学文献快速扩张，但由于在大型、异构文献语料（heterogeneous literature corpora）中进行导航需要大量人工工作量，大量知识难以被组织和复用；现有依赖出版元数据（publication metadata）的文献计量分析（bibliometric analysis）无法实现内容层面的量化。

Method: 构建可泛化的 LLM 驱动框架，在统一工作流内实现文献筛选、全文结构化信息抽取与知识综合；在三个范围、语料规模与类别不平衡（class imbalance）程度不同的材料科学筛选任务上，对 11 个先进专有与开放权重（open-weight）LLM 进行基准测试（benchmark）。

Result: 7 个模型在所有任务上的平衡准确率（balanced accuracy）超过 0.90，最佳模型达 0.96–0.98；筛选 10,000 条记录仅需约四分钟，而人工筛选约需 83 人时。框架还可从全文提取结构化信息，实现研究格局的内容级量化，揭示方法学趋势与未被充分探索的方向（以混凝土材料科学中的机器学习为例），并识别出常规检索策略难以获取的含硅酸盐玻璃密度数据的文献。

Conclusion: 结果表明 LLM 驱动的文献分析具有跨科学领域的广泛适用性，有望将大规模非结构化文献语料转化为结构化、可分析的知识，支撑下游假设生成、研究设计与数据驱动的科学发现。

Abstract: Scientific literature is expanding rapidly, yet much of its knowledge remains difficult to organize and reuse due to the substantial workload needed to navigate large, heterogeneous literature corpora. Here, we develop a generalizable large language model (LLM)-powered framework that automates literature screening, structured information extraction, and knowledge synthesis within a unified workflow. We benchmarked 11 state-of-the-art proprietary and open-weight LLMs across three materials science screening tasks differing in scope, corpus size, and class imbalance. Seven LLMs achieved balanced accuracies above 0.90 across all tasks, with the best-performing LLM reaching 0.96--0.98, demonstrating the potential of LLMs to accurately screen large, highly imbalanced scientific corpora. The framework also substantially reduces manual effort, screening 10,000 records within four minutes compared with approximately 83 person-hours for manual screening. Beyond literature screening, the framework extracts structured information from full-text articles, enabling content-level quantification of research landscapes that extends traditional bibliometric analysis based on publication metadata. This capability reveals methodological trends and underexplored directions, as shown for machine learning in concrete materials science. We further demonstrate its utility for information and data discovery by identifying publications containing silicate glass density data that are difficult to retrieve using conventional search strategies, which can support downstream hypothesis development, research design, and data-driven scientific discovery. Overall, these results demonstrate the broad applicability of LLM-powered literature analysis across scientific domains and its potential to transform large, unstructured literature corpora into structured, analyzable knowledge at scale.

</details>

### [3] [ECPack.wl: A Mathematica Package for Elliptic Curves over the Rationals and Finite Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20548)
*B. Ananthanarayan,Darsh R. Bhatt,Parastoo Taghizadeh Asl*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文介绍 Mathematica 软件包 ECPack.wl，用于在有理数域（rationals，ℚ）与有限域（finite fields，𝔽_q）上分析和计算椭圆曲线（elliptic curves）的性质与变换，服务于高能物理中的椭圆费曼积分（elliptic Feynman integrals）等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是为一般椭圆曲线及其变换的分析提供基础计算工具，支撑高能物理（尤其椭圆费曼积分）中的相关计算；同时相较 McGee 的既有软件包，扩展至所有形式的椭圆曲线。

Method: 方法为在 Mathematica 中实现 ECPack.wl，涵盖长 Weierstrass 型（long Weierstrass form）、四次型（quartic form）与 Legendre 型等曲线形式，并实现对一般情形及特征 2、3 域（characteristic 2 and 3 fields）所需的变换。

Result: 该包在 ℚ 与 𝔽_p 上计算点运算（point arithmetic）、极小性检验（minimality checks）、秩界与解析秩（rank bounds and analytic rank）、群结构（group structure）、不变量（invariants）以及 Weil 与 Tate 配对（Weil and Tate pairings），并给出单参数族椭圆曲线奇异纤维的 Kodaira 分类（Kodaira classification）及相应 F-理论数据（F-theory data）。

Conclusion: ECPack.wl 为一般椭圆曲线研究提供了一套较完整的基础计算工具，显著扩展了既有软件包的功能范围；软件包及相关 notebook 已在 GitHub 公开。

Abstract: We present a Mathematica package entitled ECPack.wl, where we implement many important properties and commands relevant to the study of elliptic curves over the rationals and finite fields, namely $\mathbb{Q}$ and $\mathbb{F}_q$, where $q=p^r$ and $p$ is prime. By streamlining the analysis of general elliptic curves and their transformations, ECPack.wl offers foundational computational tools for applications in high-energy physics, including elliptic Feynman integrals. This package significantly extends the functionalities of a package due to McGee in so far as we are able to study all versions of elliptic curves, such as the long Weierstrass, quartic and Legendre forms. We implement suitable transformations that are required for the general case, as well as those associated with fields of characteristic 2 and 3. Furthermore, the package computes many curve properties, such as point arithmetic, minimality checks, rank bounds and analytic rank, group structure, invariants, and the Weil and Tate pairings, over both $\mathbb{Q}$ and $\mathbb{F}_p$, as well as the Kodaira classification of the singular fibres of one-parameter families of elliptic curves, with the associated F-theory data. The package and its accompanying notebooks are available at https://github.com/DhamakedarDarsh/Mathematica-Code-and-worked-out-notebooks-for-Elliptic-Curves

</details>

### [4] [Computing rare events in Lévy-driven systems via optimal control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19481)
*Jinan Pan,Timo Schorlepp*

Main category: stat.CO

TL;DR: 该文将样本路径大偏差理论的最优控制表述推广至轻尾Lévy噪声系统，通过数值最小化运行代价获得瞬子（instanton），在无需解Hamilton方程的情况下处理退化与乘性噪声，并给出跳跃问题的判别与数值处理及前因子计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有基于最优控制的瞬子数值方法在高斯噪声SDE中有效，但尚未覆盖轻尾Lévy噪声系统；后者在小跳跃、高强度尺度极限下满足Freidlin-Wentzell型大偏差原理，需要相应的数值与解析工具。

Method: 在所给动力学约束下，对噪声实现数值最小化由噪声累积量生成函数（cumulant generating function）的Legendre变换给出的运行代价；由此直接得到瞬子而无需解Hamilton方程，并自然处理退化与乘性噪声；对连续瞬子导出Riccati与Fredholm行列式的前因子表达式，并讨论跳跃情形下所需的修正；跳跃通过冻结物理时间的重参数化数值求解。

Result: 当累积量生成函数定义域有界时，大噪声下代价仅线性增长，瞬子可包含跳跃；分析了跳跃在何种条件下不仅可行而且最优（取决于代价的次主导行为与漂移项）；方法在二维非线性模型、具非高斯翻滚噪声的活动run-and-tumble粒子以及Lévy扰动太阳强迫的Ghil-Sellers能量平衡气候模型上经Monte Carlo模拟验证。

Conclusion: 最优控制框架可有效推广到轻尾Lévy噪声，实现瞬子与尖锐概率估计的数值计算，并统一处理连续与含跳跃的瞬子及前因子评估，数值结果与Monte Carlo一致。

Abstract: We extend the optimal control formulation of sample path large deviation theory, which has proven effective for numerical computation of instantons and sharp probability estimates in stochastic differential equations with Gaussian noise, to systems with light-tailed Lévy noise. In the scaling limit of small jumps and high intensity, such systems satisfy a large deviation principle of Freidlin-Wentzell type, with a running cost given by the Legendre transform of the noise cumulant generating function. We propose to numerically minimize this cost over noise realizations subject to the system dynamics, which yields the instanton without having to solve Hamilton's equations, and naturally handles degenerate and multiplicative noise. When the cumulant generating function has a bounded domain, the cost grows only linearly for large noise, and instantons can contain jumps. We analyze when jumps are not only admissible but optimal, depending on the subleading behavior of the cost and on the drift, and show how jumps can be resolved numerically through a time reparameterization that freezes physical time. For continuous instantons, we derive Riccati and Fredholm determinant expressions for the leading-order prefactor, and we discuss the modifications needed when the instanton jumps. We illustrate and validate our methods against Monte Carlo simulations in several examples: a two-dimensional nonlinear model, an active run-and-tumble particle with non-Gaussian tumbling noise, and the Ghil-Sellers energy balance climate model with Lévy-perturbed solar forcing.

</details>

### [5] [Beyond Finite Dimensions: Duflo-Serganova Cohomology, the Formal Superdimension, and a Kac-Wakimoto Theorem for Unitarizable Supermodules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20729)
*Steffen Schmidt*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文引入基本经典李超代数及 sl(n|n) 上的相对全纯离散序列超模，证明其刻画了超矩阵系数模中心平方可积的酉最高权超模，并给出形式超维数、Kac-Wakimoto 定理及广义 Duflo-Serganova 上同调理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为相对全纯离散序列超表示（relative holomorphic discrete series supermodules）建立系统理论，并通过广义 Duflo-Serganova 上同调框架推进 Kac-Wakimoto 猜想。

Method: 定义相对全纯离散序列超模及其形式超维数（formal superdimension），借助导出 Zuckerman 函子（derived Zuckerman functors）实现该超模，并对非整最高权用广义 Duflo-Serganova 上同调（generalized Duflo-Serganova cohomology）进行计算；同时发展酉超模的广义 Duflo-Serganova 上同调理论。

Result: 证明相对全纯离散序列超模恰为全球化后超矩阵系数模中心平方可积的酉最高权超模；形式超维数在符号意义下等于 L-包超特征（L-packet supercharacter）在零点的取值；形式超维数非零当且仅当最高权为极大非典型（maximally atypical），整权情形下等于同非典型度有限维单超模的超维数；酉单超模的上同调仍酉且为零或单模。

Conclusion: 相对全纯离散序列超模与形式超维数构成统一框架，Kac-Wakimoto 定理在形式超维数层面成立，广义 Duflo-Serganova 上同调为 Kac-Wakimoto 猜想提供理论工具。

Abstract: The main results of this manuscript are fourfold. First, we introduce relative holomorphic discrete series supermodules over basic classical Lie superalgebras $\mathfrak{g}$ and $\mathfrak{sl}(n\vert n)$. These are unitarizable highest weight supermodules whose $\mathfrak{g}_{\bar{0}}$-constituents are Harish-Chandra modules of relative holomorphic discrete series representations. We show that these are exactly the unitarizable highest weight supermodules whose globalizations have supermatrix coefficients square-integrable modulo the center. Second, we define the formal superdimension of a relative holomorphic discrete series supermodule as the parity-weighted sum of the formal dimensions of its $\mathfrak{g}_{\bar{0}}$-constituents. We show that it equals, up to sign, the value at zero of an $L$-packet supercharacter obtained by averaging over the Weyl group. Third, we prove a Kac-Wakimoto theorem for formal superdimension: it is nonzero if and only if the highest weight is maximally atypical. For integral highest weights, it equals the superdimension of a finite-dimensional simple supermodule with the same degree of atypicality. We realize these supermodules through derived Zuckerman functors. For nonintegral highest weights, it is computed by generalized Duflo-Serganova cohomology. Finally, to establish the Kac-Wakimoto conjecture, we develop the theory of generalized Duflo-Serganova cohomology for unitarizable supermodules. We prove that the cohomology of a unitarizable simple supermodule is unitarizable and either zero or simple.

</details>


### [6] [Polynomial twisted periods and affine vertex algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20659)
*Tomoyuki Arakawa,Wenbin Yan*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文证明了 Kang–Lawrie–Song 猜想：单边（simply-laced）边界可容许仿射顶点代数的 Zhu C₂-代数同构于某不变势函数的 Jacobian 环，并将该描述推广至 Drinfeld–Sokolov 约化与非单边情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zhu 的 C₂-代数（C₂-algebra）是顶点代数表示论中的核心不变量，但其具体结构通常难以计算；已有猜想指出它与 Jacobian 环（Jacobian ring）存在联系，本文旨在给出证明并扩展其适用范围。

Method: 通过 Chevalley 同构（Chevalley isomorphism）将 Cartan 子代数上的扭周期多项式（twisted period）提升为不变势函数（invariant potential），证明相应 C₂-代数与该势的 Jacobian 环同构；并借助 Slodowy 切片（Slodowy slice）的限制刻画 Drinfeld–Sokolov 约化后的 C₂-代数。

Result: 证实了 Kang–Lawrie–Song 猜想；对应 Drinfeld–Sokolov 约化（Drinfeld–Sokolov reduction）的 C₂-代数等同于势函数在 Slodowy 切片上限制的 Jacobian 环；并将 Jacobian 表示推广到非单边主边界可容许仿射顶点代数。

Conclusion: C₂-代数可由 Jacobian 环统一刻画；在非单边情形，势函数在 Cartan 子代数上的限制满足带根长相关重数（root-length-dependent multiplicities）的 Dunkl 奇异性方程（Dunkl-singularity equation），表明该框架具有更广的适用性。

Abstract: We prove the conjecture of Kang, Lawrie, and Song that Zhu's $C_2$-algebra of a simply-laced boundary admissible affine vertex algebra is isomorphic to the Jacobian ring of an invariant potential obtained by lifting a polynomial twisted period from a Cartan subalgebra via the Chevalley isomorphism. We further identify the $C_2$-algebras of the corresponding Drinfeld-Sokolov reductions with the Jacobian rings of the restrictions of this potential to Slodowy slices. We also extend the Jacobian presentation to non-simply-laced principal boundary admissible affine vertex algebras, where the restriction of the potential to the Cartan subalgebra satisfies a Dunkl-singularity equation with root-length-dependent multiplicities.

</details>

### [7] [Third-body-corrected Two-Pion Femtoscopic Correlations in Au+Au Collisions at High Baryon Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21284)
*Youquan Qi*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: STAR 实验测量 Au+Au 碰撞中相同带电 π 介子 HBT 关联，发展修正方法扣除残余三体库仑效应后，π⁺π⁺ 与 π⁻π⁻ 半径在误差内一致，表明电荷依赖性主要源于三体库仑效应，初始态同位旋不对称的贡献被限制在 5% 以下（95% 置信度）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高重子密度区，π⁺π⁺ 与 π⁻π⁻ 的 HBT 半径差异可能同时来自三体库仑效应（third-body Coulomb effect）与初始态同位旋不对称（initial-state isospin asymmetry），后者与非对称核物质的状态方程（equation of state, EoS）相关，因此必须分离这两种来源才能提取 EoS 相关信息。

Method: 利用 STAR 实验在 √s_NN = 3.0、3.2、3.5、3.9、4.5 和 7.7 GeV 的 Au+Au 碰撞中采集的相同带电 π 介子关联数据进行测量，并建立修正流程以去除残余的三体库仑效应。

Result: 修正后，提取的 π⁺π⁺ 与 π⁻π⁻ HBT 半径在不确定度范围内一致；观测到的电荷依赖性由三体库仑效应主导，初始态同位旋不对称对相对 HBT 半径差 (R_π⁻ − R_π⁺)/R_π⁺ 的剩余贡献在 95% 置信水平下被限制为小于 5%。

Conclusion: 相同带电 π 介子 femtoscopy 中的电荷依赖差异主要归因于三体库仑效应而非初始态同位旋不对称，这为基于 HBT 半径差探测非对称核物质 EoS 的途径给出了严格的实验约束。

Abstract: Identical charged pion femtoscopy provides a sensitive probe of the space-time structure of particle-emitting sources in heavy-ion collisions. At high baryon density, the differences between $π^+π^+$ and $π^-π^-$ Hanbury Brown--Twiss (HBT) radii may originate from both the third-body Coulomb effect and the initial-state isospin asymmetry. Since the latter is related to the equation of state (EoS) of asymmetric nuclear matter, separating these two effects is essential. In these proceedings, we present the measurements of identical charged pion correlations in Au+Au collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}}=3.0$, 3.2, 3.5, 3.9, 4.5, and 7.7 GeV collected by the STAR experiment. A correction procedure is developed to remove the residual third-body Coulomb effect. After correction, the extracted HBT radii of $π^+π^+$ and $π^-π^-$ become consistent within uncertainties. The results indicate that the observed charge dependence is dominated by the third-body Coulomb effect, while any remaining contribution from the initial-state isospin asymmetry to the relative HBT radius difference, $(R_{π^-}-R_{π^+})/R_{π^+}$, is constrained to be less than 5\% at the 95\% confidence level.

</details>

Conclusion: 在线性/V 形阱这一 WKB 分析与经典 Agmon 局域化技术失效的情形下，所构造的拟设结合简化重构的 Agmon 估计可有效刻画基态行为。

Abstract: The Schrödinger operator in one dimension is considered. When the electric potential has a linear/V-shaped well, an approximation of the ground-state is established at fixed distance from the well. In this situation, the classical WKB analysis and the localization techniques à la Agmon near a non-degenerate minimum do not work. We propose an alternative to the WKB analysis better suited to the shape of the well and we prove that our Ansatz is an exponentially good approximation by means of revisited (and simplified) Agmon estimates.

</details>

Conclusion: 该工作把 Fricke-Klein-Vogt 关系推广到任意 n，并通过对矩阵核的刻画给出了特征标簇的显式矩阵描述。

Abstract: We show that the SL(2;C) character variety of the n-punctured sphere is described via the determinantal variety defined by an n x n symmetric matrix of rank at most 4 whose entries are the traces of simple closed curves on the surface. This result generalizes the Fricke-Klein-Vogt relations to arbitrary n, and we determine the structure of the kernel of the associated matrix.

</details>


### [12] [Hyperbolic string vertices as geometric chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20615)
*Hao Yu*

Main category: math.GT

TL;DR: 该文证明Costello–Zwiebach双曲弦顶点（hyperbolic string vertices）在0<L≤2arcsinh(1)时于模空间给出带角紧定向orbifold，其边界恰为twisted sewing空间，从而其生成链在不含括号项的主方程（master equation）下于几何链复形中精确成立，并给出连通形式的精确表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目的在于严格刻画双曲弦顶点在模空间（moduli space）中的几何与链层面性质，厘清主方程（master equation）在几何链（geometric chains）复形中的精确成立形式，并与Costello的同伦框架（homotopical framework）作比较。

Method: 取长度参数满足0<L≤2arcsinh(1)，考察由测地边界长度L、且内部简单闭测地线长度不小于L的有边双曲曲面构成的顶点区域，分析其作为带角紧定向orbifold（compact oriented orbifolds with corners）的结构及边界为twisted sewing的性质；并构造严格模型（双曲曲面带标记边界点）以比较Costello主方程的解与顶点流（vertex currents）。

Result: 对应模空间区域是带角紧定向orbifold，其边界（含定向与自同构群）为twisted sewing空间，故生成链精确满足无括号项的主方程；通常的连通形式（connected form）对几何链不能精确成立，文中确定其精确形式，证明其在模空间同调复形中模去粘合与覆盖关系后成立，且顶点流是唯一顶维解。此外，严格模型下Costello主方程在其初始条件下的每个解精确积分到顶点流，至多相差符号(-1)^(3g-3+n)，此类解存在且同伦唯一，并与双曲顶点对应。

Conclusion: 在比较映射（comparison map）的连续性假设及Costello同调计算输入成立的前提下，双曲顶点代表其解的同伦类（对完备化的同伦概念而言），从而将双曲弦顶点严格纳入Costello的同伦框架。

Abstract: For 0<L<=2arcsinh(1), the hyperbolic string vertices of Costello and Zwiebach consist of the bordered hyperbolic surfaces with geodesic boundary of length L whose interior simple closed geodesics have length at least L. We show that the corresponding regions in moduli space are compact oriented orbifolds with corners whose boundaries are, including orientations and automorphism groups, the spaces of twisted sewings. Hence their generating chain satisfies the master equation without the bracket term exactly in a complex of geometric chains. The usual connected form cannot hold exactly for geometric chains; we determine its exact form, show that it holds modulo gluing and covering relations in a complex computing the homology of the moduli spaces, and that the vertex currents are the unique top-dimensional solution. Finally we compare with Costello's homotopical framework. In a strict model given by hyperbolic surfaces with marked boundary points, every solution of his master equation with his initial condition integrates exactly to the vertex currents, up to the sign (-1)^(3g-3+n); such solutions exist, are unique up to homotopy, and correspond to the hyperbolic vertices. Under a continuity assumption on the comparison map and granting the inputs of Costello's homology computation, the hyperbolic vertices represent his homotopy class of solutions, for a completed notion of homotopy.

</details>

### [13] [Radar Waveform Design via Convex Semidefinite Relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20179)
*Samuel Pinilla*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文将窄带模糊函数（ambiguity function, AF）幅度恢复问题提升到半正定矩阵空间，转化为凸优化问题，并提出算法 wavex（松弛反射初始化＋凸半定代理步＋局部精修），可从完整或大量采样缺失的 AF 中准确恢复带限脉冲，噪声下性能平缓退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从 AF 幅度设计/恢复波形本质是相位恢复（phase retrieval）问题，且全局最优解仅在四个平凡模糊（trivial ambiguities）意义下唯一；现有窄带 AF 求解器为非凸方法，无法保证到达全局极小点，恢复结果依赖初始点。

Method: 将问题提升（lifting）到脉冲频带上支撑的半正定矩阵（positive semidefinite matrices）空间，使每个 AF 采样成为某线性泛函的幅度，从而把恢复写成凸规划（convex program），并用对偶证书（dual certificate）给出精确恢复的充分条件；算法 wavex 由松弛反射（relaxed reflections）初始化、凸半定代理步与局部精修（local polish）组成。

Result: 数值实验表明，在剩余采样足以确定脉冲的前提下，wavex 能从完整 AF 以及删除大部分采样的 AF 中准确恢复带限脉冲；随噪声增大，其精度呈平缓退化（graceful degradation）。

Conclusion: 该工作以凸化方法弥补了窄带 AF 相位恢复缺乏全局保证的空白，提供了可证明的精确恢复条件与实用算法；实现基于开源库 invexapi，复现代码已公开。

Abstract: The ambiguity function (AF) of a radar waveform describes its ability to resolve targets in range and Doppler. Designing a waveform that attains a prescribed AF magnitude is a phase retrieval problem with four trivial ambiguities, and a time- or band-limited pulse is determined up to these ambiguities by at most three times as many AF samples as it has time samples or frequencies. Existing solvers for the narrowband AF, however, are non-convex: they cannot guarantee that they reach the global minimizer, which is unique only up to the trivial ambiguities, so the recovered pulse depends on the starting point. This letter addresses this gap by lifting the problem to positive semidefinite matrices supported on the pulse band, where each AF sample becomes the magnitude of a linear functional. In the lifted space, recovery is formulated as a convex program, and a dual certificate provides a sufficient condition for exactness. The proposed algorithm, wavex, combines an initialization by relaxed reflections, convex semidefinite surrogate steps, and a local polish. Numerical experiments show that wavex accurately recovers band-limited pulses from complete AFs and from AFs with most of their samples removed, provided the remaining samples determine the pulse, and that its accuracy degrades gracefully as the noise increases. wavex is built on the open-source library invexapi, and the code that reproduces every result is available at https://github.com/samuelpinilla/waveX.

</details>

### [14] [MagBerry: A Julia package for modeling magnon and phonon topology and transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18731)
*Xiaonong Shen,Wei Ren*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: MagBerry 是一个基于 Julia 的开源软件包，将磁振子（magnon）、声子（phonon）及其杂化激发的拓扑与输运计算统一到玻色型 Bogoliubov–de Gennes 框架下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏统一处理磁振子、声子及磁振子–声子耦合的软件工具，难以在同一框架下同时计算能谱、拓扑不变量与多种输运响应。

Method: 复用 Sunny.jl 的磁性模型与线性自旋波哈密顿量，并自建谐波声子与磁振子–声子耦合模块，统一采用玻色型 Bogoliubov–de Gennes（Bogoliubov–de Gennes）表述；结合弛豫时间玻尔兹曼理论（relaxation-time Boltzmann theory）计算热流与角动量流。

Result: 可计算体态与边界谱、贝里曲率（Berry curvature）与陈数（Chern number），区分各模式的角动量；输运方面覆盖热霍尔、自旋与轨道能斯特、声子角动量霍尔响应，并评估扩展贝里曲率偶极（Berry curvature dipole）对非线性磁振子热霍尔的贡献。基于已发表模型的算例复现了拓扑磁振子边界谱；对交变磁体（altermagnet）杂化模型给出了 d 波声子角动量织构的玻尔兹曼输运系数及其随温度与温度梯度方向的变化。

Conclusion: MagBerry 提供了从能谱与拓扑到线性和非线性输运的统一计算平台，并可拓展已有研究至定量输运预测。

Abstract: We present MagBerry, a Julia package for studying the topology and transport of magnons, phonons, and their hybrid excitations. It combines magnetic models and linear spin-wave Hamiltonians from Sunny.jl with native modules for harmonic phonons and magnon--phonon coupling. These excitations are treated within a common bosonic Bogoliubov--de Gennes formulation. The package calculates bulk and boundary spectra, Berry curvature, and Chern numbers, and resolves the spin and phonon angular momentum carried by each mode. Transport calculations include thermal Hall, spin and orbital Nernst, and phonon angular momentum Hall responses. The package also evaluates the extended Berry curvature dipole contribution to nonlinear magnon thermal Hall transport. Relaxation-time Boltzmann theory gives heat and angular momentum currents. Examples based on published models reproduce topological magnon boundary spectra and illustrate phonon geometry and nonlinear transport. For an altermagnetic hybrid model, we extend a published study of the d-wave phonon angular momentum texture by calculating its Boltzmann transport coefficients. The resulting tensor describes how the longitudinal and transverse angular momentum currents vary with temperature and the direction of the temperature gradient.

</details>

### [15] [Determination of $V_{ud}$ from superallowed $β$ decays using effective field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18830)
*Samuel Novario,Graham Chambers-Wall,Vincenzo Cirigliano,Wouter Dekens,Jordy de Vries,Stefano Gandolfi,Martin Hoferichter,Emanuele Mereghetti*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 本文首次在有效场论框架下计算了12个超允许β衰变（superallowed β decay）的核结构相关辐射修正项$\barδ_\text{NS}$，采用耦合簇（coupled cluster）从头算方法并以量子蒙特卡罗（Quantum Monte Carlo）结果作为基准检验，发现当前$V_{ud}$的精度受限于以两核子接触相互作用参数化的短程贡献，不确定度约为$6\times10^{-4}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确确定$V_{ud}$需要严格控制辐射修正（radiative corrections），其中核结构相关效应的评估传统上依赖核模型，其固有不确定度难以量化，因此需要基于有效场论（effective field theory, EFT）的替代策略来评估核矩阵元的不确定性。

Method: 采用有效场论方法，首次对从$^{10}$C→$^{10}$B到$^{54}$Co→$^{54}$Fe的12个超允许跃迁计算关键修正项$\barδ_\text{NS}$；核结构输入来自耦合簇从头算（ab initio），并用轻核的量子蒙特卡罗计算进行基准检验，随后对超允许衰变率进行全局拟合。

Result: 全局拟合表明，$V_{ud}$的精度目前受限于EFT中参数化为两核子接触相互作用（two-nucleon contact interactions）的短程贡献，导致不确定度$δV_{ud}\simeq 6\times10^{-4}$。

Conclusion: 论文讨论了该结果对CKM矩阵第一行幺正性检验（first-row unitarity test）现状的影响，并提出了未来降低$V_{ud}$不确定度的路线图。

Abstract: A precision determination of $V_{ud}$ from superallowed $β$ decays requires exquisite control over radiative corrections, including nuclear-structure-dependent effects whose evaluation has traditionally relied on nuclear models. To assess the uncertainties inherent in the nuclear matrix elements, an alternative strategy based on effective field theory (EFT) has been established in recent years. Here, we present material progress in this program, in terms of a first EFT calculation of the crucial correction $\barδ_\text{NS}$ for 12 superallowed transitions ranging from $^{10}$C $\rightarrow$ $^{10}$B to $^{54}$Co $\rightarrow$ $^{54}$Fe. These main results rely on nuclear ab initio calculations using the coupled cluster method, benchmarked against Quantum Monte Carlo evaluations for light nuclei. The global fit to superallowed decay rates reveals that the precision in $V_{ud}$ is currently limited by short-distance contributions that in the EFT are parameterized as two-nucleon contact interactions, leading to an uncertainty $δV_{ud}\simeq 6\times 10^{-4}$. We discuss the consequences for the status of the first-row unitarity test of the CKM matrix as well as a roadmap how to reduce the uncertainty in $V_{ud}$ in the future.

</details>


### [16] [Ab initio calculations of ${\barδ_\text{NS}}$ and extraction of ${V_{ud}}$ using effective field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18843)
*Samuel Novario,Graham Chambers-Wall,Vincenzo Cirigliano,Wouter Dekens,Jordy de Vries,Stefano Gandolfi,Martin Hoferichter,Emanuele Mereghetti*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 基于耦合簇从头算核计算与有效场论，系统评估超允许β衰变中进入$\barδ_\text{NS}$修正的两体核矩阵元，并指出$\mathcal O(\alpha^2)$三体核矩阵元因数值因子与$Z^2$相干标度而重要，由此给出$δV_{ud}\simeq6\times10^{-4}$的短程贡献不确定度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辐射修正限制从超允许β衰变提取$V_{ud}$的精度，其中核矩阵元（NMEs）的模型依赖性缺乏清晰的组织原则，而有效场论（EFT）可提供该组织框架，故需在其指导下系统评估相关修正。

Method: 采用耦合簇（coupled cluster）方法的从头算（ab initio）核计算，并与轻核的量子蒙特卡罗（Quantum Monte Carlo）结果进行基准比对；对12个与$V_{ud}$相关的超允许跃迁评估所有两体NMEs，估计截断与哈密顿量不确定度，并发展基于核密度的形式体系以估算三体NMEs。

Result: 发现$\mathcal O(\alpha^2)$的某些三体NMEs因大数值前因子与$Z^2$相干标度而具有重要作用；EFT尺度不确定度已可忽略，剩余不确定度由重整化磁Gamow-Teller NMEs所需的接触项主导。

Conclusion: 低能常数的一个组合可由超允许数据全局拟合确定，第二个组合与$V_{ud}$强相关，需额外输入以解析核结构效应；此类短程贡献目前对应$V_{ud}$的不确定度约为$δV_{ud}\simeq6\times10^{-4}$。

Abstract: Radiative corrections limit the precision of $V_{ud}$ extractions from superallowed $β$ decays. In particular, the model dependence in the nuclear matrix elements (NMEs) is difficult to assess without a clear organizing principle, which is provided by effective field theory (EFT). We report substantial progress in this program based on ab initio nuclear calculations using the coupled cluster method. We find that certain three-body NMEs at ${\mathcal O}(α^2)$ play an important role, due to a combination of large numerical prefactors and coherent scaling with $Z^2$. We develop a formalism to estimate such NMEs based on nuclear densities to complete the EFT evaluation of $\barδ_\text{NS}$, validating the corresponding two-body NMEs against ab initio calculations. We further identify the corrections in the traditional phase-space factor that are captured by the EFT and remove them consistently, which renders the individual transitions mutually consistent. Profiting from a recent calculation of ${\mathcal O}(α^2 Z)$ ultrasoft photons, we find that the scale uncertainty in the EFT is now negligible, while the remaining uncertainty is dominated by contact terms required to renormalize the magnetic Gamow-Teller NMEs. One combination of low-energy constants can be determined from a global fit to the superallowed data, but the second one proves strongly correlated with $V_{ud}$, necessitating additional input to resolve the nuclear-structure effects. We argue that such short-distance contributions currently imply an uncertainty in $V_{ud}$ at the level of $δV_{ud}\simeq 6\times 10^{-4}$.

Abstract: The conjecture of Bando, Kasue, and Nakajima asserts that every simply connected Ricci-flat $4$-manifold that is asymptotically locally Euclidean (ALE), that is, asymptotic at infinity to a flat cone $\mathbb{R}^4/Γ$, is hyperkähler for one of its orientations. A toric ALE instanton is a Ricci-flat ALE $4$-manifold with an effective isometric action of the $2$-torus; such a manifold is simply connected. </details>

Abstract: We study the mean curvature flow in Riemannian manifolds evolving by the Ricci-Bourguignon flow. As a consequence, we show that, in compact ambient manifolds, type-I singularities of the mean curvature flow in a gradient shrinking Ricci-Bourguignon soliton background have, after suitable parabolic rescaling, weighted minimal hypersurfaces as their limiting models, while in the noncompact setting, we establish the corresponding result for a gradient shrinking Schouten soliton background. This provides a geometric framework for understanding how the ambient scalar curvature influences the formation and structure of singularity models for the mean curvature flow. We also show how to construct explicit examples of mean curvature flow in a gradient Ricci-Bourguignon soliton background.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $G_2$模空间上的Yukawa耦合三次型与Albert代数结构之间的关系此前缺乏逐点的、精确的代数刻画；本文旨在确定该二维三次型族中各元素的稳定子，并刻画其临界情形所对应的例外代数结构及其几何意义。

For $C_0+λC_\varphi$, the stabilizer has dimension $78$ precisely at the two non-real values $λ_\pm=(1\pm i\sqrt7)/8$. Its dimension is $21$ for $C_0$ and $14$ in all other cases. Abstract: Lurie's cobordism hypothesis classifies fully extended framed topological field theories valued in a symmetric monoidal $(\infty,n)$-category $\mathcal{C}$ with duals by the $\infty$-groupoid $\mathcal{C}^{\mathrm{fd}}$ of fully dualisable objects. Lurie observed that for a connective spectrum, this action is ``a version of the classical $J$-homomorphism.'' The sign (i.e.\ whether the action is $J_n$ or $\ominus J_n$) depends on the convention for the $O(n)$-action on framings, and is relevant for $\mathrm{pin}^\pm$ theories and hence for quantum systems with time-reversal symmetry. Abstract: We develop an analysis and a B-spline Galerkin method for the matrix-valued Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer (BCS) gap equation of unconventional superconductors with general long-range interaction kernels on arbitrary Bravais lattices. We define the BCS free energy functional on lattices, show that it is bounded from below, and characterise solutions of the gap equation as its stationary points. For non-repulsive kernels, we prove that fixed-point iteration never increases the free energy and drives the residual to zero. The algebraically decaying tails of the interaction are described by Epstein zeta functions, so the method applies to any power-law exponent $ν>0$, including exponents at or below the lattice dimension such as dipolar interactions in three-dimensional lattices. In the B-spline Galerkin basis, the singular convolutions correspond to block-circulant matrices whose eigenvalues are lattice sums, applied without quadrature at FFT cost. Each iteration takes log-linear work and linear memory, and Broyden updates accelerate convergence. By engineering a suitable dispersion for a prescribed gap, we obtain non-trivial analytic singlet and triplet solutions of the gap equation with power-law interactions on the integer lattice at zero temperature, to our knowledge the first ones. Benchmarks against these solutions and against highly resolved numerical data show convergence rates determined by the B-spline degree and the regularity of the solution. Finally, we compute a nodal superconductor with long-range interactions on the three-dimensional fcc lattice: a chiral state with a line node and two topologically non-trivial Weyl point nodes. On 16 cores, the method reaches a residual of $1.5\times10^{-13}$ for 6.3 million complex unknowns in less than two minutes.

Abstract: Quasiperiodic geometrical arrangements, which exhibit long-range order in the absence of periodicity, have attracted interest in the design of composites and mechanical metamaterials. In this context, direct numerical simulations explicitly resolving the quasiperiodic microstructure become prohibitively expensive for macroscopic problems, motivating the development of homogenized continuum models. However, the absence of a periodic unit cell in physical space hinders the use of conventional homogenization methods. Thereby, effective properties are obtained from unit-cell problems solved using the fast Fourier transform in higher-dimensional spaces. Abstract: We investigate the interplay of observables and symmetries in the framework of causal fermion systems (CFS). The crucial link is provided by the Dirac equation itself, which relates the infinitesimal generators of the geometric and CFS symmetries. In static spacetimes, this allows us to identify the generator of the induced unitary symmetry with the spatial Dirac Hamiltonian.

Abstract: The Bagci-Hoggan exponential-type orbitals, the generalization of Coulomb-Sturmians to noninteger quantum numbers, were recently identified as d-dimensional Coulomb-Sturmians. In the present study, this identification is treated in detail. The radial differential equation for these orbitals is derived directly from their closed form. It represents the general Coulomb-Sturmian differential equation for quantum numbers with real values. The solution is verified through a power series representation and a first-order matrix form based on the Green's function of the radial Laplacian. New transformation relationships between these orbitals and Slater-type orbitals with noninteger principal quantum numbers are established. The radial differential equation is expressed in terms of the radial momentum operator. The expectation values of the position and radial momentum operators are obtained in closed form. The radial and Heisenberg uncertainty products, the Shannon entropy and the Fisher information are derived in general form.

The antisymmetric part becomes a geometric constitutive relation based on the irreducible decomposition of torsion. Its contracted four-divergence yields a general vector-field equation with geometric source and response terms. Under a trace--axial reduction, $Q=0$ gives a source-free Maxwell-type equation, whereas constant nonzero $Q$ gives a Proca-type equation.

Thus gravitational, constitutive, Maxwell--Proca-type, and Lorenz-type structures arise as compatible consequences of one metric-affine variational system. The antisymmetric dynamical level is generated by the contracted four-divergence of its constitutive level. No independent electromagnetic or matter field is introduced.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hooke-Newton 对偶在标准经典与量子力学中已有讨论，但其在变形量子力学（如 Wigner-Yang 量子力学）中的适用性尚不明确，本文旨在考察该对偶能否延伸至此类变形体系。

In this work, we investigate and analytically solve the first problem in the special case of a mixture of hydrogen and helium atoms. As a consequence, the van der Waals law for a system of hydrogen and helium atoms, which was proven in [6] assuming our result, now follows as a corollary without assumptions. Moreover, we prove a new lower bound for the `size' of a stable molecule with several nuclei, which improves upon the lower bounds from [1] with even less assumptions. Using among others this lower bound, we prove that it costs energy to ionize a system consisting of an $H_2$ molecule and a perfectly conducting half-infinite plate, provided that they are well-separated. Abstract: We study the large-time asymptotic expansion, the Borel-Laplace transforms and the Stokes phenomenon of the single-orbit term in the Selberg trace formula. We prove that the divergent formal series is uniformly $1$-Gevrey asymptotic to the convergent single-orbit term and recover the single-orbit term by a Laplace integral along the positive real axis. ### [53] [High Characteristic Voltage YBCO Josephson Junctions Written With a Focused Helium Ion Beam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19718)
*Rochelle Y. Qu,Aravind R. Sreeja,Adhilsha Parachikunnumal,Alexander Brooks,Nicole Y. Chin,Jay C. LeFebvre,Roger K. Lake,Shane A. Cybart*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用聚焦氦离子束（He FIB）直接在 YBCO 薄膜中写入面内约瑟夫森结（Josephson junction），在 1.6 K 下获得 929 μV 的特征电压（IcR），显著高于本实验室以往报道的 200–400 μV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征电压 IcR 决定基于该结的电路工作频率与灵敏度，因此提升小尺寸器件中的 IcR 对 RSFQ、AQFP 逻辑及高温工作 SQUID 具有直接价值。

Method: 以 500 ions/nm 剂量、单线扫描方式在 4 μm 宽电极上写入结，并结合温度依赖电阻、电流-电压特性、临界电流的磁场调制以及 20.5 GHz 微波下的 Shapiro 台阶（Shapiro steps）进行表征。

Result: 在 1.6 K 测得 IcR 为 929 μV；输运测量显示约瑟夫森行为，过剩电流（excess current）低于临界电流的 5%；临界电流在结转变附近随温度陡降、低温下饱和。

Conclusion: 该行为归因于金属-绝缘体转变（metal insulator transition）处的势垒，有限温度下经局域态的跳跃导电（hopping conduction）在冷却时冻结；制备与储存流程的改进伴随性能提升，但数据尚无法区分各自贡献。

Abstract: We report in-plane Josephson junctions written directly into YBa$_2$Cu$_3$O$_{7-δ}$ (YBCO) thin films with a focused helium ion beam (He FIB). The characteristic voltage $I_{\mathrm{C}}R$ of these junctions, where $I_{\mathrm{C}}$ is the Josephson critical current and $R$ is the junction resistance, sets the operating frequency and the sensitivity of the circuits built from them. A single line irradiation with a dose of \SI{500}{ions/nm} across a \SI{4}{\micro\metre} wide electrode produced a junction with $I_{\mathrm{C}}R$ of 929 \si{\micro\volt} at \SI{1.6}{K}, which is `higher than the 200 to 400 \si{\micro\volt} typical of junctions fabricated previously in our laboratory. Temperature dependent resistance, current-voltage characteristics, magnetic field modulation of the critical current, and Shapiro steps under 20.5~GHz microwave irradiation show Josephson behavior with an excess current below 5 percent of the critical current. The critical current falls steeply with temperature near the junction transition and saturates at low temperature. We attribute this behavior to a barrier at the metal insulator transition, where hopping conduction through localized states is active at finite temperature and freezes out on cooling. We describe the changes in surface preparation, sample selection, beam focusing, and sample storage that accompanied the improvement, although the present data do not separate their individual contributions. Larger characteristic voltages at these dimensions are an encouraging result for rapid single flux quantum and adiabatic quantum flux parametron logic and for superconducting quantum interference devices operating at elevated temperature.

</details>


### [54] [Sharp bounds on the Eliashberg critical temperature: a comparison principle, convexity in the spectrum, and the Kiessling-Altshuler-Yuzbashyan conjectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20496)
*Davis Rens*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作为声子媒介超导（phonon-mediated superconductivity）Eliashberg理论中临界温度（critical temperature）的上界给出独立证明：对任意具有有限二阶矩的谱，证明 $T_c<C(\lambda\langle\omega^2\rangle)^{1/2}$ 且常数 $C\approx0.1827$ 为尖锐值，并附带证明 KAY 的第二个猜想与若干矩界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kiessling、Altshuler 与 Yuzbashyan（KAY）将 $T_c$ 刻画为 Matsubara 频率序列上紧算子（compact operator）的最大特征值，并猜想强耦合之外的一般耦合下仍成立 $T_c<C(\lambda\langle\omega^2\rangle)^{1/2}$；该界最早由 Leavens 于 1977 年基于部分数值依据提出，而 Kondratev 的预印本（尚未同行评审）给出了证明，本文旨在提供独立证明。

Method: 基于比较原理（comparison principle）：在每个 Matsubara 转移上增强配对相互作用、并在最小转移上严格增强时，最大特征值严格上升；该原理与 Kondratev 的核心步骤相同，Bergmann 与 Rainer 曾证至一阶。另采用非增向量的前缀分解（prefix decomposition）处理第三个 KAY 猜想。

Result: 对任意有限二阶矩的谱证明了 Leavens 型上界且常数 $C\approx0.1827$ 尖锐；固定 $\lambda$ 与 $\langle\omega^2\rangle$ 时单一 Einstein 模使 $T_c$ 最大；由谱的凸性得到以矩 $\int\omega^n\alpha^2F\,d\omega$（$0\le n\le1$）表示的尖锐界，含零阶矩界 $T_c\le c_0\int\alpha^2F\,d\omega$（$c_0\approx0.231$，中等耦合下最优）；所有界在对称截断的 Coulomb 赝势（Coulomb pseudopotential）下依然成立；并证明第三个 KAY 猜想。有限维核心（含该猜想）已在 Lean 4 中形式化，另有两个不依赖比较原理的计算机辅助证明（computer-assisted proof）确认 Einstein 界。

Conclusion: 该研究独立验证了 KAY 关于全耦合唯一性与 $T_c$ 上界的猜想，并扩展至矩界、Einstein 模最优性及 Coulomb 赝势情形；形式化与计算机辅助验证提升了结论的可靠性。

Abstract: In Eliashberg theory of phonon-mediated superconductivity with vanishing Coulomb pseudopotential, the critical temperature $T_c$ marks the linear instability of the normal state. Kiessling, Altshuler and Yuzbashyan (KAY) characterized it through the top eigenvalue of a compact operator on Matsubara-frequency sequences. They proved that $T_c$ is unique at strong coupling and conjectured $T_c<C(λ\langleω^2\rangle)^{1/2}$ for every coupling $λ$, with $\langleω^2\rangle$ the second moment of the normalized phonon spectrum and $C\approx0.1827$ sharp, a bound Leavens stated in 1977 on partly numerical grounds. A preprint by Kondratev, not yet peer reviewed, gives a proof of this bound and of all-coupling uniqueness. We give an independent proof, for every spectrum with finite second moment, based on a comparison principle that is also Kondratev's central step and was proved to first order by Bergmann and Rainer: strengthening the pairing interaction at every Matsubara transfer, strictly at the smallest one, strictly raises the top eigenvalue. At fixed $λ$ and $\langleω^2\rangle$ a single Einstein mode maximizes $T_c$. Convexity of the top eigenvalue in the spectrum gives sharp bounds on $T_c$ by the moments $\intω^nα^2F dω$, $0\le n\le1$, including Leavens's zeroth-moment bound $T_c\le c_0\intα^2F dω$, $c_0\approx0.231$ numerically, optimal at moderate coupling. All bounds persist under a Coulomb pseudopotential with symmetric cutoff. A prefix decomposition of non-increasing vectors proves a third KAY conjecture, not treated in the preprint. The finite-dimensional core, including that conjecture, is formalized in Lean 4, and two computer-assisted proofs independent of the comparison principle confirm the Einstein bound.

</details>


### [55] [Stabilization of LaH$_{10}$ with Thorium: Superconductivity in (La,Th)H$_{10}$ up to 232 K near 1 Mbar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20170)
*Dmitrii V. Semenok,Mikhail A. Volkov,Andrey V. Sadakov,Alexander T. Daniyarkhodzhaev,Alexey S. Usoltsev,Vladimir M. Pudalov,Mikhail I. Volgin,Iurii M. Nevolin,Toni Helm,Di Zhou,Viktor V. Struzhkin,Ivan A. Troyan*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过在La位引入Th，La(Th)H$_{10}$ 笼状结构（clathrate structure）在约1 Mbar下得到稳定，且超导性抑制很小；立方(La,Th)H$_{10}$ 在134 GPa下起始 $T_c$ = 232 K，并系统鉴定了La-Th-H体系的多层次氢化物相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三元镧基氢化物（lanthanum-based hydrides）是当前最佳高温超导体之一，但如何在维持高 $T_c$ 的同时稳定笼状结构、并厘清该体系的相组成与超导机理，仍需实验探索。

Method: 以较空气稳定的 La$_2$Th、La$_3$Th、La$_5$Th 合金为前驱体合成样品；采用接触电阻与电阻测量，并结合施加磁场、不同压力下的非接触射频（contactless radio-frequency）测量两种独立手段验证超导性；使用380 mA的创纪录脉冲电流研究 P6$_3$/mmc-(La,Th)H$_9$ 的临界现象。

Result: 立方(La,Th)H$_{10}$ 在134 GPa下起始 $T_c$ = 232 K；P6$_3$/mmc-(La,Th)H$_9$ 在173 GPa下可由s波模型（s-wave model）给出单一超导能隙 $Δ(0) = 37 \pm 4.5$ meV、$2Δ(0)/kT_c = 4.9 \pm 0.6$；另鉴定出纳米晶 I4/mmm-(La,Th)H$_4$（晶粒5–6 nm）、(La,Th)H$_6$，以及 $hP13$、$cF80$、$A15$ 相（成分 (La,Th)H$_{5.75-6.25}$，70–80 GPa下 $T_c \approx 83$ K），并发现可能的 (La,Th)H$_{16}$ 多氢化物（polyhydride）。

Conclusion: Th替代La可在约1 Mbar稳定La(Th)H$_{10}$ 笼状结构而几乎不牺牲超导性，La-Th-H体系化学相丰富且具有多级 $T_c$，为高温氢化物超导体的结构稳定化与能隙表征提供了实验依据。

Abstract: Ternary lanthanum-based hydrides are among the best high-temperature superconductors. Here we show that substituting La with thorium stabilizes La(Th)H$_{10}$ clathrate structure at $\sim$1 Mbar while only minimally suppressing superconductivity. The resulting cubic (La,Th)H$_{10}$ phase, synthesized from relatively air-stable La$_2$Th, La$_3$Th and La$_5$Th alloys, reaches an onset $\textit{T$_c$}$ = 232 K at 134 GPa, as confirmed by contact resistance measurements. Superconductivity was verified by two independent methods: electrical resistance and contactless radio-frequency measurements under applied magnetic fields at various pressures. Using a record-high pulsed current of 380 mA, we investigated critical phenomena in P6$_3$/mmc-(La,Th)H$_9$ at 173 GPa, which allowed for the experimental determination of a single superconducting gap within the s-wave model: $Δ(0) = 37 \pm 4.5$ meV and $2Δ(0)/kT_c = 4.9 \pm 0.6$. The La-Th-H system is chemically rich: in addition to the decahydride and nonahydride mentioned above, we also identified nanocrystalline I4/mmm-(La,Th)H$_4$ (5-6 nm grain size), (La,Th)H$_6$, as well as $hP13$, $cF80$ and $A15$ phases with compositions of (La,Th)H$_{5.75-6.25}$ and $\textit{T$_c$}$ $\approx$ 83 K at 70-80 GPa, and a possible (La,Th)H$_{16}$ polyhydride.

</details>


### [56] [Phase diagram and lattice structure of interlayer vortices in multilayer Ising superconductors in parallel magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20469)
*Subhradeep Mahata,Uddalok Nag,J. K. Jain*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文利用Bogoliubov-de Gennes方程自洽求解，研究面内磁场下多层s波Ising超导体的相图与涡旋结构随层数及层间跃迁的演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多层Ising超导体的相图与涡旋结构如何随层数演化尚缺乏自洽的微观描述，标准Ginzburg-Landau处理适用参数范围有限且需预设能隙形式。

Method: 对标量s波Ising超导体在面内磁场下自洽求解微观Bogoliubov-de Gennes（Bogoliubov-de Gennes）方程，比较双层、四层与无限层体系，并考察层间跃迁（interlayer hopping）的影响。

Result: 支持传统BCS相（无层间涡旋）的场区间随层数增加而缩小，在无限层体系中于非零磁通下基本消失；无限层极限下含层间涡旋的FFLO相在低温下延伸至更大面内场；四层体系中增大跃迁使涡旋由外侧层间结重排至内侧，强跃迁时内部涡旋引起的能隙畸变延伸至表面层。

Conclusion: 层数与层间隧穿是决定多层Ising超导体相图与涡旋排布的关键参数。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate layered $s$-wave Ising superconductors under an in-plane magnetic field using self-consistent solutions of the microscopic Bogoliubov-de Gennes equations. Comparing bilayer, four-layer, and infinite-layer systems, we determine how the phase diagram and vortex structure evolve with layer number. Compared to standard Ginzburg-Landau treatments, our approach applies over a broader parameter regime and self-consistently resolves the spatial and layer dependence of the gap amplitude without prescribing its form. We find that the field range supporting the conventional Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer (BCS) phase (defined as having no interlayer vortices) is reduced with increasing layer number and is essentially absent at nonzero magnetic flux in the infinite-layer system, consistent with recent thickness-dependent experiments on multilayer 2H-NbSe$_2$. In the infinite-layer limit, the Fulde-Ferrell-Larkin-Ovchinnikov (FFLO) phase containing interlayer vortices persists to progressively larger in-plane fields as temperature is lowered below the zero-field critical temperature, consistent with the experimentally observed low-temperature enhancement of the upper critical field. The vortex structure also evolves with interlayer hopping: in four-layer systems, for the fluxes and system sizes considered, increasing hopping reorganizes vortices from outer to inner interlayer junctions. At larger hopping, the gap distortion associated with an interior vortex extends to the surface layers, producing gap suppression comparable to that in the interior layers. Representative six-layer calculations show that interior vortices likewise produce a finite, although weaker, modulation of the surface-layer gap. These results highlight layer number and interlayer tunneling as key parameters governing the phase diagram and vortex arrangement in multilayer Ising superconductors.

</details>


### [57] [Inversion of magnetization anisotropy in low magnetic fields for textured Bi2223 superconductor due to magnetic responses from superconducting crystallites and intercrystalline boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20560)
*D. A. Balaev,S. V. Semenov,D. M. Gokhfeld,M. I. Petrov*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 对块体织构高温超导体Bi2223磁各向异性（magnetic anisotropy）的研究发现：在低最大外场下出现异常的各向异性反转（anisotropy inversion），ΔM(H||NTP) < ΔM(H⊥NTP)，该现象可由晶间边界子系统磁通俘获与晶粒片状形状导致的屏蔽效应解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有认识中，织构超导体在中高场下表现出H||NTP时磁滞回线分支差ΔM更大的典型各向异性，但低场区域的行为尚未充分澄清，本文旨在细致考察Bi2223在不同场强区间内的磁各向异性表现。

Method: 测量块体织构Bi2223在不同最大外场Hmax（最高至1 kOe，低温下低至约15 Oe）与温度（如T=78 K）下的磁化磁滞回线（magnetization hysteresis loop），比较H||NTP与H⊥NTP两种取向下的ΔM，并结合晶间边界穿透场（约30 Oe）分析磁通穿透行为。

Result: 中高场范围内ΔM(H||NTP) > ΔM(H⊥NTP)，符合织构超导体的典型各向异性；但在小Hmax（T=78 K时最高约15 Oe）下出现反转，ΔM(H||NTP) < ΔM(H⊥NTP)。在约30 Oe以下，磁滞仅由俘获于晶间边界子系统（intercrystallite boundary subsystem）的磁通决定。

Conclusion: Bi2223晶粒的显著片状（platelet）形貌使H||NTP（平行于晶粒c轴）时样品内部外场被部分屏蔽，导致晶间边界处的有效磁场较小，因而需要更大外场才能使磁通穿透样品内部，从而解释了观察到的各向异性反转现象。

Abstract: A detailed study of the magnetic anisotropy of a bulk textured high-temperature superconductor Bi2223 has been carried out. In the range of moderate and high fields (up to 1 kOe), the difference ΔM between the upper and lower branches of the magnetization hysteresis loop is larger when the external magnetic field H is parallel to the normal of the texturing plane (H||NTP) than when H is parallel to the texturing plane (H {\perp} NTP). This magnetic anisotropy is typical for such textured superconductors. However, at small values of the maximum applied field Hmax (up to 15 Oe at a temperature T = 78 K), an anomalous inversion of anisotropy has been found: ΔM(H||NTP) < ΔM(H {\perp} NTP). It has been established that in the field range up to ~30 Oe (the first penetration field into Bi2223 crystallites is about 30 Oe at T = 78 K), the magnetic hysteresis is determined only by the magnetic flux trapped in the intercrystallite boundary subsystem. The pronounced platelet shape of the Bi2223 crystallites leads to partial screening of the external field inside the sample when the field is oriented parallel to the crystallite c-axis. The observed inverse anisotropy ΔM(H||NTP) < ΔM(H {\perp} NTP) has been explained by the fact that the effective magnetic field into the intercrystallite boundaries is smaller for the H||NTP orientation than for H {\perp} NTP. Therefore, when the field is oriented H||NTP, a larger external field is required for magnetic flux to penetrate into the sample inner than for the H {\perp} NTP orientation.

</details>


### [58] [Failure of Mean-Field Critical Scaling and Two Distinct Dissipation Regimes in La$_2$SmNi$_2$O$_{7-δ}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21536)
*Yongze Ye,Feiyu Li,Di Peng,Junjie Zhang,Hai-Hu Wen*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文在压力下对 Tc=92.5 K 的 La2SmNi2O7-δ 单晶进行临界标度与磁通涡旋动力学分析，发现Ullah–Dorsey框架下二维/三维标度均无法在平均场临界温度附近良好塌缩，并观察到对数电阻率的两步下降对应两个耗散区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 镍酸盐（nickelates）在压力下出现高达96 K的超导起始转变温度，为研究超导机理提供新平台，但其临界涨落与涡旋动力学行为尚缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 针对 Tc^onset=92.5 K 的 La2SmNi2O7-δ 单晶，在Ullah–Dorsey框架内检验二维与三维临界标度行为，并分析对数电阻率随温度的演化以区分不同耗散区间，结合TAFF模型与超导涨落相关涡旋动力学进行对比。

Result: 二维与三维标度在 T_c^MF 附近均不能给出良好的数据塌缩（collapse）；对数电阻率呈两步下降，揭示两个不同耗散区间——低温区可由热助磁通流动（thermally assisted flux flow, TAFF）模型描述，高温区则无法用涉及超导涨落的涡旋动力学解释。

Conclusion: 标度失效可能源于双层镍酸盐的多带复合序参量（composite order parameter）：配对主要由 dz² 轨道的反铁磁交换驱动，而超流刚度主要由 dx²-y² 轨道决定；两个耗散区间的交叠可能与涡旋核的轨道依赖演化有关，表明复合序参量在临界涨落与涡旋运动耗散中起重要作用。

Abstract: Recently, superconductivity with an onset transition temperature ($T_{\mathrm{c}}^{\mathrm{onset}}$) up to 96 K has been observed in the $\mathrm{La}_{2-x}\mathrm{Sm}_{1+x}\mathrm{Ni}_2\mathrm{O}_{7-δ}$ system under pressure, providing an attractive platform for investigating superconducting mechanisms in nickelates. Here, we systematically analyzed the critical scaling and vortex dynamics for the $\mathrm{La}_2\mathrm{Sm}\mathrm{Ni}_2\mathrm{O}_{7-δ}$ single crystal with $T_{\mathrm{c}}^{\mathrm{onset}}=92.5$ K under pressure. Within the Ullah--Dorsey framework, neither the two-dimensional nor three-dimensional scaling provides a good collapse near $T_{\mathrm{c}}^{\mathrm{MF}}$. This failure may be associated with the multiband composite order parameter of bilayer nickelates, in which pairing is mainly driven by antiferromagnetic exchange in the $d_{z^2}$ orbital, whereas the superfluid stiffness is mainly governed by the $d_{x^2-y^2}$ orbital. The two-step decrease in the logarithmic resistivity reveals two distinct dissipative regimes. The low-temperature regime is well described by the thermally assisted flux flow (TAFF) model, whereas the high-temperature regime cannot be explained by the vortex dynamics involved with the superconducting fluctuation. The crossover between the two regimes may be associated with the orbital-dependent evolution of vortex cores. These results suggest the significant role of the composite order parameter in critical fluctuations and vortex-motion dissipation in nickelates.

</details>

### [59] [Slow and rapid mixing in $\mathbb{Z}_2$ hyperlattice gauge theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19051)
*Adrien Malacan*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文将 Ising 模型在 Glauber 动力学下的慢混合（slow mixing）结论推广至 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 超格规范理论（hyperlattice gauge theory）：在维数 $m\ge k+2$ 的环面上、$k$-胞腔自旋且 $\beta$ 足够大时，证明混合时间（mixing time）的显式指数下界，并给出更一般的多胞腔更新动力学版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ising 模型在环面上的 Glauber 动力学已知在 $\beta>\beta_c$ 时混合指数级慢、$\beta<\beta_c$ 时对数级快；作者希望把这一相变式的混合行为推广到高维类比系统，即 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 超格规范理论。

Method: 在任意维数 $m\ge k+2$ 的环面上研究以 $k$-胞腔（$k$-cells）为自旋载体的 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 规范理论的 Glauber 动力学，证明 $\beta$ 充分大时混合时间的显式指数下界，并将结论扩展到每一步允许不止一个 $k$-胞腔更新的一般动力学。

Result: $\beta$ 足够大时混合时间存在显式指数下界，且该下界对更广泛的动力学类（多胞腔同时更新）仍成立；同时，$\beta$ 足够小时热浴规则（heat-bath rule）下 Glauber 动力学满足 Dobrushin 条件，从而对数级混合，构成与慢混合相对的快速混合区。

Conclusion: $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 超格规范理论在环面上的 Glauber 动力学呈现与 Ising 模型类似的双区制：大 $\beta$ 慢混合（指数级）、小 $\beta$ 快混合（对数级），表明慢混合现象可推广到高维规范理论设定。

Abstract: The Ising model under Glauber dynamics on the torus is known to exhibit a phase transition in its mixing time. The mixing is exponentially slow when $β>β_c$ and logarithmically fast when $β<β_c$. In this paper, we generalize the slow-mixing result to $\mathbb{Z}_2$ hyperlattice gauge theory when $β$ is large enough. This is a higher-dimensional analogue of the Ising model, with spins on $k$-cells. Specifically, on the torus of any dimension $m \ge k+2$, we prove explicit exponential lower bounds on the mixing time of Glauber dynamics when $β$ is sufficiently large. More precisely, we prove that the result holds for a broader class of dynamics, allowing more than one $k$-cell to change its value at each step of the algorithm. Finally, we show that there are distinct slow and rapid mixing regimes, as for sufficiently small $β$ the Dobrushin condition is satisfied for Glauber dynamics under the heat-bath rule, which implies logarithmic mixing.

</details>


### [60] [DLR variational principle for the one-dimensional log-gas and another proof of existence of Sine-beta](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19271)
*Sylvain Chabredier,Thomas Leblé*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文证明一维对数气体（log-gas）在数值方差受控条件下的每个 DLR 方程驻点均最小化自由能泛函，由此建立连续长程系统的 DLR 变分原理；并利用有限体积对数气体极限点的驻点性质，给出 Sine-beta 点过程（Sine-beta point process）存在微观极限的新物理证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 DLR 理论中，Gibbs 测度与自由能变分原理的等价性在连续长程相互作用系统中并不自动成立，一维对数气体正属此类。本文旨在为这一连续长程系统补足 DLR 变分原理，并为高斯 Beta 系综（Gaussian Beta Ensembles）的微观极限提供物理解释路径。

Method: 以 Dobrushin–Lanford–Ruelle（DLR）方程刻画平稳测度，附加对数值方差（number variance）的定量控制，将驻点条件与自由能泛函（free energy functional）的最小性相联系；并分析有限体积对数气体测度的极限点。

Result: 在数值方差定量受控前提下，DLR 方程的每个平稳解均最小化自由能泛函，从而得到该系统的 DLR 变分原理；同时证明有限体积对数气体的任一极限点均为平稳的且满足该条件。

Conclusion: 由此获得高斯 Beta 系综微观极限（即 Sine-beta 点过程）存在性的新物理证明，并从变分原理角度刻画该连续长程系统的平衡态。

Abstract: We show that every stationary solution of the Dobrushin--Lanford--Ruelle (DLR) equations for the one-dimensional log-gas with a quantitative control on its number variance minimizes the free energy functional. This yields a DLR variational principle for this continuous long-range system.
  Moreover, we show that any limit point of the finite-volume log-gases is stationary and satisfies this condition. This allows us to give a new, physical proof of the existence of a microscopic limit for the Gaussian Beta Ensembles, known as the Sine-beta point process.

</details>


### [61] [On Hamilton-Jacobi equations and perceptron models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20870)
*Hong-Bin Chen,Jean-Christophe Mourrat*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文给出有限维 Hamilton-Jacobi 方程（Hamilton-Jacobi equation）变分表示的第三类情形：空间变量分裂为两组坐标，非线性项为两组偏梯度之内积，初始条件为一组坐标的凸函数与另一组坐标的任意 Lipschitz 函数之和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典结果仅在非线性项凸/凹（Hopf-Lax 公式）或初始条件凸/凹（Hopf 公式）时给出变分表示，存在未被覆盖的情形，而感知机模型（perceptron models）的极限自由能恰属于此类。

Method: 识别新的结构条件（变量分组、梯度内积型非线性、混合凸性与 Lipschitz 初始条件），并据此构造变分表示；通过将一类感知机模型改写为二分自旋玻璃（bipartite spin glasses），一层为 Ising 自旋、另一层为有界软自旋，以识别其极限自由能。

Result: 在该结构条件下得到 Hamilton-Jacobi 方程的变分表示，并据此给出可改写为二分自旋玻璃的感知机模型类的极限自由能表达式。

Conclusion: 该框架可推广至其他模型类，包括一般 Ising 感知机与球面感知机（spherical perceptrons），以及可能带标记模式（labelled patterns）的变体。

Abstract: The solution to a finite-dimensional Hamilton-Jacobi equation is classically known to admit a variational representation in two situations: when the nonlinearity is convex or concave, by the Hopf-Lax formula; and when the initial condition is convex or concave, by the Hopf formula. We identify a third situation: the space variable is split into two groups of coordinates, the nonlinearity is the scalar product of the two corresponding partial gradients, and the initial condition is the sum of a convex function of the first group of coordinates and of an arbitrary Lipschitz function of the second group. This structure is precisely that of the Hamilton-Jacobi equations describing the limit free energy of perceptron models. We illustrate this point by identifying the limit free energy of a class of perceptron models that can be rewritten as bipartite spin glasses, with Ising spins in one layer and bounded soft spins in the other. We also discuss how to extend the approach to several other classes of models, including general Ising and spherical perceptrons, possibly with labelled patterns.

</details>

### [62] [A Hierarchical Statistical Framework for Evaluating Agent Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19614)
*Mirco Milletarì,Tuhin Kundu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个面向智能体系统不确定性与可靠性的统计评估框架，通过场景-实例-样本的层级结构量化模型性能与波动，并将层级聚合与粗粒化过程对应，同时给出pass@k与passhat^k估计量抽样方差的有限样本精确表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂环境中的信息不完整、查询歧义、业务规则复杂以及策略的随机动态，使基于大语言模型的智能体（LLM-based agents）在生产环境中可靠性难以保证；如Thinkingbox等基准显示强模型也难以稳定表现，故需系统性评估并量化不同条件下的性能。

Method: 定义场景（scenarios）、实例（instances）、样本（samples）的层级结构，对常用指标（平均通过率、pass@k、passhat^k）附加不确定性估计；独立推导无偏pass@k与passhat^k估计量抽样方差（sampling variance）的有限样本精确表达式；并将层级聚合与统计物理中的粗粒化（coarse-graining）对应，进行建设性推导不确定性传播。

Result: 在不同层级上聚合指标可揭示策略模型（policy model）的不同结构与性质；所提出的层级不确定性传播将执行噪声（execution noise）与任务异质性（task heterogeneity）分离，并与粗粒化过程建立明确对应。

Conclusion: 该统计框架为智能体系统的不确定性与可靠性评估提供系统化工具，主要贡献在于层级不确定性传播的建设性推导及其与粗粒化的对应，从而区分执行噪声与任务异质性。

Abstract: Agents operating in complex scenarios often face uncertainty due to incomplete information about the environment they operate in, ambiguous user queries, complex business rules and the inherently stochastic dynamics of the governing policy. Several new benchmarks have been proposed to evaluate LLM-based agents on complex tasks in multi-tool environments, including settings with policy constraints and varying levels of intent ambiguity. These conditions make it important to evaluate agentic systems systematically and quantify their performance under different conditions to assess their reliability in production. This need is evident in the recently introduced Thinkingbox benchmark, where even the strongest models struggle to perform consistently across diverse business scenarios. This paper presents a statistical framework for evaluating uncertainty and reliability in agentic systems. We define a hierarchy of scenarios, instances, and samples to assess model performance and variability systematically. We apply this framework to common metrics found in the literature: mean pass rate, $pass@k$, and $passhat^k$, with uncertainty estimates for each metric. Aggregating metrics at different levels of the hierarchy reveals different structures and properties of the policy model; we connect this hierarchical aggregation with the known process of \emph{coarse-graining} as intended in the physics and statistical literature. We independently derive exact finite-sample expressions for the sampling variances of the unbiased $pass@k$ and $passhatk$ estimators; related results also appear in recent submissions. Our central contribution is a constructive derivation of hierarchical uncertainty propagation, making explicit its correspondence with coarse-graining and separating execution noise from task heterogeneity.

</details>

### [63] [Spontaneous Chiral Superradiance of a Spin-1 Condensate in a Ring Cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19139)
*Jingjun You,Haoran Jia,Xinyu Feng,Zhenhua Yu,Su Yi,Yuangang Deng*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该研究揭示自旋-1玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体与环形腔耦合时，在超辐射阈值以上会自发破缺两个拉曼通道间的对称性，形成手性超辐射相，且自旋相关碰撞可重塑相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多体腔量子电动力学（cavity QED）中对称相关超辐射通道之间的自发选择机制，以及自旋相关碰撞如何影响超辐射相结构，是该工作的核心动机。

Method: 采用自旋-1玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（spin-1 Bose-Einstein condensate）与环形腔（ring cavity）耦合的模型，其中两个对称相关的拉曼过程（Raman processes）将原子自旋态与反向传播的腔模耦合，并结合自旋相关碰撞进行分析。

Result: 超辐射阈值以上，两个拉曼通道的对称性自发破缺，形成手性超辐射（chiral superradiant, CSR）相：一个腔模被宏观占据，其反向传播伙伴保持暗态。自旋相关碰撞选择性地移动手性与对称超辐射相的边界；在足够负的自旋相关相互作用下，原子能级结构被相互作用修饰，产生内态占据反转的反转平面波相（reversed plane-wave phase）。

Conclusion: 对称相关超辐射通道间的自发选择是多体腔QED中产生手性的机制，自旋相关碰撞可调控由此形成的超辐射相结构。

Abstract: We investigate superradiant phase transitions in a spin-$1$ Bose-Einstein condensate coupled to a ring cavity, where two symmetry-related Raman processes couple the atomic spin states to counterpropagating cavity modes. Above the superradiant threshold, we find that the symmetry between the two Raman channels is spontaneously broken, giving rise to a chiral superradiant (CSR) phase. Despite the exchange symmetry of the Hamiltonian, the CSR state spontaneously selects one of the two propagation directions, with one cavity mode becoming macroscopically occupied while its counterpropagating partner remains dark. Spin-dependent collisions further reshape the superradiant phase diagram by selectively shifting the boundaries between the chiral and symmetric superradiant phases, a feature intrinsic to the multicomponent spin-$1$ manifold. For sufficiently negative spin-dependent interactions, the interaction-induced modification of the atomic level structure gives rise to a reversed plane-wave phase characterized by an inverted internal-state occupation. These results identify spontaneous selection between symmetry-related superradiant channels as a mechanism for generating chirality in many-body cavity QED and demonstrate how spin-dependent collisions control the resulting superradiant phase structure.

</details>


### [64] [Inelastic confinement-induced resonances in multiwell potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19300)
*Fabio Revuelta,Alejandro Saenz*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该论文对超冷原子量子模拟器（ultracold-atom quantum simulator）中观测到的多势阱（multiwell）共振特征提出了基于非弹性约束诱导共振（inelastic confinement-induced resonances）的互补解释，其预言的两粒子谱中的避免交叉（avoided crossings）与实验结果高度吻合，并指出双势阱模型足以解释单势阱模型中的表观对称性破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有实验将多势阱共振特征归因于超交换（superexchange）机制，即通过同一格点上的虚占据过程决定单占据莫特绝缘体（Mott insulator）中的自旋动力学；本文旨在给出一种互补的物理解释以检验该现象的起源。

Method: 从非弹性约束诱导共振的角度分析两粒子谱，将其表现为避免交叉（avoided crossings），并对比单势阱与双势阱（double-well）两种光阱（optical trapping potential）描述，同时讨论谐振近似（harmonic approximation）的局限。

Result: 基于非弹性约束诱导共振的解释与实验结果吻合良好；单势阱与双势阱描述的对比确认了实验特征的多势阱起源；双势阱模型已足以解释单势阱模型中出现的表观对称性破缺（apparent symmetry violation）。

Conclusion: 多势阱共振特征可由非弹性约束诱导共振作为超交换之外的互补机制加以解释，双势阱描述在此问题中具有充分性，谐振近似存在一定局限。

Abstract: Recently, resonance features associated with multiwell effects have been reported in a fascinating experiment conducted with an ultracold-atom quantum simulator [Y. K. Lee, H. Lin, and W. Ketterle, Phys. Rev. Lett. 131, 213001 (2023)]. The results were explained as a consequence of superexchange, which determines the spin dynamics in a singly-occupied Mott insulator, by allowing for the virtual population of two particles on the same site. In this work, a complementary interpretation of the resonance features is provided based on inelastic confinement-induced resonances, manifested as avoided crossings in the two-particle spectrum, showing excellent agreement with the experimental results. The comparison of the results of the single- and double-well descriptions of the optical trapping potential confirms the multiwell origin of the experimental features. However, the double-well model is already shown to be sufficient for providing an explanation of the apparent symmetry violation that occurs within single-well models. Furthermore, certain limitations of the harmonic approximation are discussed.

</details>


### [65] [Chirality-polarized superradiance induced by quantized orbital-angular-momentum transfer in a ring cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19910)
*Yingqi Liu,Jingjun You,Jing Tang,Yuangang Deng*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文研究携带量子化轨道角动量的拉曼光驱动的自旋-1/2玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体与环形腔两个反向传播模式的耦合，揭示OAM选择性拉曼散射通道的竞争通过自洽原子-腔反馈控制集体超辐射，并实现手性可切换的涡旋相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有原子超辐射研究多基于常规原子-光耦合，尚未充分利用量子化轨道角动量（OAM）转移作为调控集体腔发射的自由度，本文旨在探索OAM选择性散射通道对腔手性与原子涡旋结构的控制能力。

Method: 构建自旋-1/2 BEC与光学环形腔双反向模式耦合的理论模型，以携带量子化OAM的Laguerre-Gaussian拉曼光束驱动，分析OAM选择性拉曼散射通道间的竞争及自洽原子-腔反馈。

Result: OAM转移不等时单一腔模主导超辐射响应且手性可由拉曼泵浦强度切换；OAM转移相等时两反向模式共存；所得手性极化相支持不同的涡旋与涡旋-反涡旋构型及重构的合成规范场纹理；接触相互作用可改变涡旋拓扑而不改变主导腔手性。

Conclusion: 量子化OAM转移可作为超越常规原子SOAMC的集体腔发射控制机制；模式切换与涡旋有序可通过腔输出与自旋分辨成像在87Rb实验方案中检验。

Abstract: We investigate a spin-$1/2$ Bose-Einstein condensate coupled to two counterpropagating modes of an optical ring cavity and driven by Laguerre-Gaussian Raman beams carrying quantized orbital angular momentum (OAM). We demonstrate that competition between OAM-selective Raman scattering channels controls collective superradiance through self-consistent atom--cavity feedback, linking atomic orbital motion to cavity-chirality selection. For unequal OAM transfer, one cavity mode dominates the superradiant response, with its chirality switchable by the Raman pump strengths. In contrast, equal OAM transfer allows both counterpropagating modes to coexist. The resulting chirality-polarized phases support distinct vortex and vortex--antivortex configurations with reconstructed synthetic gauge-field textures. Remarkably, contact interactions can reshape the vortex topology without changing the dominant cavity chirality, revealing distinct responses of atomic and photonic degrees of freedom. The predicted mode switching and vortex ordering can be probed through cavity output and spin-resolved imaging in a proposed $^{87}$Rb implementation. Our results establish quantized OAM transfer as a mechanism for controlling collective cavity emission beyond conventional atomic SOAMC.

</details>


### [66] [Emptiness formation in quantum fluids: a hydrodynamic solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21581)
*Alexander G. Abanov,Boris A. Khanikati*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文提出辅助核方法（auxiliary-kernel method），将一维伽利略量子流体（Galilean quantum fluids）中支配宏观"空化"（macroscopic emptiness formation）的流体力学瞬子（hydrodynamic instantons）问题约化为单一积分方程求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宏观空化过程由流体力学瞬子控制，但直接求解虚时流体力学问题通常困难；现有构造往往依赖微观可积性（microscopic integrability），限制了适用范围。

Method: 借助受Bethe ansatz启发的辅助积分核表示物态方程（equation of state）；对可如此表示的物态方程，将虚时流体力学问题约化为一个单积分方程，由其解给出密度与流剖面、真空边界（vacuum boundary）及主导空化率。

Result: 对Lieb–Liniger气体导出了此前仅被猜测的主导速率积分方程，并用已知椭圆解（elliptic solution）得到速率与真空边界的显式表达；直接流体力学数值计算支持所预测的边界。另对一类多方流体（polytropic fluids）复现了已知瞬子解。

Conclusion: 辅助核构造不要求微观可积性，因而具有更广适用性，并为Lieb–Liniger气体的空化率猜想提供了严格推导基础。

Abstract: We develop an auxiliary-kernel method for constructing the hydrodynamic instantons that govern macroscopic emptiness formation in one-dimensional Galilean quantum fluids. The key step is to represent the equation of state by an auxiliary integral kernel inspired by the Bethe ansatz. For equations of state admitting such a representation, we reduce the imaginary-time hydrodynamic problem to a single integral equation. Its solution determines the full density and current profiles, the vacuum boundary, and the leading emptiness rate. Notably, the construction does not require microscopic integrability. For the Lieb--Liniger gas, we derive the previously conjectured integral equation for the leading rate and use a known elliptic solution to obtain explicit expressions for the rate and vacuum boundary. Direct hydrodynamic numerics support the predicted boundary. We also recover known instanton solutions for a family of polytropic fluids.

</details>


### [67] [Vortex solitons in spin-orbit-coupled atomic-molecular condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20871)
*Chao Kong,Jinqing Li,Chunfeng Huang,Yijia Zhu,Zhonghua Li,Jingwen Li,Huiwen Xiang,Yuzhen Tang,Zhenwang Li,Yue Liang,Haiming Deng,Gui-hua Chen,Zhiwei Fan*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文将此前三维原子-分子玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose-Einstein condensate, BEC）中半涡旋（semi-vortex, SV）孤子的研究推广到二维（two-dimensional, 2D），发现纯二次型二维系统不仅能支持基态（ground-state, GS）半涡旋孤子，还能支持作为真正完全涡旋的高阶激发态（excited-state, ES）孤子，从而通过维度约减实现纯涡旋拓扑激发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前三维系统中自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC）仅施加于原子组分，分子组分为纯涡旋而原子组分为基模与涡旋模的混合，导致整体自束缚态无法达到100%纯涡旋。作者希望通过降低维度探索能否获得完全纯涡旋的稳定孤子。

Method: 研究二维纯二次非线性（quadratic nonlinearity）原子-分子BEC模型，分析基态与高阶激发态孤子的稳定性，考察自旋轨道耦合强度λ与失谐（detuning）参数α的阈值条件。

Result: 基态与激发态孤子均仅在SOC强度λ超过最小阈值λ_min时存在稳定涡旋态，且λ_min随S_1单调增大；临界失谐α_c随S_1单调减小，表明高阶涡旋更易失稳。当λ<λ_min时系统转而形成混合模（mixed-mode, MM）态而非涡旋孤子。

Conclusion: 二维维度约减使纯涡旋激发态得以出现，为在超冷分子气体（ultracold molecular gases）中稳定高电荷（high-charge）拓扑激发提供了新途径。

Abstract: In our previous study of a three-dimensional (3D) atomic-molecular Bose-Einstein condensate (BEC) with quadratic nonlinearity and spin-orbit coupling (SOC), stable solitons are realized as semi-vortex (SV) or mixed-mode (MM) states, with SOC applied solely to the atomic component. In the SV state, the molecular part forms a pure vortex, while the atomic part remains a hybrid of fundamental and vortex modes. This prevents the overall self-bound state from achieving a fully pure vortex configuration, with the total vortex component always less than $100\%$. Here, we extend the study to two dimensions (2D) and uncover a qualitatively different behavior: purely quadratic 2D systems support not only ground-state (GS) SV solitons, but also higher-order excited-state (ES) solitons that are genuine full vortices (a feature absent in the 3D counterpart). For both GS and ES solitons, stable vortex states exist only when the SOC strength $λ$ exceeds a minimum threshold $λ_{\min}$, which increases monotonically with $S_1$. Conversely, the critical detuning $α_c$ decreases monotonically with $S_1$, indicating that higher-order vortices are more susceptible to destabilization. For $λ< λ_{\min}$, the system forms MM states instead of vortex solitons. Our results demonstrate that dimensional reduction to 2D enables the emergence of pure-vortex excited states, establishing a new pathway for stabilizing high-charge topological excitations in ultracold molecular gases.

</details>

### [68] [Bäcklund transformations of the fifth Painlevé equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18335)
*Peter A. Clarkson,Clare Dunning,Ben Mitchell*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 本文讨论第五 Painlevé 方程（fifth Painlevé equation）的 Bäcklund 变换（Bäcklund transformations），将其作用于用广义 Laguerre 多项式（generalised Laguerre polynomials）与广义 Umemura 多项式（generalised Umemura polynomials）表示的有理解（rational solutions），并导出以修正 Bessel 函数（modified Bessel functions）表示的非唯一解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机，仅表明工作围绕第五 Painlevé 方程的 Bäcklund 变换及其与有理解、修正 Bessel 函数解的关联展开。

Method: 以第五 Painlevé 方程的 Bäcklund 变换为核心工具，将其施加于广义 Laguerre 多项式与广义 Umemura 多项式形式的有理解，并通过选取适当的 Bäcklund 变换推导修正 Bessel 函数形式的解；同时讨论第五与第三 Painlevé 方程（third Painlevé equation）解之间的关系。

Result: 得到第五 Painlevé 方程以修正 Bessel 函数表示的非唯一解（non-unique solutions）；并表明在相同参数取值下还存在由广义 Umemura 多项式给出的有理解，且这些有理解并非修正 Bessel 函数解的特例。

Conclusion: Bäcklund 变换可有效联系第五 Painlevé 方程的多类解；修正 Bessel 函数解与广义 Umemura 多项式有理解在同一参数取值下并存且互不包含，提示解结构具有非唯一性；第五与第三 Painlevé 方程的解之间存在可讨论的对应关系。

Abstract: In this paper Bäcklund transformations of the fifth Painlevé equation are discussed. The Bäcklund transformations are applied to rational solutions of the fifth Painlevé equation, which are expressed in terms of the generalised Laguerre polynomials and generalised Umemura polynomials. Using appropriate Bäcklund transformations, we derive non-unique solutions of the fifth Painlevé equation in terms of modified Bessel functions. Further it is shown that there are also rational solutions in terms of generalised Umemura polynomials for the same values of the parameters, which are not special cases of the modified Bessel function solutions. The relationship between solutions of the fifth Painlevé equation and solutions of the third Painlevé equation is also discussed.

</details>


### [69] [On the equivalence of three extended Calogero--Bogoyavlenskii--Schiff models to the classical CBS equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20495)
*Manjit Singh*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 通过显式可逆点变换证明，Wazwaz等提出的三个扩展 Calogero–Bogoyavlenskii–Schiff（CBS）模型中，前两个在一般参数区域可映射回经典 CBS 方程，第三个等价于带一个旁观坐标的 CBS；因此这些模型在一般参数下并未引入新的点不等价方程或真正的四变量微分耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对新近提出的三个扩展 CBS 模型，检验其是否真正给出新的、与经典 CBS 方程点不等价的可积结构，并澄清所报道的指数色散关系（exponential dispersion relations）之来源。

Method: 构造显式可逆点变换（invertible point transformations），将第一个模型以及一般情形下的第二个模型映射为经典 CBS 方程；第三个模型则被证明一般等价于带额外旁观坐标（spectator coordinate）的 CBS，该坐标不出现于微分算子中。同时识别出例外参数区域（exceptional parameter regimes）。

Result: 三个模型均可借助上述变换约化到经典 CBS 或其带旁观坐标的形式，原有的指数色散关系可由这些变换加以解释；例外参数区域被单独指出。

Conclusion: 在一般参数区域下，所提模型并未产生新的点不等价 CBS 方程，也不构成真正的四变量微分耦合（four-variable differential coupling），其可积性质可由经典 CBS 方程继承。

Abstract: We examine the three extended Calogero--Bogoyavlenskii--Schiff (CBS) models introduced by Wazwaz, Alhejaili, and El-Tantawy [Eur.\ Phys.\ J.\ Plus \textbf{141}, 1218 (2026)]. Explicit invertible point transformations map the first model, and generically the second, to the classical CBS equation. The third model is generically equivalent to CBS with an additional spectator coordinate absent from the differential operator. We identify the exceptional parameter regimes and explain the reported exponential dispersion relations through these transformations. These equivalences show that the proposed models do not, in the generic parameter regimes, introduce new point-inequivalent CBS equations or genuine four-variable differential coupling.

</details>
