### [70] [Ternary-Valued FDTD Method for Nonlinear Optics: Third-Harmonic Generation on a Multiplier-Free Solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20329)
*Ivan S. Maksymov*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一种场变量仅取三值{-1,0,+1}的时域有限差分（FDTD）求解器，在CPU与FPGA上均能定量复现非线性光学物理，全程无需乘法运算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性光学建模通常依赖高性能计算机与浮点运算，难以部署于嵌入式与自主平台；作者希望以极低精度算术实现定量准确的求解。

Method: 在三值FDTD算法（每个Yee元胞存储累加邻域符号离散旋度的整数态）基础上，扩展至非均匀介质、Kerr介质与Mur吸收边界；介电对比用多路选择器实现，瞬态Kerr非线性用系数查找表，Mur边界系数用规范符号数字（canonical signed-digit）移位-加法链实现。

Result: 该方案复现浮点参考的三次谐波转换效率，误差在1.4%以内；并利用中心对称χ^(3)禁止偶次谐波的物理对称性，为三值与浮点模型提供无参考自检。

Conclusion: 完整求解器可部署于小型低成本FPGA，数据通路中不含任何乘法器，使非线性光学建模可进入嵌入式与自主平台而非仅限高性能计算环境。

Abstract: I show that a finite-difference time-domain (FDTD) solver with field variables restricted to the ternary alphabet $\{-1,0,+1\}$ can reproduce nonlinear optical physics quantitatively on both CPUs and field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs). Building on the general ternary-valued FDTD algorithm, in which each Yee cell stores an integer state that accumulates the discrete curl of neighbouring symbols without being drained, I extend the scheme to inhomogeneous and Kerr media and to Mur absorbing boundaries, without performing any multiplication operations. Dielectric contrast is implemented as a multiplexer, since the difference between ternary symbols takes only a few values, the instantaneous Kerr nonlinearity as a coefficient look-up table and the Mur boundary coefficients as canonical signed-digit shift--add chains. The scheme reproduces the third-harmonic conversion efficiency of a floating-point reference to within $1.4\%$. A symmetry of the governing physics, expressed through the even harmonics forbidden by centrosymmetric $χ^{(3)}$, provides a reference-free self-check for both ternary and floating-point models. The complete solver fits on a small, low-cost FPGA and contains no multiplier anywhere in its datapath, placing nonlinear optical modelling within reach of embedded and autonomous platforms rather than of high-performance computers.

</details>


### [71] [Modular Programmable Photonics with Unitary Tiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21582)
*Kevin Zelaya,Mohammad-Ali Miri*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文研究由酉变换单元（unitary tiles）交错排列构成的可编程光子（programmable photonics）模块化架构，分析电路深度、单元间连接性与可达变换之间的关系，并数值发现重构误差在 R=2K 处出现转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可编程光子学（programmable photonics）是光信息处理的通用平台，但将可编程变换扩展到大量光学模式仍具挑战性，需探索可扩展的模块化架构。

Method: 构建由酉单元（unitary tiles）以交替、错位（staggered）列排布的模块化架构；每个物理单元将固定多端口混频器（multiport mixers）与可编程相移（phase shifts）交错，使混频限于小规模模式组；分析电路深度、单元间连接性（intertile connectivity）与可达变换的关系，并区分理想可编程单元与其物理相位堆叠（phase-stack）实现。

Result: 数值目标拟合（target-fitting）显示，当 R=2K 时重构误差（reconstruction errors）出现转变，其中 K 与 R 分别为单元行数与列数。

Conclusion: 该设计旨在实现大规模、高可靠端口数（port-count）的光子处理器，为可扩展可编程光信息处理提供架构路径。

Abstract: Programmable photonics provides a versatile platform for optical information processing, but extending programmable transformations to large numbers of optical modes remains challenging. We investigate a modular architecture composed of unitary tiles arranged in alternating and staggered columns. Each physical tile interlaces fixed multiport mixers with programmable phase shifts, confining individual mixing operations to small groups of modes while connecting them across a larger processor. We analyze the relationship between circuit depth, intertile connectivity, and attainable transformations, distinguishing ideal programmable tiles from their physical phase-stack implementations. Numerical target-fitting results show a transition in the reconstruction errors at $R = 2K$, where $K$ and $R$ are the number of tile rows and columns, respectively. This design ultimately aims to enable large and reliable port-count optical photonic processors.

</details>


### [72] [Multi-output neural network surrogate for double-pulse femtosecond optothermal control of thin silicon films on fused silica](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20301)
*George D. Tsibidis*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文提出一种多输出前馈神经网络替代模型（multi-output feedforward neural network surrogate），基于耦合多反射与双温模型（two-temperature model）模拟，映射双脉冲飞秒激光（double-pulse femtosecond laser）激发硅薄膜的光热响应，实现毫秒级设计与寻优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多物理场（multi-physics）仿真计算成本高、耗时长，难以支撑实时激光加工控制与逆向光子薄膜设计，需构建高精度快速替代模型。

Method: 以2000组物理仿真数据训练双隐层（[40,30]神经元）多输出前馈网络，采用Levenberg-Marquardt优化，输入为薄膜厚度与脉冲间隔，同步预测六个强耦合光热观测量（峰值电子/晶格温度、峰值载流子密度、最小/最大吸收率、最小折射率）。

Result: 网络对所有目标的决定系数（coefficient of determination, R²）均大于0.9998；在毫秒内完成200×200密集设计网格评估，解析出薄膜干涉条纹与载流子诱导折射率调制，并定位最优光热耦合区（d=156 nm，Δt=4.572 ps）。

Conclusion: 该统一机器学习工作流消除了昂贵的多物理场运行瓶颈，适用于实时激光加工控制与逆向光子薄膜设计。

Abstract: We introduce a multi-output feedforward neural network surrogate trained on a coupled multiple-reflection and two-temperature model to map double-pulse femtosecond laser excitation (1030 nm) of crystalline silicon films on fused silica across film thicknesses (50-500 nm) and inter-pulse delays (0-10ps). Trained via Levenberg-Marquardt optimization on 2000 physical simulations, the dual-hidden-layer network ([40,30] neurons) simultaneously predicts six strongly coupled optothermal observables, peak electron temperature, peak lattice temperature, peak carrier density, minimum and maximum absorptivity and minimum refractive index achieving coefficients of determination R2 larger than 0.9998 across all targets. Evaluating a dense 200 by 200 design grid in milliseconds resolves pronounced thin-film interference fringes and carrier-induced index modulation, identifying an optimal optothermal coupling regime at d=156 nm and Dt=4.572 ps. This unified machine-learning workflow eliminates computationally expensive multi-physics runtime bottlenecks for real-time laser processing control and inverse photonic thin-film design.

</details>


### [73] [Cavity Control of Emission Dynamics from Spin Defects in Hexagonal Boron Nitride](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20751)
*Anuj Kumar Singh,Atanu Patra,Paul Konrad,Subhamoy Sahoo,Ankur Arora,Johannes Düreth,Georgy V. Astakhov,Monika Emmerling,Martin Kamp,Andreas Sperlich,Vladimir Dyakonov,Sven Höfling,Simon Betzold*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该研究将六方氮化硼（hBN）中带负电的硼空位（$\mathrm{V_B^-}$）缺陷与可调谐平凹微腔（plano-concave microcavity）集成，实现三维光学限制，观察到光致发光（photoluminescence, PL）增强与腔修饰的衰减动力学，并据此推断缺陷存在倾斜光学偶极（tilted optical dipole）几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\mathrm{V_B^-}$ 缺陷有望用于室温量子传感（quantum sensing），且 hBN 的二维结构便于近距探测目标材料；但其宽带发射与低量子产率（quantum yield）限制了光-物质相互作用效率，且 $\mathrm{V_B^-}$ 光学偶极与受限腔场的耦合机制尚不清楚。

Method: 构建 $\mathrm{V_B^-}$ 缺陷与可调谐平凹微腔的集成体系以实现三维光学限制，结合时间分辨光致发光（time-resolved PL）、光学检测磁共振（optically detected magnetic resonance, ODMR）以及激发偏振依赖的 PL 测量进行表征。

Result: 实验获得 PL 增强与腔修饰的光学衰减动力学；时间分辨 PL 识别出明显的快速衰减分量，提示缺陷具有复杂的偶极几何；采用倾斜光学偶极模型可将快、慢衰减分量分别归因于面内（in-plane）与面外（out-of-plane）偶极贡献；ODMR 与激发偏振依赖 PL 进一步揭示局域对称性破缺（local symmetry breaking）及可能的光学偶极构型。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了光学偶极取向（optical dipole orientation）在 $\mathrm{V_B^-}$ 缺陷-腔耦合中的关键作用，为理解该类缺陷的光-物质相互作用及腔增强量子传感应用提供了依据。

Abstract: Optically addressable negatively charged boron vacancy ($\mathrm{V_B^-}$) defects in hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) are promising for quantum sensing at room temperature. The two-dimensional structure of hBN allows direct access to the local environment, enabling proximal sensing of target materials. However, its broadband emission and low quantum yield hinder efficient light-matter interaction, and the coupling of $\mathrm{V_B^-}$ optical dipoles to confined cavity fields remains poorly understood. In this study, we integrate $\mathrm{V_B^-}$ defects with a tunable plano-concave microcavity, achieving three-dimensional optical confinement. This approach yields photoluminescence (PL) enhancement and cavity-modified optical decay dynamics, providing insights into the orientation of the $\mathrm{V_B^-}$ defect dipole. Time-resolved PL measurements identify a distinct fast decay component, suggesting a complex dipole geometry for the defects. Therefore, a tilted optical dipole model is considered to attribute the fast and slow decay components to in-plane and out-of-plane dipole contributions, respectively. Additionally, optically detected magnetic resonance and excitation-polarisation-dependent PL measurements provide further insights into local symmetry breaking and potential optical dipole configurations. These results highlight the role of optical dipole orientation in $\mathrm{V_B^-}$ defect-cavity coupling.

</details>

### [74] [A Gravitomagnetic Bootstrap: from Newtonian gravity to general relativity via localizing rotational and conformal symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20210)
*David Wallace*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出，牛顿引力（Newtonian gravity）可理解为伽利略惯性结构（Galilean inertial structure）的局部化；将旋转惯性与时空尺度一并局部化后，所得理论在弱假设下除一个具速度量纲的常数与整体符号外唯一，适当选取时恰与广义相对论（general relativity）的一阶后牛顿近似（first post-Newtonian approximation）一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 依据等效原理（Equivalence Principle），牛顿引力已被较好地理解为伽利略惯性结构中定义直线加速度部分的局部化，但旋转惯性及空间、时间尺度的局部化情形尚未被系统考察，本文旨在补足这一逻辑缺口。

Method: 作者在局部化直线加速度的基础上，进一步局部化旋转惯性（rotational inertia）与空间、时间尺度，并在此弱假设下考察可构造的理论形式及其唯一性。

Result: 所得理论在弱假设下除一个具速度量纲的常数与整体符号外唯一；对该常数与符号作适当选取后，该理论与广义相对论的一阶后牛顿近似相吻合。

Conclusion: 该构造提供了一种从牛顿引力自举（bootstrap）到广义相对论的方法，且无需显式预设狭义相对论（special relativity）。

Abstract: Newtonian gravity, in the light of the Equivalence Principle, is best understood as a localization of Galilean inertial structure --- or at least, of the part of Galilean inertial structure that defines rectilinear acceleration. I consider what kind of theory can be constructed if we also localize rotational inertia and spatial and temporal scales. The resultant theory, given weak assumptions, is unique up to a constant with the dimensions of velocity and an overall sign. For an appropriate choice of these, it coincides with the first post-Newtonian approximation to general relativity, so that this construction gives a method to bootstrap from Newtonian gravity to general relativity without explicitly presupposing special relativity.

</details>


### [75] [Why Cassini's Oval Could Not Replace Kepler's Ellipse?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21336)
*Pascal Descamps*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 结合卡西尼（Cassini）椭圆曲线、比内公式（Binet's formula）与等面积定律，论证“椭圆”与“卵形线”的动力学差异在于缺少普适性指数，从而解释了1687年后学界仍未定论的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 十七世纪后期开普勒椭圆（Kepler's ellipse）尚未被普遍接受：Ward以“等分点（equant）”质疑Boulliau的圆锥曲线假设，卡西尼（Giovanni Domenico Cassini）继而提出另一条曲线，作者意在比较两者并说明当时争论未决的深层原因。

Method: 考察卡西尼“卵形线（oval）”的几何性质（亏格1四次曲线（genus-1 quartic），无闭式开普勒方程（Kepler equation）），比较其偏近点角（eccentric anomaly）首项系数与空焦点位置；并利用比内公式（Binet's formula）在等面积定律（area law）下导出对应力律偏离平方反比（inverse square）的程度。

Result: 卵形线偏近点角与开普勒椭圆恰有相同首项系数；对太阳而言，其空焦点比椭圆空焦点距真实等分点近38倍。动力学上，其力律偏离平方反比的指数为 D(e)∼3/(4e²)，随参数 e 变化，不存在适用于整族曲线的单一指数。

Conclusion: 区分卵形线与椭圆的关键并非等面积定律的缺失，而是普适性（universality）的缺失——无统一力律指数；这或可解释《自然哲学的数学原理》（Principia, 1687）之后卡西尼、法国科学院（Académie）与莱布尼茨（Leibniz）仍将问题悬置的原因。

Abstract: In the later seventeenth century, Kepler's ellipse was not yet accepted. Ward claimed that Boulliau's conic hypothesis amounted to a Ptolemaic equant at the ellipse's empty focus, and built his own model on it. Around 1675--1680, Giovanni Domenico Cassini proposed another curve, the ``oval'', a genus-1 quartic with no closed-form Kepler equation; yet its eccentric anomaly shares exactly the leading coefficient of Kepler's, and its empty focus is, for the Sun, 38 times closer to a true equant than the ellipse's. Dynamically, Binet's formula shows that the force producing the oval under the area law departs from the inverse square by an index $\mathcal{D}(e)\sim3/(4e^2)$, depending on the oval's parameter $e$: no single exponent serves the whole family. This absence of universality, not of an area law, distinguishes it from the ellipse, and arguably explains why Cassini, the Académie and Leibniz kept the question open after the \emph{Principia} (1687).

</details>

### [76] [Global Fit Inference for LISA with samsara++](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17266)
*Lorenzo Valbusa Dall'Armi,Gabriele Astorino,Riccardo Buscicchio,Angelo Ricciardone,Walter Del Pozzo*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出 samsara++，一个基于 C++/CUDA 的采样器（sampler），用于解决 LISA 引力波数据中跨维度（trans-dimensional）的全局拟合（Global Fit）问题，并在模拟数据集上验证了其可扩展性与计算可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA 将观测到多个引力波信号的叠加，需在时间与频率上重叠的情况下联合推断未知数量的源、参数及仪器噪声，因此需要全局拟合分析，而现有方法缺乏针对该跨维度问题的可扩展采样工具。

Method: 在 samsara 的连续时间 MCMC（continuous-time MCMC）框架上，结合生灭过程（birth-death processes）增删源、变异移动（mutation moves）估计参数，生灭速率依赖后验；采用共享内存并行（shared-memory parallelism）、增量似然更新（incremental likelihood updates）、索引存储及并行回火（parallel tempering）降低开销并改善多峰后验探索。

Result: 在含 143 与 866 个双白矮星双星（double white dwarf binaries）的模拟一年期 LISA 数据上，从空源模型出发实现源重建与星表生成，并揭示源计数、信号重叠与混合不充分对联合推断的影响；压力测试中 16,435 个源（131,480 个参数）约 16 小时、约 3 GB 内存完成 1500 万次迭代。

Conclusion: 基准测试表明生灭采样对大规模银河系双星族群的计算可行性，并为将 samsara++ 扩展为完整的 LISA 全局拟合奠定基础。

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) will observe a superposition of gravitational-wave signals, requiring joint inference of an unknown number of sources, parameters, and instrumental noise. To disentangle these contributions, that overlap in both time and frequency, a Global Fit (GF) analysis is needed. We present samsara++, a C++/CUDA sampler designed to address this trans-dimensional GF problem. Building on the continuous-time MCMC framework of samsara, the algorithm combines birth-death processes for adding and removing sources, with mutation moves for parameter estimation. Birth and death are selected through posterior-dependent rates. Shared-memory parallelism, incremental likelihood updates, and indexed storage reduce the computational and memory costs, while parallel tempering improves the exploration of multimodal posteriors. We assess the performance using simulated one-year LISA datasets containing Galactic double white dwarf binaries and stationary Gaussian instrumental noise. Analyses of populations containing 143 and 866 binaries start from empty source models, without preliminary searches or proposals tailored to the injected population. These tests demonstrate source reconstruction and catalog generation, highlighting, at the same time, the importance of source-count, overlapping signals, and incomplete mixing on joint source and noise inference. Then we perform a separate stress test, initialized at the injected parameters, exploring a population of 16,435 binaries, corresponding to 131,480 source parameters. This run completes 15 million iterations in approximately 16 hours using about 3 GB of RAM. All together, these benchmarks show the computational feasibility of birth-death sampling for large Galactic-binary populations, and provide a foundation for extending samsara++ toward a comprehensive LISA global fit.

</details>

### [77] [Random compositions of quasiperiodic cocycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21556)
*Ao Cai,Pedro Duarte,Silvius Klein*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 论文研究环面平移（toral translations）上线性余循环（linear cocycles）随机复合构成的多重过程（multiplicative process），在一般性假设下建立有效大偏差估计（effective large deviations estimates）与Lyapunov指数（Lyapunov exponent）的Hölder连续性（Hölder continuity），并借此揭示随机性对拟周期动力学（quasiperiodic dynamics）的正则化效应（regularizing effect）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究的动机在于刻画随机性对拟周期动力学（quasiperiodic dynamics）的正则化作用，并处理相应转移算子（transition operator）缺失谱间隙（spectral gap）这一核心困难。

Method: 方法上考察环面平移（toral translations）上线性余循环（linear cocycles）的随机复合所定义的多重过程（multiplicative process），在适当的泛型假设（generic assumptions）下进行分析；其方法新颖之处在于可在转移算子（transition operator）无谱间隙（spectral gap）的情形下仍获得上述性质。

Result: 在给定假设下得到两类结果：一是有效大偏差估计（effective large deviations estimates），二是相关Lyapunov指数（Lyapunov exponent）的Hölder连续性（Hölder continuity）。

Conclusion: 上述性质表明随机性对拟周期动力学（quasiperiodic dynamics）具有正则化效应（regularizing effect）；且这些结论的成立无需依赖转移算子（transition operator）的谱间隙（spectral gap）。

Abstract: We consider the multiplicative process given by random compositions of linear cocycles over toral translations. Under appropriate generic assumptions, we establish effective large deviations estimates as well as the Hölder continuity of the associated Lyapunov exponent of the process. These properties in particular show the regularizing effect of randomness on quasiperiodic dynamics. The novelty of our approach allows for the study of such properties notwithstanding the lack of a spectral gap for the corresponding transition operator.

</details>

Result: 制备了覆盖玻色—玻色（Bose--Bose）、玻色—费米（Bose--Fermi）与费米—费米（Fermi--Fermi）的七种同位素对；与序列匹配的单同位素参考对比显示，从磁光阱装载（magneto-optical-trap loading）、压缩到光偶极阱转移（optical-dipole-trap transfer）多数组合性能接近单同位素水平，偏差仅出现在特定同位素对与制备阶段，并归因于装载期的光学敏感性与高密度条件下的额外损耗。

Abstract: Dipolar quantum mixtures offer new opportunities to explore many-body phenomena shaped by long-range anisotropic interactions, intercomponent coupling, and quantum statistics. Here we develop a reconfigurable platform for bosonic and fermionic dysprosium isotope mixtures using a single-isotope-like optical layout. We time-multiplex the 421-nm light to generate isotope-selective slow-atom fluxes, while spectrally engineered 626-nm light provides simultaneous common-path narrow-line cooling and trapping. By reconfiguring optical frequencies, we prepare seven isotope pairs spanning Bose--Bose, Bose--Fermi, and Fermi--Fermi combinations, with continuous population-balance control through the 421-nm dwell-time allocation. Systematic comparisons with sequence-matched single-isotope references, from magneto-optical-trap loading and compression to optical-dipole-trap transfer, reveal nearly single-isotope-like performance for most combinations. The larger deviations occur only for certain isotope pairs and preparation stages, with controlled measurements revealing optical sensitivities during loading and additional losses under high-density conditions. Our results make isotope composition and quantum statistics selectable within a strongly dipolar system while largely preserving single-isotope preparation simplicity and performance, opening new possibilities for exploring multicomponent dipolar quantum matter.

</details>

TL;DR: 该工作演示了基于⁸⁸Sr窄光学跃迁的真正连续运转超辐射激光（superradiant laser），连续发射超过八小时，线宽320(40) Hz，比自然线宽低20倍以上，且发射频率锁定于原子跃迁，激光本身即构成原子频率参考。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超稳激光是光钟（optical clock）的基础，但其频率稳定性继承自精密光学谐振腔（optical resonator）的长度稳定性，在实验室外难以维持，并受热噪声与技术噪声限制；超辐射激光通过将相干性存储于增益介质原子中，有望规避这些限制，但原子损耗此前阻碍了真正连续运转。

Method: 在⁸⁸Sr的窄光学跃迁上实现超辐射激光，并采用可维持连续运转的方案以克服原子损耗问题，从而获得真正连续（truly continuous）的发射。

Result: 连续发射维持超过八小时，测得线宽为320(40) Hz，比自然线宽低20倍以上；发射频率持续锚定于原子跃迁，使激光自身成为原子频率参考。

Conclusion: 该结果为研制短时频率稳定性（short-term frequency stability）可与最佳谐振腔稳频激光相媲美的激光提供了路径；真正连续运转使研究超越瞬态（transient）区域，可用于探索稳健的非平衡稳态（nonequilibrium steady states）与持续的相干集体动力学（coherent collective dynamics）。

Abstract: Lasers with ultrastable frequency are at the foundation of optical clocks, which enable precision tests of fundamental physics and relativistic geodesy at the centimetre scale. Such lasers inherit their frequency stability from the length stability of a sophisticated optical resonator, which is hard to maintain outside a well-controlled laboratory environment and limited by thermal and technical noise. Superradiant lasers promise to overcome these limitations by storing their coherence in the atoms making up the laser's gain medium. Superradiant lasing has been demonstrated on narrow optical atomic transitions with pulsed and quasi-continuous operation. However, atom loss has prevented truly continuous operation, which severely limits their suitability for clocks. Here we demonstrate a superradiant laser on a narrow optical transition in $^{88}$Sr that operates truly continuously. We sustain emission for more than eight hours and measure a linewidth of 320(40) Hz, more than 20 times below the natural linewidth. The emission frequency remains anchored to the atomic transition, making the laser itself an atomic frequency reference. Our results provide a route towards a laser that reaches short-term frequency stability comparable to that of the best resonator-stabilized lasers. Furthermore, truly continuous operation gives us access to physics beyond the transient regime, enabling the exploration of robust nonequilibrium steady states and sustained coherent collective dynamics.

</details>

### [80] [Bayesian Time-Varying Spectral Estimation for LISA with P-Splines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19293)
*Avi Vajpeyi,Giorgio Mentasti,Ollie Burke,Renate Meyer,Patricio Maturana-Russel,Quentin Baghi*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于时间-频率B样条乘积（B-spline）线性组合与高斯张量积惩罚先验的非平稳演化谱建模方法，结合Whittle型似然近似进行贝叶斯推断，并在LISA模拟数据上验证其噪声谱估计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢时变过程的演化谱（evolutionary spectrum）估计需要兼顾时频分辨率与计算成本，且LISA等任务中非平稳噪声与物理响应信息如何影响谱重建尚需系统评估。

Method: 以时间与频率依赖的B样条乘积线性组合表示演化谱，对系数施加高斯张量积惩罚先验（Gaussian tensor-product penalty prior）；以后验更新基于Wilson-Daubechies-Meyer时频系数的Whittle型似然近似，时频分别正则化，并采用自适应粗粒化似然（adaptive coarse-grained likelihood）降低计算量。

Result: 在已知谱的局部平稳过程（locally stationary process）重复模拟中，样本量增大时重建误差更低、后验区间更窄；在一年期LISA模拟数据（含平稳单链路仪器谱、演化的TDI响应与调制银河前景）上，允许时变显著优于平稳模型，引入TDI响应的物理信息在移动谱特征附近进一步提升恢复效果。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了非平稳LISA噪声估计中谱灵活性、物理假设与后验不确定性之间的权衡关系。

Abstract: We model the evolutionary spectrum of a slowly time-varying process by a linear combination of products of time- and frequency-dependent B-splines, placing a Gaussian tensor-product penalty prior on the coefficients. The posterior distribution is obtained by updating the prior using a Whittle-type likelihood approximation of the Wilson-Daubechies-Meyer time-frequency coefficients. Separate regularisation in time and frequency allows non-stationary spectral structure to be inferred. An adaptive coarse-grained likelihood reduces the computational cost while preserving fine resolution around sharp spectral features. We first validate the method using repeated simulations of a locally stationary process with known spectrum, showing lower reconstruction errors and narrower posterior intervals as the sample size increases. We then apply it to a year-long simulated LISA data set with stationary instrumental single link spectra, an evolving time-delay interferometry (TDI) response, and a modulated Galactic foreground. We compare a flexible total power spectral density (PSD) model with models incorporating progressively stronger physical information and examine both continuous and gapped observations. Allowing time variation substantially improves reconstruction relative to a stationary model, while incorporating physical information about the TDI response further improves recovery, particularly near its moving spectral features. The results illustrate the trade-off between spectral flexibility, physical assumptions, and posterior uncertainty in non-stationary LISA noise estimation.

</details>

### [81] [Schwarzschild scattering from CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18826)
*Paolo Arnaudo,Benjamin Withers*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文表明，AdS₄中小黑洞态下ℝ×S²上的推迟CFT关联函数，可用于导出渐近平直Schwarzschild分波S矩阵元及其准正规模谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻求从AdS/CFT关联函数出发、在平空间极限下恢复渐近平直时空散射信息的途径，尤其是准正规模（quasinormal modes）这类耗散性谱信息如何从厄米（Hermitian）系统内的CFT数据中获得。

Method: 识别一类算符（包括自旋1、自旋2以及共形耦合标量（conformally coupled scalars）），使对偶体场在高频下于AdS边界附近近似为平面波；据此建立CFT关联函数与反射系数（reflection coefficients）之间的解析关系，并考察其在平空间极限L→∞下的行为。另构造一个简单玩具模型（toy model）以刻画短时内耗散准正规模在厄米系统中的出现机制。

Result: 在该算符类下，CFT关联函数与反射系数间存在简洁解析关系，且在平空间极限L→∞下表现良好；由此可得到渐近平直Schwarzschild分波S矩阵元，包括其准正规模谱。玩具模型再现了小黑洞AdS情形中短时耗散准正规模的出现方式。

Conclusion: AdS₄小黑洞态的推迟CFT关联函数编码了渐近平直Schwarzschild散射数据，为在平空间极限下由全息（holography）提取S矩阵与准正规模提供了可行框架。

Abstract: We show that retarded CFT correlators on $\mathbb{R}\times \mathbb{S}^2$ in a state described by the small black hole in AdS$_4$ can be used to derive asymptotically flat Schwarzschild partial-wave S-matrix elements, including its spectrum of quasinormal modes. This is achieved by identifying a class of operators for which the dual bulk fields behave as plane waves near the AdS boundary for sufficiently high frequencies. For such operators there is a simple analytic relation between CFT correlators and reflection coefficients which behaves gracefully in the flat space limit, $L\to \infty$. These include spin-1, spin-2, and conformally coupled scalars. We provide a simple toy model that captures how dissipative QNMs can appear for short times inside Hermitian systems, closely resembling the small black hole in AdS.

</details>


### [82] [Gravitational Memory Correlators and BMS Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18827)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用 in-in 形式（in-in formalism），在微扰量子引力领头阶计算标量衰变产生引力子的 n 点记忆关联函数与混合能量-记忆关联函数。

Result: 获得了对任意 n 成立的紧凑结果，并显式验证了记忆、能量与 Bondi 质量探测器之间的超平移通量平衡关系。

Conclusion: 结果表明渐近对称性（asymptotic symmetry）约束在红外有限可观测量上得到实现，为引力波记忆效应的量子场论描述提供了支持。

Abstract: We compute the n-point memory and mixed energy-memory correlators for scalar decay into gravitons using the in-in formalism at leading order in perturbative quantum gravity. We obtain compact results for arbitrary n and explicitly verify the supertranslation flux balance relation between memory, energy, and Bondi mass detectors, realizing asymptotic symmetry constraints on infrared-finite observables.

</details>


### [83] [Rational conformal interfaces between the Ising and tricritical Ising models from a tetrality web](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18831)
*António Antunes,Jin Chen,Junchen Rong*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在系统构造 Ising 与三临界 Ising 模型之间的有理共形界面，并厘清其与折叠边界态（folded boundary state）及扭曲 Cardy 态（twisted Cardy state）之间的关系。

Method: 采用“moving up”程序将乘积理论扩展至具 S_3 自同构群的辅助陪集（coset）模型，置换给出扭曲 Cardy 态；辅以“moving down”程序约化到 S_3 不变代数，并将最小理论的普通 Cardy 态拉回至物理张量积系统。

Result: 得到 18 个因子化界面（factorized interface）与 6 个透射界面（transmitting interface），后者对应最大理论中 4 个对换与 2 个 3-循环扭曲边界，包含 Gaiotto 的 RG 墙及其与 Ising 对偶线融合所得的界面；6 个界面承载 TIM 融合环的正则表示且透射系数同为 35/48，但可由边界熵、体关联与算符谱区分。

Conclusion: 共形界面之间存在 tetrality 网结构，6 个透射界面虽具相同能量透射系数，却可由边界熵、体关联和算符谱加以区分；并计算了 24 个边界间的完整环面振幅（annulus amplitude）。

Abstract: We construct rational conformal interfaces between the Ising and tricritical Ising (TIM) models by using different chiral symmetries to prepare their folded boundary states. We use a moving up procedure which extends the product theory to an auxiliary coset with an $S_3$ automorphism group, where permutations give twisted Cardy states. We complement this with a moving down approach which reduces to the $S_3$ invariant algebra. Overall, these procedures uncover a tetrality web relating four different theories. Pulling back ordinary Cardy states of the smallest theory in the web to the physical tensor product system gives eighteen factorized interfaces and six transmitting interfaces. The latter agree with four transposition and two 3-cycle twisted boundaries in the largest theory. They include Gaiotto's RG wall and the interface obtained by fusing an Ising duality line into it. The six interfaces carry the regular action of the TIM fusion ring and have the same energy transmission coefficient, $35/48$. Their boundary entropies, bulk correlations and operator spectra distinguish them. We calculate these observables and the complete annulus amplitudes between the twenty four boundaries.

</details>


### [84] [Constants and Nonperturbative Corrections in $V^{5,2}$ Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18832)
*Seyed Morteza Hosseini*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究与非环面Sasaki-Einstein流形$V^{5,2}$的自由及固定轨迹循环商相关的两个三维$\mathcal N=2$理论中壳上有效扭曲超势的微扰与指数小修正，发现二者在非微扰系数规律上共享模结构，但微扰移动与指数修正不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对两个具有相同$N^{3/2}$领头行为的理论，其更精细的微扰与指数小修正是否存在差异、以及非微扰层面是否共享模结构，是摘要所关注的核心问题。

Method: 研究对象为两节点Chern-Simons理论（能级$(k,-k)$）与单节点带味理论（$N_f$个基本对）；在超共形化学势下构造带味理论全阶微扰表达的Chern-Simons对应物，给出与秩无关常数的收敛积分表示，并用高精度Bethe解与相对M-theory局域化处方（$b\to0^+$极限、压扁球配分函数）进行互补分析。

Result: 整数参数下常数可表为Clausen函数有限和与$\zeta(3)$的显式组合，其大参数展开Borel求和回到定义积分；在$k=1,3$、$N_f=1,3$时以权四Eisenstein级数的Eichler积分给出完整指数余项猜想，两理论在商阶为一处结果重合、在三阶处不同。

Conclusion: 两理论在非微扰系数规律上共享模结构（含Eisenstein生成元的极小模级及原函数的收敛性与自然边界），但微扰移动与指数修正可分辨出在共同领头$N^{3/2}$行为中不可见的差异。

Abstract: We study perturbative and exponentially small corrections to the on-shell effective twisted superpotentials of two three-dimensional $\mathcal N=2$ theories associated with free and fixed-locus cyclic quotients of the non-toric Sasaki-Einstein manifold $V^{5,2}$. These are the two-node Chern-Simons theory at levels $(k,-k)$ and the one-node flavored theory with $N_f$ fundamental pairs, respectively. At superconformal chemical potentials, we propose the Chern-Simons counterpart of the all-order perturbative expression for the flavored theory and obtain convergent integral representations for the rank-independent constants of both theories. At integer parameters, these constants admit explicit evaluations involving finite sums of Clausen functions and $ζ(3)$, while their large-parameter expansions Borel sum to the defining integrals. At $k=1,3$ and $N_f=1,3$, we conjecture complete exponential remainders expressed through Eichler integrals of weight-four Eisenstein series. Supported by high-precision Bethe solutions, the resulting completions coincide at unit quotient order but differ at order three. We determine the minimal modular levels of the Eisenstein generators and establish convergence and natural boundaries for their primitives. Within the relative M-theory localization prescription, a complementary bulk analysis recovers the Chern-Simons shifted rank and constant in the $b\to0^+$ limit of the squashed-sphere partition function. We propose a generalization incorporating fixed-locus contributions that yields the corresponding flavored-theory result. The two theories therefore share a modular structure in their nonperturbative coefficient laws, but distinct perturbative shifts and exponential corrections resolve differences invisible in their common leading $N^{3/2}$ behavior.

</details>


### [85] [Quadratic Sum Rules for Scalar Amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18836)
*Karol Kampf,Jiří Novotný,David Podivín,Jonah Stalknecht*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 论文发现了一类新的二次求和规则（quadratic-sum rules），可联系不同重数（multiplicity）的标量振幅，并在非线性sigma模型及其弦论启发的扩展、KLT矩阵逆的对角元以及具有非导数相互作用和一般传播子的平面标量理论中建立并推广了这些关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kleiss--Kuijf、Bern--Carrasco--Johansson关系及其弦单值性（string monodromy）推广长期为分振幅（partial amplitudes）提供强有力的约束，受其成功启发，作者寻求新的一类约束关系。

Method: 结合递推（recursion）、运动学平移（kinematic shifts）与符号回归（symbolic regression）三种手段来构造并验证这些二次求和规则。

Result: 在非线性sigma模型（nonlinear sigma model）及其弦论启发扩展中建立了该类求和规则，并将其推广至逆Kawai--Lewellen--Tye矩阵（inverse KLT matrix）的对角元，进而推广到具有非导数相互作用与一般传播子的平面标量理论。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了一类联系不同重数标量振幅的新型二次求和规则，适用范畴超出传统KK/BCJ类关系，可用于更广泛的标量理论。

Abstract: Kleiss--Kuijf and Bern--Carrasco--Johansson relations, and their string monodromy generalizations, have long provided some of the most powerful and useful constraints on partial amplitudes. Motivated by their success, we uncover a new class of quadratic-sum rules that connect scalar amplitudes at different multiplicities. Combining recursion, kinematic shifts, and symbolic regression, we establish them for the nonlinear sigma model and its string-inspired extensions. We then extend these relations to diagonal elements of the inverse Kawai--Lewellen--Tye matrix and subsequently to planar scalar theories with nonderivative interactions and general propagators.

</details>

The same method applies to the $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ gauging of two, three and four copies of the $E_1$ theory, which describes, respectively, $\mathrm{SU}(4)_0$, pure $\mathrm{Spin}(8)$ and the untwisted $\mathfrak{so}(8)$ KK theory. Conclusion: 研究建立了描述非拓扑手征反常（non-topological chiral anomalies）的更广泛流体力学框架，为凝聚态与高能物理系统中一系列此前未探索的反常诱导输运现象提供了基础。

Abstract: We derive the chiral anomaly in a thermal medium and show that, in addition to the well-established topological piece, the anomaly acquires a previously overlooked non-topological contribution, relevant for non-gauged axial symmetries. We further propose an experimentally accessible new effect in Weyl semimetals which we call the Chiral Magnetoelectric Effect: an electric field proportional to the magnetic field is generated by the non-topological anomaly, even in the absence of an externally applied electric field. </details>


### [88] [A Bound on Wormhole Probing Time from Holographic Multi-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19035)
*Takanori Anegawa,Norihiro Iizuka*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文证明：对于非旋转 BTZ 黑洞（nonrotating BTZ black hole），无论边界划分的分区数、大小与排列如何，多体纠缠熵（multi-entropy）的探测时间存在统一有限上界，增加分区数无法无限延长对虫洞（wormhole）增长的探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两体纠缠熵（bipartite entanglement entropy）最终饱和，无法追踪永恒虫洞（eternal wormhole）的持续增长；近期发现真正的三分体多熵（tripartite multi-entropy）在饱和后仍可探测虫洞增长，由此引出问题：增加分区数能否无限延长探测时间。

Method: 采用多路 maximin 处方（multiway maximin prescription），并假设其选出的代表为规则的时空平稳网络（regular spacetime-stationary networks）；通过构造比较网络（comparison network）——将含虫洞内部的中心区域整体分配给单一分区——进行长度比较论证。

Result: 对任意固定有限边界划分，探测时间存在与分区数目、大小及排列无关的共同有限上界；任一 Cauchy 切片（Cauchy slice）上的网络若长于比较网络则不能最小化长度，而平稳网络所需的中心长度随时间增长、比较网络的中心长度则一致有界，故晚期网络被 maximin 处方排除。

Conclusion: 超过该上界后，maximin 选出的网络不再进入黑洞内部，故增加分区数不能无限延长探测时间。

Abstract: Bipartite entanglement entropy eventually saturates and therefore cannot track the continued growth of an eternal wormhole. Recently, it was shown that genuine tripartite multi-entropy can probe wormhole growth even after bipartite entanglement entropies have saturated. This naturally raises the question: can increasing the number of parties extend the probing time indefinitely? Adopting the multiway maximin prescription and assuming that its selected representatives are regular spacetime-stationary networks, we prove that the answer is no for nonrotating BTZ black holes. We establish a common finite upper bound on the probing time for every fixed finite boundary partition, independent of the number, sizes, and arrangement of its parties. For any network on any Cauchy slice, there exists a comparison network that assigns the entire central region containing the wormhole interior to a single party. A network longer than this comparison network cannot minimize length on that slice. The central length required by a stationary network connecting the two boundaries grows with time, whereas the central length of the comparison network remains uniformly bounded. At sufficiently late times, such a network therefore cannot minimize length on any Cauchy slice containing it and is excluded from the maximin prescription. Consequently, beyond this bound, no network selected by maximin enters the black-hole interior.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前虫洞研究仅采用单一不可约表示，未厘清3×3不可约表示的物理内容，尤其是两个孪生表示（twin representations）与质量壳上螺旋度（helicity）的对应关系不明。

Method: 在2+1维中采用投影自旋-1 DKP方程，证明投影反对称张量与投影矢量代数对偶，并据此分析静态虫洞的一阶径向DKP系统，与主变量约化（master-variable reduction）及Darboux变换（Darboux transformation）相比较。

Abstract: We use the projected spin-1 Duffin--Kemmer--Petiau (DKP) equation in $2+1$ dimensions to clarify the physical content of the irreducible $3\times3$ representations. The projected antisymmetric tensor is algebraically dual to the projected vector, and the two twin representations select conjugate self-dual covariant-curl sectors, which reduce to opposite massive helicities in the flat-space limit. We then apply this structure to the static wormhole problem previously studied with a single irreducible representation. The first-order radial DKP system contains an exceptional normalizable bound-state branch at $ω^2=M^2$ that is absent from the master-variable reduction, whereas the generic master and circular sectors are related by a Darboux transformation. The latter preserves the transmission and reflection probabilities, while the quasinormal modes are organized into two pole families in the complex radial-momentum plane, leading to the physical pairing $(ω,-ω^*)$ rather than a global $\pmω$ prescription.

</details>


### [90] [Stochastic gauge fixing from the holographic renormalization group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19083)
*Soner Albayrak*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有随机量子化（stochastic quantization）与全息Wilson重整化（holographic Wilsonian renormalization）的对应字典此前未覆盖四维反德西特（anti-de Sitter, AdS）空间中的Yang-Mills理论，本文旨在将其扩展至该情形。

Method: 在树阶（tree level）建立对应：识别体规范场（bulk gauge field）的径向分量为Zwanziger规范固定函数，径向规范（radial gauge）即Parisi-Wu量子化，其规范模的随机游走对应纵向体传播子；并在有限截断（finite cutoff）下以闭式（closed form）推导胶子的三迹与四迹耦合。

Result: 得到有限截断下胶子三迹与四迹耦合（triple- and quadruple-trace couplings）的闭式表达；在横向数据（transverse data）上，其对体规范的依赖集中于单一零模（zero mode），该零模在平空间极限（flat-space limit）中消失。

Conclusion: 结果支持随机量子化与全息Wilson重整化之间存在可推广至非阿贝尔规范理论的对应结构，且体规范依赖性在横向扇区可约化为单一零模并在平空间极限下消去。

Abstract: We extend the dictionary between stochastic quantization and holographic Wilsonian renormalization to Yang-Mills theory in four-dimensional anti-de Sitter space at tree level. </details>


### [91] [Conditional Principle of Least Action: Gauge Algebra and Noether's Second Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19097)
*V. A. Abakumova,S. L. Lyakhovich*

Conclusion: 约束方程的规范代数与受限作用量的规范代数之间存在确定联系，可作为推广诺特定理第二定理及其逆定理的基础，通用方法可借线性化引力加 Cotton 方程的实例加以演示。

Abstract: We consider the problem of a conditional extremum of an action within a class of fields constrained by differential equations. For this setup, we establish the interconnection between the gauge symmetry algebra of the equations constraining the admissible fields and that of the action restricted to this class of fields. The general method is illustrated by the example of linearized gravity with the Einstein-Hilbert action and the class of admissible metrics restricted by the Cotton equation.

</details>


### [92] [Classification of Conformal Ising/Tricritical Ising Interfaces Preserving $\mathcal{W}_3^{\mathbb{Z}_2}$ Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19129)
*Bufan Zheng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文对保持手征代数（chiral algebra）𝒲₃^{ℤ₂} 的 Ising 与三临界 Ising 模型（tricritical Ising model）之间的共形界面（conformal interface）进行分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是刻画 Ising 与三临界 Ising 模型之间保持特定手征代数 𝒲₃^{ℤ₂} 的共形界面，并厘清其中保持完整乘积 Virasoro 对称性的可分解界面与破坏该对称性的非可分解界面的结构。

Result: 得到完整的 24 个基本边界态（fundamental boundary state），其中 18 个对应保持完整乘积 Virasoro 对称性的可分解界面（factorized interface），其余 6 个破坏该对称性；这 6 个界面被显式构造，其环面配分函数被确定，且它们之间通过拓扑缺陷线修饰（topological defect line dressing）相互关联，其中一个与 Gaiotto 提出的重整化群畴壁（RG domain wall）一致。

Conclusion: 在 𝒲₃^{ℤ₂} 手征代数保持条件下，Ising 与三临界 Ising 模型间的共形界面可被完整分类：24 个基本边界态中 18 个为保持完整乘积 Virasoro 对称性的可分解界面，6 个为非可分解界面并由拓扑缺陷线修饰相联系，其中包含 Gaiotto 的重整化群畴壁。

Abstract: We classify conformal interfaces between the Ising and tricritical Ising models that preserve the chiral algebra $\mathcal{W}_3^{\mathbb{Z}_2}$.
