We obtain a complete set of 24 fundamental boundary states, of which 18 correspond to factorized interfaces preserving the full product Virasoro symmetry, while the remaining six break this symmetry. We construct these six interfaces explicitly and determine their annulus partition functions. They are related by topological defect line dressings, and one coincides with the RG domain wall proposed by Gaiotto.

</details>


### [93] [Large-$N$ expansion in SU($N$) Yang-Mills-like theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19144)
*Marco Bochicchio,Mauro Papinutto,Francesco Scardino*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 论文系统研究 SU($N$)（而非 U($N$)）类杨-米尔斯（Yang-Mills, YM）理论在大 $N$ 't Hooft 展开下的拓扑结构，指出光滑的 't Hooft 拓扑在 SU($N$) 及重整化后的 U($N$) 理论中普遍不完备，并需引入奇异拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有认识是 U($N$) 纯 YM 及类 YM 理论的连通单迹关联函数存在按定向闭黎曼面亏格分类的大 $N$ 't Hooft 展开；由于 U($N$) 内的 U($1$) 解耦，SU($N$) 情形是否与之相同或存在本质差异需要澄清。

Method: 在拓扑层面系统分析 SU($N$) 类 YM 理论的大 $N$ 展开，论证 't Hooft 光滑拓扑已穷尽 U($N$) 情形，并识别 SU($N$) 中额外出现的拓扑类型；同时讨论重整化如何将相互作用的 SU($N$) 部分与自由的 U($1$) 部分分离。

Result: 研究表明 't Hooft 光滑拓扑在 SU($N$) 与重整化 U($N$) 类 YM 理论的大 $N$ 展开中一般是（generically）不完备的；对高扭度准部分子算子（higher-twist quasi-partonic operators）的关联函数，额外拓扑可解释为奇异收缩闭曲面（singular pinched closed surfaces）。

Conclusion: SU($N$) 理论的大 $N$ 展开需包含额外的（可能奇异的）拓扑，从而推广了此前关于扭度-2 算子（twist-2 operators）中出现收缩环面（pinched tori）的结论；U($N$) 与 SU($N$) 展开在重整化意义下本质不同。

Abstract: It has been known for fifty years that bare U($N$) pure Yang-Mills (YM) theory admits the large-$N$ 't Hooft expansion for the $n$-point connected correlators of gauge-invariant single-trace operators, where the corresponding Feynman diagrams are topologically classified by the sum over the genus $g$ of $n$-punctured smooth orientable closed Riemann surfaces. In fact, 't Hooft expansion extends verbatim to U($N$) YM-like theories whose Lagrangians only involve fields valued in the U($N$) Lie algebra - including supersymmetric (SUSY) extensions of pure YM theory such as $\mathcal{N}=1,4$ SUSY YM. The aim of the present paper is to systematically work out the topology of the large-$N$ expansion in SU($N$) - as opposed to U($N$) - YM-like theories, where a number of subtleties inevitably arise. Indeed, since the U($1$) inside the U($N$) YM-like theories decouples, the large-$N$ U($N$) and SU($N$) expansions should either essentially coincide or intrinsically differ - in the latter case necessarily by extra - possibly singular - topologies, as the 't Hooft expansion in U($N$) YM-like theories already exhausts all the smooth topologies. In fact, we demonstrate that 't Hooft smooth topologies are actually generically incomplete in the large-$N$ expansion of both the SU($N$) and renormalized U($N$) YM-like theories, where renormalization inevitably separates the SU($N$) sector that is interacting from the U($1$) sector that is free. Specifically, we interpret the extra topologies that arise in SU($N$) theories for correlators of higher-twist quasi-partonic operators as singular pinched closed surfaces, thus extending our previous investigation of correlators of twist-$2$ operators for which extra pinched tori arise.

</details>


### [94] [The $\mathbb{Z}_2$ twist line defect of the 3D Ising CFT on the fuzzy sphere from multi-target density matrix renormalization group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19233)
*Jing-Hao Jin,Yang Qi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作将 3D Ising CFT 的 Z2 扭转线缺陷实现在模糊球面上，并用多目标 DMRG 求解，获得了远超严格对角化的缺陷谱，并对缺陷原初算符的标度维度做了受控的连续极限外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模糊球正则化（fuzzy-sphere regularization）结合径向量子化（radial quantization）是研究 3D 共形场论（CFT）的有力框架，但此前共形缺陷（conformal defects）在该框架下仅能通过小体系的严格对角化（exact diagonalization, ED）处理，体系尺寸受限。

Method: 对 Z2 奇费米子味施加反周期边界条件，在模糊球面上实现 3D Ising CFT 的 Z2 扭转线缺陷（twist line defect）；采用多目标密度矩阵重整化群（multi-target DMRG）算法，在共享矩阵乘积（matrix-product）基下同时优化各对称扇区的多个低能态。

Result: 可达到 2s+1=25（即 49 个无自旋轨道）的体系尺寸，远超 ED。所得谱满足扭转线的自旋-宇称选择定则（spin-parity selection rule），并沿缺陷线组织为共形多重态（conformal multiplets）。以位移算符（displacement operator）为内禀标尺、其受保护后代为检验、体应力张量（bulk stress tensor）为独立交叉验证，识别主导有限尺寸修正并实现受控连续极限外推，从而收紧了若干已知缺陷原初算符（defect primaries）的维度，并解析出四个更高位的缺陷原初算符。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了“模糊球 + 多目标 DMRG”作为共形缺陷精密研究的框架。

Abstract: The fuzzy-sphere regularization provides a powerful framework for studying three-dimensional (3D) conformal field theories (CFTs) through radial quantization, but conformal defects have so far been studied with it only by exact diagonalization (ED) of small systems. In this work we realize the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ twist line defect of the 3D Ising CFT on the fuzzy sphere by imposing antiperiodic boundary conditions on the $\mathbb{Z}_2$-odd fermion flavor, and solve it with a multi-target density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) algorithm that optimizes several low-lying states of each symmetry sector in a common matrix-product basis. This gives the defect spectrum at system sizes well beyond the reach of ED (up to $2s+1=25$, or $49$ spinless orbitals). The spectrum obeys the spin-parity selection rule of the twist line and organizes into conformal multiplets along the line. Using the displacement operator as an intrinsic ruler, its protected descendant as a test, and the bulk stress tensor as an independent cross-check, we identify the leading finite-size corrections and obtain controlled continuum extrapolations, which sharpen the dimensions of several known defect primaries and resolve four higher-lying ones. This study establishes the fuzzy sphere combined with multi-target DMRG as a framework for precision studies of conformal defects.

</details>


### [95] [Holographic Entanglement after a Global Quench in Noncommutative Yang-Mills Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19307)
*Elahe Hassanpour,Komeil Babaei Velni,M. Reza Mohammadi Mozaffar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作利用Vaidya几何研究四维N=4非交换Yang-Mills理论在全局淬火（global quench）后全息纠缠熵（holographic entanglement entropy）与混态关联的时间演化，揭示多个随非交换尺度与壳层穿越尺度关系而变的早期增长区制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在刻画非交换性（noncommutativity）如何改变全局淬火后纠缠熵与混态关联的动力学，尤其是早期增长区制及趋于平衡的过程。

Method: 采用Vaidya几何作为全息对偶模型，考察沿非交换方向延伸的条形区域（strip-shaped regions），比较壳层穿越尺度与非交换尺度的关系，并分析互信息（mutual information）与纠缠楔截面（entanglement wedge cross section）等量。

Result: 发现两个早期增长区制：系数正比于注入能量密度与非交换尺度平方的对数增长（logarithmic growth）后接二次增长（quadratic growth）；宽条带出现由纠缠速度（entanglement velocity）主导的中间线性增长，且该速度随非交换性增大。平衡趋近受蝴蝶速度（butterfly velocity）支配，竞争分支可改变饱和时间；两条不相交条带中与宽度无关的对数项在互信息中抵消，纠缠楔截面在连通纠缠楔占优时呈现同一纠缠速度控制的线性增长。

Conclusion: 非交换性显著影响全息纠缠与混态关联的早期增长结构、纠缠速度及饱和时间，蝴蝶速度与极值面（extremal surfaces）连续演化共同决定平衡趋近行为。

Abstract: The time evolution of holographic entanglement entropy and mixed-state correlations following a global quench in four-dimensional N=4 noncommutative Yang-Mills theory is investigated using a Vaidya geometry. For strip-shaped regions extended along a noncommutative direction, two distinct early-time growth regimes emerge, depending on the relation between the shell-crossing and noncommutative scales: logarithmic growth, with a coefficient proportional to the injected energy density and the square of the noncommutative scale, followed by quadratic growth. For sufficiently wide strips, an intermediate linear-growth regime appears, governed by an entanglement velocity that increases with noncommutativity. The approach to equilibrium is also examined, revealing the role of the butterfly velocity in the continuous evolution of extremal surfaces, while competing branches can modify the physical saturation time. For two disjoint strips, the leading width-independent logarithmic contribution cancels in the mutual information, whereas the entanglement wedge cross section exhibits an intermediate linear-growth regime governed by the same entanglement velocity, provided the connected entanglement wedge remains dominant.

</details>


### [96] [On the Spontaneous symmetry breaking of Conformal Gravity to Weyl and Einstein Gravities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19338)
*Partha Nandi,Gerasimos Kouniatalis,Stelios Stefas,George Zoupanos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在 SO(2,4) 规范理论框架下，特殊共形联络（special-conformal connection）在完全动力学 Yang-Mills-Higgs 理论中仍保持辅助场（auxiliary field）性质，其消去在无源分支上给出四维 Weyl 平方作用量，并对 de Sitter 背景给出部分质量自旋-2 谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：当自发破缺的共形规范理论升级为完全动力学的 Yang-Mills-Higgs 理论时，特殊共形联络是否仍是辅助场，以及其消去在何种条件下导出 Weyl 引力（Weyl gravity）。

Method: 采用 SO(2,4) 规范理论表述处理引力；以伴随 15-plet 的自发对称性破缺（spontaneous symmetry breaking）选定引力扇区，将特殊共形联络保留为独立场，并分析其代数运动方程；随后推广至标准局域 Yang-Mills-Higgs 理论，并研究约化后 Weyl 理论在 de Sitter 时空（de Sitter spacetime）附近的线性化谱。

Result: 在无源投影分支上，该联络的代数方程将其用 Schouten 张量（Schouten tensor）表示，消去后得到四维 Weyl 平方作用量（至 Euler 密度（Euler density）项，且无需额外施加 Schouten 关系）；在 Yang-Mills-Higgs 理论中，由于 so(2,4) 不变双线性型（invariant bilinear form）的结构禁止出现独立动能算符，该联络仍为辅助场，但额外规范与标量扇区可改变其代数解，故精确的 Weyl 约化仅限于无源分支。线性化谱中识别出无质量与部分无质量自旋-2 分支，后者位于 m_PM^2 = 2Λ/3。

Conclusion: 特殊共形联络在完全动力学理论中保持辅助性质，其消去可实现 Weyl 引力相；此外讨论了由反对称标量 6-plet 引起的后续对称性破缺及其在连接 Weyl 引力相与 Einstein 引力中的作用。

Abstract: Does the special-conformal connection remain an auxiliary field when a spontaneously broken conformal gauge theory is promoted to a fully dynamical Yang-Mills-Higgs theory, and under what conditions does its elimination yield Weyl gravity? We address these questions within an $\mathrm{SO}(2,4)$ gauge-theoretic formulation of gravity. Revisiting the gravitational sector selected by spontaneous symmetry breaking through an adjoint $15$-plet, we retain the special-conformal connection as an independent field and demonstrate that, on a source-free projected branch, its algebraic equation of motion determines it in terms of the Schouten tensor. Eliminating this connection yields the four-dimensional Weyl-squared action, up to the Euler density, without imposing the Schouten relation as an additional geometrical constraint. We then extend the analysis to the standard local Yang-Mills-Higgs theory and show that the special-conformal connection remains auxiliary, owing to the structure of the invariant bilinear form of $\mathfrak{so}(2,4)$, which prevents the appearance of an independent kinetic operator. The additional gauge and scalar sectors can modify its algebraic solution, so the exact Weyl reduction is restricted to the source-free branch. We further investigate the linearised spectrum of the reduced Weyl theory around de Sitter spacetime, identifying massless and partially massless spin-2 branches, with the latter occurring at $m_{\mathrm{PM}}^2=2Λ/3$. Finally, we discuss the subsequent symmetry breaking induced by an antisymmetric scalar $6$-plet and its role in connecting the Weyl-gravity phase to Einstein gravity.

</details>


### [97] [The Goldstone--Wilczek Charge on the Rim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19464)
*Laurence A. Jacobs*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在Kaplan–Sen格点圆盘上，磁通Φ=2πα在中心束缚基态电荷Q_core=α−round(α)，边缘携带相反的Goldstone–Wilczek电荷Q_edge=−η/2。边缘算符（边界Schur补）的负本征值计数在磁通周期内不变，电荷转移改由极值本征值穿过无穷远的单极点体现，且ΔQ_edge=½Δsign λ_ext。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：磁通Φ=π时一个单位电荷穿过圆盘，而作为边界有效算符的边缘算符（rim operator，即圆盘哈密顿量在边界格点上的Schur补）如何"记录"这一电荷转移。该问题连接体态拓扑荷与边缘谱不对称性两种电荷描述。

Method: 解析上关联体态电荷Q_core与边缘阶梯的Goldstone–Wilczek电荷Q_edge=−η/2（η为谱不对称性），并研究边缘算符本征值随磁通的演化；数值上在半径R=11、19、34的圆盘上验证全部结论。

Result: 边缘算符负本征值数目在整个磁通周期内保持不变；单位电荷改以一个简单极点（simple pole）的形式进入边缘：极值本征值λ_ext在同一磁通区间内穿过无穷远，对应ΔQ_edge=½Δsign λ_ext。该极点留数可分解为内边缘模式与边缘的耦合与该模式能级斜率倒数之积，并随半径线性增长。

Conclusion: 边缘电荷转移不由谱计数（本征值符号数目）反映，而由边缘算符极值本征值的极点结构承载；留数的因子化与半径线性增长给出了该机制的定量刻画，并在多个半径下获得数值确认。

Abstract: A solenoid flux $Φ=2πα$ threaded through the center of the Kaplan--Sen lattice disk binds a ground-state charge $Q_{\rm core}=α-\mathrm{round}(α)$, and the edge carries exactly the opposite charge. The edge charge is the Goldstone--Wilczek charge of the edge ladder, $Q_{\rm edge}=-\tfrac12η$, with $η$ its spectral asymmetry. At $Φ=π$ one unit of charge crosses the disk. We ask how the rim operator, the Schur complement of the disk Hamiltonian on its boundary sites, registers this transfer. Its count of negative eigenvalues never changes over the flux cycle. Instead, the unit of charge enters the rim through a simple pole: the extremal eigenvalue of the rim operator passes through infinity in the same flux interval as the charge jump, with $ΔQ_{\rm edge}=\tfrac12Δ\,\mathrm{sign}\,λ_{\rm ext}$. The residue factorizes into the coupling of the inner edge mode to the rim and the inverse slope of that mode's level, and grows linearly with the radius. All statements are checked numerically on disks of radius $R=11$, $19$ and $34$.

</details>


### [98] [Local field shifts and the Gribov-Zwanziger horizon functional](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19513)
*Daniel Guimaraes Tedesco*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文用光滑紧支撑 profile 对 Gribov-Zwanziger 作用量做类 Maggiore-Schaden 的重心化（re-centering）研究，论证在固定背景与有限截断下该变换保持视界泛函不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于考察 Gribov-Zwanziger 作用量在 profile 变换下的稳定性，以及近第一 Gribov 视界处 Faddeev-Popov 低本征模的可观测性（visibility）。

Method: 采用光滑紧支撑 profile 实施类 Maggiore-Schaden 重心化；在强制性与围道假设下分析变换，并借助自伴与非自伴压缩（compression）中的谱与源权重分析，辅以连续横场 SU(2) 例子及自由空间树图检验。

Result: 有限截断下视界泛函不变且无 profile 残余依赖；推广到规范场积分需额外支配性假设。近第一 Gribov 视界响应同时依赖最低 Faddeev-Popov 本征值及其源权重，并在非自伴情形区分源"暗度"与截断屏蔽；复合插入保持正则高斯与预解结构，树图检验复现 RGZ 传播子的辅助质量分母。

Conclusion: 该类重心化在给定假设下不影响视界泛函，但规范场积分的推广受限；树图检验仅复现 RGZ 传播子结构，并不能决定凝聚动力学。

Abstract: We study a Maggiore-Schaden-like re-centering of the Gribov-Zwanziger action using a smooth compactly supported profile. At fixed background and finite cutoff, under the stated coercivity and contour hypotheses, this transformation preserves the original horizon functional with no residual dependence on the profile. The extension to gauge-field integration requires additional domination assumptions. Near a self-adjoint first Gribov horizon, the response depends on both the lowest Faddeev-Popov eigenvalue and its source weight. We extend this visibility analysis to non-self-adjoint compressions, separating source darkness from truncation-induced shielding, and recall continuum transverse $SU(2)$ examples. Transporting the composite insertion preserves the regulated Gaussian and resolvent structure. A separate free-space tree-level check reproduces the auxiliary-mass denominator of the RGZ propagator, without determining condensate dynamics

</details>


### [99] [Renormalization Group Flow from 4d Chern-Simons Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19517)
*Meer Ashwinkumar,Masahito Yamazaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作以四维 Chern-Simons 理论（four-dimensional Chern-Simons theory）为框架，从第一性原理的 Feynman 图计算出发，研究其所实现的二维可积量子场论（integrable QFTs）的重整化群（renormalization group, RG）流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四维 Chern-Simons 理论与曲面缺陷（surface defects）耦合可实现二维可积量子场论，但这类二维理论自身的 RG 流尚未在该四维框架内被内蕴地、从第一性原理推导。

Method: 先在 Σ×C 上耦合支撑于 Σ 的有序表面缺陷（order surface defects），利用缺陷的仿射 Kac-Moody 对称性（affine Kac-Moody symmetry）导出单圈 RG 流；再在 Batalin-Vilkovisky 形式下，用无序表面缺陷（disorder surface defects）实现无 Wess-Zumino 项的主手征模型（principal chiral model, PCM）并推导其单圈 RG 流，采用显式计算单圈 Feynman 振幅与计算四维 Chern-Simons 理论中解析挠率（analytic torsion）类比量两种互补方法。

Result: 成功获得 PCM 的非平凡单圈 RG 流，并复现其已知的渐近自由 beta 函数（asymptotically-free beta function）。

Conclusion: 二维可积量子场论的 RG 流可在四维 Chern-Simons 框架内被内蕴地导出，两种互补计算方法的相互印证支持该框架作为理解二维可积 QFT 重整化性质的自洽途径。

Abstract: The four-dimensional Chern-Simons theory coupled with surface defects is known to realize two-dimensional integrable quantum field theories (QFTs). We discuss the renormalization group (RG) flow of such two-dimensional integrable QFTs from first-principles Feynman diagram computations intrinsically in the four-dimensional framework. We first derive the one-loop RG flow of two-dimensional QFTs realized by the four-dimensional Chern-Simons theory on $Σ\times C$ coupled with the order surface defects supported on $Σ$. Here, the affine Kac-Moody symmetry of the defects underlies the one-loop RG flow. We then derive the one-loop RG flow of the principal chiral model (PCM) without Wess-Zumino term, which is realized using disorder surface defects, within the Batalin-Vilkovisky formalism. We obtain the nontrivial one-loop RG flow of the PCM, reproducing its known asymptotically-free beta function, using two complementary methods: by explicitly evaluating one-loop Feynman amplitudes, and by computing an analogue of analytic torsion for four-dimensional Chern-Simons theory.

</details>


### [100] [Confinement--Higgs Complementarity Viewed through Relative Quantum Reference Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19519)
*Kei-Ichi Kondo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文用两个独立构造的量子参考系（quantum reference frames, QRFs）之间的相对取向来表述禁闭--希格斯互补性（confinement--Higgs complementarity），并给出标量--色可观测量的关系性解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对近期无规范固定（gauge-fixing-free）的 SU(2) 格点模拟中出现的标量与色可观测量的关系性含义，以及 CDG 约化产生的非局域分离线（nonlocal separation line）的物理地位，需要一个统一的参考系框架加以刻画。

Method: 构造分别来源于规范场与标量场的两个量子参考系，以两者的相对取向表述互补性；借助精确的连续统与格点恒等式给出关系性解释，并讨论与 Fradkin--Shenker 连续性及 Osterwalder--Seiler 解析性的相容性；此外考察以 Wilson 线为根的对照可观测量（Wilson-rooted control observable）在零 plaquette 耦合下的精确结果。

Result: 给出了 CDG 约化所得额外规范不变非局域分离线的 QRF 解释，说明该候选非局域边界在适当的局域规范不变可观测量类中与 Fradkin--Shenker 连续性及 Osterwalder--Seiler 解析性相容；并表明 Wilson 线构造的对照可观测量在零 plaquette 耦合下有精确结果，且不产生此类非局域分离线。

Conclusion: 禁闭--希格斯互补性可获得基于量子参考系相对取向的关系性表述，该表述为格点模拟中的非局域分离线提供了解释，并借助对照可观测量的差异界定了此类结构的适用范围。

Abstract: We formulate confinement--Higgs complementarity in terms of the relative orientation between two independently constructed quantum reference frames (QRFs): one derived from the gauge field and the other from the scalar field. Exact continuum and lattice identities give a relational interpretation of the scalar--color observables used in recent gauge-fixing-free $SU(2)$ simulations. In particular, we provide a QRF interpretation of the additional gauge-invariant nonlocal separation line obtained from the Cho--Duan--Ge (CDG) reduction reported in lattice simulations of the radially fixed fundamental $SU(2)$ gauge--scalar model. We explain why such a candidate nonlocal boundary is compatible with Fradkin--Shenker continuity and Osterwalder--Seiler analyticity for the appropriate class of local gauge-invariant observables. Separately, we show that a Wilson-rooted control observable admits an exact results at zero plaquette coupling and that it does not cause such a nonlocal separation line.

</details>


### [101] [A proof of Nakamura's conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19558)
*Arsen R. Melikyan,Aleksandr Pinzul*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明了中村（Nakamura）关于二维Toda模型（2D Toda model）与Ernst方程解之间关系的猜想，所用工具是作者此前工作（arXiv:2610.27420）中得到的普法夫解（Pfaffian solutions）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于确立二维Toda模型与广义相对论中Ernst方程之间的猜想性联系，该联系此前仅以猜想形式存在，缺乏证明。

Method: 方法上沿用作者前期结果，即二维Toda模型的普法夫解（Pfaffian solutions），并以此构造性手段证明该猜想。

Result: 结果为该猜想获得证明，从而在普法夫解框架下建立了二维Toda模型与Ernst方程解之间的对应关系。

Conclusion: 结论是猜想成立，二维Toda模型的普法夫解可系统给出Ernst方程的解；具体对应形式与适用范围需参见正文细节。

Abstract: We prove Nakamura's conjecture on the relation between the 2D Toda model and solutions of the Ernst equation, using the Pfaffian solutions obtained in our previous work arXiv:2610.27420.

</details>


### [102] [Negative and Positive Saddle-Selection Ambiguities in the HTEE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19562)
*H. Ebrahim,M. Ali-Akbari,K. Taheranvarian*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究全息类时纠缠熵（holographic timelike entanglement entropy, HTEE）在超尺度破坏 Lifshitz 背景（hyperscaling-violating Lifshitz backgrounds）中复化极值面（complexified extremal surfaces）的鞍点选择歧义（saddle-selection ambiguity）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 Δt→0 时，真空连通与真空非连通两类复极值面同时存在，"最小实部"判据与"真空约化"判据可能选出不同的鞍点，需澄清该歧义是否普遍存在。

Method: 在 2+1 维与 3+1 维对偶场理论中，于 Lifshitz 指数（Lifshitz exponent）和超尺度破坏参数（hyperscaling-violation parameter）破坏共形对称性与洛伦兹不变性的背景下，导出两类鞍点熵 s_{v.c.} 与 s_{v.d.} 的一般表达式并比较实部。

Result: 两种判据除第一个偶然零点（accidental zero）外处处一致；歧义呈现两种形式：负族中 Re(s_{v.c.})=0、Re(s_{v.d.})<0；正族中 Re(s_{v.c.})>0、Re(s_{v.d.})=0，且正族表明歧义在共形 3+1 维情形下依然存在。

Conclusion: 鞍点选择歧义似乎是复化几何方案（complexified-geometry prescription）的普遍特征（generic feature）。

Abstract: The holographic timelike entanglement entropy (HTEE), computed via complexified extremal surfaces, suffers from a saddle-selection ambiguity: for sufficiently small time intervals (\(Δt\to0\)), both a vacuum-connected and a vacuum-disconnected complex extremal surface contribute, and the minimal-real-part criterion and the vacuum-reduction criterion can favor different saddles. We study this ambiguity in strongly coupled field theories whose dual geometries are hyperscaling-violating Lifshitz backgrounds, in which conformal symmetry and Lorentz invariance are broken by the Lifshitz exponent and the hyperscaling-violation parameter. Working in \(2+1\)- and \(3+1\)-dimensional dual field theories, we derive general expressions for the HTEE in the vacuum-connected and vacuum-disconnected saddles, \(s_{v.c.}\) and \(s_{v.d.}\), respectively, and compare their real parts. We find that the two criteria agree everywhere except at the first accidental zero. The ambiguity appears in two distinct forms. In the \emph{negative} family, \(\operatorname{Re}(s_{v.c.})=0\) and \(\operatorname{Re}(s_{v.d.})<0\), whereas in the \emph{positive} family, \(\operatorname{Re}(s_{v.c.})>0\) and \(\operatorname{Re}(s_{v.d.})=0\). The latter shows that the ambiguity survives even in the conformal \(3+1\)-dimensional case. It therefore seems that the saddle-selection ambiguity is a generic feature of the complexified-geometry prescription.

</details>


### [103] [Entanglement and Charge Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19674)
*Tom Shachar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文利用复制技巧（replica trick）计算两个不相交区间上的熵型序参量（entropic order parameter），并将其解释为电荷-反电荷缠绕算符（charge-anticharge intertwiners）所携带的互信息（mutual information），在自由费米子与紧致玻色子体系中给出精确解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在深入理解（1905.10487）中引入的熵型序参量的物理意义与其可计算性，尤其是它与电荷-反电荷缠绕算符所载互信息的联系，并探索其在不同对称性（连续与离散、阿贝尔与非阿贝尔）下的普适行为。

Method: 将问题表述为复制黎曼面（replica Riemann surfaces）上扭曲配分函数（twisted partition functions）的平均，对 U(1) 对称性及其离散 ℤ_N 子群，在自由费米子与紧致玻色子中得到精确解；进一步对两类非可缩循环（non-contractible cycles）取平均以获取额外量；对非阿贝尔对称性采用鞍点展开（saddle expansions）。

Result: 对两类循环的进一步平均给出了一个额外量，即移除缠绕算符时互补电荷测量所导致的 Holevo 信息损失（loss of Holevo information）。该量对玻色子为零；对狄拉克费米子则等于费米子宇称规范化（fermion-parity-gauged）与未规范化理论的互信息之差，且与偶数 ℤ_N 子群的选择无关。

Conclusion: 研究在近接触区间极限（nearly touching intervals limit）及大量费米子 N_F≫1 情形下，由非阿贝尔对称性的鞍点展开导出了普适行为，表明该熵型序参量的框架可推广至非阿贝尔情形。

Abstract: We compute the entropic order parameter introduced in (1905.10487) using the replica trick for two disjoint intervals. For a global symmetry, this quantity measures the mutual information carried by charge-anticharge intertwiners. We formulate the problem in terms of averages over twisted partition functions on replica Riemann surfaces, and obtain exact solutions for free fermions and compact bosons, for $U(1)$ symmetry and its discrete $\mathbb Z_N$ subgroups. We show that further averaging over both families of non-contractible cycles gives access to an additional quantity, which is the loss of Holevo information associated with complementary-charge measurements that occurs upon removing the intertwiners. For the boson, this quantity vanishes, whereas for Dirac fermions it equals the difference between the mutual information of the fermion-parity-gauged and ungauged theories, independently of the choice of even $\mathbb Z_N$ subgroup. Finally, we extend the analysis to non-Abelian symmetries using saddle expansions, deriving universal behavior in the nearly touching intervals limit and for a large number of fermions $N_F\gg1$.

</details>


### [104] [Algebraic instantons and non-Gaussian vacuum in quantum mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19761)
*Rahim Aziz,Hao Geng,Thomas Schaefer,Mithat Ünsal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究环上受势 $V(x)=16\lambda\sin^4(x/2)$ 作用的粒子：微扰真空无能隙（gapless），标准瞬子（instanton）微积分失效。作者通过将瞬子轨迹与路径积分（path integral）的精确真空部分匹配，算出一瞬子振幅，其前因子标度为 $\hbar^{4/3}$，与数值谱高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准量子力学瞬子计算依赖势的谐振极小（harmonic minima）；当微扰真空为反常非谐形式 $V\simeq\lambda x^{2n}$（$n\ge2$）时，标准构造失效，本文旨在处理这一失效情形。

Method: 选取环上 $V(x)=16\lambda\sin^4(x/2)$ 的模型，其瞬子与涨落算符（fluctuation operator）有闭式解；由于极小处 $V''=0$，零模（zero mode）位于连续谱阈值、单圈行列式（one-loop determinant）红外发散。作者将瞬子轨迹与路径积分中精确的真空部分相匹配来计算一瞬子振幅，并借助中间态求和（sum over intermediate states）给出替代推导。

Result: 量子力学产生非微扰能隙（non-perturbative gap）$E_q\propto\hbar^{4/3}\lambda^{1/3}$，瞬子嵌入于动力学上带隙的真空中，类似 Yang–Mills 理论情形。所得前因子（prefactor）标度为 $\hbar^{4/3}$，与数值谱吻合精度极高；经典瞬子间相互作用按 $T^{-3}$ 代数衰减，而正确结果为受动力学生成能隙控制的指数衰减，超出量子时间尺度。

Conclusion: 对无能隙非谐真空，瞬子方法需以路径积分精确真空部分作匹配方可给出正确的一瞬子振幅；动力学生成能隙决定了瞬子间相互作用的指数衰减，且中间态求和提供了等价推导。

Abstract: The standard instanton calculus in quantum mechanics is based on harmonic minima in the potential. When the perturbative vacuum is anharmonic, $V\simeqλx^{2n}$ with $n\ge2$, the standard construction fails. We study this problem for a particle on a circle and subject to a potential $V(x)=16λ\sin^4(x/2)$. The instanton and its fluctuation operator are known in closed form, but they differ qualitatively from the familiar ones. Because $V''=0$ at the minimum, the classical vacuum is gapless, the instanton decays algebraically (an algebraic instanton), the zero mode is located at the threshold of a continuous spectrum, and the one-loop determinant is infrared divergent. Quantum mechanics generates a non-perturbative gap $E_q\propto\hbar^{4/3}λ^{1/3}$, and the instanton is embedded in a vacuum that is dynamically gapped, similar to what happens in Yang--Mills theory. We show that the one-instanton amplitude can be computed by matching the instanton trajectory to the exact vacuum part of the path integral. The resulting prefactor scales as $\hbar^{4/3}$, and this expression agrees with the numerical spectrum to a very high accuracy. The classical interaction between instantons decays as $T^{-3}$, whereas the correct result is an exponential decay controlled by the dynamically generated gap, beyond the quantum time scale. We provide an alternative derivation of this result based on a sum over intermediate states.

</details>


### [105] [Wilsonian renormalization group flow in QED with the Pauli term](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19778)
*Sorato Nagao,Hiroshi Suzuki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 利用梯度流精确重整化群（GFERG）研究含Pauli项（Pauli term）的量子电动力学（QED）的重整化群流，在大$N_f$近似下发现$e_*=0$、$\kappa_*\neq0$的非平凡不动点，但有限$N_f$下费米子线上与$N_f$无关的单圈效应会将其消除，除非$N_f$达数十量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是考察Pauli耦合$\kappa_\tau\bar\psi\sigma_{\mu\nu}F_{\mu\nu}\psi$对QED重整化群流的影响，并与Gies与Ziebell提出的渐近安全QED（asymptotically safe QED）不动点进行比较。

Method: 采用梯度流精确重整化群（gradient flow exact renormalization group, GFERG），其规范Ward–Takahashi恒等式（gauge Ward–Takahashi identity）保持常规形式并被精确保持，规范耦合仅通过光子反常维度（photon anomalous dimension）$\gamma_A$流动；分析在领头大$N_f$近似与有限$N_f$的单圈水平分别展开。

Result: 领头大$N_f$下Pauli耦合诱导的真空极化（vacuum polarization）给出$e_*=0$、$\kappa_*\neq0$、$\gamma_A=-1$的不动点，含三个相关方向（relevant directions），与渐近安全QED的某一不动点相似。有限$N_f$下，费米子线上与$N_f$无关的单圈效应（尤指正比于$\kappa^2$的可加质量重整化）会消除该不动点，除非$N_f$至少为数十；$N_f=1$时弱Pauli耦合下无不动点。

Conclusion: $N_f=1$的完整流中$\kappa_\tau$为无关（irrelevant）并趋向诱导值$\kappa_{\text{ind}}(e_\tau)$，弱耦合下正比于$e_\tau^3$；而$e_\tau^2$如无Pauli项的QED一样为边缘无关（marginally irrelevant）。

Abstract: We study the renormalization group (RG) flow of quantum electrodynamics (QED) with a Pauli term, $κ_τ\Barψσ_{μν}F_{μν}ψ$, by using the gradient flow exact renormalization group (GFERG). In the GFERG, the gauge Ward--Takahashi identity takes the conventional form and is exactly preserved, and the gauge coupling flows only through the photon anomalous dimension~$γ_A$. In the leading large-$N_f$ approximation, the vacuum polarization induced by the Pauli coupling yields a nontrivial fixed point with~$e_*=0$, $κ_*\neq0$, and~$γ_A=-1$. This fixed point has three relevant directions, as does one of the fixed points of the asymptotically safe QED proposed by Gies and Ziebell. At finite~$N_f$, however, the $N_f$-independent one-loop effects on the fermion line, in particular the additive mass renormalization proportional to~$κ^2$, eliminate this fixed point unless $N_f$ is at least several tens; for~$N_f=1$ there is no fixed point at weak Pauli coupling. Along the full flow for~$N_f=1$, $κ_τ$ is irrelevant and is attracted to an induced value~$κ_{\text{ind}}(e_τ)$, which is proportional to~$e_τ^3$ at weak coupling, while $e_τ^2$ is marginally irrelevant as in QED without the Pauli term.

</details>


### [106] [On $ι$Quiver (BPS) Algebras and (Strict) Partitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19856)
*Jiakang Bao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究分裂型（split type）的 $\iota$quiver 代数（$\iota$quiver algebras），视为仿射 A 型 quiver BPS 代数，预期给出扭仿射 Yangian 与（椭圆）$\iota$quantum toroidal 代数，并讨论其表现、余理想结构、orientifold 识别及组合基表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 这类代数据信可能来源于带 orientifold 的 $\mathbb{C}\times\mathbb{C}^2/\mathbb{Z}_n$ 上 D-膜（D-branes）所对应的结构，因而需要构造并刻画相应的代数框架。

Method: 围绕分裂型 $\iota$quiver 代数展开，考察其有理情形（rational cases）下的极小表现（minimalistic presentations）与余理想结构（coideal structures），并探讨如何从带 orientifold 作用的 quiver 理论（quiver theories）中识别这些代数。

Result: 讨论了具有组合基（combinatorial bases）的表示；特别地，$n=1,2$ 的代数在参数某些极限下具有以（染色）严格/对称分拆（(coloured) strict/symmetric partitions）标号的表示，且 $n=1$ 情形恢复已知的某些理论的 BPS 计数（BPS counting）。

Conclusion: 该框架将 $\iota$quiver 代数与扭仿射 Yangian、（椭圆）$\iota$quantum toroidal 代数及 orientifold quiver 理论联系起来，并通过组合表示在 $n=1$ 时与已知 BPS 计数相符。

Abstract: We consider the $ι$quiver algebras of split type associated with the quiver BPS algebras of affine type A. They are supposed to give twisted affine Yangians and (elliptic) $ι$quantum toroidal algebras that could possibly arise from D-branes on $\mathbb{C}\times\mathbb{C}^2/\mathbb{Z}_n$ with orientifolds. We mention some properties of these algebras, such as the minimalistic presentations in the rational cases and the coideal structures. We also investigate how one might identify these algebras from the quiver theories with orientifold actions. Moreover, we discuss some representations with combinatorial bases of these algebras. In particular, the algebras with $n=1,2$ in certain limits of the parameters have representations labelled by (coloured) strict/symmetric partitions, which in the case of $n=1$ recovers the known BPS counting of certain theories.

</details>


### [107] [Towards a foundation model for Type IIB flux vacua](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19950)
*Sven Krippendorf,Magdalena Larfors,Zhimei Liu,Andreas Schachner,Moritz Walden*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作将生成式模型（generative models）用于 Type IIB 通量真空（flux vacua）的构建，在多个 Calabi-Yau 几何上比较 Transformer 与扩散模型（diffusion models），并结合预训练与强化学习（reinforcement learning）后训练实现定向采样，同时验证跨几何信息迁移的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画 Type IIB 通量景观（flux landscape）需要可扩展的真空构造方法，而传统逐点搜索难以覆盖多个 Calabi-Yau 几何与指定物理性质的定向需求。

Method: 训练 Transformer 与扩散模型，联合生成量子化通量（quantised fluxes）以及复结构模（complex structure moduli）和轴子-伸缩子（axio-dilaton）的近似值；采用包含预训练（pre-training）与基于强化学习（reinforcement learning）的后训练的分阶段流程，并通过在专门数据集上微调（fine-tuning）考察从小 h^{1,2} 几何向大 h^{1,2} 几何的结构迁移。

Result: 结果表明生成式模型能够学习不同紧致化（compactifications）上的通量真空分布，改善对具有选定性质真空的搜索，并实现几何之间的信息迁移；同时分析了各训练阶段对生成分布与定向采样能力的改进作用。

Conclusion: 该研究为构建 Type IIB 通量景观的基础模型（foundation model）提供了具体依据。

Abstract: Progress in characterising the Type IIB flux landscape requires scalable methods for constructing flux vacua. In this work, we investigate generative models as a framework for sampling vacua across multiple Calabi-Yau geometries and for directing searches towards specified physical properties. We compare transformers and diffusion models trained to generate quantised fluxes together with approximate values of the complex structure moduli and axio-dilaton. We exploit a staged training procedure comprising pre-training, and post-training via reinforcement learning, and assess how each stage improves the generated distributions and enables targeted sampling. We further examine whether structures learned from geometries with smaller $h^{1,2}$ can be transferred to geometries with larger $h^{1,2}$ through fine-tuning on specialised datasets. Our results provide evidence that generative models can learn distributions of flux vacua across distinct compactifications, improve searches for vacua with selected properties and transfer information between geometries. This establishes a concrete basis for developing a foundation model of the Type IIB flux landscape.

</details>


### [108] [Neumann zero modes and the locality of the derivative expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19953)
*C. D. Fosco,F. D. Mazzitelli*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究导数展开（derivative expansion, DE）在无质量场满足诺伊曼条件（Neumann conditions）时出现非解析项的问题，指出需在 DE 中加入新型非局域项，并预测了 $|{\mathbf k}_\shortparallel|^3$ 形式的非解析项及其在柱-面几何中的 $(L/R)^{3/2}$ 相对修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DE 作为近距离表面间卡西米尔相互作用能（Casimir interaction energy）的局域泛函近似，在涉及无质量场且施加诺伊曼条件时会产生非解析项，原有局域展开不足以刻画该效应，需明确其来源与补救方式。

Method: 作者定位导致非解析性的特定模式（mode），构造由其涨落（fluctuations）产生的有效作用量（effective action），并将其与有效理论中因圈图中无质量粒子（massless particles in loops）而引入的非局域项相类比，进而给出对 DE 的非局域补充。

Result: 构造的有效作用量重现了已知的非解析贡献；预测在 $3+1$ 维、零温度下的诺伊曼条件会产生 $|{\mathbf k}_\shortparallel|^3$ 量级的项，其系数不含自由参数，位于展开的（局域）二阶与四阶之间；对柱面对平面几何，零模圈（zero-mode loop）给局域展开增加相对阶为 $(L/R)^{3/2}$ 的项，其系数被显式算出，并能解释该几何现有数值拟合的行为。

Conclusion: 无质量模式导致的非解析性要求 DE 补充非局域项；所提出的有效作用量框架可自洽地重现已知结果并给出可检验的新预测，且与已有数值拟合相符。

Abstract: The derivative expansion (DE) provides a useful approximation to the Casimir interaction energy between two close surfaces. It has the form of a local functional, obtained from a zero momentum expansion. However, when the system involves a massless field satisfying Neumann conditions, that expansion may develop nonanalytic terms. We present here a study on how this property reflects itself in the need to add to the DE some novel, nonlocal terms. These are, we argue, akin to the ones introduced in effective theories because of the presence of massless particles in loops. We pinpoint the mode responsible for this effect, and construct the effective action due to its fluctuations, showing that it reproduces known nonanalytic contributions, and present the corresponding nonlocal addition to the DE. We also predict a new nonanalyticity: Neumann conditions in $3+1$ dimensions at zero temperature produce a term of order $|{\mathbf k}_\shortparallel |^3$, with a parameter-free coefficient, between the (local) second and fourth orders of the expansion. We show that, for a cylinder in front of a plane, the zero-mode loop adds to the local expansion a term of relative order $(L/R)^{3/2}$, with a coefficient that we compute explicitly, showing that it accounts for the behaviour of the existing numerical fits for that geometry.

</details>


### [109] [On reflection positivity of lattice chiral fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20084)
*Zhiyao Lu,Shu-Heng Shao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文指出，重叠外尔费米子（overlap Weyl fermion）在有限晶格间距下于二维和四维不满足反射正定性（reflection positivity），因而标准 Osterwalder–Schrader 重构无法给出具有自伴哈密顿量的希尔伯特空间；二维情形下还无法“切开”，与连续理论中引力反常（gravitational anomaly）的预期一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重叠外尔费米子是 Lüscher 构造格点手征规范理论（lattice chiral gauge theories）所依赖的时空晶格实现，其是否能通过反射正定性支撑标准量子化与希尔伯特空间重构，是一个基础性问题。

Method: 直接考察重叠外尔费米子在二维与四维有限晶格间距下的反射正定性（reflection positivity），并分析二维情形下引入边界时格点作用量的局域性。

Result: 重叠外尔费米子在二维和四维均非反射正定，导致 Osterwalder–Schrader 重构（Osterwalder–Schrader reconstruction）失效。二维下该费米子无法被切开：存在边界时格点作用量变为非局域（nonlocal），这与连续场论的引力反常相符。作为对比，重叠狄拉克费米子（overlap Dirac fermion）已知是反射正定的，但其紧致轴矢对称性（compact axial symmetry）在反射下不变，而反射不变的轴矢对称性是非紧致的，二者同属一个 Onsager 代数（Onsager algebra）。

Conclusion: 重叠外尔费米子在格点手征规范理论的反射正定性基础上存在实质性障碍；重叠狄拉克费米子虽反射正定，但其轴矢对称性的反射性质与非紧致性构成 Onsager 代数结构，需在相应代数框架内理解。

Abstract: We point out that the overlap Weyl fermion, the spacetime lattice realization of a Weyl fermion underlying Lüscher's construction of lattice chiral gauge theories, is not reflection positive in two and four dimensions at finite lattice spacing. Consequently, the standard Osterwalder--Schrader reconstruction does not yield a Hilbert space with a self-adjoint Hamiltonian. In two dimensions, we further show that the overlap Weyl fermion cannot be cut open: the lattice action becomes nonlocal in the presence of a boundary, as expected from the gravitational anomaly of the continuum field theory. By contrast, the overlap Dirac fermion is known to be reflection positive. However, its compact axial symmetry is not invariant under the reflection, whereas a reflection-invariant axial symmetry is noncompact, and both are part of an Onsager algebra.

</details>


### [110] [Equivariant Localization with Electric Monodromy in M-theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20117)
*Ji-seong Chae*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将 M 理论（M-theory）中 AdS$_3$ 超对称背景的等变局域化（equivariant localization）推广至具有非零电通量周期（electric flux periods）的解，并给出翘曲体积（warped volume）与中心荷（central charge）等物理量的计算框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的等变局域化方法适用于电通量周期为零的 AdS$_3$ 背景，无法处理带非零电通量周期的解，因此需要将该框架扩展以覆盖更一般的超引力背景。

Method: 引入具有加性单值性（additive monodromy）的局域电势，将其与 R 对称固定点（R-symmetry fixed points）和外边界（outer boundaries）一并计入翘曲体积；利用 Killing 旋量恒等式（Killing-spinor identities）将电周期与磁通量（magnetic flux）关联。

Result: 对一类紧致背景，将翘曲体积与电 Maxwell 荷（electric Maxwell charge）相联系，并导出引力反常（gravitational anomaly）与电通量之间的关系；在 AdS$_3$×S$^3$×S$^3$×T$^2$ 与 AdS$_3$×S$^3$×K3×S$^1$ 上恢复中心荷，并由十一维超引力（eleven-dimensional supergravity）直接导出前者的 R 流能级（R-current levels）。

Conclusion: 该扩展框架统一处理固定点、单值性与外边界三类贡献，并在非紧致半 BPS（half-BPS）AdS$_3$×S$^3$×S$^3$ 周期半圆柱纤维几何中展示三者共存的情形。

Abstract: We extend equivariant localization for supersymmetric AdS$_3$ backgrounds of M-theory to solutions with nonzero electric flux periods. The associated local electric potential acquires additive monodromy, contributing to the warped volume alongside R-symmetry fixed points and outer boundaries. Using Killing-spinor identities, we relate the electric periods to magnetic flux and, for a class of compact backgrounds, the warped volume to the electric Maxwell charge. We also derive a relation between the gravitational anomaly and the electric flux. We apply these results to AdS$_3$ $\times S^3 \times S^3 \times T^2$ and AdS$_3$ $\times S^3 \times K3 \times S^1$, recovering their central charges and deriving the R-current levels of the former directly from eleven-dimensional supergravity. Finally, we study a noncompact half-BPS AdS$_3$ $\times S^3\times S^3$ geometry fibered over a periodic half-cylinder, where fixed-point, monodromy and outer-boundary contributions coexist.

</details>


### [111] [On Thermodynamics of AdS Black Holes in Square-Root Nonlinear Electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20162)
*A. Belhaj,S. E. Ennadifi,M. Jemri,M. Nach*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 受指数型麦克斯韦模型（exponential Maxwell models）启发，本文在平方根非线性电动力学（square root nonlinear electrodynamics）框架下提出一种新的黑洞解，并基于黑洞力学与热力学的类比研究其稳定性、P-v 临界性与相变，发现该黑洞无需额外修正即可呈现范德瓦尔斯（Van der Waals）行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于指数型麦克斯韦模型的启发，旨在非线性电动力学（nonlinear electrodynamics）框架下构造新的黑洞解，并考察其热力学性质与相结构。

Method: 采用黑洞力学与热力学（black hole mechanics and thermodynamics）之间的类比方法，对所求解的平方根非线性电动力学黑洞进行稳定性分析，并计算 P-v 临界性（P-v criticality）与相变（phase transitions）。

Result: 结果表明，所获得的黑洞展现出范德瓦尔斯行为（Van der Waals behaviors），且该行为无需引入通常出现在普适比（universal ratio）计算中的额外修正项。

Conclusion: 平方根非线性电动力学可作为一种无需额外修正即产生范德瓦尔斯型相结构的黑洞解来源，为非线性电动力学黑洞的热力学相变研究提供了新的候选模型。

Abstract: Inspired by exponential Maxwell models, we propose a new black hole solution in square root nonlinear electrodynamics. Using the analogy between black hole mechanics and thermodynamics, we investigate the corresponding stability, the P-v criticality and the phase transitions. As a result, we find that the obtained black hole exhibits Van der Waals behaviors without additional corrections which typically appear in universal ratio computations.

</details>


### [112] [Decoding SO(5) Deconfined Multicriticality: Conformal Bootstrap and Fuzzy-Sphere Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20214)
*Zhijin Li,Tinghong Shen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作结合共形 bootstrap（conformal bootstrap）与模糊球正则化（fuzzy-sphere regularization）、量子蒙特卡洛（QMC）数据，系统刻画了 $SO(5)$ 共形场论（CFT）谱与 OPE 系数，支持去禁闭量子临界点（DQCP）由真实的多临界 $SO(5)$ CFT 描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去禁闭量子临界点（DQCP）的本质仍无定论：存在“真实多临界共形场论（CFT）”与“由邻近复固定点支配的伪临界性（pseudocriticality）”两种竞争解释，需借助独立方法加以判别。

Method: 采用共形 bootstrap 与模糊球正则化，利用最低矢量 $φ$、单态 $s$ 与无迹对称张量 $t$ 的混合关联函数确定 bootstrap 锥；并研究带 level-1 Wess–Zumino–Witten 项的 $SO(5)$ 非线性 sigma 模型，借助特殊共形生成元分类共形原初算符，配合对称性投影扩展模糊球精确对角化至 $N=12$。

Result: bootstrap 锥顶点与大规模 QMC 指示位置高度接近；在 $N=10$ Landau 轨道体系中识别出 52 个共形原初算符（其中 31 个为此前未分类者），bootstrap 谱与 OPE 系数同模糊球数据吻合，含 16 个新识别原初算符；最低 $SO(5)$ 单态标量在 bootstrap 与 QMC 中均为相关算符，模糊球给出 $Δ_s=2.816$（$N=12$）并随体系尺寸减小。

Conclusion: 混合关联函数据 bootstrap 解对应于一个含相关 $SO(5)$ 单态标量的幺正 CFT，并与 DQCP 的 QMC 与模糊球数据密切关联；三种方法共同为二维量子磁体中的 $SO(5)$ 多临界 CFT 提供了综合证据。

Abstract: The nature of the deconfined quantum critical point (DQCP) remains unsettled: competing explanations invoke a real multicritical conformal field theory (CFT) or pseudocriticality governed by nearby complex fixed points. We use the conformal bootstrap and fuzzy-sphere regularization to compute $SO(5)$ CFT data and compare our results with large-scale quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) data to investigate these scenarios. Using mixed correlators of the lowest vector $φ$, singlet $s$, and traceless symmetric scalar $t$, we characterize the bootstrap cone whose tip lies remarkably close to the position indicated by large-scale QMC. We extract an extensive spectrum across $SO(5)$ irreducible representations, together with operator product expansion (OPE) coefficients. We study a fuzzy-sphere realization of the $SO(5)$ nonlinear sigma model with a level-1 Wess--Zumino--Witten term. Using the special conformal generators, we identify 52 conformal primaries in a system with \(N=10\) Landau orbitals, including 31 not previously classified as primaries in the earlier fuzzy-sphere study. Symmetry projection extends fuzzy-sphere exact diagonalization to $N=12$. The bootstrap spectrum and OPE coefficients show close agreement with fuzzy-sphere data, including 16 of the newly identified primaries in channels accessible to the mixed-correlator bootstrap. The lowest $SO(5)$ singlet scalar is relevant in the bootstrap and QMC results; its fuzzy-sphere data is $Δ_s=2.816$ at $N=12$ and decreases with system size. Our results suggest that the mixed-correlator bootstrap solution represents a unitary CFT with a relevant $SO(5)$ singlet scalar and is closely connected to the QMC and fuzzy-sphere data for the DQCP. The conformal bootstrap, fuzzy-sphere, and QMC together provide comprehensive evidence for an $SO(5)$ multicritical CFT and critical data to decode the phases in two-dimensional quantum magnets.

</details>


### [113] [Off-shell one-loop counterterms of Palatini gravity with an independent background connection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20295)
*F. T. Brandt,G. A. Menezes*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在一阶（Palatini）表述下用背景场方法计算纯引力（pure gravity）的单圈发散（one-loop divergences），发现除曲率平方项外还需由背景联络非度性（nonmetricity）构造的不变量，且所有抵消项（counterterms）在壳消失、离壳可由场重定义消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶（Palatini）表述中联络与度规独立，其量子发散结构与度规表述（metric formulation）的差异尚需系统厘清，尤其是背景联络与背景度规不匹配时所需的不变量类型。

Method: 采用Goldberg变量下的背景场方法（background field method），背景联络与背景度规相互独立；在任意维数与任意规范下计算两点函数发散，并验证单圈背景Ward-Takahashi恒等式（Ward-Takahashi identities），再以Feynman-de Donder规范计算三点与四点函数。

Result: 两点函数发散除曲率平方项（curvature-squared terms）外，还需背景联络非度性构造的不变量；Gauss-Bonnet组合（Gauss-Bonnet combination）对非度规联络并非拓扑项，因而不足。三点、四点函数将抵消项拉格朗日量补全至非度性四阶，并提供无自由参数的检验。

Conclusion: 与度规表述一致，所有抵消项在壳消失（vanish on shell），离壳则可通过场重定义（field redefinitions）去除，表明一阶表述的单圈发散在物理上不引入新内容。

Abstract: We compute the one-loop divergences of pure gravity in the first-order (Palatini) formulation, using the background field method in Goldberg variables, with a background connection independent of the background metric. Background Ward-Takahashi identities are verified at one loop. The divergences of the two-point functions, obtained in arbitrary dimension and gauge, require, besides the curvature-squared terms, invariants built from the nonmetricity of the background connection; in particular, the Gauss-Bonnet combination, which is not topological for a nonmetric connection, does not suffice. Three- and four-point functions in the Feynman-de Donder gauge complete the counterterm Lagrangian up to fourth order in the nonmetricity and provide parameter-free tests of it. All the counterterms vanish on shell, as in the metric formulation, and off shell they can be removed by field redefinitions.

</details>


### [114] [Phase Structure and Thermodynamic Geometry of STU Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20328)
*Fil Simovic,Kenan Scavennec,David Kubiznak,Robert B. Mann*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从几何热力学（geometric thermodynamics）视角研究四维 STU 黑洞（STU black holes）的热力学性质，在成对相等电荷的非旋转解下给出热力学量的闭式表达，确定状态方程与相结构，并利用 Ruppeiner 几何的重整化曲率探测微观结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究对规范 N = 8、SO(8) 超引力（supergravity）中 STU 黑洞族的热力学相结构与微观相互作用缺乏系统的几何热力学刻画，本文旨在补足这一环节。

Method: 聚焦携带成对相等电荷（pairwise-equal charges）的非旋转解，将热力学量直接以物理电荷表示并求得闭式表达式（closed-form expressions），据此确定状态方程（equation of state）；同时以温度与热力学体积（thermodynamic volume）为涨落变量构造 Ruppeiner 几何，并将重整化曲率（renormalized curvature）用作微观结构的探针。

Result: 发现丰富的相结构：当电荷比（charge ratio）足够大时存在两个临界点（critical points）；并在参数空间中识别出对应吸引与排斥微观相互作用的区域。

Conclusion: STU 黑洞的 Ruppeiner 几何重整化曲率可作为刻画其微观相互作用的有效工具，电荷比是控制相结构与相互作用性质的关键参数。

Abstract: We study the thermodynamics of four-dimensional STU black holes, a family of charged solutions of gauged N = 8, SO(8) supergravity, from the perspective of geometric thermodynamics. We focus on non-rotating solutions carrying pairwise-equal charges, obtaining closed-form expressions for the thermodynamic quantities directly in terms of the physical charges, and use them to determine the equation of state and associated phase structure. We also construct the Ruppeiner geometry for the STU black hole, taking the temperature and thermodynamic volume as fluctuation variables, and study its renormalized curvature as a probe of the black hole microstructure. We find a rich phase structure with two critical points present whenever the charge ratio is sufficiently large, and identify regions in parameter space associated with attractive and repulsive microscopic interactions.

</details>


### [115] [Triple Exchange at the Cosmological Collider in Closed Form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20356)
*Taushif Ahmed,Sumit Banik,Syed Mehedi Hasan,Andreas Rapakoulias*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文用费曼积分（Feynman-integral）方法首次给出准单场（quasi-single-field）三交换（triple exchange）原初双谱（primordial bispectrum）完整形状的闭式（closed-form）结果，并指出普朗克（Planck）分析所用模板存在振幅与质量估计偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质量接近哈勃尺度（Hubble scale）的粒子会在原初双谱中留下特征图样，使暴胀（inflation）成为宇宙学对撞机（cosmological collider）；而在信号最强的轻场情形，准单场三交换的完整形状尚无闭式表达，需加以解决。

Method: 采用费曼积分（Feynman-integral）方法，在无质量极限与共形耦合（conformally coupled）极限附近，以多重多对数（multiple polylogarithms）展开的形式，得到对动量精确的闭式结果。

Result: 所得结果显示，普朗克分析所用模板（template）将振幅高估至多 1.8 倍并低估质量，而新模板可同时正确还原二者；此外计算了塌缩四谱（collapsed trispectrum），并结合双谱发现弱混合（weak mixing）情形下对自耦合（self-coupling）给出更紧的约束。

Conclusion: 新的闭式模板满足当前及未来宇宙微波背景（CMB）与星系巡天（galaxy surveys）的需求，并可借助双谱与四谱的联合分析更紧密地限制自耦合参数。

Abstract: Particles with masses near the Hubble scale leave a characteristic pattern in the primordial bispectrum, turning inflation into a cosmological collider. For light fields, where this signal is largest, no closed form is known for the full shape of the quasi-single-field triple exchange. Using Feynman-integral methods, we obtain the first closed-form results, exact in the momenta, as expansions in terms of multiple polylogarithms around the massless and conformally coupled limits. We show that the template used in Planck analyses overestimates the amplitude by up to a factor of 1.8 and underestimates the mass, whereas our new template recovers both, as needed for current and upcoming CMB and galaxy surveys. We also compute the collapsed trispectrum and, combining it with the bispectrum, find that at weak mixing it bounds the self-coupling more tightly.

</details>


### [116] [Holographic Homogeneous Black Branes from Anisotropic Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20673)
*Markus A. G. Amano*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文在任意维度下构造了爱因斯坦引力与爱因斯坦—麦克斯韦—伸缩子理论中的均匀各向异性黑膜，并系统研究其热力学与探针标量动力学，建立了不要求渐近局部反德西特（asymptotically locally AdS）行为的均匀各向异性标度几何研究框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑膜解多依赖渐近局部反德西特背景，难以刻画一般均匀各向异性标度几何的热力学与动力学性质，因此需要构造更一般的解并建立分析框架。

Method: 通过要求径向标度（radial scaling）与空间李代数（spatial Lie algebra）相容，将场方程约化为径向方程及对标度指数、曲率与物质场的代数约束；构造中性、电、磁与二象（dyonic）解，包括五维比安基（Bianchi）几何与由Ricci幂零孤子（Ricci nilsoliton）支撑的高维族。

Result: 得到解的欧几里得作用量、质量与熵标度等热力学量；对质量探针标量（probe scalar）导出渐近质量下界，并在特定标度几何类中获得精确的纯阻尼准正规谱（quasinormal spectrum）；数值计算展示了Nil、Solv与Bianchi VI型几何中标量弛豫对质量、动力学指数与动量的依赖。

Conclusion: 所构建的框架可用于研究一般不渐近局部反德西特（not asymptotically locally AdS）的均匀各向异性标度几何的热力学与动力学性质。

Abstract: We construct homogeneous anisotropic black branes in Einstein gravity and Einstein--Maxwell--dilaton theory in arbitrary spacetime dimensions. By imposing compatibility between radial scaling and the spatial Lie algebra, we reduce the field equations to radial equations and algebraic constraints on the scaling exponents, curvature, and matter fields. We construct neutral, electric, magnetic, and dyonic solutions, including explicit five-dimensional Bianchi geometries and higher-dimensional families supported by Ricci nilsolitons. We investigate the thermodynamics of these solutions, obtaining their Euclidean action, mass, and entropy scaling. We also study the dynamics of a massive probe scalar, deriving an asymptotic mass bound and an exact purely damped quasinormal spectrum for a particular class of scaling geometries. Numerical calculations further explore the mass, dynamical exponent, and momentum dependence of scalar relaxation in Nil, Solv, and Bianchi type VI geometries. Our results establish a framework for studying thermodynamic and dynamical properties of homogeneous anisotropic scaling geometries that are generally not asymptotically locally anti-de Sitter.

</details>


### [117] [Doubly-spinning Black Saturns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20701)
*Jay Armas*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了五维真空爱因斯坦引力中六参数、渐近平坦的稳态解，描述球面黑洞与黑环共存且双平面旋转的“双极值土星”构型，并讨论其热力学与相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于给出带两个非连通视界（球面黑洞（spherical black hole）与黑环（black ring））且双平面旋转的精确解，并检验双极值视界是否可与热力学平衡（thermodynamic equilibrium）相容，同时与黑洞褶皱（blackfold）方法的相图预言对比。

Method: 采用五维真空爱因斯坦引力（five-dimensional vacuum Einstein gravity）求解稳态渐近平坦（asymptotically flat）度规，构造六参数解族；通过参数极限检验与已知解（双自旋 Myers-Perry 黑洞、Pomeransky-Sen'kov 黑环、单自旋黑撒旦）的对应关系，并依据视界极值条件与热力学判据分析平衡性质。

Result: 在特定参数区间两个视界可同时变为极值（extremal），从而给出首个具有两个极值非连通视界的正则渐近平坦真空解；结果表明非极值黑撒旦（black Saturn）可处于热力学平衡，而双极值撒旦不能；该解在正则极限下退化为已知解，且与早期 blackfold 相图预言一致。

Conclusion: 该解族统一并拓展了五维旋转黑洞解空间，提供了双极值非连通视界的首个范例，并表明极值性破坏多视界系统的热力学平衡，同时支持 blackfold 相图的有效性。

Abstract: We construct a six-parameter family of stationary, asymptotically flat solutions of five-dimensional vacuum Einstein gravity describing a spherical black hole surrounded by a black ring, with rotation in both independent planes. In a specific regime both horizons can become extremal, thereby providing the first example of a regular asymptotically flat vacuum Einstein solution with two extremal disconnected horizons. We also show that while non-extremal black Saturns can be in thermodynamic equilibrium, the doubly extremal Saturn cannot. The solution admits the doubly spinning Myers-Perry, the Pomeransky-Sen'kov black ring, and the singly spinning black Saturn as regular limits. We also show agreement with earlier blackfold predictions for the phase diagram of these solutions.

</details>


### [118] [A note on Padmanabhan's path-integral duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20731)
*P. Fernandez de Cordoba,J. M. Isidro*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文把 Padmanabhan 关于粒子作用量与本征长度对偶（duality $\mathcal S \rightarrow L_0^2/\mathcal S$）的不变性原理推广到场论框架，由传播子与 Schwinger 固有时表示导出含修正 Bessel 函数的非局域标量场算子，并分析其红外与紫外行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Padmanabhan 的对偶不变性原理原本针对点粒子（point particle）表述，缺乏对应的场论实现；本文旨在将其自然推广至量子场论（quantum field theory）。

Method: 以粒子传播子（propagator）及其 Schwinger 固有时表示（proper-time representation）为出发点，将对偶变换对应的波算子表示为标量场（scalar field）而非点粒子的形式，从而得到由修正 Bessel 函数（modified Bessel functions）构成的非局域算子（nonlocal operator）。

Result: 分析了红外（infrared）与紫外（ultraviolet）两个能区：低能下可回到标准 Klein-Gordon 理论，高能端则出现指数型紫外压低（exponential ultraviolet suppression）。

Conclusion: 所构造的理论为 Padmanabhan 点粒子对偶原理提供了一个向量子场论的扩展，但摘要未给出定量实验约束或更进一步的物理推论。

Abstract: We develop a field-theoretic implementation of Padmanabhan's principle of invariance under the duality $\mathcal S \rightarrow {L_0^2}/{\mathcal S}$, where ${\mathcal S}$ is the proper length (the action integral along a particle's trajectory) and $L_0$ is a quantum of length. Starting from the particle propagator and its Schwinger proper-time representation, we derive the wave operator associated with the duality transformation in terms of a scalar field, rather than a point particle. The resulting theory is governed by a nonlocal operator involving modified Bessel functions. We analyse the infrared and ultraviolet regimes, demonstrate the recovery of standard Klein-Gordon theory at low energies, and show the appearance of exponential ultraviolet suppression. Our analysis provides a natural extension, to quantum field theory, of Padmanabhan's duality for point particles.

</details>


### [119] [Quantum chaos from time-dependent CFT states at large central charge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20817)
*Shaun D. Hampton,Pantelis Panopoulos,Gideon Vos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文首次研究大中心荷（large central charge）共形场论（conformal field theory）中，四个探针在重的、时间依赖背景态上的行为，借助单值性方法（monodromy method）证明外时序关联函数（OTOC）表现出最大混沌（maximal chaos），其李雅普诺夫指数（Lyapunov exponent）饱和 MSS 界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对 OTOC 与混沌的研究多基于热态或简单背景，尚缺乏对重的、时间依赖背景态上多探针行为的首次系统考察，因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用单值性方法（monodromy method），并计入对恒等块（identity block）的次领头修正，在轻算子与中重算子部门间引入小相互作用，计算重背景上两个轻算子和两个中重算子的关联函数。

Result: 通过对不连续贡献求和并由欧几里得号（Euclidean signature）解析延拓至洛伦兹号（Lorentzian signature），结果显示该 OTOC 呈现最大混沌，即李雅普诺夫指数饱和 MSS 界。

Conclusion: 在大中心荷共形场论的重且时间依赖背景态上，含次领头修正的四点关联函数仍导致最大混沌行为，支持 MSS 界饱和的普适性。

Abstract: We provide a first study of the behavior of four probes on heavy, time-dependent background states of conformal field theories at large central charge. Employing the monodromy method and including subleading corrections to the identity block, we compute the correlation function of two light and two middle-heavy operators on such a heavy background, by switching on a small interaction between the light and middle-heavy sectors. Summing the discontinuity contributions and analytically continuing from Euclidean to Lorentzian signature, we show that the out-of-time-ordered correlator (OTOC) exhibits maximal chaos, that is, the Lyapunov exponent saturates the MSS bound.

</details>


### [120] [From Quasinormal Mode Poles to Branch Cuts at Finite $N$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20912)
*Navid Abbasi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在全息（holography）框架下，首个 $1/N^2$ 体圈修正将大 $N$ 极限下的离散准正规模（QNM）极点结构转化为连续分支割，并在晚期产生一个衰减更慢的、由体量子涨落主导的新时间区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 严格大 $N$ 极限下，全息热延迟关联函数呈亚纯结构，晚期行为完全由孤立 QNM 极点决定；作者希望检验首个体圈（bulk-loop）修正是否、以及如何改变这一经典图像。

Method: 在平面 AdS$_5$ 黑洞膜上的标量场论中计算 $1/N^2$ 阶的单圈体图：该图在固定圈动量 $q$ 处给出位于两个 QNM 频率之和的极点，对连续动量的积分把这些“QNM 对”极点（QNM-pair poles）转化为分支割（branch cuts）；随后分析外部空间动量趋于零时的光锥贡献与残余频率标度。

Result: 对实部相反的 QNM 支构成的配对，大动量下领头光锥贡献相消，残余 QNM 对频率按 $q^{-1/3}$ 标度，导致无穷族割在零频率处累积，而所有树级 QNM 极点仍停留在有限非零频率。QNM 对阻尼减小与热玻尔兹曼抑制（Boltzmann suppression）的竞争产生移动鞍点，给出 $G(t)\sim N^{-2}t^{\alpha}e^{-Ct^{3/4}}$、$C>0$，其衰减慢于领头经典 QNM 贡献。

Conclusion: 大 $N$ 展开在晚期是非一致的（nonuniform）；体量子涨落生成了一个超越经典 QNM 衰减的独立晚期区域，首个有限 $N$ 修正填补了经典 QNM 谱所设定的时间间隙。

Abstract: In the strict large-$N$ limit, holographic thermal retarded correlators are typigcally meromorphic, with their late-time behavior determined by isolated quasinormal mode (QNM) poles. We show that the first bulk-loop correction, of order $1/N^2$, qualitatively changes this structure. In a scalar theory on the planar AdS$_5$ black brane, a one-loop bulk diagram generates poles at sums of two QNM frequencies at fixed loop momentum $q$; integration over the continuous momentum turns these QNM-pair poles into branch cuts. At vanishing external spatial momentum, for pairs built from QNM branches with opposite real parts, the leading light-cone contributions cancel at large momentum, and the residual QNM-pair frequency scales as $q^{-1/3}$. Consequently, an infinite family of cuts accumulates at zero frequency, while all tree-level QNM poles remain at finite nonzero frequencies. The first finite-$N$ correction therefore closes the gap set by the classical QNM spectrum. Competition between the decreasing QNM-pair damping and thermal Boltzmann suppression produces a moving saddle and a finite-$N$ contribution $G(t)\sim N^{-2}t^αe^{-Ct^{3/4}},\, C>0,$ which decays more slowly than the leading classical QNM contribution. Thus the large-$N$ expansion is nonuniform at late times, and bulk quantum fluctuations generate a distinct late-time regime beyond the classical QNM decay.

</details>


### [121] [Higher-point amplitudes and recursion relations from the worldsheet in complex Liouville string](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21091)
*Aleksandr Artemev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在世界面方法下研究复刘维尔弦理论（complex Liouville string theory, CLS）亏格零（genus zero）的多点微扰弦振幅，通过显式世界面计算与解析自举（analytic bootstrap）求出含基环算符（ground ring operators）的关联函数，并导出与矩阵模型（matrix model）方法一致的一般递推公式（recursion formula）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献在应用刘维尔理论高阶运动方程（higher equations of motion）计算含一个退化场（degenerate field）的快点子（tachyon）关联函数时存在遗漏贡献，需澄清并给出系统计算方法，同时检验其与矩阵模型结果的相容性。

Method: 在复刘维尔弦理论的世界面（worldsheet）框架下，结合显式世界面计算与解析自举，先计算含基环算符的关联函数（其额外依赖于模空间上积分回路（integration cycle）的选择），再借助刘维尔理论中的高阶运动方程计算含一个退化场的多点快子关联函数。

Result: 指出以往应用该方法时遗漏的贡献，由此导出递推公式，该公式与 CLS 的矩阵模型方法所给出的一般振幅递推关系一致；此外提出用曲线模空间（moduli space of curves）上的 tautological 类表示含基环算符插入的关联函数的表达式。

Conclusion: 世界面计算与矩阵模型结果相互印证，并讨论了将上述结果推广至有理最小弦（rational minimal strings）的情形。

Abstract: We consider multipoint perturbative string amplitudes in genus zero in complex Liouville string theory (CLS) in the worldsheet approach. Based on explicit worldsheet calculations and analytic bootstrap, we first compute correlators involving ground ring operators, which additionally depend on a choice of integration cycle on the moduli space. Using this result and higher equations of motion in Liouville theory, we calculate higher-point tachyon correlators with one degenerate field and explain what contributions were missed in previous attempts to apply this method in the literature. This allows us to derive a recursion formula, which agrees with the general recursion for amplitudes known from "matrix model" approach to CLS. We also propose an expression for correlators with ground ring insertions in terms of tautological classes on the moduli space of curves and comment on generalization of these results to the case of rational minimal strings.

</details>


### [122] [Thermal deep inelastic scattering, universality and complex geodesics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21127)
*Andrei Parnachev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用全息（holographic）方法研究有限温度强耦合量子场论中深度非弹性散射（DIS）相关流两点函数（two-point functions）在大类空虚度下的行为，揭示真空主导到热主导的转变具有高度普适性，并与共形对撞机界（conformal collider bounds）相联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度非弹性散射中，大虚度光子探针下有限温度强耦合场论的流关联函数行为及其普适性尚需系统刻画，尤其真空效应与热效应的相对权重如何受理论参数控制值得澄清。

Method: 采用全息描述（holographic description）计算有限温度强相互作用量子场论中流的两点函数，并将其对应到热 CFT 关联函数（thermal CFT correlators）的特定近光锥极限（near-lightcone limit）。

Result: 响应函数（response functions）表现出高度普适性：对三点耦合（three-point couplings）的依赖仅通过一个通道相关（channel-dependent）系数进入，该系数乘在控制真空与热效应相对大小的运动学参数上。该系数同时控制共形对撞机界，任一边界饱和时响应函数在近光锥极限下取真空形式；低温区吸收指数（absorption exponent）与近光锥几何中传播的复测地线（complex geodesic）相关。

Conclusion: 有限温度流两点函数从指数压低吸收到幂律行为的转变可由近光锥极限统一描述，其普适结构与共形对撞机界和近光锥几何性质密切相关。

Abstract: In deep inelastic scattering (DIS), a lepton scatters off a target hadron whose structure is probed by an exchanged photon with large spacelike virtuality. We consider two-point functions of currents in strongly interacting quantum field theories at finite temperature in this kinematic regime and use a holographic description to compute them. At large virtuality, the crossover from vacuum-dominated to thermally dominated physics corresponds to a transition from exponentially suppressed absorption to power-law behavior of the response functions. This regime is related to a particular near-lightcone limit of thermal CFT correlators. We show that the response functions exhibit a large degree of universality: the dependence on the three-point couplings of currents to the stress tensor enters through a channel-dependent coefficient multiplying the kinematic parameter that controls the relative size of vacuum and thermal effects. The same coefficient controls conformal collider bounds; when either bound is saturated, the corresponding response function takes its vacuum form in the near-lightcone limit. Furthermore, the absorption exponent in the low-temperature regime is related to a complex geodesic propagating in the near-lightcone geometry.

</details>


### [123] [Soft momentum expansion about the Stokes topological limit of Bogoliubov coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21271)
*Daniel J. H. Chung,Nidhi Sudhir,Junqi Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在含时宇宙学背景中，利用Bogoliubov变换的Stokes现象，解析计算了粒子产生振幅在Stokes拓扑极限附近的软动量展开，并给出粒子数密度的半解析估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含时宇宙学背景中的粒子产生可通过解析延拓时间坐标下Bogoliubov变换的Stokes现象（Stokes phenomena）计算，但近期发现的Stokes拓扑极限附近的软动量（soft momentum）行为尚缺乏解析刻画。

Method: 采用指数改进的渐近展开（exponentially improved asymptotic expansion），其中涉及由Stokes拓扑扇区内在的角阶跃函数（angular step function）部分定义的函数空间自同构（automorphisms）；并结合与色散关系ω_k^2(z)=C(k)+A z^n（n为偶数）的时间依赖性相关的特殊离散对称性。

Result: 显式计算出振幅领头动量依赖性的解析形式，该结果对参数A是非微扰的（nonperturbative in A），并据此对该类情形下的粒子数密度（number density）给出半解析估计。

Conclusion: Stokes拓扑极限结合指数改进渐近展开与离散对称性，可为软动量区粒子产生振幅及数密度提供解析与半解析描述。

Abstract: It is known that particle production in time-dependent cosmological backgrounds can be computed using the Stokes phenomena occurring in Bogoliubov transformations with an analytically continued time coordinate. We analytically compute the soft momentum expansion of the particle production amplitude about the Stokes topological limit that was recently discovered in this context. The computational technique relies on an exponentially improved asymptotic expansion that involves automorphisms in the space of functions defined partly by the angular step function intrinsic to the Stokes topological sectors. We combine this with special discrete symmetries tied to the time dependence of the dispersion relation $ω_k^2(z) = C(k) + A z^n$ for even $n$ to explicitly compute the leading momentum dependence of the amplitude which is nonperturbative in $A$. We also use this result to give a semi-analytic estimate of the number density of particles in this class of scenarios.

</details>


### [124] [Lifting asymptotic symmetries from metric to tetrad formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21296)
*Antoine Vincenti*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 揭示从度规解空间提升到标架（tetrad）解空间时，残余对称代数会出现与场相关的上循环（cocycle）障碍，并证明借助参考标架（reference tetrad）可构造无上循环的提升，使标架残余对称代数成为度规代数与场相关残余洛伦兹变换理想的半直和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度规（metric）解空间由规范与边界条件定义，并携带残余微分同胚对称代数；改用标架变量（tetrad variables）会引入额外局域洛伦兹自由度（local Lorentz freedom）。当部分自由度被附加规范与边界条件固定后，度规残余对称需由补偿洛伦兹变换补足，而残余洛伦兹自由度导致补偿变换存在歧义，最直接的选取通常无法把度规残余代数实现为标架代数的子代数。论文旨在系统解决这一提升（lift）障碍。

Method: 将标架解空间视为度规解空间上的主丛（principal bundle），引入其截面即参考标架（reference tetrad），据此构造残余对称的提升；并分析最直接提升所产生的场相关上循环（field-dependent cocycle），给出消除该上循环的显式方案。

Result: 证明参考标架总是给出无上循环的提升，从而使标架残余对称代数等于度规残余对称代数与场相关残余洛伦兹变换理想（ideal）的半直和（semi-direct sum）。该构造不依赖于规范条件、残余群、一阶拉格朗日量（first-order Lagrangian），且在度规等距（isometry）生成元上退化为 Kosmann 提升（Kosmann lift）。

Conclusion: 在具有时间依赖边界度规、残余 U(1) 洛伦兹自由度的 Weyl BMS 型解空间（Weyl BMS-type solution space）上给出实例：计算朴素提升（naive lift）的上循环并将其显式消除，验证了构造的普适性与可行性。

Abstract: Metric solution spaces defined by gauge and boundary conditions are associated with a residual symmetry algebra, consisting of the diffeomorphism generators whose action preserves these conditions. Describing a metric solution space in terms of tetrad variables adds a local Lorentz freedom. When part of this freedom is fixed by additional tetrad gauge and boundary conditions, metric residual symmetries must be supplemented by compensating Lorentz transformations. If a residual Lorentz freedom remains, there is an ambiguity in these compensating transformations. The most direct choices generally fail to realize the metric residual symmetry algebra as a subalgebra of the tetrad one, because of a field-dependent cocycle. We show that a reference tetrad, that is a section of the tetrad solution space seen as a principal bundle over the metric one, always provides a lift free of this cocycle, so that the tetrad residual symmetry algebra is the semi-direct sum of the metric one and the ideal of field-dependent residual Lorentz transformations. The construction is independent of the gauge conditions, of the residual group, of the first-order Lagrangian, and reduces to the Kosmann lift on metric isometry generators. We illustrate it on a Weyl BMS-type solution space with time-dependent boundary metric, described in tetrad variables with a residual U(1) Lorentz freedom, for which the cocycle of a naive lift is computed and then explicitly removed.

</details>


### [125] [Carroll Double Copy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21388)
*Sucheta Majumdar,Silvia Nagy,Giorgio Pizzolo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在线性化层面构造了卡罗尔引力（Carroll gravity）作为卡罗尔杨-米尔斯理论（Carroll Yang-Mills theory）之“平方”的双重拷贝（double copy），通过在洛伦兹时空中取卡罗尔极限 c→0 建立兼容对称性与场方程的字典。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有卷积双重拷贝（convolutional double copy）处方在洛伦兹时空中成立，但向非洛伦兹的卡罗尔极限延拓尚不明确，需建立能同时满足对称性与运动方程的卡罗尔双重拷贝字典。

Method: 从洛伦兹时空的卷积双重拷贝出发，取卡罗尔极限 c→0；因极限产生电型与磁型（electric/magnetic）两个不同扇区，系统考察母理论所有组合；对磁型卡罗尔场的时间无关在壳构型引入正则化（regularisation）处理，并通过场的适当修饰（dressing）引入类戈德斯通对象（Goldstone-type object）以保证卡罗尔助推下的协变性；先用标量场玩具模型建立形式，再应用于线性化卡罗尔杨-米尔斯与卡罗尔引力之间。

Result: 建立了兼容对称性与运动方程的卡罗尔双重拷贝字典，并系统给出电型与磁型卡罗尔理论所有组合下的双重拷贝构造；通过正则化处理时间无关在壳构型、通过镀饰加戈德斯通型对象恢复卡罗尔助推下的协变性；形式在标量场玩具模型上验证后成功应用于卡罗尔杨-米尔斯与卡罗尔引力。

Conclusion: 卡罗尔引力可在线性化层面被表述为卡罗尔杨-米尔斯理论的双重拷贝平方，表明双重拷贝结构可延拓至非洛伦兹的卡罗尔几何，但需借助正则化与戈德斯通型修饰来维持对称性。

Abstract: We construct a double copy for Carroll gravity as the ``square'' of Carroll Yang-Mills theory at the linearised level. Starting from the convolutional double copy prescription in Lorentzian spacetime, we take the Carrollian limit $c \rightarrow 0$ to establish a double copy dictionary compatible both with symmetries and the corresponding equations of motion. Since this limit yields two distinct sectors, namely the electric and magnetic Carroll theories, we systematically investigate double copy constructions involving all possible combinations of these parent theories. To accommodate the time-independent on-shell configurations of the magnetic Carroll fields, we introduce a suitable regularisation procedure for the convolutional double copy. In addition, the covariance of the double copy under Carroll boosts requires an appropriate dressing of the fields, leading to the inclusion of a Goldstone-type object in the construction. We first develop the formalism using toy models involving scalar fields and then apply it to the double copy between linearised Carroll Yang-Mills theory and Carroll gravity.

</details>


### [126] [Instant Folded Strings: Fractal Decay and ANEC Saturation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21424)
*Nissan Itzhaki,Uri Peleg*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文指出，瞬时折叠弦（Instant Folded Strings, IFSs）虽为弦论中经典产生的闭合弦，但其能动张量违反平均零能量条件（Averaged Null Energy Condition, ANEC）。作者证明有限弦耦合（finite string coupling）会触发衰变级联（decay cascade），在闵可夫斯基时空（Minkowski spacetime）中饱和ANEC，从而化解这一矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IFSs一方面被视为弦论内禀组成部分，并在黑洞内部与宇宙学中扮演重要角色；另一方面其能动张量违反ANEC，与标准因果性（causality）与幺正性（unitarity）论证相冲突。论文旨在解决这一张力。

Method: 通过引入有限弦耦合，分析IFS的衰变级联过程，并以半经典（semiclassical）近似刻画级联的动力学，识别出控制分支增长、压强衰减、截断-ANEC收敛及对弦耦合非整数异常依赖的单一马尔萨斯指数（Malthusian exponent）κ_IFS = 2√2 − 2。

Result: 原始IFS的残余（remnant）无限期存在，持续催生自相似碎裂级联（self-similar fragmentation cascade），在其全部历史中产生无穷多条弦；级联在半经典区由κ_IFS统一控制，最终态总能量与总动量为零，但保持高度激发，且在任意有限时间切片上携带负的积分压强。

Conclusion: 该工作给出真正弦论性低能动力学（stringy low-energy dynamics）的具体范例，其中微观层面的零能量一致性（null-energy consistency）与宏观负压强（negative pressure）得以共存。

Abstract: Instant Folded Strings (IFSs) present a puzzle. On the one hand, they appear to be an intrinsic part of string theory: they are fundamental closed strings nucleated classically in backgrounds in which the string coupling grows toward the future, and they have been argued to play an important role in black-hole interiors and cosmology. On the other hand, their energy-momentum tensor violates the Averaged Null Energy Condition (ANEC), placing IFSs in tension with standard causality and unitarity arguments. We show that finite string coupling resolves this tension by triggering a decay cascade that saturates the ANEC in Minkowski spacetime. The remnant of the original IFS persists indefinitely, continuously seeding a self-similar fragmentation cascade that produces infinitely many strings over its full history. In the semiclassical regime, the cascade is governed by a single Malthusian exponent, $κ_{\mathrm{IFS}}=2\sqrt{2}-2$, which controls the branching growth, pressure decay, cutoff-ANEC convergence, and anomalous non-integer dependence on the string coupling. The complete state has vanishing total energy and momentum while remaining highly excited and carrying negative integrated pressure on every finite time slice. This provides a concrete example of genuinely stringy low-energy dynamics in which microscopic null-energy consistency coexists with macroscopic negative pressure.

</details>


### [127] [Learning from Duality in Neural Network Field Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21503)
*Pietro Capuozzo,Brandon Robinson,Benjamin Suzzoni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文构建了正能级 Wess-Zumino-Witten 模型的神经实现，串联共形场论的精确数学结构与神经场理论的可表达性与计算性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将共形场论（conformal field theory, CFT）的精确数学结构与神经场理论（neural field theories）的表达能力及计算特性相连接。

Method: 基于玻色型 Wakimoto 场与费米型 Fourier-Berezin 场，构建正能级 Wess-Zumino-Witten 模型的神经实现；并利用能级-秩对偶（level-rank duality）连接费米构造的带电扇区。

Result: 显式神经可观测量（neural observables）可恢复共形块（conformal block）数据，并在成对理论间传递未知关联函数（correlators）的信息；同一连续流代数（current algebra）的不同神经呈现表现出不同的有限宽度（finite-width）行为，其近似精度依赖架构、正则化器（regulator）与参数分布。

Conclusion: 研究结果将共形场论的精确数学结构与神经场理论的表达及计算性质联系起来，表明神经方法可作为刻画 CFT 结构与有限宽度行为的工具。

Abstract: We develop neural realizations of positive-level Wess-Zumino-Witten models using bosonic Wakimoto and fermionic Fourier-Berezin fields. Level-rank duality connects the charged sectors of the fermionic construction, where explicit neural observables recover conformal block data and enable the transfer of information about unknown correlators between paired theories. We also show that distinct neural presentations of the same continuum current algebra exhibit different finite-width behavior, with approximation accuracy depending on architecture, regulator, and parameter distribution. These results connect the exact mathematical structure of conformal field theory with the expressive and computational properties of neural field theories.

</details>


### [128] [The Shape of the Strongly Coupled MQM Ground State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21606)
*Alexander Frenkel*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究大 N 下玻色矩阵量子力学（bosonic matrix quantum mechanics）基态的一类高效变分拟设（variational ansätze），其概率分布为具单迹作用量（single-trace action）的矩阵积分，可在少量参数下达到高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是寻找能以少参数高效刻画大 N 矩阵量子力学基态及其可观测量的变分方法，并与已有 bootstrap 区间及微扰/数值结果进行对照。

Method: 采用范数平方概率分布本身为单迹作用量矩阵积分的变分拟设，使其具有独立的 't Hooft 展开（'t Hooft expansion），并对少量参数进行优化以计算能量、单迹矩（single-trace moments）与连通关联函数（connected correlators）。

Result: 优化少量参数即可获得能量、单迹矩和连通关联函数的多位有效数字精度；无限 N 平面数据（planar data）快速收敛至已发表 bootstrap 区间内并在其外稳定于几位精度；通过连通两点函数可有效捕捉 1/N 修正，并与重求和量子微扰论（resummed quantum perturbation theory）和蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulations）相符。

Conclusion: 该变分拟设族为大 N 玻色矩阵量子力学提供了一种高效、高精度的计算途径，且能系统纳入 1/N 修正，结果与既有 bootstrap 与数值基准一致。

Abstract: We study a surprisingly efficient family of variational ansatze for the ground state of bosonic matrix quantum mechanics at large $N$. These are ansatze whose norm-squared probability distributions are themselves matrix integrals with single-trace actions, and so have their own independent `t Hooft expansions. They are efficient in the sense that optimizing over a small number of parameters produces many digits of accuracy for the energy, single-trace moments, and connected correlators. For infinite-$N$ planar data our results rapidly converge to within published bootstrap intervals and stabilize to a few digits of precision beyond them. We also demonstrate that we can efficiently capture $1/N$ corrections by computing connected two-point functions. These are matched against resummed quantum perturbation theory and Monte Carlo simulations.

</details>


<div id='physics.app-ph'></div>

# physics.app-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Tunable Multi-Frequency-Band Generator and Detector of AC Signals Based on a Spin Hall Device with Two Antiferromagnetic Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19004)
*Oleh Shtanko,Volodymyr Prokopenko,Oleksandr Prokopenko*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出由两个倾斜反铁磁层构成的 Pt/AFM/Pt/AFM/Pt 自旋霍尔多层结，利用耦合 LLGS 方程与两界面自旋霍尔磁阻模型，预测其可作为直流电流驱动的多频振荡器与混频/整流器件，并给出纳瓦至皮瓦量级的输出估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一反铁磁（antiferromagnetic, AFM）层的动力学限制了自旋霍尔振荡器与二极管可达到的频率与读出效率，需要新的器件结构突破该限制。

Method: 构建含两个倾斜 AFM 层的 Pt/AFM/Pt/AFM/Pt 自旋霍尔多层结，外侧 Pt 层通以独立直流驱动电流；模型基于四个 AFM 亚晶格（sublattice）的耦合 Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert-Slonczewski 方程，并结合相位依赖的两界面自旋霍尔磁阻（spin Hall magnetoresistance）模型进行理论与数值研究。

Result: 器件表现为直流电流驱动的振荡型自旋霍尔磁阻；施加额外直流（交流）信号电流时输出交流（直流）电压。模型预测上下 AFM 层净磁化在 Neel 矢量频率上出现电流驱动的锁定共旋与反旋态，导致和频、差频处的多频响应；反旋态额外激活相位敏感混频并产生四次谐波，共旋态提供约 100 GHz 量级的差频通道但互锁定使其无法连续调至零。估计匹配外负载时二次谐波输出交流功率 1.2 nW、四次谐波 0.8 pW，6 mA 输入信号电流下可产生约 1 mV 直流电压。

Conclusion: 双倾斜 AFM 层结构可实现多频、可混频的自旋霍尔磁阻响应，兼具太赫兹信号产生与检测潜力，并具备纳瓦级交流与毫伏级直流输出能力。

Abstract: Antiferromagnetic (AFM) spin Hall oscillators offer an electrical route to compact terahertz sources, while AFM spin Hall diodes can be used as a promising detectors of terahertz signals, however, their attainable frequency and the redout efficiency are constrained by the dynamics of a single AFM layer. We theoretically and numerically investigate a spin Hall multilayer junction Pt/AFM/Pt/AFM/Pt comprising two canted AFM layers, driven by independent drive DC currents flowing in the outer platinum (Pt) layers. Using a model based on the coupled Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert-Slonczewski equations for four AFM sublattices and a phase-dependent two-interface spin Hall magnetoresistance model, we show that the device manifests itself as a DC current-driven oscillating spin Hall magnetoresistance. When an additional DC (AC) signal current is applied to the junction, the device generates an output AC (DC) voltage. Our model predicts current-driven, locked, co- and counter-rotational states of the net magnetization at Neel-vector frequencies in the top and bottom AFM layers, respectively. These states result in a multi-frequency response of the spin Hall magnetoresistance at the sum and difference frequencies. The counter-rotation regime additionally activates the phase-sensitive mixing contribution, producing a fourth harmonic. In contrast, the co-rotation regime provides a difference-frequency channel on the order of 100 GHz, although mutual locking prevents this output from being tuned continuously toward zero. Our estimations show that the device matched to an external load can generate output AC power of 1.2 nW at the second harmonic and 0.8 pW at the fourth harmonic, and can produce a DC voltage of about 1 mV for a 6 mA input signal current.

</details>

### [130] [Magnon binding in a quantum Hall ferromagnet with a periodic electric field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18779)
*Manas Kaushik Das*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在填充因子为 1 的量子霍尔铁磁体（quantum Hall ferromagnet）中，中性自旋波（spin wave）携带垂直于动量的电偶极矩，周期性静电势可为其开辟平行通道；通道变窄会增强横向波函数交叠与有效吸引，从而改变磁振子（magnon）间的交换束缚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋波虽为中性，却因携带电偶极矩可被静电势约束，这为调控磁振子间相互作用提供了一种手段；但约束（几何受限）如何改变同向传播磁振子的交换束缚此前缺乏定量刻画。

Method: 基于已确立的偶极-静电势耦合机制，计算周期性静电势形成的通道中自旋波的横向波函数交叠，并从理论上导出孤立谐振通道内多磁振子团簇的束缚能渐近表达式；同时分析孤立通道与耦合阵列中的双磁振子谱线，以及运动势对波包的输运效应。

Result: 通道越窄，横向波函数交叠越大，有效吸引越强；孤立谐振通道中从 N 个磁振子团簇移除一个所需能量的主导项为 π(k_*ℓ)^4 N(N−1)/4 乘以横向能级间距（k_* 为沿通道平均波数，ℓ 为磁长度）。宽阵列中的隧穿可使束缚能指数级减小；窄于横向配对尺寸的窄条在足够弱吸引下恢复一维束缚；运动势可在初始群速度与势速度匹配时输运单个波包且展宽小于自由传播。

Conclusion: 空间约束是调控量子霍尔铁磁体中磁振子交换束缚的关键参数：通道宽度决定吸引强度与束缚能标度，阵列隧穿与条带宽度可令体系在指数微弱束缚与一维束缚之间切换，并可通过运动势实现低展宽的波包输运。

Abstract: A spin wave in a filling-one quantum Hall ferromagnet is neutral but carries an electric dipole perpendicular to its momentum. A spatially periodic electrostatic potential therefore creates parallel channels for spin waves. We use this established mechanism to calculate how confinement changes the exchange binding of magnons travelling in the same direction. Narrower channels increase the overlap of their transverse wavefunctions and strengthen the effective attraction. In an isolated harmonic channel, the energy required to remove one magnon from a cluster of $N$ is $π(k_*\ell)^4N(N-1)/4$ times the transverse level spacing to leading order, where $k_*$ is the mean wave number along the channel and $\ell$ is the magnetic length. We predict the frequency and momentum dependence of the spectral line for adding a second magnon to one already present, both in an isolated channel and in a coupled array. Tunnelling in a wide array can make the binding energy exponentially small, while a strip narrower than the transverse pair size recovers one-dimensional binding at sufficiently weak attraction. We also find that a moving potential can carry a single wave packet with less spreading than free propagation when its initial group velocity matches the potential's speed.

</details>


### [131] [Nonlinear spin generation and spin photocurrent as a symmetry and magnetic domain probe in unconventional magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19012)
*Ying Xiong,Er-Ping Li,L. K. Ang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出将非线性自旋极化与自旋光电流结合，作为探测非常规磁体（unconventional magnets）中自旋空间群（spin space group）对称性、磁畴与量子几何的诊断工具，并发展二阶自旋光响应张量（second-order spin photoresponse tensors）的机理分辨联合对称性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非常规磁体在无自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）条件下即可产生动量依赖的自旋劈裂（spin splitting），但其平衡态自旋织构（spin texture）所携带的信息有限，需要能超越平衡态描述、揭示对称性与量子几何的诊断手段。

Method: 建立二阶自旋光响应张量的机理分辨联合对称性分析，考察其频率依赖、磁宇称（magnetic parity）与弛豫标度（relaxation scaling），并对原型杂化宇称与奇宇称磁体模型进行数值演示。

Result: 非线性自旋极化与自旋光电流的组合可提供超出平衡自旋织构的信息；非常规磁体被证明是实现非偏振光诱导大磁偶（magnetic-even）自旋光电流的天然平台，可作为无需偏振器的自旋源（polariser-free spin sources）候选。

Conclusion: 该研究给出了将自旋空间对称性与自旋积累、自旋输运的不同微观路径联系起来的光学指纹（optical fingerprints），为非常规磁体的对称性与磁畴探测提供了新途径。

Abstract: Unconventional magnets host momentum-dependent spin splitting without spin-orbit coupling. We demonstrate that the combination of nonlinear spin polarisation and spin photocurrent is a powerful diagnostic tool for probing the spin space group symmetry, magnetic domain, and quantum geometry in unconventional magnets, providing information beyond their equilibrium spin texture.
