We develop a mechanism-resolved joint symmetry analysis of second-order spin photoresponse tensors and analyse their frequency dependence, magnetic parity and relaxation scaling. We show that unconventional magnets are natural platforms to achieve large magnetic-even spin photocurrent induced by unpolarised light, making them promising candidates for polariser-free spin sources. We present numerical illustrations for prototypical hybrid parity and odd parity magnet models. Our work provides optical fingerprints connecting spin-space symmetry to distinct microscopic pathways of spin accumulation and transport.

</details>


### [132] [Chiral-phonon-mediated spin current injection into an Ising superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19180)
*Jiayan Zhang,Gaoyang Li,Yanxia Xing*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文理论上提出通过手性声子（chiral phonon）向Ising超导体注入自旋流，利用非平衡格林函数（nonequilibrium Green's function）推导注入自旋流表达式，并揭示超导态下的双拐点（kink）行为及其温度—磁场相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探索手性绝缘体/Ising超导体异质结（heterostructure）中，由温度梯度驱动的非平衡声子通过界面声子—自旋转换（phonon-spin conversion）向电子自旋传递角动量、实现自旋流注入的机制。

Method: 采用非平衡格林函数形式（nonequilibrium Green's function formalism），推导注入自旋流的表达式，并计算不同面内磁场强度下注入自旋流密度的温度依赖关系。

Result: 注入自旋流取决于两支相反圆偏振（circular polarization）声子分支贡献之差；超导态下自旋流密度随温度或面内磁场增强而增大，除超导转变处的拐点外，转变温度以下出现第二个拐点，其位置与声子诱导的双准粒子激发（two-quasiparticle excitation）起始一致。

Conclusion: 进一步研究了拐点温度随磁场的变化，并给出温度—磁场相图（phase diagram），表明该拐点行为可作为声子诱导双准粒子激发的特征信号。

Abstract: We theoretically propose chiral-phonon-mediated spin current injection into an Ising superconductor in a chiral-insulator/Ising-superconductor heterostructure, where nonequilibrium phonons driven by a temperature gradient transfer their angular momentum to electron spins via interfacial phonon-spin conversion. Using the nonequilibrium Green's function formalism, we derive an expression for the injected spin current and show that it depends on the difference between the contributions from the two phonon branches with opposite circular polarizations. We further investigate the temperature dependence of the injected spin current density at different in-plane magnetic field strengths. In the superconducting state, the spin current density increases with temperature or in-plane magnetic field strength. In addition to the kink at the superconducting transition, a second kink appears below the transition temperature. The position of this kink is consistent with the onset of phonon-induced two-quasiparticle excitations as the superconducting gap decreases with increasing temperature. We further investigate the magnetic-field dependence of the kink temperature in a temperature--magnetic-field phase diagram.

</details>


### [133] [Thermal-energy relaxation in chiral quantum Hall edge mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19259)
*Edvin G. Idrisov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究真实欧姆接触（real Ohmic contact, ROC）在电荷分数化（charge-fractionalization）区域产生的非平衡态，并考察其在量子霍尔边缘（quantum Hall edge）上经由大电容、完美耦合的虚构欧姆接触（fictitious Ohmic contact, FOC）所介导的热能弛豫过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究常假设接触可用单一有效接触温度（effective contact temperature）刻画，本文意在检验该假设在冷、热两种欧姆接触情形下的有效性。

Method: 构建含ROC与FOC的量子霍尔边缘模型，通过计算电子分布函数（electron distribution function）与电流噪声（current noise）来分析弛豫行为。

Result: 冷接触情形下，分布函数与噪声可由平衡形式及相应费米-狄拉克分布（Fermi-Dirac distribution）完全描述；热接触情形下，强热化效应并不使出射态达到完全平衡，分布函数与噪声均保留初始非平衡态的印记。

Conclusion: 热接触下出射态不能仅由有效接触温度表征，与常见假设相反。

Abstract: We study the nonequilibrium state generated by a real Ohmic contact (ROC) in the charge-fractionalization regime. We then investigate its thermal-energy relaxation along a quantum Hall edge mediated by a fictitious Ohmic contact (FOC) with large capacitance and perfect coupling to the edge. For cold Ohmic contacts, the electron distribution function and current noise are fully described by their equilibrium forms and the corresponding Fermi-Dirac distribution. In contrast, in the case of hot Ohmic contacts, strong thermalization effects do not drive the outgoing state into complete equilibrium. Both the distribution function and the noise retain signatures of the initial nonequilibrium state and therefore cannot be characterized solely by the effective contact temperature, contrary to common assumptions.

</details>


### [134] [Universal Analytical Description of Magnetoexcitons in Two-Dimensional Semiconducting Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19415)
*Dang-Khoa D. Le,Dai-Nam Le,Ngoc-Loan Phan,Van-Hoang Le*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文通过匹配 Rytova–Keldysh 模型（Rytova–Keldysh model）的库仑极限与对数极限，给出单层激子（monolayer exciton）s 态的通用零场谱公式，并据此解析预测抗磁系数与磁场交叉尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单层材料中屏蔽依赖的激子谱缺乏简洁通用的解析描述，且光学与磁光（magneto-optical）性质通常被分别处理，需要统一的参数化框架。

Method: 以屏蔽参数 ξ 为桥梁，对 Rytova–Keldysh 势的库仑极限（Coulomb limit）与对数极限（logarithmic limit）进行匹配，构造 s 态的零场能级解析式 E_n≃−Ry*/[n−1/2+f_n(ξ)]^2，并由解析半径导出抗磁系数（diamagnetic coefficients）与磁场交叉尺度（magnetic crossover scale）。

Result: 在 0≤ξ≤100 的全部屏蔽范围内，零场偏差峰值约 1%；对取样的 hBN 封装单层，磁激子（magnetoexciton）能量与数值谱在直至 100 T 的范围内一致到亚 meV 均方根（rms），并复现了实测轨道位移。

Conclusion: 所提公式以共享参数同时联系光学与磁光光谱，为屏蔽依赖的激子能级与磁场响应提供统一的解析描述。

Abstract: Matching the Coulomb and logarithmic limits of the Rytova--Keldysh model gives a universal zero-field spectrum for monolayer exciton $s$ states, $E_n\simeq-\mathrm{Ry}^*/[n-1/2+f_n(ξ)]^2$, where $ξ$ is the screening parameter. Analytical radii determine diamagnetic coefficients and the magnetic crossover scale. Zero-field deviations peak near $1\%$ across the full screening range $0\leqξ\leq100$. For the sampled hBN-encapsulated monolayers, magnetoexciton energies agree with numerical spectra to sub-meV rms up to $100\T$ and capture the measured orbital shifts. Shared parameters link optical and magneto-optical spectra.

</details>


### [135] [Ultrastrong coupling between magnetoplasmons and higher cyclotron-resonance harmonics induced by spatially inhomogeneous electromagnetic fields in two-dimensional electron systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19425)
*Kazuyuki Kuroyama,Shimpei Nakamura,Motoaki Bamba*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文理论上研究垂直磁场中二维电子系统在空间非均匀电磁场下的磁等离激元响应，通过将麦克斯韦方程与久保公式结合建立非局域磁导率与集体模式色散，并用Hopfield型哈密顿量量化磁等离激元与回旋共振谐波的耦合强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限波矢电磁场（finite-wave-vector electromagnetic fields）为探测二维电子系统中的非局域集体动力学提供途径，但非局域磁导率、Bernstein模式物理与集体模式有效耦合之间尚缺乏定量联系。

Method: 将麦克斯韦方程（Maxwell's equations）与久保公式（Kubo formula）结合，推导非局域磁导率张量（nonlocal magnetoconductivity tensor）及集体模式的自洽色散关系；并建立该Maxwell-Kubo色散关系与Hopfield型哈密顿量（Hopfield-type Hamiltonian）的对应，解析给出磁等离激元与第n次回旋共振谐波的有效耦合强度。

Result: 有限面内波矢（in-plane wave vector）允许多阶回旋跃迁，使回旋共振的高次谐波贡献于非局域电导，并与磁等离激元模式杂化，在集体模式谱中产生Bernstein模式（Bernstein modes）特征的避免交叉（avoided crossings）。归一化耦合强度对面内波长呈非单调依赖，在短波长处达到最大值，且在高密度区几乎与电子密度无关。

Conclusion: 研究建立了非局域磁导率、Bernstein模式物理与有效集体模式耦合之间的定量联系，为太赫兹磁等离激元系统（terahertz magnetoplasmonic systems）中实现超强耦合（ultrastrong coupling）提供设计依据。

Abstract: Finite-wave-vector electromagnetic fields provide a route to accessing nonlocal collective dynamics in two-dimensional electron systems. Here, we theoretically investigate the magnetoplasmon response of a two-dimensional electron system subjected to a spatially nonuniform electromagnetic field in a perpendicular magnetic field. By combining Maxwell's equations with the Kubo formula, we derive the nonlocal magnetoconductivity tensor and obtain the self-consistent dispersion relation for collective modes. We show that a finite in-plane wave vector enables higher-order cyclotron transitions, allowing higher harmonics of the cyclotron resonance to contribute to the nonlocal conductivity. These harmonics hybridize with the magnetoplasmon mode, producing avoided crossings in the collective-mode spectrum characteristic of Bernstein modes. To quantify this hybridization, we establish a correspondence between the Maxwell-Kubo dispersion relation and a Hopfield-type Hamiltonian and derive an analytical expression for the effective coupling strength between the magnetoplasmon and the n-th cyclotron-resonance harmonic. Our analysis reveals that the normalized coupling strength exhibits a nonmonotonic dependence on the in-plane wavelength, reaching a maximum that becomes nearly independent of electron density in the high-density regime. These results establish a quantitative connection between nonlocal magnetoconductivity, Bernstein-mode physics, and effective collective-mode coupling, providing design guidelines for realizing ultrastrong coupling in terahertz magnetoplasmonic systems.

</details>


### [136] [Current-driven chiral RKKY interaction in altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19574)
*Anirban Kundu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在无自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）的自旋劈裂金属中，电荷电流（charge current）可产生局域自旋之间的Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya（DM）相互作用，且该相互作用在二维d波交替磁体（d-wave altermagnet）中具有可通过电流与奈尔矢量翻转的d波特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为DM相互作用（Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya interaction）需要自旋轨道耦合与反演对称性破缺，作者据此提出反例：在无自旋轨道耦合的自旋劈裂金属中，电流本身即可诱导DM相互作用，并关注其在交替磁体（altermagnet）中的特殊表现。

Method: 采用漂移费米海（drifting Fermi sea）下的Keldysh格林函数（Keldysh Green's functions），计算二维d波交替磁体（含额外交换劈裂）中两个磁性杂质间的RKKY相互作用；在电流线性阶，修正为费米面项，并以贝塞尔函数（Bessel functions）闭式给出所有分量，适用于任意两个独立自旋劈裂β与J⊥。

Result: 诱导的DM矢量沿自旋量子化轴（面外ẑ方向），长程按1/R衰减，比平衡耦合慢一个幂次；在交替磁体中沿节点方向（两自旋带简并处）的键上消失，对角电流下沿两条晶轴的键上符号相反，该d波特征随奈尔矢量与电流翻转，从而区别于铁磁体；电流还产生对杂质间距为奇的对称耦合。低密度二维导体中DM耦合可达平衡交换的百分之几。

Conclusion: 电荷电流可在无自旋轨道耦合的自旋劈裂金属中生成DM相互作用，且交替磁体中其d波符号结构提供了一种与铁磁体区分并可由奈尔矢量与电流调控的特征。

Abstract: The Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya (DM) interaction between magnetic moments usually requires spin--orbit coupling and broken inversion symmetry. We show that in a spin-split metal without spin--orbit coupling a charge current generates a DM interaction between localized spins. Using Keldysh Green's functions (GFs) with a drifting Fermi sea, we compute the Ruderman--Kittel--Kasuya--Yosida (RKKY) interaction between two magnetic impurities in a two-dimensional $d$-wave altermagnet with an additional exchange splitting. To linear order in the current, the correction is a Fermi-surface term, and all its components are obtained in closed form in terms of Bessel functions, valid for arbitrary values of the two independent spin splittings, $β$ and $J_\perp$. The induced DM vector points along the spin-quantization axis, which in the present model is the out-of-plane direction $\hat{\mathbf{z}}$. At long distance it decays as $1/R$, one power more slowly than the equilibrium coupling. In the altermagnet it vanishes for bonds along the nodal directions, where the two spin bands are degenerate, and, for a diagonal current, has opposite signs on bonds along the two crystal axes. This $d$-wave signature reverses with the Néel vector and with the current, and distinguishes the altermagnet from a ferromagnet. The current also generates symmetric couplings that are odd in the impurity separation. In low-density two-dimensional conductors, the DM coupling can reach a few percent of the equilibrium exchange.

</details>


### [137] [Field-Effect Magnon Transistor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19582)
*Qian Song,Sergei Prokhorenko,Thomas Cleveland,Ashish Omar,Zhi Yao,Sajid Husain,Peter Meisenheimer,Bowen Hao,Ziye Zhu,Pushpendra Gupta,Shashank Ojha,Nicholas Reiterer,Maya Ramesh,Sergio Puebla,Darrell Schlom,Tong Zhou,Laurent Bellaiche,Lane W. Martin,Ramamoorthy Ramesh*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出并实现基于铁电反铁磁体BiFeO₃的磁振子场效应晶体管（magnonic field-effect transistor），利用栅极电场重构畴壁（domain boundaries）以放大与调制磁振子输运（magnon transport），并展示其非互易（nonreciprocal）特性与电可编程、非易失操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁磁振子（antiferromagnetic magnons）具备高速、低能耗信息处理的潜力，且摆线磁序（cycloidal magnetic order）中的本征奇宇称自旋劈裂（odd-parity spin splitting）为补偿磁体中实现非零磁振子输运提供途径；因此需要一种电控方式来调制此类输运。

Method: 设计基于铁电反铁磁体BiFeO₃的磁振子场效应器件，通过栅极电场重构畴壁以放大/调制磁振子输运；结合源漏反转（source-drain reversal）、磁场测量、对称性分析（symmetry analysis）与第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）进行表征与机理验证。

Result: 畴壁可在低信号态与高信号态之间切换非局域逆自旋霍尔响应（nonlocal inverse-spin-Hall response），且该响应显著依赖于磁振子传播方向；实验与计算共同表明，非互易磁振子输运与跨越畴壁时自旋角动量（spin angular momentum）的反转相关。

Conclusion: 结果确立了一种电可编程、非易失的磁振子晶体管（electrically programmable, non-volatile magnon transistor），为反铁磁自旋电子学中的电场调控输运提供器件范式。

Abstract: Antiferromagnetic magnons offer a promising platform for highspeed, energy-efficient information processing. Intrinsic odd-parity spin splitting in a cycloidal magnetic order offers a route to nonvanishing magnon transport in compensated magnets. Here, we propose a magnonic field-effect device (akin to a transistor) based on a ferroelectric antiferromagnet BiFeO3 in which a gate electric field reconfigures domain boundaries to amplify/modulate magnon transport. These boundaries switch the nonlocal inverse-spin-Hall response between low- and high-signal states, with a pronounced dependence on the direction of magnon propagation. Source-drain reversal, magnetic-field measurements, symmetry analysis, and first-principles calculations reveal nonreciprocal magnon transport associated with the reversal of spin angular momentum across the domain boundaries. Our results establish an electrically programmable, non-volatile magnon transistor.

</details>


### [138] [Quadrupole Origin of the Orbital Hall Effect in an $sp^3$ Centrosymmetric Lattice: Robustness and Tunability under Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19651)
*Kevin J. U. Vidarte,Henrique P. Veiga,Aurélien Manchon,Tatiana G. Rappoport*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在中心对称无自旋 $sp^3$ 紧束缚模型中，用 Chebyshev 多项式展开的 Kubo-Bastin 形式研究无序对轨道霍尔输运的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨道霍尔效应（orbital Hall effect）的常用最小模型是无序研究缺失的中心对称无自旋 $sp^3$ 方格子紧束缚模型；本文旨在厘清不同无序类型如何影响其轨道霍尔响应。

Method: 采用 Chebyshev 多项式展开的 Kubo-Bastin 形式，系统比较空位（vacancies）、轨道依赖的 Anderson 在位无序以及选择性 $V_{sp\sigma}$ 键无序对轨道输运的作用；干净体系中本征轨道霍尔响应源于 $s$-$p$ 杂化（$s$-$p$ hybridization）产生的四极轨道织构（quadrupolar orbital texture）。

Result: 在 Anderson 无序与空位情形下，纵向电荷电导（longitudinal charge conductivity）比轨道霍尔电导下降更快，使轨道霍尔角（orbital Hall angle）增大，且在 Anderson 情形下于较强无序处饱和；选择性 $V_{sp\sigma}$ 键无序仅改变 $s$-$p$ 杂化通道，可产生非单调轨道霍尔响应，并在中等稀释度下增强轨道霍尔电导。

Conclusion: 轨道霍尔响应在空位与在位无序下仍保持可观幅度，而选择性调控 $s$-$p$ 杂化提供了调节轨道霍尔电导的额外途径。

Abstract: We investigate how disorder affects orbital Hall transport in the centrosymmetric spinless $sp^3$ tight-binding model on a square lattice, widely used as a minimal model for the orbital Hall effect [1]. In the pristine system, the intrinsic orbital Hall response is related to the quadrupolar orbital texture generated by $s$-$p$ hybridization. Using the Kubo-Bastin formalism expanded in Chebyshev polynomials, we analyze the effect of vacancies, orbital-dependent Anderson on-site disorder, and selective $V_{spσ}$-bond disorder on the orbital transport. For Anderson disorder and vacancies, the longitudinal charge conductivity decreases faster than the orbital Hall conductivity over the range studied, leading to an increase of the orbital Hall angle and, in the Anderson case, saturation at stronger disorder. Selective $V_{spσ}$-bond disorder behaves differently: by modifying only the $s$-$p$ hybridization channel while leaving the remaining hopping network intact, it can produce a non-monotonic orbital Hall response and enhance the orbital Hall conductivity at intermediate dilution. Overall, our results show that the orbital Hall response remains sizable under vacancy and on-site disorder, while selective modification of the $s$-$p$ hybridization provides an additional route to tune the orbital Hall conductivity.

</details>


### [139] [Ferroelectric control of the $K$--$Γ$ valley landscape in R-stacked bilayer WSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19705)
*Katsunori Wakabayashi,Souren Adhikary,Tomoaki Kameda,Kazuhito Tsukagoshi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在双层WSe2中，菱面体（rhombohedral, R）堆叠的对称性使K谷层间隧穿严格为零（上界0.017 meV），而Γ谷耦合达361.8 meV；由此产生的极化电压阶跃使K边移动41.7 meV，并给出仅依赖对称性的两条读出/探测途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有认识认为双层WSe2中K与Γ价带顶能量接近，栅压只是在固定能带"景观（landscape）"上重新分配空穴；本文旨在指出R堆叠可改变景观本身，并厘清其对称性根源与可测量后果。

Method: 基于R堆叠的三重对称性进行对称性分析，判定K谷层间隧穿为零、Γ谷耦合不为零；对耦合自由拟合得到K耦合上界0.017 meV与Γ耦合361.8 meV，并分析极化产生的层间电势阶跃与K双重态劈裂（41.7 meV）。

Result: K边移动约为劈裂的一半（41.7 meV），任何隧穿都会使其减小，Γ几乎不响应；两种具有相反面外铁电极化（ferroelectric polarization）的配准（registry）沿位移场轴互为镜像，差分模式跨导（transconductance）在零场处等值反号，理想对称极限下跨导在1.07 V/nm处过零。

Conclusion: 铁电极化以符号反转（而非电流差）方式被读出，扫场是同一宇称关系的第二条途径；两条途径的限制主要来自器件对称性而非能带结构。堆叠亦重整化景观，故极性态并非非极性参考的平移拷贝；绝对偏移依赖交换关联泛函（exchange-correlation functional），而对称性关系不依赖。

Abstract: In bilayer WSe2 the valence-band maxima at K and Γ lie within a few thermal energies of one another, and gating redistributes holes between a light and a heavy valley of different mobilities on a landscape that stays fixed. We show that rhombohedral (R) stacking moves the landscape itself. The three-fold symmetry of R stacking forbids interlayer tunneling at K but not at Γ. The K coupling is therefore exactly zero, not merely small. A fit that leaves the coupling free returns an upper bound of 0.017 meV, three orders of magnitude under k_BT at room temperature, while the Γ coupling is 361.8 meV. The polarization creates a potential step between the layers and splits the K doublet. Each K state stays on a single layer, so the K edge shifts by half of that splitting, 41.7 meV, which any tunneling would reduce. Γ barely responds. Two consequences follow from symmetry alone, whatever the size of that shift. The two registries, carrying opposite out-of-plane ferroelectric polarizations, are exact mirror images along the displacement-field axis, and reversing the polarization reverses the displacement of the landscape. Their differential-mode transconductances are equal and opposite at zero field, where the response vanishes for an ideal nonpolar reference. The stored polarization is read out as a reversal of sign and not as a difference of current. Sweeping the field is a second route to the same parity. In the ideal symmetric limit the transconductance passes through zero at the field that cancels the polarization, 1.07 V/nm, at a single temperature. Both routes are limited by the symmetry of the device more than by the band structure. Stacking also renormalizes the landscape, so the polar one is not a translated copy of the nonpolar reference. Absolute offsets follow the exchange-correlation functional. The symmetry relations do not.

</details>


### [140] [State Diagram of Dual-Free-Layer Perpendicular Magnetic Tunnel Junctions for Artificial Spiking Neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19727)
*Arthur Courberand,Ajay Jha,Ricardo C. Sousa,Stephane Auffret,Joo-Von Kim,Ursula Ebels,Liliana D. Buda-Prejbeanu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作结合实验与数值手段研究含双自由层（two free layers）的垂直磁隧道结（perpendicular magnetic tunnel junction, MTJ），给出静态与动力学相图、windmill翻转机制及自旋转移矩（spin-transfer torque）二次项的解析描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 垂直MTJ性能强烈依赖界面性质与层厚度（layer thickness），微小变化即可显著改变磁性与输运特性；同时需在保留进动动力学（precessional dynamics）的前提下简化器件制备。

Method: 在磁场及直流或脉冲偏置下测量准静态状态相图（state diagram），并进行宏自旋模拟（macrospin simulation）与Landau-Lifchitz-Gilbert（LLG）方程的稳定性分析（stability analysis）。

Result: 相图显示平行、反平行与双稳态（bistable）区域，以及一层翻转触发另一层翻转的动力学windmill区域，可产生类脉冲神经元信号（spiking neuron-like signals）；解析模型给出临界线及windmill阈值电压，并揭示焦耳热（Joule heating）无法解释的自旋转移矩电压依赖二次项。

Conclusion: 阈值电压可通过接近自旋重取向转变（spin-reorientation transition，以牺牲热稳定性为代价）或增强自旋转移矩的二次贡献来降低；研究为面向人工脉冲神经元（artificial spiking neuron）及神经形态（neuromorphic）与自旋电子学（spintronic）应用的多层MTJ堆栈优化提供准则。

Abstract: The performance of perpendicular magnetic tunnel junctions (MTJs) depends critically on interfacial properties and layer thicknesses, small variations of which can substantially modify their magnetic and transport characteristics. This work investigates, experimentally and numerically, a perpendicular MTJ with two free layers, a configuration that preserves the precessional dynamics of the device while simplifying its fabrication. Quasi-static state diagrams measured under magnetic fields and DC or pulsed bias reveal static parallel, antiparallel, and bistable regimes, as well as a dynamical windmill regime in which the reversal of one layer triggers that of the other, giving rise to spiking neuron-like signals. Macrospin simulations reproduce these diagrams, and a stability analysis of the Landau-Lifchitz-Gilbert (LLG) equation yields an analytical description of the critical lines, including the windmill threshold voltage. The comparison also reveals a quadratic term in the voltage dependence of the spin-transfer torque that Joule heating cannot explain. The analytical model shows that the threshold voltage can be lowered either by approaching the spin-reorientation transition, at the cost of thermal stability, or by enhancing the quadratic contribution to the spin-transfer torque. These results establish guidelines for optimizing multilayer MTJ stacks for artificial spiking neurons and a broader range of neuromorphic and spintronic applications.

</details>


### [141] [Electrical transport of surface-bound electrons on annealed solid neon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19853)
*Ka Wing Yip,Tomoyuki Tani,Jui-Yin Lin,Mikhail Belianchikov,Denis Konstantinov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 采用Sommer-Tanner方法研究固态氖表面束缚电子的直流输运（dc transport），发现毫米级样品的电容串扰（capacitive cross-talk）随温度和退火（annealing）显著变化，且响应不同于液氦表面电子；由此提取的电阻在16–20 K符合蒸气原子散射（vapor-atom scattering）机制，低温符合表面粗糙度散射，表明电子迁移率高但密度远低于预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究固态氖（solid neon）表面束缚电子的直流输运，并将其行为与液氦表面电子系统进行对比，以评估该体系作为表面探针的可行性及实验方法的适用性。

Method: 使用传统Sommer-Tanner方法测量固态氖表面电子的直流输运；考察电容串扰随温度与样品退火的变化，并从同相响应（in-phase response）中提取电子系统电阻，在1.4–20 K范围内分析温度依赖关系。

Result: 毫米级厚度样品的电容串扰随温度大幅变化且受退火强烈影响；即使退火后，观测响应仍显著区别于液氦表面电子且难以用常规模型描述。电阻在16–20 K与蒸气原子散射机制吻合良好，低温则与温度相关的表面粗糙度（surface roughness）散射一致；分析显示电子迁移率（electron mobility）较高，但密度比预期低两个数量级。

Conclusion: 表面电子可作为研究氖表面形貌（morphology）的合适探针，但亟需更好地理解适用于此类体系的实验方法。

Abstract: We study the dc transport of electrons bound to the surface of solid neon using the conventional Sommer-Tanner method. First, we find that the capacitive cross-talk for the thickness of solid samples on the order of a millimeter varies substantially with the temperature and is strongly affected by the sample annealing. Second, we find that, even for the annealed samples, the observed response of electrons differs significantly from that of the surface-bound electrons on liquid helium and cannot be satisfactorily described by conventional models. Despite this, we managed to extract the resistance of the electron system from its in-phase response and investigate the temperature dependence in a wide range between 1.4 and 20 K. In a high-temperature range between 16 and 20 K, the resistance behavior is found to agree very well with the vapor-atom scattering mechanism, while at lower temperatures it is consistent with temperature-dependent scattering by surface roughness. Our analysis indicates a high electron mobility of electrons on solid neon but two orders of magnitude lower density than expected. These results suggest surface electrons as a suitable probe for studying the morphology of the neon surface but call for a better understanding of the experimental method applicable to such systems.

</details>


### [142] [Theory of anisotropic magnetotransport beyond the constant relaxation-time approximation in multi-Weyl semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19869)
*Azaz Ahmad,Shantanu Mukherjee,Mohd Hashim Raza,Gautham Varma K.,Takami Tohyama*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作建立任意单极荷（monopole charge）N 的多外尔半金属（multi-Weyl semimetal）半经典玻尔兹曼（Boltzmann）输运框架，引入轨道磁矩（orbital magnetic moment, OMM）与贝里曲率（Berry curvature）修正，发现 OMM 可在足够强的谷间散射（intervalley scattering）下导致纵向磁导率（LMC）变号，且临界散射强度随 N 增大而降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多外尔半金属的节点携带更高单极荷，为超越常规外尔范式的拓扑磁输运（topological magnetotransport）提供平台；但现有理解多局限于常规手征反常（chiral anomaly）图像，需纳入 OMM 与散射机制的完整描述。

Method: 构建适用于任意 N 的广义半经典玻尔兹曼（semiclassical Boltzmann）框架，将 OMM 纳入准粒子色散与贝里曲率修正的动力学，并采用动量依赖的碰撞积分（collision integral）同时计入谷内与谷间散射；对 N=1、2、3 在非磁性与磁性杂质散射下计算 LMC 与平面霍尔电导（planar Hall conductivity, PHC）。

Result: 无 OMM 时 LMC 在所研究的散射区间内保持正值且随磁场二次方变化，符合手征反常辅助输运；计入 OMM 后，在 σ0 与 σz 杂质通道中足够强的谷间散射使 LMC 变号，且临界谷间散射强度随 N 增大系统性减小。PHC 在 N=2、3 时同样出现变号，而 N=1 在参数范围内保持正值；LMC 与 PHC 的角依赖仍分别保持 sin²γ 型与 sin2γ 型。

Conclusion: 贝里曲率、OMM、单极荷与杂质散射的共同作用可产生与常规手征反常图像定性不同的弱场磁输运响应，高单极荷体系更易出现负 LMC。

Abstract: Multi-Weyl semimetals, characterized by Weyl nodes carrying higher monopole charges, provide a platform for exploring topological magnetotransport beyond the conventional Weyl paradigm. Here, we develop a generalized semiclassical Boltzmann framework for multi-Weyl semimetals with arbitrary monopole charge $N$, incorporating the orbital magnetic moment (OMM) into the quasiparticle dispersion and Berry-curvature-modified dynamics, together with a momentum-dependent collision integral accounting for intra- and intervalley scattering. We investigate the longitudinal magnetoconductivity (LMC) and planar Hall conductivity (PHC) for $N=1$, $2$, and $3$, considering both nonmagnetic and magnetic impurity scattering. In the absence of OMM, the LMC remains positive and quadratic in the magnetic field throughout the investigated scattering regime, consistent with conventional chiral-anomaly-assisted transport. Including OMM, however, leads to a sign reversal of the LMC under sufficiently strong intervalley scattering for the $σ_0$ and $σ_z$ impurity channels. Remarkably, the critical intervalley scattering strength decreases systematically with increasing monopole charge, indicating an enhanced tendency toward negative LMC in higher-charge Weyl systems. Furthermore, we find that the PHC also undergoes a sign reversal for $N=2$ and $N=3$ in the same impurity channels, while remaining positive for $N=1$ within the investigated parameter range. The angular dependences retain their characteristic $\sin^2γ$-like and $\sin2γ$ profiles for LMC and PHC, respectively, even across the sign reversal. Our results demonstrate that the interplay of Berry curvature, OMM, monopole charge, and impurity scattering can produce qualitatively distinct weak-field magnetotransport responses beyond the conventional chiral-anomaly picture.

</details>


### [143] [Exciton-polarons in monolayer semiconductors with drifting electrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19879)
*Shadman Iqbal,Jesper Levinsen,Meera M. Parish,Dmitry K. Efimkin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 论文提出通过施加偏置电流使电子费米海（Fermi sea）在动量空间发生位移，从而让光学探测到的零动量激子极化子（exciton-polaron）相对载流子获得有限动量，为探测激子极化子的有限动量物理提供途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于光子面内动量可忽略，光学测量仅能探测零动量附近的激子极化子，其有限动量物理长期未被探索；论文旨在提出一种可实验实现的方法来进入该区域。

Method: 采用激子极化子问题的图（diagrammatic）表述，推导电流偏置下激子极化子的谱学与准粒子性质同平衡费米海中有限动量对应量之间的精确关系。

Result: 推导出的精确关系表明，漂移电子下的吸收测量可重建吸引支与排斥支两分支准粒子性质的动量依赖；估计实验可及的电流能够覆盖较宽动量范围且仍处于线性响应（linear-response）区。

Conclusion: 电流偏置吸收测量为探测激子极化子的有限动量物理提供了可行方案，并可重建两分支准粒子性质的动量依赖。

Abstract: Recent work has demonstrated that the optical absorption of moderately doped two-dimensional semiconductors can be described in terms of exciton-polarons. In this picture, interactions between a photoexcited exciton and a Fermi sea of excess charge carriers split the exciton spectrum into a lower-energy attractive polaron branch and a higher-energy repulsive polaron branch. Due to the negligible in-plane momentum of photons, however, optical measurements probe only exciton-polarons near zero momentum, leaving their finite-momentum physics largely unexplored. Here we propose accessing this regime by measuring the absorption in the presence of an electric bias current. In effect, this displaces the electronic Fermi sea in momentum space, such that an optically active exciton-polaron at rest acquires finite momentum relative to the charge carriers. Using a diagrammatic formulation of the exciton-polaron problem, we derive exact relations between the spectral and quasiparticle properties of current-biased exciton-polarons and their finite-momentum counterparts in an equilibrium Fermi sea. These relations show that absorption measurements with drifting electrons can reconstruct the momentum dependence of the quasiparticle properties for both branches. We estimate that experimentally accessible currents can probe a broad momentum range while remaining within the linear-response regime.

</details>


### [144] [Global bound-state spectrum and spin- and angular-momentum-dependent confinement in an inverse-$3/2$ magnetic quantum dot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20963)
*Luis B. Castro*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文在反 3/2 径向磁场（radial magnetic-field）下求解 Schrödinger–Pauli 方程的束缚态谱与束缚阈值，指出准精确可解（quasi-exact solvability）解仅是整个双合流 Heun 谱（biconfluent Heun spectrum）中的孤立精确点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究通常将径向问题限制在准精确多项式扇区（polynomial sector）内求解，可能遗漏非多项式的连续束缚态分支，因此有必要建立全局的束缚态判据。

Method: 径向方程约化为双合流 Heun 方程（biconfluent Heun equation）；通过谱 Wronskian（spectral Wronskian）将端点选择的 Frobenius 解（Frobenius solution）与次主导渐近解（subdominant asymptotic solution）匹配，实现全局束缚态条件；并对连续谱边缘（continuum edge）作独立精确分析。

Result: 谱中包含连续的非多项式束缚态分支，已知准精确解在其上表现为孤立精确点；多项式指标 N 仅在准精确扇区内作为终止指标（termination index），不能唯一标记谱分支；连续谱边缘给出依赖自旋与角动量（spin- and angular-momentum-dependent）的临界耦合，可低于准精确限制 U0a>4。

Conclusion: 准精确可解性应被理解为嵌入更广泛双合流 Heun 谱中的一组特殊解析点，该谱具有依赖自旋与角动量的束缚阈值。

Abstract: We investigate the bound-state spectrum and confinement thresholds of a two-dimensional electron governed by the Schrödinger--Pauli equation in an inverse-$3/2$ radial magnetic-field profile. The radial problem reduces to the biconfluent Heun equation, but instead of restricting the solution to the quasi-exact polynomial sector, we impose the bound-state condition globally through a spectral Wronskian between the endpoint-selected Frobenius solution and the subdominant asymptotic solution. The resulting spectrum contains continuous nonpolynomial bound-state branches on which the known quasi-exact solutions appear as isolated exact points. In this global description, the polynomial index $N$ remains a termination index within the quasi-exact sector but does not uniquely label the spectral branches. An independent exact analysis of the continuum edge yields spin- and angular-momentum-dependent critical couplings that can lie below the quasi-exact restriction $U_0a>4$, where $U_0$ is the Coulomb-strength parameter and $a$ is the magnetic-profile length scale. These results establish quasi-exact solvability as a set of special analytic points embedded in a broader biconfluent-Heun spectrum with spin- and angular-momentum-dependent confinement thresholds.

</details>


### [145] [Torsional Hall Viscosity of Massive Chern Insulators: Finite Temperature and Chemical Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21217)
*Weizhen Jia,Ioannis Matthaiakakis,David Rodríguez Fernández,Zhuo-Yu Xian,Johanna Erdmenger,Ewelina M. Hankiewicz,René Meyer*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究有限温度与化学势下陈绝缘体（Chern insulator）的扭转霍尔黏度（torsional Hall viscosity）$\zeta_{\rm H}$，涵盖大质量狄拉克费米子（massive Dirac fermion）情形及单块BHZ模型中的动量相关质量，揭示温度与化学势对$\zeta_{\rm H}$的修正规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转霍尔黏度的既有理论多限于零温、固定化学势的理想条件，缺乏对实验可及条件（有限温度、有限化学势、磁场及BHZ类形变）下的系统刻画，限制了其实际探测。

Method: 考虑大质量狄拉克费米子与两种形变（恒定面外磁场、由单块Bernevig-Hughes-Zhang（BHZ）哈密顿量描述的动量相关质量），在有限温度与化学势下解析计算$\zeta_{\rm H}$的修正；磁场情形借助相对论性朗道能级（Landau-level）谱进行分析。

Result: 大质量狄拉克理论中，温度与化学势给出紫外有限（UV-finite）修正，且体能隙外的化学势在零温下亦改变$\zeta_{\rm H}$；恒定磁场下零温呈现平台结构（由朗道能级谱决定），有限温度下被平滑，跳变量正比于狄拉克质量、反比于磁长度平方与新占据朗道能级能量。对(Hg,Mn)Te量子阱相关参数，只要化学势深于热能对应的能隙内位置，跨质量畴壁（mass domain wall）的跳变幅度保持BHZ形变带来的真空增强。

Conclusion: 结果将扭转霍尔黏度理论推广至实验相关条件，并可能推动其未来探测。

Abstract: We study the torsional Hall viscosity $ζ_{\rm H}$ of Chern insulators at finite temperature and chemical potential. Our analysis considers the massive Dirac fermion and two deformations: a constant out-of-plane magnetic field and a momentum-dependent mass described by the single-block Bernevig-Hughes-Zhang (BHZ) Hamiltonian. For the massive Dirac theory, we show that temperature and chemical potential lead to UV-finite corrections and that a chemical potential outside the bulk gap modifies $ζ_{\rm H}$ even at zero temperature. In a constant magnetic field, the response of massive Dirac fermions is dictated by the relativistic Landau-level spectrum, leading to a plateau structure at zero temperature that is smoothed at finite temperature. The jumps are proportional to the Dirac mass and inversely proportional to the squared magnetic length and the newly occupied Landau-level energy. Finally, we present a detailed study of the correction to $ζ_{\rm H}$ in the single-block BHZ continuum model due to temperature and chemical potential. For parameters relevant to (Hg,Mn)Te quantum wells, the magnitude of the jump across a mass domain wall retains its vacuum enhancement due to the BHZ deformation as long as the chemical potential lies sufficiently deep inside the gap compared with the thermal energy. Our results extend the theory of torsional Hall viscosity to experimentally relevant conditions and may facilitate its future detection.

</details>


### [146] [Optical spin Edelstein response of d-wave altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20235)
*M. A. Mojarro,R. Carrillo-Bastos,Jesús A. Maytorena*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究了二维d波交错磁体（altermagnet）在Rashba自旋轨道耦合下的频率域自旋Edelstein效应，揭示了带间纵向与横向响应分别由Zeeman量子度规（Zeeman quantum metric）与Zeeman贝里曲率（Zeeman Berry curvature）支配，并给出费米面拓扑变化导致的三区域谱特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁体的动量依赖自旋劈裂与补偿磁序为自旋极化的电学调控提供了可能，本文旨在探索该类体系中频率域自旋Edelstein效应的响应机制与可调控性。

Method: 构建含Rashba自旋轨道耦合的二维d波交错磁体模型，计算超越带内响应区的自旋-速度磁化率响应函数（spin-velocity susceptibility response function），并结合态密度与费米面分析响应张量的谱特征。

Result: 带间纵向与横向Edelstein响应分别由Zeeman量子度规和Zeeman贝里曲率决定；态密度在特定负能量处呈现对数van Hove奇点（van Hove singularity），该能量以下出现四条不相连的费米轮廓，从而界定费米能级位置的三个区域，各区域对应特征性的Edelstein磁化率谱结构。

Conclusion: 所得自旋磁化强度强烈依赖驱动频率与面内外加电场取向，表明交错磁体中自旋Edelstein效应可由电学与光学手段调控，具备自旋电子学应用潜力。

Abstract: The momentum-dependent spin splitting and compensated magnetic order in altermagnets offer opportunities for electrical control of spin polarization. We explore the spin Edelstein effect in the frequency domain of a two-dimensional d-wave altermagnet with Rashba spin-orbit coupling. We calculate the spin-velocity susceptibility response function beyond the intraband response regime and find that the interband longitudinal and transverse Edelstein responses are governed by the Zeeman quantum metric and Zeeman Berry curvature, respectively. The density of states presents a logarithmic van Hove singularity at exactly the negative value of the energy below which four disconnected Fermi contours emerge. This defines three distinctive regions for the position of the Fermi level, through which the Edelstein susceptibility tensor displays characteristic spectral features, reflecting the topological change of the Fermi surface. The resulting spin magnetization shows a strong dependence on the driving frequency and on the orientation of the in-plane external electric field. Our results highlight new possibilities for electrical and optical control of the spin Edelstein effect in altermagnets that could find spintronic applications.

</details>


### [147] [Strain-Engineering Giant Pseudo-Electric Fields in Multilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20323)
*Yiwen Liu,Ambikesh Gupta,Adrián Ceferino,Youngjoon Choi,Pierre A. Pantaleón,Zhen Zhan,Suman Sanki,Hari Stoyanov,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,Francisco Guinea,Andrea Young,Haim Beidenkopf,Nurit Avraham*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究在多层石墨烯中实现并观测到由原子弛豫诱导的赝标量势（pseudoscalar potential）/赝电场（pseudo-electric field），揭示其产生超平坦色散、巨大能隙、关联增强与拓扑边界态保护等效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固体中通过晶格畸变可构造电磁标量与矢量势，其中产生赝磁场的赝矢量分量已因零场量子霍尔效应（quantum Hall effect）备受关注，而许多电子系统本身敏感的赝标量分量（产生赝电场）此前尚未被实验探索。

Method: 以多层石墨烯系统为平台，利用原子弛豫重构形成由一维菱方通道（rhombohedral channels）分隔的大尺度Bernal堆垛畴，并进行原子尺度的晶格形变成像与谱学测量。

Result: 首次实现石墨烯应变下不同晶格形变的原子尺度直接成像；在菱方通道中观测到拉伸晶格形变诱导的巨赝电场，产生具有发散态密度（density of states）与大的能隙的超平坦色散，导致关联增强、平带不稳定性及条纹状态密度调制；赝标量势打开的能隙可保护无需外场的拓扑边界态。

Conclusion: 研究提供了石墨烯弹性的全面原子尺度表征及晶格畸变产生非平庸赝电场的直接谱学证据，并提供了一种内在、可调的新量子态实现途径。

Abstract: Electromagnetic scalar and vector potentials can be engineered in solids through lattice distortions, providing a versatile means of tuning electronic band structures. The pseudovector component, which generates pseudo-magnetic fields, has received much attention for its ability to realize a zero-field quantum Hall effect. However, the pseudoscalar component, giving rise to pseudo-electric fields to which many electronic systems are intrinsically susceptible, has not yet been experimentally explored. Here, we demonstrate the emergence and profound consequences of the pseudoscalar potential in a multilayer graphene system. Atomic relaxation reconstructs the system into large Bernalstacked domains separated by one-dimensional rhombohedral channels. Within this inter-dimensional system, we achieve the first direct atomic-scale imaging of distinct lattice deformations in graphene under strain. In rhombohedral channels, we observe that the tensile lattice deformation induces giant pseudo-electric fields, producing ultra-flat dispersion with divergent density of states and a large energy gap. This results in enhanced electronic correlations, leading to flat-band instabilities and striped modulation in the density of states that would otherwise require strong external displacement fields. Moreover, the gap opened by the pseudoscalar potential protects topological boundary states that persist in the absence of applied fields. Our results provide a comprehensive atomic-scale characterization of graphene elasticity and direct spectroscopic evidence for the nontrivial pseudo-electric field arising from lattice distortion. More broadly, it provides an intrinsic and tunable pathway to new quantum states

</details>


### [148] [Optical writing of Dzyaloshinskii Néel-type skyrmions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20878)
*Dinar Khusyainov,Lukas Körber,Sheng Li,Rein Liefferink,Thibaud Denneulin,András Kovács,Fabian Kammerbauer,Robert Frömter,Mathias Kläui,Dmytro Afanasiev,Alexey Kimel,Johan H. Mentink,Rafal E. Dunin-Borkowski,Theo Rasing*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究证明在超薄 Co/Pt 薄膜中，利用皮秒圆偏振激光脉冲序列可写入仅由 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用（Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction, DMI）稳定的 Néel 型、手性固定的磁斯格明子（magnetic skyrmion），并实现了跨纳米至微米尺度的自旋结构重构与手性判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超短激光脉冲为写入纳米尺度拓扑磁结构提供了途径，但受磁偶极相互作用（magnetic dipolar interaction）驱动的斯格明子生成通常保持相反手性（chirality）的简并，难以实现位置、类型尤其是手性的可控光学写入。

Method: 在超薄 Co/Pt 薄膜上施加皮秒圆偏振激光脉冲序列进行写入；结合洛伦兹透射电子显微镜（Lorentz transmission electron microscopy）与磁光显微镜（magneto-optical microscopy）在纳米至微米多尺度重构内部自旋结构；并通过非对称斯格明子膨胀测量（asymmetric skyrmion-expansion measurements）确定 DMI 的符号与大小及所写入的手性。

Result: 激光脉冲序列可写入 Néel 型、手性固定的斯格明子，其稳定机制仅来自 DMI 而非偶极相互作用；多尺度成像全面重构了其内部自旋结构，非对称膨胀测量给出了 DMI 的符号与量值并识别出写入的手性。

Conclusion: 研究将确定性光学写入与斯格明子可控自旋结构相联系，为光自旋电子学（opto-spintronic）应用中拓扑织构（topological texture）的光学调控提供了依据。

Abstract: Ultrashort laser pulses offer a route to write nanoscale topological magnetic textures for information processing. Magnetic skyrmions are prototypical examples, yet optically generating them with controlled position, type, and, in particular, chirality remains challenging. This challenge arises because magnetic dipolar interactions, which often drive skyrmion formation, preserve the degeneracy between opposite chiralities, leaving no uniquely selected spin configuration. Here, we show that in an ultrathin Co/Pt film, sequences of picosecond circularly polarized laser pulses can write Néel-type, fixed-chirality skyrmions stabilized solely by the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction. Combining Lorentz transmission electron microscopy and magneto-optical microscopy, we comprehensively reconstruct their internal spin structure across multiple length scales, ranging from nanometres to micrometers. Using asymmetric skyrmion-expansion measurements, we determined the sign and magnitude of the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction and identified the written chirality. Our findings connect deterministic optical writing with the controlled spin structure of skyrmions, advancing optical control of topological textures for opto-spintronic applications.

</details>


### [149] [Altermagnetism in moiré quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20952)
*Zi-Ming Wang,Rui Chen,Dong-Hui Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出基于对称性的方案，通过扭转共线磁性层实现准晶交错磁（quasicrystalline altermagnetism），并具体识别出30°扭转蜂窝双层体系中的i波（i-wave）交错磁相与45°扭转双层中的镜面驱动l波（l-wave）相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁（altermagnetism）是零净磁化且具动量依赖自旋极化的共线补偿磁相；将其推广至准晶（quasicrystal）可突破周期晶体限制，借助非晶体学对称性获得高阶自旋极化图样，但实现途径此前不明确。

Method: 采用基于对称性的方法（symmetry-based approach），以扭转（twist engineering）共线磁性层为手段构造准晶交错磁相，并通过平面波投影的自旋分辨谱函数（spin-resolved spectral functions）与自旋分辨电导（spin-resolved conductances）刻画其磁相图样。

Result: 30°扭转蜂窝晶格双层（honeycomb lattice bilayers）形成十二重准晶（dodecagonal quasicrystal），支持i波交错磁相；45°扭转双层保持四重旋转对称性，支持具有八条节线（nodal lines）的镜面驱动l波相。

Conclusion: 该工作为通过扭转工程（twist engineering）获得高角阶准晶交错磁提供了系统性途径。

Abstract: Altermagnetism is a collinear compensated magnetic phase with zero net magnetization and momentum-dependent spin polarization. Quasicrystalline altermagnetism extends altermagnetism beyond periodic crystals, with noncrystallographic symmetries allowing high-order spin polarization patterns. Here, we propose a symmetry-based approach to realizing quasicrystalline altermagnetism by twisting collinear magnetic layers. Specifically, we demonstrate that the $30^{\circ}$-twisted honeycomb lattice bilayers form the dodecagonal quasicrystals supporting an $i$-wave altermagnetic phase. We further find that $45^{\circ}$-twisted bilayers preserving fourfold rotational symmetry support a mirror-driven $l$-wave phase with eight nodal lines. We characterize these altermagnetic patterns through plane-wave-projected spin-resolved spectral functions and spin-resolved conductances. Our work provides a systematic route to high-angular-order quasicrystalline altermagnetism through twist engineering.

</details>


### [150] [A tunable multi-gated platform for superlattice engineering in bilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20998)
*Saisab Bhowmik,Satyarth Srivastav,Aditya Mehta,Robin Bajaj,Suvronil Datta,Rohit S Bhandari,Nikhlesh S. Mehta,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Manish Jain,Hridis K. Pal,U. Chandni*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究采用置于带三角孔阵列图案化石墨栅极上的高质量Bernal双层石墨烯，构建可通过三个独立静电栅极调控的“栅极工程”超晶格，在电荷中性点附近观察到微带（miniband）形成所对应的绝缘态与输运激活能隙，并实现无需机械扭转的静电可重构能带设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长周期势（periodic potential）可产生低能电子微带，但小角度扭转范德华异质结构（twisted van der Waals heterostructures）受扭转角不均匀、应变与无序限制，关联相难以稳定实现，因此需要一种更可控的超晶格实现途径。

Method: 将高质量Bernal双层石墨烯置于具有三角孔阵列（triangular array of holes）的图案化石墨栅极（patterned graphite gate）上，形成可由三个独立静电栅极（electrostatic gates）调控的“栅极工程”（gate-engineered）超晶格，并进行温度依赖输运与霍尔（Hall）测量。

Result: 在电荷中性点（charge neutrality point）附近观察到微带形成特征的绝缘态；温度依赖输运给出与理论一致的激活能隙（activated gaps），霍尔测量显示载流子类型反转（charge-carrier inversion）；超晶格可通过不同栅极组合静电重构。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一条无需机械扭转即可灵活设计能带结构（band-structure design）的新途径，并有望推广至天然材料难以实现的复杂周期结构。

Abstract: Periodic potentials with length scales much larger than crystal lattice constants can generate low-energy electronic minibands. While small-angle twisted van der Waals heterostructures have been widely used to realize such physics, correlated phases are often limited by twist-angle inhomogeneity, strain, and disorder. Here, we investigate high-quality Bernal bilayer graphene placed on a patterned graphite gate, featuring a triangular array of holes, forming a new class of "gate-engineered" superlattices tunable through three independent electrostatic gates. Near the charge neutrality point, we observe insulating states characteristic of miniband formation. Temperature-dependent transport reveals activated gaps consistent with theory, and Hall measurements indicate charge-carrier inversion. Notably, the superlattice can be reconfigured electrostatically using various gate combinations, enabling flexible band-structure design without mechanical twisting. The results highlight a new route to band engineering, potentially extendable to complex periodic structures that are untenable in natural materials.

</details>


### [151] [Large pseudo-orbital magnetic moment of magnons in CrI$_3$ nanotubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21066)
*David Sanz,J. Fernández-Rossier*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过线性自旋波理论证明，轴向磁场使CrI₃纳米管的径向磁序倾斜为锥形磁序，并为磁振子引入Aharonov-Bohm通量，赋予其可探测的赝轨道磁矩，可作为径向与锥形磁序的实验判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新发现的CrI₃纳米管因二维母体材料强的面外磁各向异性，预期存在异常的径向磁序态；但这类奇特磁态难以直接探测，因此需要寻找可用于实验观测的独特特征。

Method: 采用线性自旋波理论（linear spin-wave theory），分析轴向磁场下CrI₃纳米管中径向磁序向锥形磁序的转变及其对磁振子（magnon）的影响。

Result: 轴向磁场将径向相倾斜为贯穿管体的锥形相，对磁振子等效为Aharonov-Bohm通量；该通量赋予磁振子赝轨道磁矩（pseudo-orbital magnetic moment），其大小约六个玻尔磁子（Bohr magnetons），为常规磁振子自旋磁矩的三倍。

Conclusion: 该赝轨道磁矩可通过磁振子光谱学（magnon spectroscopy）探测，从而为CrI₃纳米管中的径向与锥形磁序提供确凿的实验证据（smoking gun）。

Abstract: Recently discovered CrI$_3$ nanotubes are expected to host unusual radial magnetic order states, on account of the strong out-of-plane magnetic anisotropy of the two-dimensional parent material. The detection of these peculiar magnetic states is challenging, and motivates the quest for unique features that can be probed experimentally. Using linear spin-wave theory, we show that an axial magnetic field tilts the radial phase into a conical one that threads the tube with an Aharonov-Bohm flux for magnons. This flux endows magnons with a pseudo-orbital magnetic moment, reaching approximately six Bohr magnetons, three times conventional magnon spin moment, and therefore detectable through magnon spectroscopy, providing a smoking gun for the radial and conical magnetic order in CrI$_3$ nanotubes.

</details>


### [152] [Interaction Landscapes of Structurally Distorted Toroidal Classical Spin Textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21194)
*Henry Pommer,Arkamita Bandyopadhyay,Jamal Berakdar*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文建立局域磁矩构成环形织构的经典离散静磁模型，通过椭圆、偏斜、交替与倾斜等结构变形，厘清几何变形与磁变形对环矩及环形结构间偶极-偶极相互作用的影响，并以分子自旋环形体系的第一性原理计算作定性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于分离几何变形与磁变形各自的作用，并明确环矩（toroidal moment）仅用于刻画集体织构，而非引入额外的基本相互作用项，从而澄清环序（toroidal order）在分子磁体与纳米磁性体系中的角色。

Method: 方法为构建经典离散静磁模型（classical discrete magnetostatic model），将磁矩表示为静态电流环（static current loops），系统考察椭圆、偏斜、交替与倾斜等结构变形下的磁偶极-偶极相互作用（magnetic dipole-dipole interaction），并以分子自旋环形体系的第一性原理（first-principles）计算作概念验证性对比。

Result: 所得相互作用景观（interaction landscapes）呈现特征性对称模式与各向异性（anisotropies），且对 underlying 变形高度敏感；第一性原理计算显示分子自旋环形体系具有定性相似的取向依赖行为。

Conclusion: 作者指出该相互作用在微观上仍是局域磁矩间的常规成对磁偶极-偶极相互作用，环矩仅表征集体织构；该模型为探索分子磁体与相关纳米磁性体系中自旋环形织构的取向依赖相互作用提供了平台。

Abstract: We develop a classical discrete magnetostatic model of localized magnetic moments arranged in a toroidal texture, where the moments are represented by static current loops and study the behavior of the magnetic dipole-dipole interaction under several types of structural deformations. The purpose is to disentangle effects due to geometric and magnetic deformations, including elliptical, lopsided, alternating, and tilted configurations. In particular, we investigate how distortions modify the toroidal moment and the interaction energy between pairs of toroidal structures. The resulting interaction landscapes exhibit characteristic symmetry patterns and anisotropies that are highly sensitive to the underlying deformation. As a separate proof-of-concept comparison, first-principles calculations on a molecular spin-toroidal system reveal qualitatively similar orientation-dependent behavior. The calculated interaction is microscopically the conventional pairwise magnetic dipole-dipole interaction between localized moments arranged in a spin-toroidal texture; the toroidal moment characterizes the collective texture and should not be interpreted as introducing an additional elementary interaction term. Our results provide a platform for exploring orientation-dependent interactions of spin-toroidal textures in molecular magnets and related nanoscale magnetic systems that are essential to understand the role of toroidal order.

</details>


### [153] [Low-energy states of a Su-Schrieffer-Heeger chain connected perpendicularly to a metallic lead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21227)
*A. A. Aligia*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文将孤立Su-Schrieffer-Heeger链（SSHC）端态的有限尺寸劈裂纳入分析，解析给出低能区零温电导与谱密度表达式；发现化学势与半满SSHC对齐时电导达最大值（等于无SSHC时引线的电导），偏移量为端态劈裂一半时电导为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Martínez等）研究了单端垂直连接金属引线的拓扑SSHC，但未计入孤立SSHC端态的有限尺寸劈裂；本文旨在扩展该结果，使端态劈裂对输运的描述更完整。

Method: 在紧束缚链（tight-binding chain）描述的金属引线耦合SSHC模型基础上，解析且高精度地确定孤立SSHC端态的有限尺寸劈裂，并推导低能区（low-energy regime）零温电导及相关谱密度（spectral densities）的简洁解析表达式。

Result: 当化学势（chemical potential）与半满（half-filled）SSHC的化学势一致时，电导达到最大值，且等于无SSHC时引线的电导；当化学势偏移量等于端态劈裂的一半时，电导为零。

Conclusion: 端态有限尺寸劈裂在低能输运中起关键作用，其解析刻画可给出电导的极大与零消条件，为SSHC—金属引线耦合体系的输运调控提供简洁判据。

Abstract: Recently, Su-Schrieffer-Heeger chains (SSHCs) coupled to metallic leads have attracted attention. In particular Martínez {\it et al.} [Phys. Rev. Res. {\bf 8}, 043118 (2026)] studied a topological SSHC connected at one of its ends perpendicularly to a metallic lead modeled by a tight-binding chain. Here, we extend their results by incorporating the finite-size splitting of the end modes of the isolated SSHC, which are determined analytically with high accuracy. We also derive simple analytical expressions, valid in the low-energy regime, for the zero-temperature conductance through the lead and the relevant spectral densities. We find that, when the chemical potential coincides with that of the half-filled SSHC, the conductance reaches its maximum value, equal to that of the lead in the absence of the SSHC. By contrast, the conductance vanishes when the chemical potential is shifted by an amount equal to half the end-mode splitting.

</details>


### [154] [Vortex states in submicron titanomagnetite particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21354)
*Olga N. Serebryakova,Nikolai A. Usov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 采用数值模拟研究钛磁铁矿（titanomagnetite）颗粒的磁学性质，揭示涡旋微磁态（vortex micromagnetic states）随钛含量、长径比与粒径的变化，并给出单畴粒径判据与剩磁规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钛磁铁矿颗粒的磁学性质受钛浓度、颗粒形状与尺寸共同影响，需明确其微磁状态与单畴尺寸界限，以理解稀释集合体的剩磁行为。

Method: 以数值模拟（numerical simulation）对Fe(3-x)TixO4（0.4 < x < 0.6）、半轴比0.5–1.5、横向直径D = 100–480 nm的椭球状颗粒进行计算，并利用均匀态与涡旋态的能量图（energy diagrams）确定单畴粒径。

Result: 颗粒中可出现多种涡旋微磁态，取决于钛浓度、长径比与粒径；计算了不同钛含量稀释集合体的剩磁化（remanent magnetization）随横向直径D的变化，亚微米颗粒（D ~ 500 nm）时归一化剩磁M/Ms < 0.01。

Conclusion: 钛磁铁矿颗粒的微磁状态与单畴尺寸由成分与形貌共同调控，亚微米尺寸下集合体剩磁显著降低，可用作磁学性质的预测依据。

Abstract: Numerical simulation is used to study the magnetic properties of spheroidal titanomagnetite particles Fe(3-x)TixO4 (0.4 < x < 0.6) with semi-axes ratio ranging from 0.5 to 1.5 and transverse diameters D = 100 - 480 nm. It is shown that various vortex micromagnetic states can emerge in the titanomagnetite particles depending on the titanium concentration, aspect ratio and particle diameter. The single-domain particle diameters are determined using the energy diagrams for stationary uniform and vortex states. The remanent magnetization of dilute assemblies of titanomagnetite particles with varying titanium content are calculated as a function of the transverse particle diameter D. It is demonstrated that for submicron-sized titanomagnetite particles, D ~ 500 nm, the normalized remanent magnetization of the assembly reaches very low values, M/Ms < 0.01, where Ms is the saturation magnetization of the particles.

</details>


### [155] [Energy spectrum of trions in semiconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21475)
*Pavel A. Belov,Lucas Happ,Stefan Scheel,Evgeny A. Yarevsky*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 采用有限元法与高斯展开法（Gaussian expansion method, GEM）两种数值方法计算体相半导体中三子（trion）能级，覆盖宽广空穴-电子质量比范围，获得高精度结果并确认多数半导体化合物仅存在一个零总角动量三子态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体相半导体中三子能级的精确数值数据仍不充分，且需验证现有三体计算代码的准确性，明确三子束缚态随质量比的依赖关系及其与低维简化模型的对应性。

Method: 在总角动量表象下构造薛定谔方程（Schrödinger equation）的有限元解；同时对波函数的三个 Faddeev 分量分别应用高斯展开法（GEM），针对总角动量 J=0 与 J=1 计算宽范围空穴-电子质量比下的三子能量。

Result: 能量随质量比的变化趋势与一维量子线（1D quantum wire）简化模型结果定性一致；与其他公开三体代码及分子氢离子振动能级等原子类比数据吻合良好；GEM 相较以往计算显著提升了三子能量精度。

Conclusion: 计算结果确认所用数值方法可靠，并表明对大多数半导体化合物而言，仅存在一个总角动量为零的三子态。

Abstract: Energy levels of trions in a bulk semiconductor crystal are calculated by two different numerical methods. On the one hand, we construct the finite-element solution of the Schrödinger equation in the total angular momentum representation. On the other hand, we use the Gaussian expansion method (GEM), applied to each of the three Faddeev components of the wave function. The results for the total angular momenta $J=0$ and $J=1$ of the trion were obtained for a wide range of the hole-to-electron mass ratios covering most existing semiconductor compounds. The dependencies of the energies on the mass ratio for trions in a bulk crystal qualitatively agree with the results for a simplified model of a trion in a 1D quantum wire, for which the three-body energies can be calculated with arbitrary precision as well as easily approximated using Born-Oppenheimer potential-energy curves. A comparison of our results with data from other publicly available three-body codes as well as with the energies of well-known atomic counterparts, in particular, with vibrational energy levels of the molecular hydrogen ion, shows a good agreement and confirms the accuracy of the numerical data. We found that GEM significantly improves the precision of the obtained trion energies compared to previous calculations. Our calculations demonstrate that, for most semiconductor compounds, only one trion state with zero total angular momentum exists.

</details>


### [156] [Electrostatic and magnetic control of the anomalous Josephson phase in ferromagnetic hybrid junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21593)
*D. Rana,O. S. Kurapati,N. Vanjarapu,J. Zhao,J. B. F. Wolters,Y. Liu,P. Krogstrup,A. L. R. Manesco,S. D. Escribano,E. Prada,S. Vaitiekėnas*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文报道了InAs纳米线／外延铁磁EuS／超导Al壳层约瑟夫森结（Josephson junction）的相位敏感测量，观察到随轴向磁场扫描的连续、迟滞的反常相位移动（anomalous phase shift），零磁场下存在剩磁相位偏置（remanent phase bias），并受栅压调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究铁磁—超导—半导体复合约瑟夫森结中反常约瑟夫森相位（anomalous Josephson phase）的物理起源，特别是其与纳米线横向电子结构及Rashba自旋轨道耦合（Rashba spin-orbit coupling）的关系。

Method: 采用超导干涉仪（superconducting interferometer）对InAs纳米线约瑟夫森结进行相位敏感测量，并结合静电门控（electrostatic gating）调节纳米线横向电子结构；同时利用自洽静电模拟（self-consistent electrostatic simulations）分析有效Rashba自旋轨道耦合。

Result: 在轴向磁场扫描下观察到连续、迟滞的反常相位移动；零外加磁场时仍存在与磁历史相关的剩磁相位偏置；门压强烈改变相位响应幅度，且自洽静电模拟显示有效Rashba自旋轨道耦合随门压的变化与测得的相位响应一致。

Conclusion: 结果表明Rashba自旋轨道耦合在反常约瑟夫森相位的静电调控（electrostatic control）中起核心作用，相位响应对纳米线横向电子结构具有敏感性。

Abstract: We report phase-sensitive measurements of Josephson junctions formed in InAs nanowires with epitaxial ferromagnetic EuS and superconducting Al shells. Using a superconducting interferometer, we observe a continuous, hysteretic anomalous phase shift under axial magnetic-field sweeps. A remanent phase bias persists at zero applied field and depends on magnetic history. Electrostatic gating strongly modifies the magnitude of the phase response, revealing its sensitivity to the transverse electronic structure of the nanowire. Self-consistent electrostatic simulations show that the effective Rashba spin-orbit coupling follows the measured gate-dependent phase response, suggesting its central role in the electrostatic control of the anomalous Josephson phase.

</details>


### [157] [Double quantum dots as dynamical impurities in one-dimensional transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21609)
*Yang-Zhi Chou*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文研究紧束缚链与双量子点（double quantum dots）电容耦合时的单电子散射（single-electron scattering），双量子点作为可电调控能级劈裂（level splitting）的动力学二能级系统（two-level system）杂质。单杂质情形下给出精确解并揭示类Feshbach完全弹性反射（Feshbach-like perfect elastic reflection）；多杂质强反射链中透射在中间平均能级劈裂处出现峰值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于探究可调控二能级杂质对相干输运的影响机制，并检验量子点阵列（quantum-dot arrays）中栅控透射测量能否验证相关理论预言。

Method: 采用紧束缚链（tight-binding chain）模型，链与双量子点电容耦合，杂质具有电可调的能级劈裂；对单杂质情形精确求解散射问题，并对含多个杂质、强反射的链进行透射分析。

Result: 单杂质时出现类Feshbach完美弹性反射，其共振条件可区分初始二能级态。强反射链含多个杂质时，透射在中间平均能级劈裂处达到峰值：小劈裂下与杂质交换能量削弱背散射路径间的相长干涉，导致透射初始上升；激发通道关闭后虚跃迁（virtual transitions）增强反射，使透射低于纯弹性值T_0；大劈裂下虚过程减弱，透射趋近T_0。

Conclusion: 二能级杂质的内态与虚跃迁过程显著调控电子透射，透射随能级劈裂呈非单调行为；作者提出以量子点阵列中的栅控透射测量检验这些预言。

Abstract: We study single-electron scattering in a tight-binding chain capacitively coupled to double quantum dots, which act as dynamical two-level-system impurities with electrically tunable level splittings. For a single impurity, we solve the scattering problem exactly and show Feshbach-like perfect elastic reflection, whose resonant condition distinguishes the initial two-level state. For several impurities in a strongly reflecting chain, transmission peaks at an intermediate mean level splitting. At small splittings, energy exchange with the impurities can weaken constructive interference between backscattered paths, consistent with the initial rise in transmission. As excitation channels close, virtual transitions can enhance reflection and drive transmission below its purely elastic value $T_0$. At larger splittings, these virtual processes weaken and transmission approaches $T_0$. We discuss how gate-controlled transmission measurements in quantum-dot arrays could test these predictions.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Quantum Cooling by Classical Shadows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18834)
*Xuepeng Wang,Debanjan Chowdhury*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文提出“shadow chain”算法：利用量子多体系统单次随机快照（randomized snapshot）即可平均意义上沿冷却方向驱动量子态，从而在量子计算机上实现虚时演化（imaginary-time evolution）式的基态冷却。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚时演化是量子模拟中获取多体哈密顿量基态的核心手段，但其非幺正（non-unitary）性质使其无法直接在量子计算机上实现；现有方案需从内在量子动力学之外引入额外结构（如多拷贝参数估计或递归编译）来恢复该过程，代价较高。

Method: shadow chain 算法用随机测量（randomized measurements）将量子态坍缩为其经典记录（classical record），并据此确定下一步的酉更新；跨步快照构成一条轨迹，该轨迹是辅助场路径积分（auxiliary-field path integral）的量子可执行采样，且轨迹间涨落随系统尺寸指数抑制，随机性由玻恩测量规则（Born measurement rule）内禀提供，无需外部接受-拒绝（accept-reject）步骤。

Result: 所得算法无需拟设（ansatz-free），在连续极限外推下是精确的；其指数代价仅随系统尺寸而非温度增长。

Conclusion: 该工作表明单次随机快照足以实现平均意义下的冷却方向引导，为量子计算机上的虚时演化与基态制备提供了一条内禀随机、无需拟设且连续极限精确的新路径。

Abstract: Cooling to ground states of many-body Hamiltonians by imaginary-time evolution is central to quantum simulation, but its non-unitary aspect prevents direct implementation on a quantum computer. Existing routes restore it by importing structure from outside the intrinsic quantum dynamics, e.g. by parameter estimation on many copies or recursive compilation. Here, we show that a single randomized snapshot of the quantum many-body system is sufficient to steer the state along the cooling direction on average. Our algorithm, shadow chain, uses randomized measurements to collapse the state to its classical record, which fixes the next unitary update. Snapshots across steps form a trajectory that is a quantum-executable sample of an auxiliary-field path integral; fluctuations among trajectories are exponentially suppressed in system size. With the randomness supplied intrinsically by the Born measurement rule, the trajectories sample themselves rather than going through external accept-reject steps. The resulting algorithm is ansatz-free and exact upon extrapolation to the continuous limit, with the exponential cost confined only to system size rather than temperature.

</details>


### [159] [Gapless Nature of Spin Liquid in the $S=1/2$ Kagome Heisenberg Antiferromagnet with Neural-Network Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18840)
*Andreas Raikos,Alessandro Sinibaldi,Riccardo Rende,Giuseppe Carleo,Federico Becca,Luciano Loris Viteritti*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作采用不预设自旋液体结构的神经网络量子态（neural-network quantum state）直接计算kagome海森堡反铁磁体的三重态能隙，结果显示磁序参量与三重态能隙在热力学极限下均外推为零，支持无能隙U(1)狄拉克自旋液体（U(1) Dirac spin liquid）基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋1/2的kagome海森堡反铁磁体（kagome Heisenberg antiferromagnet）基态究竟是有能隙的Z2自旋液体（Z2 spin liquid）还是无能隙的U(1)狄拉克自旋液体，是阻挫磁性（frustrated magnetism）领域的核心未解问题，传统数值方法难以定论。

Method: 采用不预设任何特定自旋液体结构的神经网络量子态，具体为时间反演对称的基于Transformer的拟设（time-reversal symmetric transformer-based ansatz），在周期性边界条件、最大达768格点的二维格点上计算变分能量，并对磁序参量与三重态能隙进行有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）分析。

Result: 在二维周期性边界格点上取得当前最优的变分能量（state-of-the-art variational energies），并估计热力学极限下每格点能量为−0.43873(12)；磁序参量与三重态激发能隙的有限尺寸标度均外推至零。

Conclusion: 结果为无能隙自旋液体基态提供了独立且直接的证据，且与U(1)狄拉克自旋液体相符；同时表明神经网络量子态能够解决传统数值方法仍具挑战的阻挫量子磁体（frustrated quantum magnets）基态性质问题。

Abstract: Whether the spin-$1/2$ kagome Heisenberg antiferromagnet hosts a gapped $\mathbb{Z}_2$ or a gapless $U(1)$ Dirac spin liquid remains a central open question in frustrated magnetism. Here, we address this question by directly computing the triplet gap with a neural-network quantum state that does not presuppose any specific spin-liquid structure. Applying a time-reversal symmetric transformer-based ansatz to clusters of up to $768$ sites, we obtain state-of-the-art variational energies on two-dimensional lattices with periodic boundary conditions and estimate an energy per site of $-0.43873(12)$ in the thermodynamic limit. Finite-size scaling of the magnetic order parameter and triplet excitation gap shows that both extrapolate to zero in the thermodynamic limit. Together, these results provide independent and direct evidence of a gapless spin-liquid ground state, consistent with the $U(1)$ Dirac spin liquid. More broadly, they demonstrate that neural-network quantum states can resolve ground-state properties of frustrated quantum magnets that remain challenging for conventional numerical methods.

</details>


### [160] [Measurement of Magnonic Quantum Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18897)
*S. Kim,M. Stone,S. E. Nagler,A. D. Christianson,Jiabin Yu,S. Okamoto,H. Miao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出利用非弹性中子散射（inelastic neutron scattering, INS）截面直接测量铁磁体中磁振子（magnon）旋量波函数的几何参数，从而在无需了解微观自旋-自旋相互作用细节的情况下提取量子几何张量（quantum geometric tensor）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子几何（quantum geometry）在量子霍尔效应（quantum Hall effects）与平带超导（flat-band superconductivity）等宏观量子现象中起基础作用，但其编码于波函数而非能谱中，难以直接实验表征；对量子材料中的准粒子而言尤为突出，其波函数通常只能由有效低能模型推断而非直接测量。

Method: 建立一个表征磁振子（magnon，磁有序态中普遍存在的玻色型准粒子）量子几何的实验框架，论证INS截面可测量铁磁体旋量波函数的几何参数，并通过追踪能量与动量依赖的INS强度提取量子几何张量。

Result: 研究表明INS截面与铁磁体旋量波函数的几何参数直接相关，能量-动量分辨的INS强度可用于提取量子几何张量，且无需详细的微观自旋-自旋相互作用知识。

Conclusion: 磁振子被确立为实验探测量子几何的理想平台，并为探索几何驱动的磁振子输运（magnon transport）与磁性材料中的量子费舍尔信息（quantum Fisher information）开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Quantum geometry plays a fundamental role in macroscopic quantum phenomena, including quantum Hall effects and flat-band superconductivity. However, since quantum geometry is encoded in wavefunctions rather than energy spectra, direct experimental characterization remains a formidable challenge. This challenge is particularly acute for quasiparticles in quantum materials, whose wavefunctions are generally inferred from effective low-energy models rather than measured directly. Here, we establish an experimental framework for characterizing the quantum geometry of magnons, a ubiquitous Bosonic quasiparticle of magnetically ordered states. We show that the inelastic neutron scattering (INS) cross-section measures geometric parameters of the spinor wavefunctions of ferromagnets. By tracking the energy- and momentum-dependent INS intensity, we extract the quantum geometric tensor without requiring detailed knowledge of microscopic spin-spin interactions. Our results establish magnons as an ideal arena for experimentally probing quantum geometry and open new avenues for exploring geometry-driven magnon transport and quantum Fisher information in magnetic materials.

</details>


### [161] [From RuP$_3$SiO$_{11}$ to Ideal Geometry: Parallel-Face Octahedra for Kitaev-Dominated Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18920)
*Do Hoon Kiem,Satoshi Okamoto,Tom Berlijn*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算重新评估了RuP₃SiO₁₁的磁相互作用，发现Heisenberg交换应为铁磁而非实验拟合的反铁磁，并识别出此前未纳入的显著面外反铁磁交换；同时将该研究扩展至八种同类取代类似物，揭示该类材料的可调控性，并提出“平行面八面体构型”作为通向Kitaev极限的新结构路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RuP₃SiO₁₁近期被中子散射识别为Kitaev自旋液体（Kitaev spin liquid）候选材料，需要评估其与Kitaev极限（Kitaev limit）的接近程度，并厘清此前分析中磁相互作用的描述是否准确完整。

Method: 采用从头算（ab initio）方法计算RuP₃SiO₁₁的磁相互作用，并与实验拟合结果对比；将研究扩展至MT₃QO₁₁家族（M = Ru, Os；T = P, As；Q = Si, Ge）的八种取代类似物（substitutional analogs）以考察相互作用的变化；进一步比较理想化几何构型，分析八面体（octahedral）排列方式对磁相互作用的影响。

Result: Kitaev交换与副对角交换（off-diagonal exchange）的计算值与实验结果吻合良好，但Heisenberg交换被计算为铁磁（ferromagnetic），与实验拟合的反铁磁（antiferromagnetic）结论相反；此外还发现了一个此前分析未捕捉到的、不可忽略的面外反铁磁交换。八种取代类似物表现出磁相互作用的显著变化，说明该类材料具有良好的可调控性（tunability）；平行面（parallel-face）八面体构型对应Kitaev主导的相互作用区间。

Conclusion: RuP₃SiO₁₁的磁相互作用描述需修正，尤其是Heisenberg交换的符号与新增的面外交换项；MT₃QO₁₁家族在磁相互作用上具有较大调控空间；平行面八面体构型可导致Kitaev主导区域，为逼近Kitaev极限提供了一条新的结构设计途径。

Abstract: RuP$_3$SiO$_{11}$ has recently been identified as a Kitaev spin liquid candidate via neutron scattering. To assess its proximity to the Kitaev limit, we perform \textit{ab initio} calculations of magnetic interactions, finding good agreement for the Kitaev and off-diagonal exchanges, while the Heisenberg exchange is ferromagnetic rather than the experimentally fitted antiferromagnetic one. We also identify a sizable antiferromagnetic out-of-plane exchange not captured in previous analyses. Extending the study to eight substitutional analogs in the $MT_3Q$O$_{11}$ family ($M$ = Ru, Os; $T$ = P, As; $Q$= Si, Ge), we observe strong variations in magnetic interactions, highlighting the tunability of this materials class. Finally, inspired by RuP$_3$SiO$_{11}$, which exhibits a near parallel-face octahedral arrangement, we compare idealized geometries and show that the parallel-face configuration yields a Kitaev-dominated regime, suggesting a new structural route to the Kitaev limit.

</details>


### [162] [Finite-time quenches across first-, second-, and infinite-order transitions in XXZ spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19022)
*Luiz Felipe Santos Dittberner,Luigi Berber,Maria C. O. Aguiar,Krissia Zawadzki,Helena Bragança*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文研究平衡态量子相变（quantum phase transitions）如何在量子淬火（quantum quench）驱动的小型非平衡系统中显现，使用自旋-1/2与自旋-1 XXZ链（XXZ chains）中不同阶数的相变进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子模拟器（quantum simulators）可实现的系统尺寸有限，且相变的阶数、对称性与扫描时间如何影响非平衡动力学尚不清楚，需系统研究不同阶数相变在有限尺度非平衡驱动下的表现。

Method: 对各向异性（anisotropy）进行线性扫描（linear ramps），考察一阶、二阶与无限阶（Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless, BKT）相变，分析时间演化态在瞬时本征基（instantaneous eigenbasis）下的分解。

Result: 相变阶数决定动力学：BKT相变中中等缓慢扫描即可接近基态（绝热演化，adiabatic evolution），而二阶Haldane-Néel相变需约三倍慢的扫描达到同一区域；一阶相变因磁化（magnetization）守恒形成铁磁畴（ferromagnetic domains），慢扫描为单一畴壁、快扫描为多畴壁。

Conclusion: 相变后不久，态表现为畴壁态之间的相干拍（coherent beating），该行为可由近期提出的量子态纹理（quantum-state texture）描述，说明相变阶数与小系统非平衡动力学之间存在明确对应关系。

Abstract: We investigate how equilibrium quantum phase transitions manifest themselves in small systems driven out of equilibrium by quantum quenches. We consider linear ramps of the anisotropy in spin-1/2 and spin-1 XXZ chains, which host transitions of first, second, and infinite (Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless, BKT) order. We consider system sizes available in current quantum simulators and investigate how the order of the transition, the symmetries, and the ramp duration affect the decomposition of the time-evolved state into the instantaneous eigenbasis. The order of the transition determines the dynamics: across the BKT transition, moderately slow ramps already keep the system close to its ground state, corresponding to an adiabatic evolution, whereas for the second-order Haldane-Néel transition a ramp roughly three times slower is required to reach the same regime. Across the first-order transition, since the dynamics conserves the magnetization of the initial state, we observe the formation of ferromagnetic domains, separated by a single domain wall for slow ramps and by multiple walls for faster ones. Interestingly, shortly after the transition, the state evolves as a coherent beating between domain-wall states, a behavior captured by the recently proposed quantum-state texture.

</details>


### [163] [Quasi-particle sector hybridization and the onset of chaos in spin ladders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19053)
*Arnav Pushkar,Abhik Kumar Saha*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究自旋-1/2 XXZ梯子（XXZ ladder）中准粒子（triplon）数目如何影响量子混沌（quantum chaos）的出现，发现谱混沌的出现并不要求三子（triplon）数扇区完全杂化（hybridization）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准粒子数目对相互作用多体系统（interacting many-body systems）中量子混沌涌现的影响是一个基本问题，本文旨在考察强梯耦合（strong-rung）区域中本征态按准粒子数扇区近似组织的情形下该问题的具体表现。

Method: 采用谱统计（spectral statistics）、基于三子（triplon）的可观测量以及冯·诺依曼纠缠熵（von Neumann entanglement entropy），刻画随腿间耦合（leg coupling）增强时三子数扇区（triplon-number sectors）的微观杂化过程。

Result: 趋向高斯正交系综（Gaussian orthogonal ensemble）统计的转变点随系统尺寸增大移向更弱耦合，而三子扇区杂化的特征转变尺度对尺寸依赖明显更弱；同时本征态间的逐态涨落表现出与本征态热化假说（eigenstate thermalization hypothesis）一致的标度行为。

Conclusion: 即使强梯结构（strong-rung structure）的残余仍然存在，谱混沌（spectral chaos）也可以出现，表明其并不必然需要扇区的完全杂化。

Abstract: How quasiparticle numbers influence the emergence of quantum chaos is a fundamental question in interacting many-body systems. We investigate this interplay in a spin-$1/2$ XXZ ladder, where the strong-rung regime supports an approximate organization of many-body eigenstates into quasi-particle(triplon) number sectors. Using spectral statistics, triplon-based observables, and von Neumann entanglement entropy, we characterize the microscopic hybridization of triplon-number sectors as the leg coupling increases. We find that the crossover toward Gaussian orthogonal ensemble statistics shifts to weaker couplings with increasing system size, whereas the characteristic crossover scale associated with triplon-sector hybridization exhibits a much weaker size dependence. Furthermore, eigenstate-to-eigenstate fluctuations exhibit scaling consistent with the eigenstate thermalization hypothesis even while remnants of the strong-rung structure persist, suggesting that spectral chaos does not necessarily require complete sector hybridization.

</details>


### [164] [Optical response of quantum metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19195)
*Erik van Heumen,Martin Dressel,Simone Fratini*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文指出在奇异金属（strange metal）中电导不能再用弛豫速率描述，而应由快速衰减的电流涨落（current fluctuations）结合量子细致平衡（quantum detailed balance）产生普朗克耗散（Planckian dissipation）；以电流-电流关联函数（current-current correlation function）分析铜氧化物（cuprate）数据可区分经典与量子金属行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有关于强关联量子系统输运的理论与唯象分析多假设电流耗散源于载流子总动量的弛豫，但该经典图像在缺乏长寿命载流子时失效，奇异金属耗散似由热平衡恢复速率决定并涉及普朗克极限（Planckian limit），其微观解释仍存争议。

Method: 在量子区域放弃以弛豫速率描述电导的做法，改以快速衰减的电流涨落并结合量子细致平衡刻画输运；在实验数据分析上，用电流-电流关联函数替代光学电导率（optical conductivity）来区分经典金属与量子金属。

Result: 快衰减电流涨落与量子细致平衡共同导致广泛观测到的普朗克耗散；将分析应用于铜氧化物超导体（cuprate superconductors）发现奇异金属响应在所有掺杂水平均存在，且在最优掺杂以上出现第二个经典组分。

Conclusion: 电流-电流关联函数分析是一种简单而有力的方法，可揭示与超导性、（赝）能隙（(pseudo-)gaps）相关的关键特征，为金属性质提供新认识，并指出理论理解亟需推进的关键方向。

Abstract: Strongly interacting quantum systems display puzzling electrical transport properties whose interpretation remains controversial. Many theoretical and phenomenological analyses assume that the complex behaviour observed in such `quantum materials' originates either from quasi-particles dressed with an anomalous electron self-energy or from collective excitations associated with hydrodynamic flow. Despite their distinctly different starting points, the underlying assumption is that dissipation of electrical current arises from the relaxation of the total momentum of charge carriers. This classical description breaks down in the absence of long-lived charge carriers. In such strange metals dissipation seems to be governed by the rate at which equilibrium is thermally restored, and it has been suggested that this constitutes an upper bound, known as the `Planckian limit'. Here we show that in this quantum regime a description of the conductivity in terms of relaxation rates no longer suffices: strange metal transport is carried instead by fast-decaying current fluctuations, which together with quantum detailed balance give rise to the widely observed Planckian dissipation. The distinction between classical and quantum metals becomes transparent when experimental data is analysed in terms of the current-current correlation function instead of the optical conductivity. When applied to the cuprate superconductors, such analysis uncovers that the strange metal response is present at all doping levels, and that a second, classical component develops above optimal doping. This simple, but powerful, approach further highlights essential features associated with superconductivity, (pseudo-)gaps, provides fresh insight in the properties of metals, and identifies key areas where progress in our theoretical understanding is needed.

</details>


### [165] [Fragmentation in Non-Hermitian Many-Body Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19205)
*Stefano Longhi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究了具有空间随机虚规范场的非厄米（non-Hermitian, NH）费米子链，发现单粒子本征态表现出无序的 NH 趋肤局域化（NH skin localization），多体密度在有限填充下发生空间碎片化，并形成亚扩展（subextensive）数量的费米畴，且该碎片化可在 NH 动力学演化中自发出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规趋肤效应与安德森局域化（Anderson localization）均已有成熟图像，但随机虚规范场下单粒子局域化的性质及其在有限填充多体层面（受泡利不相容原理约束）的后果尚不明确，尤其与厄米无序体系的差异有待厘清。

Method: 采用精确自由费米子（free-fermion）方法进行求解，并结合随机势中的库仑气体（Coulomb-gas）描述对所观察到的密度碎片化现象进行理论刻画。

Result: 单粒子本征态呈现波动强烈的亚指数衰减式 NH 趋肤局域化；有限填充下泡利排斥抑制宏观积累，使粒子密度分裂为高占据与低占据的分离区域，形成空间分离的费米畴，其数量 N_FS 随系统尺寸 L 亚扩展增长；该碎片化不仅在平衡态存在，也在 NH 演化中动力学出现。

Conclusion: 随机虚规范场导致的 NH 趋肤局域化与多体实空间碎片化之间存在直接联系，且碎片化为 NH 体系所特有（厄米体系中此类结构不会被动力学选择），为 NH 趋肤效应与多体态空间结构之间建立了桥梁。

Abstract: In non-Hermitian (NH) fermionic chains with spatially random imaginary gauge fields, single-particle eigenstates exhibit erratic NH skin localization with strongly fluctuating, subexponential decay, distinct from both conventional skin accumulation and Anderson localization. At finite filling, Pauli exclusion suppresses macroscopic accumulation and instead leads to fragmentation of the particle density, in sharp contrast to Hermitian disordered systems. Using exact free-fermion methods, we show that the density organizes into disconnected regions of high and low occupancy, forming spatially separated Fermi domains whose number $N_{\mathrm{FS}}$ scales subextensively with system size $L$. Remarkably, fragmentation is not only an equilibrium property but also emerges dynamically in the NH evolution, unlike in Hermitian systems where such structures are not dynamically selected. This behavior admits a Coulomb-gas description in a stochastic potential, establishing a direct link between erratic NH skin localization and real-space fragmentation of many-body states.

</details>


### [166] [Chiral Majorana Charge Kondo Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19595)
*Xiao Xiao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 论文提出“手性马约拉纳电荷近藤（chiral Majorana charge Kondo, CMCK）临界点”作为辨别手性马约拉纳边缘模式的多体判据；其与常规电荷近藤（charge Kondo, CK）在临界电导上可重合，但在电导修正标度、剩余熵及交叉指数上可区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性马约拉纳边缘模式的输运特征可由常规机制产生，导致其明确识别（unambiguous identification）仍具挑战，因此需要更具判别力的探测途径。

Method: 在承载单一手性马约拉纳边缘模式的带电岛（charged island）中，利用马约拉纳自共轭性（self-conjugacy）锁定正常与反常隧穿振幅，使每个物理引线无需精细调节即提供两个近藤屏蔽通道；对照分析常规亚能隙态破坏该锁定并趋向常规CK的情形。

Result: 最简单双端几何中，CMCK与常规CK共享临界电导 G*=e^2/2h，但前导电导修正分别按 T^{1/3} 与 T 标度；剩余熵（residual entropy）及栅极失谐、接触不对称下的交叉指数（crossover exponent）亦不同。对N个引线，CMCK修正按 T^{1/(N+1)} 标度，且N≥3时临界电导本身即与同引线数常规CK不同。

Conclusion: 这些普适特征（universal signatures）可借助既有电荷近藤测量技术检验，从而为手性马约拉纳边缘模式提供更具判别力的多体验证方案。

Abstract: Unambiguous identification of chiral Majorana edge modes remains challenging because the transport signatures proposed for them can also arise from conventional mechanisms. Here we identify the chiral Majorana charge Kondo (CMCK) critical point as a more discriminating many-body route to detect chiral Majorana edge modes. This criticality arises in a charged island hosting a single chiral Majorana edge mode, where Majorana self-conjugacy locks normal and anomalous tunneling amplitudes, so that each physical lead provides two Kondo screening channels without fine tuning. Ordinary subgap states generically break this locking and drive the system toward conventional charge Kondo (CK) behavior. In the simplest two-terminal geometry, CMCK and conventional CK share the critical conductance $G^*=e^2/2h$, but their leading conductance corrections scale as $T^{1/3}$ and $T$, respectively. Their residual entropies also differ, as do the crossover exponents under gate detuning and contact asymmetry. For $N$ leads, CMCK conductance corrections scale as $T^{1/(N+1)}$, and for $N\geq3$ even the critical conductance differs from that of conventional CK with the same number of leads. These universal signatures are testable using established charge-Kondo measurement techniques.

</details>


### [167] [Spectral Moments of the Projected Hubbard Configuration Graph: Connectivity, Exchange, and Virtual Doublons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19929)
*D. Souza*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文研究方晶格 Hubbard 模型中强在位排斥下投影子空间 Hamiltonian H_∞=P_0H_tP_0 的能带矩结构，给出对任意自旋组成成立的二阶矩精确公式、四阶矩对同自旋对数的线性依赖、大 U 下 Schrieffer-Wolff 修正的 Hilbert-Schmidt 正交性，以及在 Peierls 磁通下双占态（doublon）跃迁获得加倍相位的严格推导，并对无超导性作明确声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强在位排斥（on-site repulsion）下最低能带由投影到无双重占据（no double occupancy）子空间的跃迁谱给出，但该谱的矩结构（moment structure）缺乏精确刻画。论文旨在通过解析与可机检的方式给出这些矩的闭式结果，并厘清束缚对（bound pair）在磁通下的相位行为，而不涉及长程序（off-diagonal long-range order）等问题。

Method: 对任意有限图（finite graph）上自旋无关厄米跃迁（spin-independent Hermitian hopping）计算 H_∞ 的低阶矩，利用四次跃迁至多产生一个对换或 3-循环（transposition or 3-cycle）的置换论证（permutation bound）得到四阶矩；用二阶 Schrieffer-Wolff 变换处理大有限 U 的修正；在环面上施加 Peierls 磁通（Peierls flux）与边界扭转（boundary twist），解析推导单-doublon 有效 Hamiltonian；关键结论以 Lean 4 机器检验并用精确对角化（exact diagonalization）验证迹公式。

Result: (1) 任意 N≥2 图与任意自旋组成下 μ_2=n(N−n)/(N(N−1))·Σ_{i≠j}|t_ij|²；(2) μ_4 严格线性于同自旋对计数，简单图均匀跃迁下斜率由 4-循环数 C_4 固定为 α(N,n)=−8C_4(N−n)(8N−11n−2)/[N(N−1)(N−2)(N−3)]；(3) 无三角图上二阶 Schrieffer-Wolff 修正与 H_∞ 正交，能带方差无 1/U 项，U^{-2} 系数在已算低填充下为负；(4) 交换斜率 α(N,2,Φ)=−64/(N−1)·cosΦ_□ 携带普通电荷-e 谐波，而 doublon 跃迁获得加倍相位 t_de^{2iA_ij}，单-doublon 二阶 Hamiltonian 在边界扭转下严格 π-周期（h/2e）。

Conclusion: 论文建立了强关联投影能带的精确矩结构并严格导出双占束缚对的加倍相位（doubled phase），后者在边界扭转下表现为 h/2e 周期性。排斥性束缚（repulsive binding）与配对电荷 2e 属已知结果，新增的是矩的精确公式与加倍相位的推导；作者明确不对多对相刚度（phase stiffness）、非对角长程序或超导电性作任何断言。

Abstract: At strong on-site repulsion, the lowest band of $n$ lattice fermions is the spectrum of the hopping projected onto configurations without double occupancy, $H_\infty=P_0H_tP_0$. We study its moments, for few fermions in the square-lattice Hubbard model and, where the arguments allow, on any finite graph. (1) For spin-independent Hermitian hopping (no on-site terms) on any $N\geq2$-site graph, $μ_2=\frac{n(N-n)}{N(N-1)}\sum_{i\neq j}|t_{ij}|^2$, for any spin composition. (2) On any finite graph, $μ_4$ is exactly linear in the same-spin pair count, as four hops permute particles by at most a transposition or 3-cycle; for uniform hopping on a simple graph with $N\geq4$ the slope is $α(N,n)=-8C_4(N-n)(8N-11n-2)/[N(N-1)(N-2)(N-3)]$, fixed by the number $C_4$ of 4-cycles. (3) At large finite $U$, the second-order Schrieffer-Wolff correction is Hilbert-Schmidt orthogonal to $H_\infty$ on every triangle-free graph, so the band variance has no $1/U$ term; its $U^{-2}$ coefficient, an exact trace checked by exact diagonalization, is negative at the low fillings computed.
