(4) Under a Peierls flux, the exchange slope $α(N,2,Φ)=-\frac{64}{N-1}\cosΦ_\square$ ($L\times L$ tori, $L=3$ or $L\geq5$) carries the ordinary charge-$e$ harmonic, while the doublon (bound-pair) hopping acquires a doubled phase, $t_de^{2iA_{ij}}$: the second-order single-doublon Hamiltonian is exactly $π$-periodic ($h/2e$) under a boundary twist, and the exact two-electron U-band follows numerically, up to $O((2λ)^L/U^{L-1})$. Result (1), the permutation bound, the orthogonality in (3) and the doubled phase are machine-checked in Lean 4. Repulsive binding and the pair charge $2e$ are known; new are the exact moment structure and the derivation of the doubled phase. We make no claim about many-pair phase stiffness, off-diagonal long-range order or superconductivity.

</details>


### [168] [Charge-Transfer Energy Governed by $f$-$d$ Coulomb Interaction in Rare-Earth Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19781)
*Norimasa Sasabe,Shinji Watanabe,Akira Yasui,Naomi Kawamura,Kojiro Mimura,Takayuki Uozumi,Masaichiro Mizumaki,Hitoshi Sato*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究建立了一个光谱学框架，证明电荷转移能（charge-transfer energy）包含来自 d 电子占据的 f-d 库仑贡献，并在 YbInCu₄ 中通过芯能级光电子发射与 L 边 X 射线吸收谱的联合分析验证了这一图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低价态理论中描述价涨落与量子价临界性（quantum valence criticality）的 f-d 库仑相互作用（U_fd）与芯能级光谱传统使用的唯象电荷转移能（Δ）之间关系长期不明，本文旨在弥合这两种描述。

Method: 发展光谱学框架，论证电荷转移能包含 f-d 库仑贡献 U_fd·n_d（n_d 为 d 电子占据数），从而给出修正后的电荷转移能 Δ'；并将该框架应用于典型价态转变化合物 YbInCu₄，结合芯能级光电子发射（core-level photoemission）与 L 边 X 射线吸收谱（L-edge x-ray absorption spectroscopy）进行联合分析。

Result: 在 YbInCu₄ 中，跨越一级价态转变（first-order valence transition）所观测到的光谱演化可由 Δ' 的变化一致地解释。

Conclusion: 电荷转移能应被视为含 f-d 库仑贡献的量，Δ' 框架可将芯能级光谱与基于 U_fd 的低能价涨落理论统一起来，并为稀土体系中价态转变的光谱诊断提供依据。

Abstract: The $f$-$d$ Coulomb interaction ($U_{\rm fd}$) plays a central role in low-energy theories describing valence fluctuations and quantum valence criticality in rare-earth systems. In contrast, core-level spectroscopies have conventionally been analyzed using the phenomenological charge-transfer energy ($Δ$), and the relationship between these two descriptions has remained unclear. Here, we develop a spectroscopic framework demonstrating that the charge-transfer energy contains an $f$-$d$ Coulomb contribution $U_{\rm fd}n_d$ arising from the $d$-electron occupation ($n_d$), leading to the charge-transfer energy $Δ'$. We apply this framework to the prototypical valence-transition compound YbInCu$_4$ and demonstrate, from combined analyses of core-level photoemission and $L$-edge x-ray absorption spectroscopy, that the observed spectroscopic evolution across the first-order valence transition is consistently interpreted in terms of the variation of $Δ'$.

</details>


### [169] [Compact Projected Pfaffian States for Rydberg-Blockaded Quantum Spin Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19790)
*Ryui Kaneko*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作数值证明紧凑的投影 Pfaffian（projected Pfaffian）拟设可有效描述非可积 PXP 模型的基态，在一维与二维阻挫约束体系中均实现高精度变分描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PXP 模型具有指数大的 Rydberg 阻塞（Rydberg-blockade）约束希尔伯特空间，亟需紧凑且物理可解释的变分波函数来描述其基态。

Method: 将一个一般费米子配对态（fermionic paired state）分别施加 Gutzwiller 投影与 Rydberg 阻塞投影，使每个允许构型的振幅由构型选定的配对矩阵的 Pfaffian 给出；并以已知无阻挫变形（frustration-free deformation）的精确投影乘积态作为变分蒙特卡洛（variational Monte Carlo）优化初态，优化 O(N) 个对称允许的配对数。

Result: 一维 N 格点体系中归一化能量方差（V-score）达 10^-9 量级；正方格子上相对能量误差低于 0.2%，磁化强度与现有基准一致。

Conclusion: 同一投影 Pfaffian 构造可统一应用于一维和二维阻挫约束体系，为强关联量子态提供紧凑、高精度的变分表示。

Abstract: We numerically demonstrate that a compact projected Pfaffian ansatz effectively describes the ground state of the nonintegrable PXP model. The variational wave function is obtained by applying separate Gutzwiller and Rydberg-blockade projectors to a general fermionic paired state, and each blockade-allowed configuration has an amplitude given by the Pfaffian of a configuration-selected pairing matrix. The exact blockade-projected product state of a known frustration-free deformation of the PXP model lies within the variational manifold and provides a physically motivated initial state for the variational Monte Carlo optimization toward the ground state of the pure PXP model. For a one-dimensional system of $N$ sites, optimizing all symmetry-allowed pairings in an $O(N)$-parameter wave function yields a near-exact ground state with a normalized energy variance (V-score) of order $10^{-9}$ despite the exponentially large blockade-constrained Hilbert space. On the square lattice, an $O(N)$-parameter wave function with the same amplitude structure yields relative energy errors below $0.2\%$ for clusters accessible to exact diagonalization and produces magnetizations consistent with available benchmarks. These results show that the same projected Pfaffian construction can be applied to blockade-constrained systems in both one and two dimensions.

</details>


### [170] [Exact environment checkpointing for memory-limited DMRG and TDVP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19801)
*Nihal Krishna Shrivastava,Rudranil Basu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 将检查点（checkpointing）技术引入有限矩阵乘积态（matrix-product-state, MPS）扫描算法，每隔约 √N 个格点保存一个环境张量，中间块在扫描回访时通过重复原始收缩重建；在 96–384 格点柱面上环境内存降低 4.6–10 倍，并以少量时间开销换取大幅内存节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DMRG 与 TDVP 等有限 MPS 扫描算法为每个格点存储一个环境张量（environment tensor）；在阻挫与二维系统所需的大键维（bond dimension）下，这一环境栈而非量子态本身耗尽设备内存。

Method: 对扫描过程做检查点化：每 k 个环境保留一个（k≈√N，N 为格点数），其余块在扫描返回时按原收缩重建；因重建重复原始收缩，确定性收缩下能量与态可逐位（bit for bit）复现。并在 ITensorMPS.jl、TeNPy、MPSKit.jl 与 quimb 中给出实现。

Result: 96–384 格点柱面上环境内存下降 4.6–10 倍。单张 141 GB GPU 上、144 格点三角 J1–J2 柱面的键维搜索中，半扫描重建检查点的实现相对同等分块存储基线加速 1.77 倍（单层）与 2.07 倍（双层、8 个收缩分块）；跨半扫描保留检查点时单层开销为 6–15%（取决于局部本征求解器）；D=1536 时双层 8 分块方案耗时为等分块存储基线的 1.42 倍、未分块基线的 1.79 倍。内存与运行时间的简单模型可在数个百分点内复现测量结果。

Conclusion: 该方法无需主机内存或磁盘，区别于卸载（offloading）且可与之结合；以可复现的收缩换取显著内存节省，适用于大规模键维扫描。

Abstract: Finite matrix-product-state sweep algorithms, such as the density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) and the time-dependent variational principle (TDVP), store one environment tensor per site. At the bond dimensions needed for frustrated and two-dimensional systems this stack, rather than the state itself, exhausts the memory of the device. We adapt checkpointing to these sweeps: every $k$-th environment is kept, with $k\approx\sqrt{N}$ for $N$ sites, and the blocks in between are rebuilt when the sweep returns to them. The rebuild repeats the original contractions, so energies and states are reproduced bit for bit when the contractions are deterministic. Environment memory falls by 4.6-10x on cylinders of 96-384 sites. Bond-dimension searches with an implementation that rebuilds checkpoints at each half-sweep give gains of 1.77 for one level and 2.07 for two levels with eight contraction chunks on a single 141 GB GPU, using a 144-site triangular $J_1$-$J_2$ cylinder and stored baselines with matching chunking. Separate timings with checkpoints retained across half-sweeps give a one-level overhead of 6-15%, depending on the local eigensolver. At $D=1536$ the two-level schedule with eight chunks takes 1.42 times the sweep time of the equally chunked stored baseline, or 1.79 times that of the unchunked stored baseline. Simple models of the memory and the run time reproduce the measurements to within a few per cent. Unlike offloading, the method needs no host memory or disk, and the two can be combined. Implementations are provided for ITensorMPS.jl, TeNPy, MPSKit.jl and quimb.

</details>


### [171] [Spectroscopic evidence for unconventional surface magnetism in the Kondo insulator FeSb$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19804)
*Ke-Jun Xu,Erin E. Fleck Jonathan A. Sobota,Nhat Huy M. Tran,Shuyi Li,Youzhe Chen,Soonsang Huh,Takeshi Kondo,Makoto Hashimoto,Dong-Hui Lu,Zhi-Xun Shen,Dung-Hai Lee,Chunjing Jia,Robert J. Birgeneau*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）研究 d-p 电子近藤绝缘体（Kondo insulator）FeSb₂ 的 (010) 表面，观察到表面态的各向异性分裂，推测其可能源于涌现的表面交错磁序（altermagnetic order）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近藤绝缘体体态因传导电子对局域矩的多体屏蔽（many-body screening）而无磁性，但表面巡游电子密度降低可能引发近藤击穿（Kondo breakdown），使局域矩恢复并可能形成磁序；FeSb₂ 被预测在无近藤屏蔽时具有体态 d 波交错磁态（d-wave altermagnetic state），故其表面磁性值得探究。

Method: 采用角分辨光电子能谱（angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, ARPES）测量 FeSb₂ (010) 表面的表面态能带分裂，并考察其随温度及 Te 掺杂的演化，以区分磁序起源与 Rashba 效应。

Result: 表面态出现各向异性分裂：在 k_z 方向最大、在 k_x 方向最小；该分裂随温度升高或 Te 掺杂而明显被抑制，表明其更可能来自磁序而非 Rashba 效应。

Conclusion: 结合体态接近 d 波交错磁态及表面晶体对称性，观测结果可能与涌现的表面交错磁序（emergent surface altermagnetic order）一致；该种自然存在于绝缘、非磁体相衬底上的非常规二维磁序或可为多类应用提供平台。

Abstract: Kondo insulators have a non-magnetic bulk due to many-body screening of local moment by conduction electrons. However, the surface of these systems has reduced density of itinerant electrons and may experience a Kondo breakdown, leading to recovery of non-zero local moments and possible magnetic order. Here we use angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy to investigate the (010) surface of a $d$-$p$ electron Kondo insulator FeSb$_2$, which is predicted to host a bulk $d$-wave altermagnetic state in the absence of Kondo screening. We find anisotropic splitting on the surface states with a maximum in the $k_z$ direction and a minimum in the $k_x$ direction. The splitting is apparently suppressed with increasing temperature or with Te doping, suggesting that the splitting arises from a magnetic order rather than Rashba effects. Considering the proximity of the bulk to a $d$-wave altermagnetic state and considering the symmetry of the crystal on the surface, we postulate that our observations may be consistent with an emergent surface altermagnetic order. Such an unconventional 2-dimensional magnetic order naturally hosted on an insulating, non-magnet bulk substrate may offer a promising platform for versatile applications.

</details>


### [172] [Angular dependence of the low-temperature skyrmion state in Cu$_2$OSeO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19993)
*Christian Oberleitner,Andreas Bauer,Lukas Heindl,Lin Chen,Aisha Aqeel,Christian H. Back,Hans Huebl,Christian Pfleiderer*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在立方手性磁体（cubic chiral magnet）Cu₂OSeO₃中，将磁场从⟨100⟩经⟨111⟩旋转至⟨110⟩时，低温斯格明子态（low-temperature skyrmion state, LTS）仅在偏离⟨100⟩约20°内存在且解绕场基本不变，而倾斜锥态（tilted conical state）可维持至约40°，表明倾斜锥态是LTS成核的必要但非充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沿⟨100⟩方向的低温斯格明子态与亚稳倾斜锥态均由磁晶各向异性（magnetocrystalline anisotropy）稳定，但其在磁场方向改变时的演化规律尚不清楚，本文旨在厘清两种状态的稳定性范围及相互关系。

Method: 在同一块长方体样品上于5 K下结合磁化强度（magnetization）与交流磁化率（AC susceptibility）测量，并进行宽带微波谱（broadband microwave spectroscopy）测量；以吉赫兹特征激发（尤其斯格明子的逆时针旋进模，counterclockwise gyration mode）作为磁态性质及体积分数的判据。

Result: 两种技术一致显示LTS特征随旋转减弱，仅在⟨100⟩附近约20°内可观测，且该范围内斯格明子解绕（unwind）的场值基本不变；倾斜锥态特征则持续至约40°，超出后恢复螺旋态、锥态与场极化态（field-polarized state）的常规序列。

Conclusion: 倾斜锥态虽是场扫掠下LTS成核的前提条件，但并非其形成的充分条件；LTS的存在受磁场方向强烈限制，其稳定区间远窄于倾斜锥态。

Abstract: In the cubic chiral magnet Cu$_2$OSeO$_3$, a low-temperature skyrmion state (LTS) and a metastable tilted conical state emerge for magnetic field along $\langle 100\rangle$, both stabilized by magnetocrystalline anisotropies. We report how these states evolve when the magnetic field is rotated from $\langle 100\rangle$ via $\langle 111\rangle$ to $\langle 110\rangle$. Measurements of the magnetization and the AC susceptibility are combined with broadband microwave spectroscopy on the same cuboid sample at 5\,K. In the microwave spectra, the characteristic gigahertz excitations, notably the counterclockwise gyration mode of the skyrmions, provide a direct indicator of the character of the magnetic states and a qualitative measure of their volume fractions. Both techniques consistently show that signatures of the LTS decrease in strength under rotation and are observed only for field directions within ${\sim}20^{\circ}$ of $\langle 100\rangle$. In this range, the field values at which the skyrmions unwind remain essentially unchanged. In contrast, signatures of the tilted conical state persist up to ${\sim}40^{\circ}$, beyond which the conventional sequence of helical, conical, and field-polarized states is recovered. Thus, the tilted conical state, while being a prerequisite for the nucleation of the LTS under field sweeps, is not sufficient for its formation.

</details>


### [173] [Two channels of magnetic renormalization in the terahertz lattice response of FePS$_{3}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20102)
*Eduardo D. Stefanato,Nicolas M. Kawahala,Ana Carolina F. de Brito,Jan Plutnar,Zdenek Sofer,Bernardo R. A. Neves,Ingrid D. Barcelos,Felix G. G. Hernandez*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用太赫兹时域光谱测量FePS₃在各向异性电导率上的温度与磁场依赖，揭示层间剪切模与磁序的耦合：频率移动仅限有序相，而线宽与振子强度遵循短程自旋关联，且磁场在约3.2 T处诱发类临界减速行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有超快实验表明FePS₃层间剪切与磁序强耦合，但磁性如何重整化剪切刚度（shear stiffness）与阻尼（damping）尚不清楚，本文旨在给出平衡态视角的定量刻画。

Method: 采用太赫兹时域光谱（terahertz time-domain spectroscopy）在1.6–300 K温度范围及垂直层面至7 T磁场下测量FePS₃的各向异性电导率，分辨出0.6–0.8 THz与1.3–2.4 THz两个持续至顺磁相的吸收带。

Result: 低频带对应层间剪切共振（interlayer shear resonance），磁序使其一个面内分量变硬、另一分量软化，并在奈尔温度（Néel temperature）处反转劈裂；频率移动仅存在于有序相，而线宽与多数振子强度（oscillator strength）随短程自旋关联在转变温度以上持续；磁场主要作用于某一取向的高频共振，约3.2 T处软化加速且线宽出现尖点状极小。

Conclusion: 自旋-剪切耦合（spin-shear coupling）具有各向异性，并通过短程关联在奈尔温度以上延续，表现出磁场诱导序参量的临界减速（critical slowing down）特征。

Abstract: Interlayer vibrations probe how magnetic van der Waals layers are stacked and coupled. In the zigzag antiferromagnet FePS$_3$, ultrafast experiments show that the interlayer shear couples strongly to the magnetic order, but not how magnetism renormalizes the shear stiffness and damping. Here, using terahertz time-domain spectroscopy, we measure the anisotropic conductivity of FePS$_3$ from 1.6 to 300 K and in fields up to 7 T normal to the layers. We observe absorption bands at 0.6-0.8 and 1.3-2.4 THz that persist into the paramagnetic phase. The lower one is consistent with an interlayer shear resonance; the magnetic order stiffens one of its in-plane components and softens the other, reversing their splitting at the Néel temperature. We identify two channels of magnetic renormalization: frequency shifts are confined to the ordered phase, whereas the linewidths of this resonance and most oscillator strengths follow short-range spin correlations persisting well above the transition. The field acts mainly on the higher resonance in one orientation: near 3.2 T, its softening accelerates and its linewidth has a cusp-like minimum, consistent with critical slowing down of a field-induced order parameter. These results give an equilibrium view of spin-shear coupling, which is anisotropic and persists above the Néel temperature through short-range correlations.

</details>


### [174] [Multi-symmetry projected coupled cluster theory with triples corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20108)
*Ruiheng Song,Thomas M. Henderson,Gustavo E. Scuseria*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作为自旋与点群投影耦合簇单双激发方法（PCCSD）引入两种微扰三激发（perturbative triples）修正：相干 $(T)$ 与 $+(T)$。在 N$_2$、C$_2$ 解离、3d 过渡金属一氧化物及 Cr$_2$ 上评估表明，相干 $(T)$ 将六种一氧化物基准的 PCCSD 平均绝对误差由 13.4 降至 3.0 mEh，接近常规 CCSD(T) 精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称性投影（symmetry projection）与耦合簇理论（coupled cluster theory）分别擅长描述静态相关（static correlation）与动态相关（dynamic correlation），但 PCCSD 缺失三激发贡献，因此需要引入微扰三激发修正以提升精度。

Method: 提出两种近似微扰三激发修正：相干 $(T)$ 在投影网格（projection grid）上组合三激发中间量，$+(T)$ 则用 PCCSD 振幅计算常规三激发表达式；两者在无投影极限下均还原为常规 $(T)$。

Result: 投影改善了单双激发描述并可区分低能对称性扇区（symmetry sectors）；六种一氧化物基准上相干 $(T)$ 使 PCCSD 的 MAE 从 13.4 降至 3.0 mEh，达到常规 CCSD(T) 水平；两种修正均恢复 Cr$_2$ 短键极小值，相干 $(T)$ 的阱深更接近外推 DMRG，而 $+(T)$ 给出更均衡的 N$_2$ 与 C$_2$ 解离曲线。

Conclusion: 结果证明微扰三激发对 PCC 有益，但其表现依赖体系，说明仍需更系统的处理方式。

Abstract: Symmetry projection and coupled cluster theory provide complementary descriptions of static and dynamic correlation. We introduce two approximate perturbative triples corrections to spin- and point-group-projected coupled cluster with single and double excitations (PCCSD). The coherent $(T)$ correction combines triples intermediates over the projection grid, whereas $+(T)$ evaluates the conventional triples expression using PCCSD amplitudes. Both recover conventional $(T)$ in the absence of projection. We assess these corrections for N$_2$ and C$_2$ dissociation, $3d$ transition-metal monoxides, and Cr$_2$. Projection improves the singles-and-doubles description and distinguishes selected low-lying symmetry sectors. For the six-monoxide benchmark subset, coherent $(T)$ reduces the PCCSD mean absolute error from $13.4$ to $3.0~\mEh$, achieving accuracy comparable to conventional CCSD(T). Both triples corrections restore the short-bond minimum missing from the PCCSD curves of Cr$_2$, with coherent $(T)$ giving a well depth closer to extrapolated DMRG. In contrast, $+(T)$ provides more balanced N$_2$ and C$_2$ dissociation curves. These results demonstrate the benefits of perturbative triples in PCC, while their system-dependent performance highlights the need for a more systematic treatment.

</details>


### [175] [Self-doped correlated adatom lattices on cubic boron nitride](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20263)
*Niklas Enderlein,Tim Kullick,Henri Menke,Roland Gillen,Janina Maultzsch,Philipp Hansmann*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文以第一性原理计算系统筛选立方氮化硼（cubic boron nitride, cBN）(111) 面上三角吸附原子晶格，识别出十二种可用于实现强关联二维电子系统（two-dimensional electron systems）的候选平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于为三角晶格上强关联电子行为（含预言中的非常规超导）寻找清洁、可调控的固态实现平台，并利用 cBN 的极性特征调控能带填充。

Method: 方法为密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）结合约束随机相位近似（constrained random phase approximation, cRPA）与电荷自洽动力学平均场理论（charge self-consistent dynamical mean-field theory, DMFT）。

Result: 结果显示：N 终止面（N-termination）上的 III 族（group-III）与 B 终止面上的 V 族（group-V）吸附原子形成单带 Hubbard 系统；B 终止面上的 III 族及过渡金属（V、Cr、Mn）吸附原子形成两带 Hubbard 系统。cBN 的极性 III-V 特征导致自掺杂（self-doping）偏离整数填充 ±1/4 电子，多数体系为掺杂 Mott 绝缘体（doped Mott insulator），重准电子（quasielectron）质量重整化（mass renormalization）可达 4，局域磁矩（local moment）最高达 2.5 μ_B。

Conclusion: 结论认为这些体系的强 Hubbard（及 Hund）相互作用与小跳跃积分使其处于三角晶格非常规超导（unconventional superconductivity）理论预言的参数区间，具备进一步研究的价值。

Abstract: We survey triangular adatom lattices on the (111) surfaces of cubic boron nitride (cBN) for strongly correlated two-dimensional electron systems. Using density functional theory combined with the constrained random phase approximation and charge self-consistent dynamical mean-field theory, we identify twelve promising candidates. Group-III (group-V) adatoms on the N- (B-)termination realize clean one-band Hubbard systems, while group-III and transition-metal (V, Cr, Mn) adatoms on the B-termination realize two-band Hubbard systems. Importantly, the polar III-V character of cBN induces self-doping by $\pm 1/4$ electron away from integer filling. With strong Hubbard (and Hund's) interactions and small hoppings, most systems are doped Mott insulators. Heavy quasielectrons with mass renormalizations up to 4 and strong local moments up to 2.5 $μ_B$ put our systems into a regime where unconventional superconductivity on the triangular lattice has been predicted.

</details>


### [176] [Role of crystal-field degrees of freedom in square and rectangular skyrmion lattices in noncentrosymmetric tetragonal magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20661)
*Cong Guan,Yan S. Zha,Satoru Hayami*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在非中心对称方格子定域磁矩模型中，晶体场（crystal-field）自由度可调控方形与矩形斯格明子晶格（skyrmion lattice）的稳定性、空间对称性与拓扑性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨晶体场自由度在稳定方形与矩形斯格明子晶格中的作用，为多重-Q磁态（multiple-Q magnetic states）的稳定机制提供微观解释。

Method: 在方格子非中心对称（noncentrosymmetric）定域磁矩模型上，采用自洽平均场（self-consistent mean-field）计算，考虑单/双晶体场二重态（crystal-field doublet）以及交换作用和极性型Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya相互作用（polar-type Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya interactions）。

Result: 得到具有不同磁周期的方形斯格明子晶格，以及自发破缺面内四重旋转对称性的矩形斯格明子晶格；其稳定性与场致相序列强烈依赖晶体场波函数，并出现矩形相的重入（reentrant）行为。次近邻交换（second-neighbor exchange）进一步改变这些态之间的竞争；引入第二个晶体场二重态时出现新的矩形斯格明子晶格、轴向单-Q态（axial single-Q states）及多种双-Q态（double-Q states）。

Conclusion: 晶体场自由度为调控多重-Q磁态的稳定性、空间对称性与拓扑提供了一种微观机制。

Abstract: We investigate the role of crystal-field degrees of freedom in stabilizing square and rectangular skyrmion lattices in a noncentrosymmetric localized-moment model on a square lattice. The magnetic phases are determined using self-consistent mean-field calculations. We first consider a single crystal-field doublet with exchange and polar-type Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya interactions. We find square skyrmion lattices with distinct magnetic periods, as well as a rectangular skyrmion lattice that spontaneously breaks the in-plane fourfold rotational symmetry. Their stability and field-induced phase sequences are strongly controlled by the crystal-field wave function, including reentrant behavior of the rectangular phase. Second-neighbor exchange further modifies the competition among these states. When a second crystal-field doublet is retained, additional rectangular skyrmion lattices with distinct modulation patterns emerge, together with axial single-$Q$ states and various double-$Q$ states. These results demonstrate that crystal-field degrees of freedom provide a microscopic mechanism for tuning the stability, spatial symmetry, and topology of multiple-$Q$ magnetic states.

</details>


### [177] [Excitonic magnetism and competing valence bond solid instabilities in $d^4$ ruthenium compounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20682)
*Aleksandra Krajewska,Tomohiro Takayama,Hidenori Takagi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该综述以自旋-轨道纠缠（spin-orbit entangled）的 $d^4$ 钌基莫特绝缘体为对象，用三重态带宽与有效带隙之比 $W_{\rm{t}}/\Delta_{\rm{eff}}$ 作为概念性参量轴，统一梳理从孤立八面体到烧绿石等四类结构连接方式下的电子基态演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $d^4$ 莫特绝缘体中自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC）产生的非磁性 $J_{\rm{eff}}=0$ 单态与 $J_{\rm{eff}}=1$ 三重态，为非常规磁性与竞争性量子基态提供了研究平台；需厘清这些化合物中各类能量尺度竞争的普适图景。

Method: 采用综述方式，系统考察四类 Ru-配体八面体连接结构（孤立八面体的 K$_2$RuCl$_6$、角共享钙钛矿 Ca$_2$RuO$_4$、边共享蜂窝结构、角共享烧绿石），并将它们映射到 $W_{\rm{t}}/\Delta_{\rm{eff}}$ 参量轴上进行比较。

Result: 当三重态带宽 $W_{\rm{t}}$ 超过有效原子内能隙 $\Delta_{\rm{eff}}$ 时，体系发生长程磁有序，可视为自旋-轨道激子（spin-orbit exciton）的凝聚，即激子磁性（excitonic magnetism）；$d^4$ 钌化合物不仅呈现从 $J_{\rm{eff}}=0$ 莫特态到激子磁性的演化，还涵盖多相竞争。

Conclusion: 在激子量子临界点（excitonic quantum critical point，$W_{\rm{t}}\sim\Delta_{\rm{eff}}$）附近，晶格几何与化学键对基态选择起决定性作用，常促成价键固体（valence bond solid）形成，从而抢先于激子量子临界性。

Abstract: Spin-orbit entangled $d^4$ Mott insulators serve as a rich playground for unconventional magnetism and competing quantum electronic ground states. Intra-ionic spin-orbit coupling $λ_{\rm{SOC}}$ generates a non-magnetic $J_{\rm{eff}} = 0$ singlet ground state separated from an excited $J_{\rm{eff}} = 1$ triplet. The effective intra-atomic gap $Δ_{\rm{eff}}$ between the ground state and lowest excited state is determined by $λ_{\rm{SOC}}$ and non-cubic crystal field $Δ_{\rm{cf}}$. The intersite magnetic exchange $J_{\rm{ex}}$ induces dispersion of the excited $J_{\rm{eff}} = 1$ triplet. When the triplet bandwidth $W_{\rm{t}}$ overcomes $Δ_{\rm{eff}}$, the system undergoes long-range magnetic ordering which is viewed as a condensation of spin-orbit excitons and called excitonic magnetism. In this review, we systematically survey representative $d^4$ ruthenium compounds across four distinct structural connectivities of Ru-ligand octahedra, from K$_2$RuCl$_6$ with isolated octahedra, corner-sharing perovskite Ca$_2$RuO$_4$, edge-sharing honeycombs, to corner-sharing pyrochlores, and map them onto a conceptual parameter axis of $W_{\rm{t}} / Δ_{\rm{eff}}$. We demonstrate that $d^4$ ruthenium compounds not only represent evolution from a $J_{\rm{eff}} = 0$ Mott state to excitonic magnetism but encompass a broader competition of diverse electronic phases arising from spin-orbit coupling, exchange interactions, electron hopping, crystal field and bond covalency inhabiting similar energy scales. Near excitonic quantum critical point, where $W_{\rm{t}} \sim Δ_{\rm{eff}}$, lattice geometry and chemical bonds of constituent atoms play a decisive role in selecting the electronic ground state, frequently driving valence bond solid formation that pre-empts excitonic quantum criticality.

</details>


### [178] [Unconventional Magneto-Optical Kerr Effect beyond Magnetization Scaling Driven by Pulse-Field-Induced Metamagnetism in Fe$_2$Mo$_3$O$_8$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20697)
*Soichiro Yamane,Sota Nakamura,Shuya Suzuki,Atsutoshi Ikeda,Masaki Kondo,Takashi Kurumaji,Kentaro Ino,Keisuke Matsuura,Fumitaka Kagawa,Yoshinori Tokura,Masashi Tokunaga,Shingo Yonezawa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究将脉冲场磁光克尔效应（magneto-optical Kerr effect, MOKE）测量首次拓展至60 T、低至1.4 K，在极性绝缘体Fe$_2$Mo$_3$O$_8$中观察到反铁磁（antiferromagnetic, AFM）—铁磁（ferrimagnetic, FRM）变磁转变过程中异常巨大的非单调瞬态MOKE响应，并归因于实空间中AFM-FRM相界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一级相变（first-order phase transitions）常经由亚稳态（metastable states）与复杂相共存进行，但此类非平衡过程在体测量（bulk measurements）中被掩盖；MOKE可探测局域非对角光学响应，从而获得体测量之外的局域磁动力学信息，因此有必要将MOKE拓展至更高磁场以揭示隐藏的场致现象。

Method: 采用脉冲场MOKE测量技术，首次将测量范围拓展至60 T并降至1.4 K，研究极性绝缘体（polar insulator）Fe$_2$Mo$_3$O$_8$中磁场诱导的AFM-FRM转变，并分析MOKE响应与体磁化强度的标度关系及温度、场历史与脉冲协议依赖性。

Result: MOKE响应在变磁相变（metamagnetic phase transition）附近表现出意想不到的巨大非单调瞬态行为，且与体磁化强度（bulk magnetization）无任何标度关系，暗示其为非常规反常霍尔效应（unconventional anomalous Hall effect）的MOKE类比；低温下MOKE曲线细节高度可重复，而较高温度下强烈依赖磁场历史与脉冲协议。

Conclusion: 作者推断过量的MOKE异常源于实空间中的AFM-FRM相界面（phase interfaces），并提出这是仅在竞争磁序边界处出现的新一类非对角响应；将MOKE拓展至60 T区间确立了脉冲场磁光（pulsed-field magneto-optics）作为揭示瞬态与隐藏场致现象的新型探测手段。

Abstract: First-order phase transitions often proceed through metastable states and complicated phase coexistence, yet such nonequilibrium processes are obscured in bulk measurements. The magneto-optical Kerr effect (MOKE) probes the local off-diagonal optical response, offering access to local magnetic dynamics beyond bulk measurements. Here, we investigate the magnetic-field-induced antiferromagnetic (AFM)-ferrimagnetic (FRM) transition in the polar insulator Fe$_2$Mo$_3$O$_8$ using pulsed-field MOKE measurements extended for the first time up to 60 T and down to 1.4 K. The MOKE response exhibits unexpected and huge nonmonotonic transient behavior across the metamagnetic phase transition. The MOKE behavior does not show any scaling with the bulk magnetization, pointing to an MOKE analogue of the unconventional anomalous Hall effect. This is a new class of off-diagonal response emerging exclusively at the boundary between competing magnetic orders. At low temperatures, MOKE profiles are highly reproducible in detail, whereas at elevated temperatures they depend strongly on magnetic-field history and pulse protocol, suggesting changes in the driving mechanism of the phase transition. We infer that the excess MOKE anomaly originates from AFM-FRM phase interfaces in real space. Our results, extending MOKE into the 60 T regime, establish pulsed-field magneto-optics as a novel probe capable of uncovering transient and hidden field-induced phenomena through unconventional off-diagonal optical responses.

</details>


### [179] [Hund physics in the quarter-filled two-band Hubbard-Kanamori model: Crystal-field splitting and non-local Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21021)
*Diego Florez-Ablan,Carlos Mejuto-Zaera,Massimo Capone*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文利用单格点与两格点团簇动力学平均场理论（DMFT），研究四分之一填充双轨道 Hubbard-Kanamori 模型中晶体场劈裂与非局域关联对"弱洪德金属"（weak Hund's metal）零温性质的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四分之一填充时每格点仅有一个电子，洪德耦合（Hund's coupling）的作用本不明显，但已有工作报道了有限温度下的弱洪德金属迹象，其零温性质及对轨道简并解除的稳健性尚不清楚。

Method: 采用单格点动力学平均场理论（single-site DMFT）与基于选定组态相互作用求解器（configuration-interaction solver）的两格点团簇 DMFT（cellular DMFT），考察零温下弱洪德金属的行为及其对晶体场劈裂的响应。

Result: 单格点 DMFT 给出的弱洪德金属边界与先前有限温度结果一致；洪德耦合的特征对轨道简并解除相当稳健，可延续至约四分之一带宽的劈裂，远超两级近藤屏蔽（two-stage Kondo screening）消失的劈裂尺度。团簇 DMFT 表明单格点图像依然成立，仅莫特局域化（Mott localization）出现在更小的相互作用处；金属态中格点间关联较弱且对 J 不敏感，局域态中则出现近邻铁磁、反铁轨道（ferromagnetic, antiferro-orbital）关联，并体现在格点间互信息（mutual information）中。

Conclusion: 洪德耦合印记在零温下对晶体场劈裂高度稳健，非局域关联不改变单格点 DMFT 的主要物理图像，仅修正局域化临界相互作用并引入局域态中的近邻自旋—轨道关联。

Abstract: We address the effect of crystal-field splitting and non-local correlations in the quarter-filled two-orbital
  Hubbard-Kanamori model. The role of the Hund's coupling in this configuration is not obvious since the atomic ground state
  has only one electron per site. Yet, previous works identified fingerprints of the Hund's coupling defining a weak Hund's
  metal at least at finite temperature.
  Using single-site dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT) and two-site cellular DMFT based on a selected
  configuration-interaction solver we investigate the zero-temperature properties of the weak Hund's metal. Within DMFT we draw
  the boundary for the weak Hund's metal, which is consistent with previous finite-temperature results.
  Interestingly, we find that the fingerprints of the Hund's coupling are quite robust to the lifting of the orbital
  degeneracy and they survive up to splittings of a quarter of the bandwidth, well beyond those at which a two-stage Kondo
  screening disappears.
  Including non-local correlations in cluster DMFT demonstrates the robustness of the single-site DMFT picture, even if Mott
  localization occurs at smaller interactions. In the metal the intersite correlations are weak and insensitive to $J$, while
  in the localized state we find nearest-neighbor ferromagnetic, antiferro-orbital correlations that are manifest also in the
  mutual information between sites.

</details>


### [180] [Orbital-dependent electronic correlations and single-band character in layered palladates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21138)
*Jing-Xuan Wang,Jia-Ming Wang,Rong-Qiang He,Zhong-Yi Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作以多种层状钯酸盐（layered palladates）为对象，通过密度泛函理论结合动力学平均场理论，系统描述其轨道与层分辨的电子结构与关联强度，建立从弱到中等、轨道依赖的关联图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状钯酸盐（layered palladates）具有类铜氧化物（cuprate-like）的结构与轨道特征，可作为 4d 电子体系研究电子关联的平台，但缺乏对多种结构类型的系统比较。

Method: 采用完全电荷自洽的密度泛函理论加动力学平均场理论（density functional theory plus dynamical mean-field theory），对 LaPdO$_2$、RbSr$_2$PdO$_3$、BaLaPdO$_2$Cl$_2$、La$_4$Pd$_3$O$_8$ 与 La$_5$Pd$_4$O$_{10}$ 进行轨道与层分辨的电子结构计算。

Result: 在所研究条件下所有化合物均保持金属性（metallic），低能态由 Pd-$d_{x^2-y^2}$ 轨道主导且其质量重整化（mass renormalization）强于 Pd-$d_{z^2}$；LaPdO$_2$ 与 BaLaPdO$_2$Cl$_2$ 为中等关联，RbSr$_2$PdO$_3$ 更弱，三层与四层结构（La$_4$Pd$_3$O$_8$、La$_5$Pd$_4$O$_{10}$）均为弱关联且内外层 Pd 位点差异很小。

Conclusion: BaLaPdO$_2$Cl$_2$ 最接近类铜氧化物的单带（single-band）描述，RbSr$_2$PdO$_3$ 则保留具有 Pd-$d_{z^2}$ 特征的额外电子口袋；结果给出层状钯酸盐中弱至中等、轨道依赖电子关联的比较性图景。

Abstract: Layered palladates provide a $4d$ platform for examining electronic correlations in materials with cuprate-like structural and orbital characteristics. Using fully charge-self-consistent density functional theory plus dynamical mean-field theory, we investigate the orbital- and layer-resolved electronic structures of LaPdO$_2$, RbSr$_2$PdO$_3$, BaLaPdO$_2$Cl$_2$, La$_4$Pd$_3$O$_8$, and La$_5$Pd$_4$O$_{10}$. Under the conditions studied, all compounds remain metallic, with low-energy states dominated by the Pd-$d_{x^2-y^2}$ orbital. This orbital exhibits stronger mass renormalization than the Pd-$d_{z^2}$ orbital. LaPdO$_2$ and BaLaPdO$_2$Cl$_2$ show moderate correlations, whereas RbSr$_2$PdO$_3$ is more weakly correlated. Both the trilayer La$_4$Pd$_3$O$_8$ and quadlayer La$_5$Pd$_4$O$_{10}$ remain weakly correlated, with similar Pd-$d_{x^2-y^2}$ mass enhancements and little difference between inner- and outer-layer Pd sites. Among the structures studied, BaLaPdO$_2$Cl$_2$ is closest to a cuprate-like single-band description, whereas RbSr$_2$PdO$_3$ retains an additional electron pocket with Pd-$d_{z^2}$ character. These results establish a comparative picture of weak to moderate, orbital-dependent electronic correlations in layered palladates.

</details>


### [181] [Self-attention is a Dyson equation for many-body machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21398)
*Jarvist Moore Frost*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该摘要将对称和池化（sum-pooling，即DeepSets）解释为平均场（mean-field, Hartree）模型，并将自注意力（self-attention）解释为实空间Dyson迭代，其Boltzmann（softmax）核充当两体自能（two-body self-energy）。借助Löwdin两体约化密度矩阵（2-RDM）定理与Galitskii–Migdal关系，论证两体传播子的迭代可捕捉关联物质的完整能量学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于解释一个悖论：对称和池化对基态势能可达亚meV精度，却在关联波函数（correlated wavefunctions）与激发态上失效。作者试图为这一"不合理有效性"及注意力机制的物理根源提供理论说明。

Method: 方法为理论分析：把和池化刻画为平均场Hartree模型，论证其非对角雅可比（off-diagonal Jacobian）为O(1/N)阶；把自注意力表述为实空间Dyson迭代，并将softmax核视为两体自能。并以分形回流行列式（fractal backflow determinant）的拟合实验作对比。

Result: 结果表明，拟合分形回流行列式时，DeepSets将节点面（nodal surface）模糊为平均场包络，而自注意力能恢复分形吸引子。基于Löwdin 2-RDM定理与Galitskii–Migdal关系，迭代两体传播子可保证捕捉关联物质的完整能量学。

Conclusion: 结论认为，注意力机制的有效性并非偶然：在由两体相互作用（pairwise interactions）构成的世界中，两体注意力即已足够（"two-body attention is all you need"）。

Abstract: Why does symmetric sum-pooling (DeepSets) predict ground-state potentials to sub-meV accuracy, yet fail for correlated wavefunctions and excited states? Sum-pooling is a mean-field (Hartree) model: its $O(1/N)$ off-diagonal Jacobian suffices for nearsighted ground-state energies, but erases nonlocal two-body correlations. We show that self-attention is a real-space Dyson iteration whose Boltzmann (softmax) kernel acts as a two-body self-energy. Because physical Hamiltonians act pairwise, Löwdin's 2-RDM theorem and the Galitskii--Migdal relation guarantee that iterating a two-body propagator captures the full energetics of correlated matter. When fitting a fractal backflow determinant, DeepSets blurs the nodal surface onto a mean-field envelope, whereas self-attention recovers the fractal attractor. Seen through this lens, the unreasonable effectiveness of attention is not an accident; in a universe built from pairwise interactions, two-body attention is all you need.

</details>


### [182] [Hyperkagome antiferromagnetism in the iron garnet Almandine Fe$_3$Al$_2$Si$_3$O$_{12}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21610)
*Minnong Zhang,Harald O. Jeschke,Daniel Guterding*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 铁铝榴石（almandine，Fe₃Al₂Si₃O₁₂）被确认为自旋-2 的超烧绿石晶格（hyperkagome lattice）反铁磁体，其微观海森堡模型预言约 9.8 K 的反铁磁有序，并与比热实验高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超烧绿石晶格上的反铁磁体可提供三维强磁阻挫（magnetic frustration）路径，但具有明确微观哈密顿量的实验可实现体系仍很稀少；石榴石（garnet）家族中磁性离子可构成两套相互穿插的超烧绿石晶格，因而成为潜在载体。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）能量映射，为高自旋 Fe²⁺ 矩构建海森堡哈密顿量（Heisenberg Hamiltonian）；对该哈密顿量进行经典蒙特卡罗（classical Monte Carlo）模拟，并结合自旋长度的量子-经典重标度（quantum-to-classical rescaling）。

Result: 模型给出以最近邻反铁磁交换为主、并含显著长程相互作用的交换参数；模拟得到 T_N ≈ 9.8 K 的反铁磁转变，与比热实验吻合良好。基态为非共线磁结构，可自然由磁环状八极序（magnetic toroidal octupolar order）描述，并与钆镓石榴石（Gd₃Ga₅O₁₂）的已知结果相联系。

Conclusion: 研究进一步计算了磁中子衍射振幅及静态与动态结构因子（半经典动力学），为后续中子散射实验提供定量预测；由此确立铁铝榴石为石榴石家族中化学上可实现的阻挫超烧绿石磁性体系。

Abstract: Antiferromagnets on the hyperkagome lattice provide a three-dimensional route to strong magnetic frustration, but experimentally accessible realizations with well-established microscopic Hamiltonians remain scarce. Garnets are a broad family of materials in which magnetic ions can form two interpenetrating hyperkagome lattices, giving rise to spin glass, spin-liquid and hidden-order physics. Here we identify the iron aluminosilicate garnet almandine, Fe$_3$Al$_2$Si$_3$O$_{12}$, as a spin-$2$ hyperkagome antiferromagnet. Using density functional theory energy mapping, we determine a Heisenberg Hamiltonian for the high-spin Fe$^{2+}$ moments and find dominant nearest-neighbor antiferromagnetic exchange, as well as significant longer-range interactions. Classical Monte Carlo simulations of this Hamiltonian, combined with quantum-to-classical rescaling of the spin length, show a transition to antiferromagnetic order at $T_{\rm N}\approx9.8$K, in excellent agreement with specific heat experiments. The resulting non-collinear magnetic state is naturally described by magnetic toroidal octupolar order, which we connect to known results for the highly frustrated hyperkagome antiferromagnet gadolinium gallium garnet, Gd$_3$Ga$_5$O$_{12}$. We also calculate magnetic neutron diffraction amplitudes as well as static and dynamic structure factors using semiclassical dynamics, providing quantitative predictions for future neutron scattering experiments. Our results establish almandine as a chemically accessible realization of frustrated hyperkagome magnetism in the garnet family.

</details>

### [183] [Accelerating Atomistic-Continuum Simulations of Multilayer 2D Materials with Functional Principal Component Analysis-Based Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18961)
*Jonathan Oleson,Abhishek Keripale,Shashank Pathrudkar,Susanta Ghosh*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个机器学习（machine learning, ML）加速框架，基于二维函数型主成分分析（functional principal component analysis, FPCA）构建变形场低维表示，再以神经网络预测主成分并将其作为原子-连续耦合求解器的高质量初始构型，从而加速大面积多层二维材料的褶皱变形模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大面积多层二维（two-dimensional, 2D）材料的原子-连续耦合（atomistic-continuum）模拟计算代价高昂；且变形场离散维度极高、需保持曲面连续性与给定几何约束、褶皱模式具有随机性、训练数据有限，使降维本身难度很大。

Method: 采用二维 FPCA 对变形场做降维，将含数百万自由度的场压缩到仅数十维的潜空间（latent space），并通过恰当选取基函数精确满足齐次边界约束；随后训练神经网络由系统参数与加载参数预测主成分，预测所得变形场作为原子-连续求解器的初始构型，再经能量最小化（energy minimization）得到平衡解。

Result: 在多层石墨烯（multilayer graphene）的单轴压缩、双轴压缩及双轴压缩与简单剪切（simple shear）联合加载工况下验证；对计算量最大的大应变算例，加速至少 6.8 倍，且最终能量与传统原子-连续初始化相当，部分情形更低。

Conclusion: FPCA 低维表示能在保持曲面连续性与精确满足边界约束的前提下有效压缩高维变形场，结合神经网络预测初始化可显著加速复杂褶皱变形模拟而不牺牲能量精度。

Abstract: Atomistic-continuum simulations of complex wrinkling deformations in large-area multilayer two-dimensional (2D) materials are computationally expensive. To accelerate these simulations, this work presents a machine-learning (ML) framework that first constructs a low-dimensional representation of the deformation field, then learns the deformation in this reduced space. Dimensionality reduction of deformed 2D membranes is challenging because of the extremely high dimensionality of discretized deformation fields, the need to preserve surface continuity and prescribed geometric constraints, the stochastic nature of wrinkling patterns, and the limited availability of training data. We address these challenges using two-dimensional functional principal component analysis (FPCA). The proposed FPCA representation reduces deformation fields containing millions of degrees of freedom to latent spaces with only tens of dimensions while preserving the continuity of the deformed surface. In addition, prescribed homogeneous boundary constraints are satisfied exactly through an appropriate choice of basis functions in the FPCA representation. A neural network is then trained to predict the principal components from the system and loading parameters. The resulting ML-predicted deformation fields are used as high-quality initial configurations for the atomistic-continuum solver, which subsequently performs energy minimization to obtain equilibrium solutions. The framework is demonstrated for multilayer graphene subjected to uniaxial compression, biaxial compression, and combined biaxial compression and simple shear. For the most computationally intensive large-strain cases, the proposed approach achieves speedups of at least 6.8 times while converging to final energies comparable to, and in some cases lower than, those obtained using conventional atomistic-continuum initialization.

</details>


### [184] [U-HamGNN: a universal model for magnetic Hamiltonians conditioned on spin and Hubbard U](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20035)
*Hongyu Yu,Yinze Hu,Yizhou Chen,Yang Cheng,Yingwei Chen,Xingao Gong,Yang Zhong,Hongjun Xiang*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出U-HamGNN，一种等变网络，直接以自旋构型与在位Hubbard校正U为输入，预测多类晶体化学体系的自旋分辨哈密顿量，避免逐个组合运行DFT+U。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性晶体的电子结构同时依赖自旋构型与在位Hubbard校正U，传统上每个组合都需单独进行DFT+U计算，成本高昂。

Method: 构建等变网络（equivariant network）U-HamGNN，直接接收自旋构型与U两项输入；并设计谱混合优化器（spectral-hybrid optimizer），按梯度方向的一致性对各更新方向加权。

Result: 十轮（ten-epoch）基准中，哈密顿量误差较Muon降低8%、较AdamW降低26%；预测能带在训练窗口内外均再现U依赖的带隙打开与交替磁性（altermagnetic）自旋劈裂；由哈密顿量导出的交换耦合与磁序判定在标定集上与DFT+U一致。

Conclusion: 该通用模型为磁性研究提供了新工具，可替代逐组合的DFT+U计算。

Abstract: The electronic structure of a magnetic crystal depends jointly on its spin configuration and the on-site Hubbard correction U, requiring a separate DFT+U calculation for each combination. We introduce U-HamGNN, an equivariant network that takes these two inputs directly and predicts spin-resolved Hamiltonians across diverse crystal chemistries. A spectral-hybrid optimizer that weights each update direction by how consistently the gradient points along it reaches 8% lower Hamiltonian error than Muon and 26% lower than AdamW in a ten-epoch benchmark. The predicted bands reproduce U-dependent gap opening and altermagnetic spin splitting within the trained window and beyond it; exchange couplings and magnetic-order calls derived from the Hamiltonian agree with DFT+U on a calibration set. This universal model provides a new tool for magnetic research.

</details>


### [185] [FiuMD: Heterogeneous Batching for Coarse-Grained Molecular Dynamics with Neural Network Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20429)
*Hugo D. Hernández R.,Paola B. Torres,Lara Negri,Rocío L. Cecchini,Nicolás A. García*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: FiuMD 是 GPU 分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）引擎，将多个规模、拓扑、力场、周期盒、时间步与恒温器参数各异的生物分子体系拼接为单一异构批次（heterogeneous batch）统一计算；在双精度下力与孤立评估的差异至多 7×10⁻¹⁶，1000 步轨迹偏差不超过 2.5×10⁻¹¹ Å，并实现至多数倍的吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个生物分子模拟规模往往不足以填满 GPU（GPU）算力，导致吞吐（throughput）受限；而不同体系异构性（heterogeneity）使批量并行难以直接实现，因此需要一个能在统一批次中推进多个异构体系的 MD 引擎。

Method: 将所有体系拼接进全局张量（global tensors），对整批一次性计算经典相互作用项（classical interaction terms）与共享的 TorchMD-Net 神经网络势（neural network potential），同时每个体系保留自身的积分器（integrator）与恒温器（thermostat）；FiuMD 由 TorchMD 分支（fork）而来，重写了体系表示、力评估与积分模块。

Result: 在 chignolin、Trp-cage、BBL 与 λ-repressor 的粗粒化 Cα 模型（coarse-grained Cα model）上：双精度下 60 个体系的最大相对力差为 7×10⁻¹⁶；无恒温器 1000 步轨迹偏差至多 2.5×10⁻¹¹ Å（含周期像落入神经网络截断内的情况）；各体系动能温度（kinetic temperature）的均值与宽度均符合其自身恒温器预期。单 GPU 上四个不同蛋白体系相比顺序 TorchMD 每步加速 4.6-7.8 倍；相比顺序同质（homogeneous）FiuMD 批次，小批次最高约 4 倍加速，大批次性能相当。

Conclusion: 异构批量（heterogeneous batching）可在保持与孤立模拟数值一致性的同时，显著提高小体系 GPU 利用率与计算吞吐，是一种可行的多体系并行 MD 方案。

Abstract: FiuMD is a GPU molecular dynamics engine that advances many independent biomolecular simulations as a single batch, even when the systems differ in size, topology, force-field parameters, periodic box, timestep, or thermostat parameters. All systems are concatenated into global tensors, so the classical interaction terms and a shared TorchMD-Net neural network potential are evaluated for the whole batch at once, while each system keeps its own integration and thermostat. FiuMD started as a fork of TorchMD and reworks its system representation, force evaluation, and integration to handle such heterogeneous batches. We test it with coarse-grained C$α$ models of chignolin, Trp-cage, BBL, and $λ$-repressor. In double precision, forces within a heterogeneous batch agree with isolated FiuMD and TorchMD evaluations to within the precision of repeated evaluations, with a maximum relative difference of $7\times10^{-16}$ across 60 systems. Over 1000 steps without a thermostat, batched trajectories deviate by at most $2.5\times10^{-11}$ Angstrom from isolated ones, even when periodic images lie within the neural network cutoff. With a different target temperature for each system of the ensemble, the mean and width of each system's kinetic temperature match the values expected from its own thermostat. We show that when individual simulations are too small to fill the GPU, batching can improve throughput several-fold over TorchMD. On a single GPU, batching four different protein systems achieves a 4.6-7.8-fold speedup per step compared with sequential TorchMD simulations. Compared with sequential homogeneous FiuMD batches, heterogeneous batching provides up to a 4-fold speedup for smaller batches while maintaining comparable performance for larger ones.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [Counting real meromorphic differentials on $\mathbb{CP}^1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19450)
*Alexandr Buryak,Guillaume Tahar*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文研究带实结构的黎曼球面（real structure on $\mathbb{CP}^1$）上亚纯微分（meromorphic differentials）的实计数问题，给出全部情形下的显式计数公式，并在双共轭零点与无留数极点情形下建立与开WDVV方程及KP族波函数方程组的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复数情形下的计数问题（固定零点与极点阶数、指定极点留数为零，模去 $\mathrm{PGL}_2(\mathbb{C})$ 作用与非零常数乘法）已被前人完全解决，并已知其与WDVV方程、KP族的联系；作者希望建立相应的实（real）类比，以刻画反全纯对合（antiholomorphic involution）下的计数结构。

Method: 要求亚纯微分在 $\mathbb{CP}^1$ 的实结构下保持不变，构造实计数问题，并按其细化情形（如指定被反全纯对合固定的极点与零点的阶数）分别处理，推导显式计数公式。

Result: 获得所有实计数问题的显式公式；在实亚纯微分具有两个复共轭零点（complex conjugate zeros）且极点为无留数（residueless）的情形下，建立其与开WDVV方程（open WDVV equations）以及KP族波函数方程组的联系；并给出对实Hurwitz数（real Hurwitz numbers）的应用。

Conclusion: 复计数问题的实类比具备完整的显式解，并与开WDVV方程、KP族波函数系统存在对应关系，同时可用于实Hurwitz数的研究，为实代数几何与可积系统（integrable systems）的交叉提供了新工具。

Abstract: The counting problem for meromorphic differentials on $\mathbb{CP}^1$ with fixed orders of zeros and poles and vanishing residues at some specific poles, up to the action of $\mathrm{PGL}_2(\mathbb{C})$ and the multiplication by a nonzero constant, was completely solved before by several authors. In all the cases when there are finitely many such meromorphic differentials, an explicit formula for their number was found and also, in the case of residueless meromorphic differentials with two zeros, a link to the WDVV equations and the KP hierarchy was established. We consider a real analog of these counting problems by requiring that a meromorphic differential is preserved by a real structure on $\mathbb{CP}^1$. Our real counting problems admit refinements, obtained for example by specifying the orders of poles and zeros that are fixed by the antiholomorphic involution. We obtain explicit formulas for all our real counting problems and also, in the case of real meromorphic differentials with two complex conjugate zeros and residueless poles, establish a link to the open WDVV equations and the system of equations for the wave function of the KP hierarchy. An application to real Hurwitz numbers is also presented.

</details>


### [187] [Heavy-Light Wall Crossings and Motivic Asymptotics of Moduli Spaces of Curves. Part II: Tameness of Motivic Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21569)
*Giordano Cotti*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文研究稳定带点曲线模空间（moduli spaces of stable pointed curves）及其 all-light Hassett 紧化在标记数趋于无穷时的固定亏格渐近性，通过 motivic wall-crossing 建立两类母函数之间的普适变量替换，并在 H_g 假设下得到显式首项渐近，进而应用于 Hodge-Deligne 赋值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标是把模空间 motivic 测度的形式恒等式转化为可计算的渐近结果：随着标记数（number of markings）趋于无穷，需要刻画两个腔室（chambers）中稳定曲线模空间与 all-light Hassett 紧化测度的首项行为，并给出可由几何条件验证的解析充分条件。

Method: 方法包含三个层次：以 motivic wall-crossing 论证给出两类生成级数（generating series）之间仅依赖 Lefschetz 类的普适变量变换；借助稳定化图（stabilization graphs）与支撑模式（support patterns）将 all-light 级数分解为普适链因子（chain factors）与有限剩余商族（residual quotient families），隔离出有理三价（rational trivalent）贡献；随后引入顶点驯顺性（vertex tameness）与剩余商驯顺性（residual-quotient tameness），其合取即图驯顺性（graph tameness），作为 H_g 假设的几何充分条件。

Result: 在亏格（genus）至少为 2 的情形，得到两个腔室中显式的首项渐近（leading asymptotics），其仅依赖于亏格与 Lefschetz 值；亏格 0 与 1 单独处理。作为应用，证明 Hodge-Deligne 赋值满足图驯顺性，并由此推出固定乘积的普适性（fixed-product universality）与 Hodge 数在对角线上的渐近集中（asymptotic concentration）。

Conclusion: 结论是形式分解与解析假设相结合可为 motivic 测度提供系统的渐近框架：wall-crossing 变换与图分解给出不依赖具体测度的普适结构，而图驯顺性提供可验证的几何判据。该框架在 Hodge-Deligne 赋值上的成功应用表明其具备推广价值，但摘要未对亏格 0、1 的渐近形式及其他测度的适用性作进一步说明。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with the study of the fixed-genus asymptotics of motivic measures of moduli spaces of stable pointed curves and their minimal all-light Hassett compactifications as the number of markings tends to infinity. A motivic wall-crossing argument gives a universal change of variables between the two generating series, depending only on the value of the Lefschetz class. For an arbitrary motivic measure, we use stabilization graphs and support patterns to express the all-light series in terms of universal chain factors and finite residual quotient families. This formal decomposition isolates the rational trivalent contribution without requiring a fixed-locus formula for finite quotients.
  To pass from formal identities to asymptotics, we formulate analytic hypotheses (the $\mathrm{H}_g$-hypotheses) for scalar realizations with positive real Lefschetz value. We introduce vertex tameness and residual-quotient tameness, whose conjunction, graph tameness, provides geometric sufficient conditions for the $\mathrm{H}_g$-hypotheses. In genus at least two, this yields explicit leading asymptotics in both chambers, depending only on the genus and the Lefschetz value; genera zero and one are treated separately. As an application, we prove graph tameness for Hodge-Deligne evaluations and deduce fixed-product universality and asymptotic concentration of Hodge numbers on the diagonal.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.HE'></div>

# astro-ph.HE [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [A 3+1 formalism for self-similar expanding grids in computational relativistic and Newtonian magnetohydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20865)
*N. Bucciantini,L. Del Zanna*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于度规修改的膨胀网格（expanding-grid）策略，利用3+1形式体系的规范自由度，仅通过重新定义度规张量即可在无需改动数值网格或代码核心的前提下实现空间膨胀坐标，并在相对论与牛顿流体/磁流体标准测试中验证了其一致性、精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天体物理系统演化中常发生空间膨胀，使用固定网格的守恒型流体与磁流体（MHD）代码难以在大尺度变化下保持高分辨率；移动/膨胀网格或自适应网格细化（AMR）虽可缓解，但引入计算复杂度且实现鲁棒性差。作者希望提供一种易于实现、可推广为标准实践的方案。

Method: 借助弯曲流形上MHD的3+1形式体系（3+1 formalism）的规范自由度（gauge freedom）——该基础为所有广义相对论流体代码所共有——通过单纯修改底层度规（metric）来实现膨胀坐标系，无需改动数值网格或代码核心结构；所需度规项的增改在牛顿MHD中亦可轻松完成。

Result: 将该框架应用于相对论与牛顿流体力学及MHD的标准测试问题，直接比较固定坐标与膨胀坐标的结果，显示该基于度规的膨胀方法具有完全的一致性、精度与鲁棒性，且原有守恒形式被完整保留。

Conclusion: 通过简单的坐标变换与度规张量（metric tensor）重定义，任何广义相对论MHD代码均可轻松转化为膨胀网格代码；该方法优雅地保证基本守恒性质与磁场的无散条件（solenoidal condition），并可扩展至非理想效应。

Abstract: Astrophysical systems frequently undergo spatial expansion during their evolution. Simulating these systems with standard conservative codes for hydrodynamics and magnetohydrodynamics using fixed grids is challenging, particularly when attempting to preserve high resolution across vast changes in physical scale. While moving or expanding meshes, or adapting mesh refinement, can address this issue, they introduce computational complexity and are notoriously difficult to implement robustly. We aim to overcome these barriers by providing an easily implementable strategy that empowers the community to achieve high-fidelity results and establishes expanding-grid approaches as a highly accessible standard practice. Leveraging the gauge freedom of the 3+1 formalism for MHD on curved manifolds, a foundation common to all general relativistic fluid dynamics codes, we demonstrate how an expanding coordinate system can be implemented purely through a simple modification of the underlying metric. This requires zero modifications to the numerical mesh or the core structure of the code, and the necessary addition or modification of metric terms can be easily accomplished even in Newtonian MHD. We apply this framework to standard test problems in both relativistic and Newtonian hydrodynamics and MHD, directly comparing the results of fixed and expanding coordinates. Our tests demonstrate the full consistency, accuracy, and robustness of this metric-based expansion approach, as the original conservative formulations is fully preserved. Through a simple coordinate transformation and a re-definition of the metric tensor, any general relativistic MHD code can be effortlessly transformed into an expanding-grid code. This approach elegantly guarantees both fundamental conservation properties and the solenoidal condition for the magnetic field, and can be readily extended to include non-ideal effects.

</details>


<div id='hep-lat'></div>

# hep-lat [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [Nonperturbative lattice simulation of the nucleation rate in thermal first-order phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18794)
*Jing Zhou,Ligong Bian*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文对有限温度三维实标量场论中的真空衰变（vacuum decay）进行非微扰格点研究，结合扩展系综多正则混合蒙特卡罗与实时朗之万动力学计算气泡成核率，并将结果外推至连续极限后与一圈微扰预言比较，差异约8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真空衰变率的微扰计算（如一圈近似）在非微扰区域的可信度需要独立检验，因此需要一个不依赖微扰展开的格点方法来直接计算气泡成核率（bubble nucleation rate）。

Method: 采用非微扰格点方法研究有限温度三维实标量场论；用扩展系综多正则混合蒙特卡罗（expanded-ensemble multicanonical hybrid Monte Carlo）对临界气泡构型进行采样，再以实时朗之万动力学（real-time Langevin dynamics）追踪气泡的后续演化，并将成核率外推至连续极限（continuum limit）。

Result: 将外推后的成核率（nucleation rate）与对应的一圈微扰预言（one-loop perturbative prediction）比较，两者差异约为8%。

Conclusion: 该非微扰格点方案可自洽地给出有限温度真空衰变率，其结果与一圈微扰预言的约8%偏差为该近似在此情形下的精度提供了定量参照。

Abstract: We present a nonperturbative lattice study of vacuum decay through bubble nucleation in a three-dimensional real scalar field theory at finite temperature. The vacuum decay rate is computed by combining an expanded-ensemble multicanonical hybrid Monte Carlo approach, which samples the critical bubble configurations, with real-time Langevin dynamics, which follows the subsequent bubble evolution. We extrapolate the nucleation rate to the continuum limit and compare it with the corresponding one-loop perturbative prediction, finding a discrepancy of about \(8\%\).

</details>


### [190] [Fate of chiral and $U(1)_A$ symmetries in hot QCD matter under background magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19758)
*Dan Zhang,Heng-Tong Ding,José Javier Hernández Hernández*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文总结了在背景磁场下对轻夸克手征对称性与单态轴矢 $U(1)_A$ 对称性破缺的格点 QCD 研究，发现磁场在低温下增强两类磁化率差，而在相变交叉区域附近足够强的磁场反而抑制二者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁场通过不等的光夸克电荷显式破坏非单态味道对称性，使得手征对称性与单态轴矢 $U(1)_A$ 对称性（singlet $U(1)_A$ symmetry）的破缺需要在合适的观测量中分别刻画，动机在于明确磁场对这两类破缺的影响。

Method: 采用固定标度（fixed-scale）的 $(2+1)$ 味 HISQ 系综进行格点 QCD（lattice QCD）计算，取 $m_l=m_s^{\mathrm{phys}}/10$（零场π介子质量 $M_\pi\simeq220\,\mathrm{MeV}$），构造两个中性道磁化率差 $\chi_{\pi^0}-\chi_\sigma$ 与 $\chi_{\pi^0}-\chi_{\delta^0}$ 分别作为两类对称性破缺的探针。

Result: 低温下磁场增强两个磁化率差；在交叉区域（crossover）附近，足够强的磁场则抑制二者。单态轴矢磁化率差的抑制出现在更大的磁场处，且相比中性手征磁化率差更温和。

Conclusion: 磁场对手征对称性与 $U(1)_A$ 破缺的作用具有温度与场强依赖性：低温为增强、近交叉区强场转为抑制，且抑制行为在两类对称性破缺之间存在定量差异。

Abstract: We summarize our recent lattice QCD study of chiral and singlet $U(1)_A$ symmetry breaking in background magnetic fields. The magnetic field explicitly reduces the non-singlet flavor symmetry through the unequal light-quark electric charges. We therefore study two neutral-channel susceptibility differences: $χ_{π^0}-χ_σ$ for chiral symmetry breaking and $χ_{π^0}-χ_{δ^0}$ for singlet $U(1)_A$ symmetry breaking. The calculation uses fixed-scale $(2+1)$-flavor HISQ ensembles with $m_l=m_s^{\mathrm{phys}}/10$, corresponding to a zero-field pion mass of $M_π\simeq220\,\mathrm{MeV}$. Magnetic fields enhance both susceptibility differences at low temperatures. Near the crossover, sufficiently strong fields instead suppress both differences. The suppression sets in at larger magnetic fields and is milder for the singlet-axial susceptibility difference than for the neutral chiral susceptibility difference.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Matching two-dimensional statistics does not certify three-dimensional transport in battery electrodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20121)
*Maziar Raissi,Devesh Upadhyay*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 该研究检验“匹配二维统计即可重建三维性质”这一常规假设，发现统计等效的“双胞胎”电极结构在导电性上可比真实电极低10–88倍，说明标准二维统计无法保证三维输运性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由二维显微图像重建三维电池电极结构并被用于预测输运性质，其前提是匹配二维统计量即可复现相关三维性质；该前提此前缺乏严格检验。

Method: 基于三种全固态正极的X射线断层扫描（X-ray tomograms），使用可微电导率求解器（differentiable conductivity solver），在测量噪声范围内逐切片、逐层地在所有截面上优化体素结构，使其在相分数（phase fractions）、边界密度、两点与三点相关函数（two- and three-point correlations）及线性路径函数（lineal-path functions）上与真实电极匹配。

Result: 每个样品均存在统计双胞胎（statistical twins），其电导比真实电极低10–88倍，且对5³多数滤波（5³ majority filtering）稳健；而导电性最高的双胞胎至多高出真实电极3.3倍。双胞胎通过重排而非移除材料来切断或汇聚面外路径。二维截面上的分类器可识别这些结构，对现有二维到三维生成器（2D-to-3D generators）亦然。

Conclusion: 相关信息存在于图像中但不在统计量中；因此匹配标准二维统计并不能认证三维输运性质，需谨慎对待基于二维重建的输运预测。

Abstract: Three-dimensional battery electrode structures reconstructed from two-dimensional micrographs are routinely used to predict transport, assuming that matching the measured 2D statistics reproduces the relevant 3D properties. We test this premise on X-ray tomograms of three all-solid-state cathodes. With a differentiable conductivity solver, we optimise voxel structures that match the real electrode in phase fractions, boundary density, two- and three-point correlations and lineal-path functions, on all section planes, slab by slab and layer by layer, within measurement noise. Every sample has statistical twins conducting 10-88-fold less than the real electrode, robust to 5^3 majority filtering, whereas the most conductive are at most 3.3-fold above it. The twins rearrange rather than remove material, severing or funnelling through-plane paths. A classifier on 2D sections detects them, as it does current 2D-to-3D generators: the information is in the images but not in the statistics. Matching standard 2D statistics therefore does not certify 3D transport.

</details>

### [192] [Electric-magnetic asymmetry of exact dyonic Euler-Heisenberg black holes: duality-blind shadows and magnetically controlled horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18694)
*Vahideh Memari,İzzet Sakallı*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究精确双荷 Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg 黑洞中电荷交换对称性破缺的可观测记忆：光子球外几乎不可见（阴影、Lyapunov 指数、ISCO 偏移仅为百万分之几），而核心区域与极值极限判据对磁荷高度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Maxwell 理论具有电磁旋转不变性，Reissner-Nordström 黑洞仅依赖电荷平方和；Euler-Heisenberg 真空极化项破坏该对称性，因此需要判定哪些物理性质"记住"了这一破缺。

Method: 在近期得到的精确双荷 Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg 黑洞解基础上，对度规函数做电荷交换下的奇偶阶展开，分析阴影（shadow）、Lyapunov 指数与最内稳定圆轨道（innermost stable circular orbit, ISCO）的偏移，并以两个独立积分核验数值结果、用事件视界望远镜（Event Horizon Telescope）对人马座 A*（Sagittarius A*）的观测检验阴影。

Result: 耦合一阶下度规函数对电荷交换为偶；首个奇宇称项在二阶出现，按半径十次方反比衰减；其对阴影、Lyapunov 指数与 ISCO 的位移维持在百万分之几量级。核心处任何磁荷都使度规函数趋于负无穷从而强制存在视界，而场能轻于自身电场能量的电性构型可以无视界；极值极限（extremal limit）是否存在由质量函数导数的最大值这一电荷函数决定，其临界曲线沿电轴到达原点，并在磁轴上取得闭式值。

Conclusion: 电荷交换对称性破缺在黑洞外部基本不可观测，但在核心结构与极值极限的存在性判据上起决定作用；所有数值由两条独立积分路径确认，阴影与 Sgr A* 观测相容。

Abstract: Maxwell theory is invariant under electric-magnetic rotations, and the Reissner-Nordström black hole depends on its charges only through the sum of their squares. The Euler-Heisenberg vacuum-polarization terms break this symmetry, and an exact dyonic Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg black hole was found recently. We ask which properties remember the breaking. Outside the photon sphere, almost none do. The metric function is even under charge exchange through first order in the coupling, the first odd term appears at second order and falls off as the tenth inverse power of the radius, and the shifts it causes in the shadow, the Lyapunov exponent, and the innermost stable circular orbit stay at a few parts per million. The core behaves differently. Any magnetic charge drives the metric function to negative infinity at the center and so forces a horizon, while an electric configuration lighter than its field energy can be horizonless. Whether an extremal limit exists is decided by one function of the charges, the maximum of the derivative of the mass function, whose critical curve reaches the origin along the electric axis and meets the magnetic axis at a closed-form value. Two independent integrations confirm every number, and the shadow is tested against the Event Horizon Telescope image of Sagittarius A*.

</details>


### [193] [Transient Information in Moving-Mirror Quantum Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18695)
*M. Baran Ökten,Ash S. Turan,Michael R. R. Good,Kuan-Nan Lin,Pisin Chen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究在运动镜（moving mirror）回到共同热轨迹后，有限动力学历史如何仍被编码于量子场中；通过两族相关的 Carlitz–Willey 射线映射形变，区分了瞬态记忆在协方差、干涉与线性响应中的奇偶结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有运动镜模型关注渐近霍金温度（Hawking temperature）等终态热性质，但有限历史是否留下可观测残迹缺乏系统刻画；本文旨在揭示瞬态历史在量子场中的保留方式。

Method: 构造 Carlitz–Willey 射线映射的两个紧致形变，二者由控制参数反号相联系并共享同一条渐近零线（asymptotic null line）与霍金温度；借助剥离场（peeling field）与 Bogoliubov 变换（Bogoliubov transformation）的精确奇偶分级（even–odd grading），分析复连续协方差、单色二次响应及有限带宽波包的干涉。

Result: 复连续协方差在一阶保留瞬态符号，而主导单色二次响应在反号下为偶；有限带宽波包将该差异转为与热参考协方差的有符号干涉。线性响应下，所选右行波包的带符号平方关联对带宽为四次，匹配的 Rindler/Milne 波包为三次；相对交叉协方差相位随带宽线性变化，其首项系数在归一化实对称窗中以平窗（flat window）幅度最大。

Conclusion: 有限振幅修正会在平方 Rindler/Milne 响应中引入额外的线性带宽项；具有区分能力的波包在推迟时间（retarded time）上采样瞬态历史，而两条轨迹共享相同的渐近霍金温度，说明历史记忆可由带宽与相位结构读出。

Abstract: We study how a finite dynamical history remains encoded in a quantum field after a moving mirror returns to a common thermal trajectory. Two compact deformations of the Carlitz--Willey ray map are related by reversal of a control parameter and share the same asymptotic null line and Hawking temperature. The pairing induces an exact even--odd grading of the peeling field and Bogoliubov transformation. The complex continuum covariance retains the transient sign at first order, while the leading monochromatic quadratic response is even under reversal. Finite-bandwidth wave packets convert this distinction into signed interference with the thermal reference covariance. In linear response, the signed squared correlation is quartic in bandwidth for the selected right-moving packets and cubic for matched Rindler/Milne packets. The relative cross-covariance phase has a linear bandwidth dependence, with its leading coefficient maximized in magnitude by the flat window among normalized real symmetric windows. Finite-amplitude corrections introduce an additional linear bandwidth term in the squared Rindler/Milne response. The distinguishing packets sample the transient history in retarded time, while both trajectories share the same asymptotic Hawking temperature.

</details>


### [194] [Kalb-Ramond black bounces: throat-blind shadows and throat-sensitive neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18697)
*Faizuddin Ahmed,Ahmad Al-Badawi,İzzet Sakallı*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究在洛伦兹破坏（Lorentz-violating）Kalb-Ramond 背景所生成的幂律毛发黑洞上构建的 Simpson-Visser 黑洞反弹（black bounce），指出反弹只改变径向测度（radial measure）而不改变度规函数，因此观测量可被分为对喉部（throat）“盲”与“响应”两类，并给出亚环（subring）去放大效应的精确普适规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则化（regularizing）黑洞消除了奇点，但哪些观测能够探测到正则化核心仍不清楚；本文旨在系统判别对该核心敏感的观测量。

Method: 以面积半径（areal radius）度量，利用反弹仅改变径向测度、不影响度规函数这一性质对观测量分类；推导适用于任意度规函数的亚环去放大（demagnification）精确规律，并结合湍流薄盘的偏振光线追踪（ray-traced polarized imaging）、中微子振荡相位与中微子对湮灭能量沉积计算。

Result: 阴影（shadow）、最内稳定轨道（ISCO）、轨道频率与引力红移对喉部不敏感；光子环不稳定性、亚环亮度、中微子振荡相位与湮灭能量沉积通过单一普适因子响应喉部。弱场中微子相位与光线偏折（light deflection）中喉部以不同权重进入，故光子与中微子联合透镜可分离质量与喉部贡献；光线追踪显示直接像隐藏喉部而第一亚环将其暴露，恒星质量天体附近中微子的味存活模式被重塑、湮灭能量沉积增强。

Conclusion: 反弹核心的可探测性取决于观测量对径向测度的依赖方式；亚环与中微子相关观测量是探测喉部的主要通道，双通道联合可区分质量与喉部参数，且各主要数值由两项独立计算相互验证。

Abstract: Regularizing a black hole removes its singularity, but which observations could detect the regularized core? We address this question for a Simpson-Visser black bounce built on the power-law hairy black hole that a Lorentz-violating Kalb-Ramond background produces. Measured in the areal radius, the bounce leaves the metric function untouched and changes only the radial measure. This single fact sorts the observables into two classes. The shadow, the innermost stable orbit, orbital frequencies and gravitational redshifts are blind to the throat. The instability of the photon ring, the brightness of its subrings, the oscillation phase of neutrinos and the energy deposited by neutrino pair annihilation respond to it through one universal factor. We derive an exact law for the subring demagnification that holds for any metric function. We also show that the throat enters the weak-field neutrino phase and the light deflection with different weights, so that combined photon and neutrino lensing can separate the mass from the throat. Ray-traced polarized images of a turbulent thin disk confirm that the direct image hides the throat while the first subring reveals it. For neutrinos emitted near a stellar-mass compact object the throat reshapes the flavor survival pattern and enhances the annihilation energy deposition. Two independent computations reproduce each headline number.

</details>


### [195] [Metric perturbations in Einstein-Hilbert gravity: An alternative perturbative formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18698)
*Sergey Bondarenko,Raghvendra Singh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: No AI summary available.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: No AI summary available.

Method: No AI summary available.

Result: No AI summary available.

Conclusion: No AI summary available.

Abstract: We derive the ordinary gauge-fixed quadratic operators of the four-dimensional Einstein--Hilbert action in exponential and inverse-metric-density variables on an arbitrary reference background. With a single nonlinear background-harmonic gauge transported through the exact field map, the density operator is the trace-reversed Lichnerowicz operator, while the exponential operator contains an additional algebraic Ricci-curvature term. This difference arises from the nonlinear transformation of the background tadpole and vanishes on Ricci-flat backgrounds; a cosmological-constant check gives the corresponding cancellation at Einstein saddles. The construction retains the exact determinant contribution and specifies the associated Faddeev--Popov operator. At the first interacting order on a flat background, we show that the two gauge-fixed cubic actions differ by a term proportional to the free field equation, and independently evaluate the same nonzero three-point coefficient in both variables, with its normalization matched to the Einstein-gravity interaction. An exact Schwarzschild configuration satisfies the same background-harmonic gauge and is stationary under unrestricted exterior variations of the gauge-fixed action. Its density and exponential reconstructions are checked together with the bulk and boundary terms; a separate radial variation illustrates the loss of an equation after imposing a restricted ansatz. These results provide an explicit comparison of established field coordinates at the classical and tree levels; quantum loop calculations additionally require a common measure, regulator and source prescription.

</details>


### [196] [Optical Appearances and Multiple Lensed Rings of Self-Interacting Boson Stars with a Double-Well Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18700)
*Zefang Fan,Sen Guo,Xianggao Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 自相互作用玻色子星（self-interacting boson stars）作为无视界天体（horizonless objects）的光学外观与多重透镜环被系统研究；发展混合度规重构框架实现高精度光线追踪，并揭示阴影尺寸的非单调演化及其与暗物质微观耦合的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视界尺度成像（horizon-scale imaging）开启强引力检验新时代，自相互作用玻色子星被视为有竞争力的暗物质候选者（dark matter candidates）与黑洞模仿者（black hole mimickers），因而其可观测光学特征需要系统刻画。

Method: 研究几何薄吸积盘（geometrically thin accretion disk）照射下无视界天体的光线追踪；针对缓慢衰减标量尾部（scalar tail）导致的数值刚性（numerical stiffness），提出内部6参数Padé近似（Padé approximant）与外部精确Schwarzschild时空经全局C²连续三次样条（cubic spline）衔接的混合度规重构框架。

Result: 对采用的双势阱势（double-well potential），识别出正则静态解存在的严格上界（φ₀ ≲ 0.317）；发现阴影尺寸呈显著非单调演化，源于宏观质量-振幅翻转，其根源为吸引与排斥自相互作用的微观物理竞争；最致密构型中产生嵌套高阶光子环（higher-order photon rings）与明显分离的次级像，且标量势参数空间（Λ, Γ）中阴影尺寸呈现对角梯度。

Conclusion: 下一代甚长基线干涉测量（VLBI）不仅能判定天体是否具有视界，还可通过几何观测途径直接探测暗物质标量场的微观物理耦合（microphysical couplings）。

Abstract: As horizon-scale imaging opens a new era of strong-field gravity tests, self-interacting boson stars have emerged as compelling dark matter candidates and natural black hole mimickers. We systematically investigate the optical appearances and multiple lensed rings of such horizonless objects illuminated by a geometrically thin accretion disk. To overcome the severe numerical stiffness caused by the slowly decaying scalar tail, we introduce a robust hybrid metric reconstruction framework---seamlessly bridging an interior 6-parameter Padé approximant to the exact exterior Schwarzschild spacetime via a globally $C^2$-continuous cubic spline. This ensures high-precision ray-tracing across the entire domain. For the adopted double-well potential, we identify a strict upper bound ($φ_0 \lesssim 0.317$) for the existence of regular static solutions. Crucially, we uncover a striking non-monotonic evolution in the shadow size, driven by a macroscopic mass-amplitude turnover that is fundamentally rooted in the microphysical competition between attractive and repulsive self-interactions. Within the most compact configurations, extreme gravitational lensing generates prominent nested higher-order photon rings and distinctly separated secondary images. Furthermore, exploring the scalar potential parameter space ($Λ, Γ$) reveals a distinct diagonal gradient in the shadow size. Our findings demonstrate that next-generation VLBI observations can move beyond merely identifying horizonless objects, offering a novel geometric avenue to directly probe the microphysical couplings of dark matter scalar fields.

</details>


### [197] [Fourier Transform of the Scalar Propagator with Quantum Light-Cone Fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18703)
*M. V. Takook*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Krein空间量子化框架下，用Meijer G-函数给出了高斯涂抹光锥（Gaussian-smeared light-cone）对标量传播子贡献的洛伦兹傅里叶变换（Lorentzian Fourier transform）的解析表达，并刻画其分布结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确说明研究动机；仅可推断其目标是获得该光锥贡献的解析（Meijer G-函数）表示并厘清其分布结构。

Method: 采用Krein空间量子化（Krein-space quantization）框架，对高斯涂抹的光锥贡献做洛伦兹傅里叶变换并以Meijer G-函数表示；通过添加贝塞尔函数尾部（Bessel-function tail）恢复有质量传播子，并使用位置空间规定（position-space prescription）处理无质量情形。

Result: 得到该光锥贡献洛伦兹傅里叶变换的Meijer G-函数形式及其分布结构；通过添加贝塞尔函数尾部恢复有质量传播子（massive propagator）；并得到无质量单圈气泡核（massless one-loop bubble kernel）的精确表达式。

Conclusion: Meijer G-函数为该光锥贡献提供了紧凑的解析描述，结合贝塞尔函数尾部与位置空间规定可分别处理有质量与无质量情形，并给出无质量单圈气泡核的精确结果。

Abstract: We derive the Lorentzian Fourier transform of the Gaussian-smeared light-cone contribution to the scalar propagator in Krein-space quantization in terms of a Meijer $G$-function. We characterize its distributional structure, recover the massive propagator by adding the Bessel-function tail, and derive an exact expression for the massless one-loop bubble kernel using a position-space prescription.

</details>


### [198] [Thermodynamics and interior structure of density-responsive regular black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18705)
*Martin Drobczyk*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作基于密度响应引力（density-responsive gravity, DRG）标量扇区，用唯象局域跑动的代数方案构造一类似正则黑洞（regular black hole）的度规族，并检验其热力学、极值附近标度与若干诊断量。核心稳健结论是标定后的热力学与极值附近普适标度，而非回声或幺正性证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是考察受DRG标量扇区启发的正则黑洞能否给出一致的视界结构、热力学与极值行为，同时明确区分“构造性诊断”与“物理预言”。

Method: 采用代数处方（algebraic prescription）与唯象局域跑动，将高斯物质分布映射为诊断性密度分布；压力检验表明该分布并非完整静态DRG方程的解，仅用于选取含两个视界的有效度规族。对ρ(r)=ρ₀exp[-(r/r_c)^n]拟合得n=2，并用静态lapse诊断计算近极值标度，另以坐标时间正则化积分、面积熵与逆源重构作辅助分析。

Result: 极值质量标定为M_min=√(1+A/2)M_P≈1.006M_P（A=0.024）；温度非单调，在M=1.332M_P处达T_H^max=0.02547M_P，随后于M_min处消失。n=2,3,4,6及Hayward、Bardeen对照均验证近极值标度Z_tr∝(M−M_min)^{-1/2}。剩余面积熵等于在M_cross/M_0=0.707107≈1/√2处已损失的面积熵，端点熵低于初始Bekenstein-Hawking熵的2.6%（M_0=5M_P）与0.03%（M_0=50M_P）。

Conclusion: 该几何交叉不构成Page曲线（Page curve）或幺正性（unitarity）的证据；视界间的正则化坐标时间积分只是静态腔（static cavity）诊断，并非引力波回声预言；逆源重构确认各向同性de Sitter型核心但保留压力失配，坍缩计算在施加反弹因子（bounce factor）后仅得到有界构型而未能确立标定几何的形成。稳健结果限于标定热力学与极值附近普适标度。

Abstract: This work studies regular black holes motivated by the scalar sector of density-responsive gravity (DRG). An algebraic prescription with phenomenological local running maps a Gaussian matter profile to a diagnostic density profile. A pressure check shows that this profile does not solve the full static DRG equations. It serves only to select an effective metric family with two horizons. The extremal mass is calibrated to $M_{\min} = \sqrt{1+A/2}\,M_{\mathrm{P}} \simeq 1.006\,M_{\mathrm{P}}$, where $A=0.024$ is the historical scalar response amplitude. A fit to $ρ(r) = ρ_0\exp[-(r/r_c)^n]$ selects $n=2$. The temperature is nonmonotonic, reaching $T_H^{\max}=0.02547\,M_{\mathrm{P}}$ at $M=1.332\,M_{\mathrm{P}}$ before vanishing at $M_{\min}$. The calculation verifies the generic scaling near extremality $\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{tr}}\propto(M-M_{\min})^{-1/2}$ of a static lapse diagnostic for $n=2,3,4,6$ and for the Hayward and Bardeen controls. A regulated integral of coordinate time between the horizons provides a static cavity diagnostic, not a prediction of echoes of gravitational waves. The remaining area entropy equals the area entropy already lost at $M_{\mathrm{cross}}/M_0=0.707107\simeq1/\sqrt{2}$. This geometric crossing does not establish a Page curve or unitarity. The endpoint area entropy is below $2.6\%$ of the initial Bekenstein-Hawking entropy for $M_0 = 5\,M_{\mathrm{P}}$ and below $0.03\%$ for $M_0 = 50\,M_{\mathrm{P}}$. An inverse source reconstruction confirms an isotropic core of de Sitter type but retains the pressure mismatch. An exploratory collapse calculation with an imposed bounce factor produces bounded configurations with $1-2m/r<0$ without establishing formation of the calibrated geometry. The robust results are the calibrated thermodynamics and generic scaling near extremality.

</details>


### [199] [Finite Energy, Event Horizons, and a Universal Bound on the Singularity Order in Born--Infeld-type Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18711)
*Tengyang Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究连接麦克斯韦（Maxwell）与玻恩—英费尔德（Born–Infeld）极限的插值非线性电动力学理论，构建插值型带电黑洞解，并对事件视界（event horizon）构型进行分类，同时证明玻恩—英费尔德型非线性结构下 Kretschmann 标量发散阶数的普适下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 麦克斯韦理论与玻恩—英费尔德理论代表非线性电动力学的两个端点，二者之间的连续过渡缺乏系统研究；同时，该插值框架下黑洞解的存在性、视界结构及奇点性质尚待澄清。

Method: 在静电 sector 中分析插值理论的总能量行为；构造带电黑洞解并与经典玻恩—英费尔德解及 Reissner–Nordström 解比较；对具有截断阈值（truncation threshold）的一般插值理论分析事件视界的存在性与多重性并分类；对一般玻恩—英费尔德型非线性结构推导 Kretschmann 发散阶数的普适下界。

Result: 静电 sector 总能量有限，且在趋于麦克斯韦极限时仍保持有上界；黑洞解在玻恩—英费尔德解与 Reissner–Nordström 解之间单调插值，且奇点可被移至任意位置而不依赖于权重参数（weight parameters）；视界构型得到完整分类；Kretschmann 发散阶数的普适下界成立，从而排除正则电场黑洞（regular electric black hole）的存在。

Conclusion: 插值型麦克斯韦—玻恩—英费尔德理论可实现两端极限间的平滑过渡并保持能量有限性；其黑洞解保持单调插值与奇点可控性；然而对玻恩—英费尔德型非线性电动力学而言，正则电场黑洞在普适意义下被禁止。

Abstract: We investigate an interpolated Maxwell--Born--Infeld theory bridging the Maxwell and Born--Infeld limits. In the electrostatic sector the total energy is finite, and remains bounded above even as the theory approaches the Maxwell limit. We construct the electrically charged black hole, whose black hole solution interpolates monotonically between the classical Born--Infeld and Reissner--Nordström solutions; singularity can be relegated, independently of the weight parameters. For general interpolated theories with a truncation threshold, we analyze the existence and multiplicity of event horizons and classify the horizon configurations, and for general Born--Infeld-type nonlinear structures we prove a universal lower bound on the order of the Kretschmann divergence, thereby excluding regular electric black holes.

</details>


### [200] [Resonance Induced Chaos of Charged Particles Around a Magnetized, Periodically Time-Conformal Reissner-Nordstrom Black Hole with Quintessence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18712)
*Israr Ali Khan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文通过周期性时间共形变形（time-conformal deformation）建模 Reissner–Nordström 黑洞（含精质 quintessence）的缓慢演化，研究带电试验粒子的混沌起始，发现混沌主要由非线性共振（nonlinear resonance）而非单一参数强度控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天体物理黑洞（astrophysical black holes）因吸积、并合与引力辐射并非严格静态；需刻画这种几何演化如何破坏可积性并诱发混沌运动，同时寻求可解析预言的判据。

Method: 采用周期时间共形变形 Ω(t)=1+ε sin(ω_d t) 将静态 RN–精质黑洞的可积时空转为周期驱动的二自由度非线性哈密顿系统（Hamiltonian system），用自适应高阶 Runge–Kutta 积分器求解，并以 Poincaré 截面、分岔图、最大 Lyapunov 指数与 SALI（Smaller Alignment Index）进行动力学分析；同时借助近似 Noether 对称性（approximate Noether symmetry）的共振小除子结构给出阈值预言。

Result: 混沌随共形振幅 ε 因共振重叠（resonance overlap）而增强；磁场 B 呈非单调效应——在径向频率与驱动频率匹配处产生混沌，更强场则通过失谐（detuning）抑制混沌。近似 Noether 对称性的小除子结构无自由参数预言磁阈值 B_c≈0.011（实测 0.012），并在固定单一耦合常数后重现共形阈值；精质参数主要通过降低约束势垒促成粒子逃逸（escape），构成非共振的规则性破缺路径。

Conclusion: 混沌的起始由非线性共振机制主导，可由近似 Noether 对称性的共振结构定量预言对应阈值；精质则提供一条与共振无关的破坏途径，故需将共振判据与势垒效应区分开来理解该体系的规则—混沌转变。

Abstract: Astrophysical black holes are never strictly static: accretion, mergers, and gravitational radiation drive a slow evolution of their geometry. We model this evolution through a periodic time-conformal deformation, $Ω(t)=1+ε\sin(ω_d t)$, of a Reissner--Nordström black hole surrounded by quintessence, and study the resulting onset of chaos for charged test particles. The deformation converts the integrable static spacetime into a periodically driven nonlinear Hamiltonian system, so that the transition from regular to chaotic motion can be related to the breakdown of an approximate Noether symmetry. The resulting two-degree-of-freedom Hamiltonian system is solved using an adaptive high-order Runge--Kutta integrator, while the dynamical behaviour is analysed through Poincaré sections, bifurcation diagrams, the largest Lyapunov exponent, and the Smaller Alignment Index (SALI). Our results show that the onset of chaos is controlled primarily by nonlinear resonance rather than by the magnitude of any individual parameter. Chaos grows with the conformal amplitude $ε$ through resonance overlap, while the magnetic field $B$ acts non-monotonically: it produces chaos near the field strength at which the radial frequency matches the drive, and suppresses it at stronger fields by detuning. The small-divisor structure of the approximate Noether symmetry predicts the magnetic threshold without free parameters ($B_c\simeq0.011$ against the measured $0.012$), and reproduces the conformal threshold once a single coupling constant is fixed. In contrast, the quintessence parameter mainly facilitates particle escape by reducing the confining potential barrier, providing a distinct non-resonant route to the breakdown of regular motion.

</details>


### [201] [Holographic Einstein rings of a covariant quantum corrected AdS black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18716)
*Zhenglong Ban,Xiaomei Liu,Rong-Jia Yang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在协变量子修正的Schwarzschild–AdS₄时空中，用无质量标量探针研究全息爱因斯坦环。发现形变参数ζ在固定视界半径下不改变度规质量参数、坐标表面引力与光子球半径，但抬高光子势垒并改变渐近时标归一化，从而区分有限半径效应与边界时间归一化效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子引力修正时空中的全息爱因斯坦环图像尚缺乏系统刻画，尤其需要厘清形变参数带来的几何效应与渐近边界时间归一化改变之间的区别。

Method: 采用无质量标量探针（massless scalar probe），结合光线追踪（ray tracing）、局部WKB势垒分析，并在AdS边界条件下计算准正规模（quasinormal modes）谱以作有限多极数下的对比。

Result: 固定视界半径时，ζ不改变度规质量参数、坐标表面引力（coordinate surface gravity）和光子球半径，但提高光子势垒最大值与渐近失效系数A=1+ζ²；大黑洞情形下临界坐标碰撞参数趋近A^{-1/2}，而边界归一化系中的入射角趋于π/2；光线追踪与WKB数据给出一致的几何尺度。

Conclusion: 量子修正形变的效应可分解为有限半径内的几何改变与边界时间归一化的改变两部分，全息环的可观测量据此得以明确归因。

Abstract: We investigate holographic Einstein rings in a covariant quantum corrected Schwarzschild--AdS$_4$ spacetime using a massless scalar probe. At fixed horizon radius $r_h$, the deformation parameter $ζ$ leaves the metric mass parameter, coordinate surface gravity, and photon sphere radius unchanged, while increasing the photon potential maximum and the asymptotic lapse coefficient $A=1+ζ^2$. The critical coordinate impact parameter approaches $A^{-1/2}$ for large black holes, whereas the corresponding incidence angle in the normalized boundary frame approaches $π/2$. Ray tracing and local WKB barrier data recover the same geometric scale. Quasinormal spectra computed with AdS boundary conditions provide a comparison at finite multipole number. These results separate the finite radius effect of the deformation from the change in boundary time normalization.

</details>


### [202] [Polarized equatorial emission around regular compact objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18862)
*Tsanimir Angelov,Rasim Bekir,Galin Gyulchev,Petya Nedkova,Stoytcho Yazadjiev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文通过磁化流体环的唯象偏振成像模型，指出高倾角下正则（粒子样）紧致天体会产生黑洞所没有的第二个直接像，且其偏振强度可高约三个数量级，具有观测区分意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子引力（quantum gravity）动机下的正则紧致天体（regular compact objects）与广义相对论（general relativity）黑洞的观测区分尚待明确，尤其缺乏对偏振（polarization）特征的针对性研究。
