Method: 采用静态度规球对称非奇异黑洞（non-singular black holes）并延伸至粒子样时空（particle-like spacetimes）的几何，结合赤道面绕转磁化流体环（magnetized fluid ring）的唯象模型，计算直接偏振图像（direct polarized images）。

Result: 低倾角（low inclinations）下偏振图案与史瓦西黑洞（Schwarzschild black hole）高度相似；高倾角（high inclinations）下粒子样几何出现特征性第二个直接像（second direct image），偏振强度可高三个数量级并具有特定偏振方向。

Conclusion: 第二个直接像的形成机制得到物理解释，并明确了导致其偏振辐射（polarized emission）增强的等离子体（plasma）配置，为观测上区分正则紧致天体与黑洞提供潜在判据。

Abstract: We study the polarization properties of regular compact objects motivated by quantum gravity. These geometries include static spherically symmetric non-singular black holes which undergo a continuous transition to regular particle-like spacetimes in certain regions of the parametric space. Applying a phenomenological model of a magnetized fluid ring orbiting in the equatorial plane, we search for characteristic signatures in the direct polarized images which could distinguish them from black holes in general relativity. At low inclinations the regular compact objects lead to a very similar polarization pattern as the Schwarzschild black hole. At high inclinations we observe a characteristic signature for the particle-like geometries. A second direct image of the fluid ring is formed which is absent for black holes. It may possess with three orders of magnitude higher polarization intensity and a specific polarization direction making it observationally significant. We elucidate the physical mechanism for the formation of the second direct image and pinpoint the plasma configurations which lead to amplification of its polarized emission.

</details>


### [203] [Landauer erasure and horizon information in an expanding universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18879)
*Babak Vakili*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文将兰道尔原理（Landauer's principle）与视界热力学（horizon thermodynamics）结合，研究膨胀宇宙中信息擦除（information erasure）的热力学代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在把信息擦除的热力学代价引入宇宙学背景，建立信息、视界热力学与引力能量之间的简单联系。

Method: 在 Friedmann–Robertson–Walker（FRW）宇宙中，以视界温度（apparent-horizon temperature）作为有效宇宙温度，计算擦除一比特所需的最小热量，并与 Misner–Sharp 能量进行比较。

Result: 对于标准的非幽灵（non-phantom）膨胀，擦除一比特所需的最小热量随宇宙膨胀而减小，同时视界所对应的熵等效信息含量增加；兰道尔热量与 Misner–Sharp 能量虽存在简单热力学关系，但物理含义不同。

Conclusion: 结果给出了膨胀宇宙中信息擦除、视界热力学与引力能量之间的简洁联系，并说明兰道尔热量不宜与 Misner–Sharp 能量作等同解读。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamic cost of information erasure in Friedmann--Robertson--Walker cosmology by combining Landauer's principle with the thermodynamics of the apparent horizon. Adopting the apparent-horizon temperature as an effective cosmological temperature, we find that for standard non-phantom expansion, the minimum heat required to erase one bit decreases while the entropy-equivalent information content of the horizon increases. We further compare the Landauer heat with the Misner--Sharp energy and show that, despite their simple thermodynamic relation, they have distinct physical meanings. These results provide a simple connection between information erasure, horizon thermodynamics, and gravitational energy in an expanding universe.

</details>


### [204] [Primordial Non-Gaussianity and Correlation-Function Perturbativity in mLQC-I: The Hybrid Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18899)
*Rui Pan,Jamal Saeed,Anzhong Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在修正圈量子宇宙学 I（modified loop quantum cosmology I, mLQC-I）的混合方法（hybrid approach）框架下，计算了标量扰动三点相互作用与树级双谱，发现双谱可具有很大的逐点振幅，同时保留的两点修正仍远小于一，CMB 投影下存在显著相位平均。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 考察 mLQC-I 中标量扰动的原初非高斯性（primordial non-Gaussianity）与微扰一致性（perturbative consistency），检验大逐点非高斯性与两点函数修正是否可相容共存。

Method: 在二次理论的典范表示（canonical representation）中构造三次相互作用哈密顿量（cubic interaction Hamiltonian），导出树级双谱（tree-level bispectrum）的有限动量 in-in 精确表达式；采用两种初态方案：在反弹点（bounce）设定的高斯态（Gaussian state）与在遥远收缩面设定的 de Sitter 真空；以 Ξ=|ΔP_R/P_R^{(0)}| 评估保留的非线性转换修正。

Result: 反弹态下，与抵消无关的有限窗口绝对被积函数估计在五个采样动量处均低于 6×10^{-6}；远程 de Sitter 态的带符号修正由抵消主导，Ξ 从反弹曲率尺度十分之一处的约 9.9×10^{-2} 降至该尺度处的约 2.3×10^{-4}。两点修正保持在 1 以下，但双谱逐点振幅可很大：反弹态给出等边（equilateral）f_NL 的振荡相位均方根幅度约 10^3–10^4，远程态可更大；主导快振荡近似由 k_t=k_1+k_2+k_3 组织，Δk_t≈5.7×10^{-4} m_P，三角形形状包络（envelope）变化缓慢。

Conclusion: 在所采用的反弹—现今映射（bounce-to-present mapping）下，CMB 投影诊断（CMB-projection diagnostics）显示显著相位平均（phase averaging）；因此大的逐点原初非高斯性可与两点函数的小保留修正共存。

Abstract: We investigate primordial non-Gaussianity and the perturbative consistency of scalar perturbations in modified loop quantum cosmology I (mLQC-I) within the hybrid approach. We construct the cubic interaction Hamiltonian in the canonical representation of the quadratic theory and derive an exact finite-momentum in-in expression for the tree-level bispectrum. We consider two initial-state prescriptions: a Gaussian state specified at the bounce and the de Sitter vacuum specified at a remote contracting surface. We assess the retained nonlinear-conversion correction through $Ξ=|ΔP_R/P_R^{(0)}|$. For the bounce state, a conservative, cancellation-independent, finite-window absolute-integrand estimate is below $6\times10^{-6}$ at all five sampled momenta. For the remote de Sitter state, the signed correction is dominated by cancellations and decreases from $Ξ\simeq9.9\times10^{-2}$ at one tenth of the bounce-curvature scale to $Ξ\simeq2.3\times10^{-4}$ at that scale. Although the retained two-point corrections remain below unity, the bispectrum can have large pointwise amplitudes: the bounce state yields an equilateral $f_{\rm NL}$ with root-mean-square amplitude over oscillation phase of order $10^3$--$10^4$, while the remote state can yield much larger values. The dominant rapid oscillation is organized approximately by $k_t=k_1+k_2+k_3$, with $Δk_t\simeq5.7\times10^{-4}m_{\rm P}$, while the triangle-shape envelope varies slowly. For the illustrative bounce-to-present mapping adopted here, CMB-projection diagnostics indicate substantial phase averaging. Thus, large pointwise primordial non-Gaussianity can coexist with a small retained correction to the two-point function.

</details>


### [205] [Asymptotic stability of axisymmetric perturbations of near-horizon extremal Kerr with a Schwarzian-deformed throat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18922)
*Marcela Cárdenas,Benjamín Cuevas-Tapia,Claudio Muñoz,Jessica Trespalacios,Hanne Van Den Bosch*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究近极端Kerr黑洞近视界几何（NHEK及其Schwarzian形变族）在轴对称引力扰动下的渐近稳定性，发现稳定性性质直接依赖Schwarzian背景所编码的近视界结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作多针对标准NHEK几何的扰动稳定性，而对NHEK的AdS₂因子被Schwarzian模（Schwarzian mode）形变后的一族背景缺乏系统分析，需明确非线性背景结构如何影响扰动稳定性。

Method: 要求Killing参数在背景中具有局域性（locality），使Gelfand–Dikii多项式成为Killing方程的自然解，并引出Korteweg–de Vries方程（KdV）的定常解；据此线性化Einstein方程，将轴对称扇区化为近AdS₂基底上的波动方程，并用能量方法与位力方法（virial methods）分析。

Result: 对NHEK得到守恒能量、积分型局域能量估计及加权范数下的渐近稳定性；对非常数Schwarzian，能量成为Lyapunov泛函（Lyapunov functional），其单调性由导数符号决定，对可积情形给出一致界，并沿KdV孤子（soliton）得到轻度局域衰减；另发现一个非Aretakis型的视界局域单调范数。

Conclusion: 稳定性性质对Schwarzian背景编码的近视界结构具有直接敏感性；并利用时空的径向ADM叶状化（ADM foliation）在视界喉部晚期角落计算了相应的Regge–Teitelboim荷。

Abstract: We study the asymptotic stability of axisymmetric gravitational perturbations of the near-horizon geometries of (near-)extremal Kerr black holes and consider the family of backgrounds in which the AdS$_{2}$ factor of NHEK is deformed by a Schwarzian mode. Requiring the Killing parameter to be local in the background makes the Gelfand--Dikii polynomials natural solutions of the Killing equation, and that locality requirement brings stationary solutions of the Korteweg-de Vries equation. The hierarchy selects a family of backgrounds, which remain $\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb{R})\times \mathfrak{u}(1)$ throughout them: the first level gives NHEK and near-NHEK, the second, solitons that depart from one of these geometries and return to it. We linearize Einstein's equations around this family, where the axisymmetric sector reduces to a wave equation on the nearly-AdS$_{2}$ base, and analyze this equation by energy and virial methods. For NHEK we obtain a conserved energy, an integrated local energy estimate and asymptotic stability in a weighted norm; for a nonconstant Schwarzian the energy becomes a Lyapunov functional whose monotonicity is dictated by the sign of its derivative, yielding uniform bounds for integrable ones, and mild local decay along the KdV soliton. We also find a monotone horizon-localized norm, which is not of Aretakis type, showing that stability properties are directly sensitive to the near-horizon structure encoded in the Schwarzian background. We compute the associated Regge--Teitelboim charges at a late-time corner of the throat using a radial ADM foliation of the spacetime.

</details>


### [206] [The fate of hidden symmetries under magnetic Harrison transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19087)
*Shoulong Li,Fangkang Yu,Hong-Da Lyu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作建立了磁 Harrison 变换下 Carter 型种子解与共形 Carter 型种子解在 CKT 与无质量标量可分离性上的普适微扰继承律，并揭示隐藏对称性在微扰层面的层级式存续结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解生成变换（solution-generating transformations）可由已知种子解构造新几何，但新解能否、以及在多大程度上继承种子解可积性与可分离性背后的隐藏对称性（hidden symmetries）尚不明确。

Method: 在磁 Harrison 变换（magnetic Harrison transformations）层面直接建立微扰继承律，涵盖 Carter 型种子解的磁化（magnetization）与共形 Carter 型种子解的去磁化（demagnetization）。

Result: 在磁化 Kerr 与去磁化 Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson 情形中，微扰继承与精确 Carter 型可分离性的丧失并存；与种子解相连的共形 Killing-Yano 结构（conformal Killing-Yano structures）在同一微扰阶即已受阻。

Conclusion: 结果表明隐藏对称性的微扰存续存在层级差异，并为在更一般理论设定及其他解生成变换下研究对称性继承提供了框架。

Abstract: Solution-generating transformations construct new geometries from known seeds, raising the question of whether and to what extent the resulting solutions inherit the hidden symmetries underlying the seeds' integrability and separability. We have established universal perturbative inheritance laws for CKTs and massless scalar separability directly at the level of magnetic Harrison transformations, encompassing both magnetization of Carter-type seeds and demagnetization of conformal Carter-type seeds. In both magnetized Kerr and demagnetized Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson, this perturbative inheritance coexists with the loss of exact Carter-type separability, while seed-connected conformal Killing-Yano structures are already obstructed at the same perturbative order. These results reveal a hierarchy in the perturbative survival of hidden symmetries and provide a framework for investigating their inheritance in more general theoretical settings and under other solution-generating transformations.

</details>


### [207] [Drift of a Charged Particle in a Black Hole Magnetosphere: the Effect of Black Hole Spin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19098)
*Ye Shen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文以协变方法（covariant approach）研究黑洞磁层（black hole magnetosphere）中单个带电粒子的漂移（drift），重点考察黑洞自旋（black hole spin）的影响，并导出可退化为牛顿极限的协变漂移公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强磁场中的稀疏等离子体（sparse plasma）在天体物理场景中常见，需采用无碰撞（collisionless）描述；导心近似（guiding-center approximation）虽能揭示粒子偏离磁力线的漂移，但其协变化表述及强引力场诱导的漂移尚未被充分探讨。

Method: 先借助引力电磁学（gravitoelectromagnetism）语言考察牛顿框架下大质量天体旋转引起的漂移，进而推导协变漂移公式，并在克尔时空（Kerr spacetime）中具体分析黑洞自旋对漂移的影响，同时定义漂移率（drift rate）以量化漂移显著程度。

Result: 所导出的协变漂移公式在弱引力场与低速极限下可退化为牛顿结果；分析表明，在某些极端情形下，黑洞自旋对黑洞磁层中带电粒子漂移的影响可与其他因素相当。

Conclusion: 该工作作为初步研究，给出了协变的导心漂移描述框架，并指出黑洞自旋效应在极端条件下不可忽略，为后续强引力场中导心近似的完整协变化研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Sparse plasma immersed in a strong magnetic field is common in astrophysical scenarios, for which a collisionless description is more appropriate. The guiding-center approximation serves as an effective tool for describing the motion of charged particles in a strong magnetic field, revealing the drift, a sub-leading order motion that causes particles to deviate from the magnetic field lines. It is widely accepted that the charged particles drift under the induction of gravity in Newtonian framework. However, a covariant formulation of the guiding-center approximation, as well as the drift of a charged particle induced by strong gravitational field, has yet to be fully explored. As a preliminary study, we aim to analyze the drift of a single charged particle in a black hole magnetosphere via a covariant approach. Particularly, we focus on the effects of black hole spin. We first examine the drift caused by the rotation of a massive object in the Newtonian regime using the language of gravitoelectromagnetism. A covariant formula of drift is derived, which is confirmed to reduce to the Newtonian results in the weak gravitational field and low speed limit. The impact of BH spin on the drift is analyzed in detail in Kerr spacetime specifically, for which we define the drift rate whose value quantifies the significance of the drift. Our results indicate that the impact of BH spin on the drift of a charged particle in a BH magnetosphere can be comparable to that of other factors in some extreme cases.

</details>


### [208] [Chaotic motion of spinning particles in nonlocal gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19191)
*Jianhui Lin,Xiangdong Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在修正的Deser–Woodard非局域引力（nonlocal gravity, NLG）中，研究了静态球对称黑洞周围的自旋诱导混沌（spin-induced chaos）。变形参数ζ使非穿透轨道的混沌比例从37.5%（ζ=0）降到零（ζ=0.1），且该分类差异体现在近似质量四极波形（mass-quadrupole waveforms）的局部谱平坦度上；但研究未证实混沌对识别背景几何具有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是检验背景变形引起的“混沌到规则”转变能否帮助区分非局域引力（NLG）与广义相对论（general relativity, GR），并连接背景相关的轨道动力学与辐射谱。

Method: 采用与极端质量比旋近（extreme-mass-ratio inspirals）相关的保守试验粒子近似（conservative test-body approximation），以变形参数ζ刻画对Schwarzschild时空的偏离；使用庞加莱截面（Poincaré sections）、旋转曲线（rotation curves）和快速李雅普诺夫指标（fast Lyapunov indicators, FLIs）分析共振结构与混沌分类。进一步比较ζ=10⁻⁶的NLG波形与不同初始半径下生成的GR波形。

Result: 在所采样轨道族中，被归类为混沌的非穿透轨道比例从ζ=0的37.5%降至ζ=0.1的零；相邻混沌与规则轨道的波形呈现明显的局部谱平坦度反差，在两者均被判为规则的背景中该反差减弱。对分类随背景变化的轨道，调整初始半径可显著减小失配；但混沌轨道的NLG波形最小失配仍大于规则轨道。

Conclusion: 结果将背景依赖的轨道动力学与辐射谱联系起来，但未能确立混沌在识别背景几何方面的优势；初值敏感性也可能妨碍信号恢复。

Abstract: We study spin-induced chaos around a static, spherically symmetric black hole in revised Deser--Woodard nonlocal gravity (NLG), using the conservative test-body approximation relevant to extreme-mass-ratio inspirals. The deformation parameter $ζ$ describes departures from Schwarzschild spacetime. Poincaré sections, rotation curves, and fast Lyapunov indicators (FLIs) reveal changes in resonant structures and a decreasing fraction of non-plunging trajectories classified as chaotic within the sampled family, from $37.5\%$ at $ζ=0$ to zero at $ζ=0.1$. The FLI classifications of selected trajectories are reflected in their approximate mass-quadrupole waveforms: neighboring chaotic and regular trajectories show a marked local spectral-flatness contrast, which weakens in backgrounds where both are classified as regular. To assess whether a transition from chaotic to regular motion caused by the background deformation helps distinguish NLG from GR, we compare NLG waveforms at $ζ=10^{-6}$ with GR waveforms generated at different initial radii. For a trajectory whose classification changes between the backgrounds, this adjustment substantially reduces the mismatch. The selected NLG waveforms from chaotic orbits retain larger minimum mismatches than those from regular orbits, although sensitivity to initial conditions may also hinder signal recovery. These results connect background-dependent orbital dynamics with radiation spectra, but do not establish that chaos offers an advantage for identifying the background geometry.

</details>


### [209] [Numerical evidence for the validity of the inverse hoop conjecture for black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19240)
*Shahar Hod*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该短文为逆箍猜想（inverse hoop conjecture, IH conjecture）在非稳态黑洞（non-stationary black-hole）情形下提供支持证据：验证了 Croft、García-Moreno 与 Pani（CGP）近期构造的所有非稳态黑洞解均满足 ${\cal M}_{\text{irr}}\leq {\cal C}_{\text{s}}/4\pi$ 关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆箍猜想最初仅在稳态黑洞（stationary black-hole）构型的语境中讨论，其物理含义是黑洞视界面积不能发生不受约束的几何褶皱（geometric wrinkling）；该猜想是否可推广至非稳态构型此前尚不明确，构成本文的研究动机。

Method: 采用解析检验（analytic verification）方式：以不可约质量（irreducible mass）${\cal M}_{\text{irr}}=\sqrt{{\cal A}/16\pi}$ 与能包络视界的最小环周长（circumference length）${\cal C}_{\text{s}}$ 为观测量，逐一考察 CGP 近期构造的非稳态黑洞解族是否满足 IH 不等式。

Result: 结果表明，CGP（arXiv:2611.13266）所构造的全部非稳态黑洞解均满足 IH 关系，即在非稳态情形下未出现违反 ${\cal M}_{\text{irr}}\leq {\cal C}_{\text{s}}/4\pi$ 的反例。

Conclusion: 摘要据此认为 IH 猜想对非稳态黑洞构型同样成立具有支持性证据，从而将该猜想的适用范围从稳态情形扩展至非稳态情形；但摘要未给出证明，仅为证据性支持。

Abstract: The inverse hoop (IH) conjecture, ${\cal M}_{\text{irr}}\leq {\cal C}_{\text{s}}/4π$, originally discussed in the context of stationary black-hole configurations, suggests that the surface area of a black hole cannot undergo unconstrained geometric wrinkling (here ${\cal M}_{\text{irr}}=\sqrt{{\cal A}/16π}$ is the irreducible mass associated with the horizon area ${\cal A}$ of the black hole and ${\cal C}_{\text{s}}$ is the circumference length of the smallest ring that can engulf the horizon of the black hole in every direction). In the present compact paper we provide evidence supporting the validity of the IH conjecture for non-stationary black-hole configurations. In particular, we show that all the non-stationary black-hole solutions recently constructed by Croft, García-Moreno, and Pani (CGP) [arXiv:2611.13266] satisfy the IH relation.

</details>


### [210] [Two-scale system: compactified ambient metrics and Scri/Spi/Ti](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19377)
*Xavier Bekaert,Maxim Grigoriev,Mikhail Markov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一个源于共形几何（conformal geometry）的双尺度系统框架，将Fefferman-Graham环境度量、Brinkmann空间与超静态真空时空等几何构造统一起来，并由此给出Ricci平坦时空空间无穷与时间无穷的新环境构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有多种几何构造（环境度量、Brinkmann空间、超静态真空时空）各自具有共形紧化（conformal compactification），但缺乏统一描述；同时Ricci平坦时空的扩展空间无穷（Spi）与时间无穷（Ti）缺少自然的环境构造。

Method: 构建由两个满足近爱因斯坦方程（almost-Einstein equations）及特定正则性条件、权重为一的共形标量构成的双尺度系统（two-scale system），以此作为统一框架，并将Spi/Ti实现为双尺度环境空间的边界面（boundary face）与角点（corner）。

Result: 上述几何构造被纳入同一框架；作为特例得到Spi与Ti的新环境构造，其中Spi/Ti及对应时空的零无穷（null infinity）分别自然地实现为双尺度环境空间的边界面与角点。

Conclusion: 双尺度系统可作为背景环境几何，用于研究（规范）场在空间、时间与零无穷处的渐近行为（asymptotic behaviour）。

Abstract: We show that certain geometric constructions, including the Fefferman-Graham ambient metric, Brinkmann spaces, and ultrastatic vacuum spacetimes, admit natural conformal compactifications and can be unified within a single framework arising naturally from conformal geometry. The framework is based on a two-scale system comprising two weight-one conformal scalars satisfying almost-Einstein equations and certain regularity conditions. As a particular instance of this unifying framework, we obtain a new ambient construction for Spi and Ti, the extended spatial and temporal infinities of Ricci-flat spacetimes. A remarkable feature of this construction is that Spi/Ti and the null infinity of the corresponding spacetimes are naturally realised as a boundary face and the corner of the two-scale ambient space, respectively. We also demonstrate how the two-scale system can serve as a background ambient geometry for studying the asymptotic behaviour of (gauge) fields at spatial, temporal, and null infinity.

</details>


### [211] [A Stable Vector Bounce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19402)
*Nils A. Nilsson*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在立方广义普罗卡理论（cubic generalised Proca theory）中，以自由正则标量场（free canonical scalar）为物质场，构建了一个测地完备的反弹（geodesically complete bounce）解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在反弹宇宙学（bounce cosmology）中，实现从收缩到膨胀的平滑过渡通常需要违反零能量条件（null energy condition），或面临测地不完备与微扰不稳定性等问题；本文旨在寻找一个在满足零能量条件前提下仍能实现反弹且微扰稳定的方案。

Method: 采用立方广义普罗卡理论与自由正则标量场耦合的框架，令时间分量矢量沿其代数约束（algebraic constraint）演化以驱动收缩—膨胀过渡；随后分析动能系数（kinetic coefficients）的符号与短波微扰（short-wavelength perturbations）的稳定性，并将线性扰动方程推至无限时间极限（infinite-time limits）检验有界性。

Result: 物质场满足零能量条件，且反弹处允许非零的导数混合（derivative mixing）；对所有非零共动波数（comoving wavenumber），动能系数在任意有限时刻均为正，短波微扰在整个解中保持稳定；线性方程在两侧无限时间极限下给出所有非零共动波数的局域微扰均有界。

Conclusion: 结果表明，立方广义普罗卡理论可在不违反零能量条件的情况下实现测地完备且微扰稳定的反弹，为非奇异反弹宇宙学模型提供了一种可行构造。

Abstract: We construct a geodesically complete bounce in cubic generalised Proca theory with a free canonical scalar as the matter field. The temporal vector evolves along its algebraic constraint and supports the transition from contraction to expansion while matter obeys the null energy condition. This evolution permits nonzero derivative mixing at the bounce. The kinetic coefficients are positive at every finite time for all nonzero comoving wavenumbers, and short-wavelength perturbations are stable throughout the solution. Following the linear equations to both infinite-time limits also shows that local perturbations are bounded for all nonzero comoving wavenumbers.

</details>


### [212] [Black holes in Myrzakulov $F(R,T)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19500)
*Alexandre Landry,Ratbay Myrzakulov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在度规-仿射（metric-affine）Myrzakulov F(R,T) 引力（MG-I）框架中，研究具有全反对称挠率的真空黑洞解，构造出可光滑穿过选定未来 Killing 视界的带挠率 Schwarzschild-(反)de Sitter 代表解，并强调结论仅为局部的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对度规-仿射 Myrzakulov F(R,T) 引力（其中 R 为仿射 Ricci 标量、T 为二次挠率不变量而非物质能动张量迹）缺乏真空黑洞解的系统研究，本文旨在建立此类解的构造方法与局部存在性依据。

Method: 对多项式模型 F(R,T)=αR+βT+γT³（α≠0）先对度规与独立仿射联络全部分量变分，再 specialization 到度规相容且挠率全反对称的几何；在非零轴矢挠率的例外分支 α+β+3γT_*²=0 上约化为带有效宇宙学参数 Λ_*=γT_*³/α 的 Einstein 方程，并在球对称轴矢拟设下用 (fV−U)′=0 刻画 Ricci 对称仿射联络。

Result: 给出局部存在性结果并构造带挠率的 Schwarzschild-(反)de Sitter 解，其可光滑穿过选定的未来 Killing 视界；在球对称轴矢拟设下对比常数 W=fV−U 族与常数 U 族——二者共享同一度规与挠率标量，但仿射曲率不变量一般不同；并以坐标级曲率缩并与分量检验佐证所用恒等式，补充解析变分推导。

Conclusion: 识别受限轴矢二次型的零方向，并将该退化与完整的度规-仿射稳定性分析区分开；作者明确结论为局部性的，不主张全局可延拓性、所有挠率剖面的物理不等价性或动力学稳定性。

Abstract: We investigate vacuum black-hole solutions in metric-affine Myrzakulov $F(R,T)$ gravity (MG-I), where $R$ is the affine Ricci scalar and $T$ is a quadratic torsion invariant, not the trace of the matter energy-momentum tensor. For the polynomial model $F(R,T)=αR+βT+γT^3$, with $α\neq0$, we first vary the metric and all components of the independent affine connection, and only then specialize to metric-compatible geometry with totally antisymmetric torsion. Nonzero axial torsion admits an exceptional branch $α+β+3γT_*^2=0$, on which the metric satisfies the Einstein equations with effective cosmological parameter $Λ_*=γT_*^3/α$. We establish a local existence result and construct torsionful Schwarzschild-(anti-)de Sitter representatives that are smooth through a selected future Killing horizon. Within the spherically symmetric axial ansatz, we characterize Ricci-symmetric affine connections by the condition $(fV-U)'=0$ and compare a constant-$W=fV-U$ family with a constant-$U$ family. Although these connections share the same metric and torsion scalar, their affine curvature invariants generally differ. Independent coordinate-level curvature contractions and component tests of the unrestricted metric and connection variations corroborate the identities used in the construction; these checks complement the analytic variational derivation. Finally, we identify null directions of the restricted axial quadratic form and distinguish this degeneracy from a complete metric-affine stability analysis. Our conclusions are local and do not establish global extendibility, physical inequivalence of all torsion profiles, or dynamical stability.

</details>


### [213] [Relativistic precession around rotating neutron stars. I. Slow rotation: Hartle--Thorne spacetimes, particle orbits and the QPOs of atoll and Z sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19502)
*A. C. Khunt*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 基于四种状态方程（equation of state, EOS）求解二阶 Hartle–Thorne 方程，在缓慢转动中子星时空中检验相对论进动模型（relativistic precession model, RPM）对准周期振荡（quasi-periodic oscillations, QPOs）的解释力，并据此约束中子星质量、自旋与转动惯量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QPOs 的 RPM 通常依赖自旋或四极矩展开的黑洞/克尔度规近似；本文意在考察由 EOS 决定、自旋诱导四极矩显著偏离黑洞的中子星时空对进动频率与推断参数的影响。

Method: 对 WFF1、G3、FSUGarnet 与 IOPB-I 四种 EOS 求解至角速度二阶的 Hartle–Thorne 方程，在不做自旋与四极矩展开的情况下计算外部度规的轨道频率与本轮频率（epicyclic frequencies）；并构造偏心、倾斜测地线热斑的功率谱，将 RPM 应用于 atoll 源与 Z 源。

Result: 1.9 M⊙ 处自旋诱导四极矩为同自旋黑洞的 2–4 倍，使节点进动频率在 ISCO 附近最多降低 25% 并限制其可达上限；拟合给出 4U 1636−53 质量 2.02–2.15 M⊙、4U 1728−34 为 1.88–1.92 M⊙（后者低频 QPO 偏好大转动惯量 I≃2.5×10^45 g cm²）；无 EOS 时得 M=1.920(+0.014/−0.012) M⊙、j=0.162(+0.023/−0.011)、Q̄=2.2(+1.1/−0.8)，同节点频率的克尔时空会低估转动惯量 22%；对 GX 5−1 与 GX 340+0，四种 EOS 下水平支振荡均无法为节点频率的两倍，拟合被推向 730–745 Hz 自旋与最大质量。

Conclusion: 中子星自旋诱导四极矩对节点进动频率有实质影响，EOS 依赖的时空可显著改变 RPM 参数推断；Z 源水平支振荡与 RPM 的一致性存疑，而 atoll 源结果支持大质量、大转动惯量解。

Abstract: We study the relativistic precession model (RPM) of quasi-periodic oscillations (QPOs) in the spacetimes of slowly rotating neutron stars computed from the equation of state (EOS). For four EOSs (WFF1, G3, FSUGarnet and IOPB-I) we solve the Hartle--Thorne equations to second order in the angular velocity and compute the orbital and epicyclic frequencies of the exterior metric without expanding in the spin or the quadrupole. At $1.9\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$ the spin-induced quadrupole is 2--4 times that of a black hole with the same spin; it lowers the nodal precession frequency by up to 25 per cent near the innermost stable circular orbit and limits the nodal frequency that any spin can produce. An eccentric, tilted geodesic around a $1.92\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$ star spinning at 363~Hz precesses at the epicyclic rates, and the power spectrum of a hot spot on it peaks at the orbital, periastron and nodal precession frequencies. Applied to the atoll sources 4U 1636$-$53 and 4U 1728$-$34, the RPM gives masses of 2.02--$2.15\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$ and 1.88--$1.92\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$. For 4U 1728$-$34 the low-frequency QPO favours stars with large moments of inertia, $I\simeq2.5\times10^{45}$~g\,cm$^{2}$. Without an EOS the QPOs give $M=1.920^{+0.014}_{-0.012}\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$, $j=0.162^{+0.023}_{-0.011}$ and $\bar Q=2.2^{+1.1}_{-0.8}$; a Kerr spacetime with the same nodal frequency would underestimate the moment of inertia by 22 per cent. For the Z sources GX 5$-$1 and GX 340+0 the horizontal-branch oscillations cannot be twice the nodal precession frequency for any of the four EOSs: the fits are driven to spins of 730--745~Hz and to the maximum mass.

</details>


### [214] [Scalar charge of neutron stars and accreting black holes in Starobinsky gravity: quasi-universal relations, the trace anomaly and gravitational-wave sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19510)
*Yashmitha Kumaran,Ilídio Lopes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: Starobinsky标量子（scalaron）的荷由能动张量迹（trace of stress-energy）的源决定，与真空几何无关。论文给出任意静态球对称源的荷主公式（master formula），对中子星与吸积黑洞逐项评估，并联系NICER半径、GW170817潮汐形变（tidal deformability）及GW230529约束讨论可观测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Starobinsky标量子的外场荷不由物体真空几何决定，而由其周围物质的迹源决定，因此需要建立对一般静态球对称源普遍适用的荷计算框架，并探讨该荷在天体观测中是否可及。

Method: 推导静态球对称源的荷主公式；先用于内部已知的中子星（含Eardley敏感度与压强减除），再用于内部未知的吸积黑洞；采用六种真实状态方程（equation of state）以及对极构星（polytrope）标定的系数进行重拟合；自洽求解Einstein框架结构方程；并用Fisher估计结合GW230529的无质量偶极约束，投影至Einstein Telescope与Cosmic Explorer。

Result: 荷在给定Eardley敏感度下近似为致密度（compactness）的准普适函数，六种状态方程均落在极构星标定系数之上，重拟合后均值复现精度为0.007；大质量星核心处迹ρ−3p变号，荷与潮汐形变、转动惯量分别在其千分之几与百分之一量级内相关；自洽解使星沿该关系移动，固定致密度下荷偏离试验场值不超过4%。真空黑洞q=0，带盘黑洞q=sinh(m_φr_d)/(m_φr_d)由盘外缘控制；光在领头阶对该荷不可见，更高阶修正可忽略。

Conclusion: Starobinsky标量子荷可作为联系致密度、潮汐形变与转动惯量的准普适观测量，并在黑洞情形由远离中心的盘物质主导，为区分真空几何与物质源荷提供判据；未来引力波探测器对有限标量子作用范围（scalaron range）的灵敏度可被定量估计。

Abstract: The Starobinsky scalaron is sourced by the trace of the stress--energy, so its exterior charge is set by the matter around an object and not by the object's vacuum geometry. We derive a master formula for that charge for any static, spherically symmetric source, evaluate it first for neutron stars, where the interior is known, and then for accreting black holes, where it is not. For a star the pressure subtracts: on the interior metric with the Eardley sensitivity, and the charge is a quasi-universal function of the compactness. Six realistic equations of state confirm the relation but lie above the coefficient calibrated on polytropes; the refit reproduces their mean to 0.007. The trace $ρ-3p$ changes sign in the cores of massive stars, and the charge also tracks the tidal deformability and the moment of inertia to a few parts in 1000 and 100. Solving the Einstein-frame structure equations self-consistently moves a star along this relation and leaves its charge at fixed compactness within 4 per cent of the test-field value. We compare the charge with NICER radii and the GW170817 tidal deformability, and use the published massless dipole constraint from GW230529 to calibrate a Fisher estimate of the sensitivity of a similar binary to a finite scalaron range, projected to the Einstein Telescope and Cosmic Explorer; the range is a free parameter, and the laboratory bound on pure $R+αR^2$ is confronted explicitly. A vacuum black hole has q=0; a hole with a disc has $q=\sinh(m_φr_d)/(m_φr_d)$, controlled by the disc's outer edge, which makes it the counterpart in which the same integral is dominated by matter far from the centre; light is blind to the charge at leading order, and the correction beyond it is negligible for any self-consistent disc.

</details>


### [215] [Dymnikova black string](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19682)
*L. G. Barbosa,L. C. N. Santos,C. C. Barros*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在带负宇宙学常数（negative cosmological constant）的爱因斯坦方程（Einstein's equations）中，引入Dymnikova型各向异性物质分布（Dymnikova-type anisotropic matter distribution），构造出一个新的静态黑弦（static black string）解，并分析其能量条件、视界结构、曲率不变量与热力学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将正则（regular）物质源思想，即Dymnikova型各向异性分布，推广到带负宇宙学常数的黑弦（black string）背景，以考察其对几何与热力学行为的影响。摘要未进一步说明具体背景动因。

Method: 采用求解爱因斯坦方程（Einstein's equations）的解析方法，在负宇宙学常数下以Dymnikova型各向异性物质为源构建静态黑弦解；随后检验弱能量条件与强能量条件（weak and strong energy conditions）、分析视界结构（horizon structure）与曲率不变量（curvature invariants），并计算霍金温度（Hawking temperature）与热容（heat capacity）。

Result: 所得解在大径向距离处趋近Lemos黑弦（Lemos black string），中心区域行为由有效曲率参数（effective curvature parameter）刻画；Ricci标量与Kretschmann标量在原点保持有限。热力学分析给出霍金温度与热容，且其行为依赖Dymnikova标度（Dymnikova scale）。

Conclusion: 该Dymnikova型各向异性源可生成渐近趋于Lemos黑弦、且曲率在原点正则的静态黑弦解，其热力学性质受Dymnikova标度调控。摘要未给出稳定性或相变等方面的进一步结论。

Abstract: We obtain a new static black string solution of Einstein's equations with a negative cosmological constant sourced by a Dymnikova-type anisotropic matter distribution. At large radial distances, the geometry approaches the Lemos black string, while the behavior near the central region is characterized by an effective curvature parameter. We examine the weak and strong energy conditions, the horizon structure, and the curvature invariants. The Ricci and Kretschmann scalars remain finite at the origin. We also investigate the Hawking temperature and heat capacity, with particular attention to the dependence of the thermodynamic behavior on the Dymnikova scale.

</details>


### [216] [Multi-throat generalized Ellis--Bronnikov wormholes with asymptotic string-cloud behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19808)
*M. S. Cunha,C. R. Muniz,V. H. U. Borralho,G. Alencar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究一类超静态球对称可穿越虫洞（traversable wormholes），通过局部变形广义Ellis–Bronnikov面积半径剖面构造出含一至四个喉（throat）的解析族，给出分岔曲线，并完成广义相对论源重构、能量条件与光环（light ring）稳定性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将多喉虫洞的拓扑结构、物质源重构、积分形式的零能量条件（NEC）含量与背景度规圆轨道结构纳入统一的参数空间描述。

Method: 在具有渐近角亏损（asymptotic angular deficit）的时空中局部变形广义Ellis–Bronnikov面积半径剖面；用正则标量、平移对称幽灵标量（shift-symmetric phantom scalar）及与正则标量耦合的磁非线性电磁场（NED）重构源；计算无量纲分岔曲线、Nandi–Zhang–Kumar协变体积积分及光环径向稳定性。

Result: 同一解析族依参数含一、二、三或四个喉，几何正则且渐近锥形；耦合消除未耦合单值NED的分支障碍。对G(F)=F+λ√F，NED部分主导渐近应力，按r^-2标度并趋近弦云状态方程。代表性双喉与四喉解在有限腹部中心区域逐点满足能量条件，喉处违反径向NEC并延伸为可积渐近尾。ρ+p_r的体积积分为变形幅度的二次式，其零点与分岔曲线不同。喉处存在径向不稳定的背景度规光环，中间腹部存在径向稳定光环，双喉情形含内部捕获的零测地线族。

Conclusion: 拓扑结构、源重构、积分NEC含量与背景度规圆轨道结构可在同一参数空间中统一描述，分岔、能量条件违反与光环稳定性由不同参数条件决定。

Abstract: We study an ultrastatic, spherically symmetric family of traversable wormholes obtained by locally deforming a generalized Ellis--Bronnikov areal-radius profile in a spacetime with an asymptotic angular deficit. Depending on the deformation parameters, the same analytic family contains one, two, three, or four throats. The corresponding bifurcation curves are obtained in dimensionless form, and the resulting geometries are regular and asymptotically conical. We also reconstruct a source in General Relativity using a canonical scalar, a shift-symmetric phantom scalar, and a magnetic nonlinear electromagnetic field coupled to the canonical scalar. In the reference multi-throat geometry, the coupling removes a branch obstruction of an uncoupled single-valued nonlinear electrodynamics (NED); the same obstruction occurs whenever equal values of the magnetic invariant require different values of $\mathcal G_F$. For $\mathcal G(F)=F+λ\sqrt F$, the NED sector carries the leading asymptotic stress, which scales as $r^{-2}$ and approaches the string-cloud equation of state. The total stress tensor satisfies pointwise energy conditions in finite belly-centered regions of representative two- and four-throat solutions, while the throats violate the radial null energy condition (NEC) and the violation extends into integrable asymptotic tails. The Nandi--Zhang--Kumar covariant volume integral of $ρ+p_r$ is quadratic in the deformation amplitude; its zero is distinct from the bifurcation curves. Away from saddle-node boundaries, each throat supports a radially unstable background-metric light ring, and each intermediate belly supports a radially stable one, with an internally trapped null family in the double-throat case. These results place the topology, source reconstruction, integrated NEC content, and background-metric circular-orbit structure in a common parameter-space description.

</details>


### [217] [A hybrid log-likelihood-ratio statistic for single-detector SPIIR gravitational-wave searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19918)
*SPIIR Collaboration,Weichangfeng Guo,Sunil Choudhary,Victor Oloworaran,Linqing Wen,Jayden Doan,Qingyuan Liang,Tim Davies,Luke Davis,Irina Dvorkin,Apratim Ganguly,Shang-Jie Jin,JeongCho Kim,Yu-Chiung Lin,Ngai-Chun Lung,Alistair McLeod,Khun Sang Phukon,Andreas Wicenec*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出首个专用于单探测器运行模式的 SPIIR（Summed Parallel Infinite Impulse Response）搜寻流程，用于双中子星（binary neutron star, BNS）与中子星—黑洞（neutron star-black hole, NSBH）并合搜索，并在 O3 数据上给出优于 GWTC-3 GstLAL 的单探测器灵敏距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅单台探测器在运行的时间段占 O3 的 13.3%、O4a 的 29.7%，而此前 GWTC-3 中仅 GstLAL 公布过单探测器结果，因此有必要开发并验证专用的单探测器搜索流程。

Method: 构建混合对数似然比（hybrid log-likelihood ratio），将针对非高斯瞬态噪声（non-Gaussian glitches）的信号一致性模型与高斯噪声假设下的信噪比剖面似然比（profile likelihood ratio）结合；按源类别与触发率等级校准 glitch 模型，并基于同一探测器与分析数据块（analysis chunk）实测背景的高端外推来赋予虚警率（false alarm rate, FAR）。

Result: 在每两个月一次虚警的阈值下，O3 数据中 BNS 灵敏距离为 L1 的 $136.5^{+2.9}_{-3.0}$ Mpc 与 H1 的 $120.2^{+3.1}_{-3.3}$ Mpc，比 GWTC-3 GstLAL 的 110.5 与 87.2 Mpc 分别高约 24% 与 38%；NSBH 为 L1 的 $164.1^{+6.7}_{-7.3}$ Mpc 与 H1 的 $134.9^{+6.3}_{-7.0}$ Mpc，与 GstLAL 的 165.1 与 156.0 Mpc 相当或略低。定向搜索以低于该阈值的 FAR 恢复 GW190425、GW200105_162426 与 GW230529_181500。

Conclusion: SPIIR 单探测器流程在 BNS 搜索中显著提升灵敏距离，在 NSBH 上与 GstLAL 大体可比，并能以合格 FAR 恢复已知事件，支持单探测器运行期的并合搜索。

Abstract: Periods with only one detector operating accounted for 13.3 percent of the third LIGO-Virgo observing run (O3) and 29.7 percent of the first part of the fourth observing run (O4a). We present the first dedicated single-detector implementation of the Summed Parallel Infinite Impulse Response (SPIIR) pipeline for binary neutron star (BNS) and neutron star-black hole (NSBH) merger searches. Our hybrid log-likelihood ratio combines signal-consistency models for non-Gaussian glitches with a profile likelihood ratio for the signal-to-noise ratio under a Gaussian-noise model. We calibrate the glitch model by source category and trigger-rate class. We assign false alarm rates (FARs) using the measured background from the same detector and analysis chunk, extrapolating its high-ranking tail. We check the FAR assignments by comparing observed and expected cumulative background counts for BNS in O3a and NSBH in O3b at LIGO Hanford (H1) and LIGO Livingston (L1). Using O3 data and the GWTC-3 injection set, we find that, at a threshold of one false alarm per two months, the BNS sensitive distances are $136.5^{+2.9}_{-3.0}$ Mpc in L1 and $120.2^{+3.1}_{-3.3}$ Mpc in H1. These central estimates are approximately 24 and 38 percent higher, respectively, than the GWTC-3 GstLAL values of $110.5^{+3.2}_{-3.4}$ Mpc and $87.2^{+3.6}_{-3.9}$ Mpc over the same data and injection parameter ranges. GstLAL provides the only published single-detector results in GWTC-3. For NSBH, SPIIR reaches $164.1^{+6.7}_{-7.3}$ Mpc in L1 and $134.9^{+6.3}_{-7.0}$ Mpc in H1, compared with $165.1^{+6.7}_{-7.3}$ Mpc and $156.0^{+5.9}_{-6.4}$ Mpc for GWTC-3 GstLAL, respectively. Targeted searches recover triggers associated with GW190425, GW200105_162426, and GW230529_181500 with assigned FARs below the same threshold.

</details>


### [218] [Quasinormal Modes and Environmental Trapping around Charged Letelier Black Holes with Perfect-Fluid Dark Matter and Quintessence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19935)
*Semra Gurtas Dogan,Abdullah Guvendi,Hassan Hassanabadi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作计算了被对数完美流体暗物质（PFDM）与精质（quintessence）包围的带电 Letelier 黑洞的无质量标量准正态谱（准正态模式，quasinormal modes），并分别处理 $Λ=0$ 与渐近反德西特（AdS）情形，另给出 $Λ=0$ 的测试麦克斯韦谱。结果显示弦云与精质降低振荡频率与阻尼率，而电荷与正 PFDM 参数提高二者；在 AdS 情形中采用 Chebyshev 伪谱法求解，发现寿命很长的束缚模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标在于刻画弦云（string cloud）、完美流体暗物质（perfect fluid dark matter, PFDM）与精质（quintessence）等额外物质场对带电 Letelier 黑洞扰动谱的影响，并检验其在渐近平坦与渐近 AdS 两种背景下的稳定性线索。摘要未明确陈述更宏观的动机。

Method: 对 $Λ=0$，采用带 Padé 重求和（Padé resummation）的六阶 WKB 方法计算准正态频率，并与独立的时间演化（time domain evolution）交叉验证；同时考察不稳定圆形零测地线（unstable circular null orbit）的角频率与 Lyapunov 指数作为 eikonal 极限的对照。对 $Λ<0$，因标量势在 AdS 边界发散、单势垒 WKB 公式失效，改用入射 Eddington-Finkelstein 坐标下的 Chebyshev 伪谱法（pseudospectral method），边界取 Dirichlet 条件、视界取正则条件。

Result: $Λ=0$ 时基模（fundamental modes）的 WKB 值与时间演化结果在 $0.06\%$ 内一致；更强的弦云与精质使振荡频率和阻尼率同时下降，较大的电荷或较大的正 PFDM 参数 $\beta$ 则使二者上升（按文中约定 $\beta>0$ 对应负 PFDM 能量密度，负 $\beta$ 时符号反转）。零测地线量在 eikonal 极限下复现上述趋势，并在百分之一量级上逼近 $\ell=2$ 的 WKB 频率。AdS 情形下的谱包含被光子球势垒与反射边界之间捕获的长寿命模式，阻尼率跨越约五个数量级，且与振荡频率同势垒顶部频率之比相关，符合隧穿泄漏图像；所研究的测试场扇区与参数范围内未发现增长模式。

Conclusion: 准正态谱对该黑洞的弦云、精质、电荷与 PFDM 参数呈系统性依赖，且在 AdS 背景下表现出势垒泄漏主导的长寿命模式结构。未发现增长模式仅适用于测试场扇区，不能据此断定完全耦合系统的稳定性。

Abstract: We compute the massless scalar quasinormal spectra of charged Letelier black holes surrounded by logarithmic perfect fluid dark matter (PFDM) and quintessence, for $Λ=0$ and for asymptotically anti-de Sitter (AdS) spacetimes, together with the test Maxwell spectra for $Λ=0$. For $Λ=0$, sixth order WKB values with Padé resummation agree with independent time domain evolutions to within $0.06\%$ for the fundamental modes. Stronger string cloud and quintessence lower both the oscillation frequencies and the damping rates, while a larger charge or a larger positive PFDM parameter $β$ raises both. In our convention $β>0$ means negative PFDM energy density; negative $β$ gives a positive density and reverses the shift. The angular frequency and Lyapunov exponent of the unstable circular null orbit reproduce the eikonal trends and approximate the $\ell=2$ WKB frequencies at the percent level. For $Λ<0$ the scalar potential diverges at the AdS boundary and the single barrier WKB formula does not apply. We therefore solve the eigenvalue problem with a Chebyshev pseudospectral method in ingoing Eddington-Finkelstein coordinates, with regularity at the horizon and a Dirichlet condition at the boundary, and check the modes against time domain evolutions and Schwarzschild-AdS benchmarks. The AdS spectrum contains long-lived modes trapped between the photon sphere barrier and the reflecting boundary, with damping rates spanning about five orders of magnitude. The damping correlates with the ratio of the oscillation frequency to the barrier top frequency, consistent with leakage through the barrier. No growing modes are found in the test field sectors and parameter ranges studied, which does not establish the stability of the fully coupled system.

</details>


### [219] [Probing superradiant cloud level transitions in stellar-mass black hole binaries with milli-Hertz and multiband gravitational wave observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20004)
*Si-Tong Peng,Anson Chen,Si-Yang Pu,Jun Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究恒星级黑洞双星中超辐射云能级跃迁（superradiant cloud level transitions）在毫赫兹（milli-Hertz）及多波段引力波观测中的可探测性，发现云耗竭可早于共振发生、电离可提前并合，并给出云质量分数的后验约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超轻玻色子（ultralight bosons）可通过超辐射（superradiance）在黑洞周围形成云，其能级跃迁可能在引力波信号中留下印记；本文旨在评估恒星级黑洞双星中此类跃迁在毫赫兹与多波段观测下的探测前景。

Method: 在给定的云形成与存活条件下，对耦合的云—轨道演化（coupled cloud-orbit evolution）进行建模，考察精细跃迁（fine transition）$\lvert322\rangle\rightarrow\lvert31-1\rangle$ 在 LISA 波段的相位修正，以及毫赫兹波段观测之后发生的玻尔跃迁（Bohr transitions）对并合时间（merger time）的偏移；并对一个代表性近邻双星做真空注入—回收（injection-recovery）分析。

Result: 建模显示云耗竭（cloud depletion）可在共振之前开始，且电离（ionization）可能提前而非推迟并合；对代表性近邻双星，注入—回收分析给出的云质量分数后验集中于 $M_c^i/M\lesssim10^{-6}$；将 LISA 预言的并合时间与后续地面测量比较，可对后期云演化提供补充检验。

Conclusion: 结果支持利用毫赫兹与多波段引力波观测对超轻玻色子开展互补搜寻（complementary searches）。

Abstract: We investigate superradiant cloud level transitions in stellar-mass black hole binaries with milli-Hertz and multiband gravitational-wave observations. Under the adopted cloud formation and survival conditions, the $\lvert322\rangle\rightarrow\lvert31-1\rangle$ fine transition can imprint phase corrections in the LISA band, while Bohr transitions occurring after the milli-Hertz band observation can be probed through shifts in the merger time. Modeling the coupled cloud-orbit evolution, we find that cloud depletion can begin before resonance and that ionization can advance rather than delay coalescence. For a representative nearby binary, a vacuum injection-recovery analysis yields a cloud-mass-fraction posterior concentrated at $M_c^i/M\lesssim10^{-6}$. Comparing the LISA-predicted coalescence time with a subsequent ground-based measurement provides a complementary test of later cloud evolution. These results motivate complementary searches for ultralight bosons with milli-Hertz and multiband gravitational-wave observations.

</details>


### [220] [Casimir-like wormholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20168)
*Mauricio Cataldo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在能量密度 $\rho\propto 1/r^{4}$ 且径向压强满足 $p_r=\omega\rho$ 的球对称时空中，给出爱因斯坦场方程（Einstein's equations）的最一般解，统一涵盖黑洞、裸奇点（naked singularities）与可穿越虫洞（traversable wormholes）。结果表明仅 Casimir 型物质（$\omega>0$）与幽灵物质（phantom matter，$\omega<-1$）可支撑虫洞；对 $\omega=3$，横向压强 $p_t\to-\rho$ 由场方程自然涌现。将能量密度振幅视为自由经典参数，可得喉半径从微观到天体尺度的虫洞，且在 $r_0\gtrsim 10^{8}$ m 时径向自由落体满足全部人类可穿越性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于系统考察 $\rho\propto 1/r^{4}$ 这类特定物源所对应的球对称时空解族，厘清何种状态方程（equation of state）物质能够支撑可穿越虫洞（traversable wormholes），并检验 Casimir 型应力-能量张量（Casimir stress-energy tensor）的代数结构是否必须事先假定，抑或可作为场方程的推论出现。

Method: 求解爱因斯坦场方程（Einstein's equations）得到该物源类的最一般解，由积分常数与 $\omega$ 参数化；再施加 flare-out 条件筛选可穿越虫洞，并分析横向压强行为、异物质（exotic matter）总量、旅行者加速度、潮汐力（tidal forces）与穿越时间，辅以嵌入图（embedding diagrams）刻画几何分区。

Result: 仅 Casimir 型物质（$\omega>0$）与幽灵物质（phantom matter，$\omega<-1$）满足 flare-out 条件，精质（quintessence）与常规暗能量被排除。对 Casimir 值 $\omega=3$，$p_t\to-\rho$ 在中心奇点附近由场方程自然涌现，完整 Casimir 张量代数结构无需外加假定。将能量密度振幅作为自由经典参数时，喉半径可从微观延伸至天体尺度；施加量子 Casimir 能标即回到 Garattini 的普朗克尺度 Casimir 虫洞。异物质总量对任意 $\omega$ 均有限，且与喉半径线性标度；当 $r_0\gtrsim 10^{8}$ m 时径向自由落体满足全部人类可穿越性条件。

Conclusion: $\rho\propto1/r^{4}$ 物源族在同一框架下统一描述黑洞、裸奇点与可穿越虫洞，其 Casimir 型构型虽共享 Casimir 张量的函数形式，但并非由物理 Casimir 效应产生；该解族为虫洞尺度与可穿越性提供了经典参数化的普适图景，并由嵌入图揭示三类依赖于 $\omega$ 的几何区间。

Abstract: We investigate spherically symmetric spacetimes sourced by matter with energy density $ρ\propto 1/r^{4}$ and equation of state $p_r = ωρ$. We derive the most general solution of Einstein's equations for this class of sources, obtaining black holes, naked singularities and traversable wormholes, parametrized by the integration constants and $ω$. We show that only two classes of matter can support traversable wormholes: Casimir-type matter with $ω> 0$ and phantom matter with $ω< -1$, while quintessence and conventional dark energy are excluded by the flare-out condition. For the Casimir value $ω= 3$, the transverse pressure approaches $p_t \to -ρ$ near the central singularity, so the full algebraic structure of the Casimir stress-energy tensor, $ρ\propto -1/r^{4}$, $p_r = 3ρ$, $p_t = -ρ$, is not imposed but emerges from Einstein's equations. Treating the amplitude of the energy density as a free classical parameter, we obtain wormholes with throat radii from microscopic to astrophysical scales. These Casimir-like configurations share the functional form of the Casimir tensor but are not generated by the physical Casimir effect; Garattini's Planck-scale Casimir wormhole is recovered when the quantum Casimir energy scale is imposed. The total amount of exotic matter is finite for arbitrary $ω$ and scales linearly with the throat radius. Analyzing the traveler's acceleration, tidal forces and transit times, we show that radial free fall satisfies all human traversability conditions for $r_0 \gtrsim 10^{8}$ m. Finally, embedding diagrams illustrate three geometric regimes depending on $ω$.

</details>


### [221] [Canonical metrics on punctured spheres, II: compactified moduli spaces of black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20282)
*Boris Botvinnik*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究 $S^n$ 上典则标量平坦度量（canonical scalar-flat metric）在穿刺点碰撞时的渐近行为，证明质量矩阵沿显式射线对数发散，并给出正则化后光滑延伸至 Axelrod--Singer 紧化模空间的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Part I 引入了 $S^n\setminus\mathfrak{p}_\ell$ 上的典则标量平坦度量及其质量矩阵（mass matrix），但穿刺点相互碰撞（即黑洞合并）时度量与质量的极限行为尚未刻画，这关系到模空间边界上的质量数据与视界结构。

Method: 令穿刺点子集碰撞：若黑洞簇 $A$ 在相对尺度 $t$ 下塌缩，则展开质量矩阵 $η=E(A)\ln(1/t)+O(1)$；对嵌套簇族用有根树（rooted trees）组织退化结构；再减去发散项得到正则化质量矩阵，并考察其在 Axelrod--Singer 紧化模空间（compactified moduli space）各层上的延拓及泡树（bubble tree）逐顶点的分解。

Result: 正则化质量矩阵光滑延拓至 $\overline{M}^{\,\mathrm{AS}}_\ell=\overline C_{\ell-1}(\mathbb{R}^n)/\mathrm O(n)$ 的每一层，并按泡树顶点自然分裂。几何上，重标度度量在每个带穿刺点顶点收敛为填洞后的完备标量平坦度量，相邻片由 Schwarzschild 颈部（neck）连接，$n=3$ 时颈部含极小曲面即公共视界（apparent horizon）。对双洞顶点确定了各节点相对极限视界的位置，并给出颈部构成最外视界分量的判据（以猜想形式陈述）。此外比较了 Part I 的质量下界与 Riemannian Penrose 不等式；因质量矩阵在泡旋转下不变，其实际定义在紧化的 Deligne--Mumford 型商上。

Conclusion: 质量发散具有显式的射线方向与对数速率，减去发散后的正则化质量矩阵是模空间紧化上的光滑对象，其分层结构与泡树的顶点一一对应，并携带各泡的质量数据；该框架将黑洞碰撞的度量退化、视界合并与质量下界统一在同一几何图景中。

Abstract: Let $g_{\mathfrak{p}_{\ell}}$ denote the canonical scalar-flat metric on $S^n\setminus \mathfrak{p}_{\ell}$ introduced in Part~I, which for $n=3$ corresponds to the Brill--Lindquist initial data for $\ell$ black holes, and let $η\in H_{\ell}$ be its associated mass matrix. In this paper, we investigate the asymptotic behavior of the metrics and the mass matrix $η$ as subsets of punctures collide. Such collisions cause every mass to diverge; if a cluster $A$ of black holes collapses at a relative scale $t$, the mass matrix satisfies $η=E(A)\ln(1/t)+O(1)$, diverging along an explicit ray $E(A)\in H_{\ell}$. For nested families of clusters, this degeneracy structure naturally organizes into a space of rooted trees. Subtracting the divergence gives a renormalized mass matrix which extends smoothly to every stratum of the Axelrod--Singer compactified moduli space $\overline{M}{}^{\,\mathrm{AS}}_\ell= \overline C_{\ell-1}(\mathbb{R}^n)/\mathrm O(n)$ and splits naturally into one mass matrix per vertex of the bubble tree.
  Geometrically, at each vertex carrying punctures the suitably rescaled metrics converge to a complete scalar-flat metric with the nodes filled in, adjacent such pieces being joined by Schwarzschild necks, and the renormalized matrix acting as its mass data. For $n=3$, the necks contain minimal spheres, which are common apparent horizons of the merging holes. At vertices bearing two holes, we determine on which side of the limiting horizon each node lies, and we formulate as a conjecture the resulting criterion for a neck to be a component of the outermost horizon. We also compare the mass lower bound of Part~I with the Riemannian Penrose inequality. Because the mass matrix is invariant under rotations of the bubbles, it lives on a Deligne--Mumford-type quotient of the compactification.

</details>


### [222] [Repeated Gravitational-Wave Lensing from Binary Black Holes on Eccentric and Precessing Orbits around Spinning Supermassive Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20416)
*Jie Wu,Mengfei Sun,Jin Li,Zhoujian Cao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究层级三体系统（hierarchical triple system）中外轨道动力学演化如何影响恒星级双黑洞（BBH）绕超大质量黑洞（SMBH）轨道运动时的重复引力透镜（repeated gravitational lensing）事件及其参数推断，基于一年期LISA观测进行模拟分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于轨道诱导引力波调制（orbit-induced GW modulation）的研究启发考察演化层级三体系统中的重复透镜；需明确外轨道偏心率、倾角与相对论进动对连续透镜事件及参数推断的影响。

Method: 外轨道采用后牛顿（post-Newtonian）动力学，旋近波形（inspiral waveform）包含移动源效应与德西特进动（de Sitter precession）；采用事件局域点质量透镜（event-local point-mass lensing）模型追踪变化的源—透镜对准与引力波频率；对一年期LISA观测分析透镜事件识别、Fisher矩阵参数不确定度估计及忽略透镜时的波形残差。

Result: 外轨道演化改变连续透镜事件的时间与放大率，并可将初始不利构型带入透镜区域；固定圆轨道、无进动近似在相当比例的选定透镜系统中漏检全部透镜事件；不确定度中位数相对缩减以外轨道倾角与SMBH自旋最为显著；部分高于探测阈值的系统在局域调整无透镜模型参数后仍存在透镜诱导的波形差异。

Conclusion: 重复透镜可为LISA对层级BBH的观测提供关于SMBH性质与外轨道几何的互补约束。

Abstract: Gravitational waves (GWs) from a stellar-mass binary black hole (BBH) orbiting a supermassive black hole (SMBH) can experience repeated gravitational lensing as the BBH passes behind the SMBH. Our previous studies of orbit-induced GW modulation motivate this investigation of repeated lensing in evolving hierarchical triple systems. We investigate how eccentricity, inclination, and relativistic precession of the outer orbit affect successive lensing events and parameter inference. The outer orbit follows post-Newtonian dynamics, and the inspiral waveform includes moving-source effects and de Sitter precession. An event-local point-mass lensing model tracks the changing source--lens alignment and GW frequency. For a one-year LISA observation, we examine lensing-event identification, Fisher-matrix estimates of parameter uncertainties, and waveform residuals when lensing is omitted. Our results show that outer-orbit evolution changes the timing and amplification of successive lensing events and can bring initially unfavorable configurations into the lensing region. A fixed circular, nonprecessing approximation misses all lensing events for a substantial fraction of the selected lensed systems. The median fractional reductions in uncertainty are largest for the outer-orbit inclination and SMBH spin. For some systems above the adopted detection threshold, lensing-induced waveform differences persist after local adjustments to the parameters of the unlensed model. Repeated lensing can thus provide complementary constraints on SMBH properties and outer-orbit geometry in LISA observations of hierarchical BBHs.

</details>


### [223] [Harmonic branches of Einstein--ModMax--scalar field gravity: exact solutions and regularity II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20529)
*Leonel Bixano,Tonatiuh Matos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 论文在 Einstein–ModMax–Scalar 引力框架下，保持电磁相位（electromagnetic phase）\(\tan\Theta=\mathcal{G}/\mathcal{F}\) 非常数，系统积分出各调和解族并重建物理场函数，给出若干精确解的完整目录与两条定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有调和解（harmonic solution）构造多局限为 Maxwell 情形或常数电磁相位，缺乏对 ModMax 非线电动力学（nonlinear electrodynamics）耦合标量场时非常数相位解的系统刻画。

Method: 对每个解族先求解场方程 \((a(s),b(s),c(s))\) 并重构 \(f,\omega,k,A_t,A_\varphi,\varphi\)；再选取 Schwarzschild 或 Coulomb 调和函数，施加正则视界条件（regular horizon conditions），识别 ModMax 解及其 Maxwell 极限（Maxwell limit）。另用轴向 LWP 重构处理旋转背景。

Result: 目录涵盖已知 RN 与带电 Taub–NUT 解、单电荷构型、显式静态双荷子（dyon）以及受限的含活跃标量定态解族；两条定理区分常数电磁相位与精确静态电荷重标度，并说明 twist 如何阻止固定度规与标量下电磁势的对角重标度。轴向重构复现 Melvin–swirling 背景并给出其内精确 ModMax Schwarzschild 外部解。

Conclusion: 视界处光滑的度规与电磁极限不同于零赤道圆上的本构延拓（constitutive continuation）；无电荷构型与早期 Maxwell 场可回溯至先前的调和目录，表明该框架统一并推广了既有解。

Abstract: We integrate harmonic solution branches of Einstein--ModMax--Scalar field gravity, keeping the electromagnetic phase \(\tanΘ=\mathcal{G}/\mathcal{F}\) non-constant. For each family we first solve the field equations $(a(\s),b(\s),c(\s))$ and reconstruct the physical functions $f,ω,k,A_t,A_\varphi,φ$. In the same section we then select the Schwarzschild or Coulomb harmonic function, impose the regular horizon conditions, identify the ModMax solution, and state its Maxwell limit. Two theorems distinguish a constant electromagnetic phase from an exact static charge rescaling and show how twist prevents constant diagonal rescalings of the electromagnetic potentials at fixed metric and scalar. The catalogue covers known RN and charged Taub--NUT solutions, single-charge configurations, explicit static dyons with scalar-dependent couplings, and a restricted stationary family with an active scalar. We distinguish new explicit ModMax extensions of known Maxwell profiles from the nonfrozen stationary integration. The axial LWP reconstruction recovers the known Melvin--swirling background and gives an exact ModMax Schwarzschild exterior within that background. At its horizon, smooth metric and electromagnetic limits are distinguished from the constitutive continuation at a null equatorial circle. Uncharged configurations and earlier Maxwell fields are traced back to the preceding harmonic catalogue.

</details>


### [224] [Quasinormal modes of Kerr--de Sitter black holes in semi-open systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20627)
*Ming-Fei Ji,Liang-Bi Wu,Yu-Sen Zhou,Li-Ming Cao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文通过将角度与径向 Teukolsky 方程约化为 Heun 方程，数值研究具有部分反射内边界的 Kerr–de Sitter 黑洞准正规模谱，揭示回波分支与两类不稳定性机制，并指出内边界条件的表述方式显著影响谱结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半开放 Kerr–de Sitter 系统中内边界（事件视界附近）的物理性质尚不明确，需厘清部分反射内边界如何控制准正规模（quasinormal modes, QNMs）谱结构与稳定性。

Method: 将角度与径向 Teukolsky 方程约化为 Heun 方程，对 s=-2、ℓ=m=2 模式进行数值计算，扫描反射率 R₀ 与自转 a 等参数；并推导 Teukolsky 振幅反射率与能量反射率之比，比较渐近振幅形式与 Sasaki–Nakamura 变量上的有限半径 Robin 条件两种边界表述。

Result: 随反射率 R₀ 增大，QNMs 出现趋于实轴、间距近似规则的回波分支，另一些则趋近 Re ω = mΩ(r₊)；随自转 a 增大，部分模式趋近实轴或保持有限阻尼，部分呈近似水平位移且实部跟随 mΩ(r₊)。能量反射率可在 mΩ(r₊) 处发散（即使振幅反射率很小），对应“主动壁”效应；以渐近振幅表述反射边界条件会在入射连接系数各极点附近产生共振，延伸至任意大的正虚部，而 Sasaki–Nakamura 变量上的精确有限半径 Robin 条件可消除这些极点起源频率。

Conclusion: 内边界的规定方式（prescription）决定了半开放 Kerr–de Sitter 系统的谱结构与稳定性；不稳定性由超辐射放大（superradiant amplification）与反射壁能量注入两种互补机制驱动，且边界条件的数学表述本身会引入非物理的极点起源频率。

Abstract: We investigate the gravitational quasinormal mode spectrum of Kerr--de Sitter black holes with a partially reflective inner boundary near the event horizon, by reducing the angular and radial Teukolsky equations to Heun equations. We vary different parameters and study the $s=-2$, $\ell=m=2$ modes numerically. As the reflectivity $\mathcal{R}_0$ increases, QNMs develop echo branches approaching the real axis with approximately regular spacing, while others approach $\operatorname{Re}ω=mΩ(r_+)$. As the rotation $a$ increases, some QNMs approach the real axis or retain finite damping, while others exhibit approximately horizontal shifts with real parts tracking $mΩ(r_+)$. Two mechanisms driving the instabilities are identified in complementary regimes, namely superradiant amplification and energy injection from the reflective wall. We derive the ratio between Teukolsky amplitude and energy reflectivities, finding that the latter can diverge at $mΩ(r_+)$ even when the former is small, which accounts for an active wall in this regime. We also find that formulating the reflective boundary condition in terms of asymptotic amplitudes gives rise to resonances near each pole of the ingoing connection coefficient, extending to arbitrarily large positive imaginary parts. An exact finite radius Robin condition imposed on the Sasaki--Nakamura variable removes these pole-born frequencies. These results show how the inner boundary prescription controls the spectrum structure and stability of semi-open Kerr--de Sitter systems.

</details>


### [225] [Boson Stars with Ayón--Beato--García nonlinear electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20634)
*Long-Xing Huang,Yong-Qiang Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究爱因斯坦引力中最小耦合 Ayón–Beato–García 非线性电动力学（nonlinear electrodynamics）与有质量复标量场的球对称玻色子星（boson star）解，发现解仅在电荷低于临界值时存在，并揭示小电荷下的引力蓝移与负质量函数、大电荷下的"冻结"玻色子星及类极端黑洞（extremal black hole）时空特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将非线性电动力学与有质量复标量场结合，考察电荷对球对称玻色子星存在性与时空结构的影响，填补该耦合体系中解的性质研究空白。

Method: 在爱因斯坦引力框架下求解球对称玻色子星解，耦合 Ayón–Beato–García 非线性电动力学与有质量复标量场，分析电荷 q 与标量场频率 ω 对解及度规函数的影响。

Result: 解仅在电荷低于临界值时存在；小电荷解可出现引力蓝移（gravitational blueshift），部分区域径向质量函数为负而 ADM 质量（ADM mass）保持为正；较大电荷下当 ω→0 时出现冻结玻色子星，在 r_ch 附近 1/g_rr 与 -g_tt 极小，从无穷远观测者看时空类似极端黑洞，且类时轨迹（timelike trajectory）在该处强烈偏折、穿越相同径向距离需要更长坐标时间。

Conclusion: 电荷存在上限约束玻色子星解的存在性；大电荷极限下冻结解呈现类极端黑洞的时空几何与显著的轨迹偏折效应，为非线性电动力学背景下致密天体的观测特征提供理论参考。

Abstract: In this paper, we study spherically symmetric boson star solutions in Einstein gravity minimally coupled to Ayón--Beato--García nonlinear electrodynamics and a massive complex scalar field. We find that these solutions exist only when the electric charge $q$ is below a critical value. Within this range, solutions with relatively small charges can exhibit a gravitational blueshift, and the radial mass function becomes negative in some regions while the ADM mass remains positive. For larger charges, frozen boson-star solutions emerge as the scalar-field frequency $ω$ approaches zero. In these configurations, the metric function $1/g_{rr}$ and $-g_{tt}$ become very small near a special position $r_{\mathrm{ch}}$, and the spacetime resembles an extremal black hole from the perspective of an observer at infinity. Moreover, timelike trajectories in these frozen boson stars are strongly deflected near $r_{\mathrm{ch}}$, and particles require a longer coordinate time to traverse the same radial distance than they do in ordinary boson stars.

</details>


### [226] [Simultaneous tests of post-Newtonian inspiral using GW250114 and GW250207](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20738)
*Saurabh Kumar,Parthapratim Mahapatra,Ish Mohan Gupta,M. Saleem,B. S. Sathyaprakash,K. G. Arun*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 利用两个高信噪比双黑洞事件（GW250114、GW250207），同时放开至3.5PN阶的全部八个相位系数，对后牛顿（post-Newtonian, PN）旋进阶段进行高维检验；结果显示数据对三个主成分给出超出先验的约束，且均与广义相对论（general relativity, GR）一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过往后牛顿旋进检验多局限于少数相位系数或单参数偏差，缺乏对多维形变空间的同时约束，因此需要在高信噪比事件上开展更一般化的高维检验。

Method: 采用IMRPhenomXPHM_SpinTaylor波形模型，并在PN相位中引入参数化偏差，对八个至3.5PN阶的相位系数同时进行贝叶斯推断（Bayesian inference）；随后对八维形变空间做主成分分析（principal component analysis），识别数据约束最强的系数组合。

Result: 数据对前三个主成分的约束超出其先验范围，且三者均与广义相对论的预言一致。

Conclusion: 极高信噪比的引力波（gravitational-wave）事件可实现稳健的高维旋进动力学检验，并直接给出PN形变空间中可测量的子空间。

Abstract: We perform the one of the most general tests of post-Newtonian (PN) inspiral in binary black holes to date by allowing all eight phase coefficients up to 3.5PN order to vary simultaneously from their general-relativistic values. Using two of the loudest binary black hole events observed to date, GW250114 and GW250207, we carry out Bayesian inference with the IMRPhenomXPHM_SpinTaylor waveform model augmented by parametrized deviations in the post-Newtonian phasing. We then identify the combinations of phase coefficients most strongly constrained by the data through a principal component analysis of the eight-dimensional deformation space. We find that the data constrain the three leading principal components beyond their priors, and that all three are consistent with the predictions of general relativity. These results demonstrate that exceptionally loud gravitational-wave events enable robust, high-dimensional tests of inspiral dynamics and provide direct access to the measurable subspace of post-Newtonian deformations.

</details>


### [227] [Cartan geometry and the Hamiltonian analysis of gravitational actions in terms of forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20850)
*J. Fernando Barbero G.,Juan Margalef-Bentabol,Valle Varo,Eduardo J. S. Villaseñor*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文综述了以微分形式（differential forms）表述的若干广义相对论（general relativity）作用量的哈密顿形式（Hamiltonian formulation），并指出嘉当几何（Cartan geometry）可简化哈密顿分析并给出统一的几何理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目的在于梳理与广义相对论相关的、可用微分形式写出的多个作用量所对应的哈密顿表述，以克服其分析复杂性并寻求一致的几何解释。

Method: 采用综述方式，借助嘉当几何（Cartan geometry）的思想对相关作用量进行哈密顿分析（Hamiltonian analysis）。

Result: 嘉当几何被表明能有效简化哈密顿分析，并由此获得对上述作用量连贯而全面的几何理解。

Conclusion: 嘉当几何为微分形式表述的广义相对论作用量提供了统一且有力的哈密顿分析框架。

Abstract: The purpose of the paper is to review the Hamiltonian formulation for several actions related to general relativity that can be written in terms of differential forms. As we will show the powerful ideas of Cartan geometry can be used to simplify the Hamiltonian analysis and arrive at a coherent and comprehensive geometrical understanding of it.

</details>


### [228] [Gravitational collapse in Lorentz-violating gravity and the spin-0 mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21034)
*Emma Albertini,Massimo Vaglio,Enrico Barausse*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文通过球对称标量场坍缩的全非线性数值演化检验Einstein-Aether理论中标量（spin-0）模式的物理性，发现该模式确实是携带正能量、以线性理论预言速度传播的动力学波，从而反驳了Taherasghari-Will后牛顿分析的质疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Taherasghari与Will的后牛顿分析在紧致双星辐射反作用中未发现spin-0模式速度的痕迹，据此质疑该模式是否为动力学、携能的波；本文旨在用非线性演化直接检验这一疑问。

Method: 采用球对称标量场坍缩的全非线性数值演化；在此对称性下spin-0模式是唯一的辐射自由度，可直接观测其传播与能量性质，并考察大耦合情形下的坍缩结局。

Result: 坍缩发出以半径倒数衰减的出射spin-0波，速度与线性理论预言一致并携带正能量至无穷远；黑洞周围残留的静态以太（aether）构型能量为负，使视界质量超过ADM质量。大耦合下不形成黑洞，重现Akhoury-Garfinkle-Gupta的瞬态白洞（white holes）；更高分辨率下坍缩壳层持续陡峭化且不被任何囚禁面遮蔽，可能暗示裸奇点（naked singularity）。

Conclusion: spin-0模式是动力学且携能的辐射自由度，与后牛顿分析的质疑相反；同时大耦合区出现白洞与可能的裸奇点，提示该理论存在值得关注的强场行为。

Abstract: Einstein-Aether theory is a theory of gravity with a preferred frame, in which Lorentz symmetry is broken by a dynamical unit timelike vector field. The theory is closely related to Horava gravity, and in spherically symmetric collapse the two produce the same dynamics. Both propagate a scalar (spin-0) mode besides the tensor mode and, in Einstein-Aether theory, the vector mode. The physical nature of the spin-0 mode has recently been called into question following the post-Newtonian analysis of Taherasghari and Will, whose post-Newtonian calculation of radiation reaction in compact binaries finds no trace of its speed in the energy loss, suggesting that the mode may not be a dynamical, energy-carrying wave. We test this with fully nonlinear evolutions of scalar-field collapse in spherical symmetry, where the spin-0 mode is the only radiative degree of freedom. The collapse emits an outgoing spin-0 wave that falls off as the inverse of the radius, travels at the speed predicted by the linear theory, and carries positive energy to infinity. The static aether configuration left around the black hole has negative energy instead, so that the horizon mass exceeds the ADM mass. For large couplings, collapse does not produce a black hole: we recover the transient white holes found by Akhoury, Garfinkle and Gupta, but at higher resolution the collapsing shell keeps steepening, not hidden behind any trapped surface, which may signal a naked singularity.

</details>


### [229] [Maximum entropy reconstruction of the black hole greybody factors from its Korteweg-de Vries integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21043)
*Noah Alberca,Michele Lenzi,Carlos F. Sopuerta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文提出以最大熵原理（maximum entropy principle）求解由KdV积分构成的矩问题（moment problem），从而在截断阶数下重构黑洞（black hole）灰体因子（greybody factor），并在Pöschl-Teller势与Regge-Wheeler势上完成验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究表明扰动黑洞的主方程具有由KdV层级（KdV hierarchy）生成的无穷多对称性，其守恒量（KdV integrals）可通过矩问题完全决定灰体因子；但该矩问题缺乏一种构造上保证正性、且偏差最小的求解方法。

Method: 引入最大熵原理求解矩问题：在每个截断阶数下，重构与有限个KdV积分相容的最小偏置（least-biased）透射概率（transmission probability），并由构造保证其正性（positivity）。先在可精确求解的Pöschl-Teller势上验证，再应用于无精确解的史瓦西黑洞（Schwarzschild BH）轴向微扰所对应的Regge-Wheeler势。

Result: 该方法对Pöschl-Teller势的验证通过；对Regge-Wheeler势，重构结果在若干多极（multipoles）上与四阶WKB近似（fourth-order Wentzel-Kramers-Brillouin approximation）高度吻合，表明有限数量的KdV积分即可准确恢复灰体因子。

Conclusion: 本文首次将最大熵方法（maximum entropy method）应用于黑洞灰体因子的重构，说明少量KdV积分足以实现高精度重建，为利用对称性守恒量刻画黑洞扰动提供了新途径。

Abstract: The dynamics of perturbed non-rotating black holes (BHs) can be described by master wave equations with a potential barrier. Recent work has shown that these equations possess an infinite number of symmetries generated by the Korteweg-de Vries (KdV) hierarchy, leading to conserved quantities (KdV integrals) that fully determine the BH greybody factors via a moment problem. In this paper, we introduce and validate a new method to solve this moment problem using the maximum entropy principle. The method reconstructs, at each order of truncation, the least-biased transmission probability compatible with a finite set of KdV integrals, guaranteeing positivity by construction. We first validate the technique against the exactly solvable Pöschl-Teller potential. We then apply it to the Regge-Wheeler potential governing axial perturbations of a Schwarzschild BH, where no exact solution is available; the resulting reconstruction agrees closely with the fourth-order Wentzel-Kramers-Brillouin (WKB) approximation across several multipoles. This is the first application of the maximum entropy method to the reconstruction of BH greybody factors, demonstrating that a modest number of KdV integrals is sufficient to recover them accurately.

</details>


### [230] [On Chen's Extended Action for $κ(R,T)$ Gravity: Gravity in a Polarizable Medium, Material Folds and a No-Bounce Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21093)
*Ginés R. Pérez Teruel*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文将 Chen 的局域伴随作用（local adjoint action）解释为具有物理意义的变分原理，用于从 $κ(R,T)$ 理论导出场方程；在平坦 FLRW 背景下，Dirac 约化使哈密顿量在正则分支上不依赖 $κ(T)$，耦合通过第二类物质约束（second-class matter constraint）引入，并由此发展出可极化介质图像与折叠（fold）处的动力学终止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为 $κ(R,T)$ 引力的局域变分表述提供物理诠释，说明伴随场并非形式工具，而可充当物质响应变量，并厘清该理论与圈量子宇宙学（loop quantum cosmology, LQC）在守恒律及奇点处理上的异同。

Method: 采用 Dirac 约束约化（Dirac reduction）处理平坦 FLRW 中的哈密顿结构，配合有效源（effective source）的精确分解、线性耦合与尘埃（dust）情形下的解析求解，并以 Dirac 自洽性条件判断折叠处的可延拓性；另给出约化尘埃约束的 Koopman–von Neumann 输运量子化（transport quantization）。

Result: 物理应力张量一般不守恒，但“着装”粒子数（dressed particle number）守恒；线性尘埃模型的低密度膨胀分支在过去端点处终止于折叠，体积最小、密度达 $ρ_c/2$ 且曲率有限，Dirac 自洽性排除了平滑哈密顿延拓；对 $κ>0$、正密度与 $w>-1$ 排除了平坦 FLRW 反弹（bounce）。该模型可复现 LQC 的有效 Friedmann 方程（有效压强 $-ρ^2/ρ_c$），但守恒律不同；折叠密度与形变代数（deformed-algebra）途径的符号改变阈值一致。

Conclusion: $κ(R,T)$ 的局域伴随表述具有实质物理内容：$κ=0$、排斥相与物质映射折叠分别对应完全屏蔽（total screening）、过屏蔽（overscreening）与饱和（saturation）；伴随场作为响应变量支持开放系统（open-system）诠释。

Abstract: Chen's local adjoint action reproduces the field equations of $κ(R,T)$ from a local variational principle, and we show that it does physical work. In flat FLRW, Dirac reduction gives a Hamiltonian independent of $κ(T)$ on regular branches, where the coupling enters through a second-class matter constraint. The physical stress tensor is not generally conserved, but a dressed particle number is. An exact decomposition of the effective source motivates a polarizable-medium interpretation: $κ=0$ corresponds to total screening, the repulsive phase to overscreening, and a fold of the matter map to saturation. For linear coupling and dust, the expanding low-density branch has a past endpoint at the fold, where the volume is minimal and the density reaches $ρ_c/2$ at finite curvature. Dirac consistency rules out smooth Hamiltonian continuation through the fold. More generally, a flat-FLRW bounce is excluded for $κ>0$, positive density and $w>-1$. The linear dust model reproduces the effective Friedmann equation of loop quantum cosmology (LQC), but the two models obey different conservation laws. An effective pressure $-ρ^2/ρ_c$ restores the homogeneous LQC dynamics, and the fold density coincides with the signature-change threshold in the deformed-algebra approach to LQC. The reduced dust constraint admits an exactly solvable Koopman--von Neumann transport quantization and the adjoint field acts as a response variable, motivating an open-system interpretation.

</details>


### [231] [Detectability of Eccentric Intermediate Mass-Ratio Inspiral in the Decihertz Band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21323)
*Devesh Giri,Sajal Mukherjee,Apratim Ganguly,Sanjit Mitra*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文检验了以圆轨道模板（circular templates）搜索偏心（eccentric）中等质量比旋近（IMRI）信号的合理性，指出失配在初始偏心率 $e_0\simeq3\times10^{-2}$ 时超过 3% 阈值，而可分辨偏心率下限低至 $5\times10^{-4}$，因此未来分赫兹（decihertz）探测需引入偏心波形模型与模板库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分赫兹引力波频段将观测于致密星团中动力学形成（dynamical formation）、预期保留可测偏心率的 IMRI；然而现有搜寻与参数估计流程（parameter-estimation pipelines）多基于圆轨道模板，该假设在 IMRI 情形下缺乏检验。

Method: 采用 \textsc{FastEMRIWaveforms} 基于黑洞微扰理论（black-hole perturbation theory）的波形，针对代表性 IMRI（$M=10^4\,M_\odot$、$q=100$、观测末 10 小时），用差分进化（differential-evolution）搜索对模板总质量与质量比最大化拟合因子（fitting factor），覆盖 B-DECIGO、LGWA-Si、IndIGO-D 噪声曲线及平坦功率谱密度（PSD）。

Result: 失配随 $e_0$ 增大，在 $e_0\simeq3\times10^{-2}$ 时越过 3% 标准阈值；$e_0=0.1$ 时最佳圆模板对总质量与质量比产生数个百分比的偏差。结合依赖信噪比的可分辨性判据（distinguishability criterion），1 Gpc 源的可分辨偏心率低至 $e_0\simeq5\times10^{-4}$（B-DECIGO、IndIGO-D）与 $5\times10^{-3}$（LGWA-Si），比探测效率下降处低一至两个数量级。

Conclusion: 失配源于径向 Teukolsky 谐波（radial Teukolsky harmonics）激发使功率扩散至圆模板无法表示的边带（sidebands），以及固定初始半通径（semi-latus rectum）下偏心双星的轨道衰减更慢。DECIGO、LGWA 与 IndIGO-D 对 IMRI 的稳健探测与参数估计需要偏心波形模型与模板库。

Abstract: The proposed decihertz gravitational-wave (GW) band $(\sim 10^{-2}-1\,\Hz)$ will observe the late inspiral of intermediate mass-ratio inspirals (IMRIs), binaries with mass ratios $10^2 \lesssim q \lesssim 10^4$ that assemble dynamically in dense stellar environments and are expected to retain measurable eccentricity. Search and parameter-estimation pipelines are predominantly built around circular templates, and we test this assumption. Using black-hole perturbation theory waveforms from \textsc{FastEMRIWaveforms} for a representative IMRI ($M=10^4\,\modot$, $q=100$, observed for its final $10\,\hr$), we compute fitting factors between eccentric signals and circular templates, maximizing over the template's total mass and mass ratio with a differential-evolution search, for the B-DECIGO, LGWA-Si, and IndIGO-D noise curves and a flat PSD. The mismatch grows with initial eccentricity $e_0$ and crosses the standard $3\%$ threshold at $e_0\simeq3\times10^{-2}$, well within the range allowed by the waveform model and expected from dynamical formation. The best-fitting circular templates also bias the total mass and mass ratio by several percent at $e_0=0.1$. Combining the mismatch with the signal-to-noise-dependent distinguishability criterion, we find that for a source at $1\,\mathrm{Gpc}$, eccentricities as small as $e_0\simeq5\times10^{-4}$ (B-DECIGO and IndIGO-D) and $5\times10^{-3}$ (LGWA-Si) are resolvable, one to two orders of magnitude below where detection efficiency degrades. The loss of match arises from the excitation of radial Teukolsky harmonics, which spread power into sidebands that circular templates cannot represent, and from the slower orbital decay of eccentric binaries at fixed initial semi-latus rectum. Robust detection and parameter estimation of IMRIs with DECIGO, LGWA, and IndIGO-D will require eccentric waveform models and template banks.

</details>


### [232] [Critical curves and throat degeneracy in black bounces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21355)
*Francisco S. N. Lobo,Diogo Mesquita,Luís F. Dias da Silva*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: No AI summary available.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: No AI summary available.

Method: No AI summary available.

Result: No AI summary available.

Conclusion: No AI summary available.

Abstract: Identical critical curves need not imply identical null-orbit dynamics. For a class of static spherical areal-radius deformations, we show that a surviving original photon sphere retains its critical impact parameter and orbital frequency, while its coordinate-time Lyapunov exponent is multiplied by the radial Jacobian. The logarithmic strong-deflection coefficient acquires the inverse factor. At a reflection-symmetric throat, stability factorizes into a derivative of the original optical potential and the throat curvature. More generally, an order-$m$ extremum of the original potential composed with an order-$q$ areal-radius minimum produces an optical degeneracy of order $p=mq$, with critical approach $|x|\propto|t|^{-2/(p-2)}$ and reflected strong deflection $Δφ\propto(b/b_{\rm c}-1)^{-(p-2)/(2p)}$ for $p>2$. For the Simpson--Visser family with mass $M$ and bounce parameter $a$, the critical impact parameter remains Schwarzschild-like for $a<3M$, although the instability varies with $a$. The merger at $a=3M$ is quartic, separating stable and unstable throat branches. These results isolate the null-orbit information preserved by a radial deformation from that encoded in its local geometry.

</details>


### [233] [The Stability of the Higgs vacuum inside a Schwarzschild Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21356)
*Paul G. Abel*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在史瓦西黑洞内部，希格斯的径向演化由时间性的径向坐标控制；包含普朗克压低的曲率耦合后，近奇点场方程化为贝塞尔方程，结果显示场在电弱对称破缺相中保持冻结，直到曲率进入普朗克尺度才偏离真空值，且该行为与黑洞质量无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有静态分析可能误判黑洞内部希格斯场的相态，本文考察径向坐标为时间性时希格斯凝聚体的真实演化，并纳入普朗克压低（Planck-suppressed）的希格斯场—Kretschmann标量耦合。

Method: 在史瓦西（Schwarzschild）黑洞内部求解希格斯场方程，引入与Ricci平坦几何中主导曲率不变量Kretschmann标量的耦合；近奇点处方程化简为贝塞尔方程（Bessel equation），解为 H = vJ₀(x)，其中 x ∝ √ξ·t_p/τ，τ为到奇点的固有时（proper time）；并进一步加入希格斯真空涨落的一圈（one-loop）修正所给出的含源项并精确求解。

Result: 与静态分析相反，场在势的形状改变时仍冻结于破缺相，仅在曲率变为普朗克量级时偏离真空值，且不依赖黑洞质量（black-hole mass）。含一圈源项后凝聚体最多约减少百分之十。

Conclusion: 在有效场论（effective field theory）适用范围内，电弱对称（electroweak symmetry）在黑洞整个内部始终保持破缺。

Abstract: I investigate the behaviour of the Higgs field inside a Schwarzschild black hole, including a Planck-suppressed coupling between the Higgs field and the Kretschmann scalar, the leading curvature invariant in a Ricci-flat geometry. Because the radial coordinate is timelike in the interior, the Higgs condensate evolves in time rather than tracking the minimum of its potential. Near the singularity the interior field equation reduces to Bessel's equation, with solution $H=vJ_0(x)$, where $x\propto\sqrtξ\,t_p/τ$ and $τ$ is the proper time to the singularity. Contrary to static analyses, the field remains frozen in its broken phase while the potential changes shape, departing from its vacuum value only when the curvature becomes Planckian, independently of the black-hole mass. Including the one-loop correction from Higgs vacuum fluctuations yields the same Bessel equation with a source term, which I solve exactly; the condensate is reduced by at most about ten per cent. The electroweak symmetry therefore remains broken throughout the interior within the regime of validity of effective field theory.

</details>


### [234] [Geometry of squeezed states. The phase-space metric, relative vacua and cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21426)
*Alejandro Corichi,Jerónimo Cortez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 论文主张：在经典相空间上，由选择真空所确定的相容度规（metric）$g$ 才是核心对象，它在相空间上扮演量子度规在态空间上的角色；压缩态（squeezed states）由此可解释为一对度规的相对真空，其不变量为 $L=g_0^{-1}g$ 的特征值 $e^{\pm2r_i}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何以两种方式进入量子理论：态空间上的辛形式（symplectic form）生成酉演化，度规则支配不确定性与跃迁概率；而经典相空间在选择真空后亦获得自身度规。作者希望论证后者（相空间度规）应被视为基本对象，并借此给出压缩与场论真空选择的统一几何语言。

Method: 将线性系统的量子化（quantization）视为选择相容复结构（compatible complex structure），等价于选择相容度规 $g$；证明线性可观测量（linear observables）的涨落是哈密顿向量场（Hamiltonian vector fields）的 $g$-范数，且沿相干态（coherent states）有 $g/2\hbar$ 即量子度规。以 $L=g_0^{-1}g$ 的特征值刻画压缩，并对简谐振子（harmonic oscillator）作详细计算，进一步推广到场的情形。

Result: 谐振子分析解释了为何旋转后的压缩态不再最小化其展示的不确定性乘积；二次演化（quadratic evolution）会形变度规，且仅生成元中关于参考度规自伴（self-adjoint）的部分以精确给出的速率改变 $r_i$。场的层面：真空相对参考真空可酉实现（unitarily implementable）当且仅当二者在真空空间中距离有限。对 de Sitter 空间无质量场，相对标准压缩形式参考得 Bunch–Davies 态 $\sinh r_k=1/(2|k\eta|)$；相对 $η$ 时刻开始的辐射期所选真空则为 $\sinh r_k=1/(2k^2\eta^2)$。

Conclusion: 第二种比较（对辐射期真空）压缩速率加倍且可酉实现，第一种（对标准压缩形式参考）则不可酉实现。相空间度规视角由此把压缩态、二次演化与弯曲时空真空选择统一为度规与其相对比较的几何问题。

Abstract: Geometry enters quantum theory in two places. The space of quantum states carries a symplectic form, which generates unitary evolution, and a metric, which governs uncertainties and transition probabilities. The classical phase space acquires a metric of its own when a vacuum is chosen: quantizing a linear system amounts to choosing a compatible complex structure, equivalently a compatible metric $g$. We take this second metric as the primary object and show that it plays, on phase space, the role that the quantum metric plays on the space of states: fluctuations of linear observables are $g$-norms of Hamiltonian vector fields, and along coherent states $g/2\hbar$ \emph{is} the quantum metric. Squeezed states then have a simple description. A squeezed vacuum is the vacuum of another metric, and squeezing is a property of a pair of metrics, a relative vacuum, whose invariants are the eigenvalues $e^{\pm2r_i}$ of $L=g_0^{-1}g$. We work out the harmonic oscillator in detail, including why a rotated squeezed state no longer minimizes the product of the displayed uncertainties. Quadratic evolution deforms the metric, and only the part of the generator that is self-adjoint with respect to the reference changes the $r_i$, at a rate we give exactly. For fields, a vacuum is unitarily implementable relative to a reference exactly when it lies at finite distance from it in the space of vacua. For a massless field in de Sitter space we revisit the two canonical charts compared by Grain and Vennin: relative to the reference of the standard squeezing formalism the Bunch--Davies state has $\sinh r_k=1/(2|kη|)$, and relative to the vacuum that a radiation era beginning at time $η$ would select it has $\sinh r_k=1/(2k^2η^2)$. The second comparison squeezes twice as fast and is unitarily implementable; the first is not.

</details>


### [235] [Pulsar timing response and observational constraints on continuous-spin gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21512)
*Jing-Hong Han,Zhi-Chao Zhao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文从线性化连续自旋引力（continuous-spin gravity）的规范不变世界线耦合出发，推导脉冲星计时红移响应与重叠约化函数，并利用NANOGrav 15年数据给出连续自旋标度ρ_g的上限约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续自旋粒子（continuous-spin particles）是庞加莱群最一般的无质量整数螺旋度不可约表示，其效应由α≡ρ_g/(ħω)控制；纳赫兹脉冲星计时阵列（pulsar timing arrays）对超低ρ_g天然敏感，因而可用于检验此类表示。

Method: 从线性化连续自旋引力的规范不变世界线耦合（worldline coupling）出发，推导任意整数螺旋度下单向脉冲星计时红移响应及各向同性随机背景的重叠约化函数（overlap reduction function），并对NANOGrav 15年数据与脉冲星红噪声联合拟合。

Result: 光子传播受所有螺旋度调制，但仅矢量模（h=±1）扰动静态观测者的四维速度；张量模（h=±2）在ρ_g/(ħω)→0时平滑恢复广义相对论的Hellings–Downs相关。对非极化h=±2背景，得到95%上限ρ_g^U<7.03×10^-23 eV（均匀先验）与ρ_g^{log U}<4.79×10^-23 eV（对数均匀先验）；均匀先验下γ_GW=4.89^{+0.85}_{-0.96}。

Conclusion: 连续自旋引力效应对纳赫兹引力波观测给出可检验的唯象预言；所得谱指数较广义相对论更接近圆轨道超大质量黑洞双星（SMBHBs）预期的标准值γ_GW=13/3。

Abstract: Continuous-spin particles form the most general massless integer-helicity irreducible representations of the Poincaré group, characterized by a dimensionful continuous-spin scale $ρ_g$ set by a nonzero Pauli--Lubanski invariant. A single continuous-spin representation contains an infinite tower of integer helicities $h\in\mathbb Z$ that mix under Lorentz boosts. Since continuous-spin effects are controlled by $α\equivρ_g/(\hbarω)$ at gravitational-wave angular frequency $ω$, nanohertz pulsar timing arrays are naturally sensitive to ultralow values of $ρ_g$. Starting from the gauge-invariant worldline coupling of linearized continuous-spin gravity, we derive the one-way pulsar timing redshift response and the overlap reduction function of an isotropic stochastic background for arbitrary integer helicity. We show that while photon propagation is modulated across all helicities, only the vector modes ($h=\pm1$) perturb the four-velocity of a static observer, and that the tensor modes ($h=\pm2$) smoothly recover the Hellings--Downs correlation of general relativity as $ρ_g/(\hbarω)\to 0$.
