Modeling an unpolarized $h=\pm2$ background in the solar-system barycentric frame and jointly fitting the NANOGrav 15-year data with pulsar red noise, we obtain the $95\%$ upper limits $ρ^{U}_g<7.03\times10^{-23}\,\mathrm{eV}$ and $ρ^{\log {U}}_g<4.79\times10^{-23}\,\mathrm{eV}$ under uniform and log-uniform priors, respectively. Under the uniform prior, we obtain $γ_{\mathrm{GW}} = 4.89^{+0.85}_{-0.96}$, which brings the spectral index closer to the canonical value $γ_{\rm GW}=13/3$ expected for circular SMBHBs than standard General Relativity.

</details>


### [236] [On the Asymptotic Expansion of Massive Fields in Spherically Symmetric Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21557)
*Robin Fynn Diedrichs,Iris van Gemeren*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 大质量实标量场（massive real scalar field）在中心源作用下并不普遍遵循 Yukawa 衰减；渐近大半径处提取的系数既不坐标不变，也不等于点粒子作用量（point-particle action）中的标量荷（scalar charge）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常假设大质量标量场在径向下无穷远按 Yukawa 形式衰减，且大半径渐近系数被直接当作中心天体的标量荷；本文旨在检验这一假设的普适性与标量荷定义的坐标不变性。

Method: 采用微扰方法构造匹配渐近展开（matched asymptotic expansions），并结合数值方法考察非微扰结构（non-perturbative structure）；分析限于最小耦合（minimally coupled）标量场。

Result: 结果表明，即便对最小耦合场，Yukawa 衰减一般也不成立；大质量情形下渐近大半径提取的系数既非坐标不变（coordinate invariant），也不等同于点粒子作用量中出现的标量荷。

Conclusion: 大质量标量场的渐近行为及其标量荷定义需重新审视；点粒子作用量给出坐标不变的标量荷定义，而渐近系数不宜直接等同于标量荷，匹配渐近展开为相关微扰计算提供了处理框架。

Abstract: The behavior of massive real scalar fields, when sourced by a central object, is usually assumed to follow a Yukawa falloff towards radial infinity. In this paper, we show that this is generically not the case, even for minimally coupled fields. Further, while the point-particle action provides a coordinate-invariant definition of the central object's scalar charge, we show that the coefficient one usually extracts at asymptotically large radii in the case of massive scalar fields is neither coordinate invariant nor identical to the scalar charge appearing in the point-particle action. We resolve this issue perturbatively by constructing matched asymptotic expansions and investigate the non-perturbative structure using numerical methods.

</details>


### [237] [Separation of gravitational perturbations of Myers-Perry black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21591)
*Jun-Kun Zhao,Hong-Da Lyu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文构造了五维渐近平直、双向自旋不等的Myers–Perry黑洞在线性化引力扰动下的可分离解，证明Killing–Yano对称诱导的一阶角向算符可确定全部五个规范不变极化的角向剖面，并验证角谐波完备性与局部度规重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 五维双向自旋旋转黑洞的线性化引力扰动缺乏系统的可分离框架，而这是研究准正规模与引力稳定性的前提。

Method: 对两个自旋不等的五维渐近平直Myers–Perry黑洞的线性化引力扰动，针对五个耦合的规范不变极化（gauge-invariant polarizations）在任意方位角模式数下构造分离变量解；利用Killing–Yano对称（Killing–Yano symmetry）诱导的一阶角向算符与对易的二阶算符确定角向剖面，并用Carter互换关系（Carter interchange）联系标量角向与径向方程。

Result: 每个分离解对应的五个潮汐分量共享单一径向振幅；在实频率下证明了角谐波的完备性，并表明所得物理扰动可进行局部度规重构（local metric reconstruction）。在单自旋极限下，恢复了Santos针对m_ψ=0且ψ→-ψ偶宇称扰动的角向算符与径向方程。

Conclusion: 结果为研究具两个独立自旋的五维旋转黑洞的准正规模（quasinormal modes）与引力稳定性提供了系统框架。

Abstract: We study linearized gravitational perturbations of five-dimensional asymptotically flat Myers--Perry black holes with two unequal spins. We construct separated solutions for the five coupled gauge-invariant polarizations for arbitrary azimuthal mode numbers. The key observation is that the Killing--Yano symmetry induces a first-order angular operator that, together with a commuting second-order operator, determines the angular profiles of all five gravitational polarizations. The Carter interchange relates the scalar angular and radial equations, and the five tidal components of each separated solution share a single radial amplitude. We prove completeness of the angular harmonics at real frequencies and show that the resulting physical perturbations admit local metric reconstruction. In the singly spinning limit, we recover Santos's angular operators and radial equation for perturbations with $m_ψ=0$ and even parity under $ψ\to-ψ$. These results provide a framework for studying quasinormal modes and gravitational stability of five-dimensional rotating black holes with two independent spins.

</details>


### [238] [A separation criterion for chiral systems and its application to Plebański--Demiański perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21603)
*Hideo Furugori,Daisuke Yoshida*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文给出耦合两个复二重态（complex doublet）的一阶手征系统（first-order chiral system）局部可分离的构造性判据，并结合 Gram 分解（Gram decomposition）与微分输运检验（differential transport test），应用于旋转带电 Plebański–Demiański 背景下爱因斯坦–麦克斯韦扰动（Einstein–Maxwell perturbations）的径向与角向分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在为耦合复二重态的一阶手征系统建立局部可分离性（local separation）的判别工具，并将该工具用于从含加速度、NUT 荷（NUT charge）与宇宙学常数的一般 Plebański–Demiański 度规中导出可分离的扰动方程。

Method: 方法将耦合源（coupling sources）的 Gram 分解与坐标依赖性的分离处理，以及一个微分输运检验相结合；在模无关的、行列式为 1 的复矩阵变换（mode-independent complex matrix transformations with unit determinant）类中论证判据的充分必要性，并分析源唯一确定厄米系数矩阵（Hermitian coefficient matrix）所需条件。

Result: 对可恢复（recoverable）非退化源族，判据在该变换类内为充分必要条件；对固定的微分算子与容许的 Gram 分解，给出了源唯一确定厄米系数矩阵的条件。应用上构造了显式代数变换，导出了旋转带电 Plebański–Demiański 背景（含加速度、NUT 荷与宇宙学常数）耦合辐射扰动的分离径向与角向方程，并证明在附加一般性假设下 Gram 分解唯一。

Conclusion: 论文进一步给出 Bonnor 与 Misner 认同（Bonnor and Misner identifications）含锥形亏损或过剩（conical deficits or excesses）时的角向端点指标（angular endpoint indices）；在所述正则性假设下，固定实模参数（real mode parameters）的每个指定自伴角向实现（self-adjoint angular realization）均具有离散谱与完备正交归一特征基（complete orthonormal eigenbasis）。

Abstract: We give a constructive criterion for local separation of first-order chiral systems coupling two complex doublets. It combines a Gram decomposition of the coupling sources with separate coordinate dependence and a differential transport test. For recoverable, nondegenerate source families, the criterion is necessary and sufficient within the specified class of mode-independent complex matrix transformations with unit determinant. For a fixed differential operator and admissible Gram decomposition, we identify conditions under which the sources uniquely determine the Hermitian coefficient matrix required for separation. As an application, we construct an explicit algebraic transformation and derive separated radial and angular equations for coupled radiative Einstein--Maxwell perturbations of rotating, charged Plebański--Demiański backgrounds with acceleration, NUT charge, and a cosmological constant, on specified local patches. Its Gram decomposition is unique under additional generic assumptions. We also derive angular endpoint indices for Bonnor and Misner identifications with conical deficits or excesses. Under the stated regularity assumptions, each specified self-adjoint angular realization at fixed real mode parameters has a discrete spectrum and a complete orthonormal eigenbasis.

</details>


### [239] [Self-interacting $p$-forms and the shear-free anisotropic cosmological model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07288)
*Nicolas Hernandez,Ben D. Normann,Yeinzon Rodriguez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文将扩展的FLRW（eFLRW）宇宙学分析从自由微分p-形式推广到带自相互作用的非阿贝尔（non-Abelian）p-形式（p=0,…,4），考察各向异性空间曲率与各向异性应力（anisotropic stress）的平衡条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙学原理（cosmological principle）的实际要求可能低于观测所要求的内容：真正需要的只是无涡旋（vorticity）的各向同性膨胀，而eFLRW框架可以借助非零各向异性空间曲率并由各向异性应力平衡来实现这一点；此前研究仅针对自由微分p-形式，需推广至非阿贝尔情形。

Method: 要求各向异性应力按a^{-2}衰减（a为宇宙尺度因子），并与规范场连续性方程（gauge-field continuity equation）联立；对含势（potential）的非阿贝尔p-形式（p=0,…,4）逐一分析平衡条件，重点考察Bianchi A类（Bianchi class A）中的3-形式场。

Result: 一般结果表明，存在势时通常需要对势进行精细调节（fine-tuning）才能平衡曲率；例外是带势V(H²)=A+B·H²的3-形式场：在Bianchi A类中，无荷（uncharged）3-形式无需任何调节即可平衡曲率，带荷（charged）情形则在适当选取联络（connection）时成立。

Conclusion: 该3-形式情形是所发现的唯一无需调节势的候选，且可解释为宇宙学常数（cosmological constant）与一个模仿曲率的暗物质（dark-matter）扇区的组合，因而具有进一步研究价值。

Abstract: The cosmological principle may require more than observations demand: what is needed is isotropic expansion without vorticity. Extended Friedmann-Lemaitre-Robertson-Walker (eFLRW) cosmologies realise this with a non-vanishing anisotropic spatial curvature that is balanced by an anisotropic stress while the shear vanishes. Thorsrud and others investigated such scenarios for free differential $p$-forms. Here we extend the analysis to non-Abelian $p$-forms ($p=0,\dots,4$) with self interactions. The balance requires the anisotropic stress to decay as $a^{-2}$, $a$ being the cosmological scale factor, which we combine with the gauge-field continuity equation. The general results indicate that, in the presence of a potential, fine-tuning of the same is needed in order to balance the curvature. Notably, this is not the case for a $3$-form field $\underline{H}^a$ with a potential term $V(\underline{H}^2)=A+B\,\underline{H}^2$, where $A$ and $B$ are constants: in Bianchi class A, an uncharged 3-form balances the curvature with this potential without any tuning, and a charged one does so for a suitably chosen connection. This is the only candidate we have found where the potential does not require tuning. It is also an interesting candidate as it can be interpreted as a cosmological constant in combination with a dark-matter sector mimicking curvature.

</details>


<div id='physics.class-ph'></div>

# physics.class-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [240] [A Beginner's Guide to Physical Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18758)
*Aaron Winn,Eleni Katifori*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 该教程以物理网络（physical networks）为对象，将图视为胞腔复形（cell complex）的特例，借助关联矩阵（incidence matrix）统一分析电路、流体与力学网络，并辅以Python实现与习题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络理论中的节点与边通常较为抽象，而物理网络嵌入空间并承载电荷、流体体积或动量等物理量，需要一套兼顾拓扑结构与守恒原理的分析框架，以服务将图论应用于物理网络的学生、教师与研究者。

Method: 以图论（graph theory）描述底层结构，突出关联矩阵的核心作用；将图视为胞腔复形（cell complex）以揭示其拓扑结构及与微积分算子的关系；基于节点流量守恒与势梯度驱动原则，依次讨论电路理论（circuit theory）、流体流动网络与力学网络，并简要涉及随机游走者（random walkers）与非线性网络（nonlinear networks）。

Result: 教程给出统一的分析路径：满足守恒与势梯度原则的模型与电路行为类似，从而可将电路理论推广至流体与力学网络；全文配有解析示例、Python实现（替代伪代码）及脚注形式的习题。

Conclusion: 该教程源自2019年Boulder凝聚态学校短期课程与2024年宾夕法尼亚大学课程，适用于将图论应用于物理网络的读者；仅需线性代数（linear algebra）基础，不要求图论或物理学背景。

Abstract: Network theory studies entities (nodes) and the relationships between them (edges). While these entities and their relationships may be abstract in general, this tutorial focuses on the special case of physical networks: spatially embedded networks that carry physical quantities such as electric charge, fluid volume, or momentum. The underlying structure is described mathematically by a graph (Section I), with the incidence matrix playing a central role in the analysis. Viewing a graph as a special case of a cell complex reveals the topological structure of the incidence matrix and its relation to familiar operators from calculus (Section II). Because these networks are physical, flows are conserved at each node and often derive from the gradient of a potential. Models that satisfy these principles behave analogously to circuits; accordingly, we first review circuit theory (Section III), then examine related fluid flow (Section IV) and mechanical (Section V) networks. Briefly, the topics of random walkers (Section VI) and nonlinear networks (Section VII) will be discussed, followed by concluding remarks (Section VIII). Analytical examples and Python implementations, in lieu of pseudocode, are included throughout. Exercises are included as footnotes. This tutorial is based on a short course taught at the Boulder School for Condensed Matter in 2019 and a course taught at the University of Pennsylvania in Spring 2024. It should be a useful resource for students, teachers, and researchers applying graph theory to physical networks. Knowledge of linear algebra is assumed, but no prior knowledge of graph theory or physics is necessary.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [241] [Flexible ground-state search algorithm on hybrid Clifford-tensor network manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18848)
*Hari Timsina,Antonio Francesco Mello,Meghadeepa Adhikary,Marcello Dalmonte,Mario Collura*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作将稳定子形式（stabilizer formalism）直接嵌入密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）算法，通过优化Clifford变换以最小化体系能量，在固定键维（bond dimension）下相较标准DMRG取得系统性增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量网络算法在周期边界条件（PBC）、长程相互作用或高维体系等高纠缠情形下，所需键维迅速增长导致计算不可行；此前利用Clifford酉变换的方法仅通过局域两比特操作蒸馏纠缠，作用受限。

Method: 提出将稳定子形式（stabilizer formalism）整合进传统DMRG的新框架，不再以最小化纠缠为目标，而是优化Clifford变换以最小化态的能量；在环上（含长程相互作用）与环面（torus）上的Ising模型上，以固定键维与标准DMRG进行基准对比。

Result: 在局域相互作用模型中观察到系统性增益，二维情形下键维可降低达40倍。

Conclusion: 该算法可直接在标准DMRG代码中实现，提供显著降低算法成本的实用途径，为超越当前可及规模的大尺度模拟奠定基础。

Abstract: Tensor network algorithms face challenges in highly entangled regimes, such as systems with periodic boundary conditions (PBC), long-range interactions, or higher dimensions, where the required bond dimension quickly renders computations infeasible. To overcome this limitation, we introduce a novel approach that integrates the stabilizer formalism directly into the traditional density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) algorithm. While previous methods have used Clifford unitaries to minimize ground-state entanglement, fundamentally limited to distilling that away utilizing local two-qubit operations, our framework optimizes Clifford transformations to minimize the state's energy. We benchmark our method against standard DMRG at fixed bond dimensions for the Ising model on a ring (including long-range interactions) and on a torus. We observe systematic gains in models with local interactions - as drastic as a factor of 40 decrease in bond dimension in two dimensions. Our algorithm is readily implementable in standard DMRG codes and provides a practical way to substantially reduce algorithm costs, paving the way for large-scale simulation at volumes beyond what is presently achievable.

</details>


### [242] [Coupling-Response Thermodynamic Density of States for Bosonic Working Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18873)
*Francisco J. Peña,Bastian Castorene,Nicolas Vidal-Silva*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文引入“操作型热力学态密度（thermodynamic density of states, TDOS）”，用以刻画微观能谱可控的玻色工作介质（bosonic working media）；在固定化学势下导出精确标度律，并揭示固定粒子数约束下存在化学势重分布通道，其跨玻色–爱因斯坦凝聚（Bose–Einstein condensation, BEC）阈行为由空间维度决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在微观能谱可调控的平台上描述玻色工作介质的热力学响应，需要一个新的操作性量来连接能谱结构与热力学可观测量，从而服务量子热机（如斯特林循环，Stirling cycle）的可逆功（reversible work）评估与实验观测量的识别。

Method: 定义 TDOS 为 𝒢_λ=(∂S/∂λ)_{T,μ}，即固定温度与化学势下熵（entropy）对谱控制参数 λ 的响应；对均匀能谱 ε(k)=Δ+λk^α 解析推导标度律，并对二次能带（quadratic bands）与谐振阱（harmonic traps）给出精确解，同时讨论固定粒子数约束下的化学势重分布。

Result: 固定化学势下响应严格由结构态密度（structural density of states）驱动，得到精确标度律 𝒢_λ=-(d/α)S/λ；耦合依赖的能隙仅在有限边界权重下产生边缘项（edge terms）。固定粒子数下精确解表明，空间维度决定约束 TDOS 在 BEC 阈值处表现为跃变间断或保持连续；纯粹标度控制下响应精确映射为热容，λ𝒢_λ^{(N)}=-C_N。

Conclusion: 该框架可用于评估斯特林循环中的可逆功，并在磁子平台（magnonic platforms）中识别可观测量的对应关系，同时对比蜂窝铁磁体（honeycomb ferromagnets）中的全局谱边缘与拓扑狄拉克能隙（topological Dirac gaps）。

Abstract: We introduce an operational thermodynamic density of states (TDOS), $\mathcal{G}_λ=(\partial S/\partialλ)_{T,μ}$, to characterize bosonic working media under microscopic spectral control. At fixed chemical potential, the response is strictly driven by the structural density of states, yielding the exact scaling law $\mathcal{G}_λ=-(d/α)S/λ$ for homogeneous spectra $ε(k)=Δ+λk^α$, while coupling-dependent gaps generate edge terms only for finite boundary weight. Under a fixed-particle constraint, a secondary chemical-potential redistribution channel emerges. Exact solutions for quadratic bands and harmonic traps demonstrate that spatial dimensionality dictates whether this constrained TDOS exhibits a jump discontinuity or remains continuous across the Bose--Einstein condensation threshold. For pure scale control, the response maps exactly to the heat capacity, $λ\mathcal{G}_λ^{(N)}=-C_N$. This framework is applied to evaluate reversible work in Stirling cycles and to identify observables in magnonic platforms, contrasting global spectral edges with topological Dirac gaps in honeycomb ferromagnets.

</details>


### [243] [Beyond global density in a stochastic sandpile: the role of spatial signatures in the predictability of extremes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19014)
*Kamil P. Orzechowski,Julian Sienkiewicz,Piotr Fronczak*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在二维Manna沙堆（Manna sandpile）中，晶粒密度（grain density）虽能强预测极端雪崩（AUC≈0.96），但相同晶粒数的微状态并非等价：载荷在中心与耗散边界（dissipative boundaries）间的分布方式额外决定系统对极端雪崩的准备程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自组织临界（self-organized criticality）系统中全局载荷（global load）是极端事件的强前兆，密度预测力极强导致难以判断密度是否已完全决定系统准备状态；且能观察完整微状态的方法可能仅靠计数晶粒就获得高分，需排除这一混淆。

Method: 仅在晶粒数完全相同的状态之间进行比较，以排除密度信息；使用无参数统计量（parameter-free statistic）与卷积神经网络（convolutional network）区分极端雪崩前状态与典型状态，并设置打乱对照（shuffled controls）检验结果是否源于真实结构。

Result: 即使控制晶粒数，两种方法均能区分极端雪崩前状态与典型状态，而打乱对照不能。信号为中心与耗散边界之间的载荷对比（load contrast），由最低的两个对称余弦模式（symmetric cosine modes）承载；其归一化能量为单一免训练变量，与密度结合后可恢复全微状态网络相对单用密度所获增益的大部分。

Conclusion: 极端雪崩的准备程度既取决于总载荷，也取决于该载荷相对边界的分布方式；密度并非充分决定因素，微状态结构携带额外预测信息。

Abstract: In self-organized critical systems the global load is a strong precursor of extreme events. In the two-dimensional Manna sandpile, grain density alone predicts extreme avalanches with an AUC of about 0.96. We ask whether density fully determines the system's readiness for an extreme event, that is, whether microstates with the same number of grains are equivalent. Because density is such a strong predictor, a method that sees the full microstate can score well just by counting grains. We therefore compare only states with an identical number of grains. Even then, both a parameter-free statistic and a convolutional network distinguish states that precede an extreme avalanche from typical ones, while shuffled controls do not. The signal is a load contrast between the center and the dissipative boundaries, carried by the two lowest symmetric cosine modes. Their normalized energy is a single variable that needs no training. Together with density it recovers most of the gain that a network trained on the full microstate achieves over density alone. Readiness for an extreme avalanche therefore depends both on the total load and on how that load is distributed relative to the boundaries.

</details>


### [244] [Higher-order statistical structure in perfume item combinations beyond a pairwise maximum-entropy model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19161)
*Yohei Shida,Jun'ichi Ozaki*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究在香水配方数据的二元最大熵（pairwise maximum-entropy）模型残差中发现，高阶偏离围绕特定共享物品对（shared item pairs）组织；引入三体耦合（three-body couplings）并重新优化参数后，整体三元组残差下降5.44%，且线性响应（linear-response）计算能再现预测共现频率的变化（Pearson相关系数0.850）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在检验二元最大熵模型对香水物品组合的描述是否充分，并刻画其偏离（高阶统计依赖）是否具有结构性组织，而非随机分布。

Method: 对1,979款香水、50个二值变量（binary variables）建模；以合成数据（synthetic datasets）的同流程重拟合作为偏离基准；对50个选定三元组（triplets）引入三体耦合并重优化全部模型参数；辅以考虑低阶参数重调整的静态线性响应计算。

Result: 二元模型可近似再现每款香水所列物品数量的分布，但观测三元组边际分布（three-item marginal distributions）的偏离超出合成数据基准；强偏离三元组集中于共享物品对，且无法仅由单品参与频率解释。加入三体耦合后，其余19,550个三元组的标准化残差平方和（sum of squared standardized residuals）下降5.44%，与选定三元组共享物品对者降幅更大；线性响应预测与实测共现频率变化的Pearson相关系数为0.850。

Conclusion: 结果表明高阶统计依赖（higher-order statistical dependencies）具有围绕共享物品对的组织结构，并刻画了少量选定三体耦合如何影响全系统预测。

Abstract: We find that deviations from a pairwise maximum-entropy model in perfume item combinations are organized around particular shared item pairs. We analyze 1,979 perfumes using 50 binary variables representing the most frequently listed items. Although the pairwise model approximately reproduces the distribution of the number of listed items per perfume, the observed three-item marginal distributions exhibit deviations exceeding those obtained from synthetic datasets subjected to the same model-refitting procedure. Strongly deviating triplets are concentrated around shared item pairs, and this concentration cannot be explained by the participation frequencies of individual items alone. We then introduce three-body couplings for 50 selected triplets and reoptimize all model parameters. The sum of squared standardized residuals over the remaining 19,550 triplets decreases by 5.44\%, with substantially larger reductions for triplets sharing an item pair with selected triplets. Furthermore, a static linear-response calculation accounting for the readjustment of lower-order parameters approximately reproduces the resulting changes in predicted co-occurrence frequencies, with a Pearson correlation coefficient of 0.850. These results reveal the organization of higher-order statistical dependencies and characterize how selected three-body couplings affect predictions throughout the system.

</details>


### [245] [When the Boundary Changes the Bulk: Topological Edge Modes in Dynamical Quantum Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19328)
*N. Sedlmayr,J. Sirker*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 开放链中由 Loschmidt 矩阵（Loschmidt matrix）边缘模式引起的边界效应，其保护机制并非初末哈密顿量的拓扑，而是非厄米 Loschmidt 矩阵自身的拓扑；该结论在 A、BDI、AIII 类由 Toeplitz 算符的 Z 指数保护，在 D 类由 Z₂ 指数保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究注意到开放链中边界可改变体行为：在动力学量子相变（dynamical quantum phase transitions, DQPTs）界定的时间窗口内，Loschmidt 矩阵的指数局域边缘模式会使强度型回返率（intensive return rate）产生有限改变，且这些模式此前被归因于初末哈密顿量的拓扑性质。本文旨在修正这一归因。

Method: 作者论证保护边缘模式的是非厄米 Loschmidt 矩阵的拓扑而非哈密顿量的拓扑，并借助 Toeplitz 算符的 Z 指数与 K-分裂定理（K-splitting theorems）建立体-边对应（bulk-boundary correspondence）；对 D 类则引入记录淬火是否翻转边缘费米宇称（fermion parity）的 Z₂ 指数，并用周期/反周期边界条件下 Loschmidt 振幅之比或动量-时间平面内的绕数（winding number）表述体不变量。

Result: 拓扑边缘模式可在 A 类中存在，即使初末两个哈密顿量均为拓扑平庸；A、BDI、AIII 类模式由 Toeplitz 算符的 Z 指数保护，K-分裂定理给出精确体-边对应；D 类模式由 Z₂ 指数保护，其体不变量可写成 Loschmidt 振幅比或计动量与时间中相位涡旋（phase vortices）的绕数。文中对全部四个拓扑类给出了示例与数值数据。

Conclusion: 开放链中 Loschmidt 边缘模式的保护根源在于非厄米 Loschmidt 矩阵的拓扑分类，而非淬火前后哈密顿量的拓扑；不同对称类对应 Z 或 Z₂ 不变量，并均存在相应的体-边对应关系。

Abstract: For dynamical quantum phase transitions (DQPTs) in open chains it has been noted that the boundary can change the bulk: in time windows bounded by DQPTs, exponentially localized edge modes of the Loschmidt matrix produce a finite change in the intensive return rate. These modes have been attributed to topological properties of the initial and final Hamiltonians. Here we show that it is not the topology of the Hamiltonians but rather that of the non-Hermitian Loschmidt matrix which protects these edge modes. In particular, we show that topological edge modes can exist in class A, where both Hamiltonians are topologically trivial. For classes A, BDI, and AIII we show that these modes are protected by $\mathbb{Z}$ indices of a Toeplitz operator and that K-splitting theorems provide a precise bulk-boundary correspondence. In class D, they are protected instead by a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ index that records whether the quench has flipped the fermion parity at the edge. The bulk invariant for class D can be formulated either as a ratio of the Loschmidt amplitudes for periodic and anti-periodic boundary conditions or as a winding number that counts phase vortices in momentum and time. We discuss examples and provide numerical data for all four topological classes.

</details>


### [246] [Finite-size scaling of $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetry breaking decoupled from thermodynamic criticality in a complex-conjugate Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19386)
*Yihan Wang,Yuting Wang,Lihui Sun,Qionglin Dai,Haihong Li,Lufeng Zhang,Yuhai Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究复共轭耦合的二维各向异性伊辛模型（anisotropic Ising model），发现谱的 $\mathcal{PT}$ 对称性破缺边界 $K_{\rm PT}$ 处处低于热力学临界点 $K_c$，从而在二维非厄米（non-Hermitian）统计系统中确立了谱相变与热力学相变的解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在检验非厄米系统中谱的 $\mathcal{PT}$ 对称性破缺（$\mathcal{PT}$-symmetry breaking）是否必然伴随热力学临界性，此前这一问题缺乏二维统计模型层面的直接判据。

Method: 对精确各向异性伊辛临界条件（critical condition）作解析延拓（analytic continuation）得到候选热力学临界线，并用有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）验证其属于二维伊辛普适类；同时用转移矩阵（transfer matrix）精确对角化与有限尺寸外推确定谱的 $\mathcal{PT}$ 破缺边界，并计算空间关联与自由能振幅。

Result: 在研究的全部参数范围内均有 $K_{\rm PT}<K_c$，即存在谱为复数而系统仍处于热力学无序的中间区域；$K_{\rm PT}$ 处空间关联保持短程，而 $K_c$ 处呈现临界关联；计入复共轭各向异性对有限尺寸自由能振幅的修正后，可恢复伊辛中心荷（central charge）$c=1/2$。

Conclusion: 二维非厄米统计系统中谱的 $\mathcal{PT}$ 对称性破缺可以在没有热力学临界性的情况下发生，说明谱相变与热力学相变可以相互解耦。

Abstract: We study a two-dimensional anisotropic Ising model with complex-conjugate couplings. Analytic continuation of the exact anisotropic Ising critical condition yields a candidate thermodynamic critical line, which is independently supported by finite-size scaling confirming the two-dimensional Ising universality class. Exact diagonalization of the transfer matrix and finite-size extrapolation reveal that the spectrum becomes complex, defining a separate $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetry-breaking boundary in the thermodynamic limit. We find $K_{\rm PT}<K_c$ throughout the parameter range studied, leaving an intermediate regime with a complex spectrum while the system remains thermodynamically disordered. The spatial correlations remain short-ranged at $K_{\rm PT}$, in contrast to the critical correlations at $K_c$, directly distinguishing the spectral transition from thermodynamic criticality. The complex-conjugate anisotropy modifies the finite-size free-energy amplitude, and accounting for the corresponding anisotropy factor recovers the Ising central charge $c=1/2$. These results demonstrate that spectral $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetry breaking can occur without thermodynamic criticality, establishing a decoupling between spectral and thermodynamic transitions in a two-dimensional non-Hermitian statistical system.

</details>


### [247] [A Universal Algebraic Construction of Hilbert-Space Fragmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19470)
*Pramod Padmanabhan,Vladimir Korepin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过对任意有限维局域空间量子格点哈密顿量进行“提升（lifting）”以获得希尔伯特空间碎裂（Hilbert-space fragmentation）的机制，基于超对称（SUSY）与仲超对称（paraSUSY）生成元的代数表示及表示扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希尔伯特空间碎裂通常依赖特定模型或特殊构造，缺乏从任意量子格点哈密顿量出发的系统性生成机制，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 先用超对称与仲超对称生成元将哈密顿量写成代数形式，再扩大其表示定义提升程序，并分别研究格点式提升（site-wise lifting）与键式提升（bond-wise lifting）两种形式；通过考察零模密度、扇区结构与关联函数刻画碎裂性质。

Result: 格点式提升引入不可移动零模（zero modes），产生指数多个不变扇区，同时在完全活跃扇区保留原动力学，并在固定中间零模密度下对均匀提升确立强碎裂；最近邻链的关联函数显示受限传播与混合场情形下的持续局部记忆。键式提升产生可移动守恒弦、对翻转动力学（pair-flip dynamics）与涨落二聚体，并可推广至碎裂幺正电路。

Conclusion: 不同表示与嵌入方式实现不同的碎裂机制；该方法涵盖提升置换链与 Ising 自旋链、提升环面码（toric code）、\(XXC\) 自旋链族，以及由可积 \(H_{1,11}\)、\(H_{1,3}\) 种子密度导出的碎裂扩展，后两者源自 Hietarinta 的常数 \(4\times 4\) Yang–Baxter 解分类。

Abstract: We present a mechanism for obtaining Hilbert-space fragmentation from arbitrary quantum lattice Hamiltonians acting on finite-dimensional local spaces. We first express the Hamiltonian in an algebraic form using supersymmetry (SUSY) and parasupersymmetry (paraSUSY) generators. Enlarging their representations defines a lifting procedure, which we study in two forms, site-wise and bond-wise lifting. Site-wise lifting introduces immobile zero modes that generate exponentially many invariant sectors while retaining the original dynamics in the fully active sector. We determine the sector structure and establish strong fragmentation for uniform lifts at fixed intermediate zero-mode density. For nearest-neighbor chains, correlation functions reveal restricted propagation and, in the mixed-field example, persistent local memory. Bond-wise lifting produces mobile conserved strings, pair-flip dynamics and fluctuating dimers, and extends to fragmented unitary circuits. Different representations and embeddings realize different fragmentation mechanisms. Some of the fragmented models obtained by this method include lifted permutation and Ising spin chains, a lifted toric code, the \(XXC\) families of spin chains, and fragmented extensions derived from the integrable \(H_{1,11}\) and \(H_{1,3}\) seed densities. The last two models are derived from Hietarinta's classification of constant $4\times 4$ Yang-Baxter solutions.

</details>


### [248] [Bright-Dark State Interplay Governs Vibronic Decoherence in Molecular Aggregates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19483)
*Sreeja Loho Choudhury,Abhradeep Sarkar,Raja Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究构建了以光谱学（spectroscopy）为驱动的框架，将光学亮态振动电子本征态（optically bright vibronic eigenstates）与其后续退相干动力学（decoherence dynamics）联系起来，揭示纯度损失（purity loss）由三个相互关联因素共同支配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立光学光谱、振动电子能带结构（vibronic band structure）与态选择性纯度损失之间的可靠联系，弥补亮态光谱信息与退相干行为之间的关联缺口。

Method: 将多粒子Holstein形式（Multiparticle Holstein Formalism）与显式系统—环境相互作用（system–bath interactions）相结合，在激子分子聚集体（excitonic molecular aggregates）中分析弛豫诱导的纯度损失。

Result: 弛豫诱导的纯度损失受三因素支配：(i) 环境光谱窗口（bath spectral window）决定带内与带间弛豫通道的可及性；(ii) 亮态周围的暗振动电子态局域网络（local network of dark vibronic states）；(iii) 亮态的能量位置控制下坡弛豫（downhill relaxation）。局域弛豫网络相似时，不同激发能量的态呈现近乎相同的纯度动力学；H–J不对称性（H–J asymmetry）进一步揭示能量方向性的重要性。

Conclusion: 研究确立了光学光谱、振动电子能带结构与态选择性纯度损失之间的可靠联系，凸显局域弛豫网络与能量方向性在退相干中的核心作用。

Abstract: In this study, we establish a spectroscopy-driven framework that connects optically bright vibronic eigenstates to their subsequent decoherence dynamics in excitonic molecular aggregates. Combining the Multiparticle Holstein Formalism with explicit system--bath interactions, we demonstrate that relaxation-induced purity loss is governed by three interconnected factors: (i) the bath spectral window, which determines the accessibility of intra- and interband relaxation pathways; (ii) the local network of dark vibronic states surrounding the optically prepared bright state; and (iii) the energetic placement of the bright state, which controls downhill relaxation. Remarkably, states with different excitation energies exhibit nearly identical purity dynamics when their local relaxation networks are similar. The resulting H--J asymmetry further reveals the importance of energetic directionality. These findings establish a reliable connection between optical spectroscopy, vibronic band structure, and state-selective loss in purity.

</details>


### [249] [Geodesics and a Hubble Relation in Nonstationary Thermodynamic Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19524)
*Wanyan Chen,Yu-Han Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文提出非稳态热力学几何（nonstationary thermodynamic geometry），允许度规（metric）在控制参数固定时仍随时间演化，并由时间联络（temporal connection）推广测地线方程、重新分配最优控制路径上的耗散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有热力学几何（thermodynamic geometry）从平衡态延伸到非平衡态，但通常假定度规是稳态的（stationary metric），难以刻画度规本身随时间演化的非平衡系统。

Method: 构建非稳态热力学几何框架，引入随时间演化的度规与相应的时间联络（temporal connection），据此推广测地线方程（geodesic equation），并建立热力学哈勃关系（thermodynamic Hubble relation）以区分向平衡的弛豫与几何的膨胀或收缩。

Result: 布朗运动（Brownian）示例显示：外部调制的摩擦（externally modulated friction）可降低最小耗散；与有限热库（finite reservoir）的热交换可诱导几何收缩及 Mpemba 型弛豫（Mpemba-type relaxation）。

Conclusion: 该框架为理解复杂的演化非平衡系统（evolving nonequilibrium systems）提供了几何基础。

Abstract: Thermodynamic geometry offers a versatile lens from equilibrium to nonequilibrium, yet typically assumes a stationary metric. Here we study nonstationary thermodynamic geometry, where the metric evolves even at fixed control parameters. The resulting temporal connection generalizes the geodesic equation and redistributes dissipation along optimal control paths. We further establish a thermodynamic Hubble relation that distinguishes relaxation toward equilibrium from geometric expansion or contraction. Brownian examples demonstrate that externally modulated friction can reduce minimum dissipation, while heat exchange with a finite reservoir can induce geometric contraction and Mpemba-type relaxation. Our framework lays a geometric foundation for understanding complex evolving nonequilibrium systems.

</details>


### [250] [A Neural Density Functional for Spatial Structure and Orientational Ordering in Two-Dimensional Lattice Hard Rods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19553)
*Paul Bitzer,Jens Weimar,Martin Oettel*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作用两种卷积神经网络实现二维方格上水平/竖直硬棒（hard rods）的经典密度泛函理论（classical density functional theory, cDFT），比较直接学习 c⁽¹⁾↔ρ 的网络与直接学习 F_ex↔ρ 的 Φ-网络。两者密度剖面均优于 Lafuente–Cuesta 格点泛函，但自由能景观不同，导致长径比为 8 的棒在向列序（nematic ordering）转变上分别呈强一级与弱不连续。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察神经网络表示经典密度泛函时，不同学习目标（一阶直接关联函数 versus 自由能）对空间结构与取向有序（orientational ordering）预测的影响，并检验神经 cDFT 能否分辨微小的熵驱动自由能差异。

Method: 构建两种利用棒取向对称性的卷积架构：直接-c⁽¹⁾网络学习过剩自由能一阶泛函导数与密度之间的映射，Φ-网络直接学习过剩自由能与密度的映射；二者使用完全相同的非均匀巨正则蒙特卡洛（inhomogeneous grand-canonical Monte Carlo）密度剖面与单体直接关联（one-body direct correlation）目标进行训练。

Result: 两种神经表示的密度剖面预测均显著优于 Lafuente–Cuesta 格点泛函；但二者给出的自由能景观不同，直接-c⁽¹⁾网络沿不同密度路径积分得到不同自由能。长径比为 8 的棒向列转变在直接-c⁽¹⁾网络中为强一级，在 Φ-网络中仅为弱不连续，后者更接近模拟中的连续转变。

Conclusion: 结果表明神经 cDFT 能够捕捉细微的熵驱动自由能差异，且自由能景观及相变级次对网络学习目标的选择敏感；密度剖面的高精度并不保证自由能的一致性与路径无关性。

Abstract: We study spatial structure and orientational ordering of horizontal and vertical hard rods on a two-dimensional square lattice using neural classical density functional theory. We construct two convolutional architectures that exploit the symmetry relating the rod orientations: a direct-$c^{(1)}$ network (learning the map between the first functional derivative of the excess free energy and the inhomogeneous density, $c^{(1)} \leftrightarrow ρ$) and a $Φ$-network (learning directly the map between free energy and density, $F_\text{ex} \leftrightarrow ρ$). Both networks use exactly the same inhomogeneous grand-canonical Monte Carlo density profiles and one-body direct correlation targets. Both neural representations substantially improve the predicted density profiles relative to the Lafuente--Cuesta lattice functional. The resulting free energy landscapes, however, differ between the two representations. Furthermore for the direct-$c^{(1)}$ network the integration along different density paths produces different free energies. As a result, the transition to nematic ordering for rods with aspect ratio 8 is strongly first order for the direct-$c^{(1)}$ network while only weakly discontinuous for the $Φ$-network. The latter is a reasonable approximation to the continuous transition found in simulations, demonstrating that subtle entropic free energy differences are accessible in neural cDFT.

</details>


### [251] [State-space symmetry in Boolean network ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19639)
*Yi-Kai Mo,Kevin E. Bassler*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文指出布尔网络（Boolean network）动力学自身存在一个基本对称群——N维超八面体群（hyperoctahedral group），并由此解释了布尔函数（Boolean functions）为何按Zyklenzeiger对称群聚成等价类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究发现演化布尔网络中的布尔函数会聚为具有特定频率分布的等价类，该模式由Zyklenzeiger对称群支配，但其动力学起源此前未被解释。

Method: 将布尔网络的全局动力学编码为状态空间网络（state-space network），对随机交互拓扑系综（ensemble）平均后识别其动力学对称群：由节点翻转（node-flips）与节点标签置换（node-label permutations）生成的N维超八面体群；并引入输入标签置换作为动力学的隐藏规范自由度（hidden gauge freedom）。

Result: 超八面体群变换对任意拓扑的布尔网络均可行，对随机拓扑系综（含所有固定入度K<N的系综）构成完全对称群；布尔函数的Zyklenzeiger对称可直接由此群中的节点翻转与规范自由度推出。经该群关联的布尔网络具有同构的状态空间网络并构成其轨道（orbits），Burnside引理（Burnside's lemma）可给出任意固定拓扑下的轨道数。

Conclusion: 该工作为布尔网络动力学分析提供了对称性约化（symmetry reduction），并解释了演化网络的布尔函数为何落入Zyklenzeiger类以及这些类在有限网络中为何发生分裂。

Abstract: Studies of evolving Boolean network systems have found that Boolean functions cluster into equivalence classes with a characteristic frequency distribution - a pattern governed by the Zyklenzeiger symmetry group - but the dynamical origin of this symmetry has remained unexplained. Here we show that it is a consequence of a fundamental symmetry of Boolean network dynamics itself. A Boolean network consists of nodes that update according to Boolean functions of their regulatory inputs; its global dynamics is encoded in a state-space network. We identify the dynamical symmetry group of ensembles of Boolean networks that average over random interaction topologies: for networks with $N$ nodes, it is the $N$-dimensional hyperoctahedral group, generated by node-flips and node-label permutations. These transformations are admissible for every Boolean network regardless of topology; for random-topology ensembles, including all ensembles of fixed in-degree $K<N$, they constitute the complete symmetry group. We also show that input-label permutations constitute a hidden gauge freedom of the dynamics. The Zyklenzeiger symmetry of Boolean functions follows directly from the node-flips of the hyperoctahedral group together with this gauge freedom. Boolean networks related by the hyperoctahedral group have isomorphic state-space networks and form its orbits, and Burnside's lemma gives the number of these orbits for any fixed network topology. It provides a symmetry reduction of the analysis of Boolean network dynamics and explains why the Boolean functions of evolved networks fall into Zyklenzeiger classes, and why these classes split in finite networks.

</details>


### [252] [Calculating correlation functions in integrable spin chains from local conserved charges and currents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20181)
*Kohei Fukai*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出一种计算可积自旋链（integrable spin chains）关联函数的简单代数方法，借助新引入的“流子式（current minors）”证明其期望值为行列式，并据此给出因子化关联函数公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可积自旋链中关联函数（correlation functions）的计算通常十分困难，缺乏统一而简单的代数框架；同时若干短程关联函数的因子化公式仅为猜想，需要严格证明。

Method: 引入流子式（current minors），即局域守恒荷密度及其流（local conserved charge densities and their currents）的反称化乘积，证明其期望值等于密度与流均值构成的行列式；再将局域算符（local operators）分解为流子式，导出以流均值为变量的因子化公式。

Result: 所得因子化公式在任意非简并本征态（nondegenerate eigenstate）中成立；应用于自旋-1/2 XXZ链（spin-1/2 XXZ chain）及Temperley–Lieb代数（Temperley–Lieb algebra）的所有表示，证明了此前被猜想的短程关联函数因子化公式。

Conclusion: 该代数方法将关联函数计算归结为流均值的行列式与分解运算，统一处理可积自旋链的关联函数，并为Temperley–Lieb代数相关模型中的因子化猜想提供了严格证明。

Abstract: We develop a simple algebraic method for calculating correlation functions in integrable spin chains. We introduce current minors, antisymmetrized products of local conserved charge densities and their currents, and prove that their expectation values are determinants of density and current mean values. Decomposing local operators into current minors, we derive factorized formulas for correlation functions in terms of current mean values, valid in any nondegenerate eigenstate. Applying the method to the spin-$1/2$ XXZ chain and, more generally, to every representation of the Temperley--Lieb algebra, we prove previously conjectured factorized formulas for short-distance correlation functions.

</details>


### [253] [Noise-induced manifolds: reduction of slow--fast SDEs to switching processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20903)
*Jeremy R. Worsfold,Patrick Ryan,Richard G. Morris*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文提出对快噪声仅作用于单一方向的慢—快随机微分方程（slow–fast SDE）作降维，用快噪声的吸收概率（absorption probability）替代不变测度，导出切换扩散或分段确定性马尔可夫过程（PDMP），并给出切换有限时长的一阶修正。应用于Togashi–Kaneko模型可复现既有PDMP并刻画稳态组成对时间尺度分离的依赖，在食物耗竭的觅食蚂蚁模型中揭示离源风险随停留时间上升的规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复制、反应网络与观点动力学中，复制噪声在共识处消失，可使系统呈双稳态（bistability）；更一般地，噪声在整条流形上消失，流形上较慢变量持续运动并决定切换速率，但缺乏导出这些切换动力学的一般方法。

Method: 将快噪声作用于单一方向的慢—快随机微分方程降维为切换扩散或分段确定性马尔可夫过程（PDMP），以快噪声的吸收概率取代不变测度（invariant measure），并对切换的有限持续时间引入一阶修正。

Result: 在两物种Togashi–Kaneko模型中，该降维复现了此前的PDMP，且修正项捕捉到稳态组成（stationary composition）如何依赖时间尺度分离程度；在食物源耗竭的觅食蚂蚁模型中，每次停留都会提高离开食物源的风险（hazard），使蚁群在刚到达后不久便离开食物源的可能性降低。

Conclusion: 以吸收概率替代不变测度的降维框架可导出噪声消失流形上的切换动力学，并通过一阶修正刻画有限切换时长效应，为反应网络、双稳态系统及行为动力学等慢—快随机模型提供通用分析工具。

Abstract: When constituents copy one another, as in reaction networks, collective behaviour and opinion dynamics, the noise of copying vanishes at consensus and can make a system bistable. More generally this noise vanishes on whole manifolds, along which slower variables keep moving and set the switching rates, yet no general method derives these switching dynamics. Here we reduce slow--fast stochastic differential equations whose fast noise acts in a single direction to a switching diffusion or piecewise-deterministic Markov process (PDMP), using the absorption probabilities of the fast noise in place of an invariant measure, and derive a first-order correction for the finite duration of switches. For the two-species Togashi--Kaneko model the reduction recovers our earlier PDMP, and the correction captures how the stationary composition depends on the timescale separation. In a model of foraging ants with a depleting food source, the hazard of leaving a source rises through every stay, which makes the colony unlikely to leave a source soon after arriving there.

</details>


### [254] [Dynamical theory of the East model. I. A review on the Zwanzig-Mori projection-operator formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20946)
*Chong Zha,Yuliang Jin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文综述了East模型（一维动力学受约束自旋模型）动力学的理论进展，重点讨论自旋时间关联函数与弛豫时间，并系统评估Zwanzig-Mori投影算符框架下各级截断近似的有效性与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: East模型是展现玻璃态动力学（glassy dynamics）的范例性动力学受约束模型（kinetically constrained model），但其动力学尚无完整解析描述，需要在"最简"模型中厘清层级弛豫（hierarchical relaxation）的本质困难。

Method: 综述低温极限下弛豫时间超Arrhenius标度（super-Arrhenius scaling）的严格结果；在涨落基（fluctuation basis）与畴基（domain basis）两种表象下统一推导Zwanzig-Mori投影算符形式，得到时间关联函数与记忆核（memory kernel）的无穷耦合方程层级，并比较一阶、二阶截断或闭合近似与蒙特卡洛模拟。

Result: 各近似在中等温度下表现尚可，但在低温区普遍失效：涨落基的模式耦合闭合（mode-coupling closure）引入虚假的遍历性破缺转变（spurious ergodicity-breaking transitions），畴基截断则舍弃慢模、无法捕捉弛豫时间的渐近发散。

Conclusion: 二阶以上解析可处理性的丧失是核心障碍，凸显即使在玻璃态动力学"最简"模型中，描述层级弛豫仍存在根本性困难。

Abstract: The East model is a paradigmatic kinetically constrained spin model in one dimension that exhibits glassy dynamics. This review surveys the progress on the theoretical description of the dynamics of the East model, focusing on the spin time correlation function and the relaxation time. We summarize rigorous results on the super-Arrhenius scaling of the relaxation time in the low-temperature limit. We then present a unified derivation of the Zwanzig-Mori projection-operator formalism for both the fluctuation and domain bases. Within this formalism, an infinite hierarchy of coupled equations for the time correlation function and memory kernels emerges---a structure reminiscent of generalized mode-coupling theory developed for supercooled liquids. We review the analytical approximations obtained by truncating or closing the hierarchy at first or second orders and compare them against Monte Carlo simulations. Although these approaches perform reasonably at moderate temperatures, they universally fail in the low-temperature regime: mode-coupling closure schemes in the fluctuation basis introduce spurious ergodicity-breaking transitions, while truncations in the domain basis discard slower modes and fail to capture the asymptotic divergence of the relaxation time. The loss of analytical tractability beyond second order remains the central obstacle, underscoring the fundamental difficulty of describing hierarchical relaxation even in this ``simplest'' model of glassy dynamics.

</details>


### [255] [Basic introduction to Freidlin-Wentzell theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19916)
*Jiawei Yan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文是对 Freidlin-Wentzell 理论（Freidlin-Wentzell theory）的教学式物理综述，以统一的哈密顿框架（Hamiltonian framework）为核心，系统推导稀有噪声诱导跃迁的最优路径、平均逃逸时间的指数标度与亚稳态占据规律，并连接多类随机系统与数值求解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Freidlin-Wentzell 理论为随机动力系统中的稀有噪声诱导跃迁（rare, noise-induced transitions）提供定量基础，但相关工具分散；本文旨在以物理为导向、教学式地统一梳理该理论的计算流程与应用。

Method: 从连续时间马尔可夫过程（continuous-time Markov process）的无穷小生成元（infinitesimal generator）出发，推导局部哈密顿量，经 Legendre-Fenchel 对偶（Legendre-Fenchel duality）得到拉格朗日量与作用量泛函（action functional），并构造准势景观（quasi-potential landscape）。

Result: 该形式化流程被统一应用于弱噪声扩散（weak-noise diffusions）、马尔可夫跳过程（Markov jump processes）、弱相互作用粒子系综（weakly interacting particle ensembles）与非平衡随机场（nonequilibrium stochastic fields），并给出宏观粗粒化（macroscopic coarse-graining）的精确条件；同时阐明细致平衡破缺（broken detailed balance）如何分离正反向跃迁路径、局部线性稳定性与高斯近似（Gaussian approximations）的失效之处，以及用组合式 W-图（combinatorial W-graphs）组装多吸引子景观。

Conclusion: 文章将解析理论与现代数值求解器衔接，包括有序迎风方法（ordered upwind methods）、几何最小作用量算法（geometric minimum action algorithms）与神经网络参数化（neural-network parameterizations），并提供配套的 GPU 加速 Python 软件包。

Abstract: Freidlin-Wentzell theory provides the quantitative foundation for rare, noise-induced transitions in stochastic dynamical systems, predicting optimal transition pathways, the exponential scaling of mean escape times, and the long-term occupancy of competing attractors in metastable systems. Here we present a pedagogical, physics-oriented review built around a unified Hamiltonian framework: starting from the infinitesimal generator of a continuous-time Markov process, we derive the local Hamiltonian, obtain the Lagrangian and action functional via Legendre-Fenchel duality, and construct the quasi-potential landscape. We apply this streamlined formalism across weak-noise diffusions, Markov jump processes, weakly interacting particle ensembles, and nonequilibrium stochastic fields, alongside exact conditions for macroscopic coarse-graining. Along the way, we highlight how broken detailed balance drives forward and reverse transition paths apart, explain where local linear stability and Gaussian approximations break down, and assemble multi-attractor landscapes using combinatorial $W$-graphs. Finally, we bridge the analytical theory to modern numerical solvers, including ordered upwind methods, geometric minimum action algorithms, and neural-network parameterizations, and provide an accompanying GPU-accelerated Python package.

</details>


### [256] [A feedback-driven central limit theorem with $q$-Gaussian limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20201)
*G. Baris Bagci,Thomas Oikonomou*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文建立了“反馈驱动中心极限定理（feedback-driven central limit theorem）”：当独立噪声变量乘以依赖于前一归一化和的振幅时，其归一化和在正反馈下收敛到 Student 律（等价于 1<q<2 的 q-Gaussian），负反馈下收敛到紧支撑密度，零反馈回到高斯律，并证明二次型条件方差是被该平衡恒等式唯一刻画的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典鞅中心极限定理（martingale central limit theorem）要求条件方差收敛到常数，而反馈机制下条件方差 c+b·y² 并不稳定，故需要新的极限定理刻画此类情形。

Method: 研究对象为以鞅差（martingale differences）为项、条件方差 c+b·y² 二次依赖前项归一化和 y 的随机和；噪声方差有限，正反馈时如 Lyapunov 定理假设有限三阶矩，负反馈时假设有界噪声以保证条件方差非负。证明上，负反馈采用矩方法（method of moments），正反馈采用 Wasserstein 耦合论证（Wasserstein coupling），并覆盖极限方差无穷的情形。

Result: 正反馈时归一化和收敛到与噪声分布无关的 Student 律（q-Gaussian，1<q<2）；负反馈时收敛到紧支撑密度，当 −1/2<b<0 时为 q<1 的 q-Gaussian；零反馈恢复高斯律（q=1），Cauchy 律（q=2）仅在反馈随项数缓慢增长的三角阵列（triangular array）极限情形出现。此外，定理背后的平衡恒等式（balance identity）唯一选出二次型反馈：q<2 的 q-Gaussian 仅对二次条件方差成立，而 2≤q<3 的 q-Gaussian 对任何条件方差均不成立。

Conclusion: 该工作将 q-Gaussian 分布族（q-Gaussian distributions）作为反馈驱动随机和的普适吸引子，对每个固定反馈强度给出 q<2，而 q=2 仅作为极限获得，并说明二次型条件方差在此框架中具有唯一性。

Abstract: We establish a central limit theorem, the feedback-driven central limit theorem, for sums whose summands are independent noise variables multiplied by an amplitude that depends on the preceding normalized sum $y$. The summands are martingale differences whose conditional variance, $c+b\,y^{2}$, depends quadratically on $y$, with feedback strength $b$; unlike in the martingale central limit theorem, this conditional variance does not settle to a constant. The noise has finite variance; for positive feedback we assume, as in Lyapunov's theorem, a finite third moment, and for negative feedback bounded noise, so that the conditional variance stays nonnegative. For positive feedback, the normalized sum converges to a Student law, equivalently a $q$-Gaussian with $1<q<2$, independently of the noise distribution. For negative feedback, it converges to a compactly supported density, which is a $q$-Gaussian with $q<1$ when $-\tfrac12<b<0$. The Gaussian law, $q=1$, is recovered at zero feedback, while the Cauchy law, $q=2$, is obtained only as a limiting case, in a triangular array in which the feedback grows slowly with the number of terms. Thus every $q<2$ is obtained for a fixed feedback strength, and $q=2$ as a limit. We further show that the balance identity behind the theorem singles out quadratic feedback: a $q$-Gaussian with $q<2$ satisfies it only for a quadratic conditional variance, and no $q$-Gaussian with $2\le q<3$ satisfies it for any conditional variance. The proofs use the method of moments for negative feedback and a Wasserstein coupling argument for positive feedback, including cases in which the limiting variance is infinite.

</details>


### [257] [Logarithmic thermodynamic cost of prediction from rare observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20248)
*Andrey V. Dobrynin,Anastasia Stroujkova*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究自主化学记忆（autonomous chemical memory）在稀疏采样下预测环境变化的 thermodynamic cost（热力学代价），给出预测信息的普适 lower-power bound（低功耗上限），并指出记忆架构（memory architecture）决定可达到的 scaling（标度）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆中要想从罕见观测（rare observations）中预测环境变化，必须耗费能量用于采样与记录。已有理想观察者模型可保留与采样频率成正比的 predictive information（预测信息），但物理记忆实现这一信息所需的热力学代价尚不明确。

Method: 考虑由 sample-and-hold sensor（采样-保持传感器）驱动的自主化学记忆，观测以速率 gamma 切换的环境，采样频率满足 f << gamma；分析在抑制误记录与快速响应新观测之间的竞争要求，并比较不同记忆状态数与网络拓扑下的可达标度。

Result: 理想观察者获得的预测信息与 f 成正比，而自主物理记忆在每次传感变化时需付出约 kBT ln(gamma/f) 的功。作者建立了预测信息的普适低功耗上限：在功率预算 P 下最多按 P/ln(P_ref/P) 增长，且对任意有限记忆状态数、网络拓扑（network topology）与记忆弛豫时间（memory relaxation time）一致成立。在跃迁速率有界且仅从记忆读出的条件下，两态记忆（two-state memory）仅限 P^2 标度，而三态记忆（three-state memory）即可达到最优对数标度。

Conclusion: 结果表明，获取信息（acquiring information）与维持变化环境的有效记录（maintaining a useful record）之间存在根本性的热力学区别，且记忆架构是实现可用预测信息的关键因素。

Abstract: A memory predicting changes in its environment from rare observations must spend energy on sampling and recording. We consider autonomous chemical memories driven by a sample-and-hold sensor observing an environment that switches at rate gamma, with sampling frequency f<<gamma. An ideal observer with access to the full observation history can retain predictive information proportional to f. However, retaining a finite fraction of this information in an autonomous physical memory requires work of order kBT ln(gamma/f) per sensor change. This additional thermodynamic cost arises from the competing requirements of suppressing false records during long intervals between observations and responding rapidly to new observations. We establish a universal low-power bound on predictive information, which grows at most as P/ln(P_ref/P) with power budget P, uniformly over any finite number of memory states and independently of network topology and memory relaxation times. We further demonstrate that memory architecture determines the attainable scaling. With bounded transition rates and readout from the memory alone, two-state memories are limited to P^2 scaling, whereas a three-state memory is sufficient to attain the optimal logarithmic scaling. Our results establish a fundamental thermodynamic distinction between acquiring information and maintaining a useful record of a changing environment.

</details>


### [258] [Efficient Finite-Temperature Krylov Methods Based on Imaginary-Time Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20506)
*Hiroto Yamamoto,Katsuhiro Morita*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作提出两种基于虚时演化构建Krylov子空间的有限温度Krylov方法（FTiTL与FTiTK），在横场Ising模型上相比传统FTLM以更少的局域算符作用次数获得更高精度，且FTiTK可将态矢量存储内存减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限温度Lanczos方法（finite-temperature Lanczos method, FTLM）依赖对哈密顿量的直接作用来构建Krylov子空间；本文旨在通过改用虚时演化（imaginary-time evolution）生成子空间，以提升精度与计算效率并降低内存开销。

Method: 提出有限温度虚时Lanczos方法（finite-temperature imaginary-time Lanczos method, FTiTL），将Lanczos方法作用于虚时演化算符；以及有限温度虚时Krylov方法（finite-temperature imaginary-time Krylov method, FTiTK），利用非正交Krylov基（nonorthogonal Krylov basis）构造广义本征值问题（generalized eigenvalue problem）。以方晶格横场Ising模型（transverse-field Ising model）为测试对象，N=16采用精确对角化（exact diagonalization）、N=32采用量子蒙特卡洛（quantum Monte Carlo）结果作为参照。

Result: 在合理选取虚时步长（imaginary-time steps）时，两种方法在铁磁区（ferromagnetic regime）及量子临界点（quantum critical point）附近均以更少的局域算符作用次数取得高于FTLM的精度。对N=32，FTiTL达到比热（specific heat）C/N的均方根误差（root-mean-square error）不超过10^-2所需局域算符作用次数约为FTLM的一半；FTiTK仅需一个全希尔伯特空间维度（full Hilbert-space dimension）的态矢量即可实现，态矢量存储内存较FTLM减半。

Conclusion: 基于虚时演化的Krylov子空间构建可有效替代直接哈密顿量作用，在精度、局域算符作用次数和内存占用方面均具优势，适用于有限温度量子多体计算。

Abstract: We develop two finite-temperature Krylov methods that construct Krylov subspaces using imaginary-time evolution rather than direct applications of the Hamiltonian. The finite-temperature imaginary-time Lanczos method (FTiTL) applies the Lanczos method to the imaginary-time evolution operator, whereas the finite-temperature imaginary-time Krylov method (FTiTK) constructs a generalized eigenvalue problem using a nonorthogonal Krylov basis. We evaluate the accuracy and computational efficiency of these methods for the square-lattice transverse-field Ising model, using exact diagonalization for $N=16$ and quantum Monte Carlo results for $N=32$ as references. With appropriately chosen imaginary-time steps, both methods achieve higher accuracy than the conventional finite-temperature Lanczos method (FTLM) with fewer local operator applications in the ferromagnetic regime and near the quantum critical point. For $N=32$, FTiTL requires approximately half as many local operator applications as FTLM to achieve a root-mean-square error of $10^{-2}$ or less in the specific heat $C/N$. Furthermore, FTiTK can be implemented using only one state vector of full Hilbert-space dimension, halving the memory required for storing state vectors relative to FTLM.

</details>


### [259] [A Free Energy Approach to Modelling Disordered Hyperuniform Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21351)
*Alex Mamaev,Enno Igel,Axel Voigt,Marco Salvalaglio*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出一种基于自由能（free energy）的相场晶体（phase-field crystal）模型，通过特殊设计的关联核（correlation kernel）同时选择连续简并波模，实现对无序超均匀（disordered hyperuniform）图案的建模与结构选择控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序超均匀图案缺乏基于自由能的统一描述框架，且需将结构的选取与长波密度关联的控制解耦。

Method: 构建相场晶体模型，其关联核可同时选择连续的简并波模（degenerate wave modes）；在低波矢区通过反演 Ornstein-Zernike 关系构造关联函数，以近似超均匀图案的结构因子（structure factor）。

Result: 模型随自由能参数与驱动力（driving force）的变化呈现液晶、晶体、准晶（quasicrystal）与长寿命无序态之间的转变；固定驱动力时，调节关联核宽度可控制密度场选择的结构与波矢；关联函数低波矢区标度可精确复现指定超均匀指数（hyperuniform exponent），且与较大波数处结构性质无关。

Conclusion: 建立了描述无序超均匀态的自由能框架，并实现了结构选择与长波长密度关联的独立调控。

Abstract: We present a free-energy-based approach for modelling disordered hyperuniform patterns. Specifically, we devise a phase-field crystal model with a correlation kernel that simultaneously selects for a continuous range of degenerate wave modes. At small wave vectors, we construct the correlation function by inverting the Ornstein-Zernike relation to approximate the structure factor of a hyperuniform pattern. Our model demonstrates a crossover between liquid, crystalline, quasicrystalline, and long-lived disordered states depending on the specified free energy parameter and driving force. We show that, for a fixed driving force, the specific structures and wave vectors selected by the density field can be controlled by modulating the width of the correlation kernel. Furthermore, we show that the scaling of the low-wave-vector region of the correlation function can accurately reproduce a specified hyperuniform exponent, independently of the structural properties at larger wave numbers. Our results establish a free-energy-based framework for describing disordered hyperuniform states, demonstrating and enabling independent control over structural selection and long-wavelength density correlations.

</details>


### [260] [Renormalization group study of critical behavior of $O(m)$ model with cubic anisotropy and extended defects: two-loop approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21482)
*D. Shapoval,M. Dudka*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究在 $\varepsilon_d$ 维内关联、其余 $d-\varepsilon_d$ 维随机分布的淬火扩展缺陷（quenched extended defects）对具有立方各向异性（cubic anisotropy）的 $d$ 维 $O(m)$ 对称系统临界性质的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究扩展缺陷的空间关联维度如何改变立方各向异性 $O(m)$ 系统的临界行为，并确定无序在物理相关情形下变得相关的边界条件。

Method: 采用场论重整化群方法（field-theoretic renormalization-group approach），在最小减除方案（minimal subtraction scheme）下计算两圈重整化群函数，并对渐近级数应用重求和技术（resummation techniques）进行分析。

Result: 估计出 $d=3$ 时 $m=2$ 与 $m=3$ 情形下无序变得相关（disorder becomes relevant）的 $\varepsilon_d$ 边缘值，并给出无序诱导普适类（disorder-induced universality classes）的临界指数数值。

Conclusion: 扩展缺陷的关联维度 $\varepsilon_d$ 存在决定无序相关性的边缘阈值，超过该阈值后系统进入新的无序诱导普适类，其临界指数由计算给出。

Abstract: We study the impact of quenched extended defects correlated in $ε_d$ dimensions and randomly distributed in the remaining $d - ε_d$ dimensions on the critical properties of a $d$-dimensional $O(m)$-symmetric system with cubic anisotropy. Exploiting the field-theoretic renormalization-group approach within the minimal subtraction scheme, we compute the two-loop renormalization-group functions. Applying resummation techniques to analyze the asymptotic series, we estimate the marginal values of $ε_d$ above which disorder becomes relevant for the physically important cases $m = 2$ and $m = 3$ in $d = 3$. We report numerical values of the critical exponents for the disorder-induced universality classes.

</details>
