# physics.ins-det [[Back]](#toc)

### [261] [Detection of minimum ionising particles with internal gain in the first Silicon Electron Multiplier demonstrators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20211)
*Federico De Benedetti,Sara Cesare,Victor Coco,Paula Collins,Raphael Dumps,Edgar Cid Lemos,Samuel Mico,Janina Nicolini,Alfonso Puicercus Gomez,Efren Rodriguez Rodriguez,Lotte Sikkema,Hironori Sonobe,Abraham Gallas Torreira,Morag Williams*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: SiEM（Silicon Electron Multiplier）是一种利用硅体内复合电极结构实现内部增益的新型最小电离粒子（MIP）探测传感器，本文报道了由滨松光子（HPK）制备的单电极构型功能演示器的首批实验结果，验证了电荷倍增。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统增益传感器（LGAD、APD）依赖掺杂增益层，在极高辐照注量（$10^{16}\,n_{\mathrm{eq}}/\mathrm{cm}^2$ 以上）下抗辐照能力受限；SiEM 希望通过外加电势而非掺杂层实现放大，以获得更优的辐射耐受性。

Method: 在前期概念研究与仿真基础上，探索了多种制备路线，包括 CNM 的深反应离子刻蚀（DRIE）与 PSI 的金属辅助化学刻蚀（MACEtch）；本文对 HPK 采用改进几何的单电极（single-electrode）构型制备的功能性 SiEM 演示器进行 I-V 电学表征与最小电离粒子束流测试。

Result: I-V 测量与束流测试均给出电荷倍增的明确证据，且增益与仿真预测相当。

Conclusion: 首批功能性 SiEM 演示器实验证实了该传感器概念的可行性，其增益行为与仿真一致，为后续抗辐照性能研究提供了实验基础。

Abstract: The Silicon Electron Multiplier (SiEM) is a novel sensor concept for minimum ionising particle (MIP) detection that uses internal gain achieved through a composite electrode structure embedded within the silicon bulk. Unlike conventional gain sensors (LGADs, APDs), the SiEM achieves amplification through applied electric potentials rather than doped gain layers, potentially offering superior radiation hardness above $10^{16}\,n_{\mathrm{eq}}/\mathrm{cm}^2$. Following initial conceptual studies and simulation work, several fabrication approaches were explored, including Deep Reactive Ion Etching (DRIE) at Centro Nacional de Microelectrónica (CNM) and Metal Assisted Chemical Etching (MACEtch) at the Paul Scherrer Institute (PSI). This paper reports the first experimental results from functional SiEM demonstrators produced by Hamamatsu Photonics K. K. (HPK) using a single-electrode configuration with modified geometry. Electrical characterisation through I-V measurements and test beam studies with minimum ionising particles demonstrate clear evidence of charge multiplication, with gains comparable to simulation predictions.

</details>


### [262] [Directionality of low-energy electron recoils in the CYGNO experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20754)
*F. D. Amaro,E. Baracchini,L. Benussi,C. Capoccia,M. Caponero,L. G. M. de Carvalho,G. Cavoto,I. A. Costa,M. D'Astolfo,G. D'Imperio,G. Dho,E. Di Marco,J. M. F. dos Santos,D. Fiorina,F. Iacoangeli,H. P. Lima,G. Maccarrone,P. Meloni,A. Messina,C. M. B. Monteiro,R. A. Nobrega,G. M. Oppedisano,I. F. Pains,E. Paoletti,F. Petrucci,S. Piacentini,D. Pierluigi,D. Pinci,A. Russo,G. Saviano,F. F. Scamporlino,R. C. Sousa,N. J. C. Spooner,R. Tesauro,S. Tomassini,S. Torelli*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该研究基于CYGNO光学时间投影室（optical time-projection chamber）LIME原型与高粒度sCMOS读出，开发算法重建低能电子反冲的初始方向，在15–70 keV范围内优化并表征角分辨性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子反冲方向性（electron-recoil directionality）有助于区分太阳中微子（solar-neutrino）相互作用与本底，并结合反冲能量测量与三维径迹实现中微子能谱学（neutrino-energy spectroscopy），因此需评估低能电子反冲初始方向的可重建性。

Method: 采用专用算法，依据径迹光分布的不对称性确定相互作用点，并在多次散射（multiple scattering）抹除方向信息之前，利用电离径迹（ionization trail）的早期部分估计反冲方向；通过电子反冲与探测器响应的蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulation）表征方法，并以最小化重建与真实投影角分布宽度来优化算法参数。

Result: 投影角响应用两个高斯分量建模以反映其对径迹倾角的依赖；与平行于气体电子倍增器（Gas Electron Multiplier, GEM）平面的有利投影相关的较窄分量，其宽度从约20 keV处的约20°改善至60 keV处的约12°；同时量化了角核正确重建事件的比例及仅相机二维测量的主要局限。

Conclusion: 在CYGNO-LIME原型中，低能电子反冲初始方向可被有效重建，角分辨随能量提高而改善；相机-only二维测量存在固有局限，研究为方向性中微子探测的可行性评估提供了量化依据。

Abstract: Electron-recoil directionality can help distinguish solar-neutrino interactions from backgrounds and, together with recoil-energy measurements and three-dimensional tracking, enable neutrino-energy spectroscopy. We study the reconstruction of the initial direction of low-energy electron recoils in the CYGNO optical time-projection chamber, focusing on the 50 L LIME prototype and its high-granularity scientific CMOS (sCMOS) readout. A dedicated algorithm identifies the interaction point from the asymmetry of the track light distribution and estimates the recoil direction from the early portion of the ionization trail, before multiple scattering erases most of the directional information. The method is characterized with a detailed Monte Carlo simulation of electron recoils and detector response. Samples spanning 15 keV to 70 keV are used to optimize the algorithm parameters by minimizing the width of the reconstructed-minus-true projected-angle distribution. The projected angular response is modeled by two Gaussian components that account for its dependence on track inclination. The narrower component, associated with favorable projections approximately parallel to the Gas Electron Multiplier (GEM) plane, has a width that improves from about $20^\circ$ near 20 keV to about $12^\circ$ at 60 keV. We also quantify the fraction of events in the correctly reconstructed angular core and discuss the dominant limitations of a camera-only two-dimensional measurement.

</details>


### [263] [Particle Identification Studies using a Highly Granular Crystal Electromagnetic Calorimeter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21011)
*Jack Rolph,Yong Liu,Baohua Qi,Shu Li,Zhiyu Zhao,Haijun Yang*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向环形正负电子对撞机（CEPC）电磁量能器（electromagnetic calorimeter）的高粒度闪烁晶体设计方案，并探索深度学习与传统机器学习的粒子鉴别（particle identification）方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CEPC电磁量能器采用硅光电倍增器（silicon photomultiplier）读取高粒度闪烁晶体信号以测量光子物理信号，需要发展相应的粒子鉴别能力，尤其是缺乏显式标签条件下的簇射（showering）与穿透（punch-through）π介子分离问题。

Method: 先采用对比学习（contrastive machine learning）技术实现无显式标签的簇射与穿透π介子分离，再基于其结果训练监督式粒子鉴别方法，分别使用图神经网络（graph neural network）与提升决策树（boosted decision tree）区分电子、簇射强子与类最小电离粒子（minimum-ionizing-particle-like）事件，并用Shapley加性解释（Shapley additive explanations）分析模型行为。

Result: 两类分类器在接收者操作特征曲线下面积（area-under-the-receiver operating characteristic curve）上均达到0.996；Shapley加性解释分析显示提升决策树分类器对各类事件的判别符合预期；并在CERN质子同步加速器（Proton Synchrotron）T9束线实验数据上验证了粒子鉴别能力。

Conclusion: 对比学习可用于无标签的簇射/穿透π介子分离，并可作为监督分类的训练依据；图神经网络与提升决策树在该高粒度晶体量能器方案上均表现出强粒子鉴别性能，且已初步在真实束线数据中得到演示。

Abstract: A proposal for the Circular Electron Positron Collider electromagnetic calorimeter is a highly granular scintillating crystal design that uses silicon photomultipliers to measure physics signals from photons. The potential to develop particle identification methods using deep and traditional machine learning is explored. A contrastive machine learning technique was found to enable the separation of showering and 'punch-through' pions without explicit labels. A supervised particle identification method was then trained using the results, using both a graph neural network and a boosted decision tree to distinguish electron, showering hadron, and minimum-ionizing-particle-like events. The classifier achieved an area-under-the-receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.996 for both cases. A Shapley additive explanations analysis showed the boosted decision tree classifier matched expectations for each event type. The particle identification capability is then demonstrated on experimental data taken from the CERN Proton Synchrotron T9 beamline.

</details>


### [264] [Evaluation of Performance of the p-stop fabrication process variations in ATLAS18 strip sensors, Pre- and Post-Irradiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21101)
*J. Kozakova,A. Affolder,K. Affolder,A. Awais,E. Bach,G. A. Beck,P. Bernabeu,A. J. Bevan,Z. Chen,V. Cindro,B. Crick,J. Dandoy,I. Dawson,V. Fadeyev,P. Federicova,C. Fleta,P. Gallus,C. Jessiman,S. Katznelson,J. Keller,C. T. Klein,T. Koffas,J. Kroll,M. Kutova,J. Kvasnicka,C. Lacasta,I. Mandic,R. R. Marcelo Gregorio,F. Martinez-McKinney,M. Mikestikova,P. S. Miyagawa,L. Morelos-Zaragoza,R. S. Orr,Q. Paddock,C. Solaz,U. Soldevila,E. Staats,P. Tuma,M. Ullan,Y. Unno,M. Vansteenkiste,P. Wishart,S. C. Zenz,Y. Zhao*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 针对ATLAS18量产n-in-p微条传感器在质量控制中发现的部分批次p-stop密度偏低问题，制备了四种p-stop工艺变体样品，经中子与伽马辐照及系统测试，评估其对条间隔离性能的影响，结果显示各类型传感器性能均良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高亮度大型强子对撞机（HL-LHC）升级要求以全硅内径迹探测器（ITk）替换ATLAS内探测器，而在ATLAS18生产传感器与测试芯片的质量控制/质量保证（QC/QA）中，多个研究所发现某些生产批次存在p-stop密度偏低的问题，需查明成因并评估工艺改进方案。

Method: 制备两个专用样品批次，涵盖四种p-stop制造工艺变体（类型1–4），共测试56个全尺寸传感器及大量微型传感器与测试芯片；部分样品接受辐照，中子注量达1.6E15 neq/cm2、60Co源伽马剂量达66 Mrad，对应HL-LHC运行10年后的最大预期水平。

Result: 系统评估表明四种p-stop类型的传感器均表现出良好性能，工艺变体对条间隔离（interstrip isolation）性能的影响得到量化，并识别出可用于进一步优化的特定优势。

Conclusion: 评估结果确认所评价设计的可靠性，并为条型传感器制造工艺的后续优化提供反馈依据。

Abstract: The High-Luminosity upgrade of the Large Hadron Collider (HL-LHC) requires the replacement of the ATLAS Inner Detector with a new all-silicon Inner Tracker (ITk). Radiation-hard n-in-p micro-strip sensors were developed for use in the ITk. During quality control (QC) and quality assurance (QA) procedures performed on ATLAS18 production sensors and test chips, ATLAS ITk strip sensor institutes identified instances of low p-stop density in certain production batches. To investigate the origin of these issues and to try some technological improvements, two dedicated sample batches with four variations of the p-stop fabrication process were produced. These process types (1-4) were systematically evaluated to determine their effectiveness and uniformity. In total, 56 full-size sensors, along with numerous miniature sensors and test chips, were tested. A number of samples were used in the irradiation campaign reaching the maximum expected levels for the HL-LHC operation after 10 years - with neutrons up to 1.6E15 neq/cm2, and with gamma rays from a 60Co source up to 66 Mrad. The goal of this evaluation program is to assess the impact of of p-stop fabrication process variations on interstrip isolation performance and to provide feedback for future sensor development. This paper describes the applied test procedures and presents the corresponding results, which demonstrate good sensor performance for all investigated p-stop types. The findings confirm the reliability of the evaluated designs and identify specific advantages that can inform further optimization of strip sensor fabrication.

</details>


### [265] [A Compact Single-Flange Low-Energy Inverse Photoemission Spectrometer with Elliptical-Mirror Photon Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19503)
*Jong-Am Hong,Min-Jae Maeng,Kyu-Myung Lee,Seunggu Lee,In-Sung Park,Yongsup Park*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该研究比较了低能反光电子谱（LEIPS）中四种光子收集构型，其中真空内椭球镜（elliptical mirror）方案将91.9%的发射光子送达探测器，并在C₆₀薄膜上给出最高LUMO信号（271 counts/s/μA），约为无镜构型的45倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEIPS受限于微弱的光子发射强度，其灵敏度在很大程度上由光子收集光学（photon collection optics）决定，因此需要系统比较不同收集构型的几何效率与实测信号。

Method: 对比四种逐代改进的构型：无光学元件的旁置探测器（no-mirror）、将准直光束送至正对式光电倍增管（PMT）的离轴抛物面镜（OAP）、将OAP输出耦合至量子效率约两倍的侧窗式PMT的外部光学（OAP-external），以及真空内椭球镜聚焦构型（elliptical）；并用光线追迹（ray-tracing）评估收集效率。

Result: 光线追迹显示椭球镜、OAP镜与无镜构型的收集率分别为91.9%、63.3%和3.06%；对C₆₀薄膜，椭球构型的LUMO强度为271 counts/s/μA，是OAP与OAP-external的1.74倍和3.23倍，约为无镜构型的45倍。

Conclusion: 相对OAP构型的增益（约1.74倍）与光线追迹预测的1.45倍量级相当，但因滤光片与探测器亦不同，几何贡献无法单独分离；OAP-external尽管量子效率更高，信号却仅为OAP的0.54倍，表明高灵敏度需同时具备大收集立体角与短的真空内光路。

Abstract: Low-energy inverse photoemission spectroscopy (LEIPS) is limited by weak photon emission, so photon collection optics largely determine its sensitivity. We compare four photon-collection configurations, each addressing a limitation of its predecessor: a detector beside the sample without collection optics (no-mirror), an off-axis parabolic (OAP) mirror delivering a collimated beam to a head-on photomultiplier tube (PMT), external optics coupling the OAP output to a side-on PMT with about twice the quantum efficiency (OAP-external), and an in-vacuum elliptical mirror focusing the emitted light onto the detector (elliptical). Ray-tracing shows that the elliptical mirror delivers 91.9% of the emitted photons to the detector, compared with 63.3% for the OAP mirror and 3.06% without a mirror, while a plane mirror folding the light path by about 90$^\circ$ allows the electron gun, collection optics and detector to fit on a single 8-inch flange. For C$_{60}$ films, the elliptical configuration gives the highest lowest unoccupied molecular orbital (LUMO) intensity of the four, 271 counts per second per $μ$A of sample current, 1.74 and 3.23 times those of the OAP and OAP-external configurations and about 45 times that of the no-mirror configuration. The gain over the OAP configuration is comparable to the 1.45-fold increase predicted by ray-tracing, but the filter and detector also differed, so the geometric contribution could not be isolated. The OAP-external configuration gave only 0.54 times the OAP signal despite its higher quantum efficiency. High sensitivity therefore requires both a large collection solid angle and a short optical path kept inside the vacuum.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [266] [Random matrix discrepancy -- CLuP algorithm and parametric RDT approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18650)
*Mihailo Stojnic*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究比例极限（proportional regime）下随机矩阵差异性问题（random matrix discrepancy）的信息论与算法层面，提出随机对偶理论（Random Duality Theory, RDT）框架并配合负Hopfield代理模型，建立下界并与CLuP算法表现相互印证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是刻画最小偏差量 $\kappa=\min_{x\in\mathcal{B}^n}\|\sum_i \bar{A}_i x_i\|_2$ 在 $n/d^2\to\alpha$ 比例尺度下的信息论下界与算法可达性能之间的差距，即计算间隙（computational gap）。

Method: 发展RDT框架及其提升变体（lifted variant）推导下界；引入负Hopfield模型作为完全提升RDT的代理，在第2至第4提升层级进行参数化分析；并设计受控松弛（Controlled Loosening Up, CLuP）算法求解矩阵差异问题。

Result: RDT下界与一阶矩（first moment）界精确吻合；负Hopfield代理在第2提升层级恰好落在已知信息论下界与算法上界之间；第3、4提升层级预测低 $\kappa$ 区间存在显著计算间隙；CLuP算法性能与预测一致——低 $\alpha$ 区间接近信息论预测（间隙小或无），高 $\alpha$ 区间呈现明显计算间隙。

Conclusion: 矩阵差异问题的信息论与算法现象学在整个 $\alpha\in(0,\infty)$ 范围内与对称二值感知机（symmetric binary perceptron, SBP）向量类比版本一致，并与既有文献结论相符。

Abstract: We study information-theoretic and algorithmic aspects of random matrix discrepancy in proportional regime. The problem amounts to finding $κ=\min_{\x\in\mB^n}\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bar{A}_i\x_i\|_2$, where $\bar{A}_i\in\mR^{d\times d}$ is a collection of $n$ iid GOE matrices, $\mB^n$ are vertices of the unit cube in $\mR^n$, and $α=\lim_{n\rightarrow \infty}\frac{n}{d^2}$ is a proportionality constant.
  Strong progress is made in several directions: (i) We develop a Random duality theory (RDT) framework and utilize its a lifted variant to establish lower bounds on $κ$, which precisely match the bounds obtained via the first moment in [81]. (ii) To address analytical challenges, we propose a negative Hopfield model as a proxy for implementation of a fully lifted RDT. On the 2nd lifting level, this model fits remarkably well between the information-theoretic lower bounds from [81] and the algorithmic upper bounds from [38]. (iii) On the 3rd and 4th lifting levels, parametric RDT concepts predict a sizeable computational gap for lower $κ$ values in the proxy model. Extending proxy information-theoretic fitting to algorithmic suggests a similar computational gap for matrix discrepancy in low $κ$ regime, aligning with [73]. (iv) We design a Controlled Loosening Up (CLuP) algorithm for matrix discrepancy. Its performance strongly matches our analytical results -- closely approaching information-theoretic predictions in lower $α$ ranges (indicating a small or nonexistent computational gap) and following the proxy model's algorithmic predictions in higher $α$ ranges (indicating a sizeable computational gap).
  Both the information-theoretic and algorithmic predictions match the corresponding phenomenologies of the symmetric binary perceptron (SBP) vector analogue in the entire $α\in(0,\infty)$ range [1,2,14,31,51,92,111].

</details>


### [267] [Exact mobility edges in high-dimensional quasiperiodic networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19152)
*Jia-Hui Zhang,Shan-Zhong Li,Zhi Li*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文建立了一类高维准周期网络模型的广义理论框架：当周期网络作为键修饰连接（bond-decorating links）且准周期势空间可分离时，可积分掉周期格点，将系统映射为等效高维广义 Aubry-André 方程，从而恢复隐藏自对偶（hidden self-duality）并获得精确迁移率边（exact mobility edges）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受一维隐藏自对偶性工作的启发，旨在将精确迁移率边的可解析处理推广到高维准周期网络模型，弥补高维体系缺乏系统性映射与精确解的不足。

Method: 对满足条件的模型（周期网络作为键修饰连接、准周期势空间可分离）积分掉周期格点，映射为等效高维广义 Aubry-André 方程；以二维与三维模型为例进行理论预测与数值结果对比。

Result: 映射恢复了复杂模型中的隐藏自对偶对称性，从而给出精确迁移率边；二维和三维示例中理论预测与数值结果一致。

Conclusion: 该文确立了将高维准周期网络映射为高维 Aubry-André 模型的清晰范式，为高维系统中精确迁移率边的构造与探索提供了系统路径。

Abstract: Inspired by the one-dimensional hidden self-duality [Phys. Rev. Lett. 134, 246301 (2025)], we establish a generalized theoretical framework for a specific class of high-dimensional quasiperiodic network models. Specifically, as long as the periodic network acts as bond-decorating links and the quasiperiodic potentials are spatially separable, the system can be mapped onto an effective high-dimensional generalized Aubry-André equation by integrating out the periodic sites. This mapping restores the hidden self-duality symmetry in these complex models, thereby yielding exact mobility edges. Taking two-dimensional and three-dimensional models as examples, we demonstrate the consistency between the theoretical prediction and the numerical results. This paper establishes a clear paradigm for mapping high-dimensional quasiperiodic networks to high-dimensional Aubry-André models, providing a systematic pathway for the construction and exploration of the exact mobility edge in high-dimensional systems.

</details>


### [268] [Velocity weakening and stick-slip in mean-field depinning with stress overshoots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19863)
*Adrien Rezzouk,Alberto Rosso*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文研究全连接（fully connected）去钉扎（depinning）模型中瞬态应力过冲（stress overshoot）对力-速度关系与雪崩统计的影响，揭示两个刚度尺度 $k_{\rm w}=M-1$ 与 $k_{\rm q}=(M-1)^2/(4M)$ 所组织的响应分区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在厘清速度弱化（velocity weakening）、黏滑（stick-slip）与失控雪崩（runaway avalanches）之间此前缺乏统一描述的联系，并说明弹性加载如何动态选择再入式力-速度（force-velocity）关系的分支。

Method: 结合精确稳态关系（exact stationary relations）、线性稳定性分析（linear stability analysis）、直接数值模拟（direct simulations）与粒子群体动力学（population dynamics），分析由刚度 $k_0$ 弹簧驱动的全连接去钉扎模型。

Result: 当 $M>1$ 时存在两个刚度尺度：$k_{\rm w}$ 处稳态特性首次出现速度弱化分支，$k_{\rm q}$ 处对应准静态极限下有限幅黏滑的起始。二者之间形成中间区：准静态力收敛于定值，而有限速度驱动在临界速度以下出现黏滑；该区雪崩出现瞬态放大与有限幅的“幽灵王者（ghost kings）”事件。在 $k_{\rm q}$ 及以下，同样放大机制产生贯穿系统的“真实王者（genuine kings）”与有限的准静态力循环。

Conclusion: 弹性加载通过动力学方式在再入力-速度关系的各分支间进行选择，从而统一刻画速度弱化、黏滑与失控雪崩之间的关联。

Abstract: We study a fully connected depinning model with transient stress overshoots of strength $M$, driven through a spring of stiffness $k_0$. Combining exact stationary relations, linear stability analysis, direct simulations, and population dynamics, we connect the force--velocity characteristic to stick-slip motion and quasistatic avalanche statistics. For $M>1$, two distinct stiffness scales organize the response. At $k_0=k_{\rm w}\equiv M-1$, the stationary characteristic first develops a velocity-weakening branch, whereas $k_0=k_{\rm q}\equiv(M-1)^2/(4M)$ marks the onset of finite-amplitude stick-slip in the quasistatic limit. Their separation produces an intermediate regime in which the quasistatic force converges to a stationary value, whereas finite-velocity driving produces stick-slip below a critical velocity. Quasistatic avalanches in this regime undergo transient amplification and display an excess of large, asymmetric events---"ghost kings"---that remain finite. By contrast, at and below $k_{\rm q}$ the same amplification produces system-spanning "genuine kings" and a finite quasistatic force cycle. These results show how elastic loading dynamically selects among the branches of a reentrant force--velocity relation and clarify the connection between velocity weakening, stick-slip, and runaway avalanches.

</details>


### [269] [Localization transition in a spin chain with an exact Fock-space tree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21445)
*Bernhard Jobst,Simone Warzel,Alexander Altland,Frank Pollmann*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文提出一种局域无序自旋-1链（local disordered spin-1 chain），其中单个畴壁（domain wall）在精确的二叉树（binary tree）构型空间上演化，从而将多体局域化（many-body localization）问题化为可处理的树图局域化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体局域化常被类比为多体构型图上的安德森局域化（Anderson localization），但强关联无序（strongly correlated disorder）与稠密回路（loops）使该图难以分析，故需要一个既保留关联无序特征又无回路的可解模型。

Method: 构造局域无序自旋-1链模型，使单一畴壁在精确二叉树上探索构型；随机场在该树上产生与一般相互作用系统类似的强关联无序，而回路的缺失保证问题可处理。方法包括强无序下畴壁扩散的严格界（rigorous bound）与指数大规模树上的高效数值计算。

Result: 在强无序区对畴壁的扩散给出了严格上界；指数规模树上的数值结果表明，在有限无序强度（finite disorder strength）处存在局域化转变（localization transition）。

Conclusion: 该模型提供了一个可严格分析与高效数值模拟的平台，用以研究含强关联无序的多体局域化，并支持有限无序下局域化转变的存在。

Abstract: Many-body localization is often pictured as Anderson localization on the graph of many-body configurations. Strongly correlated disorder and a dense net of loops, however, make this graph hard to analyze. We introduce a local disordered spin-1 chain in which a single domain wall explores an exact binary tree of configurations. Random fields create strongly correlated disorder on this tree, as in generic interacting systems, while the absence of loops keeps the problem tractable. We rigorously bound the spreading of the domain wall at strong disorder, and efficient numerics on exponentially large trees indicate a localization transition at finite disorder strength.

</details>


### [270] [Connectivity from activity in spiking neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21592)
*Albert J. Wakhloo,Sebastian Mizera*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文提出一个统一建模框架，用一组简单方程将循环神经网络（recurrent neural network）的突触连接与单细胞响应特性同神经相关性（neural correlations）联系起来，并据此从可测的高频相关统计中恢复网络连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究缺乏一个能将突触连接、单细胞响应特性与神经相关性统一起来的通用方程体系，且从神经活动反推网络连接（连接恢复）缺乏理论依据。

Method: 考虑处于平稳、异步状态（stationary, asynchronous state）的网络，框架涵盖广泛的突触滤波器（synaptic filters）、脉冲非线性（spiking nonlinearities）以及随机加结构化连接（random-plus-structured connectivity）；所得方程按单突触（monosynaptic）、双突触（disynaptic）及高阶路径分解。

Result: 利用该分解，可将两神经元间的有效单突触连接表示为实验可测的活动统计量，特别是其高频相关性（high-frequency correlations）的函数，从而表明可从神经活动恢复网络连接。

Conclusion: 该框架为连接与活动统计之间提供了简洁的解析关系，并支持基于高频相关的连接推断方法。

Abstract: We show that a wide range of recurrent neural network models obey a simple set of equations tying synaptic connectivity and single-cell response properties to neural correlations. Our modeling framework considers networks in a stationary, asynchronous state and contains a broad class of synaptic filters, spiking nonlinearities, and random-plus-structured connectivity. The resulting equations naturally decompose into contributions from monosynaptic, disynaptic, and higher order paths through the network. We leverage this decomposition to express the effective monosynaptic connection between two neurons in terms of experimentally measurable statistics of the activity, and in particular, their high-frequency correlations. In this way, we show that it is possible to recover network connectivity from neural activity.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [271] [Variational and geometric time-dependent density-functional theory of electronic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21431)
*Éric Cancès,Théo Duez,Jari van Gog,Asbjørn Bækgaard Lauritsen,Mathieu Lewin,Jakob Oldenburg,Julien Toulouse*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该工作以几何视角统一了变分（variational）与几何（geometric）两种含时密度泛函理论（TDDFT）表述，系统比较二者并给出几何TDDFT的多项精确条件及严格数学论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TDDFT的核心难题在于构造能重现指定含时电子密度的多电子波函数；该问题具有自然的几何解释，并由此导出至少两种不同表述，但二者关系此前缺乏系统澄清。

Method: 针对连续电子体系发展统一观点：变分表述以实值局域势（real-valued local potential）施加密度约束，几何表述以虚局域势（imaginary local potential）或等价厄米非局域势（Hermitian nonlocal potential）施加同一约束；构建相应含时Kohn-Sham方案，并给出涵盖因果性（causality）、时间反演对称性、均匀坐标缩放、平移不变性、能量与动量运动方程、单电子自相互作用（self-interaction）及绝热极限（adiabatic limit）的精确条件，同时附严格的泛函分析（functional analysis）论证与算子可逆性（invertibility）的充分条件。

Result: 建立了两种表述的统一框架，推导出一系列几何TDDFT的精确条件，并针对两表述所依赖的两个算子给出可逆性的充分条件，阐明了其背后的泛函分析微妙之处。

Conclusion: 统一视角厘清了变分TDDFT与几何TDDFT在数学与概念上的关系，并为几何TDDFT中近似方法的发展奠定基础。

Abstract: A central problem in time-dependent density-functional theory (TDDFT) is to construct a time-dependent many-electron wave function that reproduces a prescribed time-dependent electron density. This problem admits a natural geometric interpretation, which in turn leads to at least two distinct formulations of TDDFT. The first is the usual variational formulation of TDDFT, in which the density constraint is enforced with a real-valued local potential. The second is geometric TDDFT, introduced in recent works, in which the same density constraint is enforced with an imaginary local potential, or equivalently with a Hermitian nonlocal potential. In this work, we develop this unified viewpoint for continuous electronic systems and systematically compare the two formulations. We formulate the corresponding time-dependent Kohn-Sham schemes and derive a number of exact conditions for geometric TDDFT, including conditions associated with causality, time-reversal symmetry, uniform-coordinate scaling, translational invariance, energy and momentum equations of motion, one-electron self-interaction, and the adiabatic limit. The paper also contains a mathematical section where we provide a fully rigorous justification of some parts of the text. We particularly discuss the mathematical properties of two operators underlying the two formulations of TDDFT and establish sufficient conditions for their invertibility. We explain the functional-analytic subtleties underlying the geometric interpretation of TDDFT and the two formulations of TDDFT. This unified perspective clarifies the mathematical and conceptual relation between variational and geometric TDDFT and provides a foundation for the development of approximations within geometric TDDFT.

</details>


### [272] [Dissipative many-body theory of electron-phonon-photon systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18748)
*Zhenlin Zhang,Enrico Perfetto,Gianluca Stefanucci*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出 Keldysh-Lindblad 非平衡格林函数（nonequilibrium Green's function）框架，统一处理电子与量子化光子、声子场的相互作用及耗散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光腔（optical cavity）可调控量子材料，但辐射泄漏（radiative leakage）与振动弛豫（vibrational relaxation）要求将相互作用与耗散置于同等地位进行微观处理。

Method: 构建 Keldysh-Lindblad 非平衡格林函数形式，兼容破坏玻色子数守恒（boson-number-nonconserving）的哈密顿量及电子跃迁与玻色子过程耦合的耗散通道；推导混合电子-玻色子（electron-boson）图展开与 Kadanoff-Baym 方程，保留环境噪声、初始关联与相互作用诱导的记忆效应。

Result: 等时（equal-time）方程给出电子密度矩阵与玻色子协方差；作为示例，构造跃迁算符（jump operator）使价带填满、光子场为空的半导体态保持稳定；在平均场（mean-field）处理下该态为定态，但双时传播子（two-time propagator）表现出耗散展宽（dissipative broadening）。

Conclusion: 该框架为研究有损光腔（lossy cavity）与振动环境中的关联动力学（correlated dynamics）开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Optical cavities offer opportunities to control quantum materials, but radiative leakage and vibrational relaxation call for microscopic theories that treat interactions and dissipation on equal footing. We develop a Keldysh-Lindblad nonequilibrium Green's function framework for interacting electrons coupled to quantized photon and phonon fields. The formulation accommodates boson-number-nonconserving Hamiltonians and dissipative channels that combine electronic transitions with bosonic processes. We derive the mixed electron-boson diagrammatic expansion and the corresponding Kadanoff-Baym equations, retaining environmental noise, initial correlations, and interaction-induced memory. The equal-time equations govern the electronic density matrix and the bosonic covariance. As an illustration, we construct jump operators that preserve a semiconductor with filled valence bands and an empty photon field. Within a mean-field treatment, this state remains stationary although its two-time propagators exhibit dissipative broadening. The framework opens new avenues for studying correlated dynamics in lossy cavities and vibrational environments.

</details>


### [273] [Real-time dynamics with bead-Fourier path integrals. III. Atomistic condensed-phase simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18749)
*Nathan London,Mohammad R. Momeni*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本工作首次将珠-傅里叶路径积分（bead-Fourier path integrals, BF-PIs）框架应用于液态水与六方冰的原子级模拟，证明仅添加单个傅里叶分量即可将所需珠子数减少4倍（水）与8倍（冰），并将模拟成本最高降低5.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确描述核量子效应（nuclear quantum effects, NQEs，含零点能与核隧穿）对含轻原子体系的结构、热力学与动力学性质预测至关重要，但结合昂贵电子结构计算时成本过高，因此需要降低路径积分模拟开销的方案。

Method: 沿用前作提出的BF-PI框架（前作中已发展BF centroid molecular dynamics与BF ring polymer MD），本文将其首次实现并应用于实际原子级体系，以液态体相水与六方冰为测试对象，通过减少力计算与简正模式变换次数来评估加速效果。

Result: 在路径中加入单个傅里叶分量后，液态水与冰达到参考路径积分（path integral, PI）模拟精度所需的珠子数分别减少4倍与8倍；相比标准PI，模拟成本最高降低5.5倍。

Conclusion: BF-PI框架在真实凝聚相体系中的可行性与加速潜力得到验证，为在保持参考精度的前提下降低含核量子效应的原子级模拟成本提供了实用途径。

Abstract: Accurately accounting for nuclear quantum effects (NQEs), including zero-point energy and nuclear tunneling, is essential for a reliable prediction of the structural, thermodynamic, and dynamical properties of chemical systems, particularly those involving light atoms. However, incorporating NQEs could render simulations of large-scale chemical systems computationally prohibitive, particularly when combined with expensive electronic-structure calculations. One potential avenue for reducing cost is to use bead-Fourier path integrals (BF-PIs). In Paper I, we introduced the BF centroid molecular dynamics (BF-CMD) method, in which BF-PIs were used to compute an effective potential for CMD, and in Paper II, we introduced BF ring polymer MD (BF-RPMD), taking the BF-PI and using it for approximate real-time dynamics. Both papers featured a series of model systems and were able to reduce the number of beads needed to converge dynamical properties by a factor of 2 to 4, depending on the system. In this work, we report the first implementation and application of the BF-PI framework to atomistic simulations using liquid bulk water and hexagonal ice as test cases. As in our previous papers, we find that adding just a single Fourier component to the path yields a 4- and 8-fold reduction in the number of beads while achieving results that agree with reference PI simulations for liquid water and ice, respectively. We also demonstrate that we can reduce simulation costs by up to 5.5x compared to standard PIs by reducing the number of force calculations and normal-mode transformations.

</details>


### [274] [Hydration-induced local diffusivity heterogeneity modulates aqueous $H_2+OH$ reaction kinetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19422)
*Ming-Gen Li,Shi-Yuan Zhang,Alessio Zaccone*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 以水合诱导的空间关联扩散系数涨落修正有效摩擦，可解释水相反应 \(H_2 + OH \to H + H_2O\) 的经典 Kramers 速率被高估约一个数量级的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室温下用均匀溶剂摩擦（homogeneous solvent friction）计算的 Kramers 反应速率（Kramers reaction rate）比水相反应 \(H_2 + OH \to H + H_2O\) 的实测速率高约一个数量级，理论与实验之间存在显著偏差。

Method: 引入空间关联的水合诱导扩散系数涨落（spatially correlated hydration-induced diffusivity fluctuations），据此评估其对有效摩擦（effective friction）的增强作用，并基于水合壳层（hydration-shell）性质进行速率估算。

Result: 该类涨落增大了有效摩擦并抑制预测的逃逸速率（escape rate），使理论速率更接近实验值；且这种抑制随温度升高而减弱。

Conclusion: 局部溶剂不均匀性（local solvent heterogeneity）与活化水相反应动力学（activated aqueous reaction kinetics）存在关联，为水相反应速率的理论修正提供了依据。

Abstract: At room temperature, a Kramers reaction rate calculated with homogeneous solvent friction exceeds the measured rate of the aqueous reaction $H_2 + OH -> H + H_2O$ by about an order of magnitude. We show that spatially correlated hydration-induced diffusivity fluctuations increase effective friction and suppress the predicted escape rate. Estimates based on hydration-shell properties bring the theoretical rate closer to experiment. The suppression weakens with increasing temperature, linking local solvent heterogeneity to activated aqueous reaction kinetics.

</details>


### [275] [Formulation of Brownian chain molecular dynamics and the path-resolved quasi-density operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19645)
*Motoyuki Shiga*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出路径分辨准密度算符（path-QDO）表示，将质心分子动力学（centroid molecular dynamics）的QDO构造推广到完整离散化虚时路径，在细离散极限下对两个位置对角可观测量给出Kubo变换关联函数的精确表达，并将布朗链分子动力学（BCMD）解释为该路径变量上精确约化传播的唯象、时间齐次马尔可夫（Markov）近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BCMD作为近似量子振动动力学的实用路径积分（path-integral）方法，其运动方程由质心变量的牛顿动力学与非质心路径变量的过阻尼布朗动力学组合而成，但缺乏严格的算符层次理论基础，且确定性传播中的伪哈密顿（pseudo-Hamiltonian）误差需可控。

Method: 构建path-QDO表示，将QDO构造扩展至完整离散化虚时路径以推导Kubo变换关联函数的精确表达式；同时提出并验证多时间步（multiple-time-step）算法，通过分离布朗刷新间隔与内部积分时间步控制伪哈密顿误差；在一维模型及q-TIP4P/F冰、常温水与热水体系上进行测试。

Result: 对两个位置对角可观测量，path-QDO在细离散极限下无需可观测线性假设即给出Kubo变换关联函数的精确表达式；多时间步算法经验证可有效控制伪哈密顿误差；获得q-TIP4P/F冰、常温水与热水的BCMD红外光谱（infrared spectra），并与其他近似量子动力学方法已发表结果进行比较。

Conclusion: path-QDO为BCMD提供了理论参照框架，表明BCMD是路径变量上精确约化传播的唯象时间齐次马尔可夫近似，但其生成元并非由path-QDO形式导出；所提多时间步算法与光谱计算表明该方法在分子与凝聚相量子振动动力学近似中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Brownian chain molecular dynamics (BCMD) [M. Shiga, J. Comput. Chem. 43, 1864 (2022)] was introduced as a practical path-integral-based approach to approximate quantum vibrational dynamics in molecular and condensed-phase systems. Its equations of motion combine Newtonian dynamics of the centroid variables with overdamped Brownian dynamics of the non-centroid path variables. Here, we introduce a path-resolved quasi-density-operator (path-QDO) representation by extending the QDO construction underlying centroid molecular dynamics to the full discretized imaginary-time path. For two position-diagonal observables, this representation gives an exact expression for the Kubo-transformed correlation function in the fine-discretization limit, without requiring either observable to be linear. BCMD can be viewed as a phenomenological, time-homogeneous Markov approximation to the exact reduced propagation in these path variables, although its generator is not derived from the path-QDO formulation. We also formulate and validate a multiple-time-step algorithm that allows pseudo-Hamiltonian errors in the deterministic propagation to be controlled by separating the Brownian refresh interval from the inner integration time step. We test the path-QDO representation on one-dimensional models and present BCMD infrared spectra of q-TIP4P/F ice, ambient water, and hot water, comparing them with published results from other approximate quantum-dynamics methods.

</details>


### [276] [GPRidge: Gaussian-process Accelerated Calculations of Minimum Energy Paths and First-order Saddle Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20591)
*Rohit Goswami,Maxim Masterov,Satish Kamath,Alejandro Pena-Torres,Andreas Lynge Vishart,Thomas Bligaard,Hannes Jonsson*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: GPRidge 是一个基于高斯过程（Gaussian process, GP）的库，用于加速最低能量路径与一阶鞍点的计算，并提供编译语言与 Python 绑定。在分子与周期性体系测试中，其能量-力评估次数较 CI-NEB、OCI-NEB 与二聚体方法显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最低能量路径（minimum energy path）与鞍点搜索方法（如 CI-NEB、OCI-NEB、二聚体方法）需大量能量与力的评估，计算成本高；本文旨在以 GP 代理模型减少评估次数并提高鞍点验证的可靠性。

Method: 采用评估控制器（evaluation controller）以 GP 预测迭代调整原子笛卡尔坐标（Cartesian coordinates），拟合并传递 GP 能量面与实测能量、力至鞍点局部精修；分子体系用联合逆距离 GP（joint inverse-distance GP），周期性体系用局部 GP 残差（local GP residuals）。接受判据为实测力收敛且独立 Hessian 仅一个负本征值，出现第二个负本征值则触发逃逸并重新精修。

Result: 在匹配的 Baker-Chan 反应上，GPRidge 的能量-力评估次数分别比 CI-NEB 和 OCI-NEB 少 7.8 倍与 7.0 倍，含验证的 CI-NEB 时间减少 41%；20 个 Birkholz-Schlegel 鞍点全部通过独立 index-one 检验（二聚体方法为 17 个），且在那 17 个反应上 GP 二聚体阶段评估次数少 6.4 倍；72 条 Pt(111) 路径全部满足 OptBench 力判据，七聚体与岛重排的中位评估次数为 30 与 23，而 CI-NEB 为 778 与 443。

Conclusion: GPRidge 通过 GP 代理与严格的鞍点验证流程，在分子反应与周期性表面重排问题中均大幅降低评估成本，并提升鞍点指数一（index-one）检验的通过率。

Abstract: GPRidge, a Gaussian-process (GP) library for accelerating calculations of minimum energy paths and first-order saddle points with compiled-language and Python bindings, is presented. An evaluation controller uses GP predictions to iteratively adjust the Cartesian coordinates of the atoms. The fitted GP energy surface and measured energies and forces pass from the path calculation to local refinement of the saddle point. New evaluations improve the surrogate and check the candidate geometry. For the molecular calculations, acceptance requires measured-force convergence and an independent Hessian with only one negative eigenvalue. A second negative eigenvalue triggers an escape and renewed refinement. The controller supports a joint inverse-distance GP for molecules and local GP residuals on periodic systems. Applications to the 24 Baker-Chan and 20 Birkholz-Schlegel molecular reactions using the Universal Model for Atoms (UMA-S-1.1), and to 72 Pt heptamer and island rearrangements on Pt(111) using a Morse potential, are presented. On matched Baker-Chan reactions, GPRidge uses 7.8- and 7.0-fold fewer energy-and-force evaluations than climbing-image nudged elastic band (CI-NEB) and off-path climbing-image nudged elastic band (OCI-NEB), respectively, reducing validation-inclusive CI-NEB time by 41%. All 20 Birkholz-Schlegel saddle points pass the independent index-one check, compared with 17 for the classical dimer method started from the same path maxima; on those 17 reactions, the GP dimer stage uses 6.4-fold fewer evaluations. All 72 Pt(111) paths meet the OptBench force criterion, with median evaluation counts of 30 and 23 for the heptamer and island problems, against 778 and 443 for CI-NEB.

</details>


### [277] [Solubility and Diffusion of Hydrogen Isotopes and Helium in Molten FLiBe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20801)
*Ayobami Daramola,Vladimir Martis,Graeme J. Ackland,Duc Nguyen-Manh*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 采用仅拟合氢的固定电荷经典势（fixed-charge classical potential）模拟FLiBe中氚的赋存形态与溶解度，发现T$_2$与TF均不在熔盐中解离，TF溶解度远高于T$_2$，从而在不假设解离的前提下解释渗透实验中的平方根压力依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熔盐FLiBe中氚可能以T$_2$或TF形式溶解，二者溶解度差异极大；渗透实验所观察到的平方根压力依赖（square-root pressure dependence）通常被当作解离（dissociation）证据，需要澄清其真实机制并定量给出各物种溶解度。

Method: 构建含原子氚、T$_2$、TF、氢化物离子T$^-$与氦的FLiBe固定电荷经典势（fixed-charge classical potential），仅拟合氢的周期性DFT（periodic DFT）结果；核按经典处理，故溶解度适用于所有氢同位素。宿主（host）模型复现实测密度与中子结构因子（neutron structure factor）。

Result: 宿主在液相线（liquidus）附近过于流动态；T$_2$与TF的计算溶解度与实验一致，且两者均不在熔体中解离。由于TF溶解度更高，除非盐强还原，溶解氚主要以TF存在；在恒定氧化还原电位（redox potential）下其浓度随T$_2$压力平方根变化，从而无需解离即可解释渗透结果。氦不改变T$_2$溶解度，气泡按亨利常数（Henry constant）吸收T$_2$，TF则在气泡表面富集。

Conclusion: 渗透实验中“接近HF溶解度＋平方根压力依赖”并不构成解离证据；TF主导的溶解机制即可自洽解释。扩散方面，T$_2$略快于实测值，TF与T$^-$比T$_2$慢约2–3倍。

Abstract: Tritium produced in molten 2LiF-BeF$_2$ (FLiBe) may dissolve as T$_2$ or as TF, which differ greatly in solubility. Permeation experiments give a solubility close to that of HF and a flux proportional to the square root of the T$_2$ pressure, which has usually been taken as evidence of dissociation. We have developed a fixed-charge classical potential for FLiBe containing atomic tritium, T$_2$, TF, the hydride ion T$^-$ and helium, fitted only to periodic DFT calculations for hydrogen. The nuclei are classical, so the solubilities apply to all hydrogen isotopes. The host reproduces the measured density and neutron structure factor but is too fluid close to the liquidus. The calculated solubilities of T$_2$ and TF agree with experiment, and neither molecule dissociates in the melt. Because TF is much more soluble, it holds most of the dissolved tritium unless the salt is strongly reducing, and at constant redox potential its concentration varies with the square root of the T$_2$ pressure, which accounts for the permeation results without dissociation. Helium does not change the solubility of T$_2$: a helium bubble in the melt takes up T$_2$ as its Henry constant predicts, while TF is enriched at the bubble surface. T$_2$ diffuses somewhat faster than measured, and TF and T$^-$ two to three times more slowly than T$_2$.

</details>


### [278] [Out-of-training properties in frozen interatomic potential embeddings are decodable and ordered by physical scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21116)
*Natalija Stepurko,Joonatan E. M. Laulainen*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该研究系统探测 ORB-v3 与 UMA-S 两种机器学习原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potentials, MLIPs）的冻结表征，在 154,875 个 Materials Project 晶体、九种性质上评估其跨层、跨读出与跨等变通道（equivariant channels）的预测能力与可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIPs 仅以能量、力、应力训练，但其冻结表征可预测其他材料性质，此潜力缺乏系统评估；需明确表征在何种深度、读出方式与等变通道下最有效，以及预训练与等变性的真实贡献。

Method: 对 ORB-v3 与 UMA-S 逐层、逐读出、逐等变通道探测九种性质，其中五种超出训练目标；设置随机权重对照（random-weight controls）与匹配模型对照；考察线性探针、最近邻预测、XGBoost 基线及跨模型嵌入的线性变换迁移。

Result: 两模型在所有目标上均优于仅成分描述符（composition-only descriptors）；最优深度因性质而异并与物理尺度排序一致；预训练收益强烈依赖性质；UMA-S 的旋转不变角向通道提升晶系分类，而全张量高分在旋转下消失，匹配模型对照未能将等变性单独归因；最近邻预测常接近 XGBoost，但材料性质并未形成明显聚类。

Conclusion: 经学习线性变换后，一个模型嵌入上训练的线性预测器基本可在另一模型嵌入上保持精度，即使两模型邻近原子结构不同；结果为冻结 MLIP 表征在材料预测中的复用提供指导。

Abstract: Machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs) are trained on energies, forces and stresses, yet their frozen representations can predict other materials properties. We probe ORB-v3 and UMA-S across layers, read-outs and equivariant channels for nine properties of 154,875 Materials Project crystals. Both models outperform composition-only descriptors on all targets, including five outside their training objectives; errors in physical units help assess potential screening applications. Optimal depth varies by property, consistent with a physical-scale ordering. Random-weight controls reveal strongly property-dependent benefits of pretraining. UMA-S's rotation-invariant angular channels improve crystal-system classification, while higher scores from its full tensor disappear under rotation; matched-model controls do not isolate equivariance as the explanation. Accuracy varies across different probes, but nearest-neighbour prediction often approaches XGBoost performance, even though materials do not form distinct clusters according to their properties. We also test whether a linear predictor trained on one model's embeddings can predict properties using the other model's embeddings after a learned linear transformation. Predictive accuracy is largely preserved, despite differences in which atoms the two models place close together. These results guide the reuse of frozen MLIP representations for materials prediction.

</details>


<div id='physics.soc-ph'></div>

# physics.soc-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [279] [Percolation under Edge-Reversal: Creation and Destruction of Strong Connectivity in Directed Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20717)
*Marián Boguñá,M. Ángeles Serrano*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究有向网络中每条边以概率 p 独立反转（reversal）时连通性的变化：推导入-出度联合分布（joint in-out-degree distribution）的精确变换，并指出仅凭变换后的度分布无法判定巨型强连通分量（giant strongly connected component）是否存在，进而提出双类型分支过程（two-type branching process）给出其生成或消失的解析判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际网络中边方向常存在变化或不确定性，而底层连接固定；已有做法仅依赖度分布（degree distribution）来判断渗流状态（percolation state），可能得出错误结论，因此需要刻画方向随机化对相互可达性（mutual reachability）的影响。

Method: 推导边反转下入-出度联合分布的精确变换及其在重复反转下的演化；针对有向配置模型（directed configuration model），通过分别追踪保持原方向与发生反转的边，构建双类型分支过程（two-type branching process），据此得到巨型强连通分量生成与破坏的显式解析判据（analytical criteria）。

Result: 由于反转不改变边所连接的顶点对，仅用变换后的度分布重建网络可能将系统置于错误的渗流状态；在 S. cerevisiae 转录调控网络（transcriptional regulation network）中随机化边方向显著增大最大强连通分量，符合生成判据；在 San Diego 有向道路网络（directed road network）中度矩满足破坏判据，最大强连通分量降至近零，但其下降点远低于配置模型预测的阈值。

Conclusion: 方向性无序（directional disorder）可在不改变底层连接的情况下增强或抑制全局连通性；该框架可用于评估相互可达性对边方向变化或不确定性的敏感性。

Abstract: We study how the connectivity of a directed network changes when each edge is independently reversed with probability $p$, while the underlying connections remain fixed. Reversal changes the in- and out-degrees, and we derive the exact transformation of the joint in--out-degree distribution and characterize its evolution under repeated reversal. However, the transformation does not rewire the network, and every edge still connects the same pair of vertices. As a result, the transformed degree distribution alone does not determine whether a giant strongly connected component exists, and a reconstruction based only on the transformed degrees can even place the network on the wrong percolation state. For the directed configuration model, we account for the preserved structure by separately tracking edges that retain or reverse their original direction, leading to a two-type branching process. From this formalism, we obtain explicit analytical criteria for the creation or destruction of a giant strongly connected component and illustrate these contrasting outcomes with empirical examples. Consistent with the predicted creation criterion, randomizing edge directions in a \emph{S. cerevisiae} transcriptional regulation network dramatically increases the size of the largest strongly connected component. In the San Diego directed road network, the degree moments satisfy the destruction criterion, and the largest strongly connected component decreases to virtually zero, although its decline occurs well below the predicted configuration-model threshold. These results provide a framework for assessing the sensitivity of mutual reachability to changes or uncertainty in edge directions and show that directional disorder can either enable or suppress global connectivity without changing the underlying connections.

</details>

# q-bio.QM [[Back]](#toc)

### [280] [Exact mean first-passage time and a Poisson-smoothing crossover in renewal shot noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18675)
*Yuxin Wang,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: 本文为指数标记更新射击噪声（exponentially marked renewal shot noise）推导了精确的有限阈值平均首次通过时间（MFPT）级数，并将其重写为辅助泊松指标上的平均，揭示了短时间幂律等待时间密度下 κ=1/2 的交叉现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有高阈值乘积近似（product approximations）在何种单一指标处取值能以趋于零的相对误差保持均值，此前尚不明确；而阈值穿越依赖于入射脉冲与先前到达残差信号的相互作用，需要更严格的刻画。

Method: 推导指数标记更新射击噪声的精确有限阈值 MFPT 级数，并重铸为辅助泊松指标上的平均；在等待时间密度呈短时幂律行为 w(t)∼ct^{κ−1} 时分析主贡献偏移与平均核涨落宽度的尺度竞争，并提出移动鞍点近似（moving-saddle approximation）。

Result: 主级数贡献相对泊松中心偏移量级为 x^{1−κ}，平均核涨落宽度量级为 √x（x=λb），二者竞争在 κ=1/2 处产生交叉：该边界以上确定性指标乘积渐近精确，临界处需有限乘性修正，以下则出现增长的对数偏差；移动鞍点近似在一般严格正、有限均值等待时间下亦给出渐近精确的 MFPT 比值。Gamma、Weibull 与广义 Gamma 数值对比验证了各近似区间及有限阈值精度。

Conclusion: 研究给出了 MFPT 的精确有限阈值基准，并表明短间隔更新统计（short-gap renewal statistics）决定了高阈值近似渐近精度的成立条件。

Abstract: Threshold crossings in exponentially relaxing shot noise depend on the interplay between incoming impulses and the residual signal left by earlier arrivals. For renewal input, existing high-threshold product approximations of the mean first-passage time (MFPT) leave open when evaluation at a single index preserves the mean with vanishing relative error. Here we derive an exact finite-threshold MFPT series for exponentially marked renewal shot noise and recast it as an average over an auxiliary Poisson index. When the waiting-time density exhibits short-time power-law behavior, $w(t)\sim ct^{κ-1}$, the dominant series contributions shift from the Poisson center by an amount of order $x^{1-κ}$, whereas the averaging kernel has a fluctuation width of order $\sqrt{x}$, with $x=λb$. The competition between these scales yields a crossover at $κ=1/2$, where the deterministic-index product is asymptotically exact in ratio above this boundary, requires a finite multiplicative correction at criticality, and exhibits a growing logarithmic discrepancy below it. A moving-saddle approximation also yields an asymptotically exact MFPT ratio under general strictly positive, finite-mean waiting times. Numerical comparisons across Gamma, Weibull, and generalized-Gamma renewal laws illustrate the approximation regimes and their finite-threshold accuracy. Together, these results provide an exact finite-threshold benchmark for the mean first-passage time and reveal how short-gap renewal statistics determine the asymptotic accuracy of high-threshold approximations.

</details>


<div id='physics.med-ph'></div>

# physics.med-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [281] [A Soft Sponge-Pillar Electrode Platform for Motion-Tolerant Ambulatory EEG and Sleep Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20964)
*Chansoo Kim,Weilun Li,Ryan Andersen,Yiheng Zhang,Shuoyan Liu,Junyi Zhao,Yuelin Li,Zhongyu Luo,Josephine Lau,Yang Huang,Diane Bracken,Matthew Singh,ShiNung Ching,Ben Julian A. Palanca,Yong Wang,Shantanu Chakrabartty,Chuan Wang*

Main category: physics.med-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种软质可穿戴海绵柱状脑电（electroencephalogram, EEG）电极，采用炭黑-聚二甲基硅氧烷（carbon black-polydimethylsiloxane, CB-PDMS）双层复合结构，兼具绕发入颅稳定接触与舒适低阻抗特性，可在运动状态下实现长期脑电记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期可穿戴电生理记录在临床与真实场景中均具重要价值，心电（electrocardiogram, ECG）与肌电（electromyogram, EMG）已有大量可穿戴方案，但多数脑电测量仍要求受试者保持静止并佩戴刚性、不舒适的设备，因此亟需一种舒适、运动耐受的长期脑电电极。

Method: 电极由两层CB-PDMS复合材料构成：较硬的实心基底用于绕过头发、稳定接触头皮，多孔海绵尖端保证舒适性、可靠接触与低皮肤-电极阻抗；采用糖模板法（sugar templating process）制备、无需微加工（microfabrication）步骤，微孔海绵结构模量低，配合导电凝胶可显著增大与皮肤的接触面积。

Result: 在10 Hz下接触阻抗低至$1.62 \times 10^{4}\,Ω$，优于传统凝胶电极，且凝胶稳定性可维持超过18小时；电极可集成于头带、耳机、棒球帽等可穿戴形态，在运动过程中实现脑电记录，信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio, SNR）达16.46 dB。

Conclusion: 海绵柱状电极兼具舒适性、稳定性与长期采集能力，可作为下一代可穿戴脑电系统（wearable EEG systems）的候选平台，支持受试者在行走等动态状态及自然场景下的脑信号采集。

Abstract: Long-term wearable electrophysiological recording systems are essential for both clinical and real-world environments. While a wide range of wearable devices have been reported for electrocardiogram (ECG) and electromyogram (EMG) recording applications, most electroencephalography (EEG) measurements still require subjects to remain stationary while wearing rigid and uncomfortable equipment. Here, a soft and wearable sponge pillar EEG electrode is presented for long-term and motion-tolerant EEG recording. The electrode is composed of two layers of a carbon black (CB)-polydimethylsiloxane (PDMS) composite, where a firmer solid base assists in bypassing hair, enabling stable access to the scalp, while a porous sponge tip ensures comfort, reliable contact, and low skin-electrode impedance. Fabricated via a sugar templating process without microfabrication steps, the microporous sponge structure offers low modulus and significantly enhances the contact area with the skin when loaded with conductive gel. This allows the electrode to achieve a lower contact impedance of $1.62 \times 10^{4}\,Ω$ at 10 Hz, outperforming conventional gelled electrodes while maintaining gel stability for over 18 hours. With the electrodes integrated into wearable form factors such as headbands, headphones, and baseball caps, EEG signal recording during motion have been demonstrated, achieving a signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) of 16.46 dB. These features establish the sponge pillar electrode as a promising platform for next generation wearable EEG systems, enabling comfortable, stable, and prolonged brain signal acquisition on ambulatory subjects and in naturalistic environments.

</details>

### [282] [Sensitivity of the SHiP experiment at CERN to sterile-neutrino-induced oscillations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18824)
*SHiP Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: SHiP 探测器的散射与中微子探测器（SND）及其下游约 70 m 处的量能器系统构成双基线（two-baseline）实验，可对惰性中微子（sterile neutrino）诱导的振荡开展近/远消失分析；基于 Geant4 与 Genie 模拟给出 95% CL 灵敏度预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用 SHiP 装置已有的两个探测站（SND 与量能器）在空间上的间隔，将其重新解释为双基线中微子振荡实验，以探测惰性中微子混合参数，无需额外装置即可拓展物理灵敏度。

Method: 由 Geant4 对 Genie 产生的事例进行模拟，提取两个站点对电子型与缪子型中微子带电流（charged-current, CC）相互作用的能量响应，以及电子型 CC 事例与中性流（neutral-current）π⁰ 本底的区分能力，并纳入通量形状 20% 与远/近归一化 1% 的不确定度进行近/远消失分析。

Result: 在 6×10²⁰ 质子打靶（protons on target）条件下，95% CL 中位灵敏度为：缪子通道 |U_μ4|² ≈ 4×10⁻⁹（质量约 1 eV 量级），电子通道 |U_e4|² ≈ 3×10⁻⁹；其中缪子通道结果在约 0.1–1 eV 质量区间优于现有 ν_μ 消失实验限制。

Conclusion: 基线 SHiP 探测器配置可同时承担惰性中微子双基线消失测量，并在缪子通道对现有 ν_μ 消失限制形成有意义的竞争或改进。

Abstract: The SHiP experiment, approved for the high-intensity beam-dump facility in the ECN3 hall at the CERN SPS in the North Area, will record neutrino interactions both in its scattering and neutrino detector (SND) and, about \SI{70}{m} further downstream, in its calorimeter system. We show that the baseline SHiP detector configuration therefore forms a two-baseline experiment for oscillations induced by a sterile neutrino. From a \textsc{Geant4} simulation of \textsc{Genie}-generated interactions we derive the energy response of both stations to $\nue$ and $\numu$ charged-current (CC) interactions and the separation of $\nue$~CC events from the neutral-current $\pizero$ background, and fold them into a near/far disappearance analysis with a $20\%$ flux-shape uncertainty and a $1\%$ far/near normalisation uncertainty. For $6\times10^{20}$ protons on target, the projected $95\%$~CL median sensitivities are $\Umufour\simeq\UmuLimit$ at $\mfour\simeq\UmuLimitMass$~eV and $\Uefour\simeq\UeLimit$ at $\mfour\simeq\UeLimitMass$~eV. In the muon channel this lies below existing $\numu$-disappearance limits for $\DnineLow\lesssim\mfour\lesssim\DnineHigh$~eV.

</details>


### [283] [Quantum-correlation of neutral charmed mesons at BESIII](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18944)
*Shenghui Zeng*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII在ψ(3770)处积累的20.3 fb⁻¹数据及4.13–4.23 GeV能区数据，利用量子关联D D̄对报告多个D介子衰变道强相位差的新改进测量，并首次观测到C-偶D D̄对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子关联的D D̄对可直接获取D⁰与D̄⁰衰变间的强相位差（strong-phase differences），而该量是CKM角γ与粲介子混合（charm mixing）的关键输入，因而需要更大样本与更精确的测量。

Method: 利用BESIII在ψ(3770)共振处积累的20.3 fb⁻¹积分亮度数据（该处中性D D̄对处于C-奇态），并结合4.13–4.23 GeV质心能量数据，按伴随光子数区分C-偶或C-奇D D̄对，发展出新方法来研究D D̄量子关联（quantum correlations）。

Result: 给出D→K_{S,L}π⁺π⁻、D→K⁺K⁻π⁺π⁻、D→K⁻π⁺π⁺π⁻与D→K⁻π⁺π⁰衰变中强相位差的新及改进测量，并测量D→K⁺K⁻π⁺π⁻的CP-偶分数（CP-even fraction）；同时首次观测到C-偶D D̄对，并给出D→K⁻π⁺衰变强相位差的新测量。

Conclusion: BESIII的大统计量ψ(3770)数据与C-偶/奇量子关联新方法，显著扩展了对D介子强相位差和CP-偶分数的测量，为γ角和粲物理提供了更精确的实验输入。

Abstract: Quantum-correlated $D\bar D$ pairs give direct access to the strong-phase differences between $D^0$ and $\bar D^0$ decays, which are essential inputs to the CKM angle $γ$ and charm mixing. The BESIII experiment has accumulated the largest data sample at the $ψ(3770)$ resonance, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $20.3\,\text{fb}^{-1}$. The neutral $D\bar D$ pairs produced at $ψ(3770)$ are in a $C$-odd state. We report new and improved measurements of the strong-phase differences in $D\rightarrow K_{S,L}π^+π^-$, $D\rightarrow K^+K^-π^+π^-$, $D\rightarrow K^-π^+π^+π^-$ and $D\rightarrow K^-π^+π^0$ decays, alongside a measurement of the $CP$-even fraction in $D\rightarrow K^+K^-π^+π^-$. Furthermore, we present a novel method that studies the $D\bar D$ quantum correlations using data collected at centre-of-mass energies from $4.13$ to $4.23\,$GeV, where the $D\bar D$ pairs are produced in $C$-even or $C$-odd states, according to the number of accompanying photons. This gives the first observation of $C$-even $D\bar D$ pairs and a new measurement of the strong-phase difference in $D\rightarrow K^-π^+$ decays.

</details>


### [284] [Cosmic ray muon seasonal variations with the ProtoDUNE-SP Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18997)
*DUNE Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用位于 CERN 中微子平台（Neutrino Platform）的 ProtoDUNE 单相探测器，基于 2018 年 12 月至 2020 年 7 月的单缪子事件，测得宇宙线缪子（cosmic ray muon）速率的季节性调制（seasonal modulation），相对无季节性变化零假设的显著性为 4 σ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙线缪子通量的时间依赖性（如季节性变化）是大气条件效应的敏感探针，而验证探测器能否在长时间尺度上稳定测量微小时间相关信号，对其物理潜力评估具有意义。

Method: 采用 ProtoDUNE 单相探测器（single-phase detector）记录的单缪子（single-muon）事例，对所收集的运行数据进行分析，将缪子速率表述为时间的函数并检验其季节性调制。

Result: 观测到缪子速率的季节性调制，显著性达 4 σ；缪子速率在冬季达到最大值，与其他具有低缪子能量阈值（muon-energy threshold）的地面实验测量结果一致。

Conclusion: 该测量得益于该探测器技术的整体优异性能，表明此类探测器能够在长时间尺度上稳定测量如宇宙缪子速率季节性变化等微小的时间相关物理信号。

Abstract: We present the measurement of the cosmic ray muon seasonal variation using single-muon events with the ProtoDUNE single-phase detector located at the CERN Neutrino Platform, using data collected from December 2018 to July 2020. Cosmic ray muon runs recorded during this period are analyzed to determine the muon rate as a function of time. The results show a seasonal modulation of the muon rate, with a significance of 4 σ relative to the null hypothesis of no seasonal variation. The observed muon rate reaches a maximum during the winter, consistent with measurements from other surface experiments with a low muon-energy threshold. This measurement is possible due to the overall excellent performance of this detector technology and serves as a clear demonstration that such a detector can stably measure small, time-dependent physical signals such as seasonal variations in cosmic muon rates over long timescales.

</details>


### [285] [High-Statistics Study of Antiproton Annihilation at Rest on Argon in a LArTPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19401)
*M. Tanaka,K. Yorita,S. Arai,K. Ishikawa,Y. Utsumi,C. Watanabe,Y. Yano,T. Akaishi,K. Aoyama,S. Arai,T. Aramaki,T. Hashimoto,G. Karagiorgi,M. Kimura,Y. Kimura,J. Leyva,Y. Ma,J. Mitchell,T. Nanamura,H. Odaka,M. Sasaki,S. Takashima,S. Tandon,R. Tatsumi,A. Barnett,N. Cannady,C. Earley,K. Sakai,J. Zeng*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报告 J-PARC T98 实验中利用单相液氩时间投影室（LArTPC）对静止反质子-氩湮灭的高统计研究，采集超过 $10^5$ 个湮灭事例并结合 Geant4 模拟，比较三种强子相互作用生成器的预言，为液氩中低能反质子相互作用建模提供高统计实验基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在为强子相互作用模型提供基准检验，并为未来基于 LArTPC 的反物质搜寻（如 GRAMS）奠定实验与计算基础。

Method: 采用单相液氩时间投影室获取超过 $10^5$ 个静止 $\bar{p}\text{--}\text{Ar}$ 湮灭事例及配套刻度数据，并开发基于 Geant4 的模拟框架以建模次级束流输运与探测器响应；随后将测量的湮灭拓扑、局域能量分布与空间簇多重数同 Fritiof、INCL++、GiBUU 三种生成器的预言进行对比。

Result: 三种模型均能再现总能量沉积的整体特征；其中 GiBUU 对湮灭顶点附近局域能量沉积及较大半径处空间簇多重数给出最一致的描述，反映次级核子敲出与介质内 π 介子吸收的关键作用。

Conclusion: 该工作为液氩中低能反质子相互作用的建模提供了高统计实验基准，并展示了实验数据与模拟框架相结合用于强子相互作用模型检验的有效性。

Abstract: This paper reports a high-statistics study of antiproton--argon ($\bar{p}\text{--}\text{Ar}$) annihilation at rest in the J-PARC T98 experiment using a single-phase Liquid Argon Time Projection Chamber (LArTPC). A dataset comprising over $10^{5}$ annihilation events was acquired together with comprehensive calibration data. In parallel, a dedicated Geant4-based simulation framework was developed to model the secondary beam transport and detector response. Together, these experimental and computational resources provide a foundation for benchmarking hadronic interaction models and for future LArTPC-based antimatter searches, such as GRAMS. As an initial application of this framework, we compare the measured annihilation topologies, localized energy profiles, and spatial cluster multiplicities with predictions from three hadronic interaction generators: Fritiof, INCL++, and GiBUU. While all models reproduce the overall features of the total energy deposition, GiBUU provides the most consistent empirical description of localized energy deposition near the annihilation vertex and spatial cluster multiplicities at larger radii, reflecting the key roles of secondary nucleon knockout and in-medium pion absorption. These results provide a high-statistics experimental benchmark for modeling low-energy antiproton interactions in liquid argon.

</details>
