Zhu,R. Žlebčík*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 首次观测到粲奇异介子 $D_{s1}(2460)^{+}$ 的辐射衰变 $D_{s1}(2460)^{+} \to D_{s}^{*+}\gamma$，显著性超过 10 个标准差，并测得分支比之比为 $0.74 \pm 0.05\,(\text{stat.}) \pm 0.06\,(\text{syst.})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该辐射衰变此前未被观测到；其测量结果可用于区分关于粲奇异介子（charm-strange meson）$D_{s1}(2460)^{+}$ 内部结构的不同理论模型（theoretical models）。

Method: 利用 Belle 与 Belle II 探测器在 KEKB 与 SuperKEKB 非对称能量 $e^{+}e^{-}$ 对撞机上采集的积分亮度（integrated luminosity）分别为 980 与 572 $\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ 的数据样本，在 $e^{+}e^{-} \to c\bar{c}$ 产生过程中寻找信号。

Result: 以超过 10 个标准差（standard deviations）的显著性首次观测到 $D_{s1}(2460)^{+} \to D_{s}^{*+}\gamma$；测得分支比之比（branching-fraction ratio）$\mathcal{B}[D_{s1}(2460)^{+} \to D_{s}^{*+}\gamma]/\mathcal{B}[D_{s1}(2460)^{+} \to D_{s}^{+}\gamma] = 0.74 \pm 0.05\,(\text{stat.}) \pm 0.06\,(\text{syst.})$。

Conclusion: 该分支比之比为判别 $D_{s1}(2460)^{+}$ 内部结构的不同理论模型提供了实验依据。

Abstract: We report the first observation of the radiative decay $D_{s1}(2460)^{+} \to D_{s}^{*+} γ$ with a significance greater than 10 standard deviations. The signal is found in $e^{+}e^{-} \to c\bar{c}$ production using data samples with integrated luminosities of 980 and 572~$\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ collected by the Belle and Belle~II detectors operating at the KEKB and SuperKEKB asymmetric-energy $e^{+}e^{-}$ colliders, respectively. The branching-fraction ratio $\mathcal{B}[D_{s1}(2460)^{+} \to D_{s}^{*+} γ]/\mathcal{B}[D_{s1}(2460)^{+} \to D_{s}^{+} γ]$ is measured to be $0.74 \pm 0.05 ({\rm stat.}) \pm 0.06 ({\rm syst.)}$. This result helps discriminate between different theoretical models regarding the internal structure of the charm-strange meson $D_{s1}(2460)^{+}$.

</details>


### [287] [Hunting dark matter: the present and future of direct particle detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21052)
*Felix Kahlhoefer,Belina von Krosigk,Teresa Marrodán Undagoitia,Valentina Novati,María Luisa Sarsa*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述，系统梳理银河系晕中暗物质（dark matter）直接探测（direct detection）的现状与前景，涵盖理论框架、探测技术、2020年以来的实验结果及未来实验规划，并指出低阈值、本底甄别与中微子本底（neutrino-induced backgrounds）将成为下一代实验的核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质本质仍是粒子物理与宇宙学（cosmology）的重大未解问题；直接探测实验灵敏度虽大幅提升，但迄今尚无确凿探测结果，因此需要对该领域现状与未来方向进行系统综述。

Method: 综述性方法：介绍解释实验结果的理论框架（核散射与电子散射、集体激发（collective excitations）、粒子吸收、时间与方向依赖信号），评述各类探测技术的原理、优势与局限，以及刻度（calibration）与本底抑制策略，并汇总2020年以来的实验进展与在建/拟建探测器。

Result: 汇总了核反冲与电子反冲（nuclear and electron recoil）搜寻、年度调制（annual modulation）测量与方向性探测（directional detection）等方面的近期结果，并涵盖从大规模惰性液体（noble-liquid）实验到针对亚GeV暗物质的低阈值技术等未来装置。

Conclusion: 下一代实验面临更低能量阈值、更完善的本底理解与甄别、以及日益重要的中微子本底等挑战；探测技术的多样性与互补性（complementarity）对探索暗物质参数空间和确认潜在信号本质仍不可或缺。

Abstract: The nature of dark matter remains one of the major open questions in particle physics and cosmology. Direct detection experiments aim to identify dark matter particles through their interactions with ordinary matter, but despite substantial improvements in experimental sensitivity, no conclusive detection has been achieved. In this review, we provide a comprehensive overview of the present status and future prospects of direct particle detection, focusing on searches for dark matter particles from the Galactic halo. We introduce the theoretical framework for interpreting experimental results, including nuclear and electron scattering, collective excitations, particle absorption, and time- and direction-dependent signatures. We review the detection technologies employed in these searches, highlighting their operating principles, advantages, and limitations, as well as the calibration techniques and background mitigation strategies required to achieve high sensitivity. Particular emphasis is placed on experimental results reported since 2020, covering nuclear and electron recoil searches, annual modulation measurements, and directional detection. We also discuss experiments under construction and proposed future detectors, ranging from large-scale noble-liquid experiments to low-threshold technologies targeting sub-GeV dark matter. Finally, we examine the challenges that will shape the next generation of experiments, including the need for increasingly low energy thresholds, improved background understanding and discrimination, and the growing importance of neutrino-induced backgrounds. The diversity and complementarity of detector technologies will remain essential for exploring the dark matter parameter space and establishing the nature of any potential signal.

</details>


### [288] [Generating a simple silicon detector simulation with an open-weight large language model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21338)
*Charlie H R Bamber,Ming-Liang Wong*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该工作提出并演示了一种由大语言模型（LLM）结合 MCP 服务器提供的工具与自定义资源生成粒子探测器模拟（Geant4 蒙特卡罗模拟）的工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在软件开发中已广泛用于生成代码，但缺乏工具接入时无法执行模拟，且对 Geant4 等工具包的知识有限，因此需要外部资源与工具来支撑模拟任务的生成。

Method: 采用 Qwen3.6-27B 模型（Q4_K_M GGUF 量化），通过 MCP 服务器（MCP servers）提供自定义资源与工具，以文本提示方式生成粒子探测器模拟，并独立运行 50 次进行评估。

Result: 在 50 次独立运行中，有 42%（95% 置信区间 29%–56%）的运行产生了与参考模拟（reference simulation）在统计上一致的能量沉积分布（energy-deposit distributions）。

Conclusion: 结果构成概念验证（proof of concept）：在具备必要资源与工具的条件下，可用基于文本的提示由 LLM 生成蒙特卡罗（MC）模拟；更复杂的模拟任务可能需要示例代码与额外文档等更丰富的资源。

Abstract: Recent LLM developments have seen AI-generated code become commonplace in the software development industry. Without tool access, LLMs cannot execute simulations, and their knowledge of toolkits such as Geant4 is limited. We demonstrate here, a detector simulation workflow with a Qwen3.6-27B model with Q4_K_M GGUF quantisation together with custom resources and tools served via MCP servers to generate a particle detector simulation. In 50 independent runs, 42(29--56)% at 95% CI produced energy-deposit distributions statistically consistent with a reference simulation, measured against a reference simulation. These results provide a proof of concept that MC simulations can be generated using text-based prompts with an LLM, given the necessary resources and tools. More extensive resources, such as example code and additional documentation, are likely to be required for more complex simulation tasks.

</details>


### [289] [Unraveling QCD dynamics via charm-tagged jets with ALICE at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21491)
*Emma Yeats*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该报告基于D⁰介子标记喷注（D⁰-meson-tagged jets），通过能量-能量关联函数、三轴定义间角差与喷注角动量（jet angularities）三类测量，系统探测粲夸克（charm quark）辐射模式及其质量与色荷效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 喷注子结构（jet substructure）研究可用于检验量子色动力学（Quantum Chromodynamics, QCD）并提供研究强子化（hadronization）机制的差分途径；QCD预言喷注辐射模式依赖于起始部分子（parton）的质量与色荷，部分子簇射（parton showers）对夸克与胶子的卡西米尔因子（Casimir factors）及死锥效应（dead-cone effect）敏感。

Method: 报告给出三组D⁰介子标记喷注测量：首次测量D⁰标记喷注的能量-能量关联函数（Energy-Energy Correlator, EEC）；测量喷注内部三种轴定义之间的角度差异；以及测量喷注角动量（jet angularities），即喷注成分的加权角分布。

Result: EEC可揭示重夸克QCD辐射的细节结构，从而区分粲夸克簇射中的微扰与非微扰效应；三种轴定义对广角软辐射（wide-angle soft radiation）具有不同灵敏度，可系统性探测粲夸克辐射模式；喷注角动量对部分子质量与卡西米尔因子相关效应敏感，且该灵敏度可通过调节广角辐射的权重来调控。

Conclusion: 上述三类可观测量构成检验QCD辐射预言与强子化机制的有力工具，能够针对重夸克喷注中的质量效应与色荷依赖提供互补的差分探针。

Abstract: The study of jet substructure provides tests of Quantum Chromodynamics (QCD) and offers differential ways to study hadronization mechanisms. QCD predicts that jet radiation patterns depend on the mass and color charge of the initiating parton. Parton showers are sensitive to the different Casimir factors of quarks and gluons, and to the parton mass through the dead-cone effect. In this talk, we present three sets of $\textrm{D}^0$-meson-tagged jet measurements. First, we present the first measurement of the Energy-Energy Correlator (EEC) for $\textrm{D}^0$-meson-tagged jets. This observable reveals the detailed structure of QCD radiation from heavy quarks, allowing for a distinction between perturbative and non-perturbative effects in charm-quark showers. We then discuss measurements of the angular differences between three different axis definitions inside the jets. The three definitions offer different sensitivity to wide-angle soft radiation and systematically probe the radiation pattern from charm quarks. The final measurement regards jet angularities, which characterize the weighted angular distribution of the jet constituents. These powerful observables probe the parton shower, with sensitivity to parton-mass and Casimir-factor related effects that can be tuned by adjusting the importance of wide-angle radiation.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [290] [Asymptotic efficiency of tests for quantum statistical models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19141)
*Rathindra Nath Karmakar,Samriddha Lahiry,Koichi Yamagata*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该文借助量子局部渐近正态性（quantum local asymptotic normality）与渐近表示定理，为量子假设检验建立渐近效率理论，给出无偏类单边与双边检验的精确 nuisance-maximin 功效上界，并证明标量对称对数导数（scalar symmetric logarithmic derivative）检验及两阶段自适应方案可达该上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典统计中局部渐近正态性（local asymptotic normality）为最优检验功效刻画与渐近有效程序构造提供了一般框架，但推广至量子统计模型时，观测量的非对易性（noncommutativity）与对量子测量（quantum measurements）的优化需求带来额外困难。

Method: 利用量子局部渐近正态性与渐近表示定理（asymptotic representation theorem），将经典—量子高斯平移实验（Gaussian shift experiments）归约为经典检验问题；该归约由可同时处理对易与非对易高斯坐标的量子 Hunt–Stein 论证（quantum Hunt–Stein argument）加以论证，并构造了两阶段自适应（two-stage adaptive）程序。

Result: 在无偏类（unbiased class）内建立了一边与双边检验的尖锐 nuisance-maximin 功效界；在适当渐近不变性（asymptotic invariance）条件下，该界可迁移至原始量子实验，并由标量对称对数导数检验达到；两阶段自适应构造在未知参考参数情形下亦达到相同局部渐近功效。应用于光滑谱泛函（smooth spectral functionals）检验时，区分了插件加权（plug-in weighting）造成的损失与最佳谱统计量与标量得分程序之间的差距，并刻画了相应方差相等的条件。

Conclusion: 量子局部渐近正态性框架可有效刻画量子检验的渐近效率，所导出的功效界具有尖锐性与可达性；对谱泛函检验的分析表明，弱 Schur 采样插件检验（weak Schur sampling plug-in test）与最优加权线性统计量的表现差异可分解为两个来源，并可由显式例子说明。

Abstract: In classical statistics, local asymptotic normality provides a general framework for characterizing optimal testing power and constructing asymptotically efficient procedures. Extending this framework to quantum statistical models introduces additional challenges arising from the noncommutativity of observables and the need to optimize over quantum measurements. In this paper, we develop a theory of asymptotic efficiency for quantum hypothesis testing using quantum local asymptotic normality and the asymptotic representation theorem. We establish sharp nuisance-maximin power bounds for one-sided tests and two-sided tests within the unbiased class by reducing the corresponding classical--quantum Gaussian shift experiments to classical testing problems. This reduction is justified through a quantum Hunt--Stein argument that accommodates both commuting and noncommuting Gaussian coordinates. Under suitable asymptotic invariance conditions, the resulting bounds transfer to the original quantum experiments and are attained by scalar symmetric logarithmic derivative tests. We further develop a two-stage adaptive construction that achieves the same local asymptotic power without knowledge of the reference parameter.
  As an application, we study testing of smooth spectral functionals of quantum states. We compare scalar symmetric logarithmic derivative tests with the weak Schur sampling plug-in test and with optimally weighted linear statistics of its fluctuations. This comparison distinguishes loss from the plug-in weighting from a gap between the best such spectral statistic and the scalar score procedure. We characterize equality of the corresponding variances and illustrate the two gaps through explicit examples.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.soft'></div>

# cond-mat.soft [[Back]](#toc)

### [291] [Liquid-liquid criticality in a fluid whose two structures favor mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18737)
*Marcio Pazetti*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文基于成对势的第二维里系数构造范德华闭合形式，推导密度弛豫后的闭式稳定性条件，指出当局部结构在尺寸或吸引上不同时，液-液分离可在“有利于混合”的非理想性下发生，突破了传统双态多形性描述的前提假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液体多形性（liquid polyamorphism）的通用双态描述通常要求存在反对混合的非理想性（non-ideality）以驱动液-液分离，该条件隐含假设底层流体的自由能与结构序无关；当局部结构在尺寸或吸引强度上存在差异时，这一假设不再成立。

Method: 采用内部状态同时决定接触距离与势阱深度的成对势（pair potential），以其第二维里系数（second virial coefficient）为基础构建范德华闭合（van der Waals closure），在密度弛豫后推导闭式稳定性条件（closed-form stability condition），并对34组参数进行考察及共存曲线计算。

Result: 该条件在结构尺寸与吸引相同时可还原熟知结论，但在两者不同时可在液体内部变号；34组参数中有10组出现由液体偏好的临界态需要“有利于混合”的非理想性，其中一例的共存曲线终止于正压下的预测临界点。吸引与排除体积（excluded volume）对密度-序耦合贡献不同，固定单占据格子上仅前者存留；去除排除体积贡献后，82个此类临界态中有58个恢复熟知条件。

Conclusion: 在该闭合框架内，液-液分离可在非理想性有利于混合的情形下发生，说明传统“必须反对混合”的判据并非普适，结构尺寸与排除体积效应是改变分离条件符号的关键因素。

Abstract: The generic two-state description of liquid polyamorphism requires a non-ideality opposing mixing for liquid-liquid separation. This condition assumes that the free energy of the underlying fluid is independent of the structural order, an assumption that fails when the local structures differ in size or attraction. We derive a closed-form stability condition after density relaxation for a van der Waals closure based on the second virial coefficient of a pair potential whose internal state determines both the contact distance and the well depth. The condition recovers the familiar result for structures identical in size and attraction but can change sign within the liquid when they differ. In ten of the thirty-four parameter sets examined, a critical state preferred by the liquid requires a non-ideality favoring mixing. For one such fluid, we construct the coexistence curve, which terminates at the predicted critical point at positive pressure. Attraction and excluded volume provide distinct contributions to the density-order coupling; only the former survives on a fixed lattice with single occupancy. Removing the excluded-volume contribution restores the familiar condition in 58 of the 82 such critical states found. Thus, within this closure, liquid-liquid separation can occur with a non-ideality favoring mixing.

</details>


### [292] [Interaction between fluids and Helfrich membranes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18808)
*Dennis Wörthmüller,Pierre Sens,Michele Castellana*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一种有限元方法（finite-element method），用于模拟流体与具有Helfrich弹性自由能的膜之间的相互作用，采用分区（partitioned）交错求解策略，能够处理微扰方法无法描述的大变形，并分析了附加质量不稳定性（added-mass instability）与鼓包形成的物理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于建立能够刻画流体与弹性膜耦合动力学的数值方法，突破微扰方法（perturbative methods）在大膜变形情形下的适用性限制。

Method: 采用有限元方法，基于分区（partitioned）策略，在交错时间步（staggered steps）中分别求解流体、膜与网格的状态；膜的弹性由Helfrich弹性自由能描述。

Result: 以弹性体膜（elastomer membrane）在气体经通道注入作用下鼓起的宏观算例验证了方法的预测能力，表明可描述微扰方法无法处理的大变形；同时刻画了方法的附加质量不稳定性（added-mass instability）这一局限。

Conclusion: 该分析可解释鼓包（bulge）形成的物理机制，其成因是弹性应力（elastic stresses）与膜边界条件（membrane boundary conditions）的共同作用。

Abstract: We propose a finite-element method to determine the dynamics of a fluid interacting with a membrane---a fluid layer equipped with a Helfrich elastic free energy [W. Helfrich, Z. Naturforschung C, 28, 693, (1973)]. Our analysis is based on a partitioned approach, where the state of the fluid, of the membrane, and of the mesh, are solved for at staggered steps. We show the predicting capabilities of the method by considering a macroscopic example of an elastomer membrane, which bulges as it interacts with air injected through a channel. We demonstrate that our method allows for describing large membrane deformations which are not accessible by perturbative methods. We discuss the limitations of our method, by characterizing its added-mass instability. Finally, we show how our analysis allows for understanding the physical mechanism of bulge formation, which is due to a combination of elastic stresses and membrane boundary conditions.

</details>


### [293] [Knitting the Ising Transition: Statistical Physics as a Tool for Stochastic Knit Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19317)
*Gabrielle Chang,Kafumi Akaho,Tomohiko G. Sano,Samuel Poincloux*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文提出一种“随机性设计（stochastic design）”概念验证：利用伊辛模型（Ising model）自旋构型作为拓扑指令，生成针织织物图案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纺织品设计多基于双周期、确定性图案，但手工与机器制造并不受周期性限制；作者希望借助非确定性物理过程作为原创图案的设计工具。

Method: 基于针织/反针（knit/purl）与自旋向上/向下（up/down spins）之间的二元类比，将不同温度下伊辛模型的自旋实现映射为织物的缠结拓扑排布。

Result: 成功生成直接反映不同温度下自旋实现的针织织物，验证了以伊辛图案作为拓扑指令安排织物缠结的可行性。

Conclusion: 该概念验证为利用底层物理过程统计特性直接调控织物性能的新型随机性织物设计开辟了路径。

Abstract: Textile designs are typically based on biperiodic, deterministic patterns. However, hand and machine manufacturing are not restricted to periodic patterns. Instead, the entanglement topology can be chosen for each base unit cell of the textile. Non-deterministic processes, such as stochastic realisations of physical systems, can then serve as design tools for creating original patterns. In this Proceedings, we present a proof of concept for stochastic design by generating knitted fabrics from Ising model spin patterns. We leverage the binary analogy between knit/purl stitches and up/down spins to produce fabrics that directly represent spin realisations at different temperatures. Ising patterns then play the role of topological instructions to arrange the entanglements in the fabric. This proof of concept paves the way for novel stochastic designs of fabrics whose properties are directly controlled by the statistical characteristics of an underlying physical process.

</details>


### [294] [Tunable topological droplets on supercoiled DNA under tension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19342)
*Enrico Skoruppa,Prithwidip Saha,Jan Brugués,Jan Lipfert,Davide Marenduzzo,Helmut Schiessel*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作结合粗粒化模拟与解析理论表明，DNA超螺旋（supercoiling）产生的盘绕结构（plectoneme）可作为力学绝缘的成核口袋，使DNA–蛋白共凝聚在临界力之上仍可发生，且液滴尺寸受外力与连接数调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在恒定拉伸力下，直链DNA的共凝聚呈现"全或无"行为，即超过临界力则无法成核、低于临界力则整条分子塌缩，这一简单图像无法解释真实细胞中DNA处于超螺旋状态时的凝聚调控，因此需要考察超螺旋如何改变凝聚基底。

Method: 采用粗粒化模拟（coarse-grained simulations）与解析理论（analytic theory）相结合的方法，并辅以磁镊（magnetic tweezers）实验，使用精胺（spermine）诱导DNA凝聚。

Result: 超螺旋使凝聚基底在力学上被区分：盘绕结构可充当力学绝缘口袋，在临界力之上仍能成核凝聚；所得液滴尺寸可通过施加力与连接数（linking number）调节；表面张力（surface tension）驱动多个盘绕结构相关凝聚体粗化（coarsening）为单一拓扑液滴。磁镊实验重现了力依赖的塌缩与超螺旋依赖的凝聚。

Conclusion: 超螺旋通过产生力学绝缘的成核位点打破了直链DNA"全或无"的凝聚限制，为基因表达与修复中的DNA–蛋白共凝聚调控提供了一种力学与拓扑耦合的机制。

Abstract: DNA--protein co-condensation is an important regulatory mechanism for gene expression and repair. For straight DNA under constant stretching forces, co-condensation is ``all-or-nothing'': above a critical force, condensates are prevented from nucleating, while below it the entire molecule collapses into a droplet. Here we show by coarse-grained simulations and analytic theory that DNA supercoiling mechanically differentiates the condensation substrate: DNA plectonemes constitute force-insulated pockets on which condensates can nucleate even above the critical force. The size of the resulting droplets is tunable through the applied force and linking number. Surface tension drives the coarsening of multiple plectoneme-associated condensates into a single topological droplet. Magnetic tweezers experiments with spermine reproduce both the force-dependent collapse and the supercoiling-dependent condensation.

</details>


### [295] [Critical corner angles and defect exchange in confined nematics with splay-bend anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19454)
*Prabakaran Rajamanickam*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文研究受限向列相（nematics）中壁面施加的指向矢转向如何由缺陷吸收，并证明角点可与液晶交换缺陷；通过约化Landau–de Gennes理论引入splay与bend弹性常数不相等的情形，预测并数值验证了角点类型、双稳区间及多边形/圆盘平衡态的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限向列相中壁面强加的指向矢转向必须由缺陷承担，而角点在其中扮演的角色（缺陷的储存与释放）此前缺乏定量判据；同时多数理论假设Frank常数相等，splay–bend各向异性（anisotropy）对角落态选择的影响未被系统刻画。

Method: 以开口角ω的切向锚定角点为对象，用半转数m标记角点类型，相邻类型相差一个指数±1/2的束缚缺陷（bound defect），以对数能量决定其脱落或吸收；在约化Landau–de Gennes理论中通过立方不变量Q_lk ∂_lQ_ij ∂_kQ_ij引入不等的splay与bend常数，并以体相序参量上界使问题适定；结合一阶微扰与求积（quadrature）解析求解，再用有限元（finite-element）计算三至八边形及圆盘。

Result: 等弹性常数下临界角为72°、120°、216°与360°，其间存在双稳窗口，可解释72°顶点附近的隐缺陷并选出多边形与圆盘的平衡态；一阶上仅splay角点感受到各向异性，其余角点与缺陷能量为其偶函数并可精确求积。当K_3/K_1由0.54升至1.86时，splay角点释放+1/2缺陷的临界角由104°移至136°，从而预测哪些多边形偏好splay角点及其内部缺陷类型；有限元结果证实缺陷交换在预测范围内通过+1/2缺陷向角点迁移实现，且零个、一个与两个splay角点的态近简并。

Conclusion: 角点作为缺陷源与汇，与液晶交换指数±1/2的束缚缺陷，其类型由几何角与弹性各向异性共同决定；该机制为受限向列相中多边形与圆盘的缺陷结构与平衡态选择提供了定量判据，理论预测与有限元计算一致。

Abstract: In confined nematics, the director turning imposed by the walls is absorbed by defects, and we show that the corners exchange these defects with the liquid. A tangentially anchored corner of opening angle $ω$ takes one of several types, labelled by the number $m$ of half-turns of the director relative to the radius; neighbouring types differ by a bound defect of index $\pm\frac12$, and logarithmic energies decide when the corner sheds or absorbs it. For equal elastic constants the critical angles are $72^\circ$, $120^\circ$, $216^\circ$ and $360^\circ$, with windows of bistability between them, explaining the hidden defect near $72^\circ$ vertices and selecting the equilibria of polygons and discs. We introduce unequal splay and bend constants through the cubic invariant $Q_{lk}\,\partial_lQ_{ij}\,\partial_kQ_{ij}$ in reduced Landau--de Gennes theory; bounding the order by its bulk value makes the problem well posed, without further elastic terms, whenever both Frank constants are positive. At first order only the splay corner feels the anisotropy; other corner and defect energies are even in it and follow exactly by quadrature. The anisotropy moves the angle above which a splay corner sheds a $+\frac12$ defect from $104^\circ$ to $136^\circ$ as $K_3/K_1$ rises from $0.54$ to $1.86$, and so predicts which polygons prefer splay corners and which interior defects. Finite-element computations for polygons of three to eight sides and the disc confirm this: the exchange occurs within the predicted range by migration of $+\frac12$ defects into the corners, near which states with zero, one and two splay corners are nearly degenerate.

</details>


### [296] [Field-Driven Active Matter Physics and Material Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19806)
*Jing Wang,Shengkai Li,Trung V. Phan,Gao Wang,Huaicheng Chen,Xiaorui Chen,Robert H. Austin,Liyu Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文将场驱动活性物质（field-driven active matter）定位为连接活性物质物理与功能材料工程的桥梁，提出"场—信息—物理—功能"统一框架，阐述其如何通过将控制负担从个体单元转移至外部场，实现可扩展的智能材料与可编程集体行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人活性物质（robotic active matter）虽具备可编程性与宏观可控性，但受限于车载供能、通信与控制复杂度，难以规模化。场驱动方式可将部分控制负担从个体智能体转移至外部场，从而突破上述可扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 采用综述与框架构建方式，以磁场、电场、声场、光场和流场等外部场为媒介，统一组织"场介导相互作用—信息处理与记忆—集体输运与重构—机械性能"的逻辑链条，即场—信息—物理—功能（field-to-information-to-physics-to-function）框架。

Result: 场驱动活性物质可同时为大量智能体供能、引导与耦合，支持定向导航、自适应协作与柔性输运等应用；并为可调相互作用（tunable interactions）、可编程相变（programmable phase transitions）、固—流切换（solid-fluid switching）、超均匀有序（hyperuniform order）及可重构集体运动等非平衡现象提供可控实验平台。

Conclusion: 通过在智能体与场之间分配控制权，场驱动活性物质为具备感知、适应、计算、重构与复杂任务执行能力的智能材料提供了路径，同时拓展了非平衡物理的科学边界与工程物质的实用能力。

Abstract: Active matter consists of energy-consuming units that generate motion, making it a powerful platform for exploring nonequilibrium physics and engineering adaptive materials. Robotic active matter brings this physics into programmable, macroscopic agents with controllable actuation, sensing, and response, but its scalability is limited by onboard power, communication, and control complexity. Field-driven active matter addresses these limitations by shifting part of the control burden from individual agents to external or environmental fields, such as magnetic, electric, acoustic, optical, and fluidic fields, which can power, guide, and couple many agents simultaneously. Beyond applications in targeted navigation, adaptive collaboration, and flexible transport, field-driven robotic active matter offers a controllable testbed for nonequilibrium phenomena that are difficult to isolate in molecular or colloidal active matter, including tunable interactions, programmable phase transitions, solid-fluid switching, hyperuniform order, and reconfigurable collective motion. Here, we present field-driven active matter as a bridge between active matter physics and functional material engineering, organized through a unified field-to-information-to-physics-to-function framework that links field-mediated interactions, information processing, and memory to collective transport, reconfiguration, and mechanical performance. By distributing control between agents and fields, field-driven active matter offers a pathway toward intelligent materials capable of sensing, adaptation, computation, reconfiguration, and performing complex tasks, while expanding both the scientific scope of nonequilibrium physics and the practical capabilities of engineered matter.

</details>


### [297] [Free energy profile computations for force-induced melting of a short B-DNA using Jarzynski equality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19877)
*S. Siva Nasarayya Chari,Prabal K. Maiti*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究通过非平衡拉伸实验（Jarzynski等式）与伞形采样（umbrella sampling）两种方法，计算溶剂化12碱基对B-DNA从无外力状态A到400 pN状态B的自由能差，发现两者高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在检验非平衡功分布方法（Jarzynski等式）与平衡采样方法（伞形采样/WHAM）在DNA拉伸自由能计算中的一致性与适用性，并比较二者所沿路径的差异。

Method: 先将指定序列的溶剂化12碱基对标准B-DNA在无外力（状态A: f=0）下平衡；再固定一端以恒定速率拉伸另一端至400 pN（状态B），由伸长量对拉伸力求积分获得非平衡功W，多次重复得到功分布P(W)；用基于Jarzynski等式（Jarzynski equality）的方法得到自由能曲线Φ(f)。同时以端到端长度ξ为反应坐标进行伞形采样（umbrella sampling），并用加权直方图分析法（WHAM）计算平均力势（PMF）Φ(ξ)。

Result: 两种方法给出的状态A与B之间的平衡自由能差ΔF_AB (=F_B−F_A) 彼此高度吻合；但Φ(f)与Φ(ξ)两条曲线本身存在差异，归因于二者不同的解链（unzipping）路径。

Conclusion: 非平衡拉伸结合Jarzynski等式可有效重现与平衡伞形采样一致的端点自由能差，验证了有限时间非平衡过程提取平衡自由能（free energy）的可行性；路径依赖性仅影响中间曲线形态而非端点自由能差。

Abstract: We initially equilibrate a system of solvated 12-base pair canonical B-DNA of a specified sequence in a state of no external force acting on it (state $A: f=0$). A switching experiment is then performed by pulling one end of the DNA while restraining its other end. The pulling force is taken up to a maximum of 400 pN (state $B$), at a specified constant rate. The non-equilibrium work done W associated with this finite-time process is determined by integrating the extension of the DNA molecule with respect to the applied force. The distribution of the work values $P(W)$ is obtained by repeating the pulling process several times, wherein each time, the process is started with macroscopic state $A$. The free energy profile $Φ(f)$ is obtained by a suitably devised method from the Jarzynski equality. The conformational space of the DNA is also sampled in an equilibrium method of umbrella sampling by considering the end-to-end length $ξ$ as the reaction coordinate. The associated potential of mean force (PMF) $Φ(ξ)$ is then computed by invoking the weighted histogram analysis method (WHAM). From the results, we observe that the equilibrium free energy difference between two states, $A$ and $B$, $ΔF_{AB} ~(=F_B -F_A)$ obtained through both methods, is in close agreement with each other, despite the differences along the profiles $Φ(f)$ and $Φ(ξ)$, attributed to their distinct pathways of unzipping.

</details>


### [298] [Growing length scales while approaching the glass transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19883)
*Alois Loidl,Peter Lunkenheimer,Konrad Samwer*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文以随机一级相变理论（random first-order transition theory）结合Vogel-Fulcher-Tammann定律建模介电高阶磁化率数据，验证过冷液体中关联粒子团簇随降温增长的行为，并主张实验数据与隐藏相变（hidden phase transition）图像一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻璃转变（glass transition）本质长期存在争议，涉及动力学冻结、局域应变介导效应或含隐藏相变的集体现象等不同解释；作者希望检验过冷液体中关联粒子团簇随接近玻璃转变而增长的报道。

Method: 聚焦可近似获取四点关联函数（four-point correlation function）的介电研究，以探测非晶序（amorphous order）的演化；将基于三点关联函数或高阶介电磁化率的温度依赖数据，用随机一级相变理论结合Vogel-Fulcher-Tammann定律进行建模，从而对归一化关联长度（correlation length）与关联粒子数随温度及脆性（fragility）的变化作出预测。

Result: 模型对所有研究材料（包括网络玻璃形成体、分子玻璃形成体与聚合物）的实验数据均给出良好拟合；不同方法确定的长度尺度与粒子数至少相近且与理论所考虑的量密切相关，关于关联分子数脆性依赖的预测也至少近似成立。

Conclusion: 针对实验关联长度温度依赖缺乏临界性（criticality）的既有批评，作者以脆性依赖加以解释：全部可用数据与隐藏相变图像一致，且该相变在最脆（most fragile）液体中体现得最清楚。

Abstract: Even after decades of research, the nature of the glass transition is controversially debated: Kinetic freezing phenomena, purely local strain-mediated effects, or collective phenomena including a hidden phase transition are usually discussed. In this work, we check reports on clusters of correlated particles in supercooled liquids which grow upon approaching the glass-transition. We specifically focus on dielectric studies providing approximate access to the four-point correlation function, which allows insight into the evolution of so-called amorphous order. Existing temperature-dependent data derived from the three-point correlation function or higher-order dielectric susceptibilities are modeled using the random first-order transition theory combined with the Vogel-Fulcher-Tammann law. This ansatz enables detailed predictions concerning normalized correlation lengths or the number of correlated particles as function of temperature and fragility. It provides a good fit to the experimental data for all materials investigated, including network and molecular glass formers and polymers. We conclude that length scales and corresponding particle numbers determined by the different methods used are at least similar and closely related to those considered by the theory. Moreover, the model predictions on the fragility dependence of the number of correlated molecules are at least approximately fulfilled. Existing criticism concerning an apparent lack of criticality in the temperature dependence of experimental correlation lengths is discussed in terms of this fragility dependence: All available data are well consistent with a hidden phase transition which is best revealed for the most fragile liquids.

</details>


### [299] [On the richness of metamaterials generated by recursive cell division](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19957)
*Alexei Vazquez*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文将“尺度丰富（scale-rich）”超材料视为递归细胞分裂（recursive cell division）家族的一员，通过三种网络映射与弹性波性质表征六个成员，揭示决定物理性质的并非分裂规则本身，而是框架图中长跨段（long spans）的尾部。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归细胞分裂可产生多种超材料，但不同分裂规则（切哪个细胞、切点位置、切割角度）是否以及如何影响弹性波性质此前并不清楚，需要区分哪些结构特征真正决定物理行为。

Method: 构建三种网络映射——细胞树（cell tree）、韧带与节点对偶图（dual graph）、跨段框架图（frame graph）——并结合有效折射率（effective refractive index）n、其各向同性（isotropy）及成立频带，对六种分裂规则进行比较分析。

Result: 对偶图度律、平均度与韧带年龄律在各规则下相同，无法区分材料；细胞树与细胞形状存在差异，其后果是框架图中长跨段尾部。以最长跨段为长度尺度，逆平方折射率的弯曲（bending）部分跨规则、密度与韧带数遵循单一幂律，并决定最低局域共振与带边；拉伸（stretching）部分由平铺的直线框架承载。随机切割角产生细长细胞与长跨段，导致高折射率与窄带；垂直长轴切割使细胞紧凑，得到低折射率与宽带；过质心切割将分裂比限定在4/9至5/9，消除面积层级而不改变折射率；切割角在两端间连续插值，各向同性在韧带取向各向同性且细胞仍紧凑时达到峰值；三维中板式与杆式分裂为拉伸主导，各规则折射率均偏低。

Conclusion: 弹性波性质主要由长跨段与细胞形状（即细胞树结构）决定，而非分裂规则下的对偶图统计；切割角与切点位置提供了调控折射率、带宽与各向同性的设计自由度，三维拉伸主导结构则难以获得高折射率。

Abstract: Scale-rich metamaterials are one member of a family of recursive cell divisions, which differ in which cell is cut, where the cut passes and at what angle. We characterise six members of this family through three network mappings, the cell tree, the dual graph of ligaments and junctions, and the frame graph of spans, and through their elastic-wave properties: the effective refractive index n, its isotropy and the band over which it holds. The dual-graph degree law, the mean degree and the age law of the ligaments hit by new ones are the same for every rule, so they cannot distinguish the materials. The cell tree and the cell shapes do differ, and one consequence of them explains the physics: the tail of long spans in the frame graph. With the longest spans as the length scale, the bending part of the inverse squared index follows a single power law across rules, densities and numbers of ligaments, and the same spans set the lowest local resonance and the band edge. The stretching part, which caps the index, is carried by the straight frame lines of the tiling rather than by the division. Random cut angles produce elongated cells and long spans, hence high index and a narrow band; cuts perpendicular to the long axis of the cell keep the cells compact, giving low index and a wide band. Cutting through the centroid instead of a random point bounds the split fraction between 4/9 and 5/9, which removes the area hierarchy without changing the index. The cut angle interpolates continuously between these limits, and the isotropy peaks where the ligament orientations become isotropic while the cells are still compact. In three dimensions the plate and strut divisions are stretching dominated and the index stays low for every rule.

</details>


### [300] [Self-assembly of dipolar nanoclusters on an attractive substrate: A coarse-grained simulation approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20002)
*Denis Gessert,Harshita Chauhan,Thi Ngoc Ha Nguyen,Christoph Tegenkamp,Michael Mehring,Varsha Banerjee,Martin Weigel*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 采用粗粒化（coarse-grained）Stockmayer粒子模型与分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulations），再现铋氧纳米团簇（BiO-NCs）在基底上的链状组装，并支持"偶极矩由粒子-基底相互作用诱导"的猜想；平衡态下链呈现低密度自避行走（self-avoiding walk）、高密度棒状（rod-like）标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验发现BiO-NCs沉积于金或热解石墨基底时形成链，铈掺杂可显著增强该现象，但其微观机制不明。作者意图以简化模型检验Thomas等提出的"偶极矩由基底相互作用诱导、体相中偶极矩很小"的猜想。

Method: 构建含吸引性基底的（磁性）Stockmayer粒子粗粒化模型，用分子动力学模拟研究；以由高温快速淬火（rapid quench）至体相共存曲线（bulk coexistence curve）以下低温的方式模拟沉积实验，并另做平衡态分析。

Result: 模拟结果与实验观察定性一致；即使在较低浓度下体相也出现显著链形成，而实验中乙醇内BiO-NCs的动态光散射（dynamic light scattering）显示无基底时几乎不聚集，此差异支持偶极矩由基底诱导的设想。平衡态方面，链在低密度下呈自避行走标度，高密度下转为棒状标度，并给出链尺寸标度与二维密度下填充层数的关系。

Conclusion: 简化的Stockmayer粒子模型可定性复现BiO-NCs的基底诱导链状组装，支持基底诱导偶极矩的图像；界面链的标度行为可与界面聚合物（polymers on interfaces）研究相类比。

Abstract: Recently, Thomas et al. [Nanoscale 17, 18291 (2025)] showed that bismuth oxido nanoclusters (BiO-NCs) of the type [Bi$_{38}$ O$_{45}$ (Boc-L-Phe-O)$_{24}$] form chains when deposited onto gold or pyrolitic graphite substrates. This chain formation is significantly enhanced when the bismuth in the BiO-NCs is partially replaced by cerium through doping. In this work, we study a simplified, coarse-grained model of this process, namely (magnetic) Stockmayer particles in the presence of an attractive substrate. The model is investigated by means of molecular dynamics simulations. The deposition experiment is mimicked by a rapid quench from high temperature to a low temperature below the bulk coexistence curve. The results are found to be qualitatively similar to the experimental observations reported earlier. In our simulations, we find significant chain formation in the bulk even at rather low concentrations, which is in contrast to the BiO-NCs in ethanol for which dynamic light scattering reveals negligible aggregation in the absence of the substrate. This lends support to a scenario, conjectured in the work of Thomas at al., where the dipole moments are induced by the particle-substrate interaction and hence there is little dipole moment in the bulk. In a second part, we study the system in equilibrium. We consider the scaling of chains, the number of layers populated as a function of two-dimensional density, and we draw analogies to polymers on interfaces, which have been extensively studied over the past decades. Most notably, it is found that the chains behave as self-avoiding walks at low densities, which gives way to rod-like scaling at higher densities.

</details>


### [301] [Bending Rigidity of Smectic Filaments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20555)
*Haotian Long,Shamiah A. Williams,Chinedum O. Osuji,Christopher A. Browne*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文研究液-液相分离形成的微尺度丝状近晶相凝聚体的弹性，通过热弯曲涨落的傅里叶分析测量持续长度，发现其随横截面积呈非经典的线性标度（linear scaling），并借助Helfrich能量模型加以解释，从而给出直接测量弯曲模量K的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多生物与工程丝状体具有内部结构或流动性，会改变其弹性行为；经典弹性丝理论预测持续长度随横截面积呈二次标度（quadratic scaling），但具内部近晶流体有序的丝状体是否遵循此规律尚不清楚。

Method: 对微尺度丝状近晶相凝聚体（filamentous smectic condensates）的热弯曲涨落（thermal bend fluctuations）进行傅里叶分析（Fourier analysis）以测定持续长度（persistence length）；并用Helfrich能量计算具内部近晶流体有序的丝弯曲自由能，以解释标度行为。

Result: 持续长度随横截面积呈非经典的线性标度，而非经典弹性丝的二次标度；该分析提供了直接测量弯曲模量（bend modulus）K的方法，为分散近晶相中首次直接测量K的手段。引入短烷基尾介晶掺杂剂（mesogen dopants）后丝体软化，K最多降低约35%。

Conclusion: 结果表明纳米尺度近晶组织（smectic organization）与分子组成决定丝尺度的弹性，可为仿生与工程材料中丝力学性能与自组装（self-assembly）的调控提供依据。

Abstract: Many biological and engineered filaments exhibit internal structure or fluidity that alters their elastic behavior. We study the elasticity of microscale filamentous smectic condensates, grown during liquid-liquid crystal phase separation. We use a Fourier analysis of their thermal bend fluctuations to measure their persistence length, which follows a non-classic linear scaling with their cross-sectional area, in contrast to the classic quadratic scaling for elastic filaments. We rationalize this scaling using the Helfrich energy to compute the free energy of bending a filament with internal smectic fluid ordering. Our analysis then provides a direct method to measure the bend modulus K, which quantifies the rigidity of smectic layers; providing the first method to directly measure K for dispersed smectic phases. We quantify the softening of filaments in the presence of shorter alkyl-tail mesogen dopants, decreasing K by up to ~35%. Out results suggest how nanoscale smectic organization and molecular composition determine filament-scale elasticity, informing the control of filament mechanics and self-assembly in biomimetic and engineered materials.

</details>


### [302] [Model-free stiffness measurement of microscale soft contacts by far-field traction force microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20918)
*Keumkyung Kuk,Shivaprakash N. Ramakrishna,Nancy A. Burnham,Robert W. Style,Lucio Isa*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 提出远场牵引力显微镜（far-field traction force microscopy, FF-TFM），无需任何接触力学模型即可从摩擦诱导的远场横向位移直接提取软基底杨氏模量，其六种硅弹性体结果与体相流变一致，而JKR拟合法低估模量最多达40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软材料的微观刚度决定其接触力学、黏附与摩擦行为，但微/纳米压痕法需借助JKR等接触力学模型（contact mechanics models）反演刚度，而这些模型的假设在软黏附接触中常不成立。

Method: 将原子力显微镜（atomic-force microscope, AFM）上的胶体探针在软表面拖动，同步测量摩擦力并成像荧光示踪颗粒的位移；在远离接触区的弹性极限内，横向位移仅由摩擦力与基底刚度决定，用点力解（point-force solution）拟合即可直接得到杨氏模量。

Result: 对六种硅弹性体（silicone elastomers），FF-TFM所得模量与体相流变学（bulk rheology）一致，而压痕数据的JKR拟合低估模量最多达体相值的40%；该方法还可追踪探针在表面的滑移，发现软接触存在力信号无可检测特征的"预滑移（pre-slip）"，与较硬接触不同。

Conclusion: FF-TFM提供了一条免接触模型的途径，用于测量软材料的微观刚度与滑移行为，可作为压痕法的替代或补充。

Abstract: The microscale stiffness of soft materials governs their contact mechanics, adhesion and friction, yet it is surprisingly difficult to measure. Micro or nanoindentation is typically used, but extracting stiffness from these measurements requires using contact mechanics models (e.g. JKR), whose assumptions often break down for soft adhesive contacts. Here, we introduce far-field traction force microscopy (FF-TFM) to extract stiffness without using any contact model. We drag a colloidal probe mounted on an atomic-force microscope across a soft surface, measuring the friction force while imaging the displacements of fluorescent tracers on the substrate. Far from the contact, in the elastic limit, these lateral displacements depend solely on the friction force and the substrate stiffness, and fitting them with the point-force solution thus gives the substrate's Young's modulus directly. We successfully match bulk rheology moduli with FF-TFM for six different silicone elastomers, where JKR fits to indentation data underestimate moduli by as much as 40\% of their bulk values. Our approach also enables tracking how the probe slips across the surface. We find that soft contacts can "pre-slip" with no detectable signature in the force signal, unlike stiffer contacts. FF-TFM thus offers a contact-model-free route to measuring microscale stiffness and slip behavior in soft materials.

</details>


### [303] [Delay-induced anomalous density-polarization relation in chiral active matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20931)
*Dongrun Jian,Jie Su,Jun Wang,Jin Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该论文研究带延迟对齐（delayed alignment）的手性活性粒子（chiral active particle, CAP）模型，揭示局域极化（local polarization）随局域密度先升后降的反常密度-极化关系（anomalous density-polarization relation, DPR）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为局域密度升高会增强局域极化，而真实活性系统中个体响应普遍存在延迟（delay）；因此需澄清延迟对齐如何影响集体运动，以及高密度是否仍能强化极性有序。

Method: 构建带延迟对齐的手性活性粒子模型，分析偏差角动力学（deviation-angle dynamics），并结合非平衡景观-流理论（nonequilibrium landscape-flux theory）进行解析与模拟对照。

Result: 在液滴内发现局域极化随局域密度先增后减的反常DPR；偏差角动力学显示延迟诱导超临界离散Hopf分岔（supercritical discrete Hopf bifurcation），分岔前后的噪声维持振荡与自持振荡展宽偏差角分布并降低极化；增大延迟使分岔移至更低密度，从而强化反常DPR，与模拟一致。

Conclusion: 非平衡流（nonequilibrium flux）被识别为反常DPR的动力学起源；时间延迟可作为手性活性物质集体行为的调控参量，并为其他非平衡系统中的反常密度-有序关系提供分析视角。

Abstract: Aligning active matter can spontaneously exhibit diverse forms of ordered collective motion, and higher local density is generally expected to enhance local polarization. Meanwhile, delays in agents' responses are widespread in real active systems, raising the question of how delayed alignment affects collective motion and, in particular, whether higher local density still strengthens polar order. Here, using a chiral active particle (CAP) model with delayed alignment, we uncover a counterintuitive anomalous density-polarization relation (DPR) within droplets: the local polarization first increases and then decreases with increasing local density. Analysis of the deviation-angle dynamics reveals a delay-induced supercritical discrete Hopf bifurcation. Noise-sustained oscillations before the bifurcation and self-sustained oscillations afterward broaden the deviation-angle distribution and reduce polarization. Increasing the delay shifts the bifurcation to lower densities, enhancing the anomalous DPR in agreement with simulations. Using the nonequilibrium landscape-flux theory, we further identify the nonequilibrium flux as the dynamical origin of the anomalous DPR. Our findings not only establish time delay as a control knob for collective behavior in chiral active matter, but also offer a useful analytical perspective on anomalous density-order relations in other nonequilibrium systems exhibiting collective order.

</details>


### [304] [Polymorphism and crystal suppression in symmetric mixtures with entropic interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20944)
*Santi Prestipino,Giuseppe Pellicane,Enrique Lomba,Franz Saija*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究通过交换蒙特卡洛（swap Monte Carlo）模拟研究等直径、负非加性硬球二元混合物的相行为，发现接近共晶点的弱非加性区间内混合物无法结晶而形成非晶态，其玻璃化与FCC和B2（CsCl型）晶体结构的竞争相关；非加性进一步增强时B2被B1（NaCl型）取代并恢复全组分有序化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元硬球混合物在负非加性下倾向异配位（hetero-coordination），其相行为与玻璃形成能力的关系尚不清晰；本文旨在揭示该模型中非晶态出现的条件及其结构起源，并检验纯排除体积（excluded-volume）相互作用下熵（entropy）对多组分玻璃形成的作用。

Method: 采用大规模交换蒙特卡洛（swap Monte Carlo）模拟，对等直径、负非加性硬球二元混合物在不同非加性强度与组成下进行致密样品平衡与相行为探测。

Result: 在接近共晶点（eutectic point）的小非加性区间内，混合物不结晶，压缩流体滞留于非晶态；中间组成处的玻璃化起始（onset of glass）与FCC和B2（CsCl型）晶体结构之间的竞争相关。非加性进一步增大时，等摩尔组成处B2让位于B1（NaCl型）晶体，混合物在各组成下均重新有序化，作者将其归因于B1晶体的FCC对称性基础。

Conclusion: 仅在排除体积相互作用（excluded-volume interactions）的模型框架下即可出现上述非晶化行为，表明熵（entropy）在多组分金属、无机及软物质玻璃形成体系（glass formers）中生成非晶结构方面起关键作用。

Abstract: We investigate the phases of a binary mixture of hard spheres with equal diameter and negative non-additivity, favoring hetero-coordination in dense samples. By performing extensive swap Monte Carlo simulations, we show that in an interval of small non-additivities, close to the eutectic point, the mixture fails to crystallize and the compressed fluid gets stuck in an amorphous state. The onset of glass at intermediate compositions is associated with the competition between FCC and B2 (CsCl-type) crystal structures. As the propensity of particles to hetero-coordinate grows further, however, the B2 crystal gives way to a B1 (NaCl-type) crystal at equimolarity, and again the mixture orders for all compositions. We argue that this happens because of the underlying FCC symmetry of the B1 crystal. These results, pertaining to a model with excluded-volume interactions only, point to the key role of entropy in generating amorphous structures in multi-component metallic, inorganic, and soft-matter glass formers.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.CO'></div>

# astro-ph.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [305] [Cosmography with gravitational waves II: Padé approximants in an extended redshift regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19006)
*Jonathan Morais,Rodrigo Gonçalves,Jailson Alcaniz*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文评估LISA、爱因斯坦望远镜（Einstein Telescope）与DECIGO利用电磁明亮标准汽笛（standard sirens）约束宇宙学运动学参数的能力，采用Padé近似（Padé approximants）将宇宙学图景分析（cosmographic analysis）拓展至更高红移，并给出H0、q0、j0、s0、l0的约束预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代引力波探测器（gravitational-wave detectors）有望提供大量明亮标准汽笛，从而独立测量宇宙膨胀历史；传统基于泰勒展开（Taylor expansion）的宇宙学图景分析受限于低红移，难以充分利用高红移事件。

Method: 采用Padé近似替代低红移泰勒展开，对H0、q0、j0、s0、l0进行宇宙学图景拟合；约束结果主要作为探测事件数（number of detections）的函数呈现，并辅以在相关信噪比—红移（SNR–redshift）区域的补充分析。

Result: LISA对H0给出百分之一量级（percent-level）约束，爱因斯坦望远镜与DECIGO在样本量增加时可达亚百分（subpercent）精度；q0可约束至数十个百分点（tens-of-percent）水平，而高阶参数j0、s0、l0的不确定性依次增大。

Conclusion: 相较低红移泰勒分析，Padé方法扩展了可用红移范围、增加了可及事件数量，并实现对膨胀历史（expansion history）的高阶重建。

Abstract: We evaluate the capability of next-generation gravitational-wave detectors, including LISA, the Einstein Telescope, and DECIGO, to probe cosmographic parameters using electromagnetically bright standard sirens. Adopting Padé approximants, we extend the cosmographic analysis to substantially higher redshifts and constrain the parameters $H_0$, $q_0$, $j_0$, $s_0$, and $l_0$. We examine the constraints primarily as a function of the number of detections and, in a complementary analysis, across correlated SNR--redshift regimes. LISA provides percent-level constraints on $H_0$, while the Einstein Telescope and DECIGO reach subpercent precision as the catalogue size increases. The parameter $q_0$ can be constrained at the tens-of-percent level, whereas the higher-order parameters $j_0$, $s_0$, and $l_0$ remain progressively less determined. Compared with the low-redshift Taylor analysis, the Padé approach extends the usable redshift range, increases the accessible event population and enables a higher-order reconstruction of the expansion history.

</details>


### [306] [Testing General Relativity by Reconstructing Dark Energy and the Growth Index with Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19117)
*Andronikos Paliathanasis*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文扩展了 Cosmo-PINN（物理信息神经网络），通过背景、物质增长与增长指数三个模块重建暗能量状态方程 w_DE(z) 与增长指数 γ(z)，并用 DESI DR2 等多源数据结合 HMC 采样给出后验约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在实现不依赖特定模型的宇宙学重建（model-independent cosmological reconstruction），同时重建暗能量状态方程与物质扰动增长指数，并借助同一背景下的两种重建路径对物理框架做一致性检验。

Method: 采用三模块结构的物理信息神经网络（physics-informed neural network, PINN）：背景模块从观测学习暗能量密度 ρ_DE(z)，并经由守恒方程（conservation equation）残差重建 w_DE(z)；另两模块基于该背景，分别由广义相对论（General Relativity）线性扰动方程直接重建 f(z)、fσ_8(z)，或通过独立重建的增长指数 γ(z) 实现；用哈密顿蒙特卡洛（Hamiltonian Monte Carlo, HMC）采样获得后验不确定度。

Result: 训练数据包括 DESI DR2 重子声学振荡（Baryon Acoustic Oscillations）、宇宙计时器（Cosmic Chronometers）、Ia 型超新星（Type Ia Supernovae）与红移空间畸变（redshift-space distortion）数据。不同数据组合下，当前暗能量状态方程后验中位数落在 w_DE,0 ∈ [-0.930, -0.867]，幽灵分界线穿越（phantom divide crossing）发生于 z ≃ 0.41–0.53；当前增长指数后验中位数约为 γ_0 ≃ 0.60–0.62。

Conclusion: 结果表明暗能量状态方程在当前值上略偏向幽灵区（phantom 一侧）并存在分界线穿越，而 γ_0 低于标准值，提示宇宙结构增长受到抑制（suppression of the growth of cosmic structure）。

Abstract: We extend the Cosmo-PINN, a physics-informed neural network for model-independent cosmological reconstruction, to reconstruct the dark energy equation of state parameter $w_{\mathrm{DE}}(z)$ and the growth index $γ(z)$ of matter perturbations. The reconstruction is performed through three modules: the background module, the matter growth module, and the growth index module. The background module learns the dark energy density $ρ_{\mathrm{DE}}(z)$ from cosmological observations, and $w_{\mathrm{DE}}(z)$ is reconstructed through the residual of the conservation equation. The reconstructed background is then passed to the other two modules, which reconstruct the observables $f(z)$ and $fσ_{8}(z)$ either directly from the linear perturbation equation of General Relativity or through an independently reconstructed growth index $γ(z)$. Comparing the two reconstructions on the same background provides a consistency test of the underlying physical framework. The network is trained on the DESI DR2 Baryon Acoustic Oscillations, Cosmic Chronometers, Type Ia Supernovae, and redshift-space distortion data. Posterior uncertainties for the reconstructed cosmological parameters and functions are obtained through Hamiltonian Monte Carlo sampling. For the different data combinations, the posterior median of the present-day dark energy equation of state lies within the range $w_{\mathrm{DE,0}}\in \lbrack -0.930,-0.867]$, while the phantom divide crossing takes place at $z\simeq 0.41$--$0.53$. The posterior median of the present-day growth index reconstructed from the data lies within $γ_{0}\simeq 0.60$--$0.62$, indicating suppression of the growth of cosmic structure.

</details>


### [307] [Galaxy number counts in area distance space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20910)
*Chris Clarkson,Roy Maartens,Stefano Zazzera*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 论文推导了以面积距离（area distance）为径向坐标时通量选择样本的线性相对论性计数（linear relativistic number counts），并系统比较其与红移空间、光度距离空间聚类的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有星系聚类（galaxy clustering）分析多基于红移或光度距离作为径向坐标，而面积距离作为径向坐标时聚类性质如何变化缺乏系统研究。

Method: 推导通量选择星系群体的线性相对论性计数，分别考察源运动、引力透镜（lensing）与观测者运动对面积距离空间聚类的影响，并将三种计数场组合以消去共同的密度、演化与放大偏置（magnification bias）。

Result: 面积距离聚类不含Kaiser畸变（Kaiser distortion）；透镜效应可增强其聚类但削弱其与红移空间聚类的相关性；观测者运动使面积距离壳层位移方向与红移、光度距离壳层相反，其运动学偶极（kinematic dipole）方向亦相反。

Conclusion: 三种计数场的组合信号在近处由速度主导、远处由光学效应主导，且可与星系尺寸放大（galaxy-size magnification）相联系，从而无需单个星系的精确距离即可统计推断该信号。

Abstract: We investigate how galaxy clustering changes when area distance is used as the radial coordinate. We derive the linear relativistic number counts for a flux-selected population and compare them with redshift and luminosity distance space. Source motion does not change the beam area at a fixed emission event, so area-distance clustering has no Kaiser distortion. Lensing changes both the angular and radial positions of sources, and can enhance area-distance clustering while weakening its correlation with redshift-space clustering. Observer motion shifts area-distance shells in the opposite direction to redshift and luminosity-distance shells, allowing their kinematic dipoles to point in opposite directions. A combination of the three count fields cancels their common intrinsic density and explicit evolution and magnification biases, leaving a signal dominated by velocities nearby and optical effects at larger distances. We relate this signal to galaxy-size magnification, so that its correlations can be inferred statistically without precise distances to individual galaxies.

</details>


### [308] [Weak lensing data beyond flexion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21226)
*Ben D. Normann,Martin H. Enoksen,Nicolò Oreste Pinciroli Vago,Hans Georg Schaathun*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文检验弱透镜（weak lensing）中高于弯曲（flexion）的高阶多极振幅是否有助于透镜质量重建，采用轮盘振幅形式体系对合成数据做最小二乘重建，发现高阶振幅对星系团透镜（cluster lenses）尤其在有噪声时带来显著改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剪切（shear）与收敛（convergence）已被充分研究，而弯曲（flexion）作为透镜势多极展开的下一项已被证明能对透镜系统质量分布提供额外约束。作者由此提出疑问：弯曲之外更高阶的弱透镜信息是否同样可用于透镜质量重建（lens-mass reconstruction）。

Method: 采用轮盘振幅形式体系（roulette-amplitude formalism），针对单一 SIE 透镜以及 SIE 与 SIS 星系团透镜构造合成数据集（含与不含模拟高斯噪声），并使用截断至三阶（弯曲）、四阶和五阶的振幅进行透镜参数的最小二乘重建（least-squares reconstruction）。

Result: 作为概念验证（proof of concept），高阶振幅确实提供额外有用信息：对单一透镜改善有限，因为弯曲已能约束多数参数；但对星系团透镜改善显著，其旋转（rotation）与椭率（ellipticity）原本恢复较差。噪声存在时改善最为明显，三阶重建可能变得不可靠，而高阶振幅可挽回大部分损失的精度。

Conclusion: 随着弱透镜巡天（weak-lensing surveys）深度与精度提升，弯曲之外的数据有望实质性改善最复杂、噪声最大的透镜系统的质量重建，并推动后续使用观测数据（而非合成数据）的进一步研究。

Abstract: In weak lensing, shear and convergence are well studied and have provided a wealth of information about the distribution of matter in the universe. More recently, flexion -- the next term in the multipole expansion of the lensing potential, responsible for the characteristic banana-shaped distortion of lensed images -- has been shown to provide additional constraints on the mass distribution of lensing systems, particularly for galaxy clusters. Here we investigate whether weak-lensing information beyond flexion is similarly useful for lens-mass reconstruction. Using the roulette-amplitude formalism, we construct synthetic datasets for a single SIE lens and for SIE and SIS cluster lenses, with and without simulated Gaussian noise, and perform a least-squares reconstruction of the lens parameters using amplitudes truncated at third (flexion), fourth, and fifth order. As a proof of concept, we find that higher-order amplitudes do provide additional, useful information: the benefit is modest for a single lens, where flexion already constrains most parameters, but becomes substantial for cluster lenses, where the rotation and ellipticity are otherwise poorly recovered. The improvement is most pronounced in the presence of noise, where third-order reconstruction can become unreliable while higher-order amplitudes restore much of the lost accuracy. These results suggest that, as weak-lensing surveys grow in depth and precision, data beyond flexion could meaningfully improve mass reconstruction for the most complex and noisiest lensing systems, motivating further work using observational rather than synthetic data.

</details>


<div id='math.CO'></div>

# math.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [309] [Peak state transfer in trees and the cost of distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21567)
*Andrew Osborne,Andrew A. Houck*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文研究了图上的峰值态转移（peak state transfer），证明邻接矩阵情形下距离 $d$ 的峰值态转移至少需要 $\frac{d^3}{24}(1+o(1))$ 条边且主系数紧，拉普拉斯情形下需要 $\Omega(d^2)$ 条边，并构造出 $\Theta(d^5)$ 条边即可实现的多项式规模图族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完美态转移（perfect state transfer）在图上的实现通常需要指数级边数，故本文转而研究约束更弱的峰值态转移，考察其是否能在多项式规模图中实现，并回答 Coutinho、Guo 与 Schmeits 提出的两个问题。

Method: 以邻接矩阵（adjacency matrix）或拉普拉斯矩阵（Laplacian matrix）生成的算子 $\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}tM}$ 的矩阵元 $|(\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}tM})_{u,v}|$ 达到谱分解所给三角不等式等号为峰值态转移的定义，结合边数下界分析与显式图族构造进行论证。

Result: 邻接情形下距离 $d$ 的峰值态转移至少需 $\frac{d^3}{24}(1+o(1))$ 条边且主系数紧；拉普拉斯情形下界为 $\Omega(d^2)$；并给出距离 $d$、$\Theta(d^5)$ 条边实现拉普拉斯峰值态转移的图族。此外，当顶点距离划分几乎等同时，拉普拉斯完美态转移至少需 $\Omega(2^{d/2})$ 条边；树上的拉普拉斯峰值态转移需 $\Omega(d!)$ 条边，且完全刻画了直径不超过 4 的此类树。

Conclusion: 峰值态转移可在多项式图规模下实现，与完美态转移的指数级代价形成鲜明对比；树上情形代价极高（$\Omega(d!)$），直径 $\leq 4$ 的树被完全分类，从而回答了 Coutinho、Guo 与 Schmeits 的两个问题。

Abstract: There is peak state transfer between vertices $u$ and $v$ in a graph $G$ if there exists a time $t$ for which $|(\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm i t M})_{u,v}|$ saturates the triangle inequality imposed by its spectral decomposition and $M$ is either the adjacency or Laplacian matrix. We show that peak state transfer at distance $d$ requires $\frac{d^3}{24}(1 + o(1))$ edges in the adjacency case, $Ω(d^2)$ edges in the Laplacian case, and that the leading coefficient in the former is tight. We find a family of graphs with Laplacian peak state transfer at distance $d$ and $Θ(d^5)$ edges, showing that peak state transfer is achievable in a polynomial graph size. In contrast, Laplacian perfect state transfer costs at least $Ω(2^{d/2})$ edges when the distance partition of vertices around one vertex is almost equitable. Finally, we show that Laplacian peak state transfer in trees requires $Ω(d!)$ edges, and we characterize all trees of diameter $\leq 4$ admitting Laplacian peak state transfer, thus answering a pair of questions posed by Coutinho, Guo, and Schmeits.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [310] [A Reinforcement Learning Method to Navigate SMEFT Operator Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21439)
*Jacky Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作将标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）算符空间的探索建模为强化学习（reinforcement learning）的序列决策问题，用一个约4万参数的小型actor-critic智能体，在132个观测量构成的唯象环境中发现多组高质量、多样化的新物理理论假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验测量与标准模型（Standard Model）预言之间的偏差通常需借助新理论模型解释，而SMEFT虽能涵盖一大类模型，但其算符空间极其庞大：多个算符可同时影响不同观测量、效应相互关联，且不同算符组合可能给出相当好的数据描述，探索因此成为困难的组合学问题。

Method: 将SMEFT算符空间探索表述为序列决策问题，采用轻量级actor-critic架构的强化学习智能体（约40K可训练参数）；目标不是寻找单一最佳拟合理论，而是发现多样化的高质量理论假设。测试环境包含粒子物理多个扇区的132个观测量，其中24个测量偏离SM预言超过1.5σ，并与随机子集搜索、随机序列搜索进行对照。

Result: 学习到的RL策略能高效构建多算符理论并显著改善全局拟合（global fit）；与独立的随机子集与随机序列搜索相比，RL策略在该大型组合空间中更具导航能力，体现出其在探索多样化新物理（new physics）相互作用模式方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 结果确立了强化学习作为SMEFT框架内系统化探索多样化新物理相互作用模式的可行框架。

Abstract: In particle physics, discrepancies between experimental measurements and Standard Model (SM) predictions are commonly interpreted within new theoretical models. In this context, the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT) provides a general framework encompassing a broad class of models. However, exploring the enormous space of possible SMEFT operators rapidly becomes a challenging combinatorial problem: many operators can contribute simultaneously to different observables, their effects are correlated, and distinct operator combinations can provide comparably good descriptions of the data. In this work, we formulate the exploration of the SMEFT operator space as a sequential decision-making problem using reinforcement learning (RL). Our RL agent employs a lightweight actor-critic architecture with approximately 40K trainable parameters in total. Rather than searching for a single best-fit theory, our method is designed to discover a diverse set of high-quality theory hypotheses. We test the method in a complex phenomenological environment comprising 132 observables across several sectors of particle physics, including 24 measurements that deviate from their SM predictions by more than $1.5σ$. We show that the learned RL policy efficiently constructs multi-operator theories that substantially improve the global fit. Comparisons with independent random-subset and random-sequential searches demonstrate the ability of the RL policy to navigate this large combinatorial space. Our results establish RL as a systematic framework for exploring diverse patterns of new physics (NP) interactions within SMEFT.

</details>


### [311] [Deep Learning for Physicists: From Linear Regression to Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21295)
*Masanori Hanada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这是一份以物理直觉为讲解路径的神经网络与深度学习入门讲义，前半部覆盖从最大似然、线性回归到反向传播与核方法的基础内容，后半部转向自然语言处理（natural language processing）与大语言模型（large language models, LLMs），包括注意力机制、缩放定律、基于人类反馈的强化学习（reinforcement learning from human feedback, RLHF）与测试时扩展（test-time scaling）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 讲义源于作者在伦敦玛丽女王大学数学科学学院开设的入门课程，作者为物理学家，因而试图以物理直觉（physical intuition）而非繁重形式化（heavy formalism）解释概念，面向有物理或数学背景、希望自洽地进入现代机器学习（machine learning）的读者；同时学生对大语言模型内容的明确需求也推动了内容取舍。

Method: 采用课堂讲义式（lecture notes）的渐进组织方式：从最大似然（maximum likelihood）、最小二乘（least squares）、线性回归（linear regression）与（随机）梯度下降（(stochastic) gradient descent）出发，依次讲授神经网络、反向传播（backpropagation）、梯度消失（vanishing gradient）、批归一化与层归一化（batch and layer normalization）、过拟合与正则化（overfitting and regularization）、对抗样本（adversarial examples）及核方法（kernel method）；后半部分转向词嵌入（word embeddings）、循环网络与长短期记忆网络（LSTM）、注意力机制（attention mechanism）与 Transformer、大语言模型及其缩放定律（scaling laws）、GPT 发展史、指令微调（instruction tuning）、RLHF、幻觉（hallucination）、对齐伪装（alignment faking）、思维链推理（chain-of-thought reasoning）与测试时扩展。假设极少，甚至从矩阵乘法讲起，供读者按需跳过。

Result: 形成一条连贯且直觉化的学习路径，覆盖传统深度学习基础与当代大语言模型主题；内容面向英国学制三、四年级学生，并因跨院系选课而保持极低的前置要求，便于自学与跳跃式阅读。

Conclusion: 讲义不主张原创性（no originality is claimed），目标是以直观方式串联基础知识；内容截至 2025 年春季，未涵盖此后进展，但作者认为所强调的基本思想短期内不易过时。

Abstract: These lecture notes are based on an introductory course on neural networks and deep learning that the author taught in the School of Mathematical Sciences at Queen Mary University of London in 2023/24 and 2024/25. The author, however, is a physicist, and it shows: wherever possible, ideas are explained with physical intuition rather than heavy formalism. Readers with a background in physics or mathematics who want a self-contained, introductory route into modern machine learning may therefore find these notes particularly friendly.
  The notes start from maximum likelihood, least squares, linear regression and (stochastic) gradient descent. They then cover neural networks, backpropagation, the vanishing gradient problem, batch and layer normalization, overfitting and regularization, adversarial examples, and the kernel method. The second half turns to natural language processing: word embeddings, recurrent networks and LSTMs, the attention mechanism and Transformers, and large language models, including scaling laws, the history of GPT, instruction tuning, reinforcement learning from human feedback, hallucination, alignment faking, chain-of-thought reasoning and test-time scaling. The emphasis on large language models reflects requests from the students.
  The notes are aimed at third- and fourth-year students in the UK system. Because the course was also taken by students from other departments, very little is assumed: even basic material such as matrix multiplication is explained from scratch, so readers familiar with it can safely skip such parts. No originality is claimed; the aim is a coherent, intuitive path through the material. Developments after spring 2025 are not covered, but the basic ideas emphasized here are unlikely to go out of date soon.

</details>


### [312] [Mesoscale Neural Operators: Learning Nonlinear Constitutive Laws from Molecular Dynamics for Scalable Fracture Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19658)
*Yue Yu,Jihong Wang,Stewart Silling,Ziwei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出中介尺度神经算子（mesoscale neural operator, MNO），一种直接从分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）加载与位移数据学习的非局部（nonlocal）本构模型，可在更大尺度连续介质断裂模拟中使用，并通过归一化核与重标定临界键伸长保证跨尺度物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MD 模拟（molecular dynamics simulations）能刻画原子尺度断裂物理，但计算成本限制了其所需时空尺度的预测能力；已有机器学习代理模型（surrogate）缺乏物理一致性保证，因此需要一个既数据驱动又具跨尺度物理一致性的本构模型。

Method: 提出的 MNO 是一种数据驱动的非局部（nonlocal）本构模型，直接由 MD 加载与位移数据学习。核心思想是利用非局部模型的内禀特征长度（horizon）实现跨尺度迁移；通过两个结构要素保证一致性：使应变能密度在 horizon 重标度下不变的归一化核（normalized kernel），以及使能量释放率（energy release rate）跨尺度一致的临界键伸长（critical bond stretch）重标定。

Result: 在合成数据（synthetic data）上，MNO 及其学习流程成功识别出以非局部核与非线性本构关系形式出现的非局部连续介质多尺度模型；在粗粒化 MD 数据测试中，所学模型对 MD 应变能密度与临界能量释放率的匹配误差在 3% 以内，在分布外（out-of-distribution）拉伸测试中峰值传递力预测误差在 6% 以内；进一步在面积为训练样本 4000 倍且含初始缺陷的板上复现了动态脆性断裂（dynamic brittle fracture）主要特征，包括分叉（branch）与级联分叉网络（cascading branch networks）。

Conclusion: MNO 通过将物理一致性结构嵌入学习框架，实现了从 MD 数据到连续介质断裂模拟的跨尺度 bridging，并能在远超训练规模的问题上再现动态脆性断裂的关键形貌特征。

Abstract: Molecular dynamics (MD) simulations can capture the nanoscale physics of fracture at the atomistic level, but their computational cost prohibits predictions at the length scales and time scales needed for many applications. Machine learning approaches, on the other hand, have been proposed as heuristic surrogates for material fracture, yet they cannot guarantee the physical consistency of the predicted response. To address this shortcoming, we propose the mesoscale neural operator (MNO): a data-driven nonlocal constitutive model that is learned directly from MD loading and displacement data. After training, it can be used in continuum fracture simulations on much larger domains and grid sizes. The key idea of the MNO is to exploit the built-in characteristic length of a nonlocal model, called the horizon, to travel across scales. Physical consistency across scales is obtained by building two structural ingredients into the MNO: a normalized kernel that renders the strain energy density invariant under horizon rescaling, and a rescaled critical bond stretch that keeps the energy release rate consistent across scales. Through tests on synthetic data, we show that the MNO and its learning workflow indeed discover the nonlocal continuum multiscale model in the form of the nonlocal kernel and the nonlinear constitutive relation. We then apply the framework to coarse-grained MD data and find that in a test problem, the learned model matches the MD strain energy density and critical energy release rate within 3%. It also predicts the peak transmitted forces within 6% in out-of-distribution tension tests. As a further demonstration, the model is applied to a plate with 4000 times the area of the training specimens and containing an initial defect. In this problem, the model reproduces the main features of dynamic brittle fracture, including branch and cascading branch networks.

</details>


### [313] [A phase diagram for training and inference in language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19848)
*Zhuo-Yang Song,Zeyu Li,Qing-Hong Cao,Ming-xing Luo,Hua Xing Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将语言模型前向传播中的表示几何（representation geometry）刻画为相图上的推理流（inference flow），发现训练模型与未训练模型的流分别驻留于相图中心区域与坍缩/噪声区域，并用离散朗之万方程揭示二者差异归结为有效力场类型的差别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的行为由 Transformer 与语料共同决定，但 Transformer 内部表示几何与实际语言模型行为之间的关系仍是开放问题，需要一个统一的分析框架。

Method: 在给定层用两个集体坐标——文档可分性（document distinguishability）与文档维度（document dimension）——刻画文档的表示几何，构建以二者为坐标轴的相图，并将推理表示为沿深度方向的流动；进一步用离散朗之万方程（discrete Langevin equation）描述流动的演化。

Result: 未训练模型依初始化尺度（initialization scale）不同，其 156 条推理流大多流向相图的坍缩与噪声区域；而来自 5 个架构族的 8 个训练模型的推理流均进入并驻留于相图中心区域。训练与未训练流动的差异可归约为该区域内有效力属于鞍点型（saddle）还是稳定结点型（stable-node）。在 Pythia-1.4B 上，训练过程可被解释为一次相变（phase transition）。

Conclusion: 上述结果为表示几何与语言模型行为之间的关系提供了一个统一入口。

Abstract: A language model is a complex system whose behavior is determined jointly by the Transformer and the corpus, yet how the representation geometry in the Transformer relates to the actual behavior of language models remains open. We analyze how the representation geometry changes along the forward propagation of actual language models and characterize the representation geometry of a document at a given layer by two collective coordinates, document distinguishability and document dimension. On the phase diagram spanned by these collective coordinates, inference is represented as a flow along depth. We find that, for untrained models, depending on their initialization scale, most of the 156 inference flows move toward the collapse and noise regions of the phase diagram. By contrast, the inference flows of 8 trained models from 5 architecture families all enter and reside in the central region of the phase diagram. Describing the evolution of the flows with a discrete Langevin equation, we find that the difference between the inference flows of untrained runs and those of trained models reduces to whether the effective force on the flows in this region is of saddle or stable-node type. On Pythia-1.4B, we interpret the training process as a phase transition. These results give a unified gateway to the relation between representation geometry and language-model behavior.

</details>

# physics.data-an [[Back]](#toc)

### [314] [Numerical Integration Instabilities in Likelihood Fits: Mechanisms and Remedies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19355)
*Manqi Zhu,Gerry Bauer,Kai Yi,Xining Wang*

Main category: physics.data-an

TL;DR: 该论文基于CMS全粲四夸克（fully charmed tetraquark）搜寻中出现的异常低NLL值，用伪数据集（pseudo-dataset）研究数值积分（numerical integration）导致归一化不可靠、进而使似然极小化（likelihood minimization）被"困"于人为偏低NLL的机制，并提出变量变换（variable transformation）等解决手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率密度函数归一化（normalization）中的数值不稳定性可产生低于真实极小值的人为负对数似然（negative log-likelihood, NLL）值，即使拟合对数据的描述更差。近期CMS全粲四夸克候选者搜寻中非收敛拟合（non-converged fits）出现异常低NLL，促使作者系统考察该问题的成因与对策。

Method: 以该分析所用似然模型生成伪数据集，考察数值积分如何给出不可靠归一化、这些误差如何影响似然极小化。具体分析积分器停止判据（stopping decision）随拟合参数变化引起的归一化积分不连续（discontinuities），以及积分网格无法分辨强局域贡献（sharply localized contribution）时的失效；并比较收紧积分容差（integration tolerance）、自适应积分方法（如Gauss–Kronrod quadrature）与变量变换的效果。

Result: 即使在固定积分容差下，积分器的停止判据仍可随拟合参数改变，在归一化积分中引入不连续并传播至似然面（likelihood surface），使极小化器停在某个数值步长上而给出人为更低的NLL。收紧容差可缓解此类不连续，Gauss–Kronrod等自适应求积亦可改善数值稳定性。更严重的失效出现在网格无法分辨强局域贡献时：积分器可能虽严重低估归一化却仍满足其估计误差判据，使极小化器进入人为低NLL区域；此时收紧容差或改用其他自适应方法未必有效。

Conclusion: 合适的变量变换可改善强局域贡献的数值分辨能力，给出更准确的归一化积分并恢复拟合收敛（fit convergence），是针对自适应积分难以奏效情形的一种有效补救。

Abstract: Numerical instabilities in the normalization of probability density functions can produce artificially lower negative log-likelihood (NLL) values than the true minimum of a likelihood fit, even when the resulting fit provides a poorer description of the data. Motivated by anomalously low NLL values found in non-converged fits associated with a recent CMS search for fully charmed tetraquark candidates, we use a pseudo-dataset generated from a likelihood model based on that analysis to investigate how numerical integration can yield unreliable normalization results, how these errors affect likelihood minimization, and how they can be addressed. We find that, even at fixed integration tolerance, the stopping decision of a numerical integrator can change with the fit parameters, introducing discontinuities in the normalization integrals that propagate to the likelihood surface. Such discontinuities can trap the minimizer at a numerical step and yield an artificially lower NLL. Tightening the integration tolerance mitigates these numerical discontinuities, while adaptive integration methods, such as Gauss--Kronrod quadrature, can also improve the numerical stability. A more severe failure occurs when the integration grid cannot adequately resolve a sharply localized contribution. In this case, the integrator may satisfy its estimated-error criterion despite substantially underestimating the normalization, allowing the minimizer to enter a region with an artificially low NLL. Tightening integration tolerances or using alternative adaptive integration methods does not necessarily resolve sharply localized contributions. We demonstrate that a suitable variable transformation can improve their numerical resolution, yielding more accurate normalization integrals and restoring fit convergence.

</details>


### [315] [A Paired Monte Carlo Intensity-Ratio Method for Incorporating Detector Resolution in Amplitude Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20508)
*Zhenxuan Li,Liping Yang,Bai-Cian Ke,Liaoyuan Dong,Minggang Zhao*

Main category: physics.data-an

TL;DR: 提出成对蒙特卡罗强度比方法（paired Monte Carlo intensity-ratio method），以参考强度之比对蒙特卡罗归一化加权，近似纳入探测器分辨率效应，在模拟 \(D^+\to K^+\pi^+\pi^-\pi^0\) 衰变的重复抽样研究中显著减小了共振宽度拟合的偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限探测器分辨率（detector resolution）会使振幅分析（amplitude analysis）中提取的共振参数与拟合份额产生偏差，需在分析中合理纳入分辨率效应。

Method: 利用在成对的真值层（truth-level）与重建层（reconstructed kinematics）运动学上评估的参考强度比值，对蒙特卡罗归一化进行重加权，从而近似考虑探测器分辨率；似然计算仅依赖强度计算与归一化求和，无需逐事件做响应卷积（response convolution）。

Result: 对模拟 \(D^+\to K^+\pi^+\pi^-\pi^0\) 衰变的重复抽样研究（resampling study）显示，该方法大幅降低了分辨率导致的共振宽度拟合偏移。

Conclusion: 该强度比重加权方案可在避免逐事件响应卷积的前提下近似纳入分辨率效应，为振幅分析中抑制分辨率偏差提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Finite detector resolution can bias resonance parameters and fit fractions extracted from amplitude analyses. We present the paired Monte Carlo intensity-ratio method for incorporating resolution effects into amplitude analysis. The method uses ratios of reference intensities evaluated at paired truth-level and reconstructed kinematics to reweight the Monte Carlo normalization and approximately account for detector resolution. A resampling study of simulated $D^+\to K^+π^+π^-π^0$ decays shows that the method substantially reduces resolution-induced shifts in fitted resonance widths. Likelihood evaluation uses intensity calculations and normalization sums, avoiding a response convolution at each data event.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [316] [Sharp Uniform Fisher Information Bounds from Projective Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17288)
*Zhiyao Yang,Bowen Xiao,Huangjun Zhu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出以“Fisher floor”量化固定测量对多参量纯态度量学（multiparameter pure-state metrology）的信息保持能力，并在投影设计（projective design）阶数约束下给出其下界（Fisher guarantees），构造出维度无关的线性规模测量方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多参量纯态度量需要固定测量，且必须在未知输入纯态下仍保持信息量；因此需要一个与态无关的量化指标，并明确设计阶数（design order）本身能提供何种保证。

Method: 定义 Fisher floor 为测量在全部纯态与切方向（tangent directions）上保留的量子 Fisher 信息（quantum Fisher information）的最小比例；仅由投影设计阶数推导各维度 d≥2 的 Fisher 下界，并对 t=2 及奇数阶构造加权设计（weighted designs）验证紧性；再通过对任意 3-设计的采样与谱补全（spectral completion）构造测量。

Result: 所得保证在 t=2 与所有奇数阶 t≥3 处是紧的，有限加权设计可达到；偶数阶下界近乎紧。随阶数增大，保证趋近二分之一（单拷贝量子极限）。由 3-设计采样与谱补全得到的测量以 1-O(1/d) 概率具有线性多个结果和维度无关的 Fisher floor，并在 Hellinger 距离（Hellinger distance）下区分所有纯态。

Conclusion: 据此，基于 N 个副本的纯态最大似然估计（maximum-likelihood estimation）在所有纯态上一致达到 O(d/N) 的平均不忠实度（mean infidelity），在通用常数因子意义下最优。

Abstract: Fixed measurements for multiparameter pure-state metrology must remain informative without knowing the input state. We quantify this requirement by the Fisher floor, the smallest fraction of the quantum Fisher information that a measurement retains over all pure states and tangent directions. We determine the Fisher guarantees, namely lower bounds on Fisher floors, implied by projective design order alone in every dimension $d\ge 2$. These guarantees are sharp at order $t=2$ and every odd order $t\ge 3$, and finite weighted designs attain them; at even orders, our bounds are nearly tight. As the order increases, the guarantees approach one half, the single-copy quantum limit. Sampling and spectral completion of any 3-design yield, with probability $1-O(1/d)$, a measurement with linearly many outcomes and a dimension-independent Fisher floor. Such a measurement separates all pure states in Hellinger distance, so pure-state maximum-likelihood estimation from $N$ copies attains mean infidelity $O(d/N)$ uniformly over pure states, optimal up to a universal constant factor.

</details>


### [317] [Optimal Frobenius light cones for power-law systems in any dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18829)
*Laura Shou,An Le,Twesh Upadhyaya,T. C. Mooney,Alexey V. Gorshkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明了任意维度下幂律系统的最优 Frobenius Lieb-Robinson 界（Frobenius Lieb-Robinson bounds），确定了代数光锥到线性光锥的转变发生在指数 α=d+1，并刻画了多重光锥转变阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 Lieb-Robinson 界多针对算子范数（operator norm），而 Frobenius 范数意义下的最优界及光锥（light cone）结构的精确阈值尚不清晰，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用谱裁剪方法（spectral clipping method）与集中结果（concentration result），以支撑多尺度算子范数论证（multiscale operator-norm argument），从而导出最优的 Frobenius 范数指数。

Result: 证明代数到线性光锥转变出现在 α=d+1；另有从多重对数（polylogarithmic）到代数光锥的转变位于可和性阈值（summability threshold）α=d，以及在 α=d/2 处由体积内衰减转为不衰减的转变。

Conclusion: 该工作给出任意维度幂律系统中 Frobenius 意义下的最优光锥刻画，明确了 α=d+1、α=d 与 α=d/2 三个关键阈值所对应的行为转变。

Abstract: We prove optimal Frobenius Lieb-Robinson bounds for power-law systems with power-law exponent $α$ in any dimension $d$, in particular showing that the algebraic to linear light cone transition occurs at $α=d+1$. The proof uses a spectral clipping method and concentration result to allow for running a multiscale operator-norm argument which produces the optimal Frobenius norm exponent. Additionally, we prove there is a Frobenius light cone transition from polylogarithmic to algebraic at the summability threshold $α=d$, and a transition from decaying in volume to non-decaying in volume at $α=d/2$.

</details>


### [318] [Certifying quantum generative models beyond the Fisher trace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18955)
*Vicente Leyton-Ortega,Kathleen Hamilton*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文分析实振幅量子电路Born机（real-amplitude quantum circuit Born machine）的Fisher迹与核方法认证指标，指出这些诊断量在无噪声态矢量模拟中多半与参数数或振幅树活节点数挂钩，而非拟合质量，故基准上的“通过”可能只是复现了基准构造方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子生成模型的训练是否成功，通常依赖在有标签基准（labeled benchmark）上验证过的诊断指标；但此类诊断可能通过“还原基准构造方式”而虚假通过，因此需检验Fisher迹与核类认证指标的真实信息含量。

Method: 在半角旋转构成的实振幅电路中，比较经典与量子Fisher信息（classical/quantum Fisher information）；以Fisher特征值秩对应振幅树（amplitude tree）活节点数；对池化Wasserstein距离与最大均值差异（maximum-mean-discrepancy）考察其与优化器收敛及评分约定的依赖；对两类比特串缺陷族解析计算容差失配窗口，并在Hamming代价上证明容差比与最坏奇偶校验极小极大值（minimax value over unit-diagonal PSD kernels）。

Result: 实振幅半角旋转电路中经典Fisher信息几乎处处等于量子Fisher信息，其迹等于参数个数，不含拟合质量信息；Fisher特征值秩在稀疏基准上等于标签、在全支撑基准上无信息。Wasserstein与MMD分数随优化器收敛和评分约定变化；失配窗口仅在认证级容差下开启，并随量子比特数增加而关闭；超过某尖锐容差比后差异度投票冗余，最坏奇偶极小极大值随量子比特数指数小。近实验可分辨容差、千次采样以上时，差异度投票未改变任何判定。

Conclusion: 所考察的Fisher迹与核类认证指标在很大程度上不能反映拟合质量，且其误通过保证（false-pass guarantee）不延伸至器件噪声（device noise），提示此类诊断在近-term实验中的实用性有限。

Abstract: Whether a trained quantum generative model has learned its target is usually decided by a diagnostic validated on a labeled benchmark, and such a diagnostic can pass that validation by recovering how the benchmark was built. We analyze Fisher-trace and kernel-based certification of the real-amplitude quantum circuit Born machine in noiseless statevector simulation. For real-amplitude circuits built from half-angle rotations, the classical Fisher information equals the quantum Fisher information almost everywhere, so its trace equals the parameter count and carries no information about fit quality. The Fisher eigenvalue rank counts the live nodes of the amplitude tree: it coincides with the label on a sparse benchmark and is uninformative on a full-support one. The pooled Wasserstein and maximum-mean-discrepancy scores move with optimizer convergence and with the scoring convention. For two bitstring defect families on which both metrics have closed forms, we compute the tolerance-mismatch window in which the discrepancy rejects generators that the transport threshold admits; it opens only at certification-grade tolerances and closes as the qubit number grows. On the Hamming cost we prove a sharp tolerance ratio above which the discrepancy vote is redundant, and we obtain the exact worst-parity minimax value over all unit-diagonal positive-semidefinite kernels, which is exponentially small in the qubit number. In a numerical campaign at the tolerances a near-term experiment can resolve, the discrepancy vote changed no verdict at a thousand shots or more. The false-pass guarantee does not extend to device noise.

</details>


### [319] [Quantum Dynamics of Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19265)
*Takano Taira,Hiroyasu Tajima,Philip Taranto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文推导了高阶量子操作（quantum operations）在连续时间下最一般的完全正可除（CP-divisible）演化方程——量子梳主方程（quantum comb master equation, QCME），用于刻画“动力学的动力学”，并给出其类GKSL生成元的微观粗粒化条件与应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散时间量子通道（quantum channels）与连续时间GKSL主方程依赖无记忆近似（Born、Markov、 secular），在非马尔可夫（non-Markovian）等情形失效；此时仅追踪量子态不足，因为中间时刻的干预会扰动环境，多时刻关联（multi-time correlations）携带态演化无法反映的物理信息，故需以量子动力学而非量子态为本原对象。

Method: 在高阶量子操作框架下，将可控操作视为过程的输入（操作到操作的映射），推导此类对象最一般的连续时间、CP-divisible演化，即QCME；其生成元具GKSL式结构并含编码器—解码器关联项（encoder–decoder correlation terms），耦合不同时刻的干预；通过推广的Born–Markov粗粒化（coarse-graining）推导确定该结构的微观条件。

Result: QCME可给出超越约化态动力学的双时刻关联量的时间局域演化方程并可推广至更多时刻；展示独立GKSL方程无法描述的薛定谔图景与海森堡图景演化之间的关联；刻画过程层析（process tomography）动机下的量子通道演化；并获得环境影响下线性响应函数（linear response functions）与乱序关联器（OTOCs）的时间局域方程。

Conclusion: QCME为“动力学的动力学”提供统一的时间局域描述，将GKSL型框架扩展至多时刻操作层面，从而在非马尔可夫与受控干预场景中捕捉态演化之外的多时刻关联信息。

Abstract: Quantum channels (in discrete time) and Gorini--Kossakowski--Sudarshan--Lindblad (GKSL) master equations (in continuous time) are the central tools of open quantum systems, but they rest on memoryless approximations (Born, Markov, secular) that fail in many physically relevant settings, most notably non-Markovian dynamics. There, tracking the state alone is insufficient: interventions at intermediate times disturb the environment, so multi-time correlations carry physical information that the state evolution cannot capture. The framework of higher-order quantum operations addresses this by treating controllable operations as \textit{inputs} to a process that maps operations to operations, so that the primitive object is a quantum \textit{dynamics} rather than a quantum \textit{state}. Here, we derive the most general continuous-time, CP-divisible evolution of such an object, the \textit{quantum comb master equation} (QCME), which governs the quantum ``dynamics of dynamics''. Its GKSL-like generator includes encoder--decoder correlation terms coupling distinct temporal interventions. A generalized Born--Markov coarse-graining derivation identifies microscopic conditions for this structure.
