The QCME yields time-local evolution equations for two-time correlators beyond reduced-state dynamics and extends to more times. We show correlations between Schr"odinger- and Heisenberg-picture evolutions inaccessible to independent GKSL equations, describe quantum-channel evolution motivated by process tomography, and obtain time-local equations for linear response functions and OTOCs under environmental influences.

</details>


### [320] [Quantum sine imaginary-time evolution: sequential and QSVT implementations of a probabilistic ground-state filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19605)
*Victor A. Penas,Tamara Bottazzi,Marcelo Losada,Diego Tielas,Federico Holik*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出量子正弦虚时演化（quantum sine imaginary-time evolution, QSITE），用精确正弦滤波近似虚时演化以制备哈密顿量基态，并给出顺序式（LCU+中间电路测量）与相干式（基于量子奇异值变换 QSVT）两种电路实现及严格收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是设计一种基于辅助比特的概率性虚时滤波器（ancilla-based probabilistic imaginary-time filters），用于基态制备，并通过将构造专门化到优化哈密顿量来更清晰地揭示其结构。

Method: QSITE 以精确正弦滤波器（exact sine filter）近似虚时演化，提供两种电路：顺序式将滤波器作为线性组合酉算子（linear combinations of unitaries, LCU）的级联，借助中间电路测量（mid-circuit measurement）在各级间复用两个辅助比特；相干式基于量子奇异值变换（quantum singular value transformation, QSVT）。通过伸缩恒等式（telescoping identity）证明两者制备相同态且成功概率相同，仅概率放大方式不同。

Result: 证明了三点收敛保证：QSITE 算子一致收敛到精确 ITE 算子；制备态收敛到理想 ITE 态；滤波成功概率随级联步数单调增加。针对 MaxCut，因特征值为整数，失败概率随演化时间呈周期性并存在安全区（safe zones，首个解析定位、第二个数值定位）；当容许能量误差为基态能量的固定比例时，失败概率可在第一安全区内以随量子比特数线性增长的级联步数被界定在 1/2 以下。在最多 24 量子比特的随机 Erdös-Rényi 实例上给出数值结果，并与 QAOA 基准比较。

Conclusion: QSITE 是一种具有严格收敛性保证的概率性虚时滤波基态制备算法，两种电路实现等价；在 MaxCut 等优化问题上，其失败概率可通过安全区与线性增长的级联步数加以控制，数值实验显示其在最多 24 量子比特实例上可与 QAOA 对比评估。

Abstract: We propose quantum sine imaginary-time evolution (QSITE), a quantum algorithm for preparing ground states of a Hamiltonian. QSITE approximates imaginary-time evolution through an exact sine filter. We give two circuit implementations of this operator: a sequential one that applies it as a concatenation of linear combinations of unitaries (LCU), reusing two ancilla qubits across steps via mid-circuit measurement, and a coherent one based on the quantum singular value transformation (QSVT). A telescoping identity shows the two prepare the same state and succeed with identical probability, differing only in how that probability can be boosted. QSITE belongs to the family of ancilla-based probabilistic imaginary-time filters and we state rigorous convergence guarantees: we prove that the QSITE operator converges uniformly to the exact ITE operator, that the prepared state converges to the ideal ITE state, and that the filter probability increases monotonically with each concatenation step. Although the construction applies to an arbitrary Hamiltonian, we specialize it to optimization Hamiltonians, and this specialization is what exposes its structure more easily. Since MaxCut eigenvalues are integers, the probability of failure is periodic in the evolution time, yielding safe zones, the first located analytically and the second numerically. For MaxCut we further show that, when the tolerated energy error is a fixed fraction of the ground-state energy, the probability of failure can be bounded below 1/2 with concatenation steps growing linearly with the number of qubits in the first safe zone. We present numerical results for random Erdös-Rényi instances with up to 24 qubits and benchmark QSITE against QAOA.

</details>


### [321] [Graph spectra and late-time concurrence in a lossy reservoir network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20309)
*Haoyuan Tian,Gourab Ghoshal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究非相互作用量子比特（qubits）的局域环境通过网络耦合后如何改变晚期纠缠（entanglement）动力学，揭示图结构谱（graph spectrum）对存活集体模式的选择机制，并给出纠缠复现（revival）与单调衰减的阈值判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究多关注单个损耗环境中的纠缠衰减，而结构化耗散环境（structured dissipative environment）的拓扑如何影响长时纠缠行为尚不清楚，因此需探究环境网络结构对晚期纠缠动力学的调控作用。

Method: 构建模型：每个量子比特与一个有损耗玻色子记忆模（lossy bosonic memory mode）相互作用，记忆模之间按给定图（graph）进行相干跃迁（coherent hopping）；初态最多含两个激发（excitations）。通过图谱分析集体模式、以Perron本征向量刻画相对并发度（concurrence）分布，并推导扇区衰减率（sector decay rates）的普遍界 γ₂≥2γ₁。

Result: 图谱决定存活最久的集体模式，从而控制对并发度（pair concurrence）的衰减与空间分布；共振时非二分图（nonbipartite graphs）趋于由Perron本征向量确定的固定相对并发度模式，而二分图（bipartite graphs）支持两个等阻尼模式的干涉，当干涉超过存活模式的指数衰减时可产生任意晚的纠缠复现。文中给出区分复现与最终单调衰减的显式阈值，并证明双激发态的损耗可在不改变图与单激发初态的情况下驱动系统跨越阈值。

Conclusion: 结构化耗散环境的拓扑与经由损耗的激发再分布共同决定纠缠的长时行为；所导出的衰减率界 γ₂≥2γ₁ 及阈值分析为调控开放量子系统的长时纠缠提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate how coupling the local environments of noninteracting qubits into a network changes their late-time entanglement dynamics. Each qubit interacts with a lossy bosonic memory mode, with coherent hopping between memory modes determined by an underlying graph. The initial state contains at most two excitations. We show that the graph spectrum selects the collective modes that survive longest, thereby controlling both the decay and spatial distribution of pair concurrence. At resonance, nonbipartite graphs generically approach a fixed pattern of relative concurrence determined by the Perron eigenvector, whereas bipartite graphs support interference between two equally damped modes. This interference can produce arbitrarily late entanglement revivals when it overcomes the exponential decay of the surviving modes. We derive explicit thresholds separating revival and eventual monotonic decay, and show that loss from two-excitation states can drive the system across these thresholds without changing the graph or the initial one-excitation state. Underlying these results is a general bound on the sector decay rates, $γ_2\ge2γ_1$, valid at arbitrary uniform detuning. We also identify an exceptional equal-rate regime in which coherence between the vacuum and two-excitation sectors contributes to the leading concurrence. Together, these results establish how the topology of a structured dissipative environment and the redistribution of excitations through loss determine the long-time behavior of entanglement.

</details>


### [322] [Exceptional structure and Lifshitz criticality in a non-Hermitian Kitaev chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20327)
*Aritra Ghosh,Akash Sinha,Miloslav Znojil*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究一个可严格求解的非厄米（non-Hermitian）N格点Kitaev链，揭示其存在使多体能谱全部坍缩至零的奇异点（exceptional point, EP），并证明该坍缩可通过连续准厄米（quasi-Hermitian）路径逼近；在另一参数区域，相反符号配对振幅导致相反费米宇称基态交叉，其累积在热力学极限下形成Lifshitz转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解非厄米多体系统中奇异点（exceptional point, EP）坍缩与准厄米性（quasi-Hermitian）之间的关系，以及非厄米配对结构如何影响基态拓扑/能带行为；选取无格点间跃迁、配对产生与湮灭振幅不等的可解Kitaev链作为研究载体。

Method: 方法包括：构造精确可解的N格点非厄米Kitaev链模型（配对产生与湮灭振幅不等、格点间跃迁为零）；通过为N维单体问题构造显式正定度规（positive metric）并延伸至完整多体哈密顿量，建立连续准厄米路径；另在格点能交替取两个不相等的正值的参数区间分析基态交叉。

Result: 结果表明：链存在一个奇异点（EP），在该点所有多体能量坍缩至零，且最大Jordan块（Jordan block）的尺寸随N二次增长；该EP坍缩可通过参数空间中的连续准厄米路径逼近。在格点能交替的区间，相反符号的配对振幅可在准厄米区域内引起相反费米宇称（fermion parity）基态之间的交叉，这些交叉在热力学极限下累积并产生Lifshitz转变，其特征为粒子/空穴口袋（particle-/hole-pocket）形成、二次型临界色散（quadratic critical dispersion）以及非解析的基态能量密度。

Conclusion: 结论指出：该Lifshitz转变与EP边界保持分离；当配对为平衡的实数配对（balanced real pairing）时，该转变不再存在。研究揭示了非厄米Kitaev链中EP坍缩、准厄米性与基态能带转变之间的结构关系。

Abstract: We study an exactly solvable non-Hermitian $N$-site Kitaev chain with unequal pair-creation and pair-annihilation amplitudes and vanishing intersite hopping. We show that the chain supports an exceptional point (EP) at which all the many-body energies collapse to zero and the largest Jordan block grows quadratically with $N$. By constructing an explicit positive metric for the associated $N$-dimensional one-body problem which admits an extension to the full many-body Hamiltonian, we demonstrate that this EP collapse can be approached through a continuous quasi-Hermitian path in the parameter space. Further, in a different regime with onsite energies alternating between two unequal positive values, we show that pairing amplitudes of opposite signs can lead to crossings between ground states of opposite fermion parity within a quasi-Hermitian regime. Their accumulation in the thermodynamic limit produces a Lifshitz transition, characterized by particle-/hole-pocket formation, quadratic critical dispersion, and a nonanalytic ground-state energy density. We show that this transition remains separated from the EP boundary and ceases to exist for balanced real pairing.

</details>


### [323] [Unruh-induced decoherence and entanglement degradation in Ramsey interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19395)
*E. D. C Brandão,O. P. de Sá Neto,F. C. E. Lima,H. A. S. Costa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种改进的拉姆齐干涉（Ramsey interferometry）方案，通过条纹可见度（fringe visibility）探测与安鲁效应（Unruh effect）相关的退相干，并表明在特定条件下联合条纹可见度与纠缠度（concurrence）完全一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安鲁效应（Unruh effect）所关联的退相干难以直接观测，需要可行的量子探测方案；同时希望建立干涉对比度与纠缠退化之间的定量联系。

Method: 采用耦合至闵可夫斯基真空（Minkowski vacuum）中无质量标量场（massless scalar field）的安鲁-德维特探测器（Unruh-DeWitt detectors），相互作用仅引起纯退相位（pure dephasing）而无内能级跃迁；针对有限空间尺寸的匀加速探测器导出解析退相位函数（dephasing function），并将方案推广至一个惯性、一个匀加速的两探测器贝尔态（Bell state）情形。

Result: 解析退相位函数可将惯性真空涨落与热安鲁贡献分离；当热贡献占主导时，条纹可见度按与固有加速度（proper acceleration）成正比的速率指数衰减；在双分体情形中，当交叉项贡献消失时，联合条纹可见度与 concurrence 完全相等。

Conclusion: 结果表明可通过干涉对比度直接量化纠缠退化（entanglement degradation），为安鲁效应相关的退相干提供了一种可操作的干涉测量判据。

Abstract: We propose a modified Ramsey interferometric protocol to probe decoherence associated with the Unruh effect through fringe visibility. The scheme employs Unruh-DeWitt detectors coupled to a massless scalar field in the Minkowski vacuum through an interaction that induces pure dephasing without transitions between their internal energy levels. For a uniformly accelerated detector with finite spatial extent, an analytical dephasing function separates inertial vacuum fluctuations from the thermal Unruh contribution. When the thermal contribution dominates, fringe visibility decays exponentially with a rate proportional to the proper acceleration. The bipartite extension considers two detectors initially prepared in a Bell state, one inertial and the other uniformly accelerated, while retaining correlations mediated by the scalar field. In particular, we find that the joint fringe visibility coincides exactly with concurrence when the cross-term contribution disappears. This suggests that the entanglement degradation can be quantified directly from the interference contrast.

</details>


### [324] [A Numerical Approach to Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19646)
*Grace Murray,Siyue Zhu,Angeline Hu,Kaitlyn S. Yasumura,Ioannis G. Kevrekidis,Trung V. Phan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种数值方法，可同时搜索经典几何曲面上的矩阵量子化（matrix quantization）与相容泊松结构（Poisson structure），并由此得到关于Weyl量子化模糊球面（fuzzy sphere）维数上界的no-go定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵量子化与相容泊松结构的联合构造缺乏系统方法，需要一种能同时确定不可约厄米矩阵表示与泊松乘子的数值途径。

Method: 通过最小化惩罚Casimir约束与对易子约束违反的损失函数，训练泊松乘子（Poisson multiplier）并获得不可约厄米矩阵表示；结合数值研究与理论分析。

Result: 证明对于具非退化轴向谱的Weyl量子化模糊球面，矩阵维数不能超过非恒定多项式轴向泊松乘子的次数；方法亦推广至非多项式泊松乘子。

Conclusion: 框架可扩展至一般几何流形与量子化方案，并为Schild弦理论及时空历史量子化的研究提供实际起点。

Abstract: We introduce a numerical approach that simultaneously searches for matrix quantizations and compatible Poisson structures on classical geometric surfaces. Our method identifies irreducible Hermitian matrix representations and a trainable Poisson multiplier by minimizing a loss function that penalizes violations of the Casimir and commutation constraints. Through numerical investigations and subsequent theoretical analysis, we establish a no-go theorem showing that, for Weyl quantized fuzzy spheres with nondegenerate axial spectrum, the matrix dimension cannot exceed the degree of the nonconstant polynomial axial Poisson multiplier. We further extend our approach to nonpolynomial Poisson multipliers, demonstrating its versatility and potential to drive novel discoveries. Although here we focus on Weyl quantized fuzzy spheres, the framework is extensible to general geometric manifolds and quantization schemes. Our approach also provides a practical starting point for investigating Schild string theory and the quantization of spacetime histories.

</details>


### [325] [A simple solvable $Z_N$ Hamiltonian in any dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20874)
*Yuguan Li,Murray T. Batchelor*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文由 Baxter-Fendley 链按任意有限有向简单横图耦合构造局域 $Z_N$ clock 哈密顿量，并在开放纵向边界下通过非局域幺正变换实现链间解耦，得到自由 parafermion 谱；周期纵向边界下则由相邻链荷固定闭合键扭曲。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标在于将一维 Baxter-Fendley 链的可解性推广到由横图耦合而成的多维局域自旋模型，构造可解析处理的高维 $Z_N$ 晶格模型。

Method: 以有界边长与顶点度数保证有限程多自旋相互作用，在 $d$ 维矩形晶格上得到含 $2d$ 格点体项的哈密顿量；对开放纵向边界构造显式非局域幺正变换使链解耦，对周期纵向边界用相邻链荷确定闭合键扭曲并分荷扇区对角化。

Result: 开放纵向边界下模型等价于自由 parafermion 谱，且在矩形晶格上对横向量子边界的开放与周期任意组合均成立；周期纵向边界下各联合荷扇区的能量为相应扭曲周期链能量之和。

Conclusion: 该构造给出了一类由 Baxter-Fendley 链编织而成的可解 $Z_N$ clock 模型族，并可推广至非矩形几何，例如垂直于 Penrose 菱形密铺（Penrose rhombus tiling）的三维链阵列。

Abstract: We construct local $Z_N$ clock Hamiltonians from Baxter-Fendley chains coupled according to an arbitrary finite oriented simple transverse graph. Bounded edge lengths and vertex degrees give finite-range multispin interactions, with $2d$-site bulk terms on a $d$-dimensional rectangular lattice. For open longitudinal boundaries, an explicit nonlocal unitary transformation decouples the chains and gives a free parafermion spectrum. On rectangular lattices this holds for any combination of open and periodic transverse boundaries. For periodic longitudinal boundaries, neighbouring chain charges fix the closing-bond twists. In each joint charge sector, the energies are sums of the corresponding twisted periodic-chain energies. As an example, we construct a three-dimensional array of chains perpendicular to a Penrose rhombus tiling.

</details>


### [326] [Static Bounds on Thermal Fast Scrambling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20958)
*Yixuan Ma,Weikang Li,Dong-Ling Deng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对SYK模型与随机k-local自旋系统严格证明了在任意固定温度下的对数型扰乱时间下界，系数为β/(2π)，无需假设实时因子化，从而为热态快速扰乱时标提供了严格基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Maldacena-Shenker-Stanford混沌界（chaos bound）限制乱序关联（out-of-time-order correlations）指数增长率为2π/β，但其前提是耗散时间后时序关联因子化；在任意固定温度下建立严格的扰乱时间对数下界仍是开放问题，尤其在无序系统（disordered systems）中Gibbs态依赖于驱动动力学的同一随机耦合。

Method: 利用Gibbs态平方根（square root of the Gibbs state）的算符尺度分布（operator-size distribution）量化局域探针如何改变其结构；该静态信息在复时间解析带（analytic strip）边缘对热关联函数给出与时间无关的一致界，再借助解析性（analyticity）限制扰乱的产生速率。此方法不假设实时因子化。

Result: 对SYK模型与随机k-local自旋系统，证明以趋于1的概率扰乱时间至少为(1-ε)[β/(2π)]log N，对任意0<ε<1成立，系数β/(2π)与混沌界所暗示的一致。该界对SYK模型中每一对固定的不同Majorana模成立，对自旋模型中任意固定单格点源的靶平均（target average）成立。

Conclusion: 结果将热态快速扰乱时标（fast-scrambling timescale）置于严格基础之上，并表明Gibbs态的静态性质可以约束实时信息传播（real-time information spreading）。

Abstract: Scrambling, the process by which initially localized quantum information spreads across an interacting many-body system, is a hallmark of quantum chaos and plays a central role in understanding black hole dynamics. In thermal systems, the chaos bound of Maldacena, Shenker and Stanford limits the exponential growth rate of out-of-time-order correlations to $2π/β$, assuming that time-ordered correlation functions factorize after a dissipation time. Establishing the corresponding rigorous logarithmic lower bounds on scrambling times at arbitrary fixed temperature has remained an open problem, most notably in disordered systems, where the Gibbs state depends on the same random couplings that drive the dynamics. Here we prove such bounds at every fixed temperature for the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) model and for random $k$-local spin systems, without assuming real-time factorization. Our approach uses the operator-size distribution of the square root of the Gibbs state to quantify how local probes modify its structure. This static information bounds the thermal correlator uniformly in time along the edges of its analytic strip in complex time, and analyticity then limits how rapidly scrambling can develop. For both families of models, we prove that with probability tending to one the scrambling time is at least $(1-ε)[β/(2π)]\log N$ for any $0<ε<1$, with the coefficient $β/(2π)$ suggested by the chaos bound. The bound holds for each fixed pair of distinct Majorana modes in the SYK model and for the target average of any fixed single-site source in the spin model. These results place the thermal fast-scrambling timescale on a rigorous footing and show how static properties of the Gibbs state can constrain real-time information spreading.

</details>


### [327] [Plenitude of ideal correlations explains quantum theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21448)
*Adán Cabello*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出一条“可实现性原则”：在一切可观测量（observable）均可进行理想测量（ideal measurement）的理论中，任何可能的相关性（correlation）都应在物理上可实现；据此唯一地导出量子理论的希尔伯特空间形式体系（Hilbert-space formalism）与玻恩规则（Born rule）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子理论的希尔伯特空间结构与玻恩规则通常作为公设引入，缺乏统一的原理性解释；本文试图以单一物理原则对其加以刻画与推导。

Method: 采用公理化/原理性推导（principle-based derivation）路径：定义理想测量为可重复且不扰动任何相容可观测量（compatible observable）的测量，并施加“凡在此类理论中可能的相关性均物理可实现”这一原则，考察满足条件的理论形式。

Result: 满足该原则的理论被限制为量子理论的形式：希尔伯特空间形式体系与玻恩规则被唯一地筛选出来（singled out）。

Conclusion: “所有可能相关性均可物理实现”可充当量子理论的一条基本原理，为希尔伯特空间表述与玻恩规则提供统一的概念基础（摘要未给出进一步的实验或推广讨论）。

Abstract: We show that the Hilbert-space formalism and the Born rule of quantum theory are singled out by the following principle: every correlation that is possible in a theory in which every observable admits an ideal measurement is physically realizable. An ideal measurement is one that yields the same outcome when repeated and does not disturb any compatible observable.

</details>


### [328] [Entanglement distillation, nonlocal magic, and state sharing in an isospectral non-Hermitian bipartite model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21490)
*Chenhuan Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作基于可逆非幺正相似变形（invertible nonunitary similarity deformation）构造的双量子比特哈密顿量族，在保持谱不变的前提下系统刻画纠缠可蒸馏性、非局域性（CHSH）与多拷贝“魔法”（magic）资源，并强调在多拷贝极限比较中须区分不同操作类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在研究谱不变但本征态在固定物理内积下变化的哈密顿量族，厘清噪声参数如何决定贝尔对蒸馏速率，并在多拷贝极限下区分量子比特稳定子（stabilizer）与费米高斯（fermionic Gaussian）等不同资源理论的资源量。

Method: 将变形后的归一化右本征态嵌入含局域退相位（local dephasing）与含噪线路制备的两参数最大相关族；利用哈希（hashing）与匹配的对角相对熵界（diagonal relative-entropy bound）计算任意有限张量幂下的精确可蒸馏纠缠与PPT比较距离；并给出CHSH优化值、有限样本界及固定Clifford解码下的CCZ集中概率公式。

Result: 相关PPT催化剂（correlated PPT catalysts）不能提升贝尔对蒸馏速率；优化CHSH值为\(2\sqrt{1+t^2}\)，任何非零相干态均违反CHSH，而完全退相位态为可分离态；纯输入下二项式Schmidt谱给出密度趋于零的对数集体优化非局域魔法，尽管纠缠密度为正且两两框架魔法为广延量；固定Clifford解码下六拷贝与八拷贝CCZ集中概率分别为\(P_6=\frac12(1-2^{-\mathcal M_\gamma})\)与\(P_8=1-2^{-\mathcal M_\gamma}\)。

Conclusion: 该族支持显式交换递归（swapping recursions）、对称分辨纠缠（symmetry-resolved entanglement）以及稳定子与费米高斯资源的比较；相关计算表明，比较多拷贝极限时必须保持相应操作类彼此分离。

Abstract: An invertible nonunitary similarity deformation leaves a two-qubit Hamiltonian's spectrum unchanged while varying a selected normalized right eigenstate in the fixed physical inner product. We embed this state in a two-parameter maximally correlated family that includes local dephasing and noisy circuit preparation. Hashing and a matching diagonal relative-entropy bound give the exact distillable entanglement and the PPT comparison distances at every finite tensor power. Correlated PPT catalysts cannot improve the Bell-pair distillation rate. The optimized CHSH value is \(2\sqrt{1+t^2}\): every state with nonzero coherence violates CHSH, while complete dephasing is separable. A calibrated finite-sample bound and two equal-fidelity preparations clarify which noise parameters determine the rate. For pure inputs, the binomial Schmidt spectrum gives logarithmic collectively optimized nonlocal magic with vanishing density, despite a positive entanglement density and extensive pairwise-frame magic. Fixed Clifford decoding gives six- and eight-copy CCZ concentration probabilities \(P_6=\frac12(1-2^{-\mathcal M_γ})\) and \(P_8=1-2^{-\mathcal M_γ}\). The same family permits explicit swapping recursions, symmetry-resolved entanglement, and a comparison of qubit stabilizer and fermionic Gaussian resources. These calculations keep the relevant operation classes separate when comparing many-copy limits.

</details>


### [329] [Gate tunable spin splitting for Andreev spin qubits in Ge quantum well heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18888)
*Avani Vivrekar,Benjamin D. Woods,Mark Friesen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究平面Ge约瑟夫森结中Andreev自旋量子比特（Andreev spin qubit, ASQ）的自旋劈裂，发现其与沟道宽度无关（不同于InAs纳米线），并探讨利用该特性抑制电荷陷阱（charge trap）影响的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ge基超导体-半导体异质结（super-semi heterostructure）可同位素纯化以消除核自旋退相干，是有前景的ASQ平台；但其空穴型材料自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC）随动量呈立方关系，与常规ASQ材料（如InAs线性SOC）不同，需理解几何与栅控参数对ASQ操作的影响。

Method: 研究沟道宽度等几何性质与化学势（chemical potential）等栅可调性质对ASQ操作的相互作用，对比平面Ge约瑟夫森结（Josephson junction）与InAs纳米线的自旋劈裂（spin splitting）行为。

Result: 平面Ge约瑟夫森结中的自旋劈裂不依赖于结宽，与InAs纳米线形成对比；但电荷陷阱同时影响化学势与沟道宽度，使利用该特性抑制电荷陷阱有害效应的设想难以实现。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了ASQ中自旋劈裂的基本机制，并为优化具有立方SOC的其他材料中的ASQ提供指导。

Abstract: Ge-based super-semi heterostructures are a promising platform for implementing Andreev spin qubits (ASQs). For example, they can be isotopically purified to eliminate nuclear spins that cause decoherence. However, spin-orbit coupling (SOC), which is a key ingredient for ASQs, is cubic in momentum for hole-based materials like Ge, in contrast with more-conventional ASQ materials (e.g., InAs wires), for which the SOC is linear in momentum. In this work, we study the interplay between geometrical properties like the channel width, and gate-tunable properties like the chemical potential, on ASQ operations. Intriguingly, we find that the spin splitting in a planar Ge Josephson junction does not depend on the junction width, in contrast with InAs nanowires. We explore the possibility of exploiting this property to suppress the harmful effects of charge traps. However, this prospect is spoiled by the fact that charge traps also affect the chemical potential, in addition to the channel width. Our results provide insights into the fundamental mechanisms governing spin splitting in ASQs and offer guidance for optimizing ASQs in other materials with cubic SOC.

</details>


### [330] [Hidden Momenta due to Magneto-Electric Coupling in Non-Reciprocal Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18975)
*William S. McGregor,Niclas Westerberg,Robert Bennett*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从宏观量子电动力学（macroscopic quantum electrodynamics）的规范量子化（canonical quantisation）出发，推导了偶极发射体（dipole emitter）与时间反演对称破缺拓扑绝缘体（time-symmetry-broken topological insulator, TSB-TI）的相互作用哈密顿量，并计算了自发衰变率（spontaneous decay rate）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSB-TI具有由轴子耦合拉格朗日量（axionic coupling Lagrangian）描述的磁电耦合（magneto-electric coupling），但该耦合对偶极发射体相关可观测量的影响尚需在量子光学框架下明确刻画。

Method: 采用宏观量子电动力学的规范量子化方法，从轴子耦合拉格朗日量导出一个额外相互作用项，其依赖于TSB-TI固有的磁电耦合强度；在Born-Markov近似下，对一般电磁格林张量（electromagnetic Green's tensor）计算自发衰变率，并具体应用于平面平行板腔（planar parallel-plate cavity）情形。

Result: 所得哈密顿量较常规形式多出与磁电耦合强度成正比的相互作用项，该项将修正相关可观测量的测量结果；并给出了对应一般格林张量及平行板腔几何下的自发衰变率表达式。

Conclusion: 结果表明TSB-TI的磁电耦合可通过额外的哈密顿量项显著影响偶极发射体的自发衰变等量子光学可观测量，为在腔量子电动力学（cavity QED）体系中探测此类拓扑响应提供了理论依据。

Abstract: From a canonical quantisation approach of macroscopic quantum electrodynamics, we derive the interaction Hamiltonian due to a dipole emitter interacting with a time-symmetry-broken topological insulator (TSB-TI), described by an axionic coupling Lagrangian. This provides an additional interaction term dependent on the magneto-electric coupling strength inherent to the TSB-TI which will modify measurements on related observables. The spontaneous decay rate is calculated in the Born-Markov approximation for a general electromagnetic Green's tensor, and then specifically for the case of a planar parallel-plate cavity.

</details>


### [331] [Finding Low-Energy States of the Bernasconi Model via Simulated Transverse-Field Quantum Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19301)
*Shaashwat Sagar Kale,Rudranil Basu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究利用实时横场动力学（real-time transverse-field dynamics）从 LABS 问题（Low-Autocorrelation Binary Sequence）的深局域极小出发，在 N=20–30 的 GPU 态矢量模拟中验证其采样效率较最优经典基线提升约一个常数因子（深陷阱 11.9–35.2 倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LABS 问题即 Bernasconi 模型的基态问题，其能量景观（energy landscape）崎岖且含深而孤立的局域极小，经典方法易陷入陷阱；研究旨在考察以这些极小为初态的横场量子动力学能否帮助抵达更低能量构型。

Method: 采用 GPU 态矢量模拟（statevector simulation）研究 N=20–30 自旋的实时横场演化，以“每个已验证量子样本的逃逸概率”对比最速下降踢（steepest-descent kicks）、随机重启（random restarts）与禁忌搜索（tabu search）的“每次能量评估成功率”；并通过短时展开（short-time expansion）、各向同性自旋翻转界与自由横场混合器（free transverse mixer）界进行景观关联分析。

Result: 在固定场强与采样窗口下，相对最优经典基线，采样器在最深陷阱处取得 11.9–35.2 倍、次一能量层级 13.4–27.2 倍的样本效率提升，且跨尺寸呈常数因子改善；该场强下汉明空间（Hamming space）广泛扩散与低能态强偏好共存，并伴随位移随场强升高的陡增；N=28 时 t=30 的基态概率约为按相同平均能量拟合的规范代价预测（canonical cost prediction）的 1.9 倍。

Conclusion: 横场动力学在此参数区可常数因子地改善样本效率，但其机制与 Bernasconi 势及混合器性质相关；硬件估算表明直接电路实现所需代价比现有处理器能力高出两至三个数量级，实用性仍受限。

Abstract: The Low-Autocorrelation Binary Sequence (LABS) problem, the ground-state problem of the Bernasconi model, has a rugged landscape with deep, isolated local minima. We study real-time transverse-field dynamics initialized in these minima as a means of reaching lower-energy configurations. Using GPU statevector simulation for N = 20-30 spins, we compare the escape probability per verified quantum sample with the success rate per energy evaluation of steepest-descent kicks, random restarts and tabu search. Using the same field and sampling window across all system sizes, we find that, relative to the best classical baseline, the sampler achieves a factor of 11.9-35.2 for the deepest traps and 13.4-27.2 for the next energy tier. These ratios describe a constant-factor improvement in sample efficiency over the sizes studied. At the adopted field, broad Hamming-space spreading coexists with a strong preference for low-energy states. This regime follows a sharp increase in displacement as the field is raised. To connect this behaviour to the landscape, a short-time expansion relates the onset of escape to target distance and intermediate energies. Bounds on isotropic spin flips and on the free transverse mixer quantify the role of the Bernasconi potential in the observed transfer. At N = 28, the ground-state probability at t = 30 exceeds a canonical cost prediction fitted to the same mean energy by a factor of about 1.9. The cost of generating each quantum sample is assessed separately through a hardware estimate, which places a direct circuit implementation two to three orders of magnitude beyond present processor capabilities.

</details>


### [332] [Dressed higher-form symmetries in mixed state quantum matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19442)
*Tibor Rakovszky,Sarang Gopalakrishnan,Curt von Keyserlingk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出在混合量子态（mixed quantum states）中构造涌现的强高形式对称性（emergent strong higher-form symmetries），其关键工具是量子纠错（quantum error correction）中的（准）局域恢复信道（recovery channels），并证明在确定性恢复下这些对称性算符保持幺正性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子态中对称性通常因退相干而弱化，需要在希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space）上真正幺正作用的强对称性；本文旨在给出这类涌现对称性的系统构造方法。

Method: 利用从混合态到邻近简单（稳定子，stabilizer）模型的（准）局域恢复信道构造算符，并证明当恢复以适当的确定性（deterministic）方式进行时，所得算符仍为幺正算符。

Result: 作用于拓扑非平凡子流形（如环、面，loops、surfaces）的算符所导出的幺正算符继承不动点模型的逻辑代数（logical algebra）；涌现对称性的幺正性还允许定义其统计规律遵循不动点极限的涌现激发（emergent excitations）。

Conclusion: 该构造将量子纠错恢复与高形式对称性联系起来，为混合态中强涌现对称性及相应任意子式统计（statistics）提供统一框架。

Abstract: We describe how to construct emergent strong higher-form symmetries in mixed quantum states that act unitarily on Hilbert space. Our construction uses (quasi-)local recovery channels from quantum error correction to some nearby simple (stabilizer) model. We prove that when the recovery is done in an appropriate (deterministic) manner, the resulting operators continue to be unitary. When applied to operators acting on topologically non-trivial sub-manifolds (loops, surfaces), the resulting unitaries inherit the logical algebra of the fixed point model. More generally, the unitarity of the emergent symmetries allows one to define emergent excitations whose statistics follow those of the fixed point limit.

</details>


### [333] [Polarization waves in open-barrier quantum-dot cellular automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20017)
*Konrad Walus*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在开势垒（open-barrier）区间建立量子点元胞自动机（quantum-dot cellular automata, QCA）中小信号极化波（polarization waves）的闭式理论，并给出色散关系、阻尼、量子验证与温度上限估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QCA 几乎总是被当作双稳态数字逻辑使用，而单元内隧穿与单元间静电耦合相当或更强时的线性响应区间缺乏解析描述。论文旨在为该开势垒工作区提供闭式理论框架。

Method: 对细胞间 Hartree 近似（intercellular Hartree approximation, ICHA）的相干矢量方程在未极化态附近线性化，得到带低频截止的晶格色散；并以精确 Jordan-Wigner 解法与精确对角化（exact diagonalization）作为对照验证。

Result: 色散呈现低频截止、其下的倏逝带（evanescent band）以及 Klein-Gordon 长波极限，阻尼率依赖波数；当隧穿能不低于元胞壁能两倍时 ICHA 频率与精确值偏差在 6% 以内，且精确链在 ICHA 预测锁存（latching）的耦合区间仍支持波传播。结处波通过晶格散射矩阵（lattice scattering matrix）散射，仅在中频带趋于传输线值。

Conclusion: 基于低温热退相干（thermal dephasing）的估算给出约 13.6 K/THz 间隙的工作温度上限（对应保持相位超过 30 个周期的载波），表明该线性波机制的实际运行温度受限。

Abstract: Quantum-dot cellular automata (QCA) are almost always operated as bistable digital logic. We develop a closed-form theory of small-signal polarization waves in the open-barrier regime, where tunnelling within each cell is comparable to or stronger than the electrostatic kink coupling between cells, so that a line of cells responds linearly instead of latching. Linearizing the coherence-vector equations of the intercellular Hartree approximation (ICHA) about the unpolarized state gives a lattice dispersion with a low-frequency cutoff, an evanescent band below it and a Klein-Gordon long-wavelength limit, and relaxation toward the instantaneous equilibrium damps the waves at a rate that depends on the wavenumber. The exact Jordan-Wigner solution and exact diagonalization confirm the mode as a sharp quasiparticle, with ICHA frequencies within 6% of the exact ones once the tunnelling energy is at least twice the kink energy, and show that the exact chain also carries waves over a range of couplings where the ICHA predicts latching. A leading low-temperature estimate of thermal dephasing sets a ceiling on the operating temperature of about 13.6 K per terahertz of gap for a carrier that keeps its phase over 30 periods. At junctions the waves scatter through a lattice scattering matrix that reaches the familiar transmission-line value only at mid-band.

</details>


### [334] [Mixed-state topological transitions with hidden long-range correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20159)
*Ze-Min Huang,Frank Pollmann,Sebastian Diehl*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究考察在保持保护对称性（平均意义上）的 onsite 退相干作用下，一维对称保护拓扑态（symmetry-protected topological state）向平庸乘积态的转变，揭示出对称twisted系综中隐藏的长程关联与临界发散，并可通过对称分辨的测量结果直接观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规局域关联在整个转变过程中保持短程，导致退相干驱动的拓扑—平庸转变难以用标准局域观测量刻画，因此需要寻找能够揭示该转变的隐藏序参量与可观测判据。

Method: 考虑 onsite 退相干且平均保持保护对称性（protecting symmetry）的设定，构建按对称荷（symmetry charge）加权状态的对称twisted系综（symmetry-twisted ensemble），分析其中的twisted关联与twisted配分函数（twisted partition function），并对测量过程进行数值模拟。

Result: 局域关联虽保持短程，但twisted系综中存在不衰减的隐藏长程关联；在转变点，twisted关联长度（twisted correlation length）发散，twisted配分函数出现奇异性。

Conclusion: 退相干驱动的拓扑—平庸转变具有隐藏临界行为，其特征可由对称分辨的测量结果（symmetry-resolved measurement outcomes）直接提取，因而在现有量子平台的中观系统尺度（mesoscopic system sizes）上即可观测，数值模拟支持了这一可行性。

Abstract: We study a decoherence-driven transition from a one-dimensional symmetry-protected topological state to a trivial product state. The decoherence acts onsite while preserving the protecting symmetry on average. Although conventional local correlations remain short-ranged across the transition, we uncover hidden nondecaying long-range correlations in a symmetry-twisted ensemble that weights states according to their symmetry charge. At the transition, the twisted correlation length diverges and the twisted partition function becomes singular. These signatures can be directly extracted from symmetry-resolved measurement outcomes, making the hidden critical behavior accessible on current quantum platforms at mesoscopic system sizes. We demonstrate this through numerical simulations of the measurement process.

</details>


### [335] [Quantum geometric spectroscopy with Schrödinger cat states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20434)
*Shohei Imai,Marcelo F. Ciappina*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种光谱学方法，利用薛定谔猫态光（Schrödinger-cat light）的量子退相干（quantum decoherence）读出物质的量子几何（quantum geometry），类比光学吸收读出能量结构。核心结论是猫分支退相干可由动力学量子几何量化，并可在光与物质间建立量子几何张量的分配律，从而实现量子度规与贝里曲率的光学读出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统吸收光谱仅读取物质的能级结构，缺乏直接探测量子态几何与涨落的谱学手段。作者希望将通常被视为量子信息损失的猫态退相干转化为物质信息的载体，建立直接探测量子几何的光谱学原理。

Method: 以两相干态叠加的猫态为探针，利用出射光分支间相干性（interbranch coherence）编码两支驱动下物质态的重叠。当两分支电磁场差异微弱时，用动力学量子几何刻画猫分支退相干；并对平衡态物质采用选频小猫探针（kitten probes），建立光与物质间量子几何张量的分配律。

Result: 分支退相干可量化为表征物质态流形上无穷小位移的动力学量子几何；平衡态下可通过出射胡西米函数（Husimi function）中“星形零点”的增亮实现量子度规与贝里曲率（Berry curvature）的光学读出；非平衡纯态下短脉冲猫探针可借助瞬态量子度规提供多体纠缠（multipartite entanglement）的时间分辨见证，并可由零拍测量（homodyne measurements）提取。

Conclusion: 把猫态退相干从信息损失转为物质信息载体，确立了直接探测量子态几何与涨落的光谱学原理，可与基于响应的传统光谱学并列使用。

Abstract: We propose a spectroscopic approach that reads out the quantum geometry of matter from the quantum decoherence of Schrödinger-cat light, in the same way that optical absorption reads out the energy structure of matter. For a cat-state probe comprising a superposition of two coherent states, the interbranch coherence of the outgoing light encodes the overlap between the matter states driven by the two branches. We show that, when the electromagnetic fields of the two branches differ only slightly, cat-branch decoherence is quantified by dynamical quantum geometry, which characterizes infinitesimal displacements on the matter-state manifold. For frequency-selective kitten probes of equilibrium matter, we further establish a partition law for the quantum geometric tensor between light and matter, enabling optical readout of matter quantum metric and Berry curvature through, for example, the brightening of a stellar zero in the outgoing Husimi function. For nonequilibrium matter in a pure state, short-pulse cat probes also provide a time-resolved witness of multipartite entanglement through the transient quantum metric, which can be extracted from homodyne measurements of the outgoing light. By turning cat-state decoherence, conventionally regarded as a loss of quantum information, into a carrier of information about matter, this approach establishes a spectroscopic principle that directly probes quantum-state geometry and fluctuations alongside conventional response-based spectroscopy.

</details>


### [336] [Relaxation Critical Dynamics of Strong-to-Weak Spontaneous Symmetry Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20581)
*Jiaqiang Li,Xianquan Yan,Han-Ze Li,Shuai Yin,Shuo Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究监控（monitored）Z₂量子线路中强弱自发对称破缺（strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking, SW-SSB）态的临界弛豫，将问题精确映射为两个嵌套的键渗流（bond-percolation）问题，并用初始标度维度（initial scaling dimension）与观测维度（observation dimension）刻画自相关指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SW-SSB是混合态（mixed states）特有的现象：强对称性破缺而弱对称性保持，需要两个序参量描述，而纯态（pure states）无法实现这种构型；论文旨在刻画其两个序参量的临界弛豫行为。

Method: 将监控Z₂线路精确映射为两个嵌套的键渗流问题，初始态作为每个问题的时间边界条件（有序则“接通”（wired），否则“自由”（free））；自相关指数分解为初始标度维度与观测维度之和，并用稳定子（stabilizer）模拟验证理论预言，同时提出无需后选择（postselection）的实验方案。

Result: SW-SSB初态仅使强对称序参量对应的边界条件“接通”，为纯态所不能实现；对SW-SSB态，弱对称序参量的初始维度为1/3、强对称序参量为0，各自处于其临界点；等时关联函数（equal-time correlator）只给出观测维度。单量子比特初始值的可解码概率（decoding probability）仅由系统本身随时间以幂律衰减，而系统与环境联合则保留该信息。

Conclusion: 结果表明存在一种纯态无法触及的边界临界性（boundary criticality），并给出可通过实验测量这些指数、无需后选择的方案。

Abstract: Strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking (SW-SSB) is unique to mixed states: strong symmetry is broken while weak symmetry is preserved, requiring two order parameters. We study the critical relaxation of both order parameters in a monitored $\mathbb{Z}_2$ circuit, which maps exactly onto two nested bond-percolation problems. The initial state acts as a temporal boundary condition for each problem, wired if the state is ordered in the corresponding order parameter and free otherwise. An SW-SSB initial state wires only the strong-symmetry order parameter, a configuration that no pure state realizes. Each autocorrelation exponent is the sum of an initial scaling dimension, set by the initial state, and an observation dimension, set by the observation time, whereas the equal-time correlator gives only the observation dimension. For the SW-SSB state, the initial dimension is $1/3$ for the weak-symmetry order parameter and $0$ for the strong-symmetry one, each at its own critical point. These initial dimensions also set how fast a stored bit is lost. The probability of decoding the initial value of a single qubit from the system alone decays as a power law, whereas system and environment together retain it. Stabilizer simulations confirm the theoretical prediction, and we propose an experimental protocol that measures these exponents without postselection. Our results reveal a form of boundary criticality inaccessible to pure states.

</details>


### [337] [Genuine multipartite entanglement in ring-shaped Hubbard models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20756)
*K. Shahzad,S. Pittalis,G. Goldoni,R. Di Felice,F. Troiani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文量化了标准与扩展 Hubbard 环中自旋态的真正的多体纠缠（genuine multipartite entanglement, GME）及其探测概率，指出在位排斥增强 GME，且强相互作用半填充下探测概率随自旋数仅弱衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋态可通过在若干格点探测电子（作为粒子标签）而从多电子系统中提取，但此类自旋态通常为混合态，其真正的多体纠缠及其可探测性需系统评估。

Method: 在标准与扩展 Hubbard 环（Hubbard rings）中，对由格点电子探测所提取的（一般为混合的）自旋态计算 GME 与相应探测概率，并考察在位排斥、填充、温度与掺杂的影响。

Result: 在位排斥通过抑制电荷涨落（charge fluctuations）增强 GME；强相互作用半填充（half filling）下探测概率随自旋数仅弱下降；GME 在低于单重态—三重态能隙（singlet–triplet gap）的温度下保持稳健，并可因掺杂而增强；扩展 Hubbard 模型中自旋密度波向电荷密度波（charge-density waves）的转变表现为 GME 与探测概率的骤降。

Conclusion: 格点电子探测所提取的自旋态可承载稳健的 GME，其大小与可探测性由相互作用、填充、温度与掺杂共同调控，并可作为区分自旋/电荷密度波相的信号。

Abstract: Spin states can be extracted from many-electron systems by detecting electrons at a set of lattice sites, which act as particle labels. Here we quantify the genuine multipartite entanglement (GME) of such spin states, which are generally mixed, in standard and extended Hubbard rings, together with the corresponding detection probability. We show that the on-site repulsion, by suppressing charge fluctuations, enhances the GME, and that in the strongly interacting regime at half filling the detection probability decreases only weakly with the number of spins. The GME is robust at temperatures below the singlet--triplet gap, and can even be enhanced by doping, in spite of the induced charge fluctuations. The transition from spin- to charge-density waves in the extended Hubbard model is signaled by an abrupt suppression of both the GME and the detection probability.

</details>


### [338] [Non-Hermitian edge accretion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20881)
*Wei Li,Ching Hua Lee,Fuxiang Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出并论证“非厄米边缘增生（NHEA）”现象：强耦合的竞争性非厄米趋肤效应（NHSE）通道使态在边缘反射时被放大而非衰减，从而违反OBC谱被PBC谱包围的通常认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为开边界条件（OBC）会阻断定向的NHSE放大并抑制Im(E)，因此OBC谱总被周期边界条件（PBC）谱包围；该工作旨在检验这一默认图像是否普遍成立。

Method: 通过解析与数值方法，分析强耦合竞争NHSE通道中非布洛赫本征旋量正交性（non-Bloch eigenspinor orthogonality）对反射系数及传播波与倏逝波平衡的调控作用。

Result: 发现态在边缘反弹时可被放大，表现为边缘反弹的周期性增长；NHEA工作于强耦合区，与由体（bulk）内放大反馈主导的传统临界NHSE标度行为本质不同。

Conclusion: NHEA仅需强耦合的拮抗NHSE链与适当的模式正交性，具有平台无关性，可在通用非厄米超材料（metamaterials）与量子模拟器中通过周期性增长的边缘反弹加以识别。

Abstract: Under the non-Hermitian skin effect (NHSE), it is generally taken for granted that the open boundary condition (OBC) spectrum is encircled by the periodic boundary condition (PBC) spectrum. Intuitively, this is because boundaries tend to block the directed NHSE amplification, thereby suppressing $\operatorname{Im}(E)$. In this work, we analytically and numerically demonstrate how this conventional scenario can be dramatically violated: In a phenomenon which we term non-Hermitian edge accretion (NHEA), strongly coupled competing NHSE channels conspire to amplify a state whenever it bounces off an edge, instead of attenuating it. This unexpected behavior arises from underappreciated consequences of non-Bloch eigenspinor orthogonality, which control the reflection coefficients as well as the balance between propagating and evanescent waves. Operating in the strongly coupled regime, NHEA is fundamentally distinct from conventional critical NHSE scaling, which is characterized by amplification feedback generated in the bulk, not the edges. Requiring only strongly coupled antagonistic NHSE chains with suitable mode orthogonality, the NHEA is platform-agnostic, and can be identified through periodically growing edge bounces in generic non-Hermitian metamaterials and quantum simulators.

</details>


### [339] [Reversal of dynamical backaction and cooling below the quantum backaction limit in Floquet-driven multimode optomechanical resonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21110)
*Menno H. Jansen,Roel Burgwal,Ewold Verhagen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作从理论与实验上证明，在多模光力谐振子中引入时变（Floquet）驱动可显著增强动力学反作用，使侧带未分辨的MHz机械模通过共享腔与侧带分辨的GHz辅助模耦合，从而将目标模的量子反作用极限替换为辅助模的占据数，实现低于单模QBA极限的冷却，并可在固定失谐下仅凭调制频率翻转冷却与放大的方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统腔光力学（cavity optomechanics）中，光阻尼的符号与大小均由激光失谐决定，冷却受量子反作用（quantum backaction, QBA）限制，该限制类似冷原子中的多普勒极限，阻碍了未进入分辨侧带区（resolved-sideband regime）机械模的量子控制。

Method: 理论结合实验：采用时变（Floquet）光场驱动多模光力谐振子，利用调制驱动使侧带未分辨的MHz模与侧带分辨的GHz模通过共享腔耦合，并以调制频率作为调控参数。

Result: 实验将MHz模冷却至47 ± 13个声子，约为其单模QBA极限的一半，且在相同腔内光子数下比单频反作用低一个数量级；同时在固定激光失谐下，仅通过调制频率即可将冷却与放大完全反转。

Conclusion: Floquet驱动将无需测量或反馈的开环量子控制（open-loop quantum control）扩展至分辨侧带区之外的机械谐振子，为激光冷却、量子态制备、量子转换与声子激光等应用提供了新的调控手段。

Abstract: Dynamical backaction describes how a driven cavity field responds with delay to mechanical motion, and can be used to cool that motion toward its quantum ground state or amplify it into self-oscillation. Control over this interaction underpins laser cooling, high-fidelity state preparation, quantum transduction, and phonon lasing. In conventional cavity optomechanics, both the sign and the magnitude of the optical damping are dictated by the laser detuning, and cooling is bounded by quantum backaction (QBA), the optomechanical analogue of the Doppler limit in cold atoms. We show, in theory and experiment, that dynamical backaction can be strongly enhanced in multimode optomechanical resonators driven by time-varying (Floquet) optical fields. A modulated drive couples a sideband-unresolved MHz mode to a sideband-resolved GHz mode through the shared cavity, replacing the QBA limit of the target mode by the occupancy of the auxiliary mode. Experimentally, we cool the MHz mode to $47 \pm 13$ phonons, about half its single-mode QBA limit and an order of magnitude below what single-tone backaction reaches at the same intracavity photon number. The character of the interaction can moreover be completely inverted at fixed laser detuning, turning cooling into amplification and vice versa, controlled solely by the modulation frequency. Floquet driving thus extends open-loop quantum control, which requires no measurement or feedback, to mechanical resonators outside the resolved-sideband regime.

</details>


### [340] [Experimentally relevant witness for non-Markovianity in discrete time crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21332)
*Rahul Ghosh,Victor Mukherjee,Yonatan Dubi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明有限关联时间 $τ_m$ 的环境会在离散时间晶体（DTC）频闪动力学上留下精确且简单的印记：Floquet谱分裂为马尔可夫谱与按 $e^{-T/τ_m}$ 均匀收缩的幺正谱副本，可作为非马尔可夫性（non-Markovianity）的实验可观测判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DTC 研究几乎总在 Born-Markov 近似（无记忆环境）下进行，而真实环境具有有限关联时间，其非马尔可夫效应对 DTC 动力学的影响缺乏清晰的解析刻画与可测判据。

Method: 分析后马尔可夫主方程（post-Markovian master equation），推导 DTC 频闪动力学的 Floquet 谱结构；并在两个结构无关的模型中验证：带非相干泵浦（incoherent pumping）的集体长程模型，以及带退相位（dephasing）的无无序 Ising 链。

Result: Floquet 谱分解为普通马尔可夫谱加一个纯幺正 Floquet 谱的副本，后者被 $e^{-T/τ_m}$ 均匀收缩（$T$ 为驱动周期）；当 $τ_m$ 超过 DTC 的马尔可夫寿命后，DTC 信号衰减率与所有系统参数无关，成为 $τ_m$ 的直接读出；阈值以下动力学已非马尔可夫但记忆隐藏在衰减中。两个模型中单一估计量（estimator）均可复原 $τ_m$。

Conclusion: DTC 可作为其自身环境非马尔可夫性的见证者（witness），其衰减行为及对可调系统参数的不敏感性提供了一种实验可行的储库关联时间测量手段。

Abstract: Discrete time crystals (DTCs) are almost always analyzed under the Born-Markov approximation, in which the environment is memoryless. We show that a reservoir with a finite correlation time $τ_m$ leaves an exact and strikingly simple imprint on the stroboscopic dynamics of DTCs, which can be used as an experimentally relevant witness for non-Markovianity. By analyzing a post-Markovian master equation we prove that the Floquet spectrum splits into the ordinary Markovian spectrum plus a replica of the purely unitary Floquet spectrum, uniformly contracted by $e^{-T/τ_m}$, where $T$ is the drive period. Consequently, the DTC signal decay rate becomes independent of any system parameter once $τ_m$ exceeds the Markovian lifetime of the DTC. The measured decay, and in particular its insensitivity to tunable system parameters, thus becomes a direct read-out of the reservoir correlation time. Below this threshold the dynamics is already non-Markovian, but the memory remains hidden in the decay. We confirm this in two structurally unrelated DTCs, a collective long-range model with incoherent pumping and a disorder-free Ising chain with dephasing, where a single estimator recovers $τ_m$. The time crystal thereby witnesses the non-Markovianity of its own environment.

</details>


### [341] [Quantum adiabatic and diabatic optimization: A Hilbert space engineering strategy with Rydberg arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21341)
*Rui Lin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究中性原子阵列中由阻塞链模型（blockaded-chain models）编码的量子退火过程，揭示基于希尔伯特空间几何（Hilbert-space geometry）工程可预测并缓解退火瓶颈，并提出利用目标兼容激发态的绝热捷径策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子阵列可编码任意连通性的组合优化问题，但已有工作仅研究单个编码小工具（encoding gadgets）的动力学，较大网络中的退火瓶颈（annealing bottlenecks）仍难以理解，缺乏预测框架。

Method: 研究一族受此类编码启发的阻塞链模型：边界项编码目标最大独立集（maximum independent set）问题，体几何（bulk geometry）诱导出与团（cliques）独立集相关的辅助优化问题；通过物理推导的团权重（clique weights）近似系统能谱，并在菱形链（diamond-chain）图族上测试利用目标兼容激发态（target-compatible excited states）的非绝热（diabatic）退火策略。

Result: 当目标问题与辅助问题一致时，低能模式（low-energy modes）主要位于目标子空间内；系统的能谱能量学可由物理推导的团权重近似刻画，据此可在菱形链图族上实施利用目标兼容激发态的非绝热退火。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了预测量子退火瓶颈的框架，并表明希尔伯特空间工程（Hilbert-space engineering）可作为改进中性原子量子优化协议的有效策略。

Abstract: Neutral-atom arrays provide a versatile platform for encoding combinatorial optimization problems with arbitrary graph connectivity [Nguyen et al., PRX Quantum 4, 010316 (2023)]. Although the dynamics of individual encoding gadgets has been studied [Bombieri et al., PRX Quantum 6, 020306 (2025)], understanding annealing bottlenecks in larger networks remains challenging. We investigate how engineering the Hilbert-space geometry controls quantum annealing in a family of blockaded-chain models motivated by these encodings. While boundary terms encode the target maximum independent set problem, the bulk geometry induces an auxiliary optimization problem associated with independent sets of cliques. Spectrally, the energetics of the quantum system can be approximated by physically derived clique weights. When the target problem and the auxiliary problems are consistent, the low-energy modes lie predominantly within the target subspace. This spectral structure motivates a diabatic annealing strategy that exploits target-compatible excited states, as tested on the diamond-chain graph family. These results provide a framework for predicting annealing bottlenecks, and demonstrate Hilbert-space engineering as a strategy for improving neutral-atom quantum optimization protocols.

</details>


### [342] [Quantitative measurement of microwave power at millikelvin temperatures: Comparison between dispersively and directly coupled superconducting qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21397)
*Asen L. Georgiev,Christoph Kissling,Fabian Kaap,Victor Gaydamachenko,Lukas Grünhaupt,Mark Bieler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作利用超导量子比特（superconducting qubit）的拉比频率（Rabi frequency）与入射电场振幅成正比的关系，将其用于毫开尔文（millikelvin）温度下的微波功率定量测量，并比较直接耦合与色散耦合（dispersive coupling）两种方案的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两级系统（two-level system）的拉比频率正比于入射电场振幅，因此可作为电场的定量传感器；此概念尚未在稀释制冷机（dilution fridge）中的超导量子比特上系统验证，而低温微波测量缺乏可溯源性（traceability）。

Method: 设计包含三个直接耦合至中央微波馈线（feedline）的 transmon 量子比特与三个经读出谐振器（readout resonator）色散耦合的 transmon 量子比特的超导电路；对两种耦合方案的功率传感方法分别建模，并基于重复测量估计不确定度。

Result: 直接耦合量子比特覆盖 −155 至 −130 dBm，色散耦合量子比特覆盖 −95 至 −55 dBm；在 6.0 GHz 下估算室温至制冷机毫开尔文级的微波线衰减，直接耦合结果为 (74.7±0.2) dB，色散耦合为 (75.4±6.8) dB。

Conclusion: 色散耦合量子比特测量不确定度更大，可靠性低于直接耦合量子比特，可能源于额外耦合谐振器的传递函数（transfer function）难以准确估计；该结果为稀释制冷机中量子比特功率传感的验证迈出第一步，朝向低温微波测量的溯源性发展。

Abstract: The Rabi frequency of a two-level system is directly proportional to the electric field amplitude being incident on this system. Taking advantage of this relationship, it is possible to use two-level systems as quantitative sensors of electric fields. Here, we apply this concept to superconducting qubits and use them for quantitative measurement of microwave power at millikelvin temperatures. Our superconducting circuit design includes three transmon qubits directly coupled to the central microwave feedline and three transmon qubits coupled dispersively via readout resonators to the feedline. We compare both coupling schemes to assess their reliability for microwave power sensing. Further, we describe the modeling behind each power-sensing method, including an uncertainty estimation based upon repeated measurements. By combining dispersively coupled and directly coupled qubits on the same device, we are able to explore different power ranges: from $-155\;$dBm to $-130\;$dBm for the directly coupled qubits and from $-95\;$dBm to $-55\;$dBm for the dispersively coupled qubits. When determining the microwave-line attenuation for a frequency of $6.0\;$GHz from room temperature to the millikelvin stage of the cryostat, we obtain best estimates and uncertainties for the directly coupled transmons of $(74.7\pm 0.2) \;$dB and for the dispersively coupled transmons of $(75.4\pm 6.8) \;$dB. The larger uncertainty of the dispersively coupled qubit measurements indicates that these qubits are less reliable for power sensing than the directly coupled qubits, most likely, due to the additional coupling resonator, whose transfer function is difficult to estimate accurately. Our results constitute the first step towards validation of qubit power sensing in dilution fridges, leading towards traceability for cryogenic microwave measurements.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [343] [Modified scattering and asymptotic completeness for a massive Dirac-wave system in dimension three](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18854)
*Vitor Borges*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文证明了三维 Dirac–Klein–Gordon 系统（有质量旋量场与无质量标量场耦合）在小初值下的整体适定性与修正散射，并给出线性散射成立的 Majorana 型初值条件，从而实现正向与反向的渐近完备性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题源自 Dong、Li、Yuan 提出的公开问题（其文献中的 Case IV），即对于有质量旋量与无质量标量场耦合的三维 Dirac–Klein–Gordon 系统，其小初值整体动力学与散射行为尚未确定。

Method: 正向论证采用双曲分层法（hyperboloidal foliation method）结合波包测试（testing by wave packets）；反向构造则在选定范数下使用压缩论证（contraction argument）。初值取小、足够正则且具有局部化性质的函数。

Result: 对充分正则且局部化的小初值，得到整体适定性（global well-posedness）与修正散射（modified scattering）；并识别出一类 Majorana 型初值条件使线性散射（linear scattering）成立，表明线性与修正散射解在该模型中并存；同时对小渐近剖面证明渐近完备性（asymptotic completeness）。

Conclusion: 该模型同时支持线性散射与修正散射两种渐近行为，取决于初值是否满足 Majorana 型条件；结合正向散射与反向渐近完备性，给出了该公开问题的完整回答。

Abstract: We establish global well-posedness and modified scattering for the three-dimensional Dirac-Klein-Gordon system with a massive spinor and a massless scalar field, for small, sufficiently regular and localized initial data. This answers a question posed by Dong, Li, and Yuan in \cite[Case IV]{DLY23}. We also identify a Majorana-type condition on the initial data that yields linear scattering, showing that both linear and modified scattering solutions coexist in this model.
  Conversely, we establish asymptotic completeness for small asymptotic profiles satisfying suitable regularity and decay assumptions. The forward argument combines the hyperboloidal foliation method with testing by wave packets, whereas the backward construction relies on a contraction argument in suitably chosen norms.

</details>


### [344] [The topological degree of the regular $SU(3)$ Toda system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20013)
*Youngae Lee,Haoyu Li,Juncheng Wei,Wen Yang,Lei Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文解决了闭可定向曲面（closed oriented surface）上正则 SU(3) Toda 系统的 Leray–Schauder 度（Leray–Schauder degree）计算这一长期公开问题，给出其按参数块的生成函数闭式表达，并由此导出基于拓扑与参数块的解存在性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则 SU(3) Toda 系统的 Leray–Schauder 度计算长期未解决；度非零可直接给出解的存在性，因而求度既是拓扑度理论的核心问题，也为该类非线性椭圆系统的可解性提供判据。

Method: 在每步度计算中证明可能的局部质量对（local mass pairs）仅为 (4π,0) 与 (8π,4π)；借助一般扰动（generic perturbations）与权重的局部二阶信息，排除影心碰撞（collisions of shadow centers）与混合爆破点（mixed blow-up points）处的分裂；再由影子度（shadow degree）与 Morse 指数（Morse index）确定各临界值处的度跳跃。

Result: 得到分块度 d_{m,k} 的生成函数恒等式：Σ_{m,k≥0} d_{m,k} x^m y^k = (1−x)^{χ(M)−1}(1−y)^{χ(M)−1}(1−xy)^{χ(M)}，其中 χ(M) 为曲面 M 的 Euler 示性数（Euler characteristic）。

Conclusion: d_{m,k} 的非零性给出以 M 的拓扑与参数块 (4πm,4π(m+1))×(4πk,4π(k+1)) 表达的存在性判据（existence criterion）；同时表明 SU(3) Toda 系统的爆破质量谱在该设定下被严格限制。

Abstract: We resolve the long-standing open problem of computing the Leray--Schauder degree of the regular $SU(3)$ Toda system on a closed oriented surface $M$. If $d_{m,k}$ denotes the degree in the block $(4πm,4π(m+1))\times(4πk,4π(k+1))$, then \[
  \sum_{m,k\geq0}d_{m,k}x^my^k
  =(1-x)^{χ(M)-1}(1-y)^{χ(M)-1}(1-xy)^{χ(M)}, \] where $χ(M)$ is the Euler characteristic of $M$. Nonvanishing of $d_{m,k}$ thus gives an existence criterion in terms of the topology of $M$ and the parameter block.
  At each step of the degree computation, we show that the only possible local mass pairs are $(4π,0)$ and $(8π,4π)$. We use generic perturbations and local second-order information on the weights to exclude collisions of shadow centers and splitting at mixed blow-up points. The shadow degrees and the Morse index then give the degree jump at each critical value.

</details>


### [345] [On the blow-up of the one-particle Lieb-Thirring constant in dimension two](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21347)
*Matthias Baur*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文建立二维单粒子 Lieb-Thirring 常数（Lieb-Thirring constant）在 γ→0 时的三项渐近展开，并确定渐近最坏位势（worst potential）的轮廓（profile），证明核心是对非线性椭圆方程 -ΔQ + Q = Q^s 正解当 s→∞ 的渐近分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是刻画二维单粒子 Lieb-Thirring 常数当 γ→0 时的精细渐近行为，并识别对应的渐近最坏位势形状，这一问题此前仅有较低阶的估计。

Method: 利用单粒子常数与 Gagliardo-Nirenberg 不等式（Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality）优化子（optimizers）积分矩之间的已知关系；再将优化子刻画为 R^2 上非线性椭圆方程 -ΔQ + Q = Q^s 的正的、消失的解，进而对 s→∞ 时的解 Q 作渐近分析。

Result: 得到该常数的三项渐近展开（three-term asymptotics），并在重新标度（rescaling）与对称意义下唯一确定了渐近最坏位势的轮廓；这些结果由对 Q 在 s→∞ 的渐近分析导出。

Conclusion: 二维单粒子 Lieb-Thirring 常数的 γ→0 行为可通过 Gagliardo-Nirenberg 优化子的半经典型极限（s→∞）精确刻画，三项渐近与最坏位势轮廓由该极限统一给出。

Abstract: We establish three-term asymptotics for the one-particle Lieb-Thirring constant in dimension two as $γ\to 0$ and identify, up to rescaling and symmetries, the profile of the asymptotically worst potential. The proof uses the well-known relation between the one-particle constant and an integral moment of optimizers of the Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality. Through the optimizers' characterization as positive, vanishing solutions of the nonlinear elliptic equation $-ΔQ + Q = Q^{s}$ in $\mathbb{R}^2$, the essence of the proof lies in the asymptotic analysis of solutions $Q$ as $s\to\infty$.

</details>


<div id='math.OA'></div>

# math.OA [[Back]](#toc)

### [346] [Entropy of Noncommutative Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21139)
*Cisca Kalmijn,Walter D. van Suijlekom*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 该文综述谱三元组（spectral triple）的二次量子化，并通过谱作用量（spectral action）计算所描述非交换空间（noncommutative space）的熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在以简洁形式回顾 Chamseddine、Connes 与第二作者的前期工作，系统整理谱三元组二次量子化的框架。

Method: 借助无穷维 Clifford 代数（infinite-dimensional Clifford algebras）实现二次量子化（second quantisation），并与谱作用量方法结合以定义熵。

Result: 以由 N 个点组成的非交换度量空间（noncommutative metric spaces）为例，显式计算了离散空间的熵，并研究了由此产生的熵力（entropic force）以及空间随时间的演化。

Conclusion: 谱三元组的二次量子化可为非交换离散空间提供熵与动力学描述，熵力与时间演化是该框架下可显式分析的对象。

Abstract: We give an overview of the second quantisation of spectral triples and compute the entropy of the noncommutative space described by it in terms of a spectral action. This reviews earlier work by Chamseddine, Connes and the second author, but presented here concisely, including the procedure of second quantisation using infinite-dimensional Clifford algebras. We exemplify the theory by considering noncommutative metric spaces consisting of $N$ points. The entropy of these discrete spaces is explicitly computed, and we study the resulting entropic force as well as the evolution of the spaces in time.

</details>


### [347] [Axioms for relative entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21549)
*Roberto Longo*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 该文在任意冯·诺依曼代数（von Neumann algebra）的正规态上，用单调性、张量可加性、乘积第二态的超可加性及前对偶范数下的下半连续性等公理，刻画了 Araki 相对熵（Araki relative entropy）的正有限倍数，并给出 C* 代数情形下的对应结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于为非简并散度（non-degenerate divergences）寻找一组可操作公理，使其在一般冯·诺依曼代数框架下唯一刻画 Araki 相对熵的正有限倍数，从而推广相对熵的公理化特征。

Method: 方法上采用正规单位完全正映射（normal unital completely positive maps）下的单调性、张量可加性（tensor additivity）、乘积第二态的超可加性（superadditivity for product second states）及前对偶范数下的分别下半连续性作为公理；替代形式用一个条件期望恒等式（conditional-expectation identity）替换两条张量假设，证明依赖序界（order bounds）与张量幂上的渐近估计。

Result: 结果表明，满足上述公理的非简并散度恰为 Araki 相对熵的正有限倍数；附录借助标准模理论（modular theory）与 Haagerup L^p 空间证明了所需解析估计，并在 C* 代数（C*-algebra）情形得到相应刻画。

Conclusion: 结论是 Araki 相对熵可由一组简洁公理在一般算子代数框架下唯一刻画（至正有限倍数），且该刻画在 C* 代数设定下同样成立。

Abstract: For normal states of arbitrary von Neumann algebras, we characterize positive finite multiples of Araki relative entropy among non-degenerate divergences. The axioms are monotonicity under normal unital completely positive maps, tensor additivity, superadditivity for product second states, and separate lower semicontinuity in the predual norm. Alternatively, a conditional-expectation identity replaces the two tensor assumptions. The proof uses order bounds and asymptotic estimates on tensor powers. An appendix proves the known analytic estimates needed here, using standard modular theory and Haagerup L^p spaces. We also obtain the corresponding characterization in the C* algebraic setting.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [348] [A Five-Dimensional Geometric Formulation of Geophysical Fluid Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19685)
*Stéphane Gaudreault,Seth Taylor,Arad Nasiri,Sanjeev S. Seahra,Raymond J. Spiteri*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文将牛顿动力学（Newtonian dynamics）的几何表述推广至无粘可压缩地球物理流动（inviscid compressible geophysical flow），借助引入辅助第五坐标，把质量与动量输运、内能及压力功统一纳入单一质量-动量张量（mass–momentum tensor），并证明其协变散度（covariant divergence）可导出完整的流体方程组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立地球物理流体常用方程与几何表述之间的对应关系，使重力及旋转/移动坐标系效应通过度规张量（metric tensor）统一处理，从而为大气动力学（atmospheric dynamics）中惯用方程组提供几何结构层面的解释。

Method: 引入辅助第五坐标，将质量、动量输运、内能及压力功合并为单一质量-动量张量；对其取协变散度，得到质量密度连续方程、三个动量方程与热力学方程，并由状态方程（equation of state）闭合系统；进一步在旋转球坐标（rotating spherical coordinates）与地形跟随坐标（terrain-following coordinates）中演示构造，并讨论薄壳（thin-shell）与球面重力位（spherical-geopotential）近似下度规的简化。

Result: 协变散度给出连续方程、三个动量方程与热力学方程，配合状态方程构成闭合体系；时间无关度规（time-independent metric）还导出总能量守恒；在薄壳与球面重力位近似、有效重力为常数时，度规可简化且仍保留垂直重力与传统科里奥利项（traditional Coriolis terms），并重现方程的常见形式。

Conclusion: 该几何表述能够系统重现并统一解释地球物理流体与大气动力学中常用的方程组，凸显其几何结构与标准方程形式之间的内在联系。

Abstract: We apply a geometric formulation of Newtonian dynamics to inviscid compressible geophysical flow, with gravity and the effects of rotating or moving coordinates incorporated in the metric tensor. This construction relies on the introduction of an auxiliary fifth coordinate, which allows mass and momentum transport, internal energy, and pressure work to be combined into a single mass--momentum tensor. The covariant divergence of this tensor leads to the continuity equation for mass density, the three momentum equations, and the thermodynamic equation, with the system closed by an equation of state. A time-independent metric also yields conservation of total energy. We recover familiar forms of the equations and illustrate the construction in rotating spherical and terrain-following coordinates. The thin-shell and spherical-geopotential approximations, with constant effective gravity, simplify the metric while retaining vertical gravity and the traditional Coriolis terms. Emphasis is placed on the connection between the geometric structure of the formulation and the equations commonly used in atmospheric dynamics.

</details>


### [349] [Wake-Powered Spreading of Encapsulated Drops Impacting Under Liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20340)
*Tian-Yu Zhang,Sushanta K. Mitra*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 在水下环境中，毫米级液滴撞击玻璃壁面时可铺展至初始直径两倍以上，且所需表面能超过液滴自身全部动能与重力势能之和；该能量缺额由液滴尾流所驱动环境液体（水）的动能提供，铺展由环境液体的韦伯数（Weber number）而非液滴自身的韦伯数控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认识认为空气中液滴撞壁的铺展源于其自身动能向表面能（surface energy）的转化。作者旨在检验该图像在壁面浸没于液体（submerged）条件下是否成立，并解释水下撞壁中出现的能量来源问题。

Method: 采用毫米级全氟聚醚（perfluoropolyether）液滴，分为裸液滴与包覆超薄硅油壳（silicone-oil shell）两类，在去离子水/水中撞击玻璃壁面。通过观察排液间隙（draining gap）中的挤压流（squeeze flow）、未破裂的水润滑膜（water lubrication film）上的铺展过程，并结合两阶段能量标度（two-stage energy scaling）进行分析。

Result: 液滴在撞击前被排液间隙的挤压流压扁，先损失大部分动能，随后在水润滑膜上铺展超过其直径两倍，其产生的表面能超过自身动能与重力能总和；两阶段能量标度（two-stage energy scaling）可解释该能量缺额，其中液滴尾流（wake）带动液体提供超过90%的铺展能量，最大铺展由环境液体的韦伯数（Weber number）而非液滴的韦伯数决定。

Conclusion: 水下撞壁铺展机制不同于空气中撞壁：能量主要来自环境液体而非液滴本身，水润滑膜始终未破裂。硅油壳主要通过其界面张力（interfacial tension）起作用，撞击前可忽略的双轴拉伸耗散（biaxial-extension dissipation）在铺展阶段变得显著。

Abstract: A drop impacting a wall in air spreads by converting its own kinetic energy into surface energy. We show that this picture fails when the wall is submerged in liquid. Millimetric perfluoropolyether drops, bare or encapsulated in an ultrathin silicone-oil shell, impacting a glass wall in water are flattened by the squeeze flow in the draining gap and shed most of their kinetic energy before impact. Nevertheless, they spread to more than twice their diameter on a water lubrication film that never ruptures, creating more surface energy than their entire kinetic and gravitational energy could supply. A two-stage energy scaling resolves this deficit. The liquid set in motion in the drop's wake supplies more than 90\% of the energy for spreading, so that the maximum spreading is governed by the Weber number of the ambient liquid rather than that of the drop. The shell acts mainly through its interfacial tension, while its biaxial-extension dissipation, negligible before impact, becomes appreciable during spreading.

</details>


### [350] [Will it bend? Modelling hydrogel box hinges with a gel Lattice Spring Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20857)
*Ellen M Jolley,Katie A Phillips,Guy Harrop,Gerard Cummins,Tom D Montenegro-Johnson*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文采用凝胶格子弹簧方法（gel lattice spring method, gLSM）对由水凝胶（hydrogel）与弹性体（elastomer）构成的双层铰链（bilayer hinge）致动器建模，计算其在多种几何与材料参数下的平衡弯曲角（equilibrium bending angle）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水凝胶（hydrogel）溶胀/去溶胀引起形状变化，可用于致动器（actuator）设计，并有望集成到折纸启发（origami-inspired）的折叠/展开结构中；因此需要针对双层铰链这类可产生弯曲的致动器单元建立可预测的模型。

Method: 采用凝胶格子弹簧方法（gel lattice spring method, gLSM）建立模型，用于模拟水凝胶与弹性体（elastomer）组成的复合结构（composite structures）。

Result: 计算了双层铰链（bilayer hinge）在一系列几何参数与材料属性下的平衡弯曲角（equilibrium bending angle）。

Conclusion: gLSM 模型可用于模拟水凝胶-弹性体复合结构并预测双层铰链的弯曲行为，从而为折纸启发的折叠/展开机构设计提供分析工具。

Abstract: Hydrogels are biocompatible smart materials comprised of a hydrophilic polymer network which can absorb a large volume of water (swelling). The changes in shape the gel undergoes as it swells or de-swells can be utilised in the design of actuators. This is of particular interest for many applications whereby hydrogel actuators would be combined into a complex origami-inspired structure which can fold and unfold according to application needs. In this paper, we therefore focus on one such actuator design, the bilayer hinge, which can induce bending (for potential use in a fold/unfold mechanism). We develop a model using the gel lattice spring method (gLSM) which can be used to simulate composite structures comprised of hydrogel and elastomer and compute the equilibrium bending angle of bilayer hinges for a range of geometries and material properties.

</details>


### [351] [Experimental Evidence of Elastic Instability in Viscoelastic Kolmogorov Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21087)
*Christophe Brouzet,Mayank Chopra,Dario Vincenzi,Jason R. Picardo,Atul Varshney*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究首次在无惯性（inertia-free）受限准二维黏弹性 Kolmogorov 流中通过实验观测到中心模态弹性不稳定性（center-mode elastic instability），速度为场中出现"箭形"结构并随驱动力增强呈混沌脉动，模拟与理论分析共同揭示了其源于无界 Kolmogorov 流的中心模态失稳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无惯性弹性不稳定性（inertialess elastic instability）对复杂流体输运影响重大，但在具有弯曲流线（curved streamlines）的流动中已理解较充分，而其在直线流（rectilinear flows）如典型 Kolmogorov 流中的起源仍未解决。

Method: 采用电磁驱动力（electromagnetic forcing）驱动受限准二维黏弹性 Kolmogorov 流进行实验，并借助 Oldroyd-B 模型（Oldroyd-B model）进行数值模拟对照。

Result: 实验首次探测到无惯性下的中心模态弹性不稳定性，表现为速度场中的"箭形"（arrow-shaped）结构，且在驱动力增强时呈混沌脉动（chaotically fluctuates）；模拟重现了该现象，并将受限流中的动力学追溯到无界 Kolmogorov 流中心模态失稳所产生的行进箭头结构（travelling arrowhead structures）。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了直线流中通向纯弹性不稳定性（purely elastic instability）的一般性路径，拓展了弹性湍流（elastic turbulence）与强化混合（enhanced mixing）可能出现的条件范围。

Abstract: Inertialess elastic instabilities can profoundly affect transport in complex-fluid flows, from mixing in microfluidic devices and displacement through porous media to polymer processing. While such instabilities are well understood in flows with curved streamlines, their origin in rectilinear flows like the prototypical Kolmogorov flow remains unresolved. Here, we report the first experimental evidence of the center-mode elastic instability, at vanishing inertia, in a confined quasi-two-dimensional viscoelastic Kolmogorov flow, driven by electromagnetic forcing. The instability is characterized by the emergence of "arrow-shaped" structures in the velocity field, which fluctuates chaotically as the forcing strength increases. We reproduce these observations in simulations of the Oldroyd-B model and trace the dynamics in the confined flow to the travelling arrowhead structures that emerge from the center-mode instability of unbounded Kolmogorov flow. Our work establishes a generic route to purely elastic instability in rectilinear flows, broadening the conditions under which elastic turbulence and enhanced mixing can arise.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [352] [$K_1^*(1870)$ and $3S$-$2D$ mixing in the strange meson sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19498)
*Ting-Yan Li,Zi-Yue Bai,Xiang Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文检验 BESIII 新观测到的 $J^P=1^-$ 奇异共振 $K_1^*(1870)$ 能否纳入 kaon 介子家族，认为它可描述为 $3S$-$2D$ 混合的较低质量态，并计算其 OZI 允许衰变与 $Kp$ 产生过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BESIII 合作组首次在 $K\eta'$ 系统中观测到 $J^P=1^-$ 奇异共振 $K_1^*(1870)$，需要判断该态是否可在 kaon 介子谱（kaon meson family）框架内得到自洽解释。

Method: 采用组分夸克模型中的 $3S$-$2D$ 态混合（$3S$-$2D$ mixing）方案指认该态，并利用夸克对产生模型（quark pair creation model）计算混合态的 OZI 允许衰变性质，进一步分析 $Kp$ 反应中的产生过程。

Result: 该态不能指认为纯 $3^3S_1$ 或 $2^3D_1$ 态，但作为 $3S$-$2D$ 混合的较低质量态其质量与实验测量值一致，混合态的衰变总宽度也与 $K_1^*(1870)$ 的测量宽度相符；文中同时预言了其高质量伙伴态。

Conclusion: $K_1^*(1870)$ 可在 kaon 介子家族中以 $3S$-$2D$ 混合态解释，但其次级 $K\eta'$ 衰变模式与观测到的 $K\eta'$ 衰变道存在张力，需进一步研究；$Kp$ 反应中的产生可为未来实验搜寻提供参考。

Abstract: Very recently, the BESIII Collaboration reported the first observation of a $J^P=1^-$ strange resonance in the $Kη'$ system, the $K_1^*(1870)$. We examine whether this state can be accommodated in the kaon meson family. Although it cannot be assigned as a pure $3^3S_1$ or $2^3D_1$ state, we show that it can be described as the lower state of $3S$-$2D$ mixing, whose mass agrees with the measured value, and we predict its higher-mass partner. Using the quark pair creation model, we calculate the OZI-allowed decay properties of both \textcolor{black}{mixed} states; the total decay width of the lower state is consistent with the measured width of the $K_1^*(1870)$. We note that the subordinate $Kη'$ decay mode of this state appears to contradict its observed $Kη'$ decay channel, which calls for further study. We further investigate the production of these two states via the $Kp$ reaction, which may provide valuable information for future experimental searches.

</details>


### [353] [Quark + diquark description of nucleon gravitational form factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19819)
*Peng Cheng,Zhen-Ni Xu,Dong-Yan Fu,Chen Chen,Zhao-Qian Yao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 基于庞加莱协变（Poincaré-covariant）夸克+双夸克Faddeev方程对核子引力形状因子（GFFs）给出无参数预言，并分析其味道与双夸克组分分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏直接实验约束的情况下，需要理论框架对核子引力形状因子（gravitational form factors, GFFs）及其夸克/胶子分解给出可检验的无参数预言，并厘清各内部组分的作用。

Method: 采用庞加莱协变夸克+双夸克Faddeev方程（quark+diquark Faddeev equation），计算完整GFFs（A、J、D、c̄）及其夸克与胶子、标量/轴矢量双夸克及u/d味道的组分分解，并与格点QCD（lQCD）结果比较。

Result: 标量双夸克（scalar diquark）组分主导核子的能量、自旋与力学性质，轴矢量组分不可或缺；B(Q²)在有限Q²下极小但非零，源于双夸克成分与u/d味道扇区的相消；c̄(Q²)满足该方法的对称性要求；D项与各空间半径较lQCD略小。

Conclusion: 系统性低估主要来自胶子扇区，可归因于介子云（meson cloud）效应的缺失，提示该效应对胶子分布影响强于夸克分布；两种外推方案下负的中心能量密度是否出现高度依赖模型。

Abstract: We present parameter-free predictions for the nucleon gravitational form factors (GFFs) using a Poincare-covariant quark+diquark Faddeev equation. We provide the full GFFs ($A$, $J$, $D$, and $\bar{c}$) along with their species decompositions into quark and gluon contributions. Based on the diquark breakdown, we find that while the scalar diquark component dominates the nucleon's energy, spin, and mechanical properties, the axial-vector component plays an indispensable role. The GFF $B(Q^2)$ is predicted to be vanishingly small but nonvanishing at finite $Q^2$; its physical origin is deeply rooted in the nucleon' s internal structure through cancellations in both the diquark content and the $u/d$ flavor sectors. Furthermore, our results for $\bar{c}(Q^2)$ confirm the fundamental symmetry requirements of the approach. Compared with lQCD, our calculated $D$-term and various spatial radii are somewhat smaller in magnitude. This systematic underestimation is primarily driven by the gluon sector and can be attributed to the omission of meson cloud effects, suggesting that these effects impact the gluon distribution more significantly than the quark distribution. Finally, our analysis of the energy density based on two extrapolation schemes reveals that the presence of a negative central energy density is highly model-dependent.

</details>


### [354] [The Future Circular Collider as a photon-photon collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20085)
*Patricia Rebello Teles,David d'Enterria*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文评述了未来环形对撞机（FCC）在各碰撞模式下利用等效光子通量开展光子-光子物理的机遇，重点为C偶共振态的独家产生及新粒子搜寻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FCC的电子-正电子、质子-质子、质子-铅与铅-铅碰撞均伴随高亮度等效光子通量，为系统研究双光子相互作用提供了独特条件，值得评估其物理潜力。

Method: 在等效光子近似（equivalent photon approximation）框架下，将相对论带电粒子的电磁场描述为准实光子通量，并综合比较FCC各运行模式在光子通量、能量与虚度上的互补性。

Result: 各模式可获得宽广的双光子不变质量范围；预期能在所有碰撞模式下观测希格斯玻色子的光子聚变产生，并对光子耦合的轴子样粒子（ALPs）与引力子样粒子（GLPs）给出灵敏度，质量覆盖至FCC-ee运动学极限及FCC-hh的多TeV区间。

Conclusion: FCC的光子-光子项目对C偶共振态研究具有显著潜力，相较现有约束可实现明显改进。

Abstract: The CERN Future Circular Collider (FCC) offers a unique programme of photon-photon interactions in electron-positron, proton-proton, proton-lead, and lead-lead collisions, by exploiting the high-luminosity equivalent photon fluxes associated with the charged beams. Within the equivalent photon approximation, the electromagnetic fields of relativistic charged particles are described in terms of fluxes of quasireal photons. The complementary photon fluxes, energies, and virtualities available across the FCC operation modes provide access to a broad range of two-photon invariant masses and associated physics measurements. Physics opportunities open to study are highlighted, focusing on the exclusive production of C-even resonances, including the SM Higgs boson and searches for spin-0 axion-like (ALPs) and spin-2 graviton-like (GLPs) particles decaying back into photon pairs. These prospective studies demonstrate the potential to observe photon-fusion production of the Higgs boson for all colliding modes, as well as sensitivity to photon-coupled ALPs and GLPs with masses extending up to the kinematic reach of FCC-ee and into the multi-TeV range at FCC-hh collisions, with significant improvements over existing constraints.

</details>


### [355] [Strong Decays of the $2^-$ Heavy-Light Mesons in the Relativistic Bethe-Salpeter Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20380)
*Jie Yu,Wan-Li Ju,Qiang Li,Tianhong Wang,Guo-Li Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 采用完全瞬时 Bethe–Salpeter（Salpeter）方程求解重轻 \(J^P=2^-\) 介子的质量谱与波函数，并结合 PCAC 与手征有效拉氏量计算 OZI 允许的两体强衰变，给出 \(D_2\)、\(D'_2\)、\(D_{s2}\)、\(D'_{s2}\) 及对应 \(\bar B\) 系统的质量谱、\(^1D_2\)–\(^3D_2\) 混合角与总宽度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对重轻 \(J^P=2^-\) 介子，现有描述常依赖非相对论谱学记号 \({}^{2S+1}L_J\)，需在相对论框架下统一处理其质量谱、态混合（\(^1D_2\)–\(^3D_2\)）与强衰变性质。

Method: 求解完整的瞬时 Bethe–Salpeter（instantaneous Bethe–Salpeter / Salpeter）方程获得质量谱与介子波函数，波函数表示直接按 \(J^P\) 量子数构造而非 \({}^{2S+1}L_J\) 记号；衰变振幅方面，\(H\to H'P\) 道采用轴矢流部分守恒（PCAC）与低能定理，使轻赝标介子仅通过其衰变常数进入，\(H\to H'V\) 道采用有效手征拉氏量（effective chiral Lagrangian）。

Result: 摘要未给出具体数值结果；所述计算产出为八种重轻 \(J^P=2^-\) 介子的质量谱、\(^1D_2\)–\(^3D_2\) 混合角，以及各 OZI 允许两体强衰变道的分宽度，并由此求和得到总宽度估计。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个相对论、以 \(J^P\) 量子数为出发点的统一框架，用于自洽描述重轻 \(J^P=2^-\) 介子的质量谱、\(D\) 波态混合与 OZI 允许强衰变，可作为相关态的实验识别与性质指认的理论参考。

Abstract: We present a relativistic study of the mass spectra, the $^1D_2$--$^3D_2$ mixing angles, and the OZI-allowed two-body strong decays of the heavy-light $J^P=2^-$ mesons $D_2$, $D'_2$, $D_{s2}$, $D'_{s2}$, $\bar{B}_2$, $\bar{B}'_2$, $\bar{B}_{s2}$, and $\bar{B}'_{s2}$. The mass spectrum and the meson wave functions are obtained by solving the full instantaneous Bethe--Salpeter (Salpeter) equation, in which the wave-function representations are constructed directly in terms of the $J^P$ quantum numbers rather than the non-relativistic spectroscopic notation ${}^{2S+1}L_J$. For the decay amplitudes we use the partial conservation of the axial-vector current (PCAC) together with the low-energy theorem for the $H\to H'P$ channels, so that the light pseudoscalar meson enters only through its decay constant, and the effective chiral Lagrangian for the $H\to H'V$ channels. The total widths of these mesons are estimated by summing over the partial widths of all OZI-allowed strong decay channels.

</details>


### [356] [Doubly strange tetraquark as a $KK^*$ virtual state: quark-model study with harmonic traps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20588)
*Kai-Kai Zhang,Zi-Yang Lin,Yan-Ke Chen,Lu Meng*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用人工谐振阱（artificial harmonic trap）与包含介子-介子及致密双夸克-反双夸克（diquark--antidiquark）组态的四夸克基，从夸克层面相互作用中提取强子-强子散射信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立从夸克层面相互作用出发提取强子-强子散射信息的框架，并获得可与直接计算对比的基准，同时厘清双奇异 $T_{ss}$ 态的性质。

Method: 将 Busch--Englert--Rzażewski--Wilkens 公式与有效程展开（effective range expansion, ERE）结合，由阱内能谱确定相移、散射长度、有效程及极点位置，并控制基截断、有限阱频率、ERE 截断与夸克模型参数带来的不确定度。

Result: 提取的 $DD^*$ 与 $BB^*$ 束缚能与直接计算一致，可作为基准；对 $I(J^P)=0(1^+)$ 的双奇异 $T_{ss}$，在稳定 $K^*$ 近似下预言位于阈值以下约 30--35~MeV 的 $KK^*$ 虚态（virtual-state）极点，且 AL1 模型及其改进版本均给出该结果。

Conclusion: 该结果不同于此前关于束缚态的预言，并推动在 HIAF 等装置上寻找 $T_{ss}$；该框架可推广到其他强子-强子通道以提取散射可观测量与极点位置。

Abstract: We extract hadron--hadron scattering information from quark-level interactions using an artificial harmonic trap and a four-quark basis that includes both meson--meson and compact diquark--antidiquark configurations. Combining the Busch--Englert--Rzażewski--Wilkens formula with the effective range expansion (ERE), we determine phase shifts, scattering lengths, effective ranges, and pole positions from the trapped spectrum. We control uncertainties from basis truncation, finite trap frequencies, truncation of ERE, and quark-model parameters. The extracted $DD^*$ and $BB^*$ binding energies agree with direct calculations, providing benchmarks. For the doubly strange $T_{ss}$ with $I(J^P)=0(1^+)$, we predict a $KK^*$ virtual-state pole about 30--35~MeV below threshold in the stable-$K^*$ approximation. Both the AL1 model and its modified version that better reproduces the measured $T_{cc}^+$ binding energy yield this result. This finding differs from earlier predictions of a bound state and motivates searches for the $T_{ss}$ at facilities such as the High Intensity heavy Ion Accelerator Facility (HIAF). This framework could be extended to other hadron-hadron channels to extract scattering observables and pole positions.

</details>


### [357] [Dimension-seven spectator effects in charm-hadron lifetimes at NLO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20747)
*Chia-Wei Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作计算了粲强子（charm hadron）衰变宽度重夸克展开中七维旁观者系数（spectator coefficients）连通流-流部分的次领头阶（NLO）QCD修正，并估计其对D介子与单粲重子宽度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的重夸克展开（heavy-quark expansion）宽度计算仅含领头阶（LO）七维系数，缺乏对NLO QCD修正的完整评估，影响对粲强子寿命预测的精度。

Method: 在NDR-MS方案下确定有限项，采用标准希腊投影并对称平均、以及使七维算符到六维算符有限混合为零的有限减除；因七维算符NLO矩阵元未知，介子采用真空插入（vacuum insertion）、重子采用袋模型（bag model）的树级矩阵元估计，并对六维QCD算符矩阵元的1/m_c修正作朴素展开。

Result: 若干通道的单圈项与LO系数相当，并生成两个LO不存在的算符族；NLO七维修正使D^+宽度提高0.78 ps^-1（由略负变为正值），约为D^0宽度的1%、D_s^+宽度的10%、单粲重子的至多2%；结合已发表的极点方案宽度得τ(D^+)=1.41^{+∞}_{-0.76} ps。

Conclusion: NLO项将单粲重子非轻子七维贡献的标度依赖性降低数倍，但对D介子并不一致；缺少O(α_s)部分时D_s^+半轻子宽度会为负，说明该修正不可忽略。

Abstract: We compute the next-to-leading-order (NLO) QCD corrections to the connected current-current part of the dimension-seven spectator coefficients in the heavy-quark expansion of charm-hadron widths. The finite terms are fixed in NDR-$\overline{\rm MS}$, with the evanescent operators defined by the standard Greek projection and averaged over Hermitian-conjugate pairs, and with a finite subtraction that sets the finite mixing of the dimension-seven operators into the dimension-six ones to zero. In several channels the one-loop term is comparable to the LO coefficient, and two operator families absent at LO are generated. Since the NLO matrix elements of the dimension-seven operators are not known, we estimate the effect of the coefficients on the widths with tree-level matrix elements from vacuum insertion for $D$ mesons and from the bag model for charmed baryons. The $1/m_c$ corrections to the matrix elements of the dimension-six QCD operators are treated by a naive expansion. Without their $O(α_s)$ part the semileptonic width of the $D_s^+$ would be negative. The NLO dimension-seven correction raises the $D^+$ width by 0.78 ps$^{-1}$, so that its central value, slightly negative as published, becomes positive. At central inputs, it is about 1% of the $D^0$ width, 10% of the $D_s^+$ width and at most 2% of the widths of the singly charmed baryons. Added to the published pole-scheme width, the $D^+$ correction gives $τ(D^+)=1.41^{+\infty}_{-0.76}$ ps. The NLO term reduces the scale dependence of the nonleptonic dimension-seven contribution severalfold for the singly charmed baryons, whose matrix elements are evolved, but not uniformly for the $D$ mesons.

</details>


### [358] [Muon and Tau Anomalies and $Δα_{had}^{(5)} (M_Z^2)$ in the Standard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20762)
*Stephan Narison*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 综述强调以最新e+e-数据（2π、3π及PDG编集的包含截面比R^ee，并加入至α_s^5的微扰QCD连续谱与凝聚项）重估最低阶强子真空极化贡献，得到a_μ|hvp_lo=(7094±37)×10^-11，据此SM预言与实验值之差Δa_μ^sm=(86±38_th±14.5_exp)×10^-11，对应约2.1–3.5σ的新物理窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于厘清标准模型（Standard Model, SM）对缪子反常磁矩（muon anomaly, a_μ）预言的现状，尤其是最低阶强子真空极化（hadronic vacuum polarization, HVP）这一主导误差来源，以判断实验与理论差异是否构成新物理迹象。
