Method: 方法为基于近期e+e-→2π、3π数据与PDG编集的包含截面比R^ee，叠加含高阶微扰QCD（至α_s^5）、夸克质量项（m_q^2至α_s^3、m_s^4至α_s^2）及QCD凝聚（condensates）的连续谱贡献；并将e+e-→π^0γ+ηγ加入CMD3数据，再与高阶（HO）强子、QED与电弱（EW）贡献合并。

Result: 结果为a_μ|hvp_lo=(7094±37)×10^-11，Δa_μ^sm=(86±38_th±14.5_exp)×10^-11；对表3各估计取平均（含拟合全部e+e-数据的尝试）得Δa_μ^sm=(84.9[90.0]±21.3_th±14.5_exp)×10^-11，与BMW20格点结果一致，仍提示(2.1~3.5)σ的新物理窗口。

Conclusion: 结论指出，除改进e+e-数据的未来项目、直接提取a_μ^tot|hvp_lo的MUonE与独立测量的J-PARC(E34)外，亟需改进图9中彼此（初步看）不一致的格点（lattice）结果，其可能动摇白皮书WP25中所用"平均的平均"的可靠性；并更新给出a_τ|hvp_lo=(3516±25)×10^-9与Δα_had^(5)(M_Z^2)=(2770.7±4.5)×10^-5。

Abstract: I review the present status of the Standard Model (SM) determination of the muon anomaly by emphasizing the estimate of the lowest hadronic vacuum polarization contributions using recent $e^+e^-$ data on $2π$ and $3π$ and the inclusive ratio $R^{ee}$ of total cross-sections compiled by PDG to which we add the QCD continuum including HO PT QCD up to $α_s^5$, quark mass $m_q^2$ up to $α_s^3$, $m_s^4$ up to $α_s^2$ and QCD condensates. Adding the $e^+e^-\to π^0γ+ ηγ$ contributions to the CMD3 data, one obtains\,: $a_μ\vert^{hvp}_{lo}=(7094\pm 37)\times 10^{-11}$. Combining this LO result with the HO estimate of the hadronic, QED and EW contributions, one obtains\,: $Δa_μ^{sm} \equiv (a_μ^{exp}-a_μ^{sm})= (86\pm 38_{th}\pm 14.5_{exp})\times 10^{-11} $. The average of the recent estimates quoted in Table\,3 [$\oplus$ attempt to fit all $\,e^+e^-$ data] gives\,: $Δa_μ^{sm} = (84.9[90.0]\pm 21.3_{th}\pm 14.5_{exp})\times 10^{-11}$\,. These results agree with the precise BMW20 lattice one and still suggest a window for new physics beyond the SM of $(2.1\sim 3.5)\,σ$. Besides some future projects for improving the $e^+e^-$ data, the MUonE for extracting directly $a_μ^{tot}\vert^{hvp}_{lo}$ and J-PARC (E34) for an independent measurement of $a_μ$, it is important to improve the Lattice results shown in Fig.\,9 which (at first sight) disagree each others despite the large errors and may question the reliability of the {\it averages of different averages} used in the White Paper (WP25) Our analysis is completed by updating the estimate of the hadronic contribution to the tau anomaly\,: $a_τ\vert^{hvp}_{lo}=(3516\pm 25)\times 10^{-9}$ and to $α(M_Z)\,: \,Δα_{had}^{(5)} (M_Z^2)= (2770.7\pm 4.5)\times 10^{-5}. $

</details>


### [359] [Inferring the neutral-current neutrino--nucleon cross section from IceCube data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20887)
*Reinaldo Francener,Victor P. Goncalves*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 利用 IceCube 12 年数据评估对中性流中微子—核子截面（neutral-current neutrino–nucleon cross section）的灵敏度，发现当前数据仅能给出上限，而 IceCube-Gen2 有望实现有效约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子仅通过弱力（weak force）相互作用，高能带电流程道（charged-current）截面已有实验测量，而中性流（neutral-current）相互作用在实验上基本未被探索，因此需要评估现有中微子天文台对该物理量的探测能力。

Method: 基于 IceCube 中微子天文台（IceCube Neutrino Observatory）12 年数据，采用两种策略：一是利用中微子穿越地球时的吸收与再生（absorption and regeneration）效应；二是利用径迹—级联事件比（track-to-cascade event ratio）。

Result: 当前 IceCube 数据只能对中性流中微子—核子截面设定上限（upper limit），尚不足以给出有意义的约束。

Conclusion: 两种方法在 IceCube-Gen2 上均有望实现对中性流截面的有效约束，表明下一代探测器在该测量上具有关键作用。

Abstract: Neutrinos interact only via the weak force, through the exchange of $W^{\pm}$ and $Z^{0}$ bosons in charged-current and neutral-current interactions, respectively. Recent experiments have measured the charged-current cross section at high energies, whereas neutral-current interactions remain largely unexplored experimentally. In this work, we investigate the sensitivity of the IceCube Neutrino Observatory to the neutral-current neutrino--nucleon cross section using 12 years of data. We consider two strategies to constrain this quantity: $(i)$ leveraging neutrino absorption and regeneration as neutrinos traverse the Earth; and $(ii)$ using the track-to-cascade event ratio. We find that current IceCube data can set only an upper limit on the neutral-current neutrino--nucleon cross section. We further show that IceCube-Gen2 should be able to constrain the neutral-current cross section with both methods considered here.

</details>


### [360] [Inclusive Heavy Quarkonium Production in Lepton-Hadron Scattering in a Joint QCD+QED Factorization Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21361)
*Jian-Wei Qiu,Kazuhiro Watanabe,Jia-Yue Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该报告首次在电子-离子对撞机（EIC）能区，采用QCD+QED联合因子化框架同时计算电子-质子（ep）与电子-核（eA）碰撞中的重夸克偶素（heavy quarkonium）产生，并扩展至横动量倒数幂展开的次领头幂（NLP）贡献以及与NRQCD固定阶计算的匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EIC为在ep与eA碰撞中精确研究重夸克偶素产生提供了条件；对比两种碰撞可在干净可控的实验环境中考察重夸克对与核物质的相互作用及夸克偶素在核介质中的形成过程，而此前缺乏针对EIC能区的相应计算。

Method: 采用将碰撞诱导的QCD与QED辐射置于同等地位的联合QCD+QED因子化（QCD+QED factorization）框架，首次计算EIC能区ep与eA碰撞中的重夸克偶素产生；并系统纳入夸克偶素横动量倒数幂展开中的次领头幂（next-to-leading power）贡献，讨论其与非相对论量子色动力学（NRQCD）固定阶计算的匹配。

Result: 给出了EIC能区ep与eA碰撞中重夸克偶素产生的首次计算结果，并将计算系统扩展至包含横动量倒数幂展开的次领头幂贡献，且讨论了该计算与NRQCD固定阶计算的匹配关系。

Conclusion: 该框架可用于探讨核介质在物理夸克偶素由产生的重夸克对演化形成过程中的作用，并为EIC上重夸克偶素产生的精确研究提供理论基础。

Abstract: The Electron-Ion Collider (EIC) will provide an opportunity for precision studies of heavy quarkonium production in electron-proton ($ep$) and electron-nucleus ($eA$) collisions. Comparing production in $ep$ and $eA$ collisions provides a clean, well-controlled experimental environment to study interactions between a heavy quark pair and nuclear matter and the formation of quarkonia in a nuclear medium. In this talk, we present the first calculation of heavy quarkonium production in both $ep$ and $eA$ collisions at EIC energies using a joint QCD+QED factorization framework that treats collision-induced QCD and QED radiation on equal footing, and discuss the role of the nuclear medium in exploring how a physical quarkonium is emerged from a produced heavy quark pair. We also systematically extend our calculation to include next-to-leading power contributions in the inverse power expansion of quarkonium transverse momentum, and discuss the matching of our calculations to fixed-order calculations in the nonrelativistic Quantum Chromodynamics (NRQCD) approach.

</details>


### [361] [Heavy double-gluon quarkonium and $\overline{b}ggc$ mesons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21380)
*G. Daylan Esmer,B. Barsbay,K. Azizi,H. Sundu,S. Türkmen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文用两点QCD求和规则（QCD sum rule）方法研究含重夸克-反夸克对与两个价胶子的“双胶子混杂介子（double-gluon hybrid mesons）”，给出$\bar{c}GGc$、$\bar{b}GGb$与$\bar{b}GGc$各允许通道的质量谱与流耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重夸克偶素（heavy quarkonium）体系中混杂态（hybrid）的胶子激发成分尚未充分刻画，双胶子混杂介子的质量谱缺乏系统预言，需要为强衰变分析与实验数据解读提供理论基准。

Method: 采用两点QCD求和规则（two-point QCD sum rule）；对$\bar{c}GGc$、$\bar{b}GGb$取$J^{PC}=0^{\pm\pm},1^{\pm\pm}$，对开味（open-flavor）$\bar{b}GGc$按$J^{P}=0^{\pm},1^{\pm}$分类；算符乘积展开（operator product expansion, OPE）算至十二维，并选取Borel参数与连续谱阈值使极点贡献占优且OPE收敛。

Result: $J^{PC}=0^{+-},0^{--},1^{++},1^{-+}$的流恒为零，故仅对余下四个通道导出求和规则；获得质量区间$M\simeq 6.2$–$6.7$ GeV（$\bar{c}GGc$）、$12.0$–$12.2$ GeV（$\bar{b}GGb$）、$9.2$–$9.6$ GeV（$\bar{b}GGc$），并给出全部通道的流耦合（current couplings）。

Conclusion: 所得质量与耦合预言可作为双胶子混杂介子强衰变（strong decays）计算及重夸克区（heavy-quark sector）未来实验数据解释的基础。

Abstract: We investigate heavy double-gluon hybrid mesons, built from a heavy quark-antiquark pair and two valence gluons, using the two-point QCD sum rule method. The charmonium-like $\bar{c}GGc$ and bottomonium-like $\bar{b}GGb$ states are considered with $J^{PC}=0^{\pm\pm}$ and $1^{\pm\pm}$, whereas the open-flavor $\bar{b}GGc$ states are classified by $J^{P}=0^{\pm}$ and $1^{\pm}$. The currents with $J^{PC}=0^{+-}, 0^{--}, 1^{++}$, and $1^{-+}$ vanish identically, so sum rules are derived for the remaining four channels. The operator product expansion is carried out up to dimension twelve, and the Borel parameter and continuum threshold are fixed so that the pole contribution dominates and the OPE converges. The resulting masses lie in the ranges $M\simeq 6.2$--$6.7$~GeV for $\bar{c}GGc$, $M\simeq 12.0$--$12.2$~GeV for $\bar{b}GGb$, and $M\simeq 9.2$--$9.6$~GeV for $\bar{b}GGc$, and the current couplings are obtained for all channels. These predictions provide a basis for analyzing the strong decays of double-gluon hybrids and for interpreting future experimental data in the heavy-quark sector.

</details>


### [362] [Black Hole Superradiance and the Gravitational Auger Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18833)
*Joshua W. Foster,Nicholas L. Rodd*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 黑洞超辐射（black hole superradiance）云中纯粹的引力相互作用可通过类似俄歇效应（Auger effect）的机制显著改变超辐射动力学；对QCD轴子（QCD axion）而言，该不可约引力耦合会快速耗散第一超辐射能级并截断更高能级的增长，进而削弱连续引力波等可观测信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究已知超辐射观测效应（黑洞自旋改变、引力波与相干玻色子发射）对四次自相互作用（quartic self-interaction，λφ⁴）敏感，但引力相互作用本身对超辐射动力学的影响尚未被系统考虑，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于解析论证指出云内部纯引力相互作用经由俄歇效应类比改变超辐射动力学，并识别出由散射至更高能级及其径向泛音（radial overtones）驱动的第一能级以上能级的非平凡演化；具体以QCD轴子为对象进行分析。

Result: 对QCD轴子，不可约引力耦合在第一超辐射能级饱和后通过标量发射与黑洞吸收迅速耗散该能级，同时截断更高能级的增长；这可能大幅抑制连续引力波信号及晚期双星旋近（binary inspiral）特征的可观测性。

Conclusion: 黑洞自旋减慢（spin-down）类效应相对更稳健，但对更高模式而言出现更早、进行更慢，因此现有观测限制需予以修正。

Abstract: Black hole superradiance is a powerful probe of nature's light degrees of freedom. In the vicinity of a spinning black hole, it exponentially amplifies ultralight bosons from vacuum fluctuations to a macroscopic cloud of enormous occupation number. Observational superradiance effects - modified black-hole spins, gravitational-wave and coherent boson emission - are known to depend sensitively on bosonic self-interactions as induced by a quartic, $λφ^4$. Here we point out that purely gravitational interactions within the cloud significantly modify superradiance dynamics, through an analog of the Auger effect. Moreover, we identify nontrivial evolution of the superradiant levels beyond the first, driven by scattering into even higher levels and their radial overtones. For the QCD axion, the irreducible gravitational coupling rapidly dissipates the first superradiant level after it reaches saturation, through scalar emission and absorption by the black hole, while truncating the growth of higher levels. This can dramatically suppress the observability of continuous gravitational-wave signals or signatures in late binary inspirals. Black hole spin-down remains more robust, but for higher modes begins earlier and proceeds more slowly, requiring revision of existing limits.

</details>


### [363] [Pulsar Timing Correlations Induced by Stochastic Spin-2 Ultralight Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21279)
*Hao-Xuan Shi,Yu-Mei Wu,Chang Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了由自旋-2超轻暗物质（ultralight dark matter, ULDM）快速度规振荡引起的脉冲星计时残差交叉关联（timing-residual cross-correlations），并允许一般偏振统计（polarization statistics）。结果表明其内在角核（intrinsic angular kernel）含单极与四极分量，角分布与自旋-1 ULDM高度相似，提示二者在脉冲星计时阵列（pulsar timing arrays, PTA）观测中可能简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ULDM可产生时变引力扰动，被视为脉冲星计时阵列的潜在信号来源；银河系晕中ULDM的速度弥散（velocity dispersion）导致有限空间相干性（finite spatial coherence），从而影响脉冲星计时信号间的相关性，需明确自旋-2情形下的关联形式。

Method: 在允许一般偏振统计的前提下，推导自旋-2 ULDM快速度规振荡所诱导的计时残差交叉关联；并在五种偏振模式等分（equipartition）假设下计算内在角核的角分布。

Result: 等分假设下，内在角核包含单极（monopole）与四极（quadrupole）分量，且其角向剖面与自旋-1 ULDM的结果十分接近。

Conclusion: 自旋-2与自旋-1 ULDM在PTA观测中可能难以区分，存在潜在简并（degeneracy），对利用PTA区分不同ULDM候选者构成挑战。

Abstract: Ultralight dark matter (ULDM) can generate time-dependent gravitational perturbations, providing a potential signal for pulsar timing arrays (PTAs). In Galactic halos, the velocity dispersion of ULDM gives rise to finite spatial coherence, which influences the correlations between pulsar timing signals. In this work, we derive the timing-residual cross-correlations induced by rapid metric oscillations of spin-2 ULDM, allowing for general polarization statistics. Under equipartition among the five polarization modes, the intrinsic angular kernel contains monopole and quadrupole components and exhibits an angular profile closely resembling that of spin-1 ULDM, suggesting a potential degeneracy between the two ULDM candidates in PTA observations.

</details>


### [364] [Lean-Verified No-Go Theorems for $R$-Symmetries and Modular Symmetries in 4D GUTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20386)
*Mu-Chun Chen,Xiang-Gan Liu,Michael Ratz,Alexander Stewart*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文用 Lean 4 形式化了一个关于超对称 GUT 中 R 对称性（R-symmetry）的不可行定理（no-go theorem），修补了原证明在一般情形下的缺口，并讨论了其对模对称性（modular symmetry）模型及规范耦合统一的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: R 对称性在超对称模型构建中十分有用，但已有不可行定理表明：基于 SU(5)、含有限个手征多重态（chiral multiplets）的四维超对称大统一理论（GUT）无法在保持离散 Z_M^R（M≥3）或连续 U(1) R 对称性的同时自发破缺到精确的最小超对称标准模型（MSSM）。作者希望形式化验证该结论并评估其对模对称性模型的含义。

Method: 使用 Lean 4 对该不可行定理的证明进行形式化（formalization）；在此过程中识别并补全了已发表论证在一般情形下的一个缺口；并进一步分析模对称性在同时描述引力的超对称框架中必然表现为 R 对称性的推论，以及在模空间（moduli space）临界点处离散 R 对称性线性实现的情形。

Result: 形式化过程中发现并闭合了原证明一般情形的缺口。对于四维 GUT，若 GUT 破缺真空保持这些对称性，则不可行定理意味着在模空间临界点处会出现无质量的奇异态（exotic states）；这些奇异态在临界点附近呈参数化地轻（parametrically light），可能破坏规范耦合统一（gauge coupling unification）。

Conclusion: R 对称性在超对称 GUT 中的使用受不可行定理严格限制，且该结论经 Lean 4 形式化验证；模对称性在含引力的超对称框架中本质上是 R 对称性，因此在模空间临界点附近会引入参数化轻的奇异态，对规范耦合统一构成潜在威胁。

Abstract: $R$-symmetries are instrumental in supersymmetric model building. However, a no-go theorem restricts us from using them in a large class of four-dimensional (4D) grand unified theories (GUTs). Specifically, it has been shown that a supersymmetric 4D GUT based on SU(5), with finitely many chiral multiplets, cannot be broken spontaneously to the exact minimal supersymmetric standard model (MSSM) while preserving a discrete $\mathbb{Z}_M^R$, with $M \geq 3$, or continuous U(1) $R$-symmetry. We use Lean 4 to formalize this proof. In the process, we identify and close a gap in the general case of the published argument. Further, we discuss the implications for GUT models with modular symmetries. We point out that modular symmetries are inevitably $R$-symmetries in a supersymmetric framework that also describes gravity. At certain critical points in moduli space, discrete $R$-symmetries are linearly realized. In the context of 4D GUTs, the no-go theorem then implies that exotic states become massless at these points, provided the GUT-breaking vacuum preserves these symmetries. In the vicinity of these critical points, these exotics are parametrically light, which may spoil gauge coupling unification.

</details>


### [365] [Cosmological Collider Signals from Fermion Bubble Loops with a Chemical Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20919)
*Chengcheng Han,Zhanhong Lei,Zhehan Qin,Wuzhou Yin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文首次对暴胀期间存在有效化学势（chemical potential）时，由大质量费米子气泡圈（fermion bubble loop）产生的局域与非局域宇宙对撞机（cosmological collider, CC）信号进行了系统性解析计算，并证明四点关联函数的折叠奇异性在完整信号中相互抵消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙对撞机信号是探测暴胀期间重粒子谱的重要手段，但此前缺乏针对化学势增强的费米子圈图信号的系统性解析研究，尤其局域与非局域贡献的解析结构及奇异性行为尚不清楚。

Method: 在 de Sitter 近似下，研究单挤压极限（single-squeezed limit）下暴胀扰动的四点关联函数，结合 CC 切割规则（cutting rules）、晚期展开与部分 Mellin-Barnes（MB）技术，解析导出局域与非局域信号；并通过重组 MB 表示与互补 MB 表示的独立计算进行一致性检验。

Result: 局域信号被识别出两个来源：谱极点与整数极点，以及圈动量积分的紫外极点（ultraviolet poles）。三点极限下，单独提取的局域与非局域贡献出现表观折叠奇异性（folded singularities），但经重组 MB 表示后证明其在完整信号中抵消，并得到可用于数值计算的双重 MB 收敛积分。此外，化学势可显著增强振荡信号并提高其对数振荡频率。

Conclusion: 该解析结果为化学势增强的费米子 CC 信号（包括费米子三角圈）的后续研究奠定了基础；局域信号的极点结构划分与折叠奇异性抵消机制构成主要理论贡献。

Abstract: We present the first systematic analytical calculation of the local and nonlocal cosmological collider (CC) signals generated by massive fermion bubble loops in the presence of an effective chemical potential during inflation. Working in the de Sitter approximation, we investigate the four-point correlator of inflaton perturbations in the single-squeezed limit. By combining CC cutting rules, late-time expansions, and partial Mellin-Barnes (MB) techniques, we derive analytical expressions for both local and nonlocal signals. In particular, we identify two distinct contributions to the local signal, arising from spectrum and integer poles and from ultraviolet poles of the loop-momentum integral. We further investigate the three-point limit, where the separately extracted local and nonlocal contributions develop apparent folded singularities. By reorganizing the MB representation, we demonstrate that these singularities cancel in the complete signal and obtain a convergent double-MB integral suitable for numerical evaluation. Independent calculations using complementary MB representations provide nontrivial consistency checks of our results. We find that the chemical potential can significantly enhance the oscillatory signal while increasing its logarithmic oscillation frequency. Our analytical results provide a foundation for further studies of chemical-potential-enhanced fermionic CC signals, including fermion triangle loops.

</details>


### [366] [A consistent (3D) EFT expansion of the bubble nucleation rate. Part I: scalar theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20938)
*Luis Gil,Javier López Miras*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文是两部分系列论文的第一篇，建立了将高维算符（higher-dimensional operators, HDOs）系统纳入标量高温有效场论中气泡成核（bubble nucleation）计算的完全微扰框架，并证明所得统计率在场重定义下不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确预测气泡成核率是将宇宙学相变（cosmological phase transitions）与其潜在观测信号相连接的关键；以往对HDO效应的系统研究多局限于临界热力学，尚未系统纳入成核计算。

Method: 构建普适的完全微扰（fully perturbative）框架：一致地包含HDOs对反弹解（bounce）及其周围涨落的影响，包括与高导数项的相互作用；证明统计率在场重定义（field redefinitions）下不变，并给出HDOs存在时微扰反弹解存在的充分条件；以含一个标量和一个费米子的模型作示例。

Result: 在标量—费米子模型中，于中等强度相变（moderately strong transitions）区域发现温和的HDO修正；统计率在场重定义下的不变性得到证明。

Conclusion: 该工作将HDO效应的系统研究从临界热力学拓展至成核过程，为确定HDOs对气泡成核的影响提供了受控方法，并为系列第二篇论文奠定基础。

Abstract: Precise predictions of bubble nucleation are essential for connecting cosmological phase transitions to their potential observational signatures. In this first paper of a two-part series, we develop a general, fully perturbative framework for incorporating higher-dimensional operators (HDOs) into nucleation calculations in scalar high-temperature effective field theories. Our approach consistently includes their effects on the bounce and the fluctuations around it, including interactions with higher derivatives. We prove that the resulting statistical rate is invariant under field redefinitions, and establish sufficient conditions for the existence of the perturbative bounce solution in the presence of HDOs. We illustrate the framework by applying it to a model containing a scalar and a fermion, where we find modest HDO corrections in its regime of moderately strong transitions. We thus extend the systematic study of HDO effects beyond critical thermodynamics and provide a controlled method for determining their impact on bubble nucleation.

</details>


### [367] [Non-Canonical Q-balls in Polyattractor Inflation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21413)
*Kimberley Siret,Rhys Abercromby,Kit Lloyd-Stubbs,John McDonald*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明在超引力（supergravity）多重吸引子暴胀（polyattractor inflation）模型中存在Q球（Q-balls）解，且仅当模型显著偏离T模型α吸引子（α-attractor）时出现；这些非标准Q球若在暴胀结束时产生且U(1)对称性未破缺，可绝对稳定并作为暗物质（dark matter）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察可插值于α吸引子T模型与多项式吸引子模型之间的多重吸引子暴胀框架中，是否可能存在Q球这类非拓扑孤子，并评估其宇宙学意义。

Method: 作者在基于超引力的多重吸引子暴胀模型中，分别考虑原始多重吸引子势（potential）及其一种自然推广形式，并在复暴胀场具有非标准动能项（non-canonical kinetic term）的条件下求解Q球解。

Result: 结果显示：当模型显著偏离T模型α吸引子时存在Q球解，而在T模型极限下不存在Q球；所得Q球因复暴胀场的非标准动能项而属于非标准Q球（non-canonical Q-balls）。

Conclusion: 若这些Q球在暴胀结束时被产生，将具有重要宇宙学意义；特别是当超引力多重吸引子模型的整体U(1)对称性（global U(1) symmetry）未破缺时，Q球可绝对稳定，从而解释暗物质。

Abstract: We show that Q-balls can exist in supergravity-based polyattractor inflation models that interpolate between $α$-attractor T-models and polynomial attractor models. We consider both the original polyattractor potential and a natural generalisation. We find that Q-balls solutions exist when the model deviates significantly from the T-model $α$-attractor, whereas in the T-model limit no Q-balls exist. These are non-canonical Q-balls due to the non-canonical kinetic term of the complex inflaton field. Such Q-balls could have significant cosmological consequences if they are produced at the end of inflation. In particular, if the global $\mathrm{U}(1)$ symmetry of the supergravity-based polyattractor model is unbroken, the Q-balls can be absolutely stable and could account for dark matter.

</details>


### [368] [The Skyrme-Dilaton and Nucleon Observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21537)
*Thomas Fischbacher,Roman Zwicky*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作在 Skyrme 模型（Skyrme model）中引入 σ/f₀(500) 介子作为伸缩子（dilaton），以联合拟合核子静态可观测量，拟合质量 χ²/dof 接近 1，并显著优于原始 Skyrme 模型，同时再现 π 介子衰变常数 F_π 的物理值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机有二：σ 介子是 QCD 中最轻的标量共振态，因而是经典 Skyrme 模型中 π 介子的自然扩展；近期关于引力形状因子（gravitational form factors）等研究表明，σ 介子可解释为自发破缺的标度对称性（scale symmetry）的赝戈德斯通玻色子（pseudo-Goldstone boson）。

Method: 将 σ/f₀(500) 介子作为伸缩子（dilaton）纳入 Skyrme 模型，扩展原模型的作用量；尽管 σ 介子是宽共振态（broad resonance），但在静态可观测量（static observables）中可视为实粒子处理，因为其线型（line-shape）预计不具本质影响。

Result: 对核子静态可观测量的联合拟合给出 χ²/dof 接近 1，相对原始 Skyrme 模型有实质改进，并同时再现模型整体标度 F_π 的物理值。

Conclusion: 引入伸缩子扩展的 Skyrme 模型能以显著改善的拟合质量描述核子静态性质，并自洽地给出物理的 F_π，支持 σ 介子作为标度对称性赝戈德斯通玻色子的图像。

Abstract: We extend the Skyrme model by incorporating the $σ/f_0(500)$-meson as a dilaton. The motivation is twofold: the $σ$-meson is the lightest scalar resonance in QCD and is therefore the natural extensions to the pions of the classical Skyrme model, while its interpretation as a pseudo-Goldstone boson of spontaneously broken scale symmetry is supported by recent studies of gravitational form factors and related developments. Although the $σ$-meson is a broad resonance, for static observables it can be treated as real particle since its line-shape is not expected to be essential. A combined fit to static nucleon observables yields $χ^2/\mathrm{dof} $ close to unity, with substantial improvement over the original Skyrme model, while simultaneously reproducing the physical value of $F_π$, the overall scale of the model.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [369] [Lower Bound of Thermal Conductivity in Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18795)
*Yan Gao,Yafei Ding,Hongbin Xu,Zhaoxuan Tang,Fuqing Duan,Wenmei Luo,Zhensheng Zhang,Xiaohang Zhang,Guimei Zhu,Yahui Xue,Baowen Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过高效图形化与转移技术制备1–30 μm悬浮双层石墨烯（bilayer graphene, BLG）热桥，发现含聚合物残留的BLG室温热导率约570 W/(m K)且与长度无关，并将其解释为无定形表面散射决定的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯本征热导率（thermal conductivity, κ）因物理与计量学复杂性长期存在争议；热桥法（thermal bridge method）虽测量可靠，但样品制备困难，制约了系统性研究。

Method: 开发高效图形化与转移技术，系统制备并表征长度1–30 μm的悬浮BLG热桥；结合均质非平衡分子动力学（homogeneous non-equilibrium molecular dynamics, HNEMD）模拟分析表面污染对声子的影响。

Result: 含不可避免聚合物残留的悬浮BLG室温κ约570 W/(m K)，与长度无关，在250–350 K近乎恒定，且对严格表面清洗与无定形氮化硅（amorphous silicon nitride）衬底保持稳健；模拟显示仅约0.01单层无定形碳覆盖即可抑制面外弯曲声学（flexural acoustic, ZA）声子。

Conclusion: 结果挑战了刚性衬底比表面污染更强抑制ZA声子的主流观点，确立了由无定形表面散射（amorphous surface scattering）主导的κ稳健下界，该下界反映实际器件条件下的热传导，为声子输运基础研究与石墨烯热管理应用提供参考。

Abstract: The intrinsic thermal conductivity ($κ$) of graphene remains highly controversial because of physical and metrological complexities. Although the thermal bridge method enables reliable measurements, sample fabrication remains challenging. Here, we develop a highly efficient patterning and transfer technique to systematically fabricate and characterize suspended bilayer graphene (BLG) thermal bridges with lengths of 1-30 $μ$m. Suspended BLG with unavoidable polymer residues exhibits length-independent thermal conductivity of approximately 570 W/(m K) at room temperature, remaining nearly constant from 250 to 350 K and robust to rigorous surface cleaning and amorphous silicon nitride substrates. Homogeneous non-equilibrium molecular dynamics (HNEMD) simulations show that amorphous carbon coverage of only approximately 0.01 monolayer is sufficient to suppress out-of-plane flexural acoustic (ZA) phonons. These findings challenge the prevailing view that rigid substrates suppress ZA phonons more strongly than surface contamination and identify a robust lower bound of $κ$ governed by amorphous surface scattering. This lower bound reflects heat conduction in graphene under realistic device conditions and provides a reference for fundamental phonon transport studies and the integration of graphene into thermal management applications in electronic devices and chips.

</details>


### [370] [A Practitioner's Guide to Materials Theory and Computation in the Age of Big Data and Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18797)
*Jack Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该专著以势能面（potential-energy landscape）与统计采样（statistical sampling）为统一主线，对计算材料科学（computational materials science）作面向实践者的教学式综述，贯通经验力场、密度泛函理论、多体电子结构方法直至高通量工作流与人工智能（AI）方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子模拟、高通量计算与人工智能的进步推动材料科学转型，但计算方法日益多样、理论根基分散，实践者难以获得一致连贯的理论理解，故需一部以统一概念组织、兼顾教学与实践的专著。

Method: 采用作者研究经验驱动的面向实践者（practitioner-oriented）教学式综述，以势能面和统计采样为统一框架，系统梳理经验力场、密度泛函理论、多体电子结构方法、统计采样与性质预测，并扩展到高通量工作流、材料数据库及人工智能的概率基础。

Result: 阐述上述概念如何支撑结构稳定性、相行为及电子与磁性质；重点讨论原子尺度表征（atomistic representations）、图神经网络（graph neural networks）与生成模型（generative models），并揭示对称性、群论、微分几何与随机过程在现代材料建模中的作用。

Conclusion: 通过连接经典物理理论与新兴数据驱动方法，专著提供计算材料科学的统一视角，并指出面向物理知情（physics-informed）、高效且可靠的材料发现所面临的机遇与挑战。

Abstract: Computational modelling has become a cornerstone of materials science, with advances in atomistic simulations, high-throughput computation, and artificial intelligence transforming how materials are understood, discovered, and designed. These developments build upon generations of theoretical innovations that have translated complex mathematical and physical principles into practical predictive tools. However, the growing diversity of computational methods presents challenges for practitioners seeking a coherent understanding of their theoretical foundations. Drawing upon the author's research experience, this monograph provides a practitioner-oriented, pedagogical overview of computational materials science, unified through the concepts of potential-energy landscapes and statistical sampling. It explores how these concepts underpin structural stability, phase behaviour, and electronic and magnetic properties, through a systematic treatment of empirical force fields, density-functional theory, many-body electronic-structure methods, statistical sampling, and property prediction. It further examines high-throughput workflows, materials databases, and the probabilistic foundations of artificial intelligence. Particular emphasis is placed on atomistic representations, graph neural networks, and generative models, highlighting the roles of symmetry, group theory, differential geometry, and stochastic processes in modern materials modelling. By connecting established physical theories with emerging data-driven approaches, this monograph offers a unified perspective on computational materials science and identifies opportunities and challenges toward physics-informed, efficient, and reliable materials discovery.

</details>


### [371] [Predictive sufficiency as an information microscope for time-resolved coherent scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18884)
*Christian Gutt,Amir Tosson,Nimmi Das Anthuparambil,Jovita Lukasik,Michael Möller*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出一种"信息显微镜（information microscope）"，用于检验一组XPCS观测量是否保留了描述后续散射动力学的必要信息，并通过学习表征与"XPCS字母表（XPCS Alphabet）"检索可实验获取的观测量以补回丢失的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规XPCS以强度关联函数（intensity correlation functions）的平均描述弛豫过程，但这类平均值可能掩盖散斑涨落在时间与倒易空间（reciprocal space）中的演化方式，故需判断所选观测量是否信息充分。

Method: 构造对当前散斑序列的学习表征（learned representation），指示信息是否仍超出所选描述之外；再由XPCS Alphabet搜索实验可及、可解释的观测量以恢复该信息。

Result: 模拟布拉格散射（Bragg scattering）中，散斑活动度与各向异性揭示关联形变与间歇滑移特征；受控SAXS模型中散斑活动度涨落对协同粒子运动的空间尺度敏感；二维玻璃形成体中保留时间分辨散斑关联与径向强度可恢复平均弛豫函数丢失的信息，且这些关联涨落在笼效应（caging）期间增强，但未选出单一主导关联模式。

Conclusion: 该方法以学习引导相干散射的物理分析，同时保持所得XPCS观测量可测量且可解释。

Abstract: X-ray photon correlation spectroscopy (XPCS) measures dynamics through the evolution of coherent speckle patterns, most commonly summarized by intensity correlation functions. These averages describe relaxation but can conceal how speckle fluctuations evolve in time and across reciprocal space. Here we introduce an information microscope that tests whether a chosen set of XPCS observables retains the information needed to describe subsequent measured scattering dynamics. A learned representation of the present speckle sequence indicates when information remains outside the chosen description; an XPCS Alphabet then searches for experimentally accessible observables that recover it. In simulated Bragg scattering, speckle activity and anisotropy reveal signatures of correlated deformation and intermittent slip. In a controlled SAXS model, fluctuations of speckle activity provide sensitivity to the spatial extent of cooperative particle motion. In a two-dimensional glass former, retaining time-resolved speckle correlations and radial intensity recovers information lost in the average relaxation function. The fluctuations of these correlations become stronger during caging, without selecting a single dominant correlated mode. This approach uses learning to guide the physical analysis of coherent scattering, while keeping the resulting XPCS observables measurable and interpretable.

</details>


### [372] [On-demand valley control in a WSe2/CrPS4 heterostructure via optically programmed magnetic order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18906)
*Muhammad Hassan Shaikh,Daria Blach,Harishankar Jayakumar,Brayan Eraso Solarte,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Yafei Ren,Archana Raja,Chitraleema Chakraborty*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用光可调的磁序在 WSe2/CrPS4 异质结中实现谷对称性破缺的按需光开关，为可扩展的谷电子学（valleytronics）提供动态调控平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维半导体中谷自由度（valley degree of freedom）的光学调控对可扩展谷电子学至关重要，但磁邻近效应（magnetic proximity effect）虽能解除谷简并，静态界面却缺乏器件所需的动态控制能力。

Method: 采用偏振与时间分辨光致发光（polarization- and time-resolved photoluminescence）研究 WSe2/CrPS4 异质结，通过激发能量相对配体-金属电荷转移（ligand-to-metal charge transfer, LMCT）能隙的位置来调控 CrPS4 中的磁序。

Result: 低于 LMCT 能隙激发时，CrPS4 保持面外 Néel 矢量（Néel vector），在界面产生净面外自旋并实现自旋极化电荷转移（spin-polarized charge transfer），使 WSe2 A 激子（A-exciton）衰减动力学出现稳健的偏振能隙（polarization gap），且无需外磁场；高于 LMCT 能隙激发则使该偏振能隙消失，表明 Néel 矢量转向面内。相同光激发还可将低能激子（low-energy excitons, XLE）衰减动力学调控约 42%，推测源于界面耦合的改变。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了首个将磁对称性、谷极化（valley polarization）与激子弛豫（exciton relaxation）集成并由光动态调控的多功能平台。

Abstract: The ability to optically control the valley degree of freedom in two-dimensional semiconductors is critical for scalable valleytronics. While magnetic proximity effects can lift valley degeneracy, static interfaces lack the dynamic control needed for devices. Here, we demonstrate on-demand optical switching of valley symmetry breaking in a WSe2/CrPS4 heterostructure by exploiting light-tunable magnetic order. Using polarization- and time-resolved photoluminescence, we show that excitation below the ligand-to-metal charge transfer (LMCT) gap preserves an out-of-plane Néel vector in CrPS4, generating a net out-of-plane spin at the interface and enabling spin-polarized charge transfer. This yields a robust polarization gap in the WSe2 A-exciton decay dynamics without an external magnetic field. Excitation above the LMCT gap extinguishes the polarization gap, indicating a rotation of the Néel vector to in-plane. This reversible switching provides direct evidence of spin-polarized charge transfer. Furthermore, the same optical excitation enables optical control of the decay dynamics of the low-energy excitons (XLE) by ~42%, which is potentially attributed to the modification of interfacial couplings. Our work establishes a first unique multifunctional platform where magnetic symmetry, valley polarization, and exciton relaxation are integrated and dynamically controlled by light.

</details>


### [373] [Adaptive nonorthogonal atomic orbitals refine charge transfer and bonding descriptors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19023)
*Emily Oliphant,Emmanouil Kioupakis,Wenhao Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文展示如何将 COGITO 工具从平面波 DFT 映射得到的高维成键信息，缩减为可解释的共价成键、电荷转移与晶格相关原子化学描述符，并验证其在成键区分与能带分解上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）隐含编码了成键与电荷转移信息，但把周期性电子结构（periodic electronic structure）转化为可解释、可迁移的描述符（descriptors）仍具挑战性。

Method: 基于此前提出的 COGITO（Crystal Orbital Guided Iteration To atomic-Orbitals）工具，将平面波 DFT（plane-wave DFT）映射回自适应原子轨道（adaptive atomic orbitals）的极简基组，进而把高维成键信息降维为化学上可用的描述符，同时保留相互作用在实空间中的来源。

Result: 相较于使用静态或正交轨道基（static or orthogonal orbital bases）的方法，COGITO 对短程与长程成键的描述符更稳定，更能区分离子型、共价型与金属型成键（ionic, covalent, metallic bonding）区域；并能将能带色散（band dispersion）可靠分解为按作用程分辨的贡献，从而验证并补充硅（silicon）低对称导带极小值（conduction band minimum）的轨道起源。

Conclusion: 基于自适应 COGITO 的描述符可编码电子结构，为揭示决定电子性质的轨道相互作用与电荷转移提供了结构化路径。

Abstract: First-principles calculations implicitly encode bonding and charge transfer, but converting periodic electronic structure into interpretable and transferable descriptors remains challenging. Recently, we introduced Crystal Orbital Guided Iteration To atomic-Orbitals (COGITO), a tool for mapping plane-wave DFT back to an interpretable, minimal basis of adaptive atomic orbitals. Here, we show how the high-dimensional bonding information from COGITO can be reduced into chemically useful descriptors of covalent bonding, charge transfer, and crystal-dependent atomic chemistry without collapsing the real-space origin of the interactions. Compared to methods that use static or orthogonal orbital bases, COGITO descriptors of short- and long-range bonding show more stability and a better ability to distinguish between ionic, covalent, and metallic bonding regimes. Lastly, COGITO reliably decomposes band dispersion into range-resolved contributions, allowing us to verify and add to the orbital origins of silicon's low-symmetry conduction band minimum. Overall, the adaptive COGITO-based descriptors encode electronic structure, offering a well-structured route to expose the orbital interactions and charge transfer responsible for electronic properties.

</details>


### [374] [Flat-band driven bipolar anomalous Nernst effect towards volt-scale transverse thermoelectric conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19039)
*Haofu Wen,Xiaodong Guo,Xiaokang Xu,Peng Di,Xuebiao Ji,Ruoqi Wang,Shijun Yuan,Kunquan Hong,Qionghua Zhou,Qian Chen,Taishi Chen,Jinlan Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出本征双极反常能斯特效应（bipolar anomalous Nernst effect, BANE）范式，实现无需金属互连的无缝串联，并通过机器学习筛选与Co$_2$TiGe单晶实验验证了近室温极性反转现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单极反常能斯特热堆（anomalous Nernst thermopiles）受金属互连（metallic interconnects）制约，产生严重焦耳耗散（Joule dissipation）并降低模块封装密度，从而限制电压输出。

Method: 聚焦平带（flat-band）拓扑体系，采用机器学习辅助筛选（machine learning assisted screening）多种目标性质；并以Weyl铁磁体Co$_2$TiGe单晶进行实验验证，结合第一性原理计算解析机制。

Result: 筛选出249种BANE材料，表现近室温极性切换；Co$_2$TiGe中本征反常霍尔电导率（anomalous Hall conductivity, AHC）直至居里温度保持不变，而反常能斯特信号在约225 K发生 sharply 极性反转。

Conclusion: 平带与Weyl节点（Weyl nodes）的相互作用使化学势（chemical potential）随热脱布居（thermal depopulation）跨越带边，经Mott关系反转$\partial \sigma_{xy}/\partial \varepsilon$符号；该机制为紧凑单片横向热堆（monolithic transverse thermopiles）的伏级发电提供途径。

Abstract: Unipolar anomalous Nernst thermopiles are critically plagued by metallic interconnects, which induce heavy Joule dissipation and compromise the module's packing density, ultimately capping voltage generation. Here, we circumvent these limitations by establishing an intrinsic bipolar anomalous Nernst effect (BANE) paradigm, enabling seamless series integration analogous to Seebeck n-p pairs without passive metallic bridges. Focusing on flat-band topological systems, through machine learning assisted screening across multiple target properties, we identified 249 BANE materials, exhibiting near-room-temperature polarity switching. Guided by this predictive framework, we experimentally realized this phenomenon in single crystals of the Weyl ferromagnet, Co$_2$TiGe. Strikingly, while its intrinsic anomalous Hall conductivity (AHC) remains rigorously temperature-invariant up to the Curie temperature, the anomalous Nernst signal undergoes a sharp polarity inversion across $T \approx 225$ K. First-principles calculations attribute this behavior to the interplay between flat bands and Weyl nodes, where thermal depopulation shifts the chemical potential across the band edge, inverting the sign of $\partial σ_{xy} / \partial ε$ via the Mott relation. This work opens an avenue toward compact, monolithic transverse thermopiles capable of volt-scale generation free from interfacial parasitic dissipation.

</details>


### [375] [Emergence of Anisotropic Defect Networks in Shocked Diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19045)
*Zipeng Xu,Kento Katagiri,Tatiana Pikuz,Wanghui Li,Anirudh Hari,Dorian P. Luccioni,Sophie E. Parsons,Yuichi Inubushi,Kohei Miyanishi,Hirotaka Nakamura,Tadashi Togashi,Makina Yabashi,Toshinori Yabuuchi,Norimasa Ozaki,Leora E. Dresselhaus-Marais*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合X射线自由电子激光的相位衬度增强射线照相与分子动力学模拟，揭示单晶金刚石在冲击压缩中形成的非晶带与层错网络具有晶面取向组织，并在卸载拉伸阶段成为损伤优先形核位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测冲击加载后的失效需理解压缩阶段形成的缺陷结构在卸载时如何演化，但金刚石中晶体塑性与局域非晶化如何三维组织、并决定拉伸损伤局域化位置尚不清楚。

Method: 在X射线自由电子激光上开展相位衬度增强射线照相（phase-contrast-enhanced radiography），结合多泵浦-探测延迟与流体动力学计算，并通过旋转视角分析投影特征角度分布；同时采用分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟压缩与卸载过程。

Result: 线性特征首先出现在塑性波前（plastic wavefront）之后；投影特征角度对应沿接近⟨112⟩方向拉长的有限{111}片层。模拟显示压缩中形成非晶带与层错的互联网络，优先出现在面内剪切牵引较大的{111}变体上，其投影可定性复现旋转导致的特征可见度下降。卸载拉伸时裂纹在既有非晶带内开启。

Conclusion: 压缩阶段形成的晶面组织化缺陷网络为卸载阶段的拉伸损伤提供优先位置，该路径可能同样适用于其他强共价固体。

Abstract: Predicting failure after shock loading requires understanding how the structures created during compression evolve as the material unloads. In diamond, both crystal plasticity and localized amorphization have been reported to accommodate shock-induced shear. However, it remains unresolved how the resulting defect structures organize in three dimensions during compression and how this organization influences where tensile damage localizes during release. Here we couple phase-contrast-enhanced radiography at an X-ray free-electron laser with simulations to address this question in single-crystal diamond. By combining radiographs at multiple pump-probe delays with hydrodynamic calculations, we show that linear features first become visible behind the plastic wavefront while material near the front remains highly compressed. By rotating the viewing direction about the shock axis, we find that the projected feature angles favor finite $\{111\}$ sheets elongated along in-plane directions close to $\langle112\rangle$. Molecular dynamics simulations show that an interconnected network of amorphous bands and stacking faults forms during compression, preferentially on $\{111\}$ variants with larger in-plane shear traction. Projections of this network qualitatively reproduce the observed loss of feature visibility with rotation. During simulated release, tension opens local cracks within these pre-existing amorphous bands. These results support a pathway in which the crystallographically organized defect network formed during compression provides preferred sites for tensile damage during release. This pathway may also operate in other strongly covalent solids.

</details>


### [376] [SPARC: Evidence-guided adaptive model composition for materials discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19050)
*Sissi Feng,Yuchen Han,Runze Zhang,Jiaru Bai,Brandon Sutherland,Ali Shayesteh Zeraati,Jason Hattrick-Simpers,Varinia Bernales,Alán Aspuru-Guzik,Yang Bai*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: SPARC 框架通过折外预测（out-of-fold predictions）与稀疏后验组合（sparse posterior composition）动态调整各模型族的影响权重，并定义“信任度”以同时驱动探索与测量验证优先级排序，在八个材料数据集与自动化析氧反应实验中均取得优于文献基线的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环实验（closed-loop experimentation）不仅需要选择下一个实验，还需判断哪些已有测量与模型导出的关系已获得足够证据支持，从而能够影响决策；现有方法缺乏在证据累积过程中持续修订这种支持度的机制。

Method: 提出 SPARC 框架：以折外预测评估相互竞争的模型族，确保每个模型在其拟合未使用的观测上受检验；通过稀疏后验组合按折外证据支持的预测贡献分配模型影响，保留多个模型族的互补贡献；以候选依赖的预测离散度与残差变异性定义信任度（trust），与预测响应共同调节探索；并通过独立诊断对已有测量进行前瞻性验证优先级排序，同时报告模型导出的信任度。

Result: 在八个已发表材料数据集上，SPARC 在其中六个取得高于文献报告值的 R²，相对提升最高达 77%，且未使用多个已发表流程所依赖的密度泛函理论（density-functional-theory）或实验导出描述符；在自动化碱性析氧反应（oxygen-evolution-reaction）实验中，11 个 SPARC 选出的实验有 8 个过电位低于 300 mV（最佳 242 mV），而 125 个初始实验中仅 18 个达到该水平；重复实验前被优先验证的测量平均偏差 76 mV，匹配对照组为 27 mV。

Conclusion: SPARC 将实验证据转化为修订模型影响的依据，既能引导探索，又能识别值得验证的测量，为闭环实验提供了兼顾模型聚合与数据可信度评估的统一框架。

Abstract: Closed-loop experimentation requires more than selecting the next experiment. It must also determine which existing measurements and model-derived relationships are sufficiently supported to influence that decision. Here we introduce SPARC (Sparse Predictive Aggregation of Regression Components), a framework that continually revises this support as experimental evidence accumulates. Competing model families are evaluated through out-of-fold predictions, so that each model is tested on observations not used in its corresponding fit. Sparse posterior composition assigns model influence according to predictive contributions supported by the out-of-fold evidence, retaining complementary contributions from several model families. Candidate-dependent predictive dispersion and residual variability define trust, which modulates exploration alongside the predicted response. Independent diagnostics also prioritize existing measurements for prospective verification, with model-derived trust reported alongside them. Across eight published materials datasets, SPARC achieved higher $R^2$ than the corresponding literature-reported values in six, with relative gains of up to 77% without the density-functional-theory- or experiment-derived descriptors used by several published pipelines. In an automated alkaline oxygen-evolution-reaction campaign, 8 of 11 SPARC-selected experiments reached overpotentials below 300 mV, with a best value of 242 mV, compared with 18 of 125 initial experiments. Measurements prioritized for verification before repetition subsequently deviated by 76 mV on average, compared with 27 mV for matched controls. SPARC therefore turns experimental evidence into a basis for revising model influence, directing exploration and identifying measurements that warrant verification.

</details>


### [377] [Interplay of interfacial energetics and selective Ni segregation in the morphology of co-precipitates in low-alloy steels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19060)
*Jia-Hong Ke,Anshul Kamboj,Mukesh Bachhav*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作在同一哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）框架下量化了低合金钢中 Fe/CRP、Fe/MNSP 与 Cu/MNSP 界面能，并结合偏聚（segregation）分析解释了核壳/附着形貌与共沉淀（co-precipitation）行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低合金钢中共存沉淀与界面偏聚会改变析出相稳定性与形貌，进而影响强化与脆化；Cu 富集析出相（CRP）与 Mn-Ni-Si 富集析出相（MNSP）常呈核壳或附着形貌，但控制该形貌转变的界面能学缺乏定量描述。

Method: 将经密度泛函理论（density-functional-theory, DFT）训练的团簇展开（cluster expansion）与格点蒙特卡洛（lattice Monte Carlo）模拟相结合，在同一哈密顿量内统一评估 Fe/CRP、Fe/MNSP 与 Cu/MNSP 三类界面；并以过剩内能（excess internal energy）定义界面系数，辅以 Gibbs 吸附分析。

Result: 界面能系数排序为 γ_Cu/MNSP < γ_Fe/CRP ≲ γ_Fe/MNSP，给出正的粘附功（work of adhesion）与负的铺展参数（spreading parameter），与 MNSP 附着于 CRP 且呈部分覆盖一致。仅 Ni 偏聚至 Fe/CRP 界面，形成约单原子层厚、过剩量 7.4–9.6 atoms/nm² 的界面层并发展出类 B2 局域有序；Mn、Si 自身偏聚弱，但在 Ni 存在时富集并与 Ni 共偏聚（co-segregation）。基体 Ni 为 1 at.% 时，Gibbs 吸附分析给出 Fe/CRP 自由能降低约 0.15 J/m²。

Conclusion: 界面能系数与偏聚趋势将偏聚、界面能与共沉淀联系起来，可为低合金钢的成分优化（compositional optimization）提供依据。

Abstract: Coupled precipitation and interfacial segregation in low-alloy steels change precipitate stability and morphology, which influence strengthening and embrittlement. Cu-rich precipitates (CRPs) and Mn-Ni-Si-rich precipitates (MNSPs) frequently form core-shell or appendage morphologies, but the interfacial energetics controlling this change are not well quantified. We combine a density-functional-theory-trained cluster expansion with lattice Monte Carlo simulations to evaluate the Fe/CRP, Fe/MNSP and Cu/MNSP interfaces within one Hamiltonian. The three coefficients based on excess internal energy follow gamma_Cu/MNSP < gamma_Fe/CRP <~ gamma_Fe/MNSP and give a positive work of adhesion and a negative spreading parameter, consistent with favorable Mn-Ni-Si attachment to a CRP and partial coverage. Only Ni segregates to the Fe/CRP boundary. Mn and Si segregate weakly on their own but become more enriched when Ni is present and co-segregate with Ni at the boundary. Ni forms an interfacial layer about one monolayer thick with an excess of 7.4-9.6 atoms/nm^2, develops B2-like local order and shows an energy reduction that increases with Ni coverage. At 1 at.% matrix Ni, a Gibbs adsorption analysis gives a Fe/CRP free-energy reduction of about 0.15 J/m^2. These interface coefficients and segregation trends connect segregation, interface energy and co-precipitation and support compositional optimization in low-alloy steels.

</details>


### [378] [A mesoscale damage model for void nucleation and growth in shock loaded microstructure informed by computer vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19159)
*Abhijith Thoopul Anantharanga,Brandon Runnels*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作将基于计算机视觉估计的空洞形核倾向与连续介质损伤模型耦合，在拉格朗日冲击模拟中实现了对钽板撞击层裂（spallation）的微观组织敏感模拟，并以自由表面速度（FSV）实验数据完成标定与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延性材料高应变率下的层裂由空洞形核、长大与合并主导，而损伤模型中的形核判据多为随机性设定，常忽视微观组织（microstructure）的影响；近期研究表明形核倾向可由计算机视觉估计，但尚需嵌入连续介质损伤框架以开展完整模拟。

Method: 以晶粒取向与晶界（grain boundary, GB）能预测的敏感场（susceptibility field）控制空洞形核概率，并以局部最大主应力设定力学势垒；将其与晶体塑性（crystal plasticity）模型耦合于拉格朗日冲击模拟中，微观组织选择性解析以降低计算成本。

Result: 模型在飞片速度196 m/s下完成标定，模拟FSV在冲击到达、峰值状态、卸载及回拉信号（pullback signal）上与实验吻合，偏差仅限晚期振荡；参数不变即迁移至214 m/s工况并准确复现FSV。合成微观组织对比显示，FSV对微观组织显著敏感，仅代表性微观组织可给出正确预测。

Conclusion: 在解析的微观组织中，空洞主要在晶界与三叉节点（triple junctions）处形核（但非唯一位置）。结果表明，将微观组织感知（microstructure-awareness）的形核模型纳入损伤框架对层裂预测至关重要，材料微观组织的代表性是获得可靠FSV预测的前提。

Abstract: Spallation in ductile materials under high strain rates occurs through the process of void nucleation, growth, and coalescence. Void nucleation is known to depend on factors ranging from microstructural to stress state. The criteria for void nucleation in damage models are stochastic, and microstructural awareness is often neglected. Recently, it was shown that nucleation propensity can be estimated using computer vision. In the present work, this is integrated with a continuum damage model framework for comprehensive simulation of spallation in tantalum under plate impact. Void nucleation probability is governed by a susceptibility field, predicted from grain orientations and grain boundary (GB) energy, with a mechanical barrier set by the local maximum principal stress. This is coupled to a crystal plasticity model within a Lagrangian shock simulation, with microstructure selectively resolved to reduce computational cost. The model is calibrated at a flyer velocity of 196 m/s, where the simulated free surface velocity (FSV) matches experimental measurements of shock arrival, peak state, release and the pullback signal measured in flyer impact experiments, with some deviations confined to the late time ringing. With no further parameter changes, the model transfers to a 214 m/s case and accurately reproduces the FSV. To demonstrate the microstructure-awareness of the nucleation model, a selection of synthetic microstructures is presented for comparison. In the resolved microstructure, voids nucleate predominantly (though not exclusively) at GBs and triple junctions. It is shown that the FSV differs significantly based on the incorporated microstructure, yielding the correct FSV prediction only in the case of representative microstructures.

</details>


### [379] [Exploring Energy Landscapes for Functional Materials: Ga vacancies in $β$-Ga$_{2}$O$_{3}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19365)
*Soon Woo Park,Philipp Pracht,Seán R. Kavanagh,David J. Wales*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种持久化套接字接口（persistent socket interface），将 GMIN、OPTIM、PATHSAMPLE 的势能面探索工具与 ASE 兼容的原子间势（interatomic potentials）连接，并用于 β-Ga₂O₃ 中 Ga 空位（vacancy）的动力学跃迁网络分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 势能面探索（energy landscape exploration）需大量能量与力的计算，高效访问原子间势是实际挑战；同时已有机器学习势（machine-learned potentials）与 ASE 生态尚未与全局优化、过渡态搜索等势能面方法顺畅衔接。

Method: 设计持久化套接字接口，使计算器（calculator）与套接字连接在整个搜索过程中保持活跃，避免重复初始化；并以两种基于 NequIP 的势对 β-Ga₂O₃ 中的 Ga 空位构建动力学跃迁网络（kinetic transition network），结合可视化、平均首次通过时间（mean first-passage time）与热容分析。

Result: 温度依赖分析显示最可能的迁移序列会随温度改变，迁移机制由网络的全局性质而非单纯最高能垒（energy barrier）决定；可视化、平均首次通过时间与热容将势能面组织与弛豫时间尺度及热力学可观测量联系起来。

Conclusion: 该接口将势能面方法扩展到 ASE 兼容的原子模型，为结合全局优化、过渡态搜索、动力学网络分析与热力学表征，并与机器学习原子间势的最新进展相衔接提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Energy landscape exploration requires extensive energy and force evaluations, making efficient access to interatomic potentials an important practical challenge. We present a persistent socket interface that connects the landscape exploration tools in the GMIN, OPTIM, and PATHSAMPLE programs to potentials available through the Atomic Simulation Environment (ASE). The persistent design keeps both the calculator and socket connection active throughout the search, avoiding repeated initialisation. We apply this framework to understand and predict the properties of Ga vacancies in $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ using two NequIP-based potentials, and construct kinetic transition networks describing complex vacancy configurations and migration pathways. Temperature-dependent analysis of the dominant paths reveals how the most probable migration sequence can change, with the mechanism governed by global properties of the network, rather than simply the highest energy barrier. Visualisation, mean first-passage times, and heat capacities enable us to connect the organisation of the landscape to relaxation time scales and thermodynamic observables. The interface extends energy landscape methods to ASE-compatible atomistic models, providing a general framework for combining global optimisation, transition state searches, kinetic network analysis, and thermodynamic characterisation with recent developments in machine-learned interatomic potentials.

</details>


### [380] [Porosification of the dielectric layer in alternating-current electroluminescence devices enables high-resolution tactile imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19384)
*Mingwei Gu,Licheng Li,Zeyuan Zhou,Ying Yang,Zixiang Nie,Xingyu Ma,Junshuai Chen,Lin Zheng,Jinhui Gu,Jia-Sen Xie,Ziling Wei,Mengxing Huang,Jin Ge*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出基于交流电致发光（alternating-current electroluminescence, ACEL）器件的自发光触觉成像策略，用可压缩多孔介质电介质将接触压力编码为空间发光图案，无需外部照明或分立传感像素，可实现100 μm分辨率并有望将器件厚度降至5 mm以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉触觉传感（vision-based tactile sensing）虽能实现高分辨率接触感知，但其笨重的光学架构（bulky optical architectures）占用大量空间，限制了灵巧机械手（dexterous robotic hands）的功能设计，因此需要更紧凑的触觉成像方案。

Method: 采用ACEL器件构建自发光触觉成像界面：以可压缩多孔电介质（compressible porous dielectric）将压力转换为空间发光图案；识别出非线性电容依赖的发光窗口（capacitance-dependent emission window）以调控压力—光转换；并通过孔径工程（pore-size engineering）抑制局部发光缺陷。

Result: 孔径工程使空间强度变异降低近九倍，实现低至100 μm接触特征的忠实成像；连续电致发光界面无需电场计算重建即可复现复杂接触图案；ACEL器件可直接层压于CMOS图像传感器（complementary metal-oxide-semiconductor image sensor），预计装配厚度小于5 mm，显著薄于常规视觉触觉传感器。

Conclusion: 该自发光触觉成像方法可减少高分辨率触觉成像所需空间，为灵巧机械手提供更大设计自由度；但摘要仅表述为"预期（is expected to）"可实现紧凑装配，尚未给出完整系统实测。

Abstract: Vision-based tactile sensing enables high-resolution contact perception, yet bulky optical architectures consume valuable space and constrain the functional design of dexterous robotic hands. Here we introduce a self-emissive tactile imaging strategy based on alternating-current electroluminescence (ACEL) devices, in which a compressible porous dielectric encodes contact pressure into spatial emission patterns without external illumination or discrete sensing pixels. We identify a nonlinear capacitance-dependent emission window that provides a basis for tunable pressure-to-light conversion. Pore-size engineering further suppresses local emission deficiencies, reducing spatial intensity variation by nearly ninefold and enabling faithful imaging of contact features down to 100 μm. The continuous electroluminescent interface also reproduces complex contact patterns without computational reconstruction of electrical fields. Direct lamination of the ACEL device onto a complementary metal-oxide-semiconductor (CMOS) image sensor is expected to enable compact contact imaging in an assembly less than 5 mm thick, substantially thinner than conventional vision-based tactile sensors. This approach could reduce the space required for high-resolution tactile imaging, offering greater design freedom for dexterous robotic hands.

</details>


### [381] [Tracking gas-solid hydration kinetics by non-contact in situ transflection micro-FTIR spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19568)
*Kunihiko Shizume,Hidetoshi Miyazaki,Yasushi Hamanaka*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究评估了非接触式原位透反射显微傅里叶变换红外光谱（in situ transflection micro-FTIR）用于追踪水蒸气与无机固体水合反应的能力，并将其与热重分析（TG）结果对比，证明该光谱方法可实现化学选择性的气固反应监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气固反应动力学（gas–solid reaction kinetics）在热化学储热（thermochemical heat storage, TCHS）等应用中十分重要，但实验评估是材料开发的瓶颈，亟需能够原位、化学选择性监测反应的手段。

Method: 将具有不同水合机制与动力学的 La₂(SO₄)₃、CaSO₄ 和 CaO 粉末层沉积于镀金玻璃基底上，在加湿 N₂ 气氛中采用非接触式原位透反射显微 FTIR 光谱跟踪水相关与羟基相关红外吸收带面积的变化，并与热重法（thermogravimetry, TG）得到的水合数进行对比。

Result: FTIR 谱带面积曲线与 TG 水合趋势总体一致，其中 La₂(SO₄)₃ 与 CaSO₄ 吻合较好；CaO 存在一定差异，可能源于其对两测量间气相条件（尤其湿度初始上升）的敏感性。光谱能够区分 La₂(SO₄)₃ 和 CaSO₄ 的分子水吸附（molecular-water uptake）与 CaO 的氢氧化物生成（hydroxide formation），并检测到 CaO 水合过程中的碳酸盐生成（carbonate formation）。

Conclusion: 原位时间分辨 FTIR 可分别监测各个化学反应，而 TG 仅记录其对样品质量的综合效应；结果表明透反射显微 FTIR 在小尺寸局域样品的气固反应化学选择性监测方面具有潜力。

Abstract: Gas--solid reaction kinetics is important in applications such as thermochemical heat storage (TCHS), but experimental evaluation remains a bottleneck in materials development. This study evaluates non-contact \textit{in situ} transflection micro-Fourier transform infrared (FTIR) spectroscopy for tracking hydration reactions between water vapor and inorganic solids. Powder layers of La$_2$(SO$_4$)$_3$, CaSO$_4$, and CaO, exhibiting different hydration mechanisms and kinetics, were deposited on an Au-coated glass substrate and exposed to humidified N$_2$. Evolution of water- and hydroxyl-related IR absorption band areas was compared with thermogravimetry (TG)-derived hydration numbers. The FTIR band-area profiles were broadly consistent with the TG-derived hydration trends, showing close agreement for La$_2$(SO$_4$)$_3$ and CaSO$_4$. For CaO, some differences were observed between the FTIR and TG results, possibly because CaO is sensitive to differences in gas-phase conditions, particularly the initial increase in humidity, between the two measurements. The spectra distinguished molecular-water uptake in La$_2$(SO$_4$)$_3$ and CaSO$_4$ from hydroxide formation in CaO. They also detected carbonate formation during CaO hydration. Thus, \textit{in situ} time-resolved FTIR separately monitored individual chemical reactions, whereas TG recorded only their combined effect on the sample mass. These results demonstrate the potential of transflection micro-FTIR for chemically selective monitoring of gas--solid reactions in small localized samples.

</details>


### [382] [Scaling laws in machine learning on non-ideal experimental data enable inverse design of radiative-cooling coatings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19596)
*Kunihiko Shizume,Jiawen Li,Yaerim Lee,Yuki Asano,Tuwei Liao,Koji Tsuda,Junichiro Shiomi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过自动化平台制备11,520个辐射制冷涂层，验证在含非理想实验变异的数据集上，神经网络与扩散模型均呈现幂律学习曲线（power-law scaling），为评估额外实验数据收益与材料信息学成本效益提供定量依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料信息学（materials informatics）日益面向多组分材料，但实验数据不可避免存在成分、涂层形成与测量中的非均匀性及不可重复性等非理想变异，机器学习预测精度是否仍随数据量增长而提升尚不明确。

Method: 构建自动化平台，在九个材料与工艺参数空间内制备并光学表征11,520个辐射制冷涂层；在10至10,000样本的子集上训练正向与逆向神经网络（forward and inverse neural networks）及光谱条件扩散模型（spectrum-conditioned diffusion model）。

Result: 所有模型均表现出幂律缩放（power-law scaling），预测误差随数据集规模增大而下降；实验逆向设计（inverse-design）验证显示，更大数据集生成的光谱更接近宽带发射体目标；扩散模型可输出多组候选参数组合，支持按可制造性、成本与可扩展性进行方案选择。

Conclusion: 结果表明非理想实验数据下存在可预测的学习曲线缩放，为估算追加实验数据的收益及材料信息学的成本效益提供了定量基础。

Abstract: Materials informatics increasingly targets multicomponent materials, yet experimental datasets inevitably contain non-ideal variations arising from imperfect uniformity and reproducibility in composition, coating formation, and measurement. Whether machine-learning prediction accuracy scales with amount of data under these conditions remains unclear. Here, we develop an automated platform that produced and optically characterized 11,520 radiative-cooling coatings spanning nine material and processing parameters. Forward and inverse neural networks, and a spectrum-conditioned diffusion model were trained on subsets of 10 to 10,000 samples. All models exhibited power-law scaling, with prediction errors decreasing with dataset size. Experimental inverse-design validation showed that larger datasets produced spectra closer to a broadband-emitter target. The diffusion model generated multiple candidate parameter sets, allowing selection among alternative solutions based on ease of fabrication, cost, and scalability. These results demonstrate predictable learning-curve scaling in non-ideal experimental data and provide a quantitative basis for estimating the benefit of additional experimental data, and thus the cost-effectiveness of materials-informatics.

</details>


### [383] [Energetics of Coherent Heterophase Interfaces: Separating Chemical and Strain Contributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19693)
*Adolfo Salgado-Casanova,Jacob Pietryga,Yi Xia,Chris Wolverton*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文构建了涵盖41种金属元素、365个共格（coherent）FCC/FCC、BCC/BCC与HCP/HCP界面的高通量DFT数据库，并用超胞方法分离共格应变能与化学界面能，揭示界面能符号主要由二元体系的整体有序倾向决定而非晶格失配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共格界面能（coherent interfacial energy）决定多相微结构的形成与演化，但计算时须扣除为容纳晶格失配（lattice mismatch）所需的弹性（应变）能，使计算复杂化；现有数据缺乏系统性的金属共格异相界面参考值。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT）与超胞方法（supercell approach），构建不同厚度的超晶格，将共格应变能（coherency strain energy）与界面能的化学贡献分离；晶格失配最高达25%，并按各二元体系的长程有序（long-range ordering）倾向及界面能符号对结果分类。

Result: 多数相分离（phase-separating）体系界面能为正，多数化合物有序（compound-ordering）体系为负，反映异类原子成键有利；存在例外，说明特定界面的化学偏好未必与体相行为一致（应变可抑制有序化、稳定化合物可取不同于超晶格的结构）。界面能与晶格失配无明显相关性，但共格应变能随失配增大；相分离体系最低界面能多沿最密排取向（closest-packed orientations），有序体系无此趋势。与文献界面能及二元相图的对比呈定性一致，定量一致性随参考态、结构约束与温度而变。

Conclusion: 该数据库为金属共格异相界面提供了系统参考，并表明在解释合金行为时分离应变与化学能贡献至关重要。

Abstract: Coherent interfacial energies govern the formation and evolution of multiphase microstructures, but their calculation is complicated by the elastic energy required to accommodate lattice mismatch. Here, we construct a high-throughput DFT database of 365 coherent FCC/FCC, BCC/BCC, and HCP/HCP interfaces involving 41 metallic elements and lattice mismatches of up to 25%. We use the supercell approach, consisting of constructing superlattices of different thicknesses to separate the coherency strain energy from the chemical contribution to the interfacial energy. We classify the results according to the long-range ordering tendency of each binary system and the sign of the calculated interfacial energy. Most phase-separating systems exhibit positive interfacial energies, whereas most compound-ordering systems exhibit negative values, reflecting favorable unlike-atom bonding. Exceptions show that the chemical preference at a particular interface need not coincide with bulk phase behavior: coherency strain can oppose ordering, and stable compounds can adopt structures distinct from the calculated superlattices. No clear correlation of interfacial energy with lattice mismatch is observed, whereas coherency strain energy increases with mismatch. For phase-separating systems, the lowest interfacial energies generally occur along the closest-packed orientations, while this trend is absent for ordering systems. Comparisons with reported interfacial energies and binary phase diagrams show qualitative consistency, although quantitative agreement varies with reference states, structural constraints, and temperature. The database provides a systematic reference for metallic coherent heterophase interfaces and demonstrates the importance of separating strain and chemical energy contributions when interpreting alloy behavior.

</details>


### [384] [Hidden Heat-Current Transport Channels Behind Observable Temperature Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19776)
*Yi Zeng,Jianjun Dong*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出两级谱理论，将有限波矢本构响应分解为弛豫通道与流动通道，揭示热流本构结构的重组可在温度动力学定性特征不变的情况下发生，故温度测量本身不足以验证约化理论的本构结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散衰减与类波振荡等温度动力学特征常被用于表征热传导，但这些特征无法唯一识别潜在的热流输运机制，因此需要区分可观测温度行为与本构输运结构。

Method: 建立两级谱理论：先在有限波矢下将本构响应分解为以极点和热流留数表征的弛豫（relaxational）与流动（streaming）通道，再通过能量守恒将其组合响应与平衡温度涨落相联系；并对17 K下的NaF进行第一性原理计算。

Result: 计算显示，Guyer–Krumhansl（G–K）约化中产生微扰非局域修正的同一流动诱导模式耦合，会驱动孤立动量组织弛豫通道发生非微扰转变，形成占主导的复共轭流动对；该转变破坏G–K的单弛豫通道本构结构，而振荡温度动力学仍存在于其预测振荡窗口短周期边界之下；更短周期下热流权重在多个碰撞耦合流动通道间重新分配，最终趋近独立阻尼的声子流动。

Conclusion: 流体力学本构描述的失效可能反映热流输运的重组而非振荡温度动力学的消失；仅与温度测量相符并不能验证约化理论的本构结构。升温则通过消除孤立动量组织扇区提供超越流体力学描述的另一途径。

Abstract: Diffusive decay and wave-like oscillations in temperature dynamics are commonly used to characterize heat conduction, yet these signatures do not uniquely identify the underlying heat-current transport mechanisms. Here we reveal a spectrum of constitutive heat-current channels whose microscopic organization can change without a corresponding qualitative change in observable temperature dynamics. We develop a two-level spectral theory that first resolves the finite-wave-vector constitutive response into relaxational and streaming channels characterized by their poles and heat-current residues, and then connects their combined response to equilibrium temperature fluctuations through energy conservation. First-principles calculations for NaF at 17~K reveal that the same streaming-induced mode coupling responsible for the perturbative nonlocal correction in the Guyer--Krumhansl (G--K) reduction drives a nonperturbative transformation of the isolated momentum-organized relaxational channel into a dominant complex-conjugate streaming pair. This transformation breaks down the single-relaxational-channel constitutive structure of G--K, while oscillatory temperature dynamics persist below the short-period boundary of its predicted oscillatory window. At shorter periods, heat-current weight redistributes among multiple collision-coupled streaming channels before approaching independently damped phonon streaming. Increasing temperature provides a distinct route beyond hydrodynamics by eliminating the isolated momentum-organized sector, even as oscillatory temperature dynamics persist. Thus, the breakdown of a hydrodynamic constitutive description can reflect a reorganization of heat-current transport rather than the disappearance of oscillatory temperature dynamics, and agreement with temperature measurements alone does not validate the constitutive structure of a reduced theory.

</details>


### [385] [IANN: A Unified Framework for Equivariant InterAtomic Neural Networks with User-Friendly Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19793)
*Changzhi Ai,Xueping Qin,Dimosthenis Sokaras,Frank Abild-Pedersen,Johannes Voss*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: IANN 是一个开源 PyTorch 框架，将 PaiNN、NequIP、MACE、Allegro、EquiformerV2/V3、UMA 等等变图神经网络（equivariant graph neural networks）统一到同一数据对象、训练器与部署路径下，并发布基于约 4190 万条 DFT 结构训练的 PaiNN 基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习原子间势（machine-learning interatomic potentials, MLIPs）已接近密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）精度且成本更低，但软件碎片化（software fragmentation）严重：各架构独立发布、数据格式与训练流程各异，导致同一数据集上的架构对比或将模型投入生产模拟均需大量重构工程。

Method: 以统一数据对象、统一训练器（unified trainer）和单一部署路径重新实现多种等变图神经网络，架构仅需在配置字典中改动一个词；力、应力与维里（virial）均由能量对原子图边矢量的单次解析微分获得，并在周期边界条件（periodic boundary conditions）下精确；分布式训练依据 SLURM、PBS 或 MPI 环境自动配置。

Result: 自描述检查点（self-describing checkpoints）可直接作为 ASE 计算器、输出不确定性的集成模型，或经 TorchScript 通过捆绑的 C++ 配对样式（pair styles）在 LAMMPS 中以单/多 GPU 推理运行；在包含数百万结构的公共数据集上实现同等条件下的架构对比，并发布在 4190 万条经筛选 DFT 结构（覆盖 PBE、RPBE、r2SCAN 泛函）上训练的 PaiNN 基础模型。

Conclusion: IANN 通过统一化框架缓解 MLIP 生态的软件碎片化，支持架构可比性与模型到生产模拟的无缝迁移，并提供智能体接口（agent interface）以便与自主智能体集成。

Abstract: Machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) now approach density functional theory (DFT) accuracy at a fraction of its cost, but the practical benefit is limited by software fragmentation: each state-of-the-art architecture is released as a separate codebase, with its own data format, configuration system, training loop and molecular-dynamics interface. Comparing two architectures on the same dataset, or moving a trained model into a production simulation, therefore requires substantial re-engineering. We introduce IANN (InterAtomic Neural Network), an open-source PyTorch framework that reimplements equivariant graph neural networks - PaiNN, NequIP, MACE, Allegro, EquiformerV2, EquiformerV3 and UMA - behind a unified data object, a unified trainer and a single deployment route, so that the architecture is a one-word change in a configuration dictionary. Forces, stresses and virials are obtained for every architecture from one analytic differentiation of the energy with respect to the edge vectors of the atomic graph, exact under periodic boundary conditions. Distributed training is configured automatically from the SLURM, PBS or MPI environment, so the same script runs unchanged from a workstation to a multi-node GPU allocation. Self-describing checkpoints serve directly as ASE calculators, as ensembles reporting uncertainties, or as TorchScript models run inside LAMMPS through bundled C++ pair styles with single- and multi-GPU inference. Trained on a common dataset of millions of structures, the architectures can be compared under identical conditions. We further release PaiNN foundation models trained on 41.9 million curated DFT structures from MPtrj, Alexandria, OMat24, MatPES, OC20, OC22, OC25, MPr2SCAN and MatPESr2SCAN, spanning the PBE, RPBE and r2SCAN functionals. Additionally, IANN provides an agent interface to facilitate integration with autonomous agents.

</details>


### [386] [Mixed-Sign Kitaev Exchange in Jahn-Teller-Distorted CrI$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19799)
*Shiyan Gao,Zekuo Yang,Chuanhang Chen,Zefeng Chen,Lianchuang Li,Hongjun Xiang,Changsong Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出以姜-泰勒畸变（Jahn-Teller distortion）作为在同一材料中于不等价键上实现铁磁（FM）与反铁磁（AFM）Kitaev耦合共存的微观途径，并以单层CrI$_2$为实例加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁磁性与反铁磁性Kitaev耦合对磁阻挫（magnetic frustration）与磁相的塑造作用不同，但二者在同一材料内的共存此前尚未被探索。

Method: 第一性原理计算识别单层CrI$_2$为候选体系；构建最小紧束缚模型（tight-binding model）关联符号反差与姜-泰勒劈裂的$t_{2g}$轨道及其向$e_g$轨道的跃迁；进一步采用团簇展开（cluster expansion）方法并训练现实可行的机器学习自旋哈密顿量（machine-learned spin Hamiltonian）。

Result: 短键与长键分别呈现反铁磁与铁磁Kitaev耦合，且该相反符号对Hubbard $U$、泛函选择与应变均鲁棒；模型复现实验观测的proper-screw几何与传播方向，并揭示高度非均匀的自旋转动，转角范围为$48.0^\circ$至$93.9^\circ$。

Conclusion: proper-screw各向异性与强非谐自旋转动均源于混合符号Kitaev相互作用；姜-泰勒畸变被确立为混合符号Kitaev交换的微观途径，并为探索新奇磁相开辟新方向。

Abstract: Kitaev couplings with ferromagnetic (FM) or antiferromagnetic (AFM) character shape magnetic frustration and phases differently in real materials, yet their coexistence within a single material remains unexplored. Here, we propose Jahn-Teller distortion as a route to realizing such coexistence on inequivalent bonds. First-principles calculations identify monolayer CrI$_2$ as such a system, with AFM and FM Kitaev couplings on the short and long bonds, respectively. These opposite signs are robust against variations in Hubbard $U$, functionals, and strain. A minimal tight-binding model links this sign contrast to Jahn-Teller-split $t_{2g}$ orbitals and their hopping to $e_g$ orbitals. We further adopt the cluster expansion method and develop a realistic machine-learned spin Hamiltonian, which reproduces the experimentally observed proper-screw geometry and propagation direction and reveals highly nonuniform spin rotation, with turning angles ranging from $48.0^\circ$ to $93.9^\circ$. Both proper-screw anisotropy and strong anharmonic spin rotation arise from the mixed-sign Kitaev interaction. These findings not only establish Jahn-Teller distortion as a microscopic route to mixed-sign Kitaev exchange, but also open a new avenue for exploring novel magnetic phases.

</details>


### [387] [Metallic or insulating magnetic phases of transition-metal oxides in light of the Slater vs Mott controversy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19888)
*Xiuwen Zhang,Jia-Xin Xiong,Alex Zunger*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作在无参数的平均场方法（DFT）框架下，通过将局域磁性基元（如NiO6八面体）嵌入宽禁带量子势垒（MgO）中以消除长程有序（LRO），发现其仍具有内禀带隙，说明反铁磁（AFM）与顺磁（PM）相的绝缘性无需依赖长程有序或强关联即可在对称性破缺的能带图像中解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Slater-Mott之争的核心在于磁性d电子氧化物绝缘性的起源：Slater观点依赖长程反铁磁有序的能带折返来开隙，据此推测无序顺磁相应为金属性；Mott-Peierls-Hubbard观点则强调局域电子排斥U，但U在周期体系中难以测量或计算，实践中常被当作可调参数。当前诸多观测与Slater的预测相冲突，因而需要一个不依赖LRO、也不依赖可调U的解释框架。

Method: 采用无参数的平均场式方法密度泛函理论（Density Functional Theory, DFT），允许能量降低的对称性破缺（symmetry breaking）；将局域磁性基元视为宏观AFM或PM相的结构单元，并把这类基元以量子势垒隔离（例如将磁性NiO6八面体局域地包埋于宽禁带MgO中），从而在电子层面消除基元间的长程有序。

Result: 即使各磁性基元之间在电子上彼此隔离（即长程有序消失），这些被势垒包埋的局域基元仍表现出内禀带隙（intrinsic band gap），表明带隙的形成不依赖于LRO。

Conclusion: AFM相与PM相中带隙的起源既不必归于长程有序，也不必归于强关联（strong correlation），而可在允许对称性破缺的平均场式能带结构框架内得到理解；这为Slater-Mott争议提供了一种不依赖可调U的处理路径。

Abstract: Magnetic d-electron oxides usually have a long-range-ordered ground state and an above-ground-state disordered para phase. The historical Slater-Mott controversy still reverberating to date pertains to understanding the origin of the insulating vs metallic nature of these two phases. Slater suggested that the insulating character evolved from the long-range order (LRO) antiferromagnetic (AFM) cell-doubling, leading to energy band repulsion and thus to forming a band gap. The reliance on LRO to explain gapping suggests that the absence of LRO in the spin-disordered para-phase would deprive the system from band repulsion, thus predicting a metallic paramagnetic (PM) phase. This, however, conflicts with numerous current observations. The alternative Mott-Peierls-Hubbard view abandons the mean-field-like band structure view emphasizing instead the local interelectronic repulsion U as a strong correlation signature of gapping both at the presence or absence of LRO. However, U is not an easily measured or calculated quantity in periodic systems, a situation often leading in practice to treating U as an adjustable parameter. The current work uses a parameter-free mean-field-like approach (Density Functional Theory, DFT), allowing energy-lowering symmetry breaking. This is done by considering local magnetic motifs as building blocks of the macroscopic AFM or PM phases. It is found that even when such motifs are isolated electronically from each other (i.e. vanishing LRO) by embedding them in quantum barriers (such as magnetic NiO6 octahedra surrounded locally by wide-gap MgO) they exhibit an intrinsic band gap. Thus, the origin of the band gaps in AFM or PM phases need not be LRO or strong correlation and can be understood within the mean-field-like band structure approach with symmetry breaking.

</details>


### [388] [Giant Raman Optical Activity in Chiral and Polar Ni$_3$TeO$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19983)
*Philipp Stein,Ravi Kaushik,Evgenia V. Komleva,Thomas C. Koethe,Vladimir Tsurkan,István Kézsmárki,Markus Grüninger,Sergey V. Streltsov,Sergey Artyukhin,Paul H. M. van Loosdrecht*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作在手性极性材料 Ni$_3$TeO$_6$ 的声子中发现比分子体系常见值高约100倍的巨大拉曼光学活性（Raman optical activity, ROA），并借助有限动量下的第一性原理计算将其归因于声子对能带几何与能带色散的调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROA 反映激发的手性，但在固体中通常非常微弱，其在固体声子体系中的来源与机制尚缺乏清晰刻画，需要找到强 ROA 的实例并建立微观解释。

Method: 基于声子选择定则（phonon selection rules）进行系统分析，将手性与非手性散射通道分离；并通过有限声子动量（finite phonon momentum）下拉曼张量（Raman tensor）的第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）建立理论与实验的联系。

Result: 在 Ni$_3$TeO$_6$ 的声子中观测到幅度约为分子常见报道值100倍的巨大 ROA；计算表明该效应与声子对光学旋光性（optical gyrotropy）中能带几何（band geometry）和能带色散（band dispersion）贡献的调制相关。

Conclusion: ROA 可作为探测绝缘体中电子—声子（electron--phonon）对能带几何调制的探针，从而将手性光谱学与能带几何概念联系起来。

Abstract: Raman optical activity (ROA) measures the difference in Raman scattering intensity for right- and left-circularly polarized light and reflects the chirality of excitations. In solids, ROA is commonly very weak. For the phonons of chiral and polar Ni$_3$TeO$_6$, we find a giant ROA with magnitude 100 times higher than commonly reported for molecules. The systematic analysis based on phonon selection rules allows the chiral and achiral scattering channels to be separated. First-principles calculations of the Raman tensor at finite phonon momentum relate the effect to the phonon modulation of the band-geometric and band-dispersion contributions to optical gyrotropy. This establishes Raman optical activity as a probe of electron--phonon modulation of the band geometry in an insulator.

</details>


### [389] [The microscopic origin of the hysteretic hydrogen adsorption in Double Wall Carbon Nanotubes bundles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20022)
*Carlos Cabrillo,Ricardo Fernández-Perea,Felix Fernández-Alonso*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合中子衍射（neutron diffraction, ND）与计算机模拟，阐明双壁碳纳米管（Double Wall Carbon Nanotubes, DWCNT）样品在超临界温度下出现大滞后氢吸附现象的物理起源，并识别出两种导致亚稳氢吸附的微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究已报道DWCNT样品在超临界温度（50 K、77 K、150 K）下表现出大滞后氢吸附（hysteretic hydrogen adsorption）这一具有实际意义的物理行为，但该现象的物理起源尚未被阐明，本文即以此为目标展开探究。

Method: 在中子衍射信号完成详细理论建模的基础上，定量研究材料纳米结构（nanostructure）对氢负载的响应；并利用DWCNT管束沿纳米管轴的平移对称性（translational symmetry），构建二维数值类比系统以澄清滞后的微观起源。

Result: 随着氢负载增加，观察到管束结构发生膨胀（bundle expansion）。滞后现象由两种微观机制解释：一是结构静力学性质的机制，即间隙位之间随H₂负载变化的有效能垒（effective energy barriers）；二是与结构形变相关的机制，即管束膨胀/收缩导致间隙间通道在H₂负载增减时开/闭。

Conclusion: 该工作将DWCNT超临界滞后氢吸附归因于“负载依赖的有效能垒”与“形变驱动的通道开闭”两种机制的共同作用，为理解该材料体系的亚稳吸附行为提供了微观图像。

Abstract: In a recent study we reported large hysteretic hydrogen adsorption in samples made out of Double Wall Carbon Nanotubes (DWCNT) at supercritical temperatures (50 K, 77 K and 150 K), a physical behavior of practical interest. Here, we center our efforts in elucidate its physical origin by means of neutron diffraction (ND) and computer simulations. After a detailed theoretical modeling of the ND signal we were able to quantitatively study how the nanostructure of the material reacts to the hydrogen loads. An expansion of the bundle structure with increasing hydrogen load was found. Taking advantage of the translational symmetry of a DWCNT bundle along the nanotube axis, a two dimensional numerical analogue of the system helped us to clarify the microscopic origin of the hysteresis. Two microscopic mechanisms are identified as responsible for the metastable hydrogen adsorption, one structurally static in character, namely, H$_{2}$-load dependent effective energy barriers between interstitials, the other related to the structural deformation, namely, the opening/closing of the channels between interstitials with increasing/decreasing H$_{2}$ loads owing to the concomitant bundle expansion/contraction.

</details>


### [390] [Surface-bulk magnetic dichotomy in intercalated transition metal dichalcogenides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20123)
*Ke Liao,Wenxin Li,Kelly Dai,Ariel Mazor,Shan Wu,Yu He,Tianyu Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作揭示层状磁性插层过渡金属硫族化合物（magnetically intercalated transition metal dichalcogenides）M1/3TX2中存在表面-体相磁性二分（surface-bulk magnetic dichotomy），即表面与体相倾向不同的磁序；该效应可解释ARPES与中子散射/体相计算之间的矛盾，并为在表面稳定实现交变磁性（altermagnetism）等体相不利磁相提供途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性插层过渡金属硫族化合物具有高度可调的磁序与非常规输运性质，但表面敏感探测（如ARPES）与体相探测/计算给出的磁序图像可能不一致，需要厘清这一矛盾的来源。

Method: 以Fe1/3NbS2为主要对象，结合角分辨光电子能谱（angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, ARPES）、中子散射（neutron scattering）、体相与平板（slab）DFT+U计算，并对比Co1/3NbS2与Co1/3TaS2中K点费米面口袋（Fermi-surface pockets）的观测与计算。

Result: ARPES结果可由A型反铁磁（A-type antiferromagnetic）电子结构重现，而中子散射与体相DFT+U指认条纹序（stripe order）为体相基态；DFT+U平板计算表明Fe终止表面弛豫使磁偏好由条纹序逆转为A型序，同一二分亦恢复Co1/3NbS2和Co1/3TaS2中观测到、但先前体相计算缺失的K点费米面口袋。

Conclusion: Fe1/3NbS2表面稳定磁相具有非相对论性自旋劈裂（nonrelativistic spin splitting）并可表现出交变磁性特征；研究提示不宜将ARPES信号普遍等同于体相磁态，表面-体相磁性二分可作为实现体相非能量有利磁相（含交变磁性）的可控路径。

Abstract: Magnetically intercalated transition metal dichalcogenides are layered materials with highly tunable magnetic orders and unconventional transport properties. Here we report a surface-bulk magnetic dichotomy in $\mathrm{M}_{1/3}\mathrm{T}\mathrm{X}_{2}$, in which the surface and bulk favor distinct magnetic orders. In $\mathrm{Fe}_{1/3}\mathrm{Nb}\mathrm{S}_{2}$, angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) results are reproduced by an A-type antiferromagnetic electronic structure, while neutron scattering and bulk DFT+$U$ calculations identify stripe order as the bulk ground state. DFT+$U$ slab calculations resolve this contradiction by showing that relaxation of an Fe-terminated surface reverses the magnetic preference from stripe to A-type order. The same dichotomy recovers the $K$-centered Fermi-surface pockets that are observed in $\mathrm{Co}_{1/3}\mathrm{Nb}\mathrm{S}_{2}$ and $\mathrm{Co}_{1/3}\mathrm{Ta}\mathrm{S}_{2}$ but absent from previous bulk calculations. Notably, the surface-stabilized magnetic phase of $\mathrm{Fe}_{1/3}\mathrm{Nb}\mathrm{S}_{2}$ carries nonrelativistic spin splitting and can exhibit altermagnetic character. These results caution against the general association of ARPES signals with bulk magnetic states in these systems, and suggest surface-bulk magnetic dichotomy as a promising route to realize magnetic phases, including altermagnetism, that are not energetically favored in the bulk.

</details>


### [391] [Robust Antiferromagnetic Ordering above 500 K in a Family of Bilayer van der Waals Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20133)
*Tengda Rong,Linxin Wu,Xingke Fu,Anqi Huang,Feiran Shen,Zixian Dong,Jianwei Huang,Hao Li,Benyuan Cheng,Lunhua He,Long Jiang,Kun Cao,Zhen Chen,Meng Wang,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作报道了层状半导体氧卤化物 Sr₃Fe₂O₅X₂（X = F, Cl, Br）家族，其中 Sr₃Fe₂O₅F₂ 的奈尔温度约 580 K，为目前已报道层状反铁磁体中的最高值，并预测该反铁磁序在单层极限下仍可维持甚至增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在层状材料中实现室温以上磁序对低维自旋电子学（spintronics）应用极具吸引力，但在层状磁体中实现室温反铁磁性（antiferromagnetism）仍具挑战性。

Method: 结合原子分辨成像确认 Sr₃Fe₂O₅Br₂ 的范德华间隙（van der Waals gap）、合成厘米级 Sr₃Fe₂O₅Cl₂ 单晶，并利用变温中子散射（neutron scattering）与磁化测量确定磁结构与转变温度，辅以密度泛函理论（density functional theory）计算分析交换相互作用来源。

Result: 测得 G 型磁结构与转变温度，Sr₃Fe₂O₅F₂ 的奈尔温度（Néel temperature，约 580 K）为已报道层状反铁磁体最高；计算表明高奈尔温度主要源于沿面外与面内方向的强最近邻超交换（superexchange）作用，尤其是双层结构内沿晶格 c 方向的磁耦合，而层间自旋耦合极弱。

Conclusion: 铁基层状氧卤化物（layered oxyhalides）可作为探索二维极限下室温以上反铁磁性的平台，在信息存储与自旋器件领域具有潜力；理论预测其稳健反铁磁序在单层极限下仍可保持或增强。

Abstract: Stabilizing magnetic order above room temperature in layered materials is highly desirable for low-dimensional spintronics applications. However, realizing room-temperature antiferromagnetism in layered magnets remains challenging. Here, we present a layered semiconducting oxyhalide material family, Sr$_3$Fe$_2$O$_5$X$_2$ ($X$ = F, Cl, Br), which exhibits robust high-temperature antiferromagnetic ordering. The two-dimensional nature and scalability of this system are demonstrated by atomically resolving the van der Waals gap of Sr$_3$Fe$_2$O$_5$Br$_2$ and synthesizing centimeter-scale single crystals of Sr$_3$Fe$_2$O$_5$Cl$_2$. Temperature-dependent neutron scattering and magnetization measurements determine their G-type magnetic structure and transition temperature. Remarkably, the Néel temperature of Sr$_3$Fe$_2$O$_5$F$_2$ ($\sim$580 K) is the highest among all reported layered antiferromagnets. Density functional theory calculations demonstrate that strong nearest-neighbor superexchange interactions along both out-of-plane and in-plane directions, particularly the magnetic couplings along lattice $c$ direction within the bilayer motif, are primarily responsible for the high Néel temperatures in Sr$_3$Fe$_2$O$_5$X$_2$.
