In contrast, inter-bilayer spin couplings are negligibly weak, without much influence on the stabilization of the antiferromagnetic order. Therefore, theoretical calculations predict that this robust antiferromagnetic order could persist or even strengthens at room-temperature even at the monolayer limit. Our results highlight iron-based layered oxyhalides as a promising platform for exploring beyond-room-temperature antiferromagnetism in the two-dimensional limit, holding immense potential in areas like information storage and spin-based devices.

</details>


### [392] [Interface-Selective Extreme Ultraviolet Second Harmonic Generation Away from Linear Absorption Edges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20198)
*Bailey R. Nebgen,Shane W. Devlin,Franky Bernal,Aga Ridhova,James Bullock,Daniel R. Schacher,Jake D. Koralek,Yuya Kubota,Shigeki Owada,Toshihide Sumi,XingYu Su,Iwao Matsuda,Keith V. Lawler,Walter S. Drisdell,Michael W. Zuerch,Craig P. Schwartz*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作测量了低于 Si L$_{2,3}$ 吸收边（60–76 eV）的极紫外二次谐波产生（XUV SHG），在原本线性吸收无特征的价带区观察到来自 Si/SiO$_2$ 界面的强结构化响应，从而把界面敏感的 XUV SHG 拓展到吸收边之外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XUV SHG 兼具界面选择性与芯能级元素特异性，但此前仅限于在线性吸收边处使用；本文旨在探究其"离边（off-edge）"工作的可行性，以区分复杂异质结中不同的埋藏界面。

Method: 对 ITO 覆盖的 MoO$_3$/SiO$_2$/Si 光伏异质结，在 60–76 eV 基频下测量 XUV SHG，并结合分层分辨的第一性原理计算（layer-resolved first-principles）与 Bethe–Salpeter 方程计算进行指认。

Result: 在线性吸收无特征的区域观察到强结构化 SHG 响应，极大值约在 70 eV 与 76 eV；计算重现两峰，并将 76 eV 峰归于桥接 Si 单层、70 eV 峰归于近界面衬底，均源自 Si/SiO$_2$ 界面；体相 Si 的 Bethe–Salpeter 计算将信号联系到 140–160 eV 的 Si 2$p$ 激子（excitons）；在 Si L$_{2,3}$ 边处，响应则由 MoO$_3$ 在氧化物界面诱导的不对称性主导。

Conclusion: 离边 XUV SHG 可作为选择性探测复杂材料异质结构中不同埋藏界面（buried interfaces）的有力手段。

Abstract: Extreme ultraviolet (XUV) second harmonic generation (SHG) combines interface selectivity with core-level element specificity but has so far been limited to linear absorption edges. Here, we measure XUVSHG from an indium tin oxide capped MoO$_3$/SiO$_2$/Si photovoltaic heterojunction for fundamental energies of 60--76\,eV, below the Si L$_{2,3}$-edge. Where linear absorption is a featureless valence background, we observe a strong, structured XUVSHG response with maxima near 70 and 76\,eV. Layer-resolved first-principles calculations reproduce both maxima and assign them to the Si/SiO$_2$ interface, the 76\,eV peak to the bridging Si monolayer and the 70\,eV peak to the near-interface substrate. Bethe--Salpeter calculations for bulk Si connect the signal to Si 2$p$ excitons at 140--160\,eV. At the Si L$_{2,3}$-edge, the calculated response is instead governed by MoO$_3$-induced asymmetry at the oxide interface. These findings establish off-edge XUV SHG as a powerful approach for selectively probing distinct buried interfaces within complex material heterostructures.

</details>


### [393] [Machine Learning the $GW$ Self-Energy Operator for Real Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20247)
*Xian Xu,Bowen Hou,Chengyan Zhang,Diana Y. Qiu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种相位感知的多体变换器（phase-aware many-body transformer），可直接预测固体中完整的、按 $\mathbf{k}$ 分辨的复数自能矩阵（complex self-energy matrix），并在对角化前后均能给出准粒子（quasiparticle, QP）能量，二维材料数据集测试 MAE 为 29–60 meV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对固体 $GW$ 的机器学习（machine learning）多集中于标量准粒子能量修正的预测，忽略了自能的相位与矩阵结构信息；本文意图将预测对象扩展至完整 $\mathbf{k}$ 分辨复数自能矩阵。

Method: 构建相位感知多体变换器，预测完整的 $\mathbf{k}$ 分辨复数自能矩阵，并据此在准粒子哈密顿量（quasiparticle Hamiltonian）对角化前后求解准粒子能量；在静态 COHSEX 近似下使用 1745 种二维材料数据集，并在等离激元极点模型（plasmon-pole model）下使用 433 种材料的有限数据集进行验证，另采用 COHSEX 预训练（pretraining）加微调（finetuning）的策略。

Result: 测试集平均绝对误差（mean absolute error, MAE）在静态 COHSEX 近似下为 29 meV（1745 种二维材料），在等离激元极点模型下为 60 meV（433 种材料）；COHSEX 预训练后微调可将该 MAE 降至 42 meV。所预测矩阵亦能准确重现准粒子波函数（QP wavefunctions）。

Conclusion: 结果作为原理性验证（proof of principle）表明，相位感知的多体变换器可有效预测完整复数自能矩阵并给出高精度准粒子能量与波函数；预训练加微调可进一步改善在更精确方法数据上的表现。

Abstract: Machine learning of $GW$ in solids has largely focused on prediction of scalar quasiparticle energy corrections. Here, we introduce a phase-aware many-body transformer that predicts the full $\mathbf{k}$-resolved complex self-energy matrix. As a proof of principle, we apply the model to predict quasiparticle energies before and after diagonalization of the quasiparticle Hamiltonian, obtaining a test set mean absolute error (MAE) of 29 meV for a dataset of 1745 2D materials in the static COHSEX approximation and 60 meV for a more limited dataset of 433 materials calculated within a plasmon-pole model. COHSEX pretraining followed by finetuning reduces this MAE to 42 meV. The predicted matrices also accurately reproduce QP wavefunctions.

</details>


### [394] [Oxygens breathe, electrons jump](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20260)
*Cristiano Malica,Nicola Marzari*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 以橄榄石型 Li$_x$MnPO$_4$ 为原型，第一性原理分子动力学表明小极化子跳跃与移动离子迁移在动力学上解耦，跳跃由 MnO$_6$ 氧配位笼的呼吸弹性控制，活化能为 0.20±0.03 eV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚阴离子电池正极的电子输运通常被认为与补偿电荷的移动离子运动紧密耦合，但该假设缺乏有限温度下的实时动力学证据，需明确小极化子跳跃的真正控制自由度。

Method: 采用结合扩展 Hubbard 泛函（extended Hubbard functionals）的第一性原理分子动力学（first-principles molecular dynamics），在有限温度下实时追踪 Mn 氧化态与原子亚晶格的绝热演化；并施加选择性约束（冻结单个 MnO$_6$ 八面体的六个氧）与虚拟氧质量缩放（mass scaling）。

Result: Mn$^{2+}$/Mn$^{3+}$ 价态交换频繁发生而插层离子保持局域，二者动态解耦；每次价态交换伴随施主与受主八面体的协同呼吸（ΔV≈2 Å$^3$），体积不对称在转变处过零，符合 Holstein-Marcus 重合条件；跳跃速率给出 $E_a$=0.20±0.03 eV，与配位笼弹性能标度 λ_V/4≈0.20 eV 吻合。

Conclusion: 氧配位笼而非移动阳离子亚晶格是小极化子输运的动力学门控：位点能否接受电子取决于其笼能否呼吸，势垒由笼弹性决定；选择性约束与质量缩放为关联电子与晶格自由度提供了通用途径，并指向插层与混合离子-电子导体中的框架工程（framework engineering）。

Abstract: Electronic transport in polyanionic battery cathodes proceeds through small-polaron hopping between transition-metal sites and is commonly assumed to be tightly coupled to the motion of charge-compensating mobile ions. Using olivine Li$_x$MnPO$_4$ as a prototypical platform, we use first-principles molecular dynamics with extended Hubbard functionals to follow, in real time and at finite temperature, the adiabatic evolution of Mn oxidation states and atomic sublattices. We find that polaron hopping and cation migration are dynamically decoupled: Mn$^{2+}$/Mn$^{3+}$ valence exchanges occur frequently while the intercalated ions remain confined to their sites, and hopping is gated by the local oxygen framework. Freezing the six oxygen atoms of an individual MnO$_6$ octahedron excludes that site from the hopping network, while increasing the fictitious oxygen mass slows down electronic transport. Each valence exchange coincides with a concerted breathing of the donor and acceptor octahedra ($ΔV \simeq 2$ $Å^3$), with their volume asymmetry crossing zero at the transition, consistent with the Holstein-Marcus coincidence condition. The hopping rate yields an activation energy of $E_a = 0.20 \pm 0.03$ eV, closely matching the elastic energy scale of the two coordination cages ($λ_V/4 \approx 0.20$ eV). These results identify the oxygen coordination cage, not the mobile cation sublattice, as the dynamical gate for small-polaron transport: a site can accept the electron only if its cage can breathe, and the barrier is governed by cage elasticity. Selective constraints and mass scaling applied to first-principles dynamics provide a general route to link electronic and lattice degrees of freedom in redox-active materials, opening the door to framework engineering in intercalation and mixed ionic-electronic conductors.

</details>


### [395] [Rashba Effect and Type-III Broken-Gap Band Alignment in MoTe$_2$/HfS$_2$ van der Waals Heterostructures for Tunnel Field-Effect Transistors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20491)
*Daniel S. Lopes,Bruno P. e Silva,Diego R. da Costa,Alexandre C. Dias,Rodrigo G. Amorim,Teldo A. S. Pereira,Jeanlex S. de Sousa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作采用第一性原理密度泛函理论（DFT）研究 MoTe₂/HfS₂ 范德华异质结（vdWH），揭示其本征 type-III（破隙）能带排列与界面 Rashba 自旋劈裂，并证明电场、应变与扭转可逆调控能带排列类型与自旋织构，指向隧穿场效应晶体管（TFET）与自旋电子学应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于过渡金属硫族化物（TMDs）的二维范德华异质结被视为下一代低功耗电子学与自旋电子学（spintronics）的候选平台，因此需要寻找兼具可调能带排列与自旋调控能力的材料体系。

Method: 采用第一性原理密度泛函理论（density functional theory）计算，系统考察 MoTe₂/HfS₂ 异质结的结构、电子与自旋性质，并引入垂直电场、扭转工程（twist engineering）以及面内/面外应变进行调控分析。

Result: 结果显示该异质结具有本征 type-III（broken-gap）能带排列；界面反演对称性破缺与强自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）在 Γ 点附近诱导显著 Rashba 自旋劈裂及螺旋自旋织构。电场与应变可实现 type-II 与 type-III 能带排列之间的可逆转变，并同时改变 Rashba 参数与带间隧穿（band-to-band tunneling）窗口；θ = 21.79° 的扭转结构亦表现出类似可调性。

Conclusion: MoTe₂/HfS₂ 范德华异质结被确立为集电可调 Rashba 自旋劈裂与可控 type-II/type-III 能带排列于一体的多功能平台，为下一代 TFET 与自旋电子器件提供潜在机会。

Abstract: Two-dimensional van der Waals heterostructures (vdWHs) based on transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDs) have emerged as promising platforms for next-generation low-power electronics and spintronics. Here, we investigate the structural, electronic, and spin properties of MoTe$_2$/HfS$_2$ vdWHs using first-principles density functional theory calculations. Our results reveal an intrinsic type-III (broken-gap) band alignment, making this heterostructure highly attractive for tunnel field-effect transistor (TFET) applications. In addition, inversion-symmetry breaking at the interface, combined with strong spin-orbit coupling, induces a pronounced Rashba spin splitting around the $Γ$ point, accompanied by characteristic helical spin textures. We further show that external vertical electric fields, twist engineering, and both out-of-plane and in-plane strain provide efficient means to tune the band alignment, charge transfer, and Rashba parameters. In particular, electric fields and mechanical strain can drive reversible transitions between type-II and type-III band alignments while simultaneously modifying the Rashba spin texture and the band-to-band tunneling (BTBT) window. The twisted heterostructure with $θ= 21.79^\circ$ exhibits similar tunability, including strain-induced transitions between distinct band-alignment regimes. These findings establish MoTe$_2$/HfS$_2$ vdWHs as versatile multifunctional platforms combining electrically tunable Rashba spin splitting with controllable type-II/type-III band alignment, offering promising opportunities for next-generation TFETs and spintronic devices.

</details>


### [396] [Anomalous temperature dependence of the band gap in TiNCl](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20499)
*Masaru Oda,Taisei Ukon,Yuma Mori,Kazuma Nakamura,Masashi Tanaka,Hisao Kondo,Aishi Yamamoto*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 层状氮卤化物（nitride halide）TiNCl 的光学带隙（optical band gap）随温度呈非单调变化：低温下降、约50 K 达极小后回升，该反常行为可由双声子能量的 Bose-Einstein 振子模型（Bose-Einstein oscillator model）描述，并归因于电子-声子相互作用（electron-phonon interaction）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规半导体（conventional semiconductors）的带隙通常随温度单调减小，TiNCl 中是否偏离这一规律及其物理来源尚待澄清，本文旨在刻画其带隙的温度依赖并甄别主导机制。

Method: 结合变温光致发光（photoluminescence, PL）与漫反射光谱（diffuse reflectance spectroscopy）测定带隙；用两特征声子能量的 Bose-Einstein 振子模型拟合；辅以变温 X 射线衍射（X-ray diffraction）与基于实验晶格参数的第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）评估热膨胀（thermal expansion）贡献，并对优化结构做声子态密度（phonon density of states）计算。

Result: 带隙在低温随升温减小、约 50 K 处出现极小值、高温区转而增大，与常规半导体的单调下降相反；模型给出约 8.9 meV 与 1.2 meV 两个特征声子能量，且计算所得声子态密度在这两个能量附近出现峰；热膨胀的贡献相较观测带隙变化可忽略。

Conclusion: TiNCl 光学带隙的反常温度依赖主要由电子-声子相互作用主导，其中低能声子贡献低温段的带隙下降、较高能声子贡献高温段的带隙上升；热膨胀作用可忽略。

Abstract: We report an unusual nonmonotonic temperature dependence of the optical band gap in the layered nitride halide TiNCl, revealed by temperature-dependent photoluminescence (PL) and diffuse reflectance spectroscopy. The band gap, determined from the PL spectra, decreases with increasing temperature at low temperatures, reaches a minimum near 50 K, and then increases at higher temperatures. This behavior contrasts with the monotonic decrease typically observed in conventional semiconductors. The observed temperature dependence is well reproduced by a Bose-Einstein oscillator model with two characteristic phonon energies of approximately 8.9 meV and 1.2 meV. Based on temperature-dependent X-ray diffraction measurements and first-principles calculations using the experimental lattice parameters, we found that the contribution of thermal expansion is negligibly small compared with the observed band-gap variation. Furthermore, first-principles phonon calculations for the optimized crystal structure revealed peaks in the phonon density of states near these two characteristic energies. These results suggest that electron-phonon interactions play an important role in the anomalous temperature dependence of the optical band gap in TiNCl, with low- and higher-energy phonons contributing to the low-temperature decrease and the higher-temperature increase, respectively.

</details>


### [397] [Scaling a Universal Graph Neural Network Force Field to Billions of Atoms on Desk-Side GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20585)
*Jaehyung Lee,Kamal Choudhary*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出基于立方-光晕分解（cube-and-halo decomposition）的方案，使通用图神经网络力场（universal graph neural network force field）ALIGNN-FF 能在台式机上对十亿量级原子进行精确的能量与力评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习力场（machine-learning force fields）虽可模拟数十亿原子，但通常依赖高性能超算集群；将其计算规模下放到单个研究组可及的桌面级硬件是本文动机。

Method: 采用立方-光晕分解，光晕包含模型 20 Å 感受野（receptive field）内的全部原子以保证结果精确；单台 GB10（128 GB 统一内存）可容纳 216,000 个原子并作为单一图（graph）计算力。

Result: 在 NVIDIA GB10 上评估超过十亿原子，九台 GB10 实现 20 亿原子评估；并给出十亿原子多晶（polycrystal）、1.02 亿原子微电子栅堆叠（gate stack）与 106 万原子水中卫星烟草花叶病毒（satellite tobacco mosaic virus）三个案例。

Conclusion: 该工作使实验相关尺度的单点评估（single-point evaluations）可由单一研究组完成，代码已开源于 https://github.com/atomgptlab/scalingmat。

Abstract: Machine-learning force fields are capable of simulating billions of atoms, but usually require high-performance supercomputing clusters. We show that a universal graph neural network force field, ALIGNN-FF, evaluates energies and forces of more than one billion atoms on desk-side computers such as the NVIDIA GB10, which has 128 GB of unified memory, by using a cube-and-halo decomposition. The halo contains every atom within the model's 20 Å receptive field, which makes the result exact. One GB10 holds 216,000 atoms with forces as a single graph. The cube-and-halo decomposition removes this limit, enabling an evaluation of 2 billion atoms on nine GB10s. We also demonstrate three case studies: a billion-atom polycrystal, a 102-million-atom microelectronic gate stack and a 1.07-million-atom satellite tobacco mosaic virus in water. This brings single-point evaluations at experimentally relevant sizes within reach of one research group. The code is available at: https://github.com/atomgptlab/scalingmat.

</details>


### [398] [Nitrogen Defect Coupling in Epitaxial LaFeO3 From Structural Reconstruction to Water Oxidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20614)
*Le Wang,Shan Lin,Min-Ju Choi,Krishna P. Koirala,Mark E. Bowden,Jeffrey A. Dhas,Zihua Zhu,George E. Sterbinsky,Tiffany C. Kaspar,Chongmin Wang,Scott A. Chambers,Ho Nyung Lee,Peter V. Sushko,Yingge Du*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在地球储量丰富的LaFeO3外延薄膜中引入微量氮（N/O<0.8%），会耦合氧空位、Fe空位与Fe金属析出等缺陷重构，显著提升初始析氧反应（OER）活性，但该高活性态在OER条件下亚稳，随近表面氮耗竭与Fe0氧化/溶出而快速衰减至准稳态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ABO3型钙钛矿氧化物是研究阴离子化学与缺陷如何调控电催化行为的理想平台，但稀浓度阴离子掺入与缺陷形成、结构重构及水氧化性能之间的关联尚不清晰，本研究拟揭示这一关系。

Method: 采用氮等离子体辅助脉冲激光沉积（pulsed laser deposition）制备外延LaFeO3-xNy薄膜，结合结构/价态表征与从头算计算（ab initio calculations），并开展电化学测试（含pH依赖动力学与OER后阴离子再分布分析）。

Result: 掺氮伴随氧含量降低、局域Fe金属析出（Fe-metal exsolution）与明显晶格膨胀，而薄膜体相仍以Fe3+为主；计算给出一类电荷平衡的扩展缺陷构型（N替位耦合氧空位与Fe空位），可复现晶格膨胀并保持体相平均Fe价态。电子态再分布改变电输运性质，初始OER活性比未掺杂LaFeO3高近两个数量级。

Conclusion: 高活性生长态在OER条件下呈亚稳性，近表面氮耗竭与Fe0氧化/去除导致活性快速衰减至准稳态；pH依赖动力学与OER后阴离子再分布提示可能存在晶格氧参与。研究建立了稀阴离子掺入、耦合缺陷重构、电子性质与电化学行为之间的关系。

Abstract: Earth-abundant ABO3-type perovskite oxides provide a versatile platform for understanding how anion chemistry and defects govern electrocatalytic behavior. Here, we use epitaxial LaFeO3-xNy thin films to reveal how dilute N incorporation (<0.8% N/O) couples to defect formation, structural reconstruction, and water oxidation. Nitrogen-plasma-assisted pulsed laser deposition introduces N and is accompanied by reduced oxygen content, localized Fe-metal exsolution, and pronounced lattice expansion, while preserving predominantly Fe3+ in the bulk of the film. Ab initio calculations identify a plausible charge-balanced extended defect configuration involving coupled oxygen and Fe vacancies with substitutional N incorporation, reproducing the experimentally observed lattice expansion while preserving the bulk-average Fe oxidation state. The resulting electronic-state redistribution is accompanied by substantially modified electrical transport and nearly two orders of magnitude higher initial oxygen evolution reaction (OER) activity than undoped LaFeO3. Under OER conditions, however, the highly active as-grown state is metastable: near-surface N depletion and Fe0 oxidation/removal accompany rapid initial activity decay toward a quasi-steady state. pH-dependent kinetics and post-OER anion redistribution are consistent with possible lattice-oxygen participation. These results establish relationships among dilute anion incorporation, coupled defect reconstruction, electronic properties, and electrochemical behavior in perovskite oxides.

</details>


### [399] [Molecular-orbital origin of weak orbital ferromagnetism and anomalous Hall effect in a trimerized kagome antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20632)
*I. V. Solovyev*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文揭示三聚化笼目晶格（trimerized kagome lattice）中非共线自旋序诱导弱轨道铁磁性与反常霍尔效应（AHE）的分子轨道起源，并将其映射为三角晶格上具有复跃迁的三聚体模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在阐明三聚化笼目晶格中弱轨道铁磁性（weak orbital ferromagnetism）与反常霍尔效应（anomalous Hall effect）的微观分子轨道来源，理解自旋手性（spin chirality）与自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）如何共同作用。

Method: 在双交换（double-exchange）区域，将孤立三聚体的分子轨道基底（molecular-orbital basis）变换应用于m=±1轨道角动量态，导出受自旋手性与自旋轨道耦合产生的有效塞曼场（effective Zeeman field）支配的低能有效模型；原晶格问题映射为三聚体的三角晶格，最近邻与次近邻跃迁产生复数跃迁振幅。

Result: 结果表明自旋手性与自旋轨道耦合可重写为有效塞曼场，解除m=±1态简并、破坏时间反演对称性并产生弱轨道铁磁性与AHE；相图包含金属相、拓扑平庸绝缘相及具有非零陈数（Chern number）和量子反常霍尔效应的多种拓扑相。

Conclusion: 研究给出弱轨道铁磁性与AHE的清晰分子轨道图像，并识别出仅由复三聚体间跃迁产生的规范场（gauge field）驱动时间反演对称破缺与轨道磁化的替代路径，无需有效塞曼场。

Abstract: We reveal the molecular-orbital origin of weak orbital ferromagnetism and the anomalous Hall effect (AHE) induced by noncollinear spin order in a trimerized kagome lattice. By transforming to the molecular-orbital basis of an isolated trimer in the double-exchange regime, we derive an effective low-energy model for the states with opposite orbital angular momenta $m= \pm 1$ subject to an effective Zeeman field arising from spin chirality and spin-orbit (SO) coupling. In this representation, the kagome-lattice problem maps onto a triangular lattice of trimers, while the nearest- and next-nearest-neighbor hoppings of the original lattice generate complex hopping amplitudes between neighboring trimers. This formulation provides a transparent picture of how spin chirality and SO coupling can be recast as an effective Zeeman field that lifts the degeneracy of the $m= \pm 1$ states, breaks time-reversal symmetry, and thereby gives rise to weak orbital ferromagnetism and the AHE. The resulting phase diagram includes metallic phases, topologically trivial insulating phases, as well as several topological phases characterized by nonzero Chern numbers and the quantum anomalous Hall effect. We further identify an alternative route to time-reversal-symmetry breaking and orbital magnetization, driven by an emergent gauge field arising solely from complex inter-trimer hoppings and requiring no effective Zeeman field.

</details>


### [400] [Intertwined Spin Chirality and Weyl Topology Driven Giant Hall Responses in Nd$_{4}$Sb$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20662)
*Anu,Subhasmita Kar,Bhagyashree Pol,Debarghya Dutta,Aftab Alam,P D Babu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该摘要将 Nd₄Sb₃ 确立为一种高度自旋极化的磁性外尔半金属（magnetic Weyl semimetal），其磁重构同时驱动实空间标量自旋手性与动量空间外尔拓扑，并由此产生交织的反常霍尔与拓扑霍尔响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性外尔材料可耦合磁序与实空间、动量空间拓扑，但二者在同一材料中同时显现的情形仍不常见，本文据此寻找并表征候选体系。

Method: 实验上观测磁性与输运行为（T_C、自旋重取向、拓扑霍尔电阻率与反常霍尔电导率）；理论上采用第一性原理计算、交换相互作用计算、蒙特卡洛模拟与贝里曲率（Berry curvature）计算。

Result: Nd₄Sb₃ 在 T_C≈72 K 以下铁磁有序，约 42 K 自旋重取向进入非共面磁态；观测到约 3.9 μΩ cm 的拓扑霍尔电阻率与约 1170 S cm⁻¹ 的反常霍尔电导率。计算给出有限局域标量自旋手性、以铁磁交换为主并含各向异性交换与 Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya 相互作用，蒙特卡洛转变温度约 78 K 接近实验 T_C；磁重构使外尔点对由共线铁磁态的 42 对增至非共面态的 115 对，贝里曲率给出的本征反常霍尔电导率约 983 S cm⁻¹，与实验本征贡献约 990 S cm⁻¹ 高度吻合。

Conclusion: Nd₄Sb₃ 是磁重构将实空间自旋手性与动量空间外尔拓扑相互交织的典型平台，且计算与实验的定量一致性支持该图像。

Abstract: Magnetic Weyl materials offer a unique platform for coupling magnetic order with real- and momentum-space topology, yet their simultaneous manifestation in a single material remains uncommon. Here, Nd$_4$Sb$_3$ is established as a highly spin-polarized magnetic Weyl semimetal exhibiting intertwined anomalous and topological Hall responses. Nd$_4$Sb$_3$ orders ferromagnetically below $T_{\mathrm{C}}\sim72$ K and undergoes spin reorientation near 42 K into a non-coplanar magnetic state. A large topological Hall resistivity of $\sim3.9 μΩ$ cm and anomalous Hall conductivity reaching $\sim1170$ S cm$^{-1}$ are observed. First-principles calculations identify an energetically preferred non-coplanar state with finite local scalar spin chirality, providing a microscopic route to the real-space Berry phase underlying the topological Hall effect. Calculated exchange interactions reveal dominant ferromagnetic exchange together with anisotropic exchange and finite Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya interactions, while Monte Carlo simulations yield a magnetic transition near 78 K, close to the experimental $T_{\mathrm{C}}$. Concurrently, magnetic reconstruction profoundly reshapes the reciprocal-space topology, increasing the number of Weyl-point pairs from 42 in the collinear ferromagnetic state to 115 in the non-coplanar state. Berry-curvature calculations yield an intrinsic anomalous Hall conductivity of $\sim983$ S cm$^{-1}$ at the experimentally determined carrier concentration, closely reproducing the experimental intrinsic contribution of $\sim990$ S cm$^{-1}$. These results establish Nd$_4$Sb$_3$ as a compelling platform where magnetic reconstruction intertwines real-space spin chirality with momentum-space Weyl topology.

</details>


### [401] [Adsorbate-Free Screening of Dissociative Water Adsorption on Nickel Nanoparticles via Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20706)
*Marta Monti,Alec Noppe,Olivier Lugier,Maria B. Brands,Bernd Ensing*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种无吸附物表示（adsorbate-free representation）的图神经网络（graph neural network, GNN）框架：以锚定原子集合及其局部Ni环境构建图，仅用裸Ni几何预测OH*+H*共吸附能并判别结合位点在DFT弛豫后能否保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限Ni纳米体系上解离水吸附的OH*与H*碎片可占据大量表面位点组合，常规密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）穷举筛选代价过高，缺少高效预筛手段。

Method: 候选O、H结合位点由Ni原子集合（anchors）定义，位点对编码为仅含锚定原子及局部环境的图；输入仅为MACE弛豫后的裸Ni构型，分别训练能量GNN预测DFT参考的OH*+H*吸附能，以及位点保留分类器（retention classifier）预测DFT弛豫后位点是否保留。

Result: 训练集覆盖Ni纳米团簇与Ni(111)/Ni(100)表面；内部验证集平均绝对误差（mean absolute error）为0.031 eV，并在未见过的Ni55、Ni91、Ni130团簇及更大表面晶胞上表现出可迁移性，相对能排序尤佳，但定量精度随体系而异。

Conclusion: 应用于约3000原子Ni纳米颗粒时，可识别有利共吸附构型，并发现O位点配位数较低通常稳定解离态、H位点配位影响较弱；该框架为吸附能筛选提供高效途径，并有望推广至其他金属与吸附物组合。

Abstract: Dissociative water adsorption is a key step in heterogeneous and electrochemical catalysis, but on finite Ni nanosystems the OH$^*$ and H$^*$ fragments can occupy many combinations of surface sites, making exhaustive density functional theory (DFT) screening impractical. We introduce a graph neural network (GNN) framework with an adsorbate-free representation: candidate O- and H-binding sites are defined by sets of Ni atoms (\textit{anchors}), and each site pair is encoded as a graph constructed solely from these anchors and their local Ni environment. Using only MACE-relaxed bare Ni geometries as input, separate GNNs predict the DFT-reference adsorption energies of OH$^*$+H$^*$ configurations and whether the proposed binding sites would be retained upon DFT relaxation, eliminating the need to construct or relax adsorbates during screening. Trained across Ni nanoclusters and Ni(111)/Ni(100) surfaces, the energy model achieves a mean absolute error of 0.031 eV on the internal validation set and demonstrates transferability to unseen Ni$_{55}$, Ni$_{91}$, and Ni$_{130}$ clusters and larger surface cells, particularly for relative-energy ranking, although quantitative accuracy varies across systems. These independent tests also support the retention classifier's use as a conservative filter for selecting configurations likely to preserve their proposed binding sites. Application to Ni nanoparticles containing up to $\sim$3000 atoms identifies favorable co-adsorption motifs and shows that lower O-site coordination generally stabilizes the dissociated state, whereas H-site coordination has a weaker influence. The framework thus offers an efficient route to screening adsorption energetics, with potential extensions to other metals and adsorbate combinations.

</details>


### [402] [Kinetic and Thermodynamic Limits of Sodium Pore Filling in Hard Carbon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20749)
*Ihor Radchenko,Andrés Felipe Usuga,Yuxin Tang,Oleksandr I. Malyi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）与大正则蒙特卡洛（grand canonical Monte Carlo）孔填充模型，揭示硬碳（hard carbon）负极闭孔（closed pores）储钠并非单一平衡平台机制，而是孔特异的多个热力学与动力学激活过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬碳负极的低电压平台容量通常被视为闭孔储钠的关键特征，但将平台行为简单归结为单一平衡机制的认识存在不足，需澄清孔径与缺陷如何分别通过热力学与动力学途径影响储钠。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）结合大正则蒙特卡洛（grand canonical Monte Carlo）孔填充模型，系统考察不同孔径下的填充电压与动力学过程（成核、聚合、生长与体相扩散），并分析孔壁缺陷（defects）浓度的影响。

Result: 热力学上，Na 填充变得有利的电压随孔径增大而降低；动力学上，小孔填充迅速，大孔同时受较小热力学驱动与显著动力学限制。孔壁缺陷可加速成核（nucleation），但缺陷浓度过高会减慢体相扩散（bulk diffusion）。

Conclusion: 并非所有闭孔对储钠贡献相同：部分孔在热力学上有利但在实际充放电倍率下动力学不活跃。硬碳电极的逆向设计（inverse design）应瞄准动力学可及的孔径与缺陷密度窗口，以兼顾高平台容量与快充性能。

Abstract: The low-voltage plateau-like capacity of hard carbon anodes is widely regarded as a key signature of sodium storage in closed pores, often defining the performance of Na-ion batteries. Here, we combine first-principles calculations with a grand canonical Monte Carlo pore-filling model to show that Na storage in closed pores is not governed by a single equilibrium-plateau mechanism but by a distribution of pore-specific thermodynamic and kinetic activation processes. Thermodynamically, the voltage at which Na pore filling becomes favorable decreases with increasing pore size. Kinetically, pore filling involves Na nucleation, coalescence, growth, and bulk diffusion. While small pores fill rapidly, large pores exhibit both a smaller thermodynamic stimulus and significant kinetic limitations. Nucleation can be accelerated by introducing defects at the pore walls, although an excessively high defect concentration slows bulk diffusion. This work shows that not all closed pores contribute equally to Na storage: some may be thermodynamically favorable but kinetically inactive under realistic charge/discharge rates. Because of this, the inverse design of hard carbon electrodes should target a kinetically accessible pore-size and defect-density window that balances high plateau-like capacity with fast-charging performance.

</details>


### [403] [Detecting False Self-Consistency in Density-Functional Calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20789)
*Mohsen Sotoudeh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文指出，密度泛函计算（density-functional calculations）中以能量变化和残差低于阈值判定收敛的标准，甚至多个独立优化器的一致结果，仍可能接受能量可继续下降或哈密顿量与能量不一致的态；作者提出两种无需修改代码的探针，并在VASP算例及339个Materials Project结构普查中发现显著比例的“假收敛”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度泛函计算结果被用于构建材料数据库（materials databases）与机器学习模型，其可靠性依赖收敛判据；然而现行阈值型判据可能遗漏更低的能量态或内部不一致的解，因此需要检验这些判据的有效性。

Method: 在Kohn-Sham哈密顿量空间中给出自洽性的变分表述（variational formulation），构造“下降探针”（descent probe）以揭示从返回解可到达的更低能量态；并采用冻结轨道Janak检验（frozen-orbital Janak test）检测哈密顿量与能量的不一致。两种方法均可对任意代码无修改使用。

Result: 在VASP中，UO$_2$的PBE+$U$计算收敛于比更低态高1.46 eV处，CoO与FeO收敛为金属并高于绝缘态0.8–0.9 eV。对339个无钨Materials Project结构以修改的静态协议重算的普查中，57个（16.8%）表现出超过阈值的能量下降，其中49个未出现超过容差的初始能量上升。

Conclusion: 常规收敛阈值与优化器一致性不足以保证自洽解的正确性，所提出的下降探针与Janak检验可作为通用诊断工具，提示材料数据库与机器学习训练数据的收敛判定需重新审视。

Abstract: Density-functional calculations feed materials databases and machine-learning models, and are declared converged when energy changes and residuals fall below thresholds. We show that these criteria, and even the agreement of independent optimizers, can accept a state from which the energy still descends or whose Hamiltonian is inconsistent with the energy. A variational formulation of self-consistency in the space of Kohn-Sham Hamiltonians yields a descent probe that exposes lower states accessible from a returned solution, and a frozen-orbital Janak test detects such an inconsistency. Both apply to any code without modification. In VASP, a UO$_2$ PBE+$U$ calculation converges 1.46 eV above a lower state, and CoO and FeO converge as metals 0.8-0.9 eV above insulating states. In a census of 339 converged calculations on tungsten-free Materials Project structures, recomputed with a modified static protocol, 57 (16.8%) show energy decreases above the threshold, 49 without an initial energy rise exceeding the tolerance.

</details>


### [404] [Multiple moiré polarization textures in twisted trilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20793)
*Haoshen Ye,Ying Zhou,Shuai Dong*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作指出扭转三层体系中相对莫尔相位（relative moiré phase）可作为额外结构自由度，突破扭转双层中极化织构拓扑被固定堆垛构型锁定的限制，从而实现极化织构的可调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转范德华层（twisted van der Waals layers）因莫尔调制堆垛与继承的滑动铁电性（sliding ferroelectricity）呈现非平庸极化织构；但任一扭转双层中，给定材料的本征扭转-堆垛构型使极化织构拓扑基本固定，需要新的结构自由度来解除该约束。

Method: 以具有不同对称性的 CrI3 与 BN 作为代表性滑动铁电体，利用相对莫尔相位作为扭转三层中的附加结构自由度，结合层间滑动，分析相对莫尔相位如何重塑三层堆垛构型并调控净极化织构。

Result: 扭转三层中可通过相对莫尔相位与层间滑动获得多种莫尔极化织构（multiple moiré polarization textures）；这些织构可归因于两个双层子系统各自原始莫尔图案（moiré patterns）的干涉。

Conclusion: 相对莫尔相位为扭转三层提供了调控极化织构的有效途径，使极化织构拓扑不再被单一本征堆垛构型固定；该机制在 CrI3 与 BN 等不同对称性的滑动铁电体中均成立。

Abstract: Twisted van der Waals layers exhibit nontrivial polarization textures from moiré-modulated stacking and the inherited sliding ferroelectricity. However, in any twisted bilayer, the topology of polarization texture is essentially fixed due to the inherent twist-stacking landscape for a given material. Here, this constraint is overcome in some twisted trilayers, where the relative moiré phase emerges as an additional structural degree of freedom. Using CrI$_3$ and BN as representative sliding ferroelectrics with distinct symmetries, it is clarified how the relative moiré phases reshape the trilayer stacking landscapes and tune the net polarization texture by layer sliding. The multiple moiré polarization textures can be attributed to the interference of original moiré patterns of the two bilayer subsystems.

</details>


### [405] [How Elasticity and Chiral Damping Shape Domain-Wall Creep](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20908)
*Adriano Di Pietro,Alessandro Magni,Stefania Pizzini,Giovanni Gandini,Frowin Dörr,Yasser Shokr,Mustafa Erkovan,Gianfranco Durin,Silvia Tacchi,Marco Madami,Giovanni Carlotti,Emily Darwin,Alexandra J. Huxtable,Christopher H. Marrows,Brian J. Hickey,Vito Puliafito,Michaela Küpferling*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文通过对称性分解速度曲线，提出一个解析模型，刻画蠕变区（creep regime）中畴壁刚度与手性阻尼（chiral damping）的耦合影响，解释了非对称畴壁膨胀与界面Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用（iDMI）测量长期不符的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非对称畴壁膨胀被视为探测界面Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用（iDMI）乃至手性阻尼的可行手段，但其结果与互补测量技术存在严重偏差，且缺乏能描述手性畴壁蠕变完整实验现象的模型，限制了该方法的应用。

Method: 采用速度曲线的对称性分解（symmetry decomposition），推导出解析模型，用以描述蠕变区中畴壁刚度（domain-wall stiffness）与手性阻尼的耦合作用。

Result: 两者的相互作用产生非对称畸变（antisymmetric distortion），使速度极小值偏离DMI场；将模型应用于Co基磁性多层膜后，所得iDMI值与BLS及流区（flow-regime）基准一致，并揭示了长期偏差的成因。

Conclusion: 该模型解释了既有的测量偏差，并为利用畴壁蠕变动力学测量手性阻尼提供了可能的策略思路。

Abstract: Asymmetric domain-wall expansion in the creep regime provides an experimentally accessible probe of the interfacial Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (iDMI) and, in principle, chiral damping. However, severe discrepancies with complementary measurement techniques and the lack of a model describing the full experimental phenomenology of the creep of chiral domain walls, limits the application of these measurements. Here, using a symmetry decomposition of the velocity curve, we derive an analytical model describing the coupled influence of domain-wall stiffness and chiral damping in the creep regime. Their interplay generates the antisymmetric distortion that shifts the velocity minimum away from the DMI field. Applied to Co-based magnetic multilayers, the model yields iDMI values consistent with BLS and flow-regime benchmarks and identifies the reasons for the longstanding discrepancy. The model provides insights for possible strategies to measure chiral damping via creep dynamics of domain walls.

</details>


### [406] [Bias Conditioning and Long-Range Electrostatics for Consistent Machine-Learning Potentials of Electrified Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21026)
*Lucas B. T. de Kam,Patrick Rinke,Christopher J. Stein,Philip Loche*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作检验带电界面机器学习原子间势（machine-learning interatomic potentials, MLIPs）是否只需标量电极电荷作为输入，以及短程模型能否描述非局域响应；结果表明短程场条件化模型显著过度极化并遗漏台阶Pt的功函数移动，而长程模型可同时重现两者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带电界面MLIP需要偏压（bias）作为输入，但标量电极电荷是否足够、短程模型能否刻画非局域响应（non-local response）尚不明确，故需系统检验。

Method: 为短程与长程MLIP同时引入电荷与电场条件化（charge and field conditioning），并比较其对厚水膜与台阶Pt体系的描述能力。

Result: 最先进的场条件化短程模型将厚水膜过度极化近两倍，且遗漏台阶Pt处的功函数移动（work-function shifts）；长程模型则能捕捉二者，在金属处仅需电极电荷作为输入，并重现Pt(111)–水界面的从头算（ab initio）模拟。

Conclusion: 电极电荷作为标量输入在长程模型中已足够，非局域响应必须由长程项描述，短程场条件化模型不足以定量刻画带电金属–水界面。

Abstract: Machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) of electrified interfaces need the bias as input, but it is unclear whether a scalar electrode charge suffices and whether short-range models capture the non-local response. We add charge and field conditioning to short- and long-range MLIPs. State-of-the-art field-conditioned short-range models overpolarize thick water films nearly twofold and miss work-function shifts at stepped Pt. Our long-range model captures both, needs only the electrode charge at metals as input, and reproduces ab initio simulations of the Pt(111)--water interface.

</details>


### [407] [Large-scale Monte Carlo/Molecular Dynamics simulations with Atomic Cluster Expansion potentials in LAMMPS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21147)
*Lorenzo Piersante,Anirudh Raju Natarajan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种 MPI/OpenMP 混合并行方案，使基于原子团簇展开（Atomic Cluster Expansion, ACE）势的蒙特卡洛/分子动力学（MCMD）模拟可在数十万原子尺度上运行，并在 Hf–Ti 与 Cu–Zr 体系中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 介观尺度（mesoscale）模拟通常涉及数十万原子，现有实现下机器学习原子间势（machine-learning interatomic potentials）的 MCMD 计算在此规模不可行，因而只能依赖经验势（empirical potentials），限制了精度。

Method: 在 LAMMPS 中实现 MPI/OpenMP 混合并行：蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）步由局部能量变化评估，并在 MPI 进程间并行；分子动力学（molecular dynamics）则由 OpenMP 线程并行化。

Result: 在 CPU 数固定时，蒙特卡洛步的墙钟时间（wall time）按 MPI 进程数成比例下降；更大模拟盒子因热涨落（thermal fluctuations）随体系尺寸减小而降低自由能计算的统计误差。应用于强非谐（strongly anharmonic）Hf–Ti 体系 HCP/BCC 相自由能计算，以及 670,000 原子纳米晶铜中锆偏聚模拟；Cu–Zr 中 ACE 势与经验势给出相近的晶界成分，但晶粒内锆固溶量不同。

Conclusion: 该方案使机器学习原子间势的 MCMD 模拟在微结构（microstructure）长度尺度上具备可行性。

Abstract: Monte Carlo/molecular dynamics (MCMD) simulations are widely used to study the properties of materials at the mesoscale. Simulations with hundreds of thousands of atoms usually rely on empirical potentials because MCMD with machine-learning interatomic potentials is impractical at this scale in existing implementations. Here, we introduce a hybrid MPI/OpenMP scheme for MCMD with Atomic Cluster Expansion (ACE) potentials in LAMMPS. Monte Carlo moves are evaluated from local energy changes and carried out in parallel over MPI ranks, while molecular dynamics is parallelized over OpenMP threads. At a fixed number of CPUs, the wall time of the Monte Carlo moves decreases by a factor equal to the number of MPI ranks. Larger simulation cells also reduce the statistical errors of free energy calculations because thermal fluctuations decrease with system size. We demonstrate the scheme by calculating free energies of the HCP and BCC phases in the strongly anharmonic Hf--Ti system and by simulating zirconium segregation in a nanocrystalline copper sample of 670,000 atoms. For Cu--Zr, an ACE potential and an empirical potential predict similar grain boundary compositions but different amounts of zirconium dissolved in the grains. The scheme makes MCMD simulations with machine-learning interatomic potentials practical at the length scales of microstructure.

</details>


### [408] [Moiré Excitons in a Chern Ferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21224)
*Shiyu Peng,Yifan Wang,Peiyao Qin,Yixuan Liu,Xi Dai,Chen Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文在 ν_h=1 的扭角双层 MoTe2 中，利用 Gutzwiller-贝特-萨尔皮特方程框架识别出两种选择性耦合相反圆偏振的近红外陈铁磁激子（Chern-ferromagnet excitons, CFEs），并揭示强电子-空穴相互作用对激子能量与动量空间结构的重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验已将激子光学共振确立为探测与调控莫尔陈铁磁体（moiré Chern ferromagnets）的有力手段，但此类相互作用驱动、时间反演对称破缺基态上激子的微观本质仍缺乏研究。

Method: 采用 Gutzwiller 方法与贝特-萨尔皮特方程（Bethe-Salpeter equation）相结合的框架，研究填充因子 ν_h=1 的扭角双层 MoTe2，在同时处理电子-电子关联与电子-空穴相互作用下求解激子态。

Result: 识别出两种近红外 CFE，分别选择性耦合相反圆偏振；强电子-空穴相互作用显著增大其能量分裂，并驱动 X- 态在动量空间的重构，超出于准粒子跃迁图像之外；该重构增强 X- 的层间电子-空穴特征，产生巨大的二次斯塔克响应（quadratic Stark response）。

Conclusion: CFE 的形成源于电子-电子关联与电子-空穴相互作用的非平凡耦合，其光学特征可作为莫尔陈铁磁序的指纹，并为其光学调控提供微观基础。

Abstract: Recent experiments have established excitonic optical resonances as powerful probes and control knobs of moiré Chern ferromagnets. However, the microscopic nature of excitons formed on such interaction-driven, time-reversal-symmetry-broken ground states remains largely unexplored. In this paper, using a Gutzwiller plus Bethe-Salpeter equation framework, we investigate twisted bilayer MoTe2 at hole filling ν_h=1 and identify two near-infrared Chern-ferromagnet excitons (CFEs) selectively coupled to opposite circular polarizations. Strong electron-hole interactions markedly enhance their energy separation and drive a pronounced momentum-space reconstruction of the emergent X- state, beyond the quasiparticle-transition picture. We further find that this reconstruction enhances the interlayer electron-hole character of X-, giving rise to a giant quadratic Stark response. These findings reveal the nontrivial interplay between electron-electron correlations and electron-hole interactions underlying CFE formation, providing optical fingerprints of moiré Chern-ferromagnetic order and a microscopic basis for its optical control.

</details>


### [409] [A boson representation of the electronic structure calculation based on the quantum Krylov subspace algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21242)
*Hiroki Katow,Hisao Nakamura*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出以电子-空穴对玻色子为基本自由度的电子体系玻色子表示，用于降低量子子空间展开（QSE）的量子线路深度，并在氢链模型上验证其基态能量精度具有实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算有望拓展第一性原理可处理强关联固体现象的范围，而量子子空间展开（QSE）因数值稳定性优于其他变分方法受到关注，但其量子线路深度仍是实际应用的障碍。

Method: 构建电子体系的玻色子表示（boson representation），以电子-空穴对玻色子（electron-hole pair bosons）为基本自由度，并针对周期性氢链 \ce{(H2)_n} 的等距（equidistant）与二聚化（dimerized）两种构型，与费米子型 QSE 及 CCSD 进行基准对比。

Result: 尽管采用了显著的近似，玻色子 QSE 方案在基态能量上仍给出具有实际用用价值的精度。

Conclusion: 结果支持该玻色子 QSE 方案可推广应用于准二维与三维实际电子体系。

Abstract: Quantum calculations are expected to broaden the range of strongly correlated phenomena in solids accessible to first-principles calculations. The quantum Krylov diagonalization method, or in a broader sense, the quantum subspace expansion method (QSE) offers an advantage over other variational methods in terms of numerical stability. In this work, we propose a boson representation of electronic systems with the aim of reducing QSE quantum circuit depth, where electron-hole pair bosons serve as the fundamental degrees of freedom. We benchmark the boson representation in comparison with a corresponding fermionic QSE and CCSD for an equidistant and a dimerized configuration of a periodic hydrogen chain \ce{(H2)_n}. Despite the drastic approximation we reveal the boson QSE scheme provides practically useful accuracy in the ground state energy. Our results support its applicability to realistic quasi-two- and three-dimensional electronic systems.

</details>


### [410] [A Density Functional Tight Binding Model for Rapid Screening of Spin-Orbit-Coupling-Driven Topological Properties in Bismuth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21244)
*Nir Goldman,Ben Hourahine*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作构建了含显式自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）的铋（bismuth）可迁移密度泛函紧束缚（Density Functional Tight Binding, DFTB）模型，并用于铋烯（bismuthene）拓扑性质的高通量筛选，效率较DFT提升约三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Kohn-Sham密度泛函理论（Density Functional Theory, DFT）在探索决定拓扑性质的结构自由度时计算代价过高，难以支撑大规模筛选，因此需要低成本且可迁移的计算工具。

Method: 构建含显式自旋轨道耦合的铋DFTB模型，开展高通量计算以获得铋烯的应变—屈曲（strain-buckling）电子与拓扑性质相图，并通过声子态密度、多相能量排序、双层几何、带反转序参量及$\mathbb{Z}_2$不变量与DFT结果比对进行验证。

Result: 模型准确复现菱方相（rhombohedral phase）能带结构，识别出带隙闭合与带反转（band inversion）随结构参数变化的区域，并成功外推至训练集未包含的铋性质；拓扑预测与DFT一致，计算效率提升约三个数量级。

Conclusion: DFTB可作为严格的拓扑表征方法，为拓扑材料发现（topological materials discovery）中的大规模计算筛选提供可行工具，用以刻画物理化学性质与拓扑分类之间的复杂关系。

Abstract: We present a transferable Density Functional Tight Binding (DFTB) model for bismuth with explicit spin-orbit coupling that enables exploration of the structural degrees of freedom governing topological properties with minimal computational resources. We have used our DFTB model to run high-throughput calculations at a scale that would be significantly more expensive with standard Kohn-Sham Density Functional Theory (DFT) to determine a strain-buckling map of electronic and topological properties for bismuthene, where we identify regions of band gap closure and band inversion as a function of structural parameters. We show that our approach yields an accurate band structure for the rhombohedral phase, where small band gaps exist at or near time-reversal-invariant momentum (TRIM) points. We then demonstrate the transferability of our model to bismuth properties not included in our training data through determination of the rhombohedral phase phonon density of states and energetic ordering of additional bismuth solid phases. Finally, we have validated our topological predictions through comparison to DFT results for the optimized bilayer geometries, a band inversion order parameter, and the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ invariant as a function of monolayer unit cell length. Our efforts establish DFTB as a rigorous approach for topological characterization while yielding approximately three orders of magnitude increase in computational efficiency relative to DFT. Our model can thus serve as a computational tool for topological materials discovery by enabling large-scale computational screening to address the potentially complex relationship between physical-chemical properties and the resulting topological classification.

</details>


### [411] [A database of optical and thermal-radiative behaviour of materials under solar radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21281)
*Vasiliki E. Alexopoulou,Nikolaos Karalis,Konstantinos Asimakopoulos,Andrei Totu,Constantin Sandu,Paul-Peter Sotiriadis*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: SpaderOptics 是一个涵盖 21 种材料（金属、氧化物、半导体、聚合物、陶瓷、碳）的光学—热辐射性能数据库，整合文献中的波长依赖折射率与消光系数，并通过统一的物理计算链推导反射率、透射率、吸收率、发射率及太阳吸收通量与平衡温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太阳—物质相互作用（solar-matter interactions）在众多科学与工程应用中具有重要性，其建模需要在全太阳光谱（full solar spectrum）上保持一致的光学与热辐射（thermal-radiative）性质，而现有数据往往缺乏这种一致性与可追溯性。

Method: 数据库汇编来自文献的波长依赖折射率（refractive index）与消光系数（extinction coefficient），逐条保留各文献来源及其原始条件；再以单一基于物理的计算链（physics-based calculation chain），在不同入射角、平板厚度与温度下计算反射率、透射率、吸收率与发射率，并结合 AM0 太阳光谱（Air Mass Zero spectrum）给出聚光太阳辐射下的吸收、反射与透射通量。

Result: 给出了理论集总体（lumped body）与考虑热传导的平板体（slab body）两类平衡温度（equilibrium temperature）；数据限定于固态，且以各材料特定的温度上限为界；当前仅适用于外星（AM0）太阳光谱，并提供用于浏览与可视化的图形化 App（graphical App）。

Conclusion: SpaderOptics 提供了光谱一致、来源可追溯的光学与热辐射性质数据库及配套计算与可视化工具，但其适用范围目前限于固态材料与外星（AM0）太阳光照条件。

Abstract: Solar-matter interactions are important in many scientific and engineering applications. Modeling them needs consistent optical and thermal-radiative properties over the full solar spectrum. Here we present SpaderOptics, a database for 21 materials: metals, oxides, semiconductors, polymers, ceramics, and carbon. It compiles wavelength-dependent refractive indices and extinction coefficients from the literature. Each literature source is kept separate, together with its original conditions. One physics-based calculation chain turns these data into reflectivity, transmissivity, absorptivity, and emissivity for different incidence angles, slab thicknesses, and temperatures. Combined with the Air Mass Zero (AM0) solar spectrum, the properties give absorbed, reflected, and transmitted solar flux for concentrated sunlight. Equilibrium temperatures are given for both a theoretical lumped body and for a slab body, in which thermal conduction is considered. All data are limited to the solid state, up to a material-specific upper temperature limit. Currently, the database applies to extraterrestrial (AM0) sunlight only. A graphical App is also provided for exploring and visualizing the database.

</details>


### [412] [Accessing Electrode Edges: Upright Mounting for Site-Specific Cryogenic Atom Probe Tomography of Lithium-Ion Batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21357)
*Syeda Ramin Jannat,James O. Douglas,Neil Mulcahy,Lukas Worch,Aigerim Omirkhan,Zhenyu Guo,Yuanzhu Zhao,Maria-Magdalena Titirici,Ifan E. L. Stephens,Mary P. Ryan,Baptiste Gault,Michele Shelly Conroy*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合直立安装（upright-mounting）流程的冷冻原子探针层析（cryo-APT）工作流，借助等离子聚焦离子束（PFIB）在低温条件下实现电极边缘局域特征与液-固界面的定点制样与分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: cryo-APT 日益用于表征异质材料中高活性物种的三维分布，但针对局域特征与液-固界面的定点制样仍具挑战，尤其当感兴趣区域（ROIs）集中于常规安装几何难以触及的电极边缘时。

Method: 提出包含直立安装步骤的 cryo-APT 工作流，利用 PFIB 显微镜在冷冻条件下进行定点样品制备；先以电解质润湿的石墨电极验证，再应用于循环后石墨电极边缘富锂区域（Li 枝晶）的制样与分析。

Result: 成功制备并分析了同时包含边缘局域电解质沉积物及下方石墨与铜集流体的样品；在循环电极上成功定位并分析电极周边富集的 Li 枝晶所在富锂区域。

Conclusion: 该直立安装工作流可实现电极边缘局域特征的定点 cryo-APT 分析，并有望推广至更广泛材料体系中常规制样几何限制 ROI 可达性的情形。

Abstract: Cryogenic atom probe tomography (cryo-APT) is increasingly being used to map the three-dimensional distribution of highly reactive species in heterogeneous materials, including those found in battery systems. However, site-specific preparation of localised features and liquid-solid interfaces remains challenging, particularly when regions of interest (ROIs) are concentrated along electrode edges that are difficult to access using conventional mounting geometries. Here, we present a cryo-APT workflow incorporating an upright-mounting procedure that enables targeted specimen preparation of localised regions using plasma focused ion beam (PFIB) microscopy and milling under cryogenic conditions. We first demonstrate the approach using an electrolyte-wetted graphite electrode, successfully preparing and analysing a specimen containing both an edge-localised electrolyte deposit and the underlying graphite and Cu current collector. We then apply the workflow to a cycled graphite electrode, targeting Li dendrites concentrated around the electrode perimeter and successfully preparing and analysing the targeted Li-rich region. Beyond battery systems, this approach offers potential for site-specific cryo-APT of edge-localised features across a broader range of material systems, where conventional specimen preparation geometries may limit access to ROIs.

</details>


### [413] [Atomistic Insights into the All-Optical Switching of GdCo Alloys with Minimal Gd Concentration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21410)
*Paul-Iulian Gavriloaea,Jackson L. Ross,Richard F. L. Evans,Roy Chantrell,Stéphane Mangin,Oksana Chubykalo-Fesenko*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 原子自旋动力学模拟表明，在均匀加热模型中，当Gd与热浴耦合显著弱于Co时，远离磁补偿点、总Gd含量低至1.5 at.%的体系仍可实现稳健的单脉冲全光螺旋度无关翻转（AO-HIS），说明磁补偿并非超快全光翻转的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单脉冲全光螺旋度无关翻转（all-optical helicity-independent switching, AO-HIS）通常见于近磁补偿（magnetization compensation）的Gd基亚铁磁体，但近期实验在仅含少量Gd的Pt/Co/Pt界面异质结构中也观测到翻转，其微观机制与最低Gd浓度要求尚不明确。

Method: 采用原子自旋动力学模拟（atomistic spin-dynamics simulations），检验有效界面GdCo合金图像，并比较两种模型：Gd限域于表面层的三单层Co薄膜与三维均匀合金化GdCo模型；通过改变Co与Gd自旋-热浴耦合（spin-bath coupling）强度及脉冲参数，考察翻转行为。

Result: 在均匀加热（uniform-heating）模型中，当Gd的热浴耦合显著弱于Co时（如λCo = 0.02与λCo = 0.001），远离补偿点仍出现稳健的瞬态toggle型翻转，特定脉冲参数下总Gd含量低至1.5 at.%即可翻转；提高Gd含量可加速翻转，符合Gd作为角动量储库（angular-momentum reservoir）的解释，而稀薄界面Gd导致动力学更慢且更具随机性。

Conclusion: 磁补偿并非超快AO-HIS的基本要求，有效Gd自旋-热浴耦合是关键调控参数；与实验的定量对比尚需显式光学吸收、叠层相关热流、偶极场以及稳定终态判据。

Abstract: Single-pulse all-optical helicity-independent switching (AO-HIS) is most commonly observed in Gd-based ferrimagnets near magnetization compensation. Recent experiments, however, demonstrated switching in Pt/Co/Pt-based heterostructures containing only a small amount of Gd at a Co/Pt interface. Here, we use atomistic spin-dynamics simulations to test an effective interfacial GdCo-alloy picture and to determine how species-dependent spin-bath coupling controls the minimum Gd concentration required for reversal. We compare a three-monolayer Co film with Gd confined to the surface layer and a three-dimensional uniformly alloyed GdCo model. Within the present uniform-heating model, robust transient toggle-like reversal is obtained far from compensation when the Gd coupling to the thermal bath is substantially weaker than that of Co; for λCo = 0.02 and λCo = 0.001, reversal occurs down to 1.5 at. % total Gd for selected pulse parameters. Increasing the Gd content accelerates reversal, consistent with the interpretation of Gd as an angular-momentum reservoir, whereas dilute interfacial Gd produces slower and more stochastic dynamics. These results show that compensation is not a fundamental requirement for ultrafast AO-HIS and identify the effective Gd spin-bath coupling as a key control parameter. Quantitative comparison with experiment will require explicit optical absorption, stack-dependent heat flow, dipolar fields, and a stable-final-state switching criterion.

</details>
